### [1] [COMED: The Missing Middle Between Routing and Collaboration in Multi-LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26913)
*Norah Alballa,Wenxuan Zhang,Salma Kharrat,Fares Fourati,Zafar Ayyub Qazi,Mohamed Elhoseiny,Marco Canini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: COMED提出了一种"后锚点"控制器，在多LLM推理中作为路由（routing）和密集协作（dense collaboration）之间的中间地带，通过Accept/Verify/Collaborate三阶段策略选择性地决定何时值得调用跨模型协作，从而在所有16个开源权重设置和前沿模型测试中均提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在两种极端：路由在选择模型后就终止（不处理是否信任所选答案的问题），而密集协作对每个查询都调用对等模型（会破坏原本正确的答案）。论文观察到协作具有非单调性：同伴既可能挽救无人能单独解决的问题，也可能破坏原本正确的答案。缺乏对"何时值得协作"的显式决策机制。

Method: COMED（Controlled Model Escalation for Multi-LLM Deliberation）是一个后锚点控制器，运行在锚点模型已生成答案之后，使用三个互补信号做三阶段决策：(1) 锚点自一致性（SC）低则直接升级到Collaborate；(2) 高SC且路由边际大则Accept；(3) 其余进入Verify阶段，用置信门控的同伴分歧检查决定是否升级。论文还形式化了"rescue-harm分解"定理：协作改善当且仅当qr > (1-q)h。默认Top-1设置每查询最多调用2个不同模型，使用summary-routing作为协作原语。

Result: 在16个开源权重设置（Medical-4和Bal-3两个模型池×MedQA/JAMA/GPQA/MP四个基准×固定/路由两种锚点机制）上，COMED在所有设置中均超越对应锚点，平均提升+4.6pp，MedQA最高提升+10.7pp。在HLE基准上，使用前沿模型（GPT-5.5等）将GPT-5.5从23.1%提升至28.1%，超越密集协作。Always Collab在10/16设置中净效果为负，而COMED在所有设置中均为净正。COMED使用比密集协作少约33%的解码token，且每查询最多调用2个模型。

Conclusion: COMED将多LLM推理的核心问题从"调用哪个模型"转变为"何时值得额外跨模型交互"，通过rescue-harm分解为选择性协作提供了原则性分析框架。其Accept/Verify/Collaborate控制层在避免密集协作的脆弱性的同时，统一改善了固定锚点和路由锚点，确立了选择性后锚点控制作为路由与始终在线协作之间的关键中间层。

Abstract: No single Large Language Model (LLM) is uniformly reliable across queries, motivating multi-model inference systems that either route among models or combine their outputs. However, routing stops after selecting an initial model, while dense collaboration invokes peers on every query. We show that collaboration is non-monotonic: peers can recover failures that no model solves alone, but can also corrupt initially correct answers. We introduce COMED (Controlled Model Escalation for Multi-LLM Deliberation), a post-anchor controller for selective cross-model collaboration. COMED uses anchor self-consistency, router margin, and a lightweight peer probe to accept confident answers, verify ambiguous cases, and escalate only when collaboration is likely beneficial. We formalize this trade-off with a rescue-harm decomposition showing that selective collaboration improves when rescued errors outweigh collaboration-induced harms. Across medical, scientific, and general reasoning benchmarks, COMED improves fixed and routed anchors in all 16 open-weight settings, with gains up to +10.7 percentage points on MedQA while invoking fewer models and using fewer decoded tokens than dense collaboration. On HLE with frontier models, COMED improves GPT-5.5 from 23.1% to 28.1%, outperforming dense collaboration and achieving the best results.

</details>


### [2] [Experts Rise Where LLMs Disagree: Using Cross-Model Disagreement to Target Expert Effort in LLM Codebook Revision for Large-Scale Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26926)
*Zeyu He,Zhuqian Zhou,Kirk Vanacore,Rene F. Kizilcec,Ting-Hao 'Kenneth' Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用多LLM标注分歧来定位专家精力的工作流，将专家数月的代码簿修订缩短为数天。在辅导会话转录数据上，对比了三种专家反馈方式（代码簿验证、问答、理由标注），发现理由标注（RL）效果最佳，标注准确率64.9%，优于耗时六个月专家修订的代码簿（57.8%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模文本标注中，开发稳健的代码簿通常需要专家耗费数月时间。作者探索能否利用LLM间的标注分歧作为信号，将专家精力精准导向最困难的标注案例，从而加速代码簿修订。

Method: 使用六个来自不同提供商的LLM独立标注未标注数据，通过归一化投票熵计算样本级和类别级分歧。三种专家反馈方式：(1) 代码簿验证（CV）—专家审核编辑LLM提出的代码簿修订；(2) 问答（QA）—专家回答LLM生成的关于分歧案例的澄清问题；(3) 理由标注（RL）—专家标注分歧样本并提供理由。在辅导转录标注项目中，与六个月专家修订的最终代码簿对比评估。

Result: 在4,032个评估样本上：RL获得最高准确率64.9%（加权F1=0.658），最佳QA设置准确率60.5%，均超过专家最终代码簿的57.8%（加权F1=0.583）。AI自动修订和CV未能稳定超越初始代码簿。RL仅需每位专家约2.8小时即可完成。跨LLM分歧与标注难度高度相关：三模型全同意时准确率66.2%，全分歧时仅12.8%。

Conclusion: 研究证明LLM分歧可有效识别困难标注案例，将有限专家精力集中于分歧样本进行理由标注，能在数小时内产出优于专家数月手工修订的代码簿。未来研究应拓展至更多领域、代码簿和专家，并探索聚焦困难案例对专家学习的影响。

Abstract: Large-scale text annotation brings expert insight to millions of documents, often through a codebook that AI annotators follow. Developing a robust codebook, however, takes months. Large language models (LLMs) could speed this process by applying an early codebook to the data, surfacing cases with strong LLM disagreement, and eliciting expert feedback to address them. We examined three ways experts can provide feedback for LLM codebook revision: (i) editing LLM-generated revisions driven by cross-LLM disagreement (Codebook Verifying), (ii) answering questions about LLM disagreements (Question Answering), and (iii) labeling disagreement cases with rationales (Rationale Labeling). Experiments on thousands of tutoring-session transcripts show that Rationale Labeling yielded the highest LLM-labeling accuracy (64.9%) against expert labels, outperforming the expert-revised codebook (57.8%). The best Question Answering setting also outperformed it (60.5%). Our work shows that LLMs can be used to strategically target expert attention, shortening months of codebook revision to days without sacrificing labeling performance.

</details>


### [3] [Recognized but Not Produced: A Generation Benchmark for Culturally Specific Kinship Terms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26942)
*Sahil Pardasani,Madhusudan Singh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型在印地语、泰米尔语、韩语三种非西方语言中亲缘称谓的"识别 vs 生成"差距问题，构建了一个基于开放生成的文化特异性亲缘称谓生成基准，并配套匹配的四选一选项支持选择基线。通过对五个开源权重模型（GPT-OSS-120B、Sarvam-M、Llama-3.3-70B、GLM-5.1、Kimi-K2.6）在生日祝福和婚礼邀请两种交际任务下的4500次生成进行评测，揭示出生成准确率远低于选择题准确率的"评估格式鸿沟"，并发现父系优势存在显著的语言特异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献主要通过多选题基准评估LLM的多语言亲缘称谓理解能力，将其视为识别问题。然而，选择显示的术语与在自然完整消息中生成该术语对模型提出了不同要求。即使模型能够选择文化特异性的术语，它是否能在自然消息中可靠地生成它仍是一个未解的问题。此外，英语中心的多语言资源常将性别、年龄或血统特异性概念合并为更广泛的类别（如英语的uncle或grandmother无法区分印地语中父亲兄长Tau/Tauji与父亲幼弟Chacha/Chachaji的区别），需要专门评估。

Method: 构建包含25个亲缘关系的基准（涵盖祖父母、父母兄弟姐妹及其配偶、兄弟姐妹子女、姻亲关系等），跨越印地语、泰米尔语、韩语三种语言。设计两种提示特异性级别：L1（使用英语上位词，如uncle/aunt）和L3（完整指定关系，如father's elder brother）。两种交际任务：生日祝福和婚礼邀请。每条提示运行三次，共产生4500次生成响应。评估方式采用基于规则的字符串匹配器（检查生成的本地文字中是否包含接受的亲缘称谓）。配套构建77个四选一选项支持的选择基线（仅对GPT-OSS-120B和Llama-3.3-70B评估），评估模型从显示的候选术语中选择正确术语的能力。生成使用temperature=0.7、top-p=0.95；选择使用temperature=0。还分析了父系-母系配对的准确率差异，并以泰米尔语共享术语对作为测量变异的对照。

Result: （1）L3生成准确率分布广泛且上限较低：GLM-5.1为72.29%，Kimi-K2.6为48.70%，Sarvam-M为40.48%，GPT-OSS-120B为35.06%，Llama-3.3-70B为24.24%。（2）选项支持选择与生成之间存在显著差距：GPT-OSS-120B选择准确率为90.67%但生成仅36.00%（差距54.67个百分点），Llama-3.3-70B选择为77.92%而生成仅24.24%（差距53.68个百分点），两者均远超25%的四选一随机基线。（3）任务差异较小（生日44.07% vs 婚礼44.24%）。（4）父系-母系优势呈语言特异性：印地语中父系优势显著（+43.3点），韩语中微弱或逆转（+4.0点），泰米尔语对照对为+9.2点。（5）Llama-3.3-70B在所有162条韩语L3响应中均未产生任何Hangul字符，表现出系统性的文字生成失败。（6）失败模式分析：2758次失败中，57.90%包含其他清单术语，24.62%包含目标文字但无清单术语，17.01%不包含目标语言文字。（7）姻亲关系尤其困难（如husband's sister's husband仅4.44%），但elder brother's wife达58.33%。

Conclusion: 研究得出三个核心结论：（1）即使关系被明确指定（L3），文化特异性亲缘称谓的生成仍然困难；（2）选择题与开放生成之间存在巨大评估格式鸿沟，选择成绩无法代表真正的词汇知识完整性，因为四选一条件显示了候选术语且无需文字生成；（3）父系-母系差异是模型和语言特异性的模式，并非一致的多语言偏见。这些发现表明，多语言亲缘称谓能力应通过生成任务而非仅通过多选题准确率来评估。研究还指出未来工作应包括加入更接近的干扰项、平衡答案位置、缺失答案项、自由回忆、文字控制等评估设计，以及对所有评估模型进行选择测试，并补充更小规模和语言专家基线。

Abstract: Current literature evaluates large language models (LLMs) on multilingual kinship understanding using multiple choice benchmarks, treating it as a recognition problem. We instead prompt five open weight LLMs to generate kinship terms in three non Western languages (Hindi, Tamil, and Korean) across two communicative tasks and pair this with a matched option-supported selection baseline. On identical relation language cells, GPT OSS120B selects the correct term in 90.67% of 75 valid cells but produces an accepted term in 36.00% of the corresponding attempts; Llama 3.370B shows the same pattern (77.92% versus 24.24%). Since the four-option condition displays the candidate terms and does not require script production, the difference is interpreted as an evaluation format gap rather than direct proof that lexical knowledge is intact. On explicitly specified L3 prompts, accuracy varies sharply, from GLM-5.1 at 72.29% to Llama-3.370B at 24.24%. The paternal-lineage advantage is language specific; it is large in Hindi but weak or reversed in Korean, while Tamil shared-term pairs provide a control for measurement variation. These results show that culturally specific kinship generation remains difficult even when the relationship is explicitly stated and motivate generation-based evaluation alongside multiple-choice testing.

</details>


### [4] [Count Evidence, Not Sentences: Tempered Evidence Fusion of LLM Judgments for Long-Text Value Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27165)
*Yuhe Wu,Rui Qian,Guangyu Wang,Yuran Chen,Yuanchao Zhu,Junjie Yang,Zhengheng Li,Jiulin Cai,Tianyi Zhang,Zihan Dong,Jiaxin Liu,Yujie Chen,Guang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的证据融合规则TEF（Tempered Evidence Fusion），用于从长文本社交媒体帖子中测量公众价值取向。TEF将句子级LLM判断视为局部软决策，通过熵归一化信息增益加权对数几率（log-odds），使不确定句子的贡献趋近于零，同时保留决定性证据的贝叶斯最优权重。同时构建了MIND基准数据集（8,358条中英帖子，六个价值维度）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长社交媒体帖子通常混合背景、引用、让步等非立场内容与少数无态度表述。现有的直接标注（标签生成）方法容易过度自信，而多数投票或软投票将确定性与不确定性句子同等对待，可能导致多数不确定句子压倒少数决定性句子。如何让决定性证据主导最终判断、不确定句子被合理折扣，是亟需解决的问题。

Method: 将长文本价值测量建模为决策融合问题。每个句子由LLM产生类别概率分布，TEF为每个句子计算熵归一化信息增益权重 $w_i = 1 - H(\mathbf{p}_i)/\log K$，并用one-vs-rest对数几率 $g(p) = \log(p/(1-p))$ 作为证据打分，最终文档级得分为 $\operatorname{Score}(c) = \sum_i w_i \log(p_i^{(c)}/(1-p_i^{(c)}))$，取最大者为输出。理论证明该方法保持信息保留性并实现温度化贝叶斯池化（tempered Bayesian pooling），对不确定句子产生软剔除（soft censoring）效果。同时构建MIND基准：8,358条帖子（中英双语，五个年份，六个价值维度），经三阶段（采集、标注、LLM智能体驱动的数据演化）构建。

Result: 在五个LLM（Qwen2.5-7B、LLaMA3-8B、Qwen3-14B、DeepSeek-V3.2、GPT-4o-mini）和两种语言上的实验显示，TEF比Direct、Majority Vote、Soft Vote中最强基线平均提升4.5个准确率点和4.6个宏观F1点。在120次维度级比较中116次为最佳或并列最佳。消融实验表明，熵加权和对数几率变换互补，缺一不可（在DeepSeek-V3.2英语环境维度上，移除任一部分准确率下降约23点）。TEF在Qwen2.5-7B上实现最低的期望校准误差（ECE）和过度自信差距。提示词扰动（详细/极简）下TEF仍保持最优。代价方面，TEF与投票方法查询量相同，仅比Direct多约三倍开销，但这是唯一能稳定将开销转化为性能提升的方法。

Conclusion: TEF将长文本价值测量形式化为句子级软决策融合，通过温度化贝叶斯后验对每个句子的对数几率按信息增益加权，在保留决定性证据权重的同时抑制不确定句子。TEF在跨模型、跨语言、跨提示词条件下均优于基线，并提供更好的校准，适用于需要选择性人工审核的科学测量场景。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to measure public value orientations from long social media posts, yet such posts often mix background, quotations, concessions, and only a few stance-bearing sentences. Existing approaches either ask the model to predict a document-level label directly, which can be overconfident, or aggregate sentence-level predictions by majority or soft voting, which treat uncertain and decisive sentences as equally informative. We formulate long-text value measurement as a decision-fusion problem and propose Tempered Evidence Fusion (TEF), a training-free rule that weights each sentence's log-odds by its normalized information gain, as derived from a generalized Bayesian posterior. This makes the fused score nearly vanish for uncertain sentences while preserving the Bayes-optimal weight of decisive evidence. We further introduce Multi-event Insight Network Dimensions (MIND), a benchmark of 8,358 Chinese and English posts spanning five years of public events and six value dimensions. On MIND, TEF outperforms the strongest baseline among Direct, Majority Vote, and Soft Vote by an average of 4.5 accuracy points and 4.6 macro-F1 points across five LLMs and two languages. MIND dataset and code are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [5] [Phonemizing User-Generated Text: A Benchmark, Taxonomy, and Compositional Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27205)
*MinJu Jeon,Younghan Park,Han Sung Park,Jong-Hwan Kim,Dong-Jin Kim,Hoyeon Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了第一个面向用户生成文本（UGT）的图到音（G2P）基准 UGTPhon，覆盖英语、越南语和韩语，并设计了一种基于检索增强与分阶段解码的组合式 G2P 方法。该方法通过精确匹配查找提供规范形式提示，并以规范形式作为中间目标进行解码，在两种主干网络（ByT5 与 Qwen2.5-0.5B）上一致降低了非规范词的音素错误率，且 0.5B 规模变体可与更大的前沿 LLM 相媲美。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 TTS 系统越来越多地处理用户生成文本（如缩写、谐音替代、网络俚语等），其发音需从规范形式而非表面形式推断。现有 G2P 模型在规范词典上表现稳健，但在非规范 UGT 上会出现系统性退步，最高规范-非规范 PER 差距可达 66.8 个百分点，因此亟需针对 UGT 的 G2P 基准与有效方法。

Method: 方法包含三个组件：(1) 上下文输入编码——用语言标签和管道标记包裹目标词，将每个目标作为独立前向传播处理；(2) 检索增强的规范形式推断——构建基于训练集 (G_nc, G_c) 对的键值存储，通过精确匹配查找为输入提供规范形式提示（Hit/Miss 前缀），并可选地使用 GPT-5.4-mini 进行数据扩充；(3) 分阶段解码——在自回归生成中以 <Target> G_c <Phoneme> P_c 的结构，将规范形式作为显式中间目标。对规范词则直接生成音素序列。该方法骨干无关，在 ByT5-small 和 Qwen2.5-0.5B 上均适用。

Result: 在 UGTPhon 上，ByT5 主干下非规范 PER 从 29.8/43.2/47.5（英/越/韩）降至 22.5/26.7/32.0；Qwen2.5-0.5B 进一步降至 14.3/20.0/32.4。配对自举检验所有六个对比的置信区间均排除零。检索存储本身接近 oracle 上限（英差 2.1 点、越差 0.8 点）。在 9/12 个 Pass-through 子类上取得最佳。在下游 TTS-ASR 评测中（英语 100 句、越南语 297 句），ByT5 版本获得最低英语 WER（0.359），Qwen 版本获得越南语预测输入系统中的最低 WER（0.640），均与 PER 排序一致。0.5B Qwen 变体在性能上与 GPT-4o、Gemini-3-flash、Claude-Sonnet-4.6 等前沿 LLM 相当或更优。

Conclusion: 论文将 UGT 表征为 G2P 必须组合"表面对规范形式推断"与"音素化"的场景，并提供了首个涵盖三种语言多样性的 UGTPhon 基准与诊断框架。检索增强的组合式基线通过精确匹配提示与分阶段解码使规范形式推断显式化，在三种语言上均改善了匹配监督骨干的表现。增益在 Reconstruct 类型上最大，0.5B 变体可与前沿 LLM 竞争。仍存在的局限包括：仅覆盖三种书写系统、未包含主观 MOS 评测、未涉及零样本扩展与数据存储刷新的动态问题等。

Abstract: Text-to-speech systems increasingly process user-generated text (UGT) such as ppl and imo, whose pronunciation must be inferred from the canonical rather than surface form. We introduce UGTPhon, the first grapheme-to-phoneme (G2P) benchmark for UGT in English, Vietnamese, and Korean, together with an inference-grounded taxonomy for fine-grained diagnosis. Existing G2P models and frontier LLMs exhibit a systematic canonical-to-non-canonical performance gap, reaching up to 66.8 PER points. As a benchmark baseline, we propose a simple compositional G2P approach that incorporates canonical-form evidence through exact-match lookup and staged decoding. Across matched ByT5 and Qwen2.5-0.5B backbones, explicit canonical-form modeling consistently reduces non-canonical G2P errors. The 0.5B variant also performs competitively with much larger few-shot frontier LLMs, highlighting the benefit of explicitly modeling canonical-form inference for UGT phonemization.

</details>


### [6] [Meet, Compare, or Abstain: LatWeave for Deterministic Multi-Hop Question Answering on Knowledge Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27225)
*Yuze Ren,Shaoheng Fan,Tao Wang,Yabo Yan,Han Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LatWeave提出了一种基于多维知识格（knowledge lattice）的多跳问答方法，将多跳QA编译为三个确定性算子：meet（约束求交）、compare（格序比较）和abstain（结构性拒答）。LLM仅用于知识抽取和查询规划，回答路径零LLM调用、零任务训练、完全可审计。该方法在六个公开基准上刻画了"工作包络"（operating envelope）：在知识完整时近乎无损（MetaQA any-hit 0.9975），在模板化多跳任务上表现优异（2WikiMultihopQA EM 0.865），在长链开放文本和提取覆盖不足时如实报告性能下降，并在信息不完整时实现结构性拒答（IIRC abstention准确率0.971）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率性QA系统（包括LLM本身、RAG和训练的多跳检索器）将"已知什么"和"如何推理"混为一体，导致：1）幻觉无法根除；2）证据链不可审计；3）系统在不知道时也会给出答案。作者认为根源在于"工程级可审计性"——来源可追溯，但从证据到结论的推理仍是黑箱。作者主张"机制级可审计性"——答案本身是逐步展开的meet链，每一步都是可独立验证的格代数操作。

Method: 1）知识格模型：定义偏序集、维度和乘积格（product lattice），每个实例绑定到乘积格坐标，谓词必须携带source_text（反幻觉规则）。2）三个确定性算子：(a) Meet：对每维取最大下界（GLB）并组合坐标，冲突触发abstain；(b) Compare：沿格序执行布尔比较、极值选择和完整集返回；(c) Abstain：约束冲突或命中集为空时结构性拒答——非低置信度回退，而是格语义的直接后果。3）两阶段架构：构建时（离线，一次性）LLM从问题无关文档中提取类型化谓词，经去重、规范化和维度白名单治理后构建格；查询时（在线）LLM规划器将问题编译为类型化计划，确定性核执行三个算子。4）查询落地四级（L0-L3）：从自由生成到每跳重规划，Hotpotwall配图内部化了L1/L2。5）评估协议（双率/双精度框架）：将recall measure（可回答问题上的EM）与prior measure（abstain精度×非泄漏率）分离，使"何时不答"可报告、可消融、可回归测试。

Result: 主要结果：1）2WikiMultihopQA（held-out n=1,258）：EM 0.865，F1* 0.872，resolved 92.2%；bridge_comparison类EM 0.975，comparison类0.888；相对同协议Naive RAG（0.198）和最强发表graph-RAG值HipPoRAG 2（0.650）分别提升337%和33%。2）MetaQA测试（n=39,093）：any-hit 0.9975（1跳0.9990，2跳0.9983，3跳0.9955），在零训练条件下与全监督KBQA方法持平或超越；三跳衰减跨度仅0.0035，远好于监督方法。3）边界数据集如实报告退化：HotpotQA EM 0.1687（90.6%残余断链缺最终答案边）、MuSiQue EM 0.0248（48.3%在第一跳断裂）、FRAMES dev EM 0.0256（或acle上限仅4.9%）。4）IIRC abstention（held-out n=130）：abstain准确率0.971，泄漏率0.029，tune pool零漂移；价值清洗消融使泄漏率从0.052降至0.017（-67%），EM从0.0156升至0.0279（+79%）。5）可扩展性：2Wiki含120万实例，构建词法扫描235 ms，检索p50低于18.7 ms，p95低于50 ms。

Conclusion: LatWeave将多跳QA编译为三个确定性格算子，在工作包络内确定性执行无性能损失，失败以诚实拒答收尾。该方法已在实际生产环境中部署测试。确定性推理并非要取代LLM，而是将回答路径从概率黑箱提升为可审计白箱。核心贡献：1）知识格的机制级形式化与系统实现，回答路径零LLM调用零任务训练；2）上界证据——在完整或模板化知识下近乎无损；3）边界证据与归因——将退化逐层归因于格代数之外的原因；4）拒答作为一等能力——双率评估框架在IIRC上得到结构性拒答证实。

Abstract: Probabilistic question-answering systems -- whether large language models (LLMs) themselves, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), or trained multi-hop retrievers -- conflate "what is known" and "how to reason" into a single probabilistic computation: hallucination cannot be eradicated, evidence chains cannot be audited, and the system answers even when it does not know. We present LatWeave, which organizes knowledge into a multidimensional knowledge lattice and compiles multi-hop QA into three deterministic operators -- meet (constraint intersection), compare (lattice-order comparison), and abstain (structural abstention); LLMs appear only on the construction side (one-shot extraction) and the query-planning side, while the answer-generation path is zero-LLM, zero-task-training, and auditable end to end -- so that question answering over Web-published knowledge becomes reproducible item by item. Rather than claiming across-the-board SOTA, we characterize the operating envelope of this paradigm on six public benchmarks: when knowledge is complete (MetaQA, 39,093 questions) meet chains are near-lossless over three hops (any-hit 0.9975, on par with fully supervised KBQA); on templated multi-hop home ground (2WikiMultihopQA held-out n=1,258) EM 0.865, well above published structure-augmented RAG reproductions; on open-text deep composition (MuSiQue) and extraction-coverage gaps (HotpotQA) we report degradation honestly and attribute it to causes outside the lattice-algebra layer; and when information is incomplete (IIRC) we achieve structural abstention with abstain accuracy 0.971 and leak rate 0.029. Within the operating envelope, deterministic execution pays no performance penalty, and every step on the answer path can be recomputed -- precisely the source of end-to-end auditability.

</details>


### [7] [Ruby-ASR: Evidence-Preserving Supervision for Joint Orthographic and Lexical-Reading Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27289)
*Hao Shi,Yun Liu,Xuehao Yang,Jun Liu,Chuanbo Hua,Xuanjun Chen,Lianbo Liu,Shiao Zhu,Zixiong Su*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Ruby-ASR,一种面向日语 ASR 的"逐词假名注音(ruby)"监督方法,将传统正写法转写目标细化为"正写法-词汇读音"的局部绑定序列(如「今日[きょう]」),以解决正写法监督下不同读音被映射到同一目标(目标级读音坍缩)的问题。基于 Qwen3-ASR 框架,结合 mora 级 CTC 辅助单调读音监督,在五个日语基准上提升词汇读音识别能力,同时保持正写法转写性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日语正写法与读音并非一一对应,同一书写形式可对应多个读音。传统正写法 ASR 将不同读音的语音映射到同一目标,导致读音信息在监督信号中丢失,后处理 G2P 仅基于文本无法可靠恢复语音实际读音。

Method: 1. 将训练目标从纯正写法序列改为局部绑定的 ruby 序列(O_j[R_j]形式),读音标准化为平假名,可在推断时确定性恢复正写法和读音两种视图;2. 采用 Qwen3-ASR 作为语音-语言主干,自回归预测 ruby 序列;3. 引入 mora 级 CTC 作为辅助单调读音监督,联合损失 L = (1-λ)L_ruby + λL_CTC;4. 通过证据约束的闭集标注管道(多 ASR 过滤 + CTC 集成对齐 + OpenJTalk 候选集)自动构建 ruby 目标;5. 训练两个变体:Ruby-ASR-sub(字幕风格)和 Ruby-ASR-ver(逐字风格)。

Result: 五个日语基准(B5K、CSJ、Book、CV8、TEDx)上:Ruby-ASR-sub 加权 Raw CER 8.51%(最优),Ruby-ASR-ver 加权 SA-CER 6.59%(最优);加权 Kana CER 分别 3.75%/4.01%,优于 Qwen3-ASR(5.74%)和 Kana-Whisper(4.66%);在正写法识别正确的跨词上,两变体读音精确恢复率约 97%;G2P-hard 子集上 B5K 的 Kana CER 从 oracle G2P 的 6.07% 降至 1.59%,句级精确读音准确率达 57.9%。

Conclusion: Ruby-ASR 通过 ruby 形式监督在不牺牲正写法可读性的前提下显著提升日语词汇读音识别能力,尤其在传统 G2P 无法恢复的难例上效果突出;主要局限在于罕见/未见的正写法-读音配对;模型与推理代码已开源。

Abstract: Conventional Japanese automatic speech recognition (ASR) is supervised by an orthographic transcript, although the same written form can correspond to different lexical readings realized in speech. Such utterances receive an identical target, so their reading distinction is absent from the supervision interface and cannot be recovered reliably by post-hoc text-only grapheme-to-phoneme conversion. We present Ruby-ASR, which refines the conventional target into a span-bound orthographic--lexical-reading sequence. Unlike separate full-sentence orthographic and phonological outputs, the ruby representation locally binds each written span to its realized reading and permits deterministic recovery of both views. We instantiate the target under subtitle-style and verbatim-style transcription conventions using a Qwen3-ASR backbone; a mora-level CTC objective provides auxiliary monotonic reading supervision. The experimental results across five Japanese benchmarks show that refining the recognition target can improve lexical-reading recovery without sacrificing readable orthographic transcription. We release the checkpoints and inference code.

</details>


### [8] [Neither Silence nor Overlap Is Failure: Intent-Conditioned Evaluation of Turn-Taking in Full-Duplex Spoken Dialogue Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27372)
*Kian Shamsaie,Iman Modarressi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TACT基准，用基于说话者意图条件化的连续概率评分（twCRPS）替代全双工语音对话模型中二元固定窗口的轮替评估，涵盖9728个回合和73.2小时数据，并证明其在与人类判断一致性上显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的全双工语音对话模型评估采用二元固定窗口规则，仅奖励即时响应或沉默，忽视了沉默与重叠的适当性取决于说话者潜在意图这一会话分析中的核心观点。这种评估盲点导致模型退化为"快速响应一切、永不提前插入"的策略，无法适应不同说话者的行为模式。

Method: 1) 构建TACT基准：9728回合、73.2小时，来自5个公开双人语料库，每回合包含多轮历史、说话者记忆画像和6类意图后验；2) 标注6类意图（ans/ref/rhe/hold/bck/abn），由3-5名标注者+校准LLM法官融合生成意图后验；3) 提出阈值加权连续排序概率评分（twCRPS），权重由意图条件化时序核拟合人类话语转换偏移分布推导；4) 证明其有界性、严格适当性、二元约简和意图混合可识别性；5) 评估11个系统并进行记忆置换消融、公平性切片分析。

Result: 最佳系统gpt-realtime-2得0.47，人类上限0.86；TACT与人类判断的Spearman相关性达0.81，而二元指标仅0.46；模型在反射类/修辞类/话轮持有类意图上严重退化（得分为人类的1/2至1/10），且记忆置换消融显示模型行为几乎对说话者画像不变（JSD₂≤0.024），而人类行为显著变化（JSD₂=0.23）；排名与二元v1指标Spearman相关性仅0.55。

Conclusion: TACT将二元固定窗口评估替换为基于人类话语转换偏移律、说话者记忆和校准后验的严格适当、意图条件化的twCRPS评分。"何时发言"本质上是对说话者潜在意图的推理问题，而非延迟问题。

Abstract: Benchmarks for full-duplex spoken dialogue models score turn-taking with binary fixed-window rules that reward immediate response or silence by completeness of the prior turn. We argue that the appropriateness of a response offset, whether delayed silence or anticipatory overlap, is conditional on the speaker's latent intent, identifiable only from that speaker's behavior. We introduce TACT, a benchmark of 9,728 episodes and 73.2 hours from five dyadic corpora; each episode carries dialogue history, a per-speaker memory profile, and an annotator-derived posterior over six intent classes. Scoring replaces binary windows with a strictly proper threshold-weighted continuous ranked probability score whose weights are intent-conditioned timing kernels fitted to human floor-transfer-offset distributions, proving boundedness, consistency, and binary reduction. Across eleven systems the best model reaches 0.47 against a human topline of 0.86, is nearly invariant to speaker profiles, and TACT agrees with human judgments at Spearman 0.81 versus 0.46 for binary metrics.

</details>


### [9] [Planned Test-Time Scaling with Coordinated Reasoning Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27374)
*Xueqing Wu,Langxing Bai,Hritik Bansal,Po-Nien Kung,Shuo Li,Hao Liu,Nanyun Peng,Kai-Wei Chang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Planned Test-Time Scaling (PTTS)框架，通过规划器-执行器的分解结构，在测试时显式协调多条推理分支走向互补的推理路径，避免重复采样的冗余问题。在五个数学推理基准上，该方法相较重复采样在pass@64上最高带来13.4点的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重复采样作为测试时扩展的主流方法，各分支独立采样，容易产生冗余尝试而无法充分利用额外推理计算，尤其是在RL训练后策略分布收窄导致pass@k退化的问题更为突出，亟需显式的分支协调机制。

Method: 提出PTTS框架，将k个分支的联合策略分解为规划器（生成k个解题大纲）和执行器（基于大纲生成完整解答）。具体实例化为两个变体：PTTS-ZS利用基础模型在单次自回归过程中联合生成所有大纲；PTTS-RL则使用GRPO直接对规划器针对pass@k奖励进行优化，并采用截断执行策略（4k token）以提升训练效率并锐化奖励信号。理论方面证明PTTS严格泛化重复采样，并在风格化设置中证明其能促进互补推理模式的覆盖，以更快的指数速率缩小残差。

Result: 在MATH-500、AIME 2024-2026、HMMT-Feb26五个数学推理基准上，使用Qwen3-1.7B和4B模型：PTTS-ZS相较重复采样在pass@64上最高提升6.7点；PTTS-RL进一步将增益提升至最高13.4点。PTTS-RL的pass@32即超过重复采样的pass@64，计算效率翻倍。消融实验表明最优分支因子为k=4，最优截断预算为4k token，小型规划器可有效引导大型执行器，且PTTS与多样性导向RL训练（如e3）互补。

Conclusion: PTTS提供了一种通过协调推理分支来提升测试时扩展效果的原则性框架。其零样本和可训练两种实例化均带来显著性能增益，证明了显式协调和预算分配是释放测试时扩展潜力的关键。该方法与执行器侧的改进互补，具有良好的可扩展性和迁移性。

Abstract: Test-time scaling with parallel branches is widely adopted to improve performance on challenging reasoning tasks. The predominant approach, repeated sampling, draws branches independently from a single policy, which can produce redundant attempts and thereby limit the gains from additional inference compute. To address this limitation, we propose Planned Test-Time Scaling (PTTS), which replaces independent sampling with a coordinated joint policy: a planner generates a solution outline for each branch, steering the branches toward distinct reasoning paths, and an executor produces a full solution conditioned on each outline. Formally, we show that PTTS strictly generalizes repeated sampling and, in a stylized setting, provably promotes coverage of complementary reasoning modes and yields better pass@k scaling. We instantiate PTTS on top of strong reasoning models, keeping them fixed as executors while replacing repeated sampling with PTTS inference to further enhance test-time scaling. Concretely, we develop two variants: PTTS-ZS prompts a model to jointly generate outlines for all branches in a single autoregressive pass, while PTTS-RL directly optimizes the planner against the pass@k reward using truncated execution rollouts for efficient training and a sharper reward signal. Across five mathematical reasoning benchmarks with Qwen3-1.7B and 4B, PTTS-ZS improves pass@64 over repeated sampling by up to 6.7 points, while PTTS-RL further increases the gain to up to 13.4 points. Further analysis indicates that broader coverage of distinct reasoning paths contributes to these gains. Overall, PTTS provides a general framework for improving test-time scaling by coordinating reasoning branches, with zero-shot and trainable instantiations that yield substantial performance gains.

</details>


### [10] [Uncheatable Eval: Dynamic Compression-Based Evaluation of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27510)
*Kaifeng Tan,Yudong Li,Linlin Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Uncheatable Eval，一种基于无损压缩率的动态评估基准，通过定期收集新发布文本来评估基础语言模型，以缓解训练数据污染风险。在80个模型、14个文本类别上的实验发现：压缩性能随模型规模呈现幂律缩放趋势；注意力、混合和循环架构在长上下文下压缩率变化模式不同；更低的压缩率与更高的MMLU零样本准确率高度相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代大语言模型在大规模互联网语料上预训练，基准数据可能已混入训练集，导致评估结果不可靠；同时基础模型指令遵循能力有限，传统基于任务的评估难以直接衡量其能力。需要一种难以被"作弊"、且适用于基础模型的评估方法。

Method: 基于"预测能力等价于无损压缩能力"这一理论联系，将语言模型视为无损压缩器：模型对文本的下一token概率决定其算术编码的理想码长。定义压缩率(CR)为理想码长占原始UTF-8大小的百分比，CR越低表示预测越好。数据采集上定期收集新发布的小说、新闻、百科、论文、代码等多类别文本，经质量过滤、去重、Unicode标准化和多分词器统一截断等流程处理。评估80个模型（涵盖注意力、混合、循环架构），并分析CR与模型规模（幂律拟合）、文档位置（长上下文分析）以及MMLU准确率之间的相关性。

Result: （1）CR与参数规模呈幂律关系：CR(P)=3.200·P^{-0.290}+5.328，全类别拟合R²=0.915，RMSE=0.293；代码类缩放最陡（b=-0.317），英文小说最浅（b=-0.146）；Pareto前沿拟合R²=0.992。（2）长上下文下，混合架构Falcon-H1压缩率下降最快（斜率-0.454），注意力架构Qwen3次之（-0.426），循环架构RWKV7最慢（-0.314）；RWKV短上下文优势随位置增长而衰减，但更长训练上下文（16K vs 4K）可缓解此衰减。（3）技术文本CR与零样本MMLU准确率的Spearman相关系数ρ=-0.884（95% CI: [-0.942, -0.783]），各技术类别ρ介于-0.883至-0.853。（4）跨类型残差相关分析显示科学论文与代码强相关（ρ=0.838），新闻与英文小说强相关（ρ=0.812），代码与新闻几乎无残差相关（ρ=-0.015）。

Conclusion: 基于新发布文本的无损压缩率可作为评估基础语言模型的有效且抗污染手段：CR随模型规模系统下降并服从幂律加常数规律，不同架构在长上下文下表现模式存在系统性差异，且技术文本的CR与MMLU等任务表现高度相关。该方法无需指令或参考答案，但仅适用于基础模型评估，且需定期更新数据集以维持抗污染效力。

Abstract: Modern large language models are pretrained on massive datasets, making it difficult to prevent benchmark data from entering their training sets and undermining the reliability of evaluation results. Reliable evaluation is particularly challenging for base models, whose limited instruction-following ability complicates task-based assessment. We introduce Uncheatable Eval, a dynamic benchmark that regularly collects newly published text to evaluate base language models and reduce the risk of data contamination. Drawing on the relationship between a model's predictive ability and its ability to compress data losslessly, we use compression rate to evaluate how well models predict new text. We evaluate 80 models across 14 text categories, study how compression changes with context length, and examine the correlation between compression rate and zero-shot MMLU accuracy. Our results yield three main findings: (1) compression performance follows a consistent scaling trend with model size; (2) attention-based, hybrid, and recurrent models differ in how their compression performance changes as more context becomes available; and (3) lower compression rates are strongly associated with higher zero-shot MMLU accuracy. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [11] [When Context Misleads: In-context Learning with Jurisdiction in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27603)
*Pei-lin Li,Qingle Liu,Junyang Feng,Siyu Li,Sunqi Fan,Xin-Sheng Chen,Shuojin Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"上下文权威判别"作为ICL的缺失维度，构建FakeContext-bench基准（700个理论族、3500实例、7个领域）来评估模型能否区分可适用的上下文规则与一致但不适用的误导规则，并提出J-ICL后训练框架，通过J+/J-配对训练在保持常规ICL能力的同时提升对误导上下文的抵抗力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICL后训练方法存在关键盲点：擅长从演示中提取模式，但忽视"上下文权威"——即判断上下文信息是否应主导最终答案的能力。模型规模和现有ICL微调方法反而可能加剧对误导上下文的易感性，揭示了上下文适应性与事实真实性之间的权衡困境。

Method: 1. FakeContext-bench基准：包含700个理论族（200个真实伪科学理论+500个仿造），每族5个实例变体，涵盖7个领域；每个实例注册现实正确答案yR和上下文诱导答案yP，采用确定性规则评分器进行评估。2. J-ICL后训练框架：构建三种训练单元——标准J+集、对比性J+/J-配对集、负精炼J-/J-配对集，使用查询仅因果语言建模损失（无显式权威标签），通过三种损失的加权组合进行联合优化，采用平滑课程调度。

Result: 在四个模型骨干上，J-ICL相对基础模型平均提升ICLEval 5.84个百分点、Reality Rate 9.20个百分点；相对MetaICL和Symbol Tuning平均提升Reality Rate 18.09个百分点。Qwen3系列呈现逆缩放趋势（更大模型Reality Rate反而下降），前沿模型中除GPT-5.6 Sol外Reality Rate均低于50%。消融实验表明J+/J-对比和J-/J-负精炼均有贡献，且提升效果在未见过的语句形式改写下依然稳健。

Conclusion: 可靠的ICL需要对演示进行选择性使用而非无条件跟随。上下文权威判别不能仅靠模型规模解决，常规ICL微调存在"模式提取能力增强但误导易感性也增加"的权衡；J-ICL通过自然语言管辖线索和查询仅训练目标，在不使用权威标签、辅助分类器或中间推理监督的情况下，同时提升常规ICL能力和对误导上下文的抵抗力。

Abstract: In-Context Learning (ICL) has become a cornerstone of modern LLM deployment. However, existing ICL post-training methods have a critical blind spot: they excel at extracting patterns from demonstrations while often neglecting context authority, the ability to determine whether contextual information should govern the final answer. To benchmark this capability, we introduce FakeContextBench, which contains pseudoscientific claims across seven domains. Our evaluation of commercial and open-source models shows that large-scale pre-training alone is insufficient for reliable context-authority discrimination. Moreover, prevalent ICL fine-tuning methods can increase susceptibility to misleading context, reducing reality accuracy by up to 14.95 percentage points relative to the base model. To address this trade-off, we propose Jurisdiction In-Context Learning (J-ICL), a post-training framework that incorporates context validation into the training objective. Across four model backbones, J-ICL improves ICLEval by an average of 5.84 percentage points and reality accuracy by 9.20 points over the corresponding base models. It also raises the Reality Rate by an average of 18.09 points relative to MetaICL and Symbol Tuning. These results demonstrate that ICL capability and resistance to deceptive context can be improved together. The benchmark is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [12] [Can Jev Judge Radiology Reports? Evaluating a System One Model for Clinical Factuality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27607)
*Jiaju Huang,Hao Yang,Xinyu Ma,Xinglong Liang,Kunyan Cai,Junqiang Ma,Shaobin Chen,Yue Sun,Tao Tan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了 Jev（一种 System One 决策模型）作为放射学报告事实性评估器的有效性，提出了一种仅需"每条陈述一个支持问题"的极简配置，在专家错误计数上达到竞争性一致性（RadEvalX 上 Kendall τ=0.573，RadEvalExpert 上 τ=0.398），并在受控错误测试中对假阴性检测达到 AUROC 0.977，同时将判断 API 成本降至每百份报告对低于 3 美分（节省 43-45% 输入 token）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 生成的放射学报告可能在表面上与医生报告相似，但遗漏异常、添加无依据发现或反转其存在。准确衡量这些事实差异对于评估报告生成器至关重要，但现有方法要么成本高昂，要么与专家评估的相关性有限。本研究旨在探索一种简单、低成本的判断模型（Jev）能否有效检测报告间的事实性差异，并找到最小化复杂度与成本的配置。

Method: 方法框架包括：(1) 语句提取——采用 L1 粒度，使用本地 Qwen3.5-9B 模型将句子拆分为原子发现，同时保留否定、不确定性、侧别、严重程度等信息；(2) 双向判断——对每条候选陈述与参考报告进行支持性判断，对每条参考陈述与候选报告进行支持性判断；(3) OneQ 配置——仅使用一个支持问题（分为支持、矛盾、未涉及三类），获得三类概率输出；(4) 聚合评分——将候选侧矛盾/未涉及概率与参考侧未涉及概率相加作为不一致总分（越低越好）；(5) 软精度/召回/F1 作为辅助指标。此外对比了 Full 配置（七问题）、开放 NLI 判官（DeBERTa-v3-large）、以及 GREEN、RadCliQ、RadGraph F1、BERTScore 等基线方法，在 RadEvalX、RadEvalExpert、ReXErr 等专家标注数据集上用 Kendall τ_b 评估一致性。

Result: 主要结果：(1) OneQ 在 RadEvalX 上达到 τ_b=0.573（总错误）/0.346（显著错误），RadEvalExpert 上达到 0.398/0.352，与 Full 七问题配置相当；(2) OneQ 在匹配分解与聚合条件下优于开放 NLI 判官，差异为 0.236（RadEvalX）和 0.075（RadEvalExpert）；(3) OneQ 较 Full 节省 43-45% 输入 token，判断成本约 2.3-2.6 美分/百份报告对（不含本地分解）；(4) ReXErr 句子级测试中，假阴性检测 AUROC=0.977、位置变化 0.984、严重程度变化 0.975；(5) 发现计数基线 τ_b=0.288（RadEvalX）/0.261（RadEvalExpert），表明 Jev 判断超越单纯报告大小信号；(6) 报告排名在五次运行间高度一致（Jev-OneQ 排序一致性 0.990，τ_b 标准差 0.00381）；(7) RadMatch 在临床显著错误上一致优于 Jev（RadEvalX 0.491 vs 0.346，共同子集 0.485 vs 0.259），但在总错误上差距较小或可忽略；(8) 错误范围分析显示，包含拼写与重复扰动时 Jev AUROC 低于 NLI（0.5505 vs 0.6179），但仅限事实性错误时 Jev 优于 NLI（0.9680 vs 0.9614）。

Conclusion: Jev 可作为放射学报告事实性评估的低成本判断组件，单问题配置 OneQ 在专家一致性、判断成本与排名稳定性间达成良好平衡；与开放 NLI 判官相比，Jev 在匹配分解与聚合条件下表现更优；对多种医学意义变化敏感，假阴性检测能力突出；但对拼写/重复等语言扰动敏感性较低，且在临床显著错误判断上弱于更精细的 RadMatch。基准一致性同时反映报告大小、错误定义范围与医学错误检测能力三方面因素，使用者应根据评估目标选择合适方法。

Abstract: An AI-generated radiology report can resemble a physician's report while omitting an abnormality, adding an unsupported finding, or reversing its presence. Measuring these factual differences is essential for evaluating report generators. We study Jev, a System One decision model, as a simple, low-cost judge of agreement with physician-written reference reports. Our evaluator checks whether each statement is supported by the other report and combines these judgments in both directions to capture unsupported claims and omissions. A single-question configuration reaches Kendall correlations of 0.573 on RadEvalX and 0.398 on RadEvalExpert with expert error counts, outperforming an open natural language inference judge under matched decomposition and aggregation. One support question per statement retains similar expert agreement to seven while using 43-45% fewer judgment input tokens. At the documented API price, judgments cost under three cents per hundred report pairs, excluding local decomposition. In a separate controlled-error test, Jev detects false negation with an AUROC of 0.977. Local RadMatch achieves stronger agreement on clinically significant errors in both expert datasets and on total errors in the shared RadEvalExpert subset. Finding-count and error-scope analyses show that benchmark agreement reflects report size and error definitions as well as medical error detection. These results support Jev as a practical judgment component for measuring factual differences in generated radiology reports and identify where more elaborate evaluation remains valuable.

</details>


### [13] [The Path Matters: Evaluating Small Language Models Beyond Answer Accuracy in KGQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27669)
*Eduin E. Hernandez,Sergio A. Diaz,Luis F. Garcia,Nurassyl Askar,Stefano Rini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文针对小语言模型（SLM）在知识图谱问答（KGQA）中的多跳导航能力，提出应在终端答案准确率之外独立评估路径保真度。作者基于THESEUS框架，使用冻结的SLM作为局部动作策略，在Kinship和MQuAKE-ST数据集上对8个SLM进行零样本与单样本导航测试，引入路径编辑距离（PED）作为主要轨迹度量。研究发现：相似规模的模型在答案准确率和路径保真度上差异显著，且二者有时倾向不同模型；单样本演示的效果也呈强模型依赖性；ToG风格搜索框架的加入反而降低了轨迹保真度，证明应将图谱导航中的各组件分离开来分别评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的端到端KGQA系统将图谱访问、搜索、导航、推理和答案生成等多个环节耦合在一起，难以判断SLM是否能忠实执行问题隐含的推理路径，也无法将失败归因于导航阶段还是其他阶段。在紧凑型KG代理的研究中，多数工作侧重于通过任务特定训练提升性能，而非评估冻结模型的内在导航能力。此外，答案准确率并不能保证路径保真度——模型可能通过与参考路径差异较大的轨迹到达正确答案。因此，有必要在受控环境下分离出"局部多跳图谱导航"这一能力进行独立评估。

Method: 论文基于THESEUS框架将多跳KGQA形式化为问题条件下的图谱导航任务。给定知识图谱𝒢=(ℰ,ℛ,𝒯)、自然语言问题q、已知主题实体e_s和有效答案集合𝒜(q)，模型在每一步从当前实体的合法出边动作集合𝒩(e_t)中选取一个可执行的三元组动作并决定是否停止，使用冻结的、零任务特定参数更新的SLM作为局部动作策略。评估指标包括：终端答案Hits@1（衡量答案正确性）、路径编辑距离PED（衡量有序路径一致性，主要指标）和子图F1分数F1_SG（衡量无序边重叠）。在Kinship和MQuAKE-ST（SA/MA）三个数据集上，对8个SLM（Gemma 4 E4B、Granite 3.3 8B、Llama 3.1 8B、Ministral 3 8B、OLMo-3 7B、Phi-4 Mini 3.8B、Qwen2.5 7B、Qwen3 8B）进行零样本和单样本（提供一个完整示范轨迹）条件下的对比，辅以随机游走和最短路径Oracle参考基线。还在InMQuAKE-ST SA上使用ToG风格表现替代（搜索宽度w∈{1,3}，含LLM引导的关系/实体剪枝、证据充分性检查和自由形式答案生成）作为补充实验。

Result: 主要结果：（1）答案准确率与路径保真度可指向不同模型——在Kinship上Ministral 3 8B获得最高Hits@1（0.941），而Gemma 4 E4B的路径保真度最优（PED=0.158）；但在MQuAKE-ST上Ministral 3 8B在两项指标上均领先（SA：Hits@1=0.894，PED=0.362）。（2）单样本演示效果强模型依赖——Gemma 4、Ministral 3和Qwen3普遍受益，而Granite 3.3和OLMo-3常出现退化；Llama 3.1可提升Hits@1但不改善PED。（3）参考路径长度约2.5–2.7跳时，多数强模型（PED≈0.2–0.5）优于最短路径Oracle（PED≈1.7–2.1），表明其导航比纯靠答案信息的最短路径更贴合问题隐含的推理结构。（5）补充的搜索控制行为表明加入ToG风格搜索（w=1或3）后，三款强模型在MQuAKE-ST SA上的Hits@1和PED均下降，而生成答案准确率却高于终端Hits@1，说明答案合成可掩盖轨迹失败；扩大搜索宽度w=3未能带来一致收益，反而使指标恶化。（6）每问LLM调用次数（Calls/Q）变化无统一规律，强模型常以更少调用获得更优轨迹，弱模型则相反。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在冻结SLM的局部多跳图谱导航评估中，终端答案准确率与路径保真度可分别排名模型，且对单样本演示和搜索框架加入的响应呈模型依赖性。端到端KGQA中嵌入的搜索与生成支架未必能保留隔离导航中观察到的强行为，而自由形式答案生成可部分弥补轨迹缺陷。这些发现支持对关系选择、实体选择、搜索控制、停止决策和答案生成进行组件级（而非系统级）评估，并激励未来研究任务特定适应方法如何系统地改善这些组件。

Abstract: Small language models (SLMs) are increasingly paired with knowledge graphs (KGs), yet end-to-end KG question answering conflates graph access, search, navigation, reasoning, and answer generation. This coupling makes it difficult both to determine whether an SLM can faithfully execute the reasoning path implied by a question and to attribute failures to navigation rather than to other stages of the pipeline. We isolate this capability by employing the THESEUS navigation and traceability framework and using frozen, off-the-shelf SLMs as local action policies. At each hop, the environment exposes the legal outgoing graph actions, and the model selects one executable graph action and decides whether to stop, without task-specific parameter updates, model-controlled beam search, or free-form answer generation. This controlled setting allows us to evaluate terminal-answer accuracy with Hits@1 together with path fidelity, using Path Edit Distance (PED) as the primary trajectory metric. Across the Kinship and MQuAKE-ST KGQAs, similarly sized local models differ substantially in answer accuracy and path fidelity, with the two metrics sometimes favoring different models. This model-dependent behavior also extends to prompting, as a single demonstrated trajectory can improve or degrade navigation depending on the model. These results motivate evaluating SLM graph reasoning beyond endpoint accuracy alone.

</details>


### [14] [Hard Negatives Reveal What Easy Negatives Hide: Cross-Lingual Harmfulness Representations Degrade with Resource Tier Under Hard Negatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27758)
*Paras Balani,Subhrakanta Panda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了大语言模型跨语言安全对齐研究中一个被忽视的评估缺陷：先前基于"简单负例"得出的"有害性表征跨语言保持完整"的结论，在使用表面形式相似但内容无害的"困难负例"（如XSTest对比集）时失效。在九种覆盖高/中/低资源等级的语言中，困难负例下跨语言探测性能随资源等级下降而崩溃（低资源语言AUROC下降高达0.416），且该现象在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct和Aya-Expanse-8B两个模型家族上一致复现，不能简单归因于翻译质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期跨语言安全研究（如[1]）使用PolyRefuse等明显无害、与有害指令表面形式不同的"简单负例"，得出"有害性表征跨语言保持完整，跨语言拒绝失败主要是校准问题"的结论。然而[9][4]已证明此类简单负例可使AUROC虚高。本文旨在检验：在控制表面形式线索后（使用困难负例），跨语言有害性表征是否真正保持完整。

Method: 1) 数据：固定200条有害+250条无害提示，构建两组配对——简单负例（AdvBench+Alpaca）与困难负例（XSTest对比集），用NLLB-200翻译成9种语言（高资源：西/法/中；中资源：印地/越/阿；低资源：泰卢语/斯瓦希里/阿姆哈拉语）；2) 模型：Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct与Aya-Expanse-8B（4-bit量化）；3) 探针：仅用英语激活训练逻辑回归（ℓ₂，C=0.05），取每条件峰值层（简单负例早期层、困难负例晚期层）的末提示token表征，跨语言零样本迁移评估，用5,000次分层自助法估计置信区间；4) 控制：回译chrF、tokenizer生育率与资源等级的偏相关分析。

Result: 1) 简单负例下跨语言迁移AUROC>0.98（包括阿姆哈拉语0.977、斯瓦希里语0.991），复现了[1]的"表征完整"结论；2) 困难负例下出现资源等级单调崩溃：Qwen均值AUROC下降从英语0.003、高资源0.017、中资源0.042到低资源0.276；阿姆哈拉语从0.977跌至0.561；3) 在Aya上复制相同模式与排序；4) 控制回译chrF后资源等级效应仍显著（偏相关r=0.70, p=0.03）；Telugu/阿语/越南语chrF≈68但崩溃幅度相差5–10倍，排除翻译质量为唯一解释；5) tokenizer生育率与崩溃相关（Qwen r=0.59, Aya r=0.72），但印地语作为反例表明生育率不能完全解释资源等级效应。

Conclusion: 先前的"跨语言表征完整"结论依赖于简单负例的评估协议。在表面形式受控的困难负例下，有害性表征本身随语言资源等级下降而退化，而非仅是决策边界校准问题。简单负例AUROC无法区分真正的语义表征与表面形式捷径。跨语言安全监控与探针必须在困难负例下评估，才能就跨语言迁移能力作出可靠论断；此外，tokenizer生育率是值得干预的潜在机制方向。

Abstract: Safety alignment in large language models is trained primarily in English, and recent work reports that the underlying harmfulness representation survives translation: English-trained probes separate harmful from harmless prompts almost as well in low-resource languages as in English. This has been taken as evidence that cross-lingual refusal failures mainly reflect calibration rather than representation quality. We show that this conclusion depends on the choice of negative examples. Across nine languages spanning three resource tiers, we replicate near-perfect transfer (AUROC > 0.98) when harmless prompts come from an unrelated distribution (easy negatives). With XSTest contrast prompts, which are benign but surface-similar to harmful requests (hard negatives), transfer collapses in low-resource languages while remaining largely stable in high-resource languages. On Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, mean AUROC drop increases from 0.003 in English to 0.017 in high-resource, 0.042 in mid-resource, and 0.276 in low-resource languages. The pattern replicates on Aya Expanse. Back-translation chrF controls and a matched-chrF comparison across three languages reduce the likelihood that translation quality explains the effect. The collapse remains after controlling for chrF (partial r = 0.70, p = 0.03). Tokenizer fertility correlates with the collapse and explains part of the resource-tier effect, but not all of it. The results show that easy-negative transfer can coexist with substantial degradation under hard negatives. Easy-negative evaluation alone therefore cannot establish that the harmfulness representation survives translation.

</details>


### [15] [Beyond Unsafe Detection: Counterfactually Anchored Evidence Attribution for Multi-Turn LLM Safety Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27773)
*Srinivasan Subramanian,Kazi Aminul Islam,Md. Abdullah Al Hafiz Khan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种超越二元安全检测的多轮LLM护栏失败归因框架，构建了一个包含1,762个多轮对话的数据集，并训练了一个轻量级分层归因模型，能够同时预测安全违规并将失败归因到具体的用户轮次和词片段，在检测性能和定位证据的特异性方面均优于基于关键词的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多轮LLM安全护栏评估框架仅判断请求安全/不安全的二元结果，无法解释不安全的对话如何逐步偏离安全轨迹，尤其在对抗意图分散于多个轮次的多轮失败场景中。仅仅标记某个词为"风险词"很容易将讨论安全/防御话题的良性对话误判为对抗性。

Method: 1) 构建多轮数据集：采用交互式对抗生成循环（Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct作生成器、Llama-3-8B-Instruct作目标），结合跨模型行为验证（Mistral-7B-Instruct验证器）和分层因果/行为监督标注。2) 提出分层归因模型：以冻结DeBERTa-v3-base为逐轮编码器，通过跨轮Transformer层（2层，256维）建模对话结构，使用门控融合模块结合标准表示和归因加权表示，配合词级归因头和辅助轮级定位头。3) 三阶段训练：分类预热→归因训练→反事实一致性微调。4) 评估框架：涵盖检测、删除行为、特异性（含Attribution Utility指标）、定位精度、人标注对齐及鲁棒性。

Result: 检测F1达0.988，删除前15%归因词使对抗置信度下降51.1%。相比关键词基线（高风险词良性场景FPR=94.7%），该方法在高风险词良性对话上FPR降至1%以下。关键证据区域（轮次+片段）的跨模型传递有效（ShieldGemma翻转率90.9% vs 关键词基线63.6%）。人标注评测中，前5归因轮次包含人标记证据轮次的比例为84.5%，显著优于Qwen2.5-7B作judge的基线（F1=0.580 vs 0.988）。

Conclusion: 将多轮护栏失败重新定义为对话历史的反事实锚定证据归因问题，将安全评估从二元检测扩展到诊断性定位。所提方法在行为效应、片段对齐和良性对话特异性之间取得更优权衡，Attribution Utility=0.504（远超表面风险基线0.195）。在agentic工作流中，轮级因果归因为安全审计提供天然诊断接口，但需指出局限性：反事实验证标注稀缺（仅2.3%对话有cf证据）、归因仅限用户轮次、模型规模限于7B–14B、外部检测泛化能力较弱。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) move from conversational assistants to advanced agentic systems, guardrail failures can convert adversarial intents into harmful executions. However, most guardrail evaluation frameworks focus only on the result and assess whether a user request is safe or unsafe. This approach is insufficient for multi-turn failures, where adversarial intent is distributed across multiple turns. This motivates us to go beyond detection to identify the turns and tokens that push the conversation toward unsafe trajectories. To support this, we construct a multi-turn dataset with behavioral validation and tiered evidence supervision. The dataset contains 1,762 conversations, including adversarial conversations, benign twins, and benign variants with high-risk vocabulary. We train a lightweight hierarchical attribution model that predicts safety violations and attributes them to contributing user turns and token spans. The model achieves strong detection performance (F1=0.988), and removing the top 15% of attributed tokens reduces the adversarial classification confidence by 51.1%. The model preserves low false positive rates on benign conversations with high-risk vocabulary, with false positives below 1% on both borderline benign and benign high-risk vocabulary conversations, compared to 37.3% and 94.7% for a keyword-based surface-risk baseline. Independent human annotation supports the model's attribution performance, with the top-five attributed turns containing a human-identified evidence-bearing turn in 84.5% of adversarial cases.

</details>


### [16] [LabourCrew: A Multi-Agent RAG Framework for Trustworthy Adversarial Deliberation and Statutory Reasoning over Labour Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27814)
*Fatema Tuj Johora Faria,Mukaffi Bin Moin,Jubayer Al Mahmud,M. F. Mridha,Md. Alam Hossain*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LabourCrew是一个用于孟加拉国劳动法（Bangladeshi Labour Act, 2006）法定问答的多智能体RAG框架，核心创新在于将"忠实性"作为架构约束而非提示工程，通过StatuteGraph（结构化法律图谱）、Evidence Exchange Protocol（证据交换协议，限制智能体只能在共享账本上引用证据）和Calibrated Trust Gate（共形风险控制校准的置信阈值）三个机制，实现对不可验证引用的结构性杜绝，同时附带新构建的500题孟加拉语评测集LabourActQA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法定问答场景中每一个声明都必须可追溯到具体证据，而非仅是"相关"，因为错误答案（如错误通知期或工资权益）可能直接影响劳动者收入。现有RAG系统存在两类缺陷：单轮RAG无法检测证据不足；多智能体法律辩论系统将"接地"作为提示约定执行，允许智能体引用未经检索的证据，导致幻觉率高达17-33%且表面正确不等于来源正确。孟加拉国劳动法作为低资源法律语境的代表，缺乏专门基准。

Method: 方法由五个阶段构成：(1) StatuteGraph离线索引——确定性单遍解析器按法律自身的章节、条款、但书（proviso）、交叉引用结构切分语料，保留类型化有向边（parent/proviso/cross_ref），避免固定长度分块切断规则与限定性但书的联系；(2) 智能体检索——由Issue Spotter、Retrieval Planner、Statute Retriever、Link Hopper组成，在interleaved think-then-act循环中按需沿图谱链接扩展；(3) 证据门控辩论——Worker Counsel与Employer Counsel对抗辩论，Legal Interpreter陈述规则，但所有智能体仅通过Formal Case Ledger (FCL)通信，协议上断绝外部检索工具，使引用未检索文本"结构上不可能"；(4) 信任审计与容错编排——Trust Auditor计算确定性的复合信任分数（validity+fidelity+provenance+confidence四项加权求和），Supervisor并行运行并隔离单智能体故障；(5) 校准发布门——用Conformal Risk Control在标定集上设定信任阈值τ，对未来可交换声明提供关于错误接受率的分布无关保证（α=0.10），若没有阈值满足则"故障关闭"拒收全部声明。新发布500题评测集LabourActQA覆盖7类推理类别（直接事实检索、定义分类、程序推理、条件推理、比较推理、多跳推理、假设法律推理）和3个难度等级。

Result: 框架在LabourActQA上将实证误接受率驱动至0.081（目标α=0.10内），并在RAGAS评估中所有指标优于HyDE RAG、Graph-RAG、Hierarchical RAG三大基线：Answer Relevancy达0.862（vs 0.839/0.815/0.828）。消融实验显示：StatuteGraph将初始检索精度从0.52提升至0.78，EEP将不可支持声明从6.4%降至1.2%并将内部忠实度从0.77提升至0.82，故障隔离监督将失败运行从14.2%降至1.0%且延迟同步降低，校准门0.081≤α而类别化基线0.147>α违反保证。聚合门验证引用精度0.991，TGLO率99.0%；性能随难度"梯度退化而非崩溃"——门验证引用精度与决断率在7个推理类别上几乎保持平直，仅Citation-Based Precision在多跳推理上显著下降（0.412 vs 直接事实检索0.763）。人类评估六维度平均分均≥4.52，引用质量4.79±0.42最高。

Conclusion: 论文主张：法定问答的可审计性源于"校准的弃答机制"而非单纯的检索质量提升，忠实性必须是架构约束而非约定。LabourCrew通过结构感知检索、工具级证据门控、故障隔离的多智能体协调和统计校准的发布决策，证明了这些机制的联合必要性；Citation Checker与Trust Auditor捕获不同类型的错误，二者均不可省略。局限性在于：仅在单一法典（孟加拉国劳动法2006）和单一低资源司法管辖区验证，跨章交叉引用尚未链接，检索预算由每问题类型的固定上限控制而非学习得到，错误率保证以标定集的代表性为条件；跨法典/语言/管辖区的泛化性是公开的待验证问题。

Abstract: In statutory question answering, every claim must be traceable to evidence, not merely relevant, since unverifiable labour-rights answers carry serious legal consequences. Current systems fall short: single-pass RAG cannot detect insufficient evidence, while multi-agent legal-debate systems treat grounding as a prompting convention, letting agents cite unretrieved evidence. To address this gap, we introduce LabourCrew, a multi-agent RAG framework built around three grounding mechanisms: StatuteGraph, a graph index that explicitly links chapter, section, proviso, and cross-reference structure rather than fixed-length spans; an Evidence Exchange Protocol that confines advocates and an interpreter to an evidence ledger, making citation to unretrieved text impossible, while a fault-tolerant supervisor board runs advocates in parallel so individual failures degrade rather than crash the system; and a Calibrated Trust Gate that replaces categorical accept/reject decisions with a trust score, thresholded via conformal risk control for a distribution-free bound on the false-accept rate. We evaluate on LabourActQA, a 500-item Bangla question set from the Bangladesh Labour Act, 2006, spanning seven reasoning categories and three difficulty tiers. The framework drives the empirical false-accept rate to 0.081, within the target level ($\alpha = 0.10$), achieves the highest Answer Relevancy among HyDE RAG, Graph-RAG, and Hierarchical RAG (0.862 $>$ 0.839, 0.815, 0.828), and degrades gradually rather than catastrophically as question difficulty increases. These results show that calibrated abstention, not retrieval quality alone, is what makes legal question answering auditable in low-resource statutory domains.

</details>


### [17] [Controlled Attribute-Specific Summarization of Interrogative Dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28004)
*A Aditya Bhardwaj,Arjit Singh Arora,Md Shad Akhtar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了CASPER框架，用于法医场景下询问对话的属性可控摘要生成，并构建了MINDSum数据集。通过思维链属性提示和分层角色评估机制（RoleEval），在事件详情、事事实详情和人物描述三个维度上生成高质量摘要，优于现有基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法医调查中的证人证词通常冗长、碎片化且不一致，现有摘要系统主要优化表面流畅性或词汇重叠，无法满足法医场景对事实准确性、时序一致性和多属性约束的结构化需求。摘要错误可能扭曲调查推理、误述意图或遗漏法律关键事实。

Method: CASPER包含三个阶段：(1) 引导提示增强对话以提取实体链；(2) 基于属性的提示生成属性特定问题与答案，合成摘要；(3) RoleEval分层评估框架，依次由Officer、Inspector、Senior Inspector三个角色基于五个评估维度（覆盖度、一致性、清晰度、属性相关性、事实完整性）迭代评估并精炼摘要。使用LLaMA 3.1-8B进行摘要生成，GPT-4o-mini进行角色评估。数据集MINDSum扩展自MIND语料库，包含5,785个问答对，按65:20:15划分。

Result: 在MINDSum上，CASPER在事件详情（ED）和事实详情（FD）上RoleEval得分均最优（86.06和81.62），优于SumCoT、ToT、Reflexion等基线；字符描述（CD）上略低于GPT-4o和SumCoT。错误分析显示CASPER摘要错误密度最低（Pe=0.18），抗幻觉因果关系和属性重叠能力强。人类评估（30名评估者，α=0.91）中CASPER在五个维度均取得最高分（4.7-4.9）。RoleEval与人类判断的Pearson相关性达0.87，显著优于ROUGE（0.42）和BERTScore（0.58）。统计显著性检验（p<0.05）验证了改进的有效性。

Conclusion: CASPER通过属性可控提示和分层角色评估机制，有效提升法医对话摘要的事实完整性、一致性和相关性。MINDSum为法医摘要提供了有价值的基准。局限性在于目前仅支持英语，未来需扩展多语言场景以增强适用性。

Abstract: Effective summarization of interrogative dialogues is a critical task in forensic and investigative settings, requiring high factual accuracy, coherence, and attribute-specific relevance. In this work, we introduce CASPER, a novel Chain-of-Thought Attribute-Specific Prompting for Evaluative Summarization framework that leverages structured prompting and iterative refinement to generate high-quality summaries of interrogator-witness interactions. We construct MINDSum, a dataset extending the MIND corpus, comprising 6,000 utterance pairs annotated with event details, factual statements, character descriptions, and fillers. CASPER employs RoleEval, a hierarchical evaluation mechanism where multiple roles (officer, inspector, senior inspector) iteratively assess summaries based on predefined criteria. By integrating entity extraction and structured feedback loops, CASPER significantly improves factual consistency and contextual completeness compared to existing baselines. Experimental results demonstrate that our framework outperforms standard summarization models on both lexical (ROUGE) and semantic (BERTScore) metrics, while human evaluation confirms its alignment with expert reasoning. Our findings underscore the potential of controlled summarization in high-stakes domains, paving the way for AI-driven forensic intelligence.

</details>


### [18] [Evaluating Feedback Focus and Pedagogical Adaptivity in LLM-Generated Feedback on Student Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28026)
*Norah Almousa,Shayan Peyghambari Oskoui,Raquel Coelho,Gayle Rogers,Xiang Lorraine Li,Diane Litman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出FeedType基准，系统评估大语言模型（LLM）在学生写作反馈中的"反馈焦点"分布及其对草稿阶段和学生表现的适应性，发现LLM虽能覆盖大多数反馈类型，但无法复现专家教师的反馈分布与适应性行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究多关注LLM反馈的质量、准确性和目标，但对反馈焦点（即教师关注什么）及其适应性（是否随草稿阶段/学生水平调整）缺乏系统评估。LLM被证明在写作教学上存在教学对齐不足的问题，亟需在反馈焦点维度上进行量化分析。

Method: 1) 基于Narciss (2008) 分类法扩展为七类反馈焦点（任务约束、概念、详述、澄清、错误、表扬、其他）；2) 基于SEFORA语料库构建FeedType，涵盖三门大学写作课程（作文、ESL、虚构写作），共1,966个段落反馈；3) 通过四轮试点训练标注者（Cohen's κ=0.774），并选用Gemini 2.0 Flash作为自动标注者（与人类κ=0.78，通过Alt-Test）；4) 测试六个LLM（Mistral-7B/24B、LLaMA-3.1-8B、Qwen2.5-70B、Gemini 2 Flash、LearnLM）在三种提示策略（zero-shot、加类别定义、加类别+示例）下的反馈生成；5) 使用覆盖率（Cov_all）和Jensen-Shannon散度（JSD）评估分布相似性，使用Fisher精确检验评估跨草稿阶段和学生表现的适应性。

Result: 主要发现：(1) 覆盖度：大多数LLM覆盖全部七类反馈焦点，但Gemini缺失表扬类，Qwen-70B缺失"其他"类；(2) 分布相似性：LearnLM的JSD最低（0.1344），最接近教师分布，而多数模型偏好单一类型（如Llama-7B偏好错误类>60%，Gemini/Qwen/Mistral-7B偏好详述类>50%），提示策略改善效果有限；(3) 草稿阶段适应性：教师在5类反馈焦点上展现显著变化（任务约束↓、表扬↑），表现最佳的模型（Mistral-24B）仅在3类上有显著变化，部分模型无任何适应性；(4) 学生表现适应性：教师在任务约束和表扬上对高低表现学生有显著差异，仅少数模型展现适应性，且仅限于其最常生成的反馈类型。无一模型完全匹配教师的适应性行为。

Conclusion: LLM在反馈焦点覆盖度上表现尚可，但无法反映教师的反馈分布规律，也缺乏跨草稿阶段与表现水平下的真实教学适应性。模型仅在其偏好的反馈类型上展现有限的适应性。研究发布了FeedType基准（包含教师与LLM反馈标注），以支持未来在LLM反馈生成中实现教学对齐的研究。

Abstract: We investigate whether state-of-the-art large language models (LLMs) generate feedback that reflects the pedagogical practices of expert teachers in terms of feedback focus and adaptivity. Previous evaluation efforts have examined feedback characteristics, its impact on learning, and its target, yet the focus of feedback and its adaptivity remains largely overlooked. To bridge this gap, we adopt and refine Narciss's taxonomy into seven feedback focus types to annotate teacher and LLM-generated feedback across three university writing courses. We release FeedType, a benchmark containing annotated teacher and LLM feedback from six LLMs under three prompting strategies. We assess the coverage and distribution of feedback focus types, and examine whether LLMs adapt their feedback across draft stages and student performance levels as an expert instructor does. Our findings show that while most LLMs cover most feedback focus types, they fail to reflect teacher feedback distributions and show varying levels of adaptivity, with none matching the teachers' adaptive behavior. We believe FeedType will support future research on pedagogical alignment in LLM feedback generation.

</details>


### [19] [TEMPS: Temporal Sentence Embeddings for Temporal Information Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28048)
*Mourad Hassani,Julien Romero,Amel Bouzeghoub,Christian Jacquelinet*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 TEMPS——一种面向时间敏感信息检索的模块化时序嵌入框架，通过定义 Temporal Textual Similarity (TTS) 任务，将锚定时间表达式解析为区间并通过矩匹配转换为高斯分布监督信号，无需人工标注即可训练；在 TimeQA、TempReason、TS-Retriever 三个基准上全面提升 MRR，并在 TS-Retriever 上将 R@1 从 19.92 提升至 25.39，超过此前最优时序检索模型 TSContriever。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代稠密检索器与 RAG 管道擅长主题匹配但忽视时间维度，导致在临床、新闻、法律等场景中返回主题相关但时间错误的内容；现有 STS 资源只衡量语义相关性，且对称相似度函数无法表达时间包含关系的方向性（如"某天属于某年"与其逆关系应被区别对待）。

Method: 1) 形式化 TTS 任务：以四元组 (s₁, d₁, s₂, d₂) 输入，衡量两段锚定文本的时间兼容性，与主题相似度解耦；2) 弱监督标签生成：合成 TimeML 表达式（年份跨度 1000–2030）+ HeidelTime 标注的 Multilingual MLM 数据，通过时刻匹配将均匀区间分布投影为高斯 p_I = N(μ_I, σ²_I)，再用有界非对称 KL 散度 ts = 1/(1+D_KL(p_{I_1}‖p_{I_2})) 作为监督信号，共生成 6000 万训练元组；3) 模型架构：时间分支以冻结的 all-MiniLM-L6-v2 编码文本，正弦位置编码锚定日期，注意力池化后映射为对角高斯嵌入，与冻结的语义检索器得分通过 α 加权融合（α 在验证集上选择：TimeQA=0.6、TempReason=0.8、TS-Retriever=0.3）；4) 训练损失：CoSENT 排序损失 λ=20；5) SUTime 规则基线作为对照实验，量化学习编码器的边际贡献。

Result: 在三个时序基准上：TimeQA 上所有四个骨干（MPNet、E5、BGE、Mistral）的 +TEMPS 变体 MRR 提升 +31.92% 至 +79.29%，16 项对比全部显著正向；TS-Retriever 上 E5+TEMPS 在所有指标上达到最优（R@1 从 19.92→25.39，R@100 从 94.57→98.95），超越 TSContriever 及其 query-router 混合架构；TimeQA RAG 管道（Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct）EM/F1/Containment 三项指标均显著提升；TempReason 上结果混杂：MRR 全部正向但 Recall@5 全部下降（SUTime 规则基线在此基准上更强）；统计检验中 48 项对比 36 项显著正向、9 项显著负向（集中于 TempReason 的 Recall@5/Precision@5）；E5+TEMPS（136M 参数）在三个数据集上 MRR 超过未增强的 Mistral（7.1B 参数）。

Conclusion: TEMPS 通过将时间表达式锚定为区间并以矩匹配高斯作为弱监督信号，为稠密检索器附加轻量（约 27M 参数）时序能力，无需手工标注即可训练；在主题与时间冲突显著的检索任务（TimeQA、TS-Retriever）上稳定超越各规模语义基线和 TSContriever 时序 SOTA，证明显式时间建模比单纯扩大模型规模更有效；但在 TempReason 上当正则规则已足够时学习编码器收益有限，且高 α 融合会牺牲 top-5 覆盖率换取 top-1 精度；局限在于仅评估英语、依赖合成与规则标注、弱监督继承底层标注器错误、未涵盖隐式时间引用与事件-事件关系，且下游证据仅限于单一 TimeQA RAG 任务。

Abstract: Modern information retrieval (IR) systems rarely represent time, yet many information needs depend on it: in clinical, journalistic, and legal search, when an event occurred can decide whether a document is relevant. Dense retrievers and Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) pipelines match queries to documents well on topic but poorly on time, so they surface content that is on-topic yet temporally wrong. We introduce Temporal Textual Similarity (TTS), a task that measures how well two anchored texts align in time, independent of their topical similarity. We then present TEMPS (Temporal Embedding Model for Precise Search), a modular temporal branch that attaches to a frozen semantic retriever and trains on that signal. It resolves anchored temporal expressions to intervals and moment-matches each one to a Gaussian; the resulting ordering supervises an anchor-date-conditioned encoder, whose score we fuse with the semantic score at inference. Grounding supplies the supervision, so training uses no hand-labeled temporal data. The temporal score itself is the Gaussian-KL inclusion measure from distributional embeddings; what TEMPS adds is the grounding and the moment-matched supervision. On three temporal benchmarks, TEMPS improves MRR for every semantic backbone tested and, on TS- Retriever, lifts R@1 from 19.92 to 25.39 over the prior temporal state of the art.

</details>


### [20] [Can LLMs Catch a Rigged Backtest? A Clean-Control Calibration Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28090)
*Makar Ulesov,Vladislav Smirnov,Omar Ibrahim,Arsenii Bobovnikov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个96项配对的回测审计基准测试,每个有缺陷的回测都有一个匹配的干净对照版本（保持策略、代码风格等固定,仅改变一个方法论细节）。研究发现:仅报告召回率不足以区分LLM审计器的优劣,因为不同模型可能在召回率相同的情况下,在干净对照上的误报率差异高达79个百分点;加入"干净对照预警"的提示词可将DeepSeek的代码误报率从20.8%降至0%,同时保持100%召回率;审查者角色（人设）的设置显著影响误报率,但几乎不影响召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回测审计是一个校准问题:仅追求高缺陷召回率而不控制干净策略上的误报,会浪费审查资源并可能掩盖真正需要修复的问题。现有LLM金融领域基准测试缺少对干净对照组的联合评估,无法区分"发现缺陷"与"高怀疑先验"两种能力。

Method: 1. 构建96项配对基准(48有缺陷+48干净对照),覆盖8类经典回测缺陷(前视偏差、幸存者偏差、数据挖掘、标签泄漏等),每个类6项,三个难度层级,两个表面(代码/自然语言)。2. 提示词网格交叉{开放、封闭、干净感知}×{代码/自然语言}。3. 主审计器为DeepSeek V4 Flash(温度0),代码切片增加GPT-4o-mini、Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite、GPT-4.1 mini作为对比。4. 确定性评分器分别打分:干净对照误报率、证据定位(需命中植入缺陷行)、修复相关性(类别特定关键词)、三者同时正确率。共1440次缓存审计调用。

Result: 1. DeepSeek封闭提示和干净感知提示在代码和自然语言上均达到100%召回率。2. 开放提示代码审计召回率仅72.9%,但干净对照误报率高达93.8%。3. 干净感知提示将DeepSeek代码误报率从20.8%(95%CI 11.7-34.3)降至0%(0.0-7.4),召回率不变。4. 四模型在干净感知代码条件下,召回率可能相同(如三者均为100%),而干净对照误报率差异达79个百分点(GPT-4o mini 79.2% vs DeepSeek 0%)。5. 干净感知代码三者同时正确率仅87.5%,失败完全集中在修复建议列(指对了行但给出了错误类别的修复方案)。6. 审查者角色影响显著:风险经理和怀疑型审查者贡献了12个误报中的11个,而基线召回率几乎不变。7. 自然语言表面始终比代码表面校准更好。8. 高自信度审计(>0.85)中仍有35.0%的干净对照被误判。

Conclusion: 回测审计LLM基准测试必须分别报告缺陷召回率、干净对照误报率、证据定位、修复相关性和置信度,而不能使用单一聚合指标。仅报告召回率会掩盖高达79个百分点的模型间校准差异,并错误地将多个模型评为近优解。干净感知提示是有效的低成本干预手段。该基准测试不证明LLM可以验证生产环境中的交易系统。

Abstract: Backtest auditing is a calibration problem: high flaw recall is not useful when the model falsely flags matched clean strategies. We build a 96-item paired benchmark in which every flawed backtest has a clean control that holds strategy, dates, code style, labels, and reporting scaffold fixed while changing one methodology detail. A deterministic scorer separates flaw recall, clean-control false positives, evidence localization, and fix relevance. Over 1440 cached audits from four text endpoints, the primary DeepSeek auditor reaches 100.0\% closed and clean-aware code recall, but open prompts over-flag 93.8\% of clean code controls, and clean-aware all-three specificity is 87.5\% even where recall saturates. A clean-aware warning drops DeepSeek code false positives from 20.8\% (95\% CI 11.7--34.3) to 0.0\% (0.0--7.4) at unchanged recall, while the budget anchor still flags 38/48 clean controls under the same prompt. Reporting recall alone would rank three of these four models identically; reporting the clean-control rate separates them by 79 points.

</details>


### [21] [Scaling Attention Head Analysis via Gradient-Based Attribution in Context-Aware Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28117)
*Paweł Mąka,Yusuf Can Semerci,Jan Scholtes,Gerasimos Spanakis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于梯度的注意力头归因方法，通过将Token级Max-Margin损失反向传播到注意力图，自动识别对上下文利用至关重要的头部-关系对。该方法将大规模LLM的因果分析计算成本降低超过96%，使分析50种上下文消歧现象、4个模型和4个语言方向成为可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM（如EuroLLM 9B）拥有数千个注意力头-关系对，对所有进行因果干预（如Modifying Heads）在计算上不可行。这限制了可解释性研究向更大模型、更多语言方向和语言现象的扩展。现有基于梯度的可解释性方法主要关注输入归因（局部解释），而非模型内部组件的全局解释。

Method: 核心方法是梯度归因框架：(1) 对对比评估中正确样本和错误样本进行前向传播；(2) 计算Token级Max-Margin损失（TMM Loss），该损失仅在模型错误或边际小于μ时激活，拉开正确token与错误token的概率差距；(3) 将损失反向传播到注意力分数S^{l,h}（而非模型参数或输入）；(4) 沿数据集聚合每个头部-关系对的最大梯度；(5) 仅选择top-k（如前3%）最具潜力的头部-关系对进行Modifying Heads干预。比较了7种损失函数（NLL、Contrastive、Token-level Contrastive、MM及其log版本），TMM损失在平均精度、NDCG和Spearman相关性上大多最优。

Result: 主要结果：(1) TMM损失在大多数场景中超越基线，平均精度达到0.45-0.68，Spearman相关性达到0.36-0.55（ΔA>0子集）；(2) 聚焦top 3%头可将计算成本降低96%以上（EuroLLM 9B从5824 GPU小时降至175小时）；(3) 发现"通用"注意力头——单个头部可同时改善多种关系、多种语言方向甚至多种现象的性能（如EuroLLM 1.7B的18-6和18-14头）；(4) 平均注意力分数与模型性能无强相关，证实模型训练中形成功能冗余；(5) Formality和Inflection现象结果统计上不显著，原因是关系集有限且模型在这些任务上表现已较高；(6) Head Tuning实验显示，当干预被模型学习时（4个头-关系对），BLEU/COMET保持稳定，ContraPro准确率提升1-3.58个百分点；(7) 在IOI任务上，使用logTC损失时Pearson和Spearman相关性分别为0.67和0.69，平均精度0.65，证实方法可泛化。

Conclusion: 梯度归因方法能有效且高效地识别对上下文利用至关重要的注意力头，使大规模LLM可解释性研究在大规模跨语言跨现象分析中成为可能。发现了注意力头的多功能性（"通用头"）与跨语言/跨现象迁移存在关联，并证实模型发展出显著的功能冗余（平均注意力≠性能贡献）。未来工作将扩展到其他上下文任务和更大模型。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce a gradient-based head attribution strategy where the Token-level Max-Margin loss is backpropagated to the attention maps. This framework enables a large-scale causal analysis of attention heads, making it suitable for LLMs. We evaluate our method on the task of disambiguation in Context-aware Machine Translation, where we analyze 50 phenomena across 4 models and 4 language directions. We empirically show the alignment of our method with the effects of increasing the attention scores of token-to-token relations on three models and two language directions, ensuring the robustness of our method. Our analysis reveals the presence of the "general-purpose" attention heads that improve the model's performance when attending to different relations. We find that the average attention a head assigns to a relation does not necessarily relate to the model's performance, which suggests that the models developed redundancies during training in terms of the head functions.

</details>


### [22] [Beyond Poetry: Can Large Language Models Generate Classical Arabic Maqamat?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28245)
*AbdulRahman A. Morsy,Aya Zirikly*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了大型语言模型生成古典阿拉伯"玛卡梅"(maqama)文体文本的能力。作者采用受控实验设计，在零样本、少样本和规则型提示策略下评估了五个LLM（GPT-4o、GPT-5.4-mini、ALLaM-7B-Instruct、Qwen3-8B、LLaMA-3-8B-Instruct），通过人工标注与LLM-as-a-judge框架，对生成文本在修辞丰富度、saj'押韵密度、结构连贯性和风格真实性等维度进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM创意文本生成研究主要集中于现代语言变体和诗歌生成，对具有严格文体、韵律与修辞约束的古典阿拉伯散文文体（如玛卡梅）关注不足。该文体需要维持押韵散文(saj')、密集修辞手法以及古典语域，是检验LLM是否具备深层文学能力（而非仅表面流畅性）的理想基准。

Method: 构建14个主题提示 × 3种提示策略 × 5个模型 × 5个独立样本的全因子合成数据集（共1050篇）。评估采用双轨制：(1) 绝对评分（Claude Sonnet 4.5作为评判者，1-5量表跨八个维度评分）；(2) 配对比较评判（含位置偏置缓解）。辅以Gemini 3.5 Flash作为第二独立评判者、60篇样本的人工标注（两位古典阿拉伯语专家，κ=0.9231）、以及基于韵结尾一致性和节奏规律性的非LLM代理指标验证。统计方法涵盖配对Wilcoxon符号秩检验（Holm-Bonferroni校正）和Elo排名。

Result: 结果揭示了清晰的四层级性能梯队：(1) GPT-5.4-mini以Elo 1980.40绝对领先，在saj'、修辞、真实性等玛卡梅特异维度上表现最强且最稳定；(2) GPT-4o（Elo 1638.93）语法流畅但真实性不足；(3) ALLaM（Elo 1261.28）作为阿拉伯专用模型优于Qwen和LLaMA；(4) Qwen和LLaMA（Elo约1020-1100）表现最弱。提示策略影响因模型而异：GPT-4o从少样本提示获益最大（+0.54），而GPT-5.4-mini在各策略下表现稳定。维度敏感度分析显示saj'是最受提示影响的维度（Δ=0.429），而修辞和创造性主要取决于模型内在能力。两评判者间Spearman ρ=0.878（绝对评分）和Cohen κ=0.907（配对比较），绝对与配对评估间ρ=1.00，表明排名结果具高度鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 研究表明，玛卡梅生成本质上要求超越语法流畅性的深层文体重现能力。强模型（GPT-5.4-mini）表现出对文体内化能力，较少依赖提示工程；弱模型虽能从示例中获得表面风格改善，但缺乏稳定的文学控制能力。提示策略对saj'等表面韵律特征最有效，而修辞深度与创造性基本属模型固有属性。阿拉伯专用训练并不必然转化为古典文体能力（ALLaM案例）。该工作为古典受约束文学生成提供了首个系统基准，并指出未来应向长篇叙事与更深度古典对齐方向延伸。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong performance in creative text generation, yet their ability to produce culturally grounded and stylistically constrained literary forms remains underexplored. Prior work has focused largely on modern language varieties and poetry, while classical prose traditions such as maqama remain largely unstudied. The maqama is a classical literary genre characterized by rhymed prose (saj), dense rhetorical ornamentation, and episodic narrative structure, making it a challenging testbed for evaluating whether LLMs can move beyond surface fluency toward deeper literary competence. In this paper, we present the first controlled evaluation study of maqama generation with LLMs, comparing five models under zero-shot, few-shot, and rule-based prompting, and evaluating outputs through both human annotation and an LLM-as-a-judge framework across dimensions such as rhetorical richness, saj density, structural coherence, and stylistic authenticity. Our results show that prompting strategy plays a strong role in stylistic quality: few-shot prompting most consistently improves saj density, while its effects on rhetoric and coherence vary by model, with the strongest models (GPT-4o and GPT-5.4-mini) benefiting most from rule-based prompting on these dimensions, though zero-shot prompting yields the highest aggregate scores across all five models. We further observe systematic differences between models in stylistic alignment with Arabic maqama conventions, and corroborate our findings with a second independent LLM judge, paired statistical significance testing, and non-LLM proxy measures of saj.

</details>


### [23] [Agent-Editing World Model: Rethinking World Modeling for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28416)
*Shuang Sun,Guoxin Chen,Fanzhe Meng,Jia Deng,Huatong Song,Jinhao Jiang,Wayne Xin Zhao,Hongteng Xu,Ji-Rong Wen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Agent-Editing World Model (AEWM)，将语言世界模型从预测工具响应改为建模决策效果并直接编辑智能体状态（推理-动作对），以应对长程任务中的错误累积（任务状态污染）。通过Action Judge与State Revision两个能力，在真实环境执行前替换噪声决策，配套EditAct推理流程与AEWM-RFT拒绝采样微调，在搜索、终端、软件工程三类任务上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统语言世界模型聚焦预测高熵、与执行强耦合的环境反馈（搜索结果、终端输出、测试结果），在真实反馈可得时价值有限；同时智能体在长程交互里积累未经验证的假设与陈旧计划（任务状态污染），仅靠真实环境观察无法纠正推理-动作层面的偏差，亟需一种针对"智能体状态"的预测与干预机制。

Method: 1) Action Judge：将候选推理-动作对分类为Critical/Exploratory/Noisy，基于回溯标注的前缀-only CoT训练；2) State Revision：对被判为Noisy的提案，从同一历史生成替换的推理-动作，真实执行后筛选有效样本；3) EditAct：推理时先判断，仅对Noisy替换并执行，否则保留原提案；4) AEWM-RFT：将AEWM引导的轨迹进行拒绝采样微调，使智能体内化编辑能力。两阶段训练：约52B token的中间训练 + 120K样本的SFT。

Result: Action Judge基准上macro-F1 70.5%，超过最强前沿基线10.6分；EditAct在六个基准与Qwen3.5-4B/9B/35B-A3B三种骨干上平均得分较最强基线提升3.2–6.7分；AEWM-RFT在三个领域比Self-RFT提升2.2–2.6分且无需在线引导；Qwen3.5-9B+EditAct (44.1) 甚至超过Qwen3.5-35B-A3B+ReAct (42.2)。消融验证了学习式干预、联合推理-动作编辑、两阶段训练各自的必要性。

Conclusion: AEWM为长程LLM智能体提供了一种以"决策效果预测+智能体状态编辑"为核心的世界建模新范式，相对传统观察预测范式更契合任务导向需求，并具备跨域、可迁移的特性。AEWM-RFT进一步表明该范式的修正模式可被内化到智能体本身，为可靠的长期智能体推理与训练提供了新路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have enabled agents to tackle long-horizon tasks across diverse environments. To further improve agent performance, existing language world models typically predict environment observations, yet reconstructing high-entropy, execution-dependent tool responses offers limited value when real feedback is available. Meanwhile, agents suffer from \emph{task-state contamination}, where unsupported assumptions and outdated plans persist in history and distort subsequent decisions. We propose the \textbf{Agent-Editing World Model (AEWM)}, which models how reasoning and actions shape future task progress rather than simulating tool responses. AEWM combines \textbf{Action Judge} to distinguish \textsc{Critical}, \textsc{Exploratory}, and \textsc{Noisy} decisions with \textbf{State Revision} to edit noisy reasoning--action continuations from the same observed history. \textbf{EditAct} integrates these capabilities with real execution, directly changing the state underlying subsequent decisions rather than merely providing critiques. We train AEWM across Search, Terminal, and Software Engineering through mid-training and supervised fine-tuning. AEWM achieves 70.5\% macro-F1 on our Action Judge benchmark, exceeding the strongest frontier baseline by 10.6 points. Across six benchmarks and three agent backbones, EditAct improves average scores by 3.2--6.7 points over the strongest baseline. Furthermore, rejection sampling fine-tuning on verified EditAct trajectories, termed \textbf{AEWM-RFT}, improves over Self-RFT by 2.2--2.6 points across three domains without online AEWM guidance.

</details>


### [24] [Geometric Uncertainty for Detecting and Correcting Hallucinations in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.13813)
*Edward Phillips,Sean Wu,Soheila Molaei,Danielle Belgrave,Anshul Thakur,David Clifton*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于几何框架的黑盒LLM幻觉检测与修正方法，通过原型分析(Archetypal Analysis)估计语义分布的凸包支撑集，引入全局指标Geometric Volume(语义熵上界)和局部指标Geometric Suspicion(三维度异常性聚合)分别用于提示级幻觉检测和Best-of-N响应选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在高风险应用(医疗、法律等)中产生的幻觉构成严重风险。现有不确定性量化方法缺乏统一的框架同时评估提示级和答案级的可靠性，且缺乏坚实的理论支撑来建模语义分布。

Method: 1) 为每个提示用非零温度采样n个答案，经句子编码和PCA降维；2) 应用Archetypal Analysis(AA)学习K个极端原型，通过Gram行列式计算其凸包体积的精确表达式——证明其与KL散度相关，构成语义熵的紧上界；3) 全局层面用凸包体积的归一化对数作为Geometric Volume；4) 局部层面结合三个异常性度量(局部稀疏性、距离共识、原型使用稀有度)经秩变换和Fisher聚合构成Geometric Suspicion。理论上证明了log-volume是微分熵的上界(Theorem 1)，且AA体积比box-constrained外近似紧m!倍(Proposition 2)；局部聚合在可交换零假设下具有分布无关、依赖鲁棒的极值保证(Proposition 3)。

Result: 在CLAMBER、TriviaQA、ScienceQA、MedicalQA、K-QA五个基准上，使用GPT-4o Mini、GPT-3.5-Turbo、Qwen3-8b、Llama3.1-8b四个LLM进行评估。全局幻觉检测：Geometric Volume在K-QA上F1达75.4(AUROC 67.8)显著优于基线；在医疗数据集上表现最佳。局部幻觉修正：Geometric Suspicion在Best-of-N选择中几乎全面降低幻觉率，尤其在MedicalQA和K-QA上ΔH达15.3-19.0。消融研究表明性能对K稳健，且对嵌入模型选择(包括轻量级22M参数的all-MiniLM-L6-v2)稳健。

Conclusion: 该几何框架为LLM不确定性量化提供了理论支撑的黑盒方法，统一了全局和局部不确定性评估，并在高风险医疗领域表现出色。凸包体积作为熵上界、AA对box约束外近似的m!因子改进、以及可交换零假设下的Fisher聚合，为语义分布作为LLM不确定性研究对象提供了新的理论基础。局限在于：(1)依赖单向量语义嵌入的充分性；(2)在模型"自信犯错"场景下失效；(3)未探究温度对分布的影响。

Abstract: Large language models are known to hallucinate, generating linguistically plausible but incorrect answers to questions. Uncertainty quantification has been proposed as a strategy to detect such behaviour, but existing methods lack a unified framework to assess reliability at both the prompt and answer level. We introduce a geometric framework which quantifies language model uncertainty at both levels by explicitly modelling a prompt-conditioned semantic distribution in answer embedding space. Our approach is black-box and sampling-based; we generate multiple answers per prompt, and use archetypal analysis to estimate a geometric support for the answer distribution. At the prompt level, we approximate the distribution entropy to quantify uncertainty; for each individual answer, we then use notions of atypicality to assess its reliability relative to the batch. We employ our framework to not only detect hallucinations but correct them, by selecting the batch example deemed most reliable. Experiments show that our framework performs comparably to or better than prior methods on short form question-answering datasets, and achieves superior results on medical datasets where hallucinations carry particularly critical risks. Beyond pure performance, we suggest the theoretical grounding of our work provides support for semantic distributions as useful objects of study for language model uncertainty.

</details>


### [25] [Semantic Self-Distillation for Language Model Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.04577)
*Edward Phillips,Sean Wu,Fredrik K. Gustafsson,Boyan Gao,David A. Clifton*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出语义自蒸馏（SSD），将LLM通过多次采样得到的语义答案分布蒸馏到轻量级学生模型（MDN）中，以单次前向传播实现低延迟的不确定性量化，同时提供预生成的熵风险评分和后生成的似然验证能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM不确定性量化方法存在两难：基于采样的语义分散度方法（如Semantic Entropy）计算成本过高，难以用于延迟敏感场景；而基于探针的方法虽然低延迟，但只能输出标量不确定性，丢失了输出分布的拓扑结构信息。SSD旨在弥合这两类方法之间的差距。

Method: 核心方法包括：(1) 从LLM特定层提取prompt的隐藏状态h作为提示表征；(2) 用语言模型采样S=32个回答，通过EmbeddingGemma编码为语义嵌入z；(3) 用MDN（混合密度网络）学习条件分布q_φ(z|h)，以对角协方差的高斯混合建模；(4) 使用Rényi-2熵提供解析的分散度评分；(5) 通过似然log q_φ(z'|h)进行后生成验证。

Result: 在TriviaQA和MMLU数据集上，对7个LLM族进行实验：(1) SSD在AUPRC上普遍优于教师分散度（TD），在AUROC上4/7模型优于TD；(2) OOD检测任务中AUROC达0.89-0.99；(3) MMLU多选题选择中显著超过0.25随机基线；(4) 跨数据集迁移损失仅0.04-0.05 AUROC；(5) 蒸馏保真度与幻觉检测性能高度相关（ρ_meta=0.65）。

Conclusion: SSD作为通用框架，成功将采样式不确定性蒸馏到单次前向的轻量模型中，统一了预生成风险估计与后生成验证，在保持低延迟的同时保留了语义分布的结构信息。未来可应用于扩散语言模型、EHR基础模型等更广泛的序列生成领域。

Abstract: Large language models present challenges for principled uncertainty quantification, in part due to their complexity and the diversity of their outputs. Semantic dispersion, or the variance in the meaning of sampled answers, has been proposed as a useful proxy for model uncertainty, but the associated computational cost prohibits its use in latency-critical applications. We show that sampled semantic distributions can be distilled into lightweight student models which estimate a prompt-conditioned density before the language model generates an answer token. The student model predicts a semantic distribution over possible answers; the entropy of this distribution provides a prompt-level uncertainty signal, and the probability density allows answer-level reliability evaluation. Across experiments on TriviaQA and MMLU, we find our student models perform competitively relative to the teacher's sampled semantic dispersion on a hallucination prediction task, whilst offering additional uncertainty primitives for out-of-domain detection and multiple-choice answer selection. We term this technique Semantic Self-Distillation (SSD), which can serve as a general framework for distilling predictive uncertainty in complex output spaces beyond language.

</details>


### [26] [LexLattice: Multilingual Extractive Summarization via Neural Cellular Automata on Document Hierarchies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27032)
*Sujay Uday Rittikar,Sheela Ramanna*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LexLattice 提出了一种用于多语种法律文本抽取式摘要的方法,将文档层次结构表示为二维语义格(semantic lattice),并使用掩码二维神经细胞自动机(2D-NCA)在该格上局部迭代地整合证据,再进行预算约束选取。该方法仅有一个约 1.8M 参数的可训练整合器,作用于冻结的 mT5-base 多语编码器之上,在 EUR-Lex-Sum 的全部 24 种语言上取得了 SOTA ROUGE,超越了参数量达数十亿、经过指令调优的基线模型;且仅在 12 种高资源语言上训练即可近乎无损地零样本迁移到 12 种未见语言(平均保留率 0.99)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律文本摘要对忠实性(factiveness)要求极高,这使得抽取式摘要因其可追溯到原文而具有天然吸引力。然而现有方法通常对段落或结构单元进行孤立打分,忽视了文档内跨远距离、跨结构位置共享显著性的证据整合。此外,法律文本长度远超标准编码器上限,且需统一处理资源差异巨大的 24 种欧盟语言,因此需要一种紧凑、可追溯且语言无关的方法替代暴力扩规模。

Method: 1) 层次解析:基于 EU 法律起草惯例的确定性规则解析器,从纯文本中恢复 preamble/recitals/articles/annexes 的层级结构(节为行,段为列);2) 语义格:将解析得到的层级映射为固定 48×32 的二维网格,每格承载对应段落的冻结 mT5-base 嵌入,含掩码以处理填充;3) 整合器:对格状态运行 T=8 步的共享 2D-NCA 更新规则——感知阶段为 [h_t ‖ DWConv_{3×3} h_t] (768→1536),更新 MLP 为 1×1 conv (1536→256→768, ReLU),末层零初始化使其等价于恒等映射;4) 读取与选取:残差 MLP 读取 [h^0 ‖ h^T] 得到段落分数 s_i,推理时按词预算贪心选取文档序排列;5) 训练两阶段——Stage 1 用 Plackett–Luce listwise(预排序)的负对数似然 + λ=0.3 的 BCE 损失做 warm-start,以贪婪 ROUGE-1/2 oracle 为目标;Stage 2 用 REINFORCE Leave-One-Out(RLOO)直接优化三 ROUGE 变体均值奖励,并按语言做奖励标准化。为分离结构轴的贡献,额外训练结构匹配的 1D-NCA 对照(同样步数、宽度、初始化与读取器)。

Result: 在 EUR-Lex-Sum 全部 24 种语言上,LexLattice + RLOO 取得 SOTA ROUGE-1/2/L,在六种有 LLM/SLM 基线的语言(英、法、德、意、西、葡)上均胜过参数量高达 8B 的指令调优模型(英语 ROUGE-1 提升最多 7.7)。结构性 2D 格相对容量匹配的 1D 对照在每种语言、每项指标上均稳定提升 1.7–2.0 ROUGE-1。LLM-as-judge(Claude Sonnet 4.5)的 LDP-F1 评测显示 LexLattice 较 1D 对照提升 +7.8(+17%),主要来自覆盖率;RLOO 再贡献 +1.3,集中于相关性;全部改进在配对 Wilcoxon 检验下 p<0.05。零样本跨语言迁移方面,仅在 HIGH(12 种)上训练、在 LOW(12 种含仅 16 篇的爱尔兰语)上评估,R-2 平均保留率 0.993、最低 0.974,5 种语言匹配或超过联合训练版本;英语→西班牙语零样本下,R-1 与西班牙语监督版本仅差 1.8。定性可视化显示显著度经 8 步 NCA 演化由弥散收敛至结构连贯的连续区域,佐证利用层级邻域的机制。

Conclusion: LexLattice 证明:显式地对文档结构进行局部迭代整合,可以成为规模扩张的紧凑且可追溯的替代方案。仅 1.8M 参数的整合器冻结 mT5-base 表征,即可在 24 种语言上取得 SOTA,并展现近乎无损的零样本跨语言迁移,说明所学规则作用于语言无关的语义几何而非表层形式。未来工作包括将该格范式推广至其他长文档层级语料,以及将可追溯抽取与抽象生成器耦合以兼顾忠实与流畅。局限性:依赖规则解析器要求文本具有显式结构标记;抽取式受源文与固定词预算限制,无法保证完备与忠实,且低资源语言质量较弱。

Abstract: Faithfulness is a central concern in legal text summarization, which motivates extractive approaches that select verbatim content traceable to its source. Such methods typically rank paragraphs or other structural units in isolation, yet give little attention to consolidating evidence that is distributed across, and shares salience between, distant parts of a document. We introduce LexLattice, an extractive summarizer that reifies a legal act's hierarchy as a two-dimensional semantic lattice and consolidates over it with a masked 2D neural cellular automata before selection. LexLattice attains state-of-the-art ROUGE across all 24 languages of EUR-Lex-Sum in both multilingual and cross-lingual settings, surpassing instruction-tuned baselines with billions of parameters, despite concentrating all trainable capacity in a 1.8M parameter consolidator over a frozen multilingual encoder. A consolidator trained only on high-resource languages further transfers to unseen languages with near-lossless retention (0.99), indicating that the model operates on language-agnostic semantic geometry rather than surface form. Our results position explicit consolidation over document structure as a compact and traceable alternative to scale for multilingual legal summarization.

</details>


### [27] [EduBehaviors: Assertion-based Schemas for Auditable Coding of Educational Dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27043)
*Julian Bernado,Ana Trindade Ribeiro,Xander Beberman,Susanna Loeb*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了EduBehaviors框架，通过"可观察行为断言"将教育对话标注任务分解为可审查的步骤，结合LLM标注与监督学习分类器，在TalkMoves数据集上达到与直接提示LLM相当甚至更优的性能，并发布了两个开源工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接使用LLM进行教育对话标注存在黑箱问题：研究者无法了解模型为何选择某个标签，且当标签与人类判断不一致时难以诊断错误来源（是模型错误、证据模糊还是构念定义不清）。现有方法在抽象构念和需要细微判断的任务上表现有限。

Method: EduBehaviors框架将标注过程分解为两个步骤：(1) 由LLM对可观察行为进行二元断言标注（corpus-derived自下而上和construct-derived自上而下两类）；(2) 使用L1正则化逻辑回归等可解释分类器将断言值映射为最终构念标签。通过跨模型一致性（Krippendorff's α ≥ 0.5）筛选可靠断言。发布了EduBehaviors-Studio（Web界面用于开发断言）和EduBehaviors-kit（Python包+SetFit编码器模型用于复用）。

Result: 在TalkMoves数据集的教师话语七类标注任务上：GPT-5.6 Luna达到Macro-F1=0.673、Cohen's κ=0.688；Gemini 3.6 Flash达到Macro-F1=0.664、κ=0.702。超过此前直接提示LLM的最佳结果（Macro-F1=0.61, κ=0.58），但仍低于微调RoBERTa（Macro-F1=0.76）。三类断言源（词汇、corpus-derived、construct-derived）均对性能有贡献，跨模型一致性过滤在大多数标注器上提升性能。

Conclusion: EduBehaviors框架通过将可观察行为识别与构念标签分离，使标注决策可审查和修订，行为标注可在多个构念间复用。在TalkMoves评估中通常优于直接LLM提示但仍低于专家数据微调的专用分类器。该框架配套工具支持研究者在自有数据上应用该方法，但使用跨模型一致性作为断言有效性代理时仍需人工审计。

Abstract: Large language models have allowed the rapid deployment of pedagogical annotations corresponding to constructs of interest, allowing a natural language interface for generating classifications on a conversational dataset. However due to the opaque nature of LLM reasoning, we have no verifiable, mechanistic insight into why a model chose a label for an utterance. We introduce the EduBehaviors framework, an interpretable, scalable approach to annotating educational data that uses LLMs to measure repeated observable behaviors relevant to many constructs of interest and then learns a classifier for the construct based on these observable behaviors. We evaluate the framework on the TalkMoves dataset, predicting the Teacher TalkMoves labels. Our best configuration results in a macro-F1 of 0.673 and 0.688 Cohen's kappa, proving competitive with direct prompting approaches. In addition, we release EduBehaviors Toolkit, two tools allowing researchers to operationalize the EduBehaviors framework in their own data.

</details>


### [28] [Giving Credit Where It's Due: Redundancy-Aware Learning for Efficient Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27156)
*Yuqing Zhou,Hong Wang,Manqing Mao,Zhuoer Wang,Samson Koelle,Jie Yuan,Yanjun Lin,James Feng,Nikki Lijing Kuang,Ziwei Zhu,Wei Niu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RECAP提出了一种冗余感知的信用分配方法，通过建模推理步骤间的语义依赖关系（结构责任）和步骤对正确答案的贡献（步骤效能），将GRPO的轨迹级优势重塑为步骤级更新信号，无需额外的过程奖励模型即可在保持准确率的同时显著减少推理token数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型常生成正确但冗长的推理轨迹。现有的推理效率优化方法存在局限：轨迹级方法对整条轨迹施加统一信号，无法区分冗余步骤和关键步骤；细粒度方法（token级或步骤级）仅捕捉步骤与最终答案的直接关系，忽略了步骤通过后续推理间接产生的贡献；结构感知方法仅建模信息流但未考虑步骤是否朝正确方向推进。这些局限使得在缩短推理的同时难以保持准确率。

Method: RECAP包含三个阶段：(1)使用LLM-as-a-Judge标注步骤间的语义依赖关系，构建有向无环图（DAG），包含三种边类型：support（后续推导必需）、context（提供有用上下文）和restatement（冗余重复）；(2)从最终答案节点反向传播credit，计算每个步骤的结构责任分数；(3)通过计算每步加入后黄金答案对数似然的变化（ΔL）衡量步骤效能，将结构责任与步骤效能通过指数变换结合为步骤得分w，经token加权归一化后，重塑GRPO优势为步骤特定的优势A_RECAP=[(1-β)+β·w]·A_GRPO。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-Math-7B和DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B两个7B模型上、GSM8K、MATH-500、AIME 2024和AIME 2025四个数学推理基准上：Qwen2.5-Math-7B上pass@1提升2.0-3.7个百分点，推理token减少8%-31%；消融实验表明结构责任是主要贡献来源，步骤效能起互补校准作用；token节省主要来自减少推理操作数量（而非压缩表达），死胡同推理从GRPO的19.5%-25.3%降至5.4%-5.7%。

Conclusion: RECAP通过建模推理步骤间的语义依赖关系和答案导向进展，成功地在GRPO框架内实现了步骤级的冗余感知信用分配。该方法无需独立训练过程奖励模型，也不需要预构建的简洁轨迹，即可在数学推理任务上同时提升准确率和效率。token节省来源于减少了不必要的推理操作（特别是死胡同推理），而非仅仅压缩表达方式。

Abstract: Large reasoning models can produce correct yet unnecessarily long reasoning traces. Existing methods improve reasoning efficiency with trajectory-level objectives or local token- and step-level signals, but rarely model inter-step semantic dependencies. This limits their ability to distinguish redundant steps from those that support later deductions, making it harder to shorten reasoning without sacrificing accuracy. We introduce RECAP (REdundancy-aware Credit Assignment via Propagation), which addresses this limitation by assigning credit where it is due based on both a step's downstream role in the reasoning structure and its contribution to solving the problem correctly.
