We define structural responsibility to capture the step's downstream role by measuring how strongly later reasoning depends on it, using credit propagated backward from the final-answer node through an outcome-independent, LLM-annotated semantic dependency graph. However, a step can have high structural responsibility yet steer the reasoning away from the correct solution. RECAP therefore introduces step efficacy to measure answer-directed progress through changes in gold-answer log-likelihood as each step is added. Together, these signals reshape rollout-level GRPO advantages into step-specific updates. RECAP requires neither a separately trained process reward model nor preconstructed concise trajectories. Across two 7B models and four mathematical reasoning benchmarks, RECAP improves the accuracy-efficiency trade-off. On Qwen2.5-Math-7B, it improves pass@1 by 2.0-3.7 percentage points while reducing reasoning tokens by 8%-31% relative to GRPO across all four benchmarks. Analysis suggests these savings reflect fewer reasoning operations and less dead-end reasoning, rather than more compact expression.

</details>


### [29] [Attention Routing Stabilizes Early: Working-Set Inference for Recurrent Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27373)
*Ke Wan,Chen Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现循环语言模型中存在"路由早于表示收敛"的时间尺度分离现象，并据此提出无需训练的 WISE 方法：早期使用全局注意力发现稀疏工作集，后期复用该路由支持，将注意力成本从 O(n²) 降至 O(nm)。在多跳 QA 基准上保持质量的同时，在 4K 上下文实现 1.76× 的注意力加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环语言模型在每个循环步都重新计算全局注意力，但作者观察到注意力路由相关量（支持集与分布）远早于表示相关量（隐藏状态与注意力输出）趋于稳定。这意味着后期循环步中"在哪里看"已确定，但"如何使用"仍在精炼，存在大量冗余的全局注意力计算。

Method: WISE（Working-set Inference with Support Exploitation）：（1）两阶段策略——前 t_d=12 步用全局注意力发现块结构化（块大小 B=32）的 η=0.95 累积质量支持集，来源为步 9–12 的并集；（2）后期 t>12 步固定该路由支持，但 H、Q、K、V 和支持集内注意力权重仍动态更新；（3）推导有限步识别定理，说明支持集可在有限循环深度内稳定；（4）用 Triton 实现 B=32 的块稀疏注意力核，跳过非活跃块并融合评分-掩码-softmax-聚合。

Result: 机制：在 HotpotQA 和 GSM8K 上路由相关量一致早于表示相关量稳定（默认标准下路由-表示间隔 4.97 步），并在 Recurrent-Llama-T32 上 60/60 全部复现。控制干预：WISE 在 HotpotQA 上 F1=0.2317，优于 Truncate@12、Static-95、MassMatched、SizeMatched、Freeze-A@12，答案变化仅 24/100。效率：4K 上下文下工作集密度从 512 时的 57% 降至 37%，late-reuse 速度提升 1.76×，完整 32 步轨迹 1.36×；2K 内质量无显著损失，4K 出现 3.3 点 F1 下降。B=32 是稀疏度与执行效率的最佳折中点。边界：早期路由越能预测后期注意力，延迟发现收益越小（如 Recurrent-Llama）。

Conclusion: 循环语言模型中存在路由与表示收敛时间尺度的稳健分离，使路由结构可在不冻结表示精炼的前提下被复用。WISE 以无需训练的方式利用此分离，在保持多跳问答质量的同时，将后期注意力成本从 O(n²d) 降至 O(nmd)，并通过专用块稀疏核获得实际 GPU 加速。路由支持级稀疏与 FlashAttention 互补，更紧密的核协同设计可进一步提升效率。

Abstract: Recurrent language models repeatedly apply shared network blocks to refine latent representations, but standard inference recomputes global attention at every recurrent step. We study attention dynamics across recurrent depth and find that attention support and distributions stabilize substantially earlier than hidden states and attention outputs. This suggests a two-stage structure: early steps discover a sparse working set of relevant context, while later steps refine representations over largely the same routing support. Motivated by this structure, we introduce WISE (Working-set Inference with Support Exploitation), a training-free method that uses unrestricted global attention during early recurrence and later reuses directly discovered block-structured support while keeping recurrent depth and within-support attention computation dynamic. Controlled interventions show that recurrent discovery is important and that support-only reuse better preserves model behavior than more restrictive attention-reuse alternatives. Across multi-hop QA benchmarks, WISE largely preserves full-attention performance, while context scaling reveals increasingly sparse working sets and greater efficiency gains. Quality is largely preserved through 2K context, with a measurable loss at 4K. An optimized sparse-attention implementation achieves up to a 1.76x attention speedup over native FlashAttention at 4K and a 1.36x speedup for the full 32-step attention trajectory. Our code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [30] [From Sentiment Classification to Actionable and Responsible Feedback: A Scoping Review and Evidence Map of NLP in Student Evaluation of Teaching, 2015-2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27939)
*Jeff Eicher,Rafael da Silva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文是一篇范围综述(PRISMA-ScR),梳理了2015—2026年间421项将NLP技术应用于开放式学生评教文本(SET)的研究,从技术演进(RQ1)与四项价值维度(RQ2–RQ5)双轴绘制研究图景,核心贡献在于揭示“行动性断层”——尽管多数研究产出可演示输出,但很少进入面向真实用户的评估阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NLP在SET评论分析中的技术路径从词典/传统分类器发展到Transformer与LLM,但技术的多样化是否带来了相应的教育价值提升与证据稳健性并不清晰。作者希望系统描绘这一领域的技术-价值耦合状况,识别研究与实践落地之间的差距。

Method: 采用PRISMA-ScR范围综述框架,纳入2015–2026年的421项研究。文献筛选使用双盲LLM筛选并辅以抽样人工裁决;信息编码覆盖七个抽取领域,在综合阶段进行针对性的编码手册边界审查。分析沿两条主轴展开:技术轴(RQ1)与四项价值维度(RQ2–RQ5),并构建“行动性等级”A2+(产出演示)与A3+(真实用户评估)进行量化对比。

Result: 1)行动性断层:A2+为258/421(61.3%),A3+仅49/421(11.6%),相差49.7个百分点。2)任务分布:情感分析仍是主流任务(300/421)。3)诊断与生成深度:D4–D5仅占已解决案例的28.2%。4)公平性:正式公平性度量罕见,仅1.9%。5)技术多样性(词典到LLM)与价值证据之间的对应关系并不明确。

Conclusion: 该综述为描述性研究,不能支持技术“进步”的因果论断。研究图景呈现技术共存与报告不均衡的特征;真正的贡献在于刻画A2+到A3+之间的“悬崖”,即从可演示输出到面向真实用户评估之间存在显著断层,而非构建一个隐含的质量阶梯。

Abstract: Natural language processing (NLP) applied to open-ended teaching-evaluation comments (Student Evaluation of Teaching, SET) has tracked the field's technical evolution--from lexicons and conventional classifiers to transformers and large language models (LLMs)--but it is not evident that this technical diversification has been accompanied by corresponding gains in educational value and robustness of the evidence. This scoping review (PRISMA-ScR) maps 421 studies (2015-2026, 2026 partial) along a technical axis (RQ1) and four value dimensions (RQ2-RQ5). Dual mutually blinded LLM screening with sampled human adjudication coded seven extraction domains, with targeted codebook-boundary review at synthesis. The joint map's sharpest quantified gap is the actionability discontinuity: demonstrated output or stronger (A2+: 258/421; 61.3%) versus intended-user evaluation or stronger (A3+: 49/421; 11.6%), a 49.7 percentage-point drop. Sentiment analysis remains the modal task (300/421); diagnostic and generative depth is a substantial minority (D4-D5: 28.2% of resolved cases); a formal fairness metric is rare (1.9%). The findings are descriptive and do not support causal claims of progress: technological coexistence and uneven reporting are part of the map, but the A2+ to A3+ cliff is the contribution, not a quality ladder.

</details>


### [31] [Six Layers Less: Encoder Pruning for Whisper with Label-Free Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27980)
*Rasmus Aagaard,Nicki Skafte Detlefsen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对Whisper-large-v3-turbo编码器的层级剪枝方法，通过留一法（leave-one-out）测量各层对词错误率（WER）的影响，移除6个最不重要的层（占编码器18.5%），并使用无标签的英语语音数据通过MSE蒸馏恢复性能，最终以浅层网络形式实现无需自定义推理代码的高效部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Whisper压缩研究主要聚焦于解码器（如turbo变体从32层减至4层），而编码器压缩虽有关注却未广泛采用，主要原因是现有方法需要自定义推理代码实现。本文旨在探索一种简单实用的编码器层级剪枝方法，并利用无标签知识蒸馏恢复性能，从而实现即插即用部署。

Method: 1）层级重要性度量：在FLEURS测试集上对丹麦语、英语、德语、法语四种语言计算留一层的平均ΔWER，据此对32层编码器排序；2）层级移除：通过直接操作nn.ModuleList移除6个最不重要的层（L5、L6、L7、L9、L11、L12），产生26层的浅层编码器，无需架构变更；3）无标签知识蒸馏：以完整编码器为教师，剪枝编码器为学生，使用英语People Speech数据集，通过AdamW优化器在2000步内最小化MSE损失以对齐隐藏状态。

Result: 编码器参数从637M减至519M，模型大小从1543MB减至1318MB（bf16）；模型对比基准情况如下：基线WER 18.2%，零样本剪枝后升至21.9%（+3.8%），蒸馏后恢复至20.1%（+1.9%）；在Apple M4 Pro上编码器推理加速1.22-1.24倍，批大小8下端到端加速1.75倍；研究表明该方法仅可剪枝6层，剪第7层（即使是最优选择）将导致2.6倍性能退化；蒸馏过程恢复所有语言性能（即使只用英语数据），表明MSE目标重建的是声学表征而非语言特定特征；50次随机剪枝实验显示性能退化从+5到+471不等，证明基于ΔWER的数据驱动选择至关重要。

Conclusion: 本文验证了Whisper-large-v3-turbo编码器层级剪枝的可行性，仅需移除6层即可获得一致的1.22-1.24倍编码器加速和高达1.75倍的端到端加速，且作为浅层网络可作为推理框架的直接替代品；留一ΔWER是有效的层级重要性度量；k=6存在明显性能悬崖现象；未来工作应探索此悬崖现象的成因，并将方法推广至更现代的ASR模型。

Abstract: Pruning large pre-trained transformer-based ASR models such as OpenAI's Whisper has seen great adoption, as pruning the decoder led to significant end-to-end transcription speedups. For instance, the {\tt whisper-large-v3-turbo} variant reduced the decoder from 32 to 4 layers, while Distill-Whisper similarly reduced the decoder to only 2 layers. Although some attention has been put towards reducing the size of the encoder, no approach has seen wide adoption. This could be due to the need for custom inference implementations to take advantage of the compressed model. We present an approach that ranks encoder layers by the leave-one-layer-out change in Word Error Rate (WER). The six layers that cause the least change are removed, corresponding to $18.5\%$ of the encoder stack. The pruned model requires no custom inference code as it is simply a more shallow encoder with fewer layers. We further distill using unlabeled monolingual speech data to recover performance degradation caused by the zero-shot layer pruning. Mean WER across four languages increases to $20.1\%$ after distillation, compared to $21.9\%$ zero-shot, going from a baseline of $18.2\%$. We release all of our code ( this https URL ) and the pruned model ( this https URL ).

</details>


### [32] [Risk-Controlled KV-Cache Eviction: From Memory Budgets to Risk Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27981)
*Beomgu Kang,SoJin Yun,Hojoon Kim,Hyunseok Seo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于Learn-then-Test (LTT)的风险控制框架，将KV-cache淘汰从"内存预算-平均质量"的传统评估范式转变为"部署可靠性契约-请求级风险"的新范式。通过在校准数据上应用固定序列检验，从预指定的候选策略中选择最激进的保留策略，并对任务平衡混合人群提供有限样本风险保证；若所有压缩策略均未通过认证，则回退到全KV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV-cache淘汰方法通常以平均质量-内存权衡来评估，但平均性能良好并不保证请求级别的可靠性：小平均损失可能掩盖某些请求的严重退化。部署场景需要一个能控制请求级"实质性退化"事件发生频率的可靠性保证，而非仅仅优化平均指标。

Method: 方法核心包括：(1) 将退化定义为相对全KV推理的配对效用差Δ_π(X) = U_full(X) - U_π(X)，并定义"部署风险"(ρ)为超过容差τ的请求级事件概率；(2) 可靠性契约(τ, ε, δ)，要求风险≤ε且以概率≥1-δ通过校准样本保证；(3) 任务分层校准设计，将请求级违反指标建模为独立Bernoulli变量，使用Hoeffding比较定理和二项分布构造保守有效p值；(4) 固定序列LTT检验，从保守到激进依次检验候选策略，遇到第一个未通过则停止并返回最后认证策略或全KV回退；(5) 该框架与压缩器无关，可应用于固定预算方法（SnapKV、AdaKV、DefensiveKV、Layer-DefensiveKV）和请求自适应方法（ReFreeKV）。

Result: 主要实验结果：(1) LongBench上，Llama-3.1-8B下SnapKV认证保留率0.75，AdaKV为0.65，DefensiveKV为0.40，Layer-DefensiveKV为0.35；Mistral-7B下SnapKV/AdaKV均为0.70，DefensiveKV为0.40，Layer-DefensiveKV为0.45；(2) RULER-32K上，SnapKV的首个候选(0.80保留率)未通过（CAL事件率10.38%），触发全KV回退；其他三种压缩器分别认证为0.70/0.45/0.35；(3) 经验风险低于5%目标但仍未通过认证的策略存在：所有四个Llama LongBench压缩器的第一个未通过候选的经验风险均低于5%，但因有限样本约束未达δ=0.05认证水平；(4) 任务级异质性显著：RULER的CWE任务在Layer-DefensiveKV下风险达29%，LongBench TriviaQA在ReFreeKV下达20%；(5) 合同敏感性分析显示，更严格的τ或ε会导致更高保留率或全KV回退。

Conclusion: 该论文将KV-cache淘汰重构为部署风险控制问题，提供了一种从部署可靠性需求（而非固定内存预算）出发选择KV保留策略的原则性框架。实验证明同一契约下不同压缩器支持的淘汰程度差异显著，经验阈值法可能选择未通过有限样本认证的策略（保留率低5-10个百分点）。该工作将KV-cache压缩从预算优先评估转向风险受控部署，为长上下文LLM服务中的内存利用提供了基于可靠性契约的决策方法。局限性包括证书对声明的人群特定、不提供任务级保证、不保证分布偏移鲁棒性。

Abstract: KV-cache eviction is typically evaluated through average quality-memory trade-offs, yet a small average loss can hide requests whose utility degrades materially. We reformulate eviction as a deployment risk-control problem: a material degradation occurs when eviction lowers task utility by more than a deployment-specified tolerance relative to full-KV inference on the same request, and deployment risk is the population frequency of such events. Given a reliability contract specifying a target risk level and confidence requirement, we use a compressor-agnostic post-hoc certification procedure to select a retention policy from calibration data with a finite-sample guarantee, falling back to full KV when no compressed policy is certified. Across multiple eviction methods, Llama and Mistral models, and LongBench and RULER-32K, the same contract supports substantially different levels of eviction: on Llama, it certifies SnapKV at 75% retention on LongBench but no tested compressed policy on RULER-32K, triggering full-KV fallback. Policies with empirical degradation rates below the 5% target can still fail finite-sample certification; on Llama LongBench, empirical thresholding selects uncertified policies that retain 5-10 percentage points less cache across fixed-budget methods. The proposed framework converts a deployment-level reliability requirement into a KV-memory operating point.

</details>


### [33] [Evaluating Open-Weight LLMs for Turkish Domain Documents Under Retrieval and Hardware Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28007)
*Imtiaz Ul Hassan,Öykü Akbulut,Onur Kaya,Ardhendu Behera,Swagat Kumar,Peter Matthew,Yonghuai Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对土耳其语长篇领域文档的问答任务，在消费级GPU（RTX 3050, 6GB显存）严格资源约束下，对5个7B-8B开源权重大模型进行了端到端评估，并通过证据标注协议将检索失败与模型推理失败分离，验证了模型选择、检索行为与硬件限制需分别评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有土耳其语大模型评估多基于通用、教育性或多选题基准，缺乏针对长篇、结构复杂的领域文档（尤其是需要本地部署处理敏感工业文档）的真实场景评估；同时，传统端到端评估混淆了检索失败与模型推理失败两种错误模式，无法针对性地进行改进。

Method: 构建两个土耳其语文档问答基准（109页工业R&D报告+112页公共部门报告，各100题，含易/中/难/弃答四类能力档）；设计证据标注评估协议，每题记录支撑证据；统一采用4比特量化、4096 token上下文、温度0解码，在RTX 3050上测试5个模型（Trendyol-8B、Qwen2.5-7B、Cosmos-8B、Mistral-7B、Kocdigital-8B）；比较7种检索配置（字符/词项TF-IDF、BM25、E5-small/base、两种RRF混合），用95% Wilson区间和配对McNemar检验统计显著性；按题型使用定制评分规则。

Result: 主基准端到端准确率49%-75%（Trendyol-8B最高75%，Qwen2.5-7B次之65%，效率最佳），副基准51%-82%，三强模型排名一致；Trendyol在Medium档领先（22/30），但延迟约为Qwen2.5的14倍；Kocdigital弃答仅9/20，不适合事实报告检索；7种检索配置中无一种在统计上显著优于字符TF-IDF基线；主报告证据召回率57%，副报告91%，且主报告随上下文预算增至7000-9000字符召回提升至72-76%，说明检索是该报告的瓶颈约束，而副报告接近饱和。

Conclusion: 在土耳其语领域文档问答的本地部署场景下，模型选择、检索行为与硬件限制构成三个相互独立且需分别评估的约束因素；简单的字符TF-IDF在固定上下文预算下已具备竞争力，更复杂的稠密/混合检索未必带来显著提升；研究结果鼓励针对不同文档单独诊断瓶颈，并在合法、医疗等专业领域文档上做进一步复现验证。

Abstract: Most Turkish-capable large language models (LLMs) are evaluated using general-purpose benchmarks rather than long, structurally complex domain documents. This paper evaluates five open-weight 7B-8B models for Turkish document question answering under a resource-constrained local deployment setting. The primary benchmark contains 100 systematically validated questions derived from a 109-page industrial R&D report, and the evaluation protocol is replicated using a second 112-page public-sector report and an independently constructed 100-question set. All models are evaluated locally on an NVIDIA RTX 3050 laptop GPU with 6 GB VRAM using controlled prompting, decoding, and 4-bit quantisation. The principal methodological contribution is an evidence-annotated evaluation protocol that separates retrieval failure from downstream model reasoning failure without requiring additional model calls. On the primary benchmark, end-to-end accuracy ranges from 49% to 75%. Seven lexical, dense, and hybrid retrieval configurations are additionally compared using 95% Wilson intervals and exact paired McNemar tests; none significantly outperforms the character TF-IDF baseline on either document. Evidence recall saturates differently across the two reports, showing that retrieval and effective context capacity can be binding constraints for some documents but not others. These results demonstrate that model selection, retrieval behaviour, and hardware limits must be evaluated separately when deploying open-weight LLMs for Turkish domain documents.

</details>


### [34] [Predicting Quantization Price for Selecting PTQ Configurations Before Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28270)
*Junbin Qiu,Jian Mu,Weitong Zhang,Yao Shu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文将权重量化(PTQ)重新建模为部署前的配置选择问题,通过前向KL散度的二次"量化价格"统一比较格式、粒度、码本、等价变换和比特分配,在校准阶段无需部署即可完成预算约束的配置搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PTQ方法(OBQ/GPTQ/AdaRound的重建误差、HAWQ的Hessian敏感度、QuaRot/SmoothQuant的变换效果、GuidedQuant的下游损失)各自在固定量化几何或独立配置族内评分,缺乏一个能在部署前跨族比较所有可部署权重空间配置的统一标准。

Method: 方法核心是"定价的层输出误差":(1)从全精度到量化的前向KL展开,在参考模型处一阶项抵消,留下二次价格 ρ_l(α_l)=½Tr(H_l Σ_l(α_l)),其中候选提供误差协方差 Σ_l、参考模型提供下游曲率 H_l;(2)有限格式改变扰动矩,等价变换改变坐标和输入度量,部署成本约束可部署性;(3)各向同性迹归约给出可计算的标量评分 ρ̂_l=½σ̂²_l ω̂_l τ̂_l,τ̂_l 用Gauss-Newton Fisher迹估计器(Hutchinson)一次前向反向获得;(4)最终的预算约束选择问题通过二值MILP求解。

Result: 控制扰动实验在OPT-125M和Qwen3-0.6B上:实现价格与KL漂移的对齐ρ分别为0.9470/0.9249,迹价格ρ分别为0.9483/0.9438。在Llama-3.2-1B/3B/8B的权重空间PTQ决策中:比特分配任务上价格引导选择器时间大幅低于AMQ(1B上579s vs 11635s,8B上5967s vs 23337s)且平均下游任务得分具竞争力;变换选择和粒度分配在三个模型上一致取得最低WikiText2困惑度,并优于CALM-CKA和固定group-128基线。

Conclusion: 论文将权重量化的部署前决策形式化为一个跨配置族的统一价格搜索问题,把重建误差和对角评分解释为该完整价格的简化代理,使固定几何的比特分配成为特例而非组织性问题。该方法在局部、矩稳定、曲率对齐的区间内最可信,适用于预算受限、可部署的多族PTQ配置自动选择。

Abstract: Weight-space post-training quantization (PTQ) must choose finite formats, granularities, quantizer families, transformations, and bits before the completed quantized model reveals its output-distribution drift. Existing PTQ methods predict important pieces of this degradation, including reconstruction error, Hessian sensitivity, transformation effects, and downstream loss, but these pieces are usually scored after fixing the quantization geometry or inside separate configuration families. We formulate weight-space PTQ as pre-deployment configuration selection using priced layer-output error. Each admissible layer configuration is treated as an error generator with a deployment cost, which induces a layer-output error covariance $\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_l(\alpha_l)$, and the full-precision model prices that covariance by downstream curvature, $\widehat{\rho}_l(\alpha_l)=\frac{1}{2}\operatorname{Tr}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_l\,\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}_l(\alpha_l)\right)$. The price follows from full-precision-to-quantized forward KL, whose first-order term cancels at the reference model. It turns reconstruction and diagonal scores into reduced proxies that drop price factors, while finite formats, codebooks, granularities, and equivalent transformations become comparable candidates through the covariances they induce and the costs they pay. A trace reduction then yields a calibration-time price table and a budgeted price-guided selector, making fixed-geometry bit allocation a special case rather than the organizing problem.

</details>


### [35] [Fine-Tuning LLMs for Translation: General Forgetting Mitigation Does Not Preserve MT-Specific Instruction Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28395)
*Niklas Scholz,David Thulke,Abdallah Nasir,Will Allred,Evgeny Matusov,Hermann Ney*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了缓解LLM微调灾难性遗忘的多种方法在机器翻译任务上的效果，发现通用基准上的遗忘缓解效果不能代表MT特定指令遵循能力（MT-IF）的保留。EWC在保留通用能力上表现最佳，但无法保留MT-IF；只有混入对应控制任务数据的方法才能保留特定MT-IF能力，且该效果不能泛化到同一任务的未见提示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遗忘缓解方法的评估通常基于通用基准，但其在MT微调场景下的适用性以及能否保护MT特定指令遵循能力（如正式度、语法性别、长度控制）尚不清楚。作者质疑通用基准得分能否反映MT-IF的保留情况。

Method: 两阶段实验设计：第一阶段用Llama 3.2 1B Instruct在三种单向语言对（阿姆哈拉语、阿拉伯语、西班牙语→英语）上筛选11种遗忘缓解方法；第二阶段用Llama 3.1 8B Instruct在双向阿拉伯语-英语和西班牙语-英语数据上评估。方法按锚点分为三组：辅助数据锚定（Data Mixing、Model Merging、EWC）、模型输出锚定（KL正则化、STM、CC、EAFT）、基模型参数锚定（Freeze、SPD、LoRA）。评估包括翻译质量（COMET/BLEU）、通用能力（Tülu 3套件）和MT-IF（正式度、语法性别、长度控制）。

Result: Stage 1：EWC在保留通用能力上最优，在1B模型上Pareto前沿。Stage 2（8B模型）：EWC将通用能力下降控制在1.7分（西班牙语）和0.7分（阿拉伯语），相比标准SFT的11.0和8.3分。但EWC在MT-IF上与标准SFT接近（正式度和语法性别控制）。只有混入对应控制任务数据的Data Mixing能保留该MT-IF能力（如语法性别从17.8%恢复到77.9%），但该效果（1）不迁移到其他MT-IF任务，（2）不泛化到同一任务的未见提示（提示记忆化）。SPD是唯一不需控制数据而部分缓解MT-IF遗忘的方法。

Conclusion: 通用基准上的遗忘缓解效果不能代表MT-IF的保留。MT微调评估应测量应用所需的控制能力；当前保留MT-IF的唯一可靠方法是在训练数据中匹配相应任务示例。EWC保留通用能力但无法保留MT-IF的原因在于MT-IF本身依赖翻译行为，而EWC的固定二次惩罚无法阻止MT梯度持续更新承载MT-IF的参数。开放问题包括：能否用更广泛的合成控制任务数据保留MT-IF而不过拟合到见提示，以及这些发现是否能推广到词汇约束和性别中立翻译等更多控制。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models on parallel data improves translation quality but can cause catastrophic forgetting. Mitigation methods are generally evaluated by retention on general benchmarks. We ask whether these findings transfer to machine translation (MT) fine-tuning and to MT-specific instruction following (MT-IF): instructions that modify a translation, such as formality, grammatical gender, and length control. We compare methods anchored to auxiliary data, to model outputs, and to the base model parameters, first in a screening study with Llama 3.2 1B Instruct, then on Llama 3.1 8B Instruct fine-tuned on bidirectional Arabic-English or Spanish-English data. Elastic Weight Consolidation preserves general capabilities best in both stages; on the 8B Spanish model the average score on general benchmarks drops 1.7 points versus 11.0 for standard fine-tuning, yet its scores for formality and grammatical gender control remain close to standard fine-tuning. Only data mixing with control-task examples preserves these controls, but its gains do not transfer to unseen prompts for the same task.

</details>


### [36] [Contrastive Learning for Authorship Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28471)
*Peter Kirby*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对作者验证任务，系统对比了对比学习与分类两种训练范式，提出基于 ModernBERT 的 Bi-Encoder 模型，在 PAN21 数据集上达到 98.4% 的 SOTA 准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者验证任务通常被建模为二分类问题，但交叉编码器受限于上下文长度（如 BERT 的 512 token），且现有研究对不同训练目标（对比学习 vs 分类）在控制其他变量下的效果对比不充分。本文旨在通过严格的对比实验，澄清对比学习是否真正优于分类方法，并探究影响性能的关键因素。

Method: 1. **训练范式**：对比学习（Bi-Encoder，使用 InfoNCE/SupCon 损失，cosine 相似度）vs 配对分类（Cross-Encoder，拼接输入 + sigmoid）。2. **模型**：TinyBERT、DistilBERT-cased、BERT-base-cased、ModernBERT-base/large。3. **数据增强**：将固定配对转换为按作者 ID 的独立文档，实现动态配对重组；随机文本旋转（random text rotation）作为增强手段。4. **超参策略**：使用 μ-Transfer 思想，在 TinyBERT 上网格搜索 batch size 和学习率，再按宽度比例迁移到更大模型。5. **关键技术**：mean pooling、temperature scaling、Platt scaling 校准。

Result: **核心结论**：在控制其他条件下，对比学习稳定优于分类方法（即使在更短上下文和更少计算量下）。**最佳模型**：ModernBERT-large Bi-Encoder，4096 token 上下文长度，batch size 4096，温度 0.01，40 epochs，在 PAN21 测试集上 Accuracy=0.984, F1=0.984, AUC=0.995, Overall=0.979，超过 Boenninghoff21（0.955）和 BigBird Cross-Encoder（0.960）等先前 SOTA。**关键发现**：(1) InfoNCE 损失优于 Circle、Multi-Similarity、Semi-Hard 等损失；(2) 上下文长度从 512 增至 4096 带来显著提升（F1 从 0.925 升至 0.984）；(3) 随机文本跨度数据增强至关重要（去掉后 F1 从 0.86 降至 0.68）；(4) 最后层 mean pooling 优于首末层平均池化。

Conclusion: 对比学习在作者验证任务上是比分类更有效的训练范式，其优势在于可独立编码文档、延迟配对评分、支持大批量负样本。ModernBERT 的长上下文能力（4096 tokens）与对比学习相结合是该任务的新 SOTA。代码与数据已在 GitHub 开源。

Abstract: Our results show that contrastive learning outperforms a classification-based approach to authorship verification under the tested settings. We identify loss function, batch size, training duration, pre-trained model, input context length, and random text span data augmentation as important factors of model performance. Based on these considerations, we develop a ModernBERT Bi-Encoder model that achieves 98.4% accuracy on the PAN21 authorship verification task.

</details>


### [37] [Classifying Interpretive Canons at the Sentence Level: A Benchmark from the German Federal Constitutional Court](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26945)
*Felix Ringe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个句子级基准测试，用于评估大语言模型（LLM）在德国联邦宪法法院判决中对Larenz传统解释规则进行分类的能力。作者将Savigny/Larenz的四类经典解释 canon（语法、系统、历史、目的-客观）操作化为分类标准，构建了专家标注的数据集，并基于四款LLM在专家手写提示与GEPA优化提示下进行了基线评估。平均F1在70.4–79.2之间，语法解释最易识别，系统解释最难；GEPA优化提示在测试配置下并未系统性超越专家提示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 司法推理中解释方法的使用是大规模实证法律研究的核心议题，但以往工作以段落为单位，无法精确定位论证位置；同时人工标注成本高昂，限制了对解释方法历史演变的研究。LLM有望大幅降低分析成本，但需要可靠的细粒度基准来验证其能力。

Method: 方法包含三部分：(1) 基于Larenz的定义将解释操作化为四类canon（语法/系统/历史/目的-客观）并定义解读的前提条件（抽象性、断言性、具体法条参照、自行提出）；(2) 设计级联式标注管道，以句子为标注单位，包含三个阅读子标准、五个论证子任务（论证门控+四个canon），从L.L.Con语料中选取28份判决（15份穷尽标注，13份选择性标注）由法学博士单独标注；(3) 评估四个LLM（DeepSeek-V4 Pro/Flash、Gemini 3.5 Flash Lite、MiniMax M3）在专家手写德语提示与GEPA遗传帕累托优化提示下的表现，使用受约束解码、LLM法官进行法条引用匹配、bootstrap置信区间。

Result: 七个二元子任务的平均F1在70.4（DeepSeek-V4-Flash+GEPA）至79.2（MiniMax M3+GEPA）之间，模型间差异不足九分，无单一模型族占优。语法解释在八种模型-提示配置中有七种得分最高（均值82.9），系统解释最低（均值58.4），历史与目的-客观居中。GEPA优化提示未系统性超越专家提示：Gemini和MiniMax下领先，DeepSeek-Flash下落后，DeepSeek-Pro基本持平。错误分析显示多句论证导致假阴性，表层线索（如"Wortlaut"一词、并列法条引用）驱动假阳性。法条引用任务的集合重叠F1（64.1–78.6）被空引用句子主导，span级F1仅28.4–42.4，存在过度抽取。

Conclusion: 论文得出两点结论：(1) canon难度的相对排序与法律方法论直觉一致——语法解释最易识别，系统解释最难；(2) 测试配置下的GEPA优化提示未系统性超越专家手写提示，证实专家提示作为基线的参考价值。但由于标注单一、语料限于一国一法院、测试集较小（置信区间较宽），结论应视为起点，未来工作将扩展语料并引入多标注者。数据集、提示与评估运行均已公开发布，且可通过Harbor框架中的AI智能体从原始数据精确复现所有208个分数点估计和2,200条记录。

Abstract: Judicial reasoning remains challenging for large language models (LLMs) to analyze. This paper contributes a sentence-level benchmark for evaluating the ability of LLMs to classify interpretive canons as articulated by Larenz in the tradition of Savigny. Our contributions are threefold. First, we operationalize this conception of interpretation as classification criteria. Second, we provide a dataset of decisions of the German Federal Constitutional Court annotated at the sentence level. Third, we report baseline evaluations of four LLMs from three model families under expert hand-written prompts, compared against prompts optimized with Genetic-Pareto (GEPA). Mean F1 over the seven binary subtasks clusters between 70.4 and 79.2 across models, with grammatical interpretation usually the easiest canon to identify and systematic interpretation usually the hardest; under the tested configuration, GEPA-optimized prompts do not systematically outperform the hand-written ones, suggesting that the expert prompts provide a meaningful baseline.

</details>


### [38] [When Learned Context Planning Fails to Beat Strong Retrieval: A Controlled Study of Planning, Routing, and Reranking for Long-Context QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26976)
*Yingrui Li,Han Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在LongBench-v2多选问答基准上，系统比较了"学习式上下文规划器"与传统强检索方法。结果表明：在加入BM25、锚定混合检索、预算选择器与交叉编码器重排序等强对照后，学习式规划器并不能稳健地超越传统检索，更适合被视为一种弱相关性信号，而非检索的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习式上下文规划（learned context planning）通过SFT训练的模型在推理前选择证据片段，被认为可能比传统检索更精准。然而，以往评估往往缺乏强对照（如BM25、锚定混合检索、重排序器），且可能因数据泄露或弱基准而高估规划器的价值。本文旨在严格检验：当与强检索、路由、预算选择器和重排序控制对比时，学习式证据选择是否仍能带来稳健提升。

Method: 任务：LongBench-v2全部503道多选QA题（按301/50/152划分训练/开发/测试集，存在部分转导性）。模型：Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct作为答题模型；规划器为其LoRA SFT适配器（基于140训练+28开发样本的outcome-rewarded采样轨迹，rank 16，3 epoch）。检索基线：BM25、sentence-transformers/all-MiniLM-L6-v2稠密检索、RRF混合检索，以及"锚定混合"（top BM25原子置前）。重排序器：cross-encoder/ms-marco-MiniLM-L-6-v2。预算：18k/9k/6k字符。统计：Wilson区间、配对符号检验精确p值、配对自助法95%区间、5折分层与留域交叉验证。B-H校正用于多重检验。

Result: （1）18k全预算下，锚定混合检索36.18%与BM25 35.98%并列最优，最佳规划器引导方法仅34.19%；Planner+BM25低于BM25 3.78分（p=0.061）。（2）152题无泄露测试集：42.11% vs 36.84%（p=0.215，CI跨零）。（3）Oracle路由上界46.52%–56.26%，但泄露安全路由器无法转化为实际增益。（4）紧预算下，6k规划器仅领先+0.40（p=0.930），9k反输1.19分；规划器重排序6k估计+1.79分但置信区间跨零（p=0.478）。（5）打包顺序、原子Jaccard、得分平坦性等92项机制检验均无B-H校正后显著项。

Conclusion: 在该LongBench-v2多选QA设定中，学习式上下文规划仅表现为弱相关性特征，无法替代强检索；其表面增益在配对统计检验下不稳健。研究强调：评估规划器必须以锚定混合检索、预算选择器、泄露安全路由与强重排序器作为对照，否则易高估其价值。受限条件包括单模型、单基准、单一规划器训练配方及无原子级标注的机制分析。

Abstract: Learned context planning selects evidence atoms before an answer model reasons over them. We test whether this learned selection improves long-context multiple-choice QA after strong retrieval, routing, budgeted-selector, and reranking controls. Our primary diagnostic uses all 503 LongBench-v2 MCQ questions with Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct. The planner is SFT-trained on outcome-selected traces from 140 training and 28 development questions; because the 503-question analysis includes those questions, it is partly transductive. At an 18k-character budget, anchored hybrid retrieval reaches 36.18% accuracy and BM25 reaches 35.98%, while the best direct planner-guided method reaches 34.19%. On the untouched 152-question test split, anchored hybrid remains higher (42.11% versus 36.84%). Leakage-safe routers cannot convert a large oracle gap. Under tight budgets, the best planner is ahead by only 0.40 points at 6k and loses at 9k; planner-guided reranking has a +1.79-point estimate at 6k with a paired interval crossing zero and ties the control at 9k. Packing-order and score-flatness analyses did not identify a stable mechanism. Under this setup, learned planning is a weak relevance signal rather than a replacement for strong retrieval.

</details>


### [39] [LEGO: Synergizing Expert GraphRAG and Expert Chain-of-Thought for Legal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27009)
*Qingjing Chen,Junkai Zhang,Shaochun Wang,Jiahao Ding,Siyuan Zheng,Yukun Yan,Zhi Zheng,Antonino Rotolo,Yun Liu,Weixing Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LEGO是一个双模块框架，将ExpertGraphRAG（基于专家标注的民法典图谱和规范性覆盖贪心检索）与ExpertCoT（Provision-Fact-Conclusion三段论推理链）相结合，以8B参数的Qwen3-8B为骨干，在复杂民法推理任务中达到40.53%准确率，优于同类RAG基线，并与GPT-5、DeepSeek-V3 671B等更大模型相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG和GraphRAG方法依赖词法或语义相似度，忽略了法条之间的规范性关系（如一般-特殊、例外、优先适用）；而普通CoT提示生成的推理过程缺乏法律推理的规范性结构约束。这两个瓶颈阻碍了LLM在法律领域的复杂推理能力。

Method: LEGO采用三段论法律推理框架：(1) ExpertGraphRAG：基于专家标注的民法典图谱（318个概念、409个规则卡片、1211条文、4152条支持边），使用规范性覆盖贪心算法(NCG)选择条文子图，通过边际法律效用最大化覆盖相关规范性规则；(2) ExpertCoT：将检索到的条文与案件事实组织成Provision-Fact-Conclusion结构化推理，配备11条显式指令约束推理过程，确保前提核实、规则层级解析和法律效果匹配；(3) 以Qwen3-8B为生成骨干，贪心解码，温度为0。

Result: 在LawExamQA_Civil数据集（723题）上达到40.53%精确匹配准确率，优于所有评估的同骨干RAG基线（Naive RAG 28.91%、HippoRAG2 29.88%、RAPTOR 29.32%、G-Retriever 31.67%、LightRAG 28.77%），且统计显著（调整后p值范围1.5×10⁻⁹至4.4×10⁻⁶）；在≥4-hop问题上达38.71%，远超第二名6.45个百分点；在LexRAG_Civil和PLawBench_Civil开放式基准上也取得所有评估基线中的最高分；消融实验表明两个组件具有互补贡献（NCG检索将Recall@8从27.99%提升至72.53%），但统计上无法确认严格的超加性。

Conclusion: LEGO通过将专家法律知识结构同时注入检索和推理两个阶段，有效解决了复杂法律推理中检索相关性不足和推理过程缺乏规范性约束的问题；实验证明专家结构化的检索和三段论推理能让小规模开源模型达到与大规模专有模型相当的性能水平；局限性包括依赖专家标注构建知识图谱带来的跨法域可扩展性挑战，以及对生成推理忠实性的验证不足。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly applied to high-risk domains such as law, yet complex legal reasoning remains limited by two structural challenges. First, existing RAG and GraphRAG methods emphasize lexical or semantic similarity while overlooking normative relations among legal provisions. Second, vanilla Chain-of-Thought prompting may generate plausible rationales without enforcing the normative structure of legal reasoning. To deal with the bottleneck of pipelines in the legal reasoning domain, we propose LEGO, a dual-module framework that synergizes Legal Expert GraphRAG and expert Chain-of-thought for complex legal reasoning. ExpertGraphRAG uses an expert-annotated civil code graph encoding these normative relations with a greedy normative-coverage retrieval algorithm to dynamically extract instance-specific provision subgraphs, while ExpertCoT organizes the retrieved provisions and case facts into structured Provision-Fact-Conclusion reasoning. With a Qwen3-8B backbone, LEGO achieves 40.53% exact-match accuracy on LawExamQA_Civil, outperforming the evaluated RAG and CoT baselines and performing comparably to the evaluated larger models, while remaining robust on multi-hop questions. It also achieves the best results among the evaluated baselines on the open-ended benchmarks. Ablation studies confirm the individual and complementary contributions of both modules, demonstrating LEGO's effectiveness in improving LLMs' complex legal reasoning ability. Code and dataset can be found in the link: this https URL

</details>


### [40] [The Illinois Social Attitudes Aggregate Corpus (ISAAC): An Open Tool and Reproducible Pipeline for Analyzing Social Group Discourse at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27059)
*Babak Hemmatian,Sarah Hadjarab,Jessica Chen,Benedek Kurdi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ISAAC（Illinois Social Attitudes Aggregate Corpus），一个超过5.27亿条英语Reddit帖子的开放、模块化语料库，覆盖2007至2023年间六个社会群体维度（种族、性取向、年龄、能力、体重、肤色）的相关讨论，并提供多维度的语义标注与空间标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前社会群体态度相关研究面临数据碎片化、可复现性差、难以跨平台和跨主题扩展等问题，亟需一个统一、公开、可扩展的基础设施来支持大规模社会话语研究。

Method: 采用多步骤、人工审核的过滤流水线筛选Reddit帖子，将无关内容控制在10%以下；然后通过算法为每条帖子标注用户估计的居住地区，并附加道德化、情感、情绪、语言概括等语义标签；最后通过与社会搜索行为、社会事件时间序列及长期态度变迁的关联分析验证语料库有效性。

Result: 构建了包含5.27亿+帖子的ISAAC语料库，验证显示其与社会宏观趋势（如在线搜索行为、重大社会事件的全国及区域时间峰值、长期公共态度转变）具有收敛性证据，且支持跨类别比较、长期时间追踪和空间异质性分析。

Conclusion: ISAAC作为一个完全公开、模块化的基础设施，消除了社会态度研究中的碎片化问题，支持多种研究工作流（无代码网站、SQL Playground、Python包、HuggingFace），并可便捷扩展至新平台、语言和社会类别。

Abstract: We introduce the Illinois Social Attitudes Aggregate Corpus (ISAAC), an open, modular, and accessible corpus of 527 million+ English-language Reddit posts selected for relevance to six key social group distinctions based on race, sexuality, age, ability, body weight, and skin tone, covering the 17-year period from 2007 to 2023. A multi-step, human-audited filtering pipeline was used to keep irrelevant content in the curated dataset below 10%, both overall and for each social group distinction. Each post was then algorithmically annotated with the user's estimated home region, along with a suite of validated off-the-shelf and custom semantic labels including moralization, sentiment, emotion, and linguistic generalization. We confirm the validity of the resulting corpus through convergent evidence linking ISAAC to macro-level societal trends, such as online search behavior, temporal spikes during major societal events (both nationally and regionally), and long-term shifts in public attitudes. By offering a unified, public infrastructure, ISAAC eliminates research fragmentation and enables seamless replication while supporting diverse empirical workflows at scale. Specifically, ISAAC allows investigators to perform cross-category comparisons, conduct high-precision tracking of long-term temporal shifts in social group discourse, and map spatial variation onto localized public opinion and policy outcomes. ISAAC's fully public, modular pipeline facilitates easy extension of the corpus to new platforms, languages, and social categories. To accommodate various research needs, ISAAC is accessible both without coding through a point-and-click website and labeler web-apps, and programmatically via an SQL playground, a Python package, and HuggingFace.

</details>


### [41] [What Changes When Fact-Verification Scores Improve? Evidence and Answer Accounting Across Trained Verifiers and LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27064)
*Han Chen,Yingrui Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种 2×2 分数分解方法，将联合事实核查分数的提升拆解为"证据替换"和"答案替换"两个分量，并通过两项研究验证：研究1使用四个DCUF训练的DeBERTa检验点在FEVEROUS上进行证据替换，发现9.61分的总增益中7.92-9.08分来自固定答案下的证据替换；研究2使用Qwen3-8B和Llama-3.1-8B在三个基准上测试，发现FEVEROUS上的证据增益对上下文预算敏感（256→2048 token增加3.10-3.84分），但跨数据集的复制标准未达成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合事实核查分数将答案正确性与证据完整性捆绑评分。当分数提升时，研究者无法区分改善来自答案质量、证据质量还是两者的组合变化。这种归因缺失阻碍了对模型和系统改进效果的理解，尤其是在评估源证据替换（如从DCUF到UnifEE）的实际收益时。

Method: 核心方法是构建2×2分数表（固定答案×固定证据），通过两次额外的评分通道产生非对角线元素，从而将端点增益G分解为E₀（先换证据再换答案）和E₁（先换答案再换证据）两个证据分量，以及A₀和A₁两个答案分量。定义交互项I = E₁ - E₀。两个证据分量的均值等于Shapley分配。研究1使用四个固定权重的DeBERTa-large-MNLI检验点（同一基模型微调）在FEVEROUS 7,890条开发集声明上比较DCUF与UnifEE证据。研究2使用Qwen3-8B和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct（固定权重）在FEVER、FEVEROUS和SciFact上，采用BM25与BGE排序、两种答案格式（代码vs标签）、两种上下文预算（256/2048 token），共生成470,400个回答。决策规则预设跨数据集复制标准。使用10,000次配对声明自助法（研究1）和50,000次声明族×解码种子自助法（研究2）。额外提出协方差诊断（公式4）以判断聚合比率何时能近似证据增益。

Result: 研究1：FEVEROUS严格分数从32.41提升至42.02，总增益9.61分[8.77, 10.43]；答案准确率仅提升1.96分。固定DCUF答案仅替换评分证据保留7.92分；固定UnifEE答案保留9.08分。交互项I = 1.15[0.86, 1.45]。边际证据资格率乘以答案准确率可近似E₀（8.07预测 vs 7.92观测，协方差贡献-0.14），但对E₁近似较差（8.30预测 vs 9.08观测，协方差贡献0.78）。研究2：FEVEROUS上下文效应（256→2048 token）使E₀增加：Qwen 3.84分[1.83, 5.91]、Llama 3.10分[1.20, 5.01]，全部5个种子方向一致。但未满足预设的跨数据集复制标准（SciFact和FEVER效应较小或区间包含零）。SciFact的FEVEROUS大小效应仅对Llama部分可见。在FEVER/Qwen上，边际乘积仅预测0.110分（观测1.165分），协方差变化贡献1.055分；FEVEROUS/Llama上乘积预测3.068分（观测3.100分），吻合较好。证据保留比例差异显著：FEVEROUS在256 token时仅约5%被保留，2048 token时约44%。

Conclusion: 该2×2分解能精确量化联合事实核查分数中证据替换与答案替换各自的贡献。研究1证明在DCUF训练的DeBERTa中（不预测NEI类），证据替换贡献了9.61分总增益的绝大部分（7.92-9.08分）。研究2证明LLM的证据增益对接口选择（上下文预算）敏感，但跨数据集复制失败。聚合的准确率和证据覆盖率有时可近似证据增益（如研究1的E₀），但有时严重低估（如FEVER/Qwen遗漏1.055分），关键差异在于哪些声明同时具备正确答案与合格证据。该方法为报告事实核查系统改进提供了可复现的诊断框架，超越了端点指标的局限。

Abstract: A joint fact-verification score assesses answers and submitted evidence together. When the score improves, how much of the gain remains if the answers are held fixed? On FEVEROUS, strict score is the percentage of claims with a correct answer and a complete annotated evidence group in the submitted evidence. Across four trained DeBERTa checkpoints and 7,890 claims, replacing DCUF evidence with UnifEE evidence raises strict score by 9.61 percentage points, compared with 1.96 percentage points in answer accuracy. The paired 95% interval for the strict-score gain is [8.77, 10.43], conditional on these checkpoints. Replacing only the evidence passed to the scorer accounts for 7.92 or 9.08 percentage points when we retain the answers generated from DCUF or UnifEE evidence, respectively. To examine how this evidence gain depends on evaluation choices, we generate 470,400 responses from two 8B LLMs on FEVER, FEVEROUS, and SciFact under two answer formats and two context budgets. Increasing context from 256 to 2,048 tokens raises the fixed-answer evidence gain on FEVEROUS by 3.84 and 3.10 percentage points for Qwen and Llama, respectively. The effects fall short of the prespecified cross-dataset criterion, while some intervals extend beyond the two-point small-effect bound. Post-hoc analyses quantify changes in answers and submitted evidence, and show when aggregate accuracy and evidence-coverage rates miss the claim-level pattern. The four answer-evidence score combinations reveal changes that endpoint and aggregate metrics leave unresolved.

</details>


### [42] [NADI 2026: The Second Multidialectal Arabic Speech Processing Shared Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27086)
*Peter Sullivan,Bashar Talafha,Ahmed Ashraf,Fethi Bougares,Haroun Elleuch,Chiyu Zhang,AbdelRahim Elmadany,Youssef Mohamed,Salima Mdhaffar,Yannick Estève,Mohamed Elhoseiny,Hamzah Luqman,Nizar Habash,Muhammad Abdul-Mageed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: NADI 2026是第七届NADI共享任务系列，专注于多方言阿拉伯语语音处理，包含ASR、SDID、TTS、SLT、SLU五大任务共八个子任务，强调真实场景评估（低带宽、混合方言、码切换、域外、零样本），吸引21支团队参与。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语音系统在标准阿拉伯语(MSA)基准上表现良好，但对话语方言识别、转录质量仍存在显著差距。原因包括：方言覆盖不足（ISO 639-3列出的28种阿拉伯语变体仍未充分代表城际与城乡差异）、方言无标准正字法、形态复杂、以及与英语/法语/MSA的频繁码切换。此外，最大型转录语料库（MGB2、QASR）以MSA为主，专用方言语料库规模有限。NADI系列旨在集中社区力量弥合这一差距，2025年转向语音处理，2026年进一步深化。

Method: NADI 2026采用"真实优先(realism优先)"和"范围(scope)"双轴设计：(1)真实轴——在分布偏移条件下重新评估成熟任务，包括低带宽降采样（4kHz降采样再上采样至16kHz）、混合方言语音（6国15+亚方言，参赛者无法获得国家标签）、突尼斯语码切换（法语/英语频繁切换）、域外SDID（6国18城市方言，标签集对参赛者保密）、零样本域迁移SLU（4个未见应用域）；(2)范围轴——首次引入TTS（10国方言）、SLT（8种方言到英语）、SLU（包含意图识别和槽位填充两个子任务），覆盖感知（ASR、SDID）、理解（SLU）、跨语言迁移（SLT）、生成（TTS）的完整口语语言栈。基线系统：ASR和SLT使用whisper-large-v3；SDID使用在ADI20数据集上微调的ECAPA-TDNN；TTS使用XTTS-v2（单一参考说话人，无方言条件）；SLU使用whisper-small。评估在Codabench平台上进行，分为开发期（6月16日至7月20日）和测试期（7月20日至28日）。

Result: 主要结果：(1) 域外泛化是关键瓶颈——SDID基线域内准确率81.19%降至域外28.70%，最佳参赛系统仅恢复至56.15%；(2) 码切换ASR前三名差距极小（0.12% WER），完全微调的阿拉伯编码器-解码器、3.6%参数的LoRA适配和10系统集成方法成绩接近；(3) 阿拉伯专用基础模型表现突出——出现在5/6的顶级SDID系统中、一项ASR子任务的冠军和另两项ASR子任务的亚军，相比NADI 2025（通用多语言模型主导）发生明显转变；(4) 系统集成在几乎所有任务中都有效（41个有效测试提交中10个为集成系统）。各任务最佳结果：鲁棒ASR——Wifaq（43.24% WER，w2v-BERT 2.0 CTC + KenLM + ROVER集成4模型）；混合方言ASR——Salesteq（44.22% WER）；码切换ASR——Abjad AI（14.41% WER，Cohere模型完全微调五检查点参数平均）；SDID——Thakaa（56.15%准确率，多模态方法Ara-BEST-RQ为主+30%权重文本分支）；TTS——仅KAND CA提交（UTMOS 2.72，NISQA-MOS 3.57，使用lahgtna-omnivoice-v2零样本声音克隆，但基线XTTS-v2在所有指标上更优，因XTTS-v2对所有方言渲染一致暴露了自动指标的局限）；意图识别——Lynx（加权F1 76.86%，ASR+TF-IDF流水线+MARBERTv2未知类别过滤）；槽位填充——Aslema（CoER 59.53%，Qwen3-Omni-30B LoRA微调+合成数据）；SLT——Sense（BLEU 14.19，约旦方言最佳27.46，阿联酋最差0.39）。

Conclusion: NADI 2026为多方言阿拉伯语语音处理提供了更广泛和更具挑战性的基准。研究发现：(1) Cohere Transcribe Arabic模型在多个任务中频繁出现并表现优异，成为新的基础骨干；(2) 多模态方法（声学+文本分支）在SDID中占据主导地位（NADI 2025由端到端声学模型主导的进化）；(3) 集成方法普遍有效但计算成本高，未来应考察低计算场景下的模型效率；(4) 多方言模型的增长可能导致"一刀切"方法，混合方言ASR中无团队将SDID集成到管道中，未来需直接比较SDID集成管道与方言特定ASR与通用方法的优劣。局限性包括：方言覆盖仍有限（最广TTS仅10种方言，相比ISO 28种）；TTS评估缺乏人类评估且仅一支团队参与，需要重新设计；正字法变异性（哈姆扎归一化降低前五名约0.29点WER，绝对分数可靠性较低）；SDID子任务可能过难，建议提供方言覆盖信息并重构为多个域评估而非纯零样本评估。未来工作将考察低计算场景下的跨任务性能、明确调查SDID系统作为ASR管道组件的益处，并可能扩展到新方言。

Abstract: NADI 2026 is the seventh edition of the Nuanced Arabic Dialect Identification (NADI) shared task series and the second dedicated to multidialectal Arabic speech processing. This edition comprises five tasks and eight subtasks spanning Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR), Spoken Dialect Identification (SDID), Text-to-Speech (TTS), Spoken Language Translation (SLT), and Spoken Language Understanding (SLU). NADI 2026 emphasizes realistic evaluation through low-bandwidth, mixed-dialect, code-switched, out-of-domain, and zero-shot settings, while introducing TTS, SLT, and SLU to the series for the first time. The shared task attracted 21 participating teams from at least 13 countries, with 48 test-phase submissions and 14 submitted system-description papers. Results show that out-of-domain generalization remains a major bottleneck and highlight the effectiveness of recent Arabic-specialized speech models, multimodal dialect identification approaches, and ensemble methods. Overall, NADI 2026 provides a broader and more challenging benchmark for robust Arabic dialect speech processing.

</details>
