An ideal model architecture should lower all above computation costs to facilitate scaling to a larger model, while ensure the larger model indeed outperforms smaller baselines. We introduce KV-Invariant Transformer Expansion (KITE), a scaling paradigm that achieves this goal. It trains the model from a smaller size to a larger size (i.e., saving training costs via upcycling), while places newly added parameters in regions that do not affect attention KV. Consequently, during inference, prefilling KV only relies on the smaller part of the model, so the inference costs are saved. As a concrete instantiation, we present Step Scale Transformer (SST), a two-tower decoder in which one tower produces KV and the other reads them. At comparable cumulative training compute, SST, a 67B MoE model with 2.15B active body parameters per decode token, achieves lower training loss than 47B and 63B MoE Transformers with 1.48B and 2.02B active body parameters, respectively, while reducing estimated inference cost by 6.7% and 31.6%.

</details>


### [89] [Quantization-Robust Unlearning through the Lens of Retain-Forget Loss Landscapes Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27355)
*Jialu Wang,Jianing Deng,Shuqing Luo,Yuanzhe Li,Dongwei Wang,Jingtong Hu,Huanrui Yang,Song Wang,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种量化鲁棒的LLM遗忘框架，通过曲率引导的正则化和关键层选择性更新，解决遗忘后的LLM在量化部署时遗忘效果崩溃的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM部署中通常采用量化压缩，但现有遗忘方法在量化后遗忘效果严重退化（"灾难性遗忘失败"），遗忘行为比模型效用退化更剧烈。需要使遗忘对量化具有鲁棒性。

Method: 框架包含两个组件：(1) 灵敏度引导的噪声正则化——利用Fisher信息定义参数灵敏度比ρ_i = F_f/F_r，识别敏感权重并在反向传播中施加模拟量化舍入的扰动，使收敛趋向平坦极小值；(2) 遗忘关键层优化——基于层梯度范数比S_ℓ = G_ℓ,f/G_ℓ,r仅更新对遗忘贡献大的层，冻结其余层以保护保留知识。理论基础来自Taylor展开分析和定理1关于保留/遗忘损失沿连接极小值连线的曲率关系。

Result: 在MUSE（News和Books）和TOFU基准上，针对GA/NPO及其与GDR/KLR组合的多种遗忘方法，4-bit量化下遗忘鲁棒性显著优于SURE基线，retain-forget差距改善最高达8.0%（MUSE-News），MUSE-Books上遗忘效果提升约15%、效用提升约5%；仅扰动约0.1%参数即可获得显著鲁棒性提升；在AWQ、GPTQ等多种量化方法上均泛化有效。

Conclusion: 通过分析遗忘失败的根因为遗忘损失景观的尖锐曲率，提出了结合二阶曲率正则化与一阶关键层选择的量化感知遗忘框架，有效平衡了量化后遗忘鲁棒性与模型效用，为实际部署提供了更可靠的隐私保护方案。

Abstract: Unlearning ensures LLM compliance by removing the influence of private or copyrighted training data. However, since LLM models typically undergo post-training compression, like quantization, in practical deployment, it has been observed that the unlearning effect can be substantially weakened, with the forgetting behavior degrading more severely than that of model utility. This paper proposes a quantization-robust unlearning framework that makes forgetting robust to quantization while maintaining overall model utility. We analyze this gap through the lens of loss landscape. Specifically, our analysis reveals a curvature-based criteria that pinpoints sensitive weights in the unlearned model that leads to both non-robust forgetting and reduced utility. We therefore propose sensitivity-guided noisy regularization, which is applied on the sensitive parameters to steer the model convergence towards a smoother minima of uniformly low forget and retain losses. Balancing unlearning and utility, we further propose forget-critical optimization, which updates only forget-critical layers, preserving most of the network to retain useful knowledge. Extensive experiments on the MUSE and TOFU benchmarks across multiple LLM unlearning algorithms show that our approach achieves substantially more quantization-resilient forgetting while maintaining utility.

</details>


### [90] [Forecast Workflow Bench: Evaluating Language-Model Decisions with Budgeted Forecast Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27385)
*Shunya Nagashima*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FWBench是一个评估语言模型在成本约束下进行时间序列预测选择与决策能力的基准测试。它基于1,251个电力和共享单车案例，通过固定预测工具和模拟容量合同，评估智能体在最小化损失-成本目标时的表现。实验涵盖两个托管模型和八个本地配置，结果显示GPT-6 Astra以选择性购买短期预测（仅2.5%预算）的策略，在三种损失-成本权重评分下均优于固定策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的预测基准主要评估预测准确性或预测器选择，但未衡量预测是否足以改善容量决策并证明其成本的合理性。本文旨在填补这一空白，评估语言模型代理在成本约束下如何选择和使用时间序列预测来做出运营决策。

Method: FWBench采用固定预测工具（Chronos-2、Bolt Tiny等）和模拟容量合同，让代理选择模型、历史长度和预测时间范围，然后提交容量以最小化损失-成本目标S。实验在1,251个案例上进行（1,091个电力案例+160个共享单车案例），评估了两个托管模型（GPT-6 Astra、nano）和八个本地配置（含小语言模型），并对本地模型进行了有/无TSFM访问的配对消融研究。所有代理使用相同的工具、容量约束和预测预算（B=113,726 credits，12次工具调用限制）。

Result: GPT-6 Astra仅购买Bolt Tiny的短期预测，使用2.6%的预测积分，在S评分上排名第一，在所有八个cohort-lead子集中均居首位，在损失-成本权衡上显著优于最佳免费策略和三个选择器。TSFM访问消融显示没有本地配置从TSFM访问中获得显著的评分改善（所有配对95% CI均包含零）；在包含成本目标时，Astra的积分使用减少98.5%（决策损失仅增加1.6%）。所有本地配置得分均低于hour-of-day empirical基线；Gemma 4 E4B完成全部案例但得分是其两倍多；Qwen3.6无thinking时在1,245个案例中耗尽12次调用限制。权重敏感性测试中，Astra在0.25/0.75和0.75/0.25权重设置下均保持第一。

Conclusion: FWBench支持在通用工具和成本约束下评估LLM和SLM的预测选择和决策质量改进。Astra的选择性购买策略以更低的预测成本实现了与固定Chronos-2相似的损失，但完全有效的SLM计划仍落后于免费策略。更好的预测选择可缩小与事后目录oracle的差距；更强的TSFM可进一步改善决策质量。未来工作包括评估任务特定决策模型、扩展时间和领域覆盖、比较完整代理系统、以及在边缘设备上测量端到端延迟、内存和能耗。

Abstract: Time-series foundation models (TSFMs) provide forecasts for operational decisions, but accuracy alone does not determine their value. Evaluating agents that use these models requires measuring decision quality and forecast cost. FWBench evaluates this capability on 1,251 electricity and cycle-hire cases using fixed forecast tools and simulated capacity contracts. Agents select models, histories and horizons, then submit capacities to minimize a stated loss-cost objective. We evaluated two hosted and eight local configurations, including small language models, and tested local models with and without TSFMs. GPT-6 Astra bought inexpensive short-horizon forecasts selectively, using 2.5% of the budget, and outperformed fixed policies when the saved decisions were scored with three loss-cost weightings. FWBench enables reproducible evaluation of how language models select and use time-series forecasts to make decisions under cost constraints.

</details>


### [91] [Quantum Reinforcement Learning for Cost and Delay Tradeoffs in Quantum Cloud Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27446)
*An N. H. Phan,Dang Van Huynh,Muhammad Usman,Hoa T. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QRLQ框架，将参数化量子电路(PQC)与Dueling Double DQN(D3QN)相结合，用于同质化定价的量子云环境中以成本-延迟感知的方式进行任务编排。该方法利用PQC作为紧凑的函数逼近器，在保持调度性能的同时显著减少可训练参数数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子即服务(QaaS)模型下，统一的时间计费模式应用于异构的量子资源会使任务编排复杂化，特别是在执行成本和系统性能之间的权衡方面。现有启发式方法依赖预定义规则，而经典深度强化学习模型在此场景下需要大量可训练参数。同时，目前尚无先前工作将量子强化学习(QRL)应用于同质化定价的量子云资源编排。参数化量子电路作为紧凑函数逼近器具有潜在优势，尤其是在当前NISQ硬件对深度量子电路存在限制的背景下。

Method: 1. **问题建模**：将成本-延迟感知的量子任务编排建模为在线序贯决策问题，并形式化为马尔可夫决策过程(MDP)。状态空间包含任务特征和QNode硬件配置，奖励函数通过权衡参数β联合考虑执行时间和等待时间。
2. **QRLQ架构**：混合量子-经典架构，将Double DQN和Dueling DQN与PQC相结合。PQC通过Rx、Ry、Rz旋转门进行数据编码，应用变分层（Ry、Rz旋转），通过环形拓扑的CZ门实现量子纠缠。采用Pauli-Z期望值测量进行量子-经典特征读出。
3. **训练策略**：采用经验回放、Huber损失函数、梯度裁剪(‖∇‖≤1)、软目标更新(τ=0.001)等技术稳定训练过程。学习率设为0.001，折扣因子γ=0.99。
5. **噪声鲁棒性分析**：通过高斯扰动模拟NISQ硬件噪声，评估参数扰动对调度性能的影响。

Result: QRLQ在21,000个未训练QTask的评估中：
1. 相比可用性(GEA)和轮询(CRR)启发式方法，成本降低5-11%
2. 相比最强和最弱启发式基线，延迟分别降低17%和82%
3. 执行保真度保持在保真度贪婪策略(GLE)的2%以内(0.612 vs 0.624)
4. 相比经典D3QN基线(758个参数)，仅使用214个可训练参数(减少72%)
5. 平均成本$3.70，平均延迟29.99秒，优于所有评估的启发式和DRL基线
6. 在参数扰动测试中，QRLQ的等待时间变化较小，比QDQN表现出更好的噪声鲁棒性

Conclusion: QRLQ证明了量子强化学习在量子云资源编排中的可行性，特别是在同质化定价模型下实现成本-延迟感知决策。通过将PQC集成到D3QN架构中，该框架以显著更少的可训练参数实现了与经典方法相当的调度性能。该研究为未来容错量子计算环境中的量子-经典混合调度模型提供了基础。未来研究方向包括：扩展QRLQ以支持量子多编程、探索更大动作空间下的可扩展性、集成电路切割和分布式量子执行，以及在物理量子硬件上验证该方法。

Abstract: Quantum cloud computing, delivered through the quantum-as-a-service (QaaS) model, provides access to quantum computing resources. However, applying uniform time-based pricing across fundamentally heterogeneous quantum resources significantly complicates task orchestration, particularly when addressing the tradeoff between execution costs and system performance. While heuristic methods rely on predefined scheduling rules, classical deep reinforcement learning (DRL) models may require more trainable parameters in this setting. Motivated by the potential of parameterised quantum circuits (PQCs) as compact function approximators, we propose QRLQ, a cost-delay-aware quantum cloud scheduling framework integrating PQCs with a dueling double deep Q-network (D3QN) to dynamically account for both cost and delay. Our simulation results show that QRLQ achieves lower mean cost and delay than the heuristic baselines, achieving a 5-11% lower mean cost relative to availability-based and rotation-based heuristics and reducing mean delay by 17% and 82% relative to the strongest and weakest heuristic baselines, respectively, while retaining execution fidelity within 2% of a fidelity-greedy policy. Compared with the classical DRL baseline, QRLQ achieves comparable scheduling performance while using 72% fewer trainable parameters. This work explores the feasibility of using QRL for task orchestration in quantum cloud environments and demonstrates its potential for cost-delay-aware quantum resource management.

</details>


### [92] [PhyMo: A Physical-Field Modality for Multimodal AI4Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27554)
*Henan Sun,Haitao Hu,Jin Liu,Jianfeng Zhang,Lujia Pan,Nuo Chen,Jia Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"物理场模态"(physical-field modality)概念及PhyMo框架，将物理测量通过PDE算子组织而非简单文本/数值token，三阶段训练(PDE预训练—多模态对齐—预测)在5个数据集达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI4Physics多模态方法将物理量与方程视为通用数值或文本token，忽略了由PDE约束的时空交互与动力学结构，导致模型仅学到统计相关性而无法利用物理规律。

Method: 三阶段框架：(1)物理场编码器预训练——基于PDE算子token(时间/空间/混合导数、Laplacian)与物理量数值，用Transformer编码器+场重建+PDE残差监督ℒ_PDE；(2)多模态对齐——视觉与物理场表征经适配器投影至共享空间，用对比损失ℒ_con+回归损失ℒ_reg；(3)预测头训练——拼接融合特征，水平专属MLP头输出预测。理论证明融合、PDE约束的结构性优势。

Result: 在SKIPP'D、Folsom、NREL、MeteoNet、Boreas五个数据集上与10个基类对比，PhyMo在SKIPP'D(84.80)、NREL(79.31)、MeteoNet(98.36)四个上达到SOTA，Boreas上以96.84位列第二接近最优；消融证实双模态+早融合最优；超参敏感性分析表明模型稳健。

Conclusion: PhyMo将物理场确立为AI4Physics的独立模态，为可扩展地整合异构物理量与PDE结构化表示提供原则性框架，理论分析与多领域实验验证其有效性；未来工作将扩展至分类、缺失/异步观测、轻量化编码器与高效PDE算子计算。

Abstract: Multimodal learning is emerging as a powerful paradigm for AI for Physics (AI4Physics), where predicting physical systems requires the joint interpretation of heterogeneous observations, measurements, and domain knowledge. However, existing approaches typically represent physical quantities and governing equations as generic numerical or textual tokens, overlooking the physical constraints that determine their spatiotemporal interactions. To address this limitation, we introduce the \textbf{physical-field modality} and propose \textbf{PhyMo}, a physics-grounded multimodal framework that organizes heterogeneous measurements through PDE-associated operators. PhyMo follows a three-stage learning procedure: the physical-field encoder is first pretrained through field reconstruction under PDE residual supervision, its representations are subsequently aligned with visual embeddings in a shared latent space, and the fused multimodal representations are finally processed by corresponding downstream prediction heads. Experiments on five datasets spanning diverse physical environments show that PhyMo achieves state-of-the-art performance, compared to the strongest baseline on each dataset, demonstrating the superiority of PhyMo on multimodal representation learning in AI4Physics.

</details>


### [93] [TNLearn: An Open Source Python Package for Task-based Neurons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27564)
*Meng Wang,Tieyun Li,Juntong Fan,Hanyu Pei,Jing-Xiao Liao,Yaodong Yang,Jianwei Ma,Fenglei Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TNLearn是一个开源Python包，旨在自动构建"基于任务的神经元"（task-based neurons）并将其集成到神经网络中。与传统依赖单一通用神经元的设计不同，它主张针对特定任务定制神经元的聚合函数，通过向量化的符号回归方法从任务数据中提取归纳偏置，作为方法论文献使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前深度学习网络几乎统一使用基于内积和非线性激活的通用神经元，忽略了大脑中神经元形态与功能的多样性。作者提出"基于任务的神经元"范式，认为不存在适用于所有任务的通用神经元，应像大脑那样为不同任务定制神经元，以捕获任务相关的先验知识。现有研究缺乏系统化、易用的开源工具来落地这一理念。

Method: TNLearn围绕"神经元发现引擎→任务神经元构建→网络集成"的三段式流水线设计。核心是向量化的符号回归（VSR），强制所有输入变量共享同一运算结构，从而得到适合并行计算的齐次公式。具体实现了四种发现方法：1）GPSymRegressor——基于遗传编程的树形搜索（含自定义内积算子和SymPy简化）；2）LLMSymRegressor——利用大语言模型提出候选表达式，配合经验缓冲区（多岛屿、按分数聚类）和BFGS系数拟合；3）RLSymRegressor——使用REINFORCE策略网络从幂项与交互项库中采样项，再用Ridge回归拟合；4）PolyTensorRegressor——可微的基于CP张量分解的混合流（多项式流+交互流+周期流），通过ℓ₁稀疏门控实现结构剪枝。得到的公式被参数化为可学习模块，兼容PyTorch生态，并提供覆盖MLP、Conv2d、RNN、Transformer的可定制任务神经元层（TNConv2d等），遵循scikit-learn API。

Result: 在7个OpenML基准数据集（satimage、phoneme、adult、pol、fried、bike、sarcos）上的实验表明，任务神经元在相同网络架构下始终优于线性神经元（准确率或R²显著提升，例如bike数据集R²从0.6702提升至0.8838）。在多项式基准（pol）上甚至用5-1的小架构取得R²=0.9908。与Bu and Karpatne (2021)、Fan et al. (2018)、Goyal et al. (2020)、Xu et al. (2022)四种二次神经元相比，任务神经元在多数数据集上达到最优或具有竞争力。与随机生成的公式相比，基于发现公式的网络以更少参数获得更高性能，验证了符号回归引擎确实提取了任务相关归纳偏置。在MNIST上，TNConv2d相对标准Conv2d收敛更快、最终准确率略高（99.21% vs 99.05%）。TNLearn已开源并成为PyTorch生态系统项目。

Conclusion: TNLearn为任务神经元理念提供了首个系统化、用户友好的开源实现，通过模块化架构兼顾新手易用性与研究可扩展性，可同时作为建模工具和基准平台。实验验证了任务神经元在多种数据模态和任务类型上的有效性与优越性。未来工作包括：集成更多架构（如Mamba）、为GPSymRegressor引入GPU并行化（参考EvoGP）、持续社区维护以推动人工神经网络的研究与落地。

Abstract: The brain does not rely on a single type of neuron to perform all kinds of tasks; instead, it designs different neurons for different tasks. The concept of task-based neurons represents a paradigm shift compared to task-based architectures. It argues that solving a specific problem requires customized neurons, as task-based neurons capture useful prior knowledge from task-related data. To facilitate the use of task-based neurons in scientific research and industrial applications, we introduce TNLearn, an open-source Python package that provides automated construction of task-based neurons and networks, enabling smooth training of task-based networks. Comprehensive documentation, including technical exposition, API reference, and representative examples, is available online. TNLearn is open-sourced at this https URL and has become a PyTorch ecosystem project.

</details>


### [94] [DCRL: Decoupling and Coupling Reinforcement Learning via Policy-Reward Manifold Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27572)
*Henan Sun,Zehua Li,Haitao Hu,Qifan Zhang,Jianfeng Zhang,Nuo Chen,Jia Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DCRL框架，从几何视角将LLM推理建模为逻辑推导、评估和表示三个子流形的耦合，将现有基于规则和奖励模型的RL局限归因于策略-奖励流形失配，并通过三段论提示演化机制与策略-奖励参数重耦合机制实现协同进化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL奖励系统（基于规则和奖励模型）存在优化不稳定和奖励黑客问题，根本原因是策略模型的推理流形与奖励模型的评估流形之间存在失配——规则系统忽视评估子流形，奖励模型系统的评估子流形静态不变，无法适应策略流形的持续演化。

Method: DCRL包含两大核心组件：(1) 基于三段论逻辑的提示演化机制，将奖励rubric分解为公理提示（固定）、定理提示（域级更新）和推论提示（动态更新）三层结构；(2) 策略-奖励重耦合机制，每个epoch末将奖励模型参数同步为策略模型参数，实现二者协同进化。理论证明在提示平滑性假设下，策略与奖励推理流形间的对称KL散度有界（$G_t \leq 2L\delta$）。

Result: 在数学、代码、常识三大推理领域的10个基准上，基于Qwen3-4B训练的DCRL-4B在GSM8K(95.59)、AIME24(40.0)、AIME25(36.67)、CodeContest(36.84)、CRUX(79.12)、MMLU-Pro(71.20)、GPQA(77.27)等任务上超越Qwen3-32B，并接近Qwen3-235B的性能；消融实验表明完整DCRL优于静态奖励模型变体(DCRL-SRM)和规则基变体(DCRL-RB)；流形对齐分析显示DCRL具有更高的Linear CKA和更低的Procrustes距离。

Conclusion: DCRL通过显式建模并维护策略与奖励流形之间的结构对齐，提供了一种有效的RL后训练范式，使4B模型能超越32B基线并接近235B模型；局限性在于：(1) 尚未适配智能体RL场景；(2) 提示演化机制引入额外计算开销，且对提示设计和更新稳定性较敏感。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a key paradigm for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs). However, existing reward systems, such as rule-based and reward-model-based, often exhibit issues such as unstable optimization and reward hacking. In this work, we revisit the general reasoning of LLMs from a geometric perspective, conceptualizing it as a coupled manifold composed of three interdependent sub-manifolds: logical deduction, evaluation, and representation. Based on this perspective, response generation in RL can be interpreted as a decoupling process from the evaluation manifold, while reward estimation corresponds to a decoupling process from the logical deduction manifold. The limitations of rule-based and reward-model RL systems can be geometrically interpreted as the mismatch of policy-reward manifolds during RL process. To address the aforementioned misalignment, we propose Decoupling and Coupling Reinforcement Learning (DCRL) framework, which incorporates two key components: (1) a syllogistic logic-based prompt evolution mechanism that dynamically refines reward rubrics to enhance the expressiveness of the reward manifold; and (2) a policy-reward re-coupling mechanism that jointly updates the reward and policy models, ensuring consistent evaluation and mitigating manifold mismatch during training. Theoretical analysis and extensive experiments across multiple reasoning domains demonstrate that DCRL consistently outperforms both rule-based and reward-model baselines. Notably, a Qwen3-4B model trained under DCRL surpasses a Qwen3-32B baseline and approaches the performance of a Qwen3-235B model, highlighting superior effectiveness and generalization in RL.

</details>


### [95] [The Capability Manifold and ML Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27588)
*Syed Ali Raza Zaidi,Maryam Hafeez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了"能力流形"（capability manifold）框架，将下游多维能力（推理、规划、工具使用等）映射到预训练、后训练和测试时资源，通过有界缩放函数和解析雅可比矩阵量化资源-能力关系，统一了Kaplan、Chinchilla等现有缩放规律及测试时计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ML缩放定律仅用标量损失度量性能，但模型正以agentic方式部署，损失相似的模型在推理、检索、规划、适应等下游能力上差异显著；缺乏统一框架将多维能力与ML全生命周期中的耦合资源联系起来。

Method: 定义能力向量C(X)=[C₁,...,C_M]，将传统幂律缩放转化为有界函数φⱼ(Xⱼ)=1-(Xⱼ₀/Xⱼ)^αⱼ，引入中间变量Zᵢ包含线性资源贡献矩阵A和张量B建模资源间交叉耦合，最终通过sigmoid饱和函数σ(Z)映射到[Cᵢ,min, Cᵢ,max]区间；推导雅可比矩阵J_C = (∂C/∂Z)·(A+∂B/∂φ)·(∂φ/∂X)，分三部分分别对应饱和、能力映射和缩放规律。定义了7种操作化能力（知识、推理、规划、工具使用、记忆、适应、协调）。

Result: 以X=[N,D,T]映射到C=[C_K, C_R, C_M]为例，数值模拟显示：预训练分配决定基线推理速度∂C_R/∂s，而新兴数学能力速度∂C_math/∂s由测试时搜索指数T_mult控制；纯预训练路径（Path A）进入能力失配区，测试时搜索路径（Path B）在T>T_c=1.8附近触发相变以克服指数性能量势垒。Kaplan与Chinchilla策略在能力流形上产生不同轨迹。

Conclusion: 能力流形将异构缩放资源整合为多维能力空间中的轨迹而非单一损失指标，揭示了能力依赖的边际收益，为高效缩放、蒸馏、安全和能力权衡分析提供统一基础。

Abstract: Existing machine learning (ML) scaling laws relate predictive loss to compute, model parameters, and data. However, as models are increasingly deployed through agentic harnesses, loss alone is insufficient to characterize downstream performance: models with similar loss can exhibit different capabilities in reasoning, retrieval, planning, and adaptation. Yet, no unified framework connects such capabilities to the coupled resources available across the ML lifecycle. We bridge this gap by introducing a capability manifold, a multidimensional framework mapping downstream capabilities to pre-training, post-training, and test-time resources through bounded scaling functions. Analytical Jacobians quantify capability sensitivity to resource changes and interactions. As an initial application, we embed Kaplan- and Chinchilla-type scaling laws and test-time compute within the framework, demonstrating how existing scaling relationships can be unified as trajectories on a common capability manifold.

</details>


### [96] [Hidden not Deleted: How Networks Suppress Entangled Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27593)
*Akash Samanta,Manish Pratap Singh,Debasis Chaudhuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在Elhage等人提出的Toy Model of Superposition（玩具叠加模型）框架下，研究了概念擦除（concept erasure）方法在纠缠特征表示下的失败机制。作者证明：当两个特征被迫形成共享单一子空间的反极对（antipodal pair）时，最先进的线性擦除方法（如LEACE类方法）会同时摧毁两个特征，而不仅仅是目标特征。通过冻结编码器并使用梯度下降训练解码器和输出偏置进行抑制（作者称之为"excision"），网络会收敛到两种不同的吸引子解——"镜像（mirror）"解和"影子（shadow）"解——其分布取决于特征间的纠缠程度。通过单标量因果干预（标量修补），作者证明两种解都保留了被擦除特征的可测量表征痕迹，使其可通过单一标量修补恢复，无需进一步训练。这一发现为LLM中观察到的"抑制而非删除"失败模式提供了机制性、可因果验证的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期实证研究发现，大语言模型中的遗忘方法往往产生的是抑制（suppression）而非真正的删除（deletion）：被遗忘的行为可以通过少量额外的微调恢复。现有的实证证据缺乏对底层机制的因果性精确刻画。本文旨在通过一个完全可控的玩具模型设定，建立"隐藏而非删除"这一失败模式的机制性、因果验证的解释。

Method: 1. 在Toy Model of Superposition（TMS）框架下构建设定，使用编码器-解码器自编码器，控制两个特征A、B的纠缠度 ρ = cos(wA, wB)；2. 测试线性擦除（rank-1零空间投影，类似LEACE）：分析证明其会移除A表征能量的 ρ² 比例；3. 提出"切除（excision）"程序：冻结编码器，仅训练解码器和输出偏置，损失函数为重建损失加 λ·E[x̂_B²] 项；4. 在 ρ∈[-1,0] 的15个纠缠值、每点100个种子上扫描；5. 使用二维高斯混合模型（按偏置排序）将解分类为"镜像（mirror）"或"影子（shadow）"吸引子；6. 使用Wilson 95%置信区间报告吸引子比例；7. 因果验证：对影子解进行输出偏置的标量剂量响应修补（α∈[0,1]）；对镜像解进行绝对值修补 z_B ↦ |z_B|；8. 充分性和特异性检查：通过修补无关特征作为负对照；9. 鲁棒性测试：增加隐藏层（深度2）和替换ReLU为GELU；10. 自然涌现验证：扫描稀疏度、瓶颈维度和反相关强度，确认反极几何是自然涌现的；11. 变压器规模检查：使用2层2头的因果变压器进行小规模定性验证。

Result: 1. 线性擦除失败：在 ρ=-1 处，A的保留MSE从正交情况下的0.098升至0.411（4.2倍），且破坏程度随纠缠度单调增加；在瓶颈表征层面，线性擦除精确移除了A表征能量的 ρ² 比例。2. 梯度切除的分叉现象：存在两种稳定吸引子——镜像解（解码器余弦相似度中位数-0.86，接近完全反射）和影子解（解码器旋转较少，输出偏置大幅为负）。3. 相变：吸引子比例作为纠缠度的函数是锐利的——在完全反极纠缠时75%的运行落入镜像吸引子，在 ρ=0 时反转为87%落入影子吸引子，交叉点在 ρ≈-0.75。4. A的重建在两个吸引子中都基本保持在干扰底线附近（MSE约0.019-0.021），远优于线性擦除。5. 因果可恢复性：在影子解中，B的平均读出从0平滑恢复至0.38；镜像解中通过绝对值修补从0恢复至0.13；两者均通过单标量干预实现，无需任何额外训练。6. 充分性和特异性：负对照（修补无关特征，20次试验）在两个吸引子中都产生零效应。7. 鲁棒性：分叉在增加隐藏层（91.0%输出层主导抑制）和GELU下均持续存在。8. 自然涌现：在严格互斥条件下，56.2%的收敛种子达到反极几何。9. 变压器检查（15个种子）：k=2的混合模型被BIC选中，偏置恢复使B的预测概率从0.0031增加到0.0035（Wilcoxon p<0.001）。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了概念擦除中的一个基本失败模式：当特征以反极几何纠缠时，梯度下降发现的不是真正的删除，而是两种不同电路级别的抑制解（镜像和影子），其分布由特征纠缠度决定。更关键的是，两种解都保留了被擦除特征的可测量表征痕迹，可通过单标量因果修补恢复，无需进一步训练。本文将这些发现与近期LLM遗忘研究中的实证观察直接关联——遗忘方法往往产生抑制而非删除，使被遗忘的知识可以被恢复——为这一失败模式提供了机制性、可因果验证的解释。结论指出，仅通过行为是否消失来评估遗忘可能不够充分；未来的遗忘方法需要能够区分抑制和真正的删除。

Abstract: Concept erasure methods that operate via linear projection assume that features occupy separable subspaces. We show this assumption fails under dense superposition: when two features are forced into an antipodal pair sharing a single subspace, state-of-the-art linear erasure destroys both, not just the target. Networks trained with gradient descent instead solve this problem non-linearly, but not uniformly: they converge to one of two distinct circuit-level solutions depending on initialization, which we call mirror and shadow solutions. We map this bifurcation as a function of feature entanglement, show it reflects a stable attractor structure rather than an artifact of our setup, and use targeted causal interventions to demonstrate that both solutions leave a substantial, measurable trace of the erased feature's representation intact, recoverable through a single scalar patch rather than requiring any further training. This mirrors a failure mode recently observed empirically in LLM unlearning, where suppression rather than deletion allows forgotten knowledge to resurface; our results offer a mechanistic, causally-validated account of why that failure mode occurs.

</details>


### [97] [Learning Local Heterogeneity and Cross-Region Context for Large-Scale Traffic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27637)
*Qi Feng,Zidong Wang,Bo Li,Xiaoguang Gao,Jiayu Zhang,Chenfeng Wang,Kaifang Wan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LoReST框架，通过节点级关系感知局部聚合与区域级跨区域注意力机制，在LargeST基准四个大规模交通数据集上实现SOTA性能，平均MAE/RMSE/MAPE相对降幅分别为4.78%、3.60%、5.75%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模交通路网中，节点间空间依赖存在异质性（道路类型与行驶方向差异），而全节点对交互计算成本高昂。需同时兼顾局部异质性建模与长程上下文的高效捕获。

Method: LoReST采用双粒度空间建模：(1)关系感知局部聚合（RLA）——基于Haversine距离选取k近邻，根据道路身份与行驶方向将邻居分为三类（同向同行、同向逆行、不同道路），使用三类专属变换矩阵聚合；(2)跨区域空间交互（CRSI）——通过递归二分法将路网划分为R个均衡区域，经区域均值池化构建区域表示，再通过区域间自注意力交换长程信息并广播回节点。两者与时空身份嵌入结合，经M层空间编码器后由线性解码器输出预测。

Result: 在SD/GBA/GLA/CA四个数据集上LoReST全面优于13种基线方法，平均MAE降幅3.33%–6.74%，RMSE降幅1.99%–5.63%，MAPE降幅4.40%–6.84%。在CA数据集上参数量仅1.79M，训练/推理时间较PatchSTG降低约49.2%/44.4%。消融实验验证RLA与CRSI互补有效，去除任一模块均导致性能下降。超参数研究表明k=24、R=16、M=4为较优配置。

Conclusion: LoReST通过融合空间先验知识与多粒度空间建模，在大规模交通预测中实现了精度与效率的良好平衡。未来工作将探索将空间先验融入预训练时序基础模型，以实现未见路网的零样本预测。

Abstract: Traffic flow forecasting is essential to intelligent transportation systems. Large-scale traffic forecasting requires jointly modeling local spatial dependencies and cross-region this http URL dependencies between geographically neighboring nodes are heterogeneous due to differences in road identity and travel direction, while acquiring global information through allpairs node interactions incurs substantial computational costs. Therefore, capturing local heterogeneity while efficiently acquiring long-range context remains an important challenge in largescale traffic forecasting. To address these challenges, we propose LoReST, a Local-Region Spatial Temporal network that models spatial dependencies at two complementary granularities: node neighborhoods and road network regions. Specifically, relation-aware local aggregation captures heterogeneous dependencies within geographic neighborhoods through road and direction specific feature transformations. Cross-region interaction constructs region representations through mean pooling, exchanges long range context via inter-region attention, and broadcasts it back to nodes. By integrating local information aggregation with crossregion interaction, LoReST is able to effectively achieve spatial dependency learning in large-scale road networks. Experiments on four datasets of the LargeST benchmark show average relative reductions of 4.78%, 3.60%, and 5.75% in MAE, RMSE, and MAPE, respectively.

</details>


### [98] [FLEET: From Logits Entropy to Enhanced Trajectories in Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27657)
*Oleksii Streltsov,Oleksandra Vitko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FLEET是一种用于LLM文本生成的测试时搜索框架，通过VectorDSU数据结构和熵-变熵触发机制，将无记忆的温度采样转化为有状态、确定性的轨迹探索，以解决重复采样的样本效率低下问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准温度采样是无记忆的，无法利用历史生成及其评估反馈，导致随着采样数量增加，语义上重复的答案比例增大，出现收益递减现象。此外，标量式的温度调节无法区分需要探索的分支节点与稳定的解码步骤，造成样本效率低下和缺乏选择性探索。

Method: 方法包含三个核心组件：(1) 基于Logit Lens从中间层提取隐藏状态，利用条件熵H和变熵V联合阈值检测高不确定性的分支节点；(2) VectorDSU数据结构，通过余弦相似度将连续隐藏状态映射到离散状态聚类，存储历史轨迹元数据(action, next state, reward)；(3) 基于pUCT的搜索策略，对经验上不理想的token施加logit惩罚λ，引导概率质量转向有潜力的分支。超参数通过单次校准流程自动确定，无需网格搜索。

Result: 在LiveCodeBench（222题）和GSM8K上使用Llama 3.2-3B进行评估。真实奖励下：LiveCodeBench Pass@32从59.9%提升至66.2%（+6.31个百分点，多解14题）；GSM8K从97.27%提升至97.80%（多解7题）。ORM引导下：LiveCodeBench从19.36%提升至25.22%（+5.86个百分点），但GSM8K略降（85.44%→83.62%）。在相同精度下实现约3倍加速。

Conclusion: FLEET提供了一种实用的、结构化的补全空间搜索方法，增强了LLM的测试时计算最优扩展能力。它通过定向探索而非盲目随机性提高了样本效率，但在代理奖励模型选择场景下存在排序瓶颈，且当前需要直接修改模型架构以访问内部激活，不适用于高度依赖反思过程的推理模型。

Abstract: Solutions based on large language models (LLMs) often rely on temperature sampling to improve accuracy and stability by aggregating multiple samples from the completion distribution. However, this memoryless approach is inherently suboptimal: because it lacks awareness of prior generations and their evaluations, it produces an increasing proportion of semantically duplicate answers as more samples are drawn, leading to diminishing returns. To address this limitation, we introduce FLEET, a novel method that integrates a memory mechanism into the generation process. FLEET represents each generation as a sparse trajectory through states whose entropy exceeds a predefined threshold and uses these trajectories to infer per-token utility scores that adjust the logits. Benchmark evaluations demonstrate that FLEET achieves the same accuracy as the repeated sampling baseline, with a 3x speedup, and substantially improves accuracy on complex coding tasks (LiveCodeBench Pass@32 increases from 59.9% to 66.2%) under the same budget. Furthermore, in the greedy-decoding configuration evaluated here, the approach is deterministic and uses a single calibration pass to derive its principal hyperparameters, requiring only minimal modifications to existing LLM pipelines.

</details>


### [99] [Backdoors Leave Structural Traces: FedMAST for Backdoor Detection and Containment in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27760)
*Srinivasan Subramanian,Kazi Aminul Islam,Md. Abdullah Al Hafiz Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedMAST是一种面向联邦学习的服务器端后门防御方法，通过整合结构、谱与历史多轴证据实现对隐身后门攻击的检测与抑制，并在六种隐身攻击及一种自适应攻击上取得显著优于现有基线的防御效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的联邦学习后门防御方法通常仅依赖单一证据源（如距离、方向、聚类或频域），而隐身受限攻击可以针对这些孤立信号进行显式规避，因此需要一种融合多维结构痕迹的防御机制。

Method: FedMAST采用多轴证据提取：1) 提取22维结构特征（涵盖幅度、方向对齐、层级能量、谱结构与区域一致性）；2) 设计挤压配对（Squeeze-Pair）一致性评分以揭示耦合特征间的联合畸变；3) 引入带符号的时间谱漂移（Spectral-Drift）追踪，对客户端每次出现的谱集中度与谱熵进行EMA累积；4) 通过2-of-3硬共识、谱轴独立否决、锚点反演与聚类二分等机制实现轮级筛选与可疑轮次的隔离（Coordinate Median等鲁棒聚合）。同时构建了针对FedMAST的自适应攻击CovertLayers作为压力测试。

Result: 在CIFAR-10上的六种隐身攻击（Constrain-and-Scale, Neurotoxin, BC-Layers, LGA, DBA, 3DFed）中，FedMAST在所有九组攻防对比中ASR均低于基线，200轮平均ASR为1.51%，主任务准确率94.84%。针对自适应CovertLayers攻击，FedAvg/MultiKrum/AlignIns/FLAME的ASR分别为100.00%/99.67%/99.53%/32.84%，FedMAST将ASR降至1.53%，主任务准确率92.26%。消融实验表明带符号时间谱漂移对LGA检测至关重要（移除后TPR降至29.63%）。在EMNIST与FEMNIST上也验证了跨数据集泛化能力，代价是9.1%端到端运行时开销与较强异质性下较高的FPR（部分设置可达23%）。

Conclusion: 多轴结构追踪能有效捕捉隐身受限攻击难以同时抑制的互补痕迹；签名化的时间谱漂移、挤压配对一致性与历史锚点共同构成一种难以绕过的检测机制。但存在权衡：在强非IID场景下误拒率上升，且依赖稳定的伪匿名标识符；未来需要更大模型、更强异质性与更长周期自适应攻击下的进一步验证。

Abstract: Federated learning enables distributed training of a shared model without requiring clients to share their raw data. However, its reliance on the integrity of the client-submitted updates exposes the global model to stealthy backdoor poisoning. Although existing defenses often inspect isolated evidence sources, stealth-constrained attacks can adapt to these signals. In this paper, we show that such attacks can suppress isolated anomaly signals, but their poisoned updates still leave residual structural traces. We propose FedMAST, a Federated Multi-Axis Structural Tracing defense for backdoor detection in federated learning. FedMAST scores client updates using complementary structural, spectral, and historical evidence and then applies tiered filtering and round-level containment to limit adversarial influence. To capture traces that isolated signals may miss, FedMAST uses squeeze-pair coherence scoring to expose coupled feature distortions and signed spectral-drift tracking to reveal persistent directional changes over time. Across six federated backdoor attacks, namely Constrain-and-Scale, Neurotoxin, BC-Layers, LGA, DBA, and 3DFed, FedMAST achieves lower ASR than baseline defenses in all nine evaluated attack--defense comparisons. Across the complete 200-round runs, it attains an average ASR of 1.51% while maintaining 94.84% average main-task accuracy. Under the method-aware CovertLayers attack, FedAvg, MultiKrum, AlignIns, and FLAME yield full-run ASRs of 100.00%, 99.67%, 99.53%, and 32.84%, respectively. FedMAST achieves the lowest ASR among all evaluated methods, reducing it to 1.53% while maintaining 92.26% main-task accuracy.

</details>


### [100] [What Changed? Drift Detection with Real, Virtual, and Incomparable Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27865)
*Kentaro Oda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于条件Jensen-Shannon散度的双轴漂移检测监控器，能够同时区分真实漂移（机制变化）、虚拟漂移（仅协变量变化）和不可比漂移（支持几乎不重叠）。该方法通过单一池化判别器对同时估计协变量统计量Ix和功能统计量DCJS，在多种基准测试中实现了零误报、零漏报和100%正确类型识别，并通过行动价值实验验证了类型诊断的实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有漂移检测方法存在结构性缺陷：错误流检测器（ADWIN、DDM、EDDM、Page-Hinkley）将所有警报归因于真实漂移，在纯协变量流上误报；输入空间检测器（D3风格域分类器）只能报告虚拟漂移而遗漏所有纯概念变化。漂移检测通常被视为二元警报问题，但自适应系统需要知道"什么变了"才能采取正确行动（重训练 vs. 重加权）。

Method: 维护参考窗口R和滑动近期窗口St，在每个块边界通过双判别器交叉熵差异估计(Îx, D̂CJS)。其中Ix = I(Z;X)测量协变量轴，DCJS = I(Z;Y|X)是功能轴，在虚拟漂移下群体值恰好为零。监控器有三个输出状态：真实（功能统计量超过阈值）、虚拟（仅协变量统计量超过）、不可比（协变量统计量超过可比性上限0.9ln2）。连续监控采用投注e-process保证任意时刻有效性。

Result: 在SEA/STAGGER/协变量/零流基准测试（10个种子）中，双轴监控器是唯一实现零误报、零漏报和100%正确类型识别的方法（二项式95%上限约9%）。在INSECTS基准测试中，成功将每个真实漂移分解为协变量和功能组件，并恢复温度循环的递归结构。行动价值实验显示类型化监控在每个制度下都是Pareto最优的，在真实漂移上匹配始终重训练策略，在虚拟漂移事件上节省2000个标签。

Conclusion: 漂移检测应从二元警报问题转向类型诊断问题。双轴监控器通过单一统计处理同时估计协变量和功能分量，提供了精确的类型识别能力，在零误报保证下实现了正确的干预决策，证明了诊断在实践中的重要价值。

Abstract: Sharing a deep encoder does not, by itself, fix the central confound of task-comparison scores. We show that cross-evaluated heads on a frozen shared representation inherit the extrapolation confound of shallow exchange scores: pure input rotations with fixed labels inflate a deep exchange score from about 0 to 0.80, while representation-novelty scores are blind in the complementary direction (flat under label permutations that change the task completely). Transplanting a conditional two-discriminator discrepancy into the embedding space resolves both blind spots: the functional axis stays within +-0.001 under rotations and tracks label-permutation drift mass monotonically. Built into a mixture-of-heads lifecycle, the two-axis gate attains better decision quality with fewer heads than exchange or novelty triggers at a matched training budget. On generalized category discovery, the same chunk-level functional axis separates semantic novelty from photometric shift with AUROC 0.98-0.99 where per-input OOD scores (MSP, Energy, Mahalanobis, KNN) sit near chance for that distinction. All findings replicate across frozen ImageNet-21k ViT-B/16 and self-supervised DINOv2 backbones on CIFAR-100, and extend to residual adapter pools with recurrence, where a null-calibrated novelty trigger never fires on mechanism changes while the two-axis gate handles them with full recurrence reuse. We state explicitly the common-factoring condition under which embedding-space conclusions transfer to the original mechanism.

</details>


### [101] [A Shared Encoder Is Not a Shared Task: Conditional Comparison for Deep Expert Pools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27866)
*Kentaro Oda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出深度持续学习中"共享编码器"并不能消除任务比较分数的混淆问题：基于头部交叉评估的深度交换分数（exchange score）会因纯输入旋转而无中生有地升高，而表示新颖性分数（representation novelty）对标签置换完全盲目。作者将条件两判别器差异（conditional Jensen-Shannon discrepancy, CJSD）移植到嵌入空间，分解出"功能轴"（机制是否改变）和"协变量轴"（输入分布是否改变），并在混合头部生命周期、广义类别发现（GCD）以及适配器池中验证其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在深度持续学习与专家池系统中，是否复用、生成或合并头部/适配器的决策通常依赖于两类分数：表示级交叉评估分数（深版 exchange score）和表示新颖性分数（如 Mahalanobis）。作者认为这两类分数都是单轴度量器，分别在相反方向上失明：前者对纯协变量偏移（如输入旋转）产生虚假高估，后者对纯机制偏移（如标签置换）完全无响应。共享编码器本身并不修复这种结构性的混淆，因为冻结编码器会放大分布外输入的协变量偏移。

Method: 1. 在冻结编码器上构建"深度交换分数"（cross-evaluated heads）作为基线对照。2. 将条件 Jensen-Shannon 差异移植到嵌入空间：训练两个小型 MLP 判别器，分别基于 [φ(x)] 和 [φ(x), onehot(y)]，得到功能轴 D_CJS 和协变量轴 I_x 的链式分解。3. 明确陈述"公共因子化"假设（common factoring），以保证嵌入空间的协变量-零结论可回推到原始机制。4. 将两轴门控规则嵌入混合头部生命周期（spawn/reuse 决策），并在 CIFAR-100 上用冻结的 ImageNet-21k ViT-B/16 和自监督 DINOv2 验证。5. 在残差适配器池中加入复发阶段，对比空校准的 SEMA 风格新颖性触发器。6. 在 CIFAR-10 GCD 分割上验证功能轴对语义新颖性 vs 光度偏移的区分能力。

Result: 1. 在 MNIST 上：纯 15°–90° 旋转下深度交换分数从 0.02 虚高至 0.80，而嵌入功能轴保持在 [-0.013, 0.012]，协变量轴从 0.25 升至 0.84；标签置换下 Mahalanobis 新颖性完全平坦，功能轴单调跟踪漂移质量。2. 混合头部生命周期：CJSD 两轴规则以 2.7 个头部取得最佳联合决策（false-spawn 0.044），深交换规则产生 11.3 个头部且 false-spawn 0.246。3. CIFAR-10 GCD：功能轴对新颖类别 vs 光度偏移的 AUROC 达 0.99，MSP/Energy/Mahalanobis 仅约 0.50–0.57。4. ViT-B/16 + CIFAR-100：旋转使交换分数升至 0.065–0.179，功能轴维持在 |D_CJS|≤0.001；标签置换下功能轴跟踪漂移质量（0.149/0.371/0.780）。5. 适配器池+复发：SEMA 风格新颖性触发器因标签置换不移动嵌入而永不触发（坍缩至单适配器，准确率 0.686），两轴门控以 3 个适配器达到 0.847 准确率并 100% 复用复发概念。6. DINOv2 上结果一致复现。

Conclusion: 共享编码器并不等同于共享任务：深度交换分数继承了浅层交换分数的核心混淆，冻结编码器本身是协变量偏移放大器。条件 CJSD 嵌入空间分解同时消除了两族单轴分数的盲点：功能轴在纯输入旋转下保持近零并在标签置换下单调跟踪漂移质量，协变量轴吸收输入偏移。该两轴门控在头部生命周期、GCD 和适配器池中均以更少专家实现更优决策。作者将"公共因子化"作为将嵌入空间结论回推原始机制的显式经验兼容性假设，并指出该工作针对的是分数家族而非完整系统的端到端对比。

Abstract: Sharing a deep encoder does not, by itself, fix the central confound of task-comparison scores. We show that cross-evaluated heads on a frozen shared representation inherit the extrapolation confound of shallow exchange scores: pure input rotations with fixed labels inflate a deep exchange score from about 0 to 0.80, while representation-novelty scores are blind in the complementary direction (flat under label permutations that change the task completely). Transplanting a conditional two-discriminator discrepancy into the embedding space resolves both blind spots: the functional axis stays within +-0.001 under rotations and tracks label-permutation drift mass monotonically. Built into a mixture-of-heads lifecycle, the two-axis gate attains better decision quality with fewer heads than exchange or novelty triggers at a matched training budget. On generalized category discovery, the same chunk-level functional axis separates semantic novelty from photometric shift with AUROC 0.98-0.99 where per-input OOD scores (MSP, Energy, Mahalanobis, KNN) sit near chance for that distinction. All findings replicate across frozen ImageNet-21k ViT-B/16 and self-supervised DINOv2 backbones on CIFAR-100, and extend to residual adapter pools with recurrence, where a null-calibrated novelty trigger never fires on mechanism changes while the two-axis gate handles them with full recurrence reuse. We state explicitly the common-factoring condition under which embedding-space conclusions transfer to the original mechanism.

</details>


### [102] [False-science induction in autonomous scientific discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27883)
*Hanbing Liang,Fujun Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"伪科学归纳"(false-science induction)概念，揭示闭环自主科学发现系统中记录绑定错误如何系统性误导实验预算分配。研究表明，当合法物理对象与测量值被错误配对时，神经代理模型会忠实学习这种记录诱导的虚假关联——尽管各边际数据分布保持不变——从而将实验资源引向低性能区域。关键发现是错误的一致性结构（coherence）而非原始错误频率才是控制预算错配的主要变量，并据此提出监控轴隔离与反馈冲突分诊的运行协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着闭环科学发现系统（机器人化学、自驱动材料实验室等）日益自主地执行实验并更新决策，记录完整性已成为实验装置的一部分。然而，现有关于标签噪声、对抗性投毒和数据溯源的研究多聚焦于分类层面或静态基准测试，未能捕捉闭环系统中记录绑定错误如何被主动转化为实验预算承诺这一独特的失效模式。作者旨在系统隔离"绑定错误→预算分配"这一因果通路，确立记录绑定作为闭环发现可靠性的一阶控制问题。

Method: 在两个领域进行受控闭环重放：(1) 基于Sarkisyan等人GFP适应性景观的蛋白质适应性预测，候选池45,520条，每轮采集100条，运行5轮；(2) 基于Matbench实验带隙任务的材料预测，候选池2,396个，每轮采集50条，运行5轮。每种设置比较干净数据、同预算随机交换与定向配对错误绑定三种条件，通过匹配共享初始历史、候选池和交换预算实现配对设计。代理模型以MLP为主，并使用TabM集成、MC-dropout UCB采集进行验证；外部在BEAR、CAMEO和SAMPLE公共轨迹上进行归档预言重放。关键方法学创新是引入经验风险评分 R=ρ(Δ_DT/s_Y)·√(m_c/n_T) 来量化错误一致性结构对预算分配的影响，并通过轨迹浓度比 Γ_r(T)=b_r(T)/q_r(T) 实现执行前在线隔离。

Result: 在GFP和材料两个领域，10个配对种子中定向配对错误绑定将诱导低值轴的采集占比从基线2.7/250（GFP）和5.6/250（材料）分别提升至36.1–47.1和41.2–49.7（配对符号检验p=0.00195），而同预算随机交换几乎无影响（0.0–0.1）。TabM在两个领域均以10/10恢复目标轴，BEAR重放中RF-UCB和MLP UCB分别产生99.0和108.8次目标轴采集。一致性扫描显示材料领域呈渐进响应（相干度0→1时采集从0.0升至15.0），GFP领域呈阈值式响应（相干度0.50–0.75间出现跃升）。执行前隔离机制在主设置中阻止99.97%的GFP虚假轴分配和100%的材料虚假轴分配，控制组零误报；反馈冲突分诊在定向轨迹中将目标轴排序为第1（GFP、材料、BEAR均为10/10种子前2恢复），全轴监控在无预命名目标时仍能标记目标轴（GFP和材料10/10，CAMEO 9/10）。

Conclusion: 本文确立了闭环科学发现中记录绑定作为一阶可靠性问题的地位，并精确定义了绑定可识别性边界：边际标签检查按构造对此类错误完全盲视，同分布预测验证可认证与损坏记录函数的一致性，但闭环采集将该记录函数转化为预算承诺。基于此提出操作协议：(1) 起源感知连接与样本身份检查；(2) 候选池基线校准；(3) 候选归一化浓度追踪作为执行前止损信号；(4) 反馈冲突历史指导起源审计。错误一致性而非错误频率是审计的主要度量，板孔、批次、排序键等结构化记录操作是需优先审查的误差类别。该框架要求数据绑定与模型拟合和采集策略置于同等控制层地位，从根本上将自主发现的数据治理从被动簿记转变为主动实验可靠性组件。

Abstract: Closed-loop discovery systems increasingly execute experiments and update decisions autonomously, turning record integrity into part of the experimental apparatus. We show that false-science induction arises when legitimate physical objects and measurements are paired incorrectly, driving neural surrogates to faithfully learn record-induced associations that do not correspond to the true object-outcome relationship while marginal data distributions remain unchanged. Across green fluorescent protein fitness and materials band-gap prediction loops, coherent paired misbinding systematically redirects experimental budgets toward low-performing basins, whereas same-volume random swaps have negligible effects. These observations identify error coherence, rather than raw error frequency, as the primary variable controlling this budget misallocation in the tested loops. The resulting binding identifiability boundary supports monitored-axis quarantines and feedback-conflict triage, which intercept over-concentrated proposals before execution and isolate the corrupted hypothesis axis.

</details>


### [103] [Spread and Scale: What Determines Whether Test-Time Budget Allocation Pays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27917)
*Jinhyung Bae*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过预注册验证实验，研究神经组合优化求解器中固定采样预算的重新分配策略。作者发现：决定重新分配是否有效的关键属性是工作负载中实例难度的多样性（而非平均难度），且预算感知的自适应策略能恢复大部分潜在收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经组合优化（NCO）求解器通常对所有实例使用相同的采样预算，但实例难度差异显著。已有研究指出按难度重新分配预算可能改善解质量，但存在评估偏差（在同数据上决策并评估会产生虚假增益）。这留下了两个未解问题：（1）工作负载的何种属性决定重新分配的收益？（2）若分配策略需消耗部分预算来获取难度信息，收益是否依然存在？

Method: 方法采用预注册确认性实验（preregistered confirmatory experiments），即在数据收集前固定分析方案与判定标准。实验涵盖：三个独立训练的NCO求解器；两种构建"更难工作负载"的构造方法；以旅行商问题（TSP）为基准。对每个实验均独立依据书面规范重新计算，并报告所有早期版本的修正（包括两项削弱本文自身结论的修正）。

Result: 结果揭示：（1）决定重新分配有效性的核心属性是工作负载内实例难度的异质性（variety in difficulty），而非平均难度——均匀易或均匀难的工作负载重新分配空间有限，混合型工作负载则有显著空间；（2）预算感知策略（需付费获取难度信息）恢复了大部分（虽非全部）在难度信息免费假设下可获得的改进；（3）所有早期版本的修正均被透明记录，包括其对结论方向的影响。

Conclusion: 本文为NCO预算再分配提供了具体实证答案：当工作负载实例难度异质时，重新分配预算有效；同时提供了验证模板，确保该结论非方法学伪影。其核心贡献在于区分了"难度异质性"与"平均难度"两个概念，并诚实地报告了所有削弱性修正。

Abstract: Neural combinatorial optimization solvers generate many candidate solutions per instance and report the best one found, using the same sample budget for every instance regardless of difficulty. A companion study showed that reallocating a fixed budget toward harder instances can improve solution quality, but that the standard way of measuring this improvement is biased: deciding an allocation and evaluating it on the same data can manufacture an apparent gain even when none exists. This left open what property of a workload determines whether reallocation is worth doing, and whether a policy that spends part of the budget to decide how to allocate the rest still pays once that cost is counted. This paper answers both questions through pre-registered confirmatory experiments -- analysis and verdict criteria fixed before data collection -- across three independently trained solvers and two ways of constructing harder workloads on the traveling salesman problem. Within the workloads we study, the deciding property is how varied the instances within a workload are in difficulty, not how difficult the workload is on average: a uniformly easy or uniformly hard workload offers little room for reallocation, while a mixed workload offers substantial room. A budget-aware policy that pays for its own information about instance difficulty recovers most, though not all, of the improvement available when that information is assumed free. Every experiment was independently recomputed from its written specification, and every correction to an earlier version -- including two that weakened the paper's own claims -- is reported with the direction it moved the conclusion. The paper offers a specific empirical answer and a template for verifying that answer is not an artifact of how it was measured.

</details>


### [104] [Riemannian Structure and Optimization for a Class of Low-Parametric Orthogonal Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27982)
*Ali Aliev,Maxim Rakhuba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了由块对角因子与固定置换矩阵交替相乘所构成的矩阵族（GS矩阵）的黎曼几何与优化算法。重点关注正交双因子情形，推导计算了切空间投影、回缩、自动微分（AD）梯度计算及参数共享等关键工具，并应用于矩阵逼近和大语言模型参数高效微调等任务。研究还发现高阶（m≥3）分解不一定构成光滑流形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GS矩阵在表达能力与计算效率之间取得了良好平衡，已在深度学习架构（尤其是参数高效微调）中展现出实用性，但缺乏高效且理论完备的优化方法。现有欧氏优化方法忽视了流形几何结构，本文旨在通过黎曼几何填补这一空白。

Method: 方法包括：(1) 通过紧李群作用证明正交双因子情形构成光滑嵌入子流形，并给出反例说明一般矩形或方块情形不构成光滑流形；(2) 针对Perfect Shuffle置换，推导切空间投影的闭式解，并分k₁≥b₂（不相交支撑）和k₁<b₂（重叠支撑）两种情形讨论；(3) 利用自动微分计算黎曼梯度，无需构造稠密矩阵；(4) 扩展至参数共享情形，证明AD梯度与解析解的关系（AD返回求和，需除以共享数）；(5) 定义块状回缩并证明其有效性；(6) 证明映射Φ是局部等距映射，从而可通过块状测地线得到GS流形上的测地线；(7) 对m=3情形与CS分解建立联系，证明A₃ᵒʳᵗʰ=A₃∩O(N)；(8) 通过纤维论证与定向性反例证明m=3、q≥3时GS正交流形在子空间拓扑下不构成拓扑流形。

Result: 理论结果：(a) 给出反例证明m=2时矩形/方块块不形成光滑流形；(b) 证明m=2正交情形为光滑流形，m=3时A₃ᵒʳᵗʰ=A₃∩O(N)，但当q≥3、d为奇数时子空间拓扑下非流形；(c) 复杂度为O(C_f + N(b₁+b₂) + u₁b₁³+u₂b₂³)。实验结果：(a) GS-Procrustes问题上黎曼梯度下降在精度和稳定性上与Cayley基线相当或更优；(b) RoBERTa-base在GLUE基准上的微调实验中，黎曼SGD（momentum）平均得分84.85，欧氏AdamW达86.04，黎曼方法仅带来5-7%额外开销；(c) 参数共享可显著压缩参数量（如b₁=8时从1.38M降至0.65M），且部分配置下性能接近非共享版本。

Conclusion: 本文为GS正交矩阵提供了完整的黎曼优化框架，包括基于AD的高效梯度计算、回缩、参数共享及向量传输等工具。复杂度分析表明该框架对标准训练的开销有限。在大语言模型微调任务中，黎曼方法在保持参数效率的同时表现出竞争力。未来工作包括扩展至高阶优化器（如超越AdamW）、不同块约束及更深入的高阶分解研究。

Abstract: In this paper, we are concerned with matrices formed by block-diagonal factors interleaved with fixed permutations -- a flexible family of structured matrices. This class has recently drawn interest in deep learning architectures for its balanced expressivity-efficiency trade-off, yet efficient computational strategies for working with it remain to be found. We approach this problem through Riemannian geometry and examine under what conditions this class admits a smooth manifold structure. For the practically important case of orthogonal two-factor matrices, we derive the essential Riemannian tools and propose efficient algorithms for their implementation. The algorithms leverage automatic differentiation, support parameter sharing within each factor, and avoid explicit dense matrix construction. We test them within the Riemannian optimization framework on the best matrix approximation problem and for parameter-efficient fine-tuning of large language models. Beyond the two-factor setting, we study the geometric and matrix-theoretic properties of factorizations with a larger number of block-diagonal factors.

</details>


### [105] [PISCES: Physics-Informed Solar-wind Convolutional autoEncoder for Space-weather Anomaly Detection and Early Warning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28022)
*Kevin Lee,Alison J. March*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PISCES是一个物理信息卷积自编码器，用于在日地L1点就地测量中检测太阳风瞬变事件并提供空间天气早期预警。该模型在无标签的OMNI数据上训练，结合了磁场一致性、温速关系、Parker螺旋角等物理约束，将异常分数分解为可解释的物理子分数。经过拖尾中值滤波后，预警可先于独立观测到的急始事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定阈值方法会遗漏磁场与等离子体的组合特征，且现有监督学习方法依赖不完整的事件目录标签。需要在L1点快速、可靠地检测太阳风瞬变（包括行星际日冕物质抛射ICME、行星际激波、共转相互作用区CIR），为地磁影响提供预警时间（通常约40分钟至1小时）。

Method: 1) 数据：OMNI HRO一分钟分辨率数据，按时间顺序划分（训练2005-2015，验证2016-2017，测试2018-2024），7个输入通道（Bx, By, Bz, Bt, np, V, Tp）。2) 架构：一维卷积自编码器U-Net结构，含跳跃连接与跳过dropout（p=0.8），瓶颈压缩比10.5:1，约12555参数。3) 物理损失：磁矢量一致性、温度-速度经验关系、Parker螺旋锥角、熵/总压/β/动压的逐分钟变化惩罚。4) 异常评分：分解为15个子分数（重建误差+物理约束+派生量），加权组合。5) 跳跃衰减：推理时按验证集选择衰减尺度改善性能。6) 评估指标：PR-AUC、事件POD、FAR、Heidke分数；与OC-SVM、Conv-VAE、PCA、LSTM-AE等基线比较；分析警告提前时间。

Result: PISCES-Opto在2018-2024测试集（848个有效目录事件）上PR-AUC达0.365（11次运行均值0.306±0.029）；验证集选择跳跃衰减后可提升至0.406（11次运行均值0.376±0.020）。ICME事件POD为69%，激波24.8%，高速流10.7%。使用拖尾15分钟中值滤波后，警告覆盖112个急始事件中的32-39个，中位提前时间+24至+28分钟；未训练网络仅匹配12个，中位提前时间1-12分钟。15个子分数的效应大小排序在训练运行间高度一致（Spearman ρ=0.88），而在未训练运行间一致性较低（ρ=0.53），表明排序与训练权重相关。

Conclusion: PISCES将异常分数分解为15个可解释的物理子分数，提供事件类型的描述性特征。物理信息损失未显示对聚合检测精度的稳定改进；全跳跃条件下未训练网络平均精度（0.337±0.005）高于10/11个训练运行，训练对排序的增益未被确定。验证集跳跃衰减可使所有训练运行的平均精度提升0.070±0.028。警告分析显示训练模型在急始事件提前时间上明显优于未训练模型（24-28分钟 vs 11-12分钟）。未来工作需要在开发期间未使用的数据上进行盲法验证、比较不同窗口长度，并加入电子温度以改进总压估计。

Abstract: Space weather early warning depends on detecting solar wind transients in in-situ measurements at the first Sun-Earth Lagrange point (L1), before they reach Earth. Fixed thresholds can miss combined magnetic and plasma structure, and many learning methods provide a single anomaly score. We present the Physics-Informed Solar-wind Convolutional autoEncoder for Space-weather (PISCES), a convolutional autoencoder trained without catalog labels on OMNI solar wind measurements under physics constraints. Its loss includes magnetic field consistency, an empirical relation between temperature and velocity, the Parker spiral angle, and penalties on changes between consecutive one-minute samples in derived quantities calculated from the reconstruction. At inference, PISCES separates the anomaly score into magnetic and plasma reconstruction errors, physics relations, and residual corrections, and reports the magnitude of each contribution. Attenuation of the skip connections, selected on validation data, improves average precision for the trained models, while the untrained scores remain nearly the same. The trained models also give a more consistent ordering of these physical contributions. After smoothing with a trailing median, the alarms can precede independently observed sudden commencements, including positive sudden impulses.

</details>


### [106] [Curriculum Learning with GNN-based Reinforcement Learning for Job Shop Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28085)
*Jayakrishnan K. Vasudevan,Jonathan Hoss,Noah Klarmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基于图神经网络（GNN）的强化学习（RL）方法求解作业车间调度问题（JSSP）时，采用课程学习（CL）策略的有效性。作者将按规模递增的三阶段课程学习（先训练小规模实例再逐步迁移到大规模目标实例）与直接单规模训练进行了对比，目标尺寸分别为20×20、25×25和30×30。实验在相同的GNN-PPO架构、硬件配置和总训练预算（30M环境步）下进行，并在8×8到30×30的未见实例上评估。结果表明，课程学习在所有目标规模上均能缩短训练时间，且随着目标规模增大，优势越发显著：在30×30时，平均最优性间隙在所有评估尺寸上降低约8.1个百分点，在目标尺寸上降低约8.6个百分点，同时节省约50小时训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于GNN的强化学习在求解JSSP时具有跨实例规模泛化的潜力，但直接在大型JSSP实例上训练计算成本高昂（因episode更长、图表示更大、决策依赖更复杂）。现有的JSSP课程学习研究要么在固定规模内按难度排序实例，要么主要关注最终解的质量，对课程学习如何影响跨规模泛化、目标规模特化以及收敛行为缺乏系统性研究，且很少将训练时间作为主要评估指标。因此，本文旨在研究基于规模的课程学习是否能降低计算开销而不牺牲解的质量。

Method: 方法包括：(1) 采用线性复杂度的GNN（GIN，3层，隐藏维度64）作为策略骨干，结合actor-critic架构和PPO训练，将JSSP建模为在析取图上的MDP，采用action masking保证前驱可行性；(2) 设计三阶段课程学习方案C=[c1, c2, c3]，每阶段分配10M步，总预算30M步与单规模训练相同，阶段间保留策略参数θ和价值函数参数φ，仅重新配置环境和size-dependent超参数；(3) 训练实例采用随机JSSP生成协议（机器路线为随机排列，处理时间均匀采样U{1,...,99}），限定J/M=1，目标尺寸分别为20×20（课程：10×10→15×15→20×20）、25×25（10×10→15×15→25×25）和30×30（10×10→20×20→30×30）；(4) 评估在140个未见实例（每规模20个，尺寸8×8到30×30）上进行，采用相对FDD/MWR启发式的最优性间隙（OG），并从泛化（所有尺寸平均OG）、特化（目标尺寸平均OG）和训练效率（wall-clock时间）三个维度进行评估，每个设置使用3个随机种子重复。

Result: 实验结果汇总于表IV：对于20×20目标规模，课程学习节省约20小时（约34%）训练时间，泛化性能相近，但目标尺寸OG增加约1.2个百分点（轻微特化损失）；对于25×25目标规模，节省约40小时（约42%）训练时间，所有评估尺寸平均OG降低约1.6个百分点（泛化改善），但目标尺寸OG增加约1.3个百分点（轻微特化损失）；对于30×30目标规模，节省约50小时（约43%）训练时间，所有评估尺寸平均OG降低约8.1个百分点，目标尺寸OG降低约8.6个百分点（泛化和特化均显著改善）。这验证了核心假设：随着目标实例规模增大，直接训练的计算成本急剧增加，使课程学习将部分学习过程转移到更廉价的早期阶段的优势越发显著。

Conclusion: 本文得出结论：基于实例规模的课程学习能够一致地减少GNN-RL在JSSP上的训练时间，且绝对时间节省随目标规模增大而增加。在解的质量方面，课程学习主要改善泛化性能，而特化效果取决于目标规模——在小到中等目标规模上存在轻微特化权衡（因为单规模模型将全部预算用于目标规模，而课程模型仅在最后阶段训练目标规模），但在较大目标规模（30×30）上课程学习同时改善泛化和特化。因此，课程学习在直接训练计算成本高昂的大型JSSP实例时尤为有用。研究的局限性包括课程切换间隔固定为10M步、仅限J/M=1的实例、未采用自适应课程调度策略以及缺乏与难度型课程和先进调度方法的比较；未来工作应扩展到更大规模、不同J/M比，并研究基于收敛行为或性能阈值的自适应课程。

Abstract: The job shop scheduling problem is a challenging combinatorial optimization problem, and recent reinforcement learning approaches using graph neural networks have shown promise for learning scheduling policies directly from problem instances. However, training on large instances remains computationally expensive, and generalization across instance sizes remains challenging. This paper studies curriculum learning for graph neural network-based reinforcement learning in the job shop scheduling problem by comparing it with single-size training across three target sizes: 20 x 20, 25 x 25, and 30 x 30. In the curriculum setting, the policy is first trained on smaller instances and then progressively adapted to larger target sizes, allowing scheduling behavior learned in earlier stages to support learning on larger instances. Models are evaluated on unseen instances from 8 x 8 to 30 x 30 using the optimality gap, considering both generalization across all evaluation sizes and specialization on the target size. Results show that curriculum learning consistently reduces wall-clock training time, with larger benefits as the target size increases. The strongest advantage is observed at 30 x 30, where curriculum learning reduces the mean optimality gap across all evaluation sizes by approximately 8.1 percentage points, reduces the target-size mean optimality gap by approximately 8.6 percentage points, and saves approximately 50 hours of training time.

</details>


### [107] [LAYERSCOPE: A Layerwise Characterization of Video and Multimodal Learned Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28086)
*Sandra Arcos-Holzinger,Debashish Chakraborty,Rohita Mocharla,Will Walden,Andrew Yates,Reno Kriz,Sarah M. Erfani,James Bailey,Vishal M. Patel,Sanjeev Khudanpur*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LayerScope提出一个无需标签的逐层评估框架，利用局部、全局、分布性和配对感知的几何度量（GRIDS-LID、RankMe、协方差有效秩、SW₂、Procrustes误差）在视频与多模态模型中比较各层表征。结论是：中间层常优于最终层/默认输出；没有单一几何指标能稳定预测或选择最优层；配对感知度量比纯分布距离更能解释检索行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视频/多模态基准（如MVEB）只用模型默认输出做评估，把内部表征当成黑盒。要理解网络深度内表征如何变化并识别高性能中间层，依赖大量标注数据和重复任务评估，成本高昂。本文希望用无标签的几何诊断来刻画和预测逐层表示结构。

Method: 1) 在7个架构异构模型（V-JEPA 2、PE-AV S/L、X-CLIP、Gemma 4、Qwen3-VL、LCO）上冻结参数，提取每个transformer块的隐藏状态（分类/聚类用均值池化，检索用模型自身的共享空间读出）。2) 计算五类几何度量：局部（GRIDS-LID，k=100）、全局（RankMe、标准化协方差有效秩）、跨模态分布（Sliced 2-Wasserstein）、配对感知（held-out正交Procrustes误差）。3) 在MVEB/MVEB+任务上评估：Breakfast/UCF101/HMDB51分类、UCF101聚类、TUNA/VATEX文本-视频检索、UCF101零样本分类。4) 用层选择遗憾（regret）衡量纯几何选层与oracle选层的性能差距。5) 用1,000次对应关系置换做配对结构检验。

Result: 1) 逐层几何轨迹在不同模型族间差异显著（如V-JEPA 2和X-CLIP呈GRIDS-LID早峰、RankMe晚峰；PE-AV的GRIDS-LID随深度递增），形成"几何签名"。2) 没有单一几何指标能稳定选最优层：RankMe在21个分类/聚类比较中平均遗憾最低(1.43%)，优于末层(2.49%)，但并非通用层选择器；GRIDS-LID与下游性能相关性高度依赖任务（LCO在Breakfast上+0.94，UCF101分类上-0.37）。3) 7个模型中有6个存在中间层在分类/聚类上超越最终层（最大增益为Gemma 4在UCF101聚类上ΔV=+0.1683）。4) 跨模态检索的最优层对多在末层附近，SW₂与nDCG@10负相关（10次比较中8次），但最小SW₂层对的选择遗憾(0.2994)远高于模型默认输出(0.0097)；Procrustes误差在32次比较中31次正确识别真实配对。

Conclusion: LayerScope表明：（1）逐层几何诊断是表征结构的有用描述工具，但不能替代任务评估；（2）没有通用的"最佳层"或通用几何层选择准则，最优深度因模型与任务而异；（3）配对感知度量揭示了边际分布距离无法捕捉的跨模态结构。几何度量宜作为互补诊断，而非预测代理。未来工作需在受控条件下分离训练目标、架构、数据规模等设计选择对逐层几何特征的影响。

Abstract: We propose LAYERSCOPE, a label-free, layerwise framework that aims to characterize a model's learned representations in video and multimodal settings. Evaluating downstream performance using representations from final or intermediate layers typically requires large amounts of labeled data, repeated task-specific evaluations, and substantial computation. To address these limitations, LAYERSCOPE uses local, global, distributional, and correspondence-based geometric metrics to compare layerwise representation structure within and across models without requiring task-specific labels. We evaluate seven architecturally diverse models across video and multimodal classification, clustering, and text-to-video retrieval tasks from MVEB/MVEB+. We find that intermediate-layer representations can outperform final-layer and model-default outputs. We also find that no single geometric metric consistently predicts downstream performance, but note that distinct layerwise geometric signatures emerge across model families. LID shows task-dependent relationships with performance, while RankMe provides the strongest measure for classification and clustering, but is not a universal layer selector. We also find that pairing-aware metrics explain retrieval better than distributional distances alone. LAYERSCOPE therefore offers a framework for comparing representations across models and layers, enabling a more systematic evaluation in video and multimodal settings.

</details>


### [108] [Fed-ReMasker: Federated Tabular Imputation under Feature-Level Missingness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28105)
*Ioannis Papathanail,Rooholla Poursoleymani,Lubnaa Abdur Rahman,Stavroula Georgia Mougiakakou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Fed-ReMasker：将ReMasker掩码自编码器适配到联邦学习框架，解决多中心临床研究中表格数据的特征级缺失问题（整个特征在某些中心完全未观测）。该方法在324个同质场景和45个异质性场景中均优于现有联邦插补基线（Fed-MIWAE、Cafe、FedHF-Impute），在93.2%的值级和96.7%的特征级场景中达到最低NRMSE，且与集中式模型仅差3.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多中心临床研究中，数据保护法规限制原始数据共享，且不同中心可能仅采集部分重叠的特征集。现有联邦插补方法主要处理值级缺失（个别值缺失），而对特征级缺失（某些中心完全缺失整个特征）的研究不足。这种设置下，客户端本地无任何该特征的观测信息，需要跨中心协作学习特征间关系。

Method: 1）将ReMasker掩码自编码器（基于Transformer的编解码器）适配到水平联邦学习设置，架构和掩码重建目标不变，仅改变训练方式；2）各客户端在本地观测数据上训练ReMasker，通过FedAvg按样本量加权聚合参数；3）观测某特征的客户端更新共享模型，未观测该特征的客户端通过全局模型获得插补能力；4）比较了FedAvg、FedProx、FedAdam三种联邦优化策略；5）在6个合成数据集（线性和非线性，d∈{20,50,100}）和3个真实数据集（Codon、PhysioNet、NHANES）上构建系统基准，控制缺失特征率MFR∈{0,0.1,0.2,0.3}、缺失值率MVR∈{0.1,0.3,0.5}、客户端数K∈{3,5,10}；6）构建4种异质性条件（数量偏斜、缺失率偏斜、特征分布偏斜/非IID）评估鲁棒性；7）采用MCAR机制但特征分布偏斜条件下缺失变为数据依赖型；8）使用NRMSE作为评估指标，分值级和特征级分别报告。

Result: 1）同质基准（324个场景）：Fed-ReMasker在9个数据集上均取得最低NRMSE，平均值级NRMSE=0.061，特征级NRMSE=0.065，优于第二名Cafe（0.079和0.090）；在93.2%值级和96.7%特征级场景中胜出；2）Fed-MIWAE在特征级缺失时退化至最差（0.203），低于均值插补（0.165）；3）异质性实验（45个场景）：Fed-ReMasker在所有36个值级场景和至少35/36个特征级场景中优于所有基线；4）优化策略：FedAvg在30格中的29格取得最佳，FedProx和FedAdam反而略差，提示共享插补目标已对齐客户端更新；5）特征分布偏斜条件下误差增大最显著；6）与集中式模型差距：平均3.0%，随客户端数从K=3增至K=10时差距从1.2%扩大至7.2%。

Conclusion: Fed-ReMasker是首个系统性解决联邦表格插补中特征级缺失问题的方法，通过将掩码自编码器与联邦学习结合，使客户端能够插补本地从未观测的特征。实验证明其在合成和真实数据集上均显著优于现有联邦插补基线，对客户端异质性具有鲁棒性，且简单FedAvg即可达到最优。集中式与联邦模型的性能差距很小（3.0%），证明联邦化代价低。该方法支持多中心研究在特征集不同时协作，无需集中原始数据，为隐私保护的跨机构医学研究提供了实用工具。局限性：仅评估MCAR、仅数值特征、缺少正式隐私机制（如安全聚合、差分隐私）、未评估下游任务影响。

Abstract: Multi-center clinical studies and biomedical research collaborations increasingly seek to utilize data across centers to build models that generalize beyond any single center. This creates two distinct challenges: data protection regulations may restrict the sharing of raw patient data across institutions, while centers may collect only partially overlapping sets of features under different protocols. Federated learning enables collaborative model training without centralizing raw data. However, existing federated imputation methods rarely evaluate feature-level missingness, in which entire features are unobserved at some centers. To address this setting, we adapt the ReMasker masked autoencoder to federated learning (Fed-ReMasker), enabling centers to impute features never observed locally by leveraging knowledge learned across collaborating centers. We evaluate Fed-ReMasker in a benchmark spanning synthetic datasets with linear and nonlinear relationships and real-world tabular datasets, including clinical data. The benchmark varies the number of centers, the missingness ratios, and client heterogeneity. Fed-ReMasker achieves the lowest imputation error in 93.2% of value-level and 96.7% of feature-level scenarios in the homogeneous benchmark. It also remains robust to client heterogeneity using simple federated averaging, outperforming all baselines in all 36 value-level scenarios and each baseline in at least 35 of 36 feature-level scenarios, and comes within 3.0% on average of a centralized model trained on the pooled data.

</details>


### [109] [When and Where to Trust the Teacher: Unifying On-Policy Distillation and GRPO through Entropy-Calibrated Credit Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28385)
*Jie Zhang,Jingxiao Yang,Zhehao Huang,Yuhang Liu,Xiaolin Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UECR-GRPO是一种统一的强化学习方法，将GRPO验证器奖励与on-policy蒸馏(OPD)教师信号在轨迹级和token级两个层面进行整合。PUU在组归一化前合并验证器和教师效用，ECR通过零和缩放在保持任务信用预算的前提下重新分配token级信用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GRPO与on-policy蒸馏的混合方法存在两个问题：(1)教师证据在验证器组归一化之后才进入，无法改变响应排序；(2)token级重加权不保持每个响应的总任务信用分配。作者通过实证分析发现教师信号能区分84.3-98.4%的验证器退化组，但与验证器正确性存在39.1-50.8%的排序冲突，因此需要一种保留验证器作为任务定义信号、仅用教师提供局部分辨率的统一框架。

Method: 方法包含两个组件：(1) Path-Utility Unification (PUU)——通过KL正则化目标将验证器奖励与教师-锚点路径对数比合并为单一响应效用，在组归一化和PPO裁剪前统一处理，使教师证据能影响响应排序；(2) Entropy-Calibrated Redistribution (ECR)——利用教师-旧策略token间隙的有符号方向和教师熵做不确定性衰减，通过零和缩放在裁剪前重新分配验证器派生分量，保持每个响应的总任务信用预算和token符号。

Result: 在五个数学推理基准上，UECR-GRPO使用Qwen3-1.7B和Qwen3-4B学生分别达到17.21%和65.09%的Avg@12平均准确率，超过各规模最强基线0.89和0.56个百分点。消融实验表明PUU的轨迹效用统一和ECR的token信用重新分配均有贡献，完整方法表现最优。

Conclusion: UECR-GRPO成功将验证器和教师信号的整合分为两个独立层级——轨迹排序由PUU在归一化前完成，token信用分配由ECR在保持预算约束下完成。实验和离线诊断证实该方法在数学推理任务上实现了更高的平均准确率，同时支持预期的组级分辨率和token定位行为。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) supervises mathematical reasoning through final-answer correctness, but provides little guidance on individual tokens. On-policy distillation (OPD) supplies dense feedback on student-generated responses, yet teacher preference need not reflect correctness. Recent hybrids combine OPD and verifier-derived advantages or reweight task credit using teacher ratios. However, teacher guidance enters after verifier-based group normalization, and token reweighting need not preserve the total task credit assigned to each response. We introduce Unified Entropy-Calibrated Credit Redistribution for GRPO (UECR-GRPO), which integrates verifier and teacher signals within a single GRPO-style update at both the response and token levels. \emph{Path-Utility Unification} (PUU) combines verifier reward and a teacher-to-anchor path log-ratio in a single KL-regularized objective. Its on-policy implementation uses a length-normalized teacher score and combines both rewards before group normalization and PPO clipping, allowing teacher evidence to influence the response ranking. \emph{Entropy-Calibrated Redistribution} (ECR) then uses the signed teacher--old-policy token gap to redistribute the verifier-derived component. Full-vocabulary teacher entropy attenuates uncertain guidance, while a response-wise zero-sum projection preserves the total task credit and its token-wise sign before clipping. Across five mathematical reasoning benchmarks, UECR-GRPO achieves average \(\mathrm{Avg@12}\) accuracies of 17.21\% and 65.09\% with Qwen3-1.7B and Qwen3-4B students, respectively, exceeding the strongest baseline at each scale by 0.89 and 0.56 percentage points.

</details>


### [110] [Learning Holographic Reduced Representations with Clifford Variational Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28409)
*Mohamed Malek Abid,P. Michael Furlong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Clifford-VAE，一种用变分自编码器将非结构化数据投影到任意维Clifford环面上的方法，学习与全息简约表示（HRR）代数兼容的酉向量嵌入，从而为神经-符号系统的感知接地提供无监督解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向量符号代数（VSA）如HRR通过随机生成的原子向量和分数幂编码操作高维分布式表示，但如何将非结构化感知数据嵌入到HRR兼容表示（特别是具有单位幅值傅里叶系数的酉向量）仍是开放问题；现有方法依赖随机初始化，无法实现感知与认知的接地。

Method: 将标准VAE的高斯先验替换为定义在(d−1)维Clifford环面(𝓢¹)^{d−1}上的分布，由独立von Mises或Power Spherical分布参数化；通过逆变换施加共轭对称约束，确保采样的复数表示可经IFFT转换为实值酉向量（所有非DC频率分量的傅里叶系数均为1），规避高维下的"超球瓶颈"问题（单浓度参数vMF/Power Spherical在d>17后表面积骤降）。

Result: 在MNIST/FashionMNIST/CIFAR-10上：半监督k-NN分类与高斯/超球VAE基线相当（CIFAR-10平均提升29%）；VSA基准测试（自绑定/解绑、角色-填充符恢复、捆绑容量）优于所有其他VAE变体，匹配FHRR/酉HRR的state-of-the-art性能；可扩展至高维（d=4096）而vMF在d=128即发散。代价是FID重建质量略逊于高斯VAE，但远优于Power Spherical。

Conclusion: Clifford-VAE提供了一种原则性方法将感知数据接地到VSA符号推理框架中，解决了VSA文献中嵌入非结构化数据的长期问题；学习的表示具有语义可解释性（可解码回像素域）且在HRR代数下行为良好，可作为HRR认知架构和超维计算工程系统的可微分"感知前端"。

Abstract: Vector Symbolic Algebras project data structures into a hyperdimensional vector space through the application of their vector algebras to randomly generated atomic vector symbols and fractional power encodings of real-valued data. Embedding unstructured data remains an open question. We present \textit{Clifford-VAE}, a variational autoencoder that learns to project data onto a Clifford torus in arbitrary dimensions. Experiments using the MNIST, FashionMNIST, and CIFAR-10 datasets demonstrate that Clifford-VAE produces representations that are competitive with those produced by Gaussian and Hyperspherical VAEs for semi-supervised classification tasks while outperforming Gaussian and Hyperspherical counterparts in the VSA benchmark tests of self-binding and unbinding, role-filler recovery, and bundle capacity. Clifford-VAE provides a principled technique for grounding perceptual data into a symbolic reasoning framework, providing a new approach to a long-standing problem in the VSA literature.

</details>


### [111] [Order-Invariant Answers, Order-Sensitive Representations in Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28442)
*Zhixu Silvia Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探讨语言模型在数学推理中"答案不变性"与"表征不变性"的区别。通过合成函数组合任务，发现更准确的模型反而在内部表征中更明显地区分不同的规则排列顺序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学规则重排后答案不变，一个直观的假设是：能准确处理重排问题的模型，其内部表征也应相似（对排列顺序不敏感）。该研究质疑此假设，探究答案不变的任务是否也必然伴随表征不变。

Method: 1. 构造合成多步函数组合任务，包含相关链条与干扰链条，每题在固定正确答案下使用50种排列呈现。2. 定义"排列信噪比"（Permutation SNR）来量化隐藏表征中不同排列模式之间的分离程度（组间方差/组内方差）。3. 在16个1B–8B参数的语言模型上，跨三种合成设置测量准确率与层平均排列SNR，并计算Spearman秩相关。

Result: 在所有三种合成设置中，层平均排列SNR与准确率呈正相关，Spearman相关系数分别为0.86、0.73和0.62。控制模型大小后相关性仍然为正（前三步设置中显著，深度3设置中p=0.078处于边缘）。这表明推理更准确的模型对不同排列模式的内部表征分离更明显。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了答案不变性与表征不变性的关键区分：在等价的规则排列上实现正确答案并不要求内部表征相似。成功推理可与对顺序敏感的表征共存。该结论为数学推理提供了超越答案准确率的表征视角。但证据为相关性，局限于合成任务和部分共享训练谱系的模型，未证明编码顺序能改善推理。

Abstract: Reordering a set of mathematical rules without changing its meaning should preserve the correct answer, but must a model's internal representations stay invariant too? We investigate this question using synthetic multi-step function-composition problems, each presented under multiple rule orderings with the same correct answer. We measure accuracy and permutation signal-to-noise ratio (SNR), which quantifies how distinctly ordering patterns are represented relative to variation across problem instances. Across 16 language models ranging from 1B to 8B parameters, we find a pattern: models that solve reordered problems more accurately represent different rule orderings more distinctly. Layer-averaged permutation SNR is positively rank-correlated with accuracy in every synthetic setting we evaluate, with Spearman correlations reaching 0.86. These findings highlight a distinction between answer invariance and representation invariance: successful mathematical rule composition can accompany distinct internal representations between equivalent rule orderings. This motivates distinguishing answer invariance from representation invariance, and offers a representational perspective on mathematical reasoning beyond answer accuracy alone.

</details>


### [112] [EBRL: Asynchronous Embodied RL by Multi-Grained Resource Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27547)
*Liang Mi,Weijun Wang,Bowen Gao,Tianze Yu,Zixu Hao,Han Xiao,Xin Ding,Mingzhe Huang,Xin He,Lu Shi,Hao Wu,Haipeng Dai,Guihai Chen,Yunxin Liu,Ting Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EBRL是一种面向具身强化学习的异步训练系统，通过多粒度资源管理（异步流水线调度器+细粒度资源管理器）解决现有系统中GPU独占分配和rollout内同步屏障导致的硬件资源浪费问题，在多种模型、模拟器与硬件平台上实现显著吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身RL系统将整个GPU独占分配给单一阶段，且在rollout（仿真+生成）内部仍存在同步屏障，导致SM占用率低于20%、GPU空闲时间高达47%，严重浪费CPU与GPU资源。

Method: EBRL包含两大核心技术：① 异步流水线调度器——迭代间重叠rollout与训练、按环境组流水线模拟与生成、步骤级独立推进并动态重绑定空闲CPU核；② 细粒度资源管理器——构建GPU SM配额与CPU核心池，基于离线profile与运行时反馈联合选择各阶段的SM配额、批大小与CPU配置，并通过MPS实现共享GPU上的并发执行。

Result: 在4种具身策略（OpenPI、GR00T、OpenVLA-OFT、DreamZero）与4种模拟器（LIBERO、ManiSkill、RoboCasa、BEHAVIOR）上，跨H100/A100/H20/RTX 4090异构测试平台，EBRL端到端轨迹吞吐达SOTA系统的1.30×–3.47×，训练收敛时间缩短约2.5×，最终成功率约98%。消融实验表明各组件贡献约10%–31%的吞吐提升，且对策略陈旧度不敏感（变化<2%）。

Conclusion: EBRL通过异步流水线与多粒度资源管理的协同设计，有效回收了具身RL训练中空闲的CPU/GPU容量，在不牺牲收敛性的前提下显著提升训练效率，为异构硬件下的具身RL系统设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Embodied reinforcement learning (RL) improves model capabilities with a pipeline of environment simulation, action generation, and model updates. These stages show heterogeneous CPU and GPU demands, making efficient resource utilization difficult. Recent systems overlap rollout (simulation and generation) with training for efficiency, but exclusive GPU allocation and synchronized barrier in rollout still leave substantial hardware resource waste. In this paper, we present EBRL, an asynchronous embodied RL training system with two core techniques. The asynchronous pipelined scheduler overlaps rollout and training, pipelines simulation and generation across environment groups, and carries out each environment independently, eliminating synchronization stalls. The fine-grained resource manager pools CPU cores and GPU streaming multiprocessors, and uses stage profiles and runtime feedback to adjust resource quotas and batch sizes to meet the shifting demands among stages. We implement EBRL on RLinf and evaluate it with four embodied policies and four simulation benchmarks across heterogeneous GPU testbeds. Experiments show that EBRL achieves 1.30-3.47 times the end-to-end rollout throughput and 2.5 times of training convergency compared to the SOTA embodied RL systems.

</details>


### [113] [When Adaptation Hurts: Split Sensitivity and Person-Level Negative Transfer in Federated Wearable Onboarding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27819)
*Rahil Aftab,Vineet Kumar Rakesh,Soumya Mazumdar,Tapas Samanta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对联邦可穿戴设备模型在部署到未见用户时，通过无标签校准数据进行测试时自适应（onboarding）的安全性问题，提出了一套严格的评估协议与基准测试。研究发现：单一切分下的高准确率结论在完成全部用户轮转后会显著下降（HHAR上降低13.8-17.8个百分点）；没有任何自适应方法能同时在所有数据集上获得正均值收益且无显著个体级伤害；均值指标会掩盖尾部质量、校准可用性及跌倒检测特异性等失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦可穿戴模型最终服务于未参与源训练的用户，但现有的onboarding评估常因数据泄漏、单人折、窗口级置信区间等问题高估方法效果，且平均准确率无法反映个体级别的负迁移（negative transfer）。本文旨在揭示这些评估陷阱并提供可审计的失败图谱。

Method: 1) 提出"onboarding有效性契约"，包含联邦源溯源、不可变的参与人切分、录音级分离的校准/评估、外层人物推理、匹配的零样本分支及显式校准可用性六项要求；2) 在5个公开可穿戴数据集（HHAR、PAMAP2、WISDM、SisFall、UniMiB SHAR及UP-FALL子集）上构建冻结双层基准（567个广筛+270个轮转扩展=837个工作单元）；3) 评估六种核心方法（FedAvg、FedPer-GH、FedPer-matched、FedBN-recalibration、ATP-style port、Feature-only）；5) 使用TCN+GroupNorm骨干网络进行联邦训练，并在32窗口预算下测量平衡准确率、分支匹配增益G_{u,m,b}、负迁移率及材料伤害率（<-2pp）。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) RQ1：原冻结HHAR折BA达95.6-97.2%，完成9用户轮转后降至78.3-83.0%，下降13.8-17.8pp；显示领导者从参数匹配FedPer变为FedBN，但配对bootstrap区间含零，未能证明方法优越性。(2) RQ2：FedBN在五个数据集均有正均值增益但有一个材料伤害；ATP-style在WISDM获+7.18pp但在PAMAP2损失-2.64pp，22/114的种子-人员实现出现伤害；Feature-only无种子平均后的2pp伤害但精确单侧95%上界为7.6%。(3) RQ3：p10与最差单元BA会改变PAMAP2/SisFall/UniMiB的描述性领导者。(4) RQ4：Feature-only在UP-FALL上达92.9%敏感度但特异性仅36.0%，暴露假阳性问题。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一份可审计的开发基准测试与失败图谱，而非主张普遍优越性或部署安全性。核心结论是：无标签onboarding对未见联邦可穿戴客户端的收益是有条件的而非自动获益；应在显式有效性契约下进行评估，包括泄漏控制、完整或前瞻采样的外层人物单元、匹配的源检查点、分离的参与者与实现估计量、可用性分母及保留负面结果，方可考虑部署声明。联邦onboarding首先是评估问题，其次才是优化问题。

Abstract: Federated wearable models eventually serve people absent from source training, but favorable average accuracy does not establish that unlabeled onboarding helps each person. We evaluate six core onboarding strategies on five wearable datasets under a leakage-controlled protocol that fixes source checkpoints, estimates normalization from source data only, separates calibration from evaluation recordings, and performs inference over held-out people rather than windows, devices, or random seeds. Completing all eligible HHAR and PAMAP2 outer-person rotations materially changes the conclusion obtained from the original frozen fold. On HHAR, balanced accuracy on that single person is 95.6-97.2% across methods versus 78.3-83.0% over all nine users, a reduction of 13.8-17.8 percentage points (pp). The displayed mean leader changes on both datasets, while paired leader-runner bootstrap intervals include zero and do not resolve a superior method. No adaptive core mechanism combines positive mean gain in all five datasets with zero seed-averaged person-level losses greater than 2 percentage points (pp). FedBN has one such loss and ATP-style adaptation has eight; Feature-only has none after seed averaging, but its exact one-sided 95% upper bound is 7.6%. A complementary seed-person stress audit records 4, 22, and 10 harmful realizations out of 114 for FedBN, ATP-style, and Feature-only, respectively; these are repeated realizations, not independent participants. Tail quality, calibration availability, and fall-window specificity reveal additional failures hidden by mean accuracy. The study therefore provides an auditable development benchmark and failure map rather than a universal-superiority or deployment-safety claim.

</details>


### [114] [The Drift Contract: Spectral Updates for Depth-Robust Local Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26811)
*Fabien Polly*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将Muon风格的谱更新几何（动量正交化+谱步长缩放）应用于逐层局部学习（无全局反向传播），在CIFAR-10 MLP基准上同时解决了局部学习的两大历史痛点：深度退化和超参数脆弱性。作者提出"漂移契约"（drift contract）：lr = ε / RMS(input)，为每层提供基于输入的、每步权重诱导的预激活变化上界。单一设置 ε=3e-3 在宽度128–2048、深度12–48范围内均为最优，而局部Adam需逐深度重调且在深度48崩溃（31.3% vs 42.7%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部学习因可实现结构性并行（作者实测12层4.34×吞吐）而具有吸引力，但长期受限于两大问题：(1) 随深度增加精度下降（深度病理），(2) 超参数脆弱（需逐层、逐深度调优）。Muon优化器在全局训练中通过谱更新几何成功将Transformer训练成本减半，但其理论基于全局梯度，能否迁移至局部梯度场景未知。本文旨在探索谱更新几何与逐层局部学习这一交叉点，并寻求适用于无全局反向传播场景（如边缘设备、分散流水线、反向传播不友好硬件）的鲁棒优化方案。

Method: 方法核心是逐层漂移有界谱更新：
1. 动量更新：m ← μm + G（μ=0.95）
2. Newton-Schulz5正交化：O = NewtonSchulz5(m)，将m的奇异值近似映射为1
3. 权重更新：W ← W − lr·√max(1, d_out/d_in)·O
4. 漂移契约：将学习率表达为 lr_l = ε / RMS_ema(h_{l-1})，为每层提供输入条件化的预激活漂移上界 ε·κ_l（κ_l≈1）
5. 偏置和局部头使用Adam（保持元素级优化器）
6. 协议：前瞻性指定控制（fixed-lr归因、辅助参数混淆、归一化），验证为单一库文件加每实验一个runner的纯NumPy代码

Result: 主要结果：
1. **宽度迁移**：ε*=0.003在宽度128/512/1024/2048均为最优（内点最优，10×更小则欠拟合，更大则衰减）；Adam最优lr随宽度漂移（1e-3→3e-4）
2. **深度结果**（宽度128）：Adam逐深度重调仍从深度12的44.4%崩溃至深度48的31.3%（损失13.1点）；谱更新仅从45.7%降至42.7%（损失3.0点），使用同一设置
3. **五种子统计**：契约在所有宽度优于局部Adam（+1.2, +2.3, +2.9, +1.0点）
4. **稳定性**：合成任务上局部谱更新在333×学习率范围内稳定，vs 局部Adam 100×、全局10×，呈超加性
5. **归一化负面结果**：加入RMSNorm+权重衰减后，稳定性优势转向全局Muon；谱局部更新的优势集中在无归一化场景
6. **机制**：谱更新下特征有效秩27–47 vs Adam的6–23，梯度谱更平坦；漂移上界经验验证比率RMS(Δz)/ε均值接近1，严格缩放变体p99=1.09

Conclusion: 谱更新几何在CIFAR MLP局部学习基准上同时消除了深度退化和超参数脆弱性两大历史成本。单一数值（3e-3）无论解读为固定谱步长还是漂移预算，均在16×宽度范围和4×深度范围内有效。漂移契约为标准优化器提供了独有的性质：每层、输入条件化的行为变化上界。对自主重训练系统，可一次按域校准ε，随后跨规模/深度复用。对于分散训练，优化层面的两大瓶颈（深度退化、逐节点调优）已被解除，但前向传播延迟和语言模型规模下局部损失质量仍是开放问题。局部学习与契约的结合呈现出其结构本质：由简单双边协议约束的一组自主工作者。

Abstract: Local learning trains each layer with its own auxiliary loss and no global backward pass, which makes layer updates structurally parallel. Two problems have kept it marginal: accuracy degrades as depth grows, and hyperparameters are fragile. We apply Muon-style spectral update geometry (momentum orthogonalization with spectral step scaling) to per-layer local updates, an intersection not previously studied. On CIFAR-10 MLP benchmarks with local linear heads, a single step-size setting is the best value in our tested grids from width 128 to 2048 and from depth 12 to 48, while local Adam requires re-tuning along both axes and still collapses at depth 48 (31.3 percent re-tuned per depth, 19 percent with its depth-12 setting transferred, vs 42.7 percent for the spectral update at its unchanged setting). At five seeds and width 512 the spectral update leads local Adam by a clear margin (48.9 +/- 0.5 vs 46.6 +/- 0.3). Prospectively specified controls attribute the transfer and most of the depth robustness to the spectral geometry itself rather than to any step-size rule on top of it. We additionally formulate the step size as a drift contract, lr = epsilon / RMS(input), which bounds each layer's weight-induced pre-activation change per step, conditioned on its current input. The contract yields a small gain over the best fixed learning rate where that baseline is measured, makes the step size interpretable, and provides a per-layer, input-conditioned drift bound that standard optimizers do not offer. We report one negative result: with RMSNorm and weight decay in the trunk, the stability benefit of spectral updates accrues to global rather than local training, so the local advantage concentrates precisely where normalization is absent.

</details>


### [115] [HARN: Hierarchical Associative Resonance Network for Event-Driven Multi-Timeframe Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26822)
*Nabeel Ahmad Saidd*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HARN（Hierarchical Associative Resonance Network），一种面向金融多时间框架的事件驱动预测架构。核心创新在于形式化"已完成K线对齐"协议与异步状态更新机制，而非追求普适预测优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融时间序列在不同时间分辨率下异步完成K线（如5分钟K线收线时，对应的1小时K线可能尚未成型）。标准序列模型在固定窗口上推进状态，无法区分"新可用信息"与"未变化的表示"，容易引入前瞻泄露并产生冗余计算。现有方法缺乏同时规定：每个时间框架的完成事件、非激活高层状态的保留、事件内跨层通信、以及显式因果排序的统一协议。

Method: 1) **已完成K线对齐协议**：以最细粒度为锚定事件流，定义更新指示符 u=1{选中时间戳等于锚定时间戳}，通过二元掩码实现"未更新则保持原状态"的精确恒等转换（Eq.2/4）；2) **因果多尺度编码器**：深度可分离膨胀因果卷积（左填充，膨胀率{1,2,4,8}）+ 学习分支混合 + 衰减池化，输出72维表示；3) **门控联想记忆**：键-值-查询投影，计算检索残差 s=v−Mk（"surprise"），通过 tanh 有界外积写入矩阵记忆 (B,72,18)，门控融合记忆与直接通路；4) **跨尺度共振**：层级轴上的全连接注意力（Eq.20-22），先验与后验各应用一次；5) **自底向上证据**：相邻层间注意力+最近读数加权（系数0.35/15为人工设定），建立有序同事件路径；6) **BPS目标**：在基点变化空间训练，重建至绝对价格评估。

Result: 在四个资产（AAPL、EURUSD、USDCHF、XAUUSD）上，HARN重建价格误差数值上低于单时间框架的PatchTST和TimeXer基线（如AAPL MAE: 0.508 vs 2.074/2.882；EURUSD MAE: 0.000306 vs 0.000759/0.010838）。但作者明确强调该比较为"描述性、范围特定"，因HARN与基线在信息访问、目标表述（BPS vs 归一化绝对价格）、训练协议（状态流 vs 无状态洗牌、余弦调度 vs 常学习率、patience 15 vs 5、AMP开/关）上均不同，未隔离架构效应。消融研究（A1替换联想记忆为门控循环、A2去除共振、A3置零证据）为单次运行，差异小且非单调，无法确定各组件独立贡献。代码级审计（14项检查）与事件级因果契约一致，但存在同事件证据排序限定。

Conclusion: HARN的主要贡献是**形式化并实现了一个基于已完成K线对齐、异步更新、持久跨层状态的预测协议**，而非声明通用预测优越性。在报告配置下实现"可行"的多时间框架预测，但：1) 基线对比非受控架构实验，差异来源（层级、目标、训练配方）无法归因；2) 单次消融不具统计稳健性；3) 缺少持久性基线、置信区间、配对检验（如Diebold-Mariano）；4) 三种子间极小方差未获解释；5) 手工设定的证据系数未经敏感性分析。作者将HARN定位为"持久多时间框架预测框架"而非"普适优越性的证据"，并提出八项后续工作（含实证扰动测试、容量匹配消融、配对统计检验等）。

Abstract: Financial time series evolve across multiple temporal resolutions, challenging forecasting systems to incorporate newly available information without repeatedly recomputing unchanged representations. We introduce HARN, a Hierarchical Associative Resonance Network for event-driven multi-timeframe forecasting. HARN maintains persistent representations across temporal levels and updates each level only when its corresponding completed bar becomes available. The architecture combines causal multi-scale temporal encoding, gated associative memory, cross-level resonance, and hierarchical evidence aggregation, with forecasting performed in basis-point space and reconstructed to the original price scale. We evaluate HARN on four assets spanning equity, foreign exchange, and commodity markets using multiple random seeds and component ablations. HARN achieves competitive reconstructed-price forecasting errors against single-timeframe PatchTST and TimeXer baselines, while ablations reveal the effects of removing individual components across assets and timeframes. A code-level audit further examines consistency between the implementation and the defined event-driven causal protocol. The results position HARN as a persistent multi-timeframe forecasting framework rather than evidence of universal predictive superiority.

</details>


### [116] [Marginally Correct Tool Caches Can Reverse Group-Normalized Policy Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26866)
*Shivam Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过一个简单的两动作模型证明：当工具缓存共享每个组内同一随机结果时，即使每个rollout的条件奖励边际分布不变，组归一化策略更新方向可能被反转；仅靠均值居中可避免此类反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具结果缓存是降低智能体训练成本的重要工程手段，但其安全性通常仅以"状态一致性"或"边际输出有效性"来证明。然而对于组相对策略优化(如GRPO)这类在同一组rollout内部进行比较的学习器，缓存改变了组内随机结果的联合分布结构，可能破坏梯度更新的期望方向。该研究旨在以可审计的方式严格回答："若缓存保持每个rollout的条件奖励分布，是否必然保持组归一化更新的期望？"。

Method: 1) 构建两动作观察者模型：动作A返回常数c，动作B返回Y~Bern(q)，由logit θ参数化；2) 定义两种执行法则（独立执行 vs 组内共享单次采样），分析其对U₀（标准化更新）与V（仅居中更新）期望与方差的影响；3) 推导闭式定理（Theorem 1–4）与推论，包括反方向区间、持久方差下限、稳定化阈值分析；4) 通过穷举有限和（540配置×2执行模式×3估计量=3240次评估）验证；5) 对TVCache提交3a4f95a6582e进行pinned运行时审计（256次脚本化rollout），复现共享路径。

Result: 1) Theorem 1：组内共享下U₀ = w_G(N)·sgn(Y−c)，期望 = S_G(p)·[Pr(Y>c)−Pr(Y<c)]，只取决于"赢"的频率而非期望收益差；2) Corollary 2：在Bernoulli特化下，当½<q<c<1或0<c<q<½时，共享归一化更新方向与J'相反，而独立执行更新方向正确；3) Proposition 3：仅居中估计V在两种法则下都保持(1−1/G)p(1−p)(μ−c)的期望；4) Theorem 4：当G→∞时，共享更新方差收敛到4q(1−q)p(1−p)非零常数，独立执行方差收敛到0；5) 预选见证点(p=0.5,c=0.9,q=0.8,G=64)：独立归一化更新=−0.078867，共享归一化更新=+0.297628，居中更新=−0.024609；6) TVCache运行时审计成功复现共享路径（共享身份模式下2次物理调用 vs 直接执行32次）。

Conclusion: 边际输出有效性不足以证明随机缓存与训练等价。在组相对策略优化下，共享同一随机工具结果可将学习器从"最大化期望收益"重塑为"偏向中位数/排序"的优化问题。实用结论明确：(1)审计训练优化需指定执行法则与所服务估计量；(2)物理工具调用数与rollout数应分开计量；(3)Dr.GRPO类的仅居中估计器在此模型下保持期望梯度方向，但仍有梯度噪声代价。本文未否定TVCache的确定性输出契约，未测量生产部署的频率或实际训练回归。

Abstract: Tool-result caching reduces repeated execution in agent training, but also couples rollout randomness. We study a two-action model in which independent and shared execution preserve every rollout's conditional reward distribution. Despite this marginal agreement, sharing one stochastic result per group can reverse the expected group-normalized policy update. We derive an exact finite-group expression: against a constant alternative, the shared update follows the probability of winning minus the probability of losing, rather than the difference in expected reward. A Bernoulli specialization yields a wrong-direction region and a non-vanishing update-variance floor as group size grows. Centering without group standard-deviation scaling preserves the expected-return direction in this model, using an existing estimator control. Exhaustive finite sums verify 540 configurations and 3,240 estimator evaluations, with a separate ordered-sequence checker. An implementation audit reproduces the sharing path in a pinned, unmodified TVCache stack using 256 scripted rollouts. These results do not measure language-model training performance or refute TVCache's deterministic-output contract. They establish that marginal output validity alone cannot certify a stochastic cache as training-equivalent.

</details>


### [117] [PR-Smoother: Simulator-Preserving Non-Gaussian Smoothing for Data Assimilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26890)
*Yuta Tarumi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PR-Smoother是一种针对"预设模拟器数据同化"(prescribed-simulator DA)场景的摊销变分平滑器，核心设计原则是保持预设动力学和观测算子在ELBO和变分族中显式存在，仅在模拟器滚动周围学习未来条件修正，从而在物理空间中表示非高斯平滑后验，并支持联合状态、参数和传感器偏置学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理数据同化(DA)工作流需要能够表示非高斯后验、可扩展到高维模拟器、仅从观测窗口训练且与预设模拟器标定兼容的平滑方法。现有方法存在权衡：保留模拟器的方法（如4D-Var、EnKF）通常依赖高斯分析或窗口MAP估计，在多对一或强非线性观测下性能不佳；而获得更丰富后验灵活性的方法通常将推理移至学习的潜空间或学习轨迹先验，放弃了对预设物理算子的标定能力。

Method: PR-Smoother将初始状态后验q_φ(X_1|Y_{1:T})用条件归一化流(NF)建模，将转移q_φ(X_t|X_{t-1},Y_{t:T})参数化为以f_θ(X_{t-1})为中心的未来条件高斯修正。通过镜像平滑器因式分解，同时保持(f_θ, h_θ)在ELBO中显式可微，提供了物理轨迹上的显式非高斯密度。确定性变分族的转移项在ELBO中相消；含噪变分族添加未来感知高斯修正。论文证明了该变分族在确定性动力学极限和线性高斯SSM极限下包含精确平滑后验。

Result: 在三组实验中验证：(1) 4维Lorenz-96中，flow模型准确捕捉平方观测h(x)=x²导致的双峰后验，随观测积累正确消歧；(2) 40维Lorenz-96中，在线性/非线性(含min(x²,5))/含噪动力学三种设定下，Noisyflow变体RMSE最低，且能恢复真实过程噪声水平(lnσ_Q=-2.29±0.04 vs 真实-2.30)；(3) 16,384维Kolmogorov流中，在"full"观测下状态RMSE(0.28)低于观测噪声水平并匹配oracle 4D-Var，能联合学习16,386个参数(状态+偏置+物理参数F和Re)，"realistic"混合覆盖下dense区域识别(F, logRe)并通过标定动力学传播到稀疏区域。摊销推理速度远快于4D-Var(快数十至数百倍)。

Conclusion: PR-Smoother通过仅学习预设滚动周围的未来条件修正，实现了物理空间中灵活的非高斯平滑后验，同时保持预设动力学和观测算子在观测唯一ELBO中显式存在，支持联合状态-参数-偏置学习。变分族在确定性和线性高斯极限下包含精确后验。该方法在Lorenz-96和16K维Kolmogorov流上得到验证，能捕捉多峰后验并扩展到高维非线性同化，对科学数据同化工作流中不确定性感知状态估计和参数标定具有积极影响。
