Abstract: Many physical data assimilation (DA) workflows require smoothing methods that represent non-Gaussian posteriors over physical state variables, scale to high-dimensional simulators, train from observation windows alone, and remain compatible with calibration of the prescribed simulator. We introduce PR-Smoother, a simulator-preserving amortized smoother designed for this prescribed-simulator DA regime. Its key design principle is to keep the prescribed simulator explicit in both the evidence lower bound and the variational family: rather than learning replacement dynamics or a learned trajectory prior, PR-Smoother learns only future-conditioned corrections around the prescribed rollout. This yields an explicit non-Gaussian smoothing distribution over physical trajectories and supports joint state, parameter, and sensor-bias learning from observations alone. The variational family contains the exact smoother in deterministic and linear-Gaussian limits. Empirically, PR-Smoother captures multimodal posteriors in 4-dimensional Lorenz-96, remains accurate under ambiguous nonlinear observations and process noise in 40-dimensional Lorenz-96, and scales to joint state-parameter-bias inference in 16,384-dimensional Kolmogorov flow.

</details>


### [118] [CORE-STACK+: Meta-Learning for Deep Stacked Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26905)
*Noor Islam S. Mohammad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CORE-STACK+ 提出了一种用于异构视觉骨干网络堆叠泛化的预处理流水线，通过四个核心组件（CKA 核化冗余过滤、可微分元特征门、谱自适应 Ridge 惩罚、Laplace 近似贝叶斯混合器）联合解决预测空间多重共线性和校准崩溃两大病态问题，在六个视觉基准上同时提升精度、校准和鲁棒性，同时减少保留模型数量和推理 FLOPs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构视觉骨干网络（CNN、ViT、混合模型）的堆叠集成虽已成为提升性能的标准做法，但面临两个耦合的病态问题：(1) 预测空间多重共线性导致元学习器的 Gram 矩阵严重病态，使解不稳定；(2) 朴素线性堆叠导致校准崩溃，添加更多模型反而恶化 ECE。现有方法（岭正则化、贪心选择、模型汤、SWAG）至多解决其中一个，无法联合处理异构预测池中的条件化和校准问题。

Method: 提出 CORE-STACK+ 预处理流水线，包含四个组件：(S1) 使用 Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA) 替代 Pearson 相关性进行核化冗余过滤，检测非线性模型间依赖（Nyström 近似加速）；(S2) <15K 参数的可微分元特征门，学习样本级注意力以加权集成统计量；(S3) 基于 Marchenko-Pastur 信噪分解的谱自适应 Ridge 惩罚 λ*=λmax(Ĉ)/SNR(Ĉ)，消除嵌套交叉验证；(S4) Laplace 近似贝叶斯混合器替代逆 RMSE 启发式。理论证明包括：Proposition 3.1（CKA 严格优于 Pearson）、Proposition 3.2（谱自适应最优 Ridge 惩罚）、Theorem 3.3（首个联合考虑预测空间冗余和元学习器容量的 PAC-Bayes 风险边界）。

Result: 在六个基准测试上的表现：ImageNet-1K 分类 top-1 +1.8%（85.4% vs 83.9% 简单平均），ECE 降至 0.018（2.1× 优于深度集成无需温度缩放）；ImageNet-C 鲁棒性 mCE 降低 4.2 点；ADE20K 语义分割 mIoU +0.9（53.0）；COCO 检测 AP +1.3（49.7）；iNaturalist-2021 长尾识别 +1.1%（74.2%），尾部类 +2.3%；DomainNet-126 域迁移 +2.1%。效率方面：保留模型减少 35-57%，推理 FLOPs 减少达 41%，吞吐量从 312 img/s 提升至 528 img/s（1.69×）。消融实验显示各组件贡献：S1 提供 +0.5% 和 28% 条件数降低；S3 提供 3.2× 训练加速；S4 提供最大校准改善（ECE 0.023→0.018）。

Conclusion: CORE-STACK+ 将堆叠集成中的每个启发式替换为有原则的替代方案，结果表明该方法在精度、校准和鲁棒性方面均达到最优，同时保留更少模型并降低推理成本。理论上的首个边界先验条件化边界，约束 KL 惩罚通过 Gram 谱直接控制冗余减少，将集成元学习重新框架为条件化问题。作者认为核心挑战不在于聚合函数本身，而在于确保输入预测矩阵的良好条件。论文还指出所有代码和 OOF 矩阵已发布以确保可复现性。

Abstract: Stacking heterogeneous vision backbones (CNNs, ViTs, and hybrids) is the de facto recipe for accuracy, calibration, and robustness, yet two coupled pathologies limit its returns. Prediction-space multicollinearity ill-conditions the meta-learner's Gram matrix, inflating weight variance and producing brittle solutions on a thin manifold. Calibration collapse compounds constituent miscalibration through naive linear stacking, so adding more models can hurt expected calibration error (ECE). Existing remedies, ridge regularization, greedy selection, model soups, and SWAG address at most one of these issues, and none jointly target conditioning and calibration in heterogeneous prediction pools. We introduce CORE-STACK+, a preconditioning pipeline with four components: (i) a kernelized redundancy filter that removes non-linear inter-model dependencies invisible to Pearson correlation, using Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA) [23]; (ii) a $<15$K-parameter differentiable meta-feature gate that learns per-sample attention over ensemble statistics; (iii) a spectrum-adaptive Ridge penalty $lambda^{star}=lmax(Chat)/SNR(Chat)$ derived from a Marchenko-Pastur signal-noise decomposition, eliminating nested cross-validation; and (iv) a Laplace-approximate Bayesian blender replacing inverse-RMSE heuristics. We prove a PAC-Bayes excess-risk bound that, for the first time, jointly accounts for prediction-space redundancy and meta-learner capacity. Across six benchmarks, CORE-STACK+ delivers $+1.8\%$ top-1 on ImageNet-1K, $-4.2$ mCE on ImageNet-C, $+0.9$ mIoU on ADE20K, and $+1.3$ AP on COCO, while reducing retained models by 35-57% and inference FLOPs by up to $41%$. ECE improves $2.1\times$ over deep ensembles without post hoc temperature scaling.

</details>


### [119] [When Post-Processing Fairness Constraints Help and When They Harm: Evidence from Eight Cross-Domain Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26955)
*Nithin Raghava Ramachandra Narla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FAPE（Fairness Auditing for Production Environments）框架，针对生产环境中ML模型公平性审计的局限性，对单一后处理干预（Fairlearn的ThresholdOptimizer）在8个异构领域中进行四阶段评估，并探索持续监控方法，以证明单次部署审计的不可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统生产ML的公平性审计通常仅在部署时对单一领域执行一次，这在实践中存在两大失败：(1)公平性可能在重新训练或用户群体变化后发生偏移；(2)在单一数据集上验证有效的干预措施很少在组织部署的异构领域中进行测试。因此需要更系统、跨领域的审计框架和持续监控机制。

Method: 提出FAPE四阶段框架：(1)评估Fairlearn的ThresholdOptimizer后处理干预；(2)跨8个领域（刑事司法、收入预测、法律招生、信贷放款、农业放款、多领域基准语料库、医疗、教育）进行评估；(3)使用人口统计均等性差异和均等化赔率差异指标评分，并在可计算时辅以差异影响比和准确度成本；(4)提出CUSUM监控器在部署时启动，测试模拟移位下能否区分满足0.1均等性约定的模型与随后回归的模型，并采用最小组规模和保留数据阈值拟合两种测量检查。

Result: 跨模型-领域对的干预效果跟踪基线差异程度：在9/14的高差异案例中约束改善了差异，但在3/4的近乎公平的案例中反而恶化了差异。5个高差异例外中的每一个在两种测量检查之一下都会逆转；CUSUM监控器成功区分了从未达到0.1均等性约定的约束模型与达到后回归的模型。

Conclusion: 单次部署时审计是不可靠的；研究主张采用基线差异筛选和持续监控机制，以确保生产环境中ML模型的公平性。

Abstract: Fairness audits in production ML typically occur once, at deployment, on a single domain. Both fail in practice: fairness can shift after retraining or a changing user base, and interventions validated on one dataset are rarely tested across the heterogeneous domains an organization deploys. We present FAPE (Fairness Auditing for Production Environments), a four-stage framework evaluating a single post-processing intervention, Fairlearn's ThresholdOptimizer, across eight domain evaluations: criminal justice, income prediction, legal admissions, credit lending, agricultural lending, a multi-domain benchmark corpus, healthcare, and education. Each is scored on demographic parity and equalized odds difference, plus disparate impact ratio and accuracy cost where computable. Intervention effectiveness tracks baseline disparity magnitude: across model-domain pairs the constraint improved disparity in 9 of 14 high-disparity cases and worsened it in 3 of 4 near-fair ones. Each of the five high-disparity exceptions reverses under one of two measurement checks, a minimum group size or thresholds fit on held-out data. A CUSUM monitor started at deployment, tested on a simulated shift, separates constrained models that never met a 0.1 parity convention from those that met it and later regressed. A single deployment-time audit is therefore an unreliable guide, which argues for baseline-disparity screening and continuous monitoring

</details>


### [120] [Transfer Learning with Conformalized Quantile Regression for Solar PV Forecasting Under Load-Shedding-Driven Data Scarcity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26959)
*Rakib Abdullah,K. M. Tahlil Mahfuz Faruk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对发展中地区因负荷切除导致历史数据稀缺而难以进行可靠日前光伏预测的问题，提出将LSTM迁移学习（以澳大利亚DKASC Alice Springs数据为源域）与保形分位数回归（CQR）相结合的框架，在模拟的孟加拉国10 kWp屋顶微网场景下验证。结果显示：仅1个月目标数据时迁移学习降低RMSE 23.7%（并使预测技巧从负转正），3个月时降低13.7%；3个月时Transfer+CQR实现94.3%经验覆盖率且预测区间比Scratch+CQR窄14%，而Scratch+CQR仅达84.2%覆盖。研究还通过小时级诊断发现晨间斜升时段是覆盖率最薄弱的窗口，并指出源域与目标域气候/半球差异会引起黎明时刻的系统性误校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展中国家新部署的光伏微网（如孟加拉国）面临双重难题：①新装机站点历史观测严重稀缺；②负荷切除（load shedding）导致白天高辐照时段出现结构性数据缺失——这既破坏模型训练，也破坏CQR等保形方法所依赖的交换性假设。先前工作要么假定干净连续的目标域数据，要么未在迁移学习框架下系统考察由停电导致的非随机缺失对点预测与概率校准的耦合影响。

Method: ①数据管线：用pvlib+NASA POWER合成孟加拉国达卡地区10 kWp屋顶系统2023年小时级发电数据，并依据BPDB停电统计构造以小时为粒度的Bernoulli掩膜（13:00–17:00 p≈0.33最高）以引入结构化缺失；测试集（2023年12月）保持干净。②模型：共享LSTM骨干（24小时滑窗 + 4个工程特征，α=0.05/0.95分位头），在DKASC Alice Springs（148 537小时）上预训练后微调，对比1个月/3个月预算下Scratch与Transfer两套共4个变体。③不确定性：在各模型输出的分位区间上后置施加CQR（目标90%），并按小时分层诊断覆盖率。

Result: 在2023年12月744小时干净测试集上：点预测RMSE——Scratch 1m=0.659 kW、Transfer 1m=0.503 kW（↓23.7%）、Scratch 3m=0.488 kW、Transfer 3m=0.421 kW（↓13.7%）；1个月Scratch技巧分FSS=−0.033（劣于24h持续性），Transfer=+0.212。CQR（90%名义）：3个月Scratch+CQR覆盖率仅84.17%（违约），Transfer+CQR=94.31%且区间宽度1.98 kW，比Scratch的2.31 kW窄14%；1个月时两者均严重过覆盖（98%+）且区间过宽。小时诊断显示清晨07–09点斜升时段为最薄弱区间，Transfer在06:00出现0%覆盖，揭示南北半球黎明时差导致的系统误校准。

Conclusion: 迁移学习在数据极稀缺场景（1个月）下贡献最显著，RMSE改善幅度接近既往全年预算研究的两倍；同时，预训练表征间接稳定了CQR校准，使覆盖率与区间宽度同时改善，但收益随目标数据量增加而递减。结构性缺失使保形交换性假设受违，对Scratch模型尤其严重；小时级诊断指向晨间斜升为最脆弱窗口，并提示需引入晴空模型修正与在线自适应保形方法。主要局限在于目标域为模拟数据且源域单一，未来需在印度IIEST Shibpur实测站点及在线自适应CQR上做外部验证。

Abstract: Solar photovoltaic (PV) forecasting in regions affected by load shedding is challenging because reliable historical observations are scarce. This study proposes a transfer learning framework combined with Conformalized Quantile Regression (CQR) to improve PV power forecasting and provide reliable uncertainty estimates under severe data scarcity. A source-domain PV dataset from Alice Springs, Australia, is used to pretrain a temporal forecasting model, which is then adapted to simulated Bangladesh PV data representing different levels of historical availability. Experimental results show that transfer learning reduces RMSE by up to 23.7% when only one month of target-domain data is available and by 13.7% with three months of data. The proposed Transfer Learning plus CQR framework achieves 94.3% empirical coverage with three months of target data while producing prediction intervals that are 14% narrower than those obtained without transfer learning. These results demonstrate that combining transfer learning with conformal uncertainty quantification can improve both point forecasting accuracy and uncertainty reliability when target-domain PV data are severely limited.

</details>


### [121] [CRISP: Scalable Importance-Stratified Coresets for Imbalanced Tabular Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26962)
*Hardhik Mohanty,Indrayana Rustandi,Mohamadreza Sheibani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CRISP提出了一种线性时间的核心集选择方法,通过代理模型评分分位数分层、按平均重要性分数分配负类预算,并用逆概率加权修正非等概率抽样带来的分布偏移,在高度类别不平衡的表格数据上,在极高负类削减率下显著优于Random、CCS和CoreTab等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模不平衡表格数据(如欺诈检测)的反复梯度提升树训练成本高昂。现有核心集方法在移除大量多数类样本时常常损失精度,直接重要性抽样在高削减率下会丢失评分覆盖,非等概率抽样又会改变有效训练分布,需要一种兼顾覆盖、分配和加权修正的方法。

Method: 1)保留全部正例,仅削减负类;2)在固定大小子样本上训练轻量代理GBDT得到负例重要性评分;3)用等数量分位数将负例分成Q层,避免分布塌缩;4)按每层平均分数按比例分配负类预算k_q;5)层内以Bernoulli抽样(可选按分数比例或均匀),被选负例赋予截断逆概率权重w_i=min{1/π_i, w_max};6)实现采用Spark分布式,评分、分位、聚合、抽样均为线性复杂度。理论上证明了未截断IPW估计量在条件设计下无偏,并给出截断带来的偏差界。

Result: 在生产欺诈数据集(25M行,1.9%正例)95%负类削减下,CRISP用约1.70M行(总行数削减93.2%)保留99.7%的月度全量AP和99.2%的周度AP,而Random和CCS分别仅保留约94%;在CriteoPrivateAds(85M行,0.77%正例)90%-99.4%各削减率下CRISP均取得最高均值AP,优势随削减率增大而扩大;Sparkov结果不稳定,但CRISP在99.2%和99.4%最高;消融显示分层(Q=10 vs Q=1)和IPW加权是生产增益的主要来源,代理模型容量影响较小;跨LightGBM/XGBoost迁移保持优势。

Conclusion: CRISP在高负类削减率场景下通过"分位数分层+按重要性分配预算+逆概率加权"的组合,实现了线性时间的可复用核心集选择,在生产数据和公开广告基准上均显著优于均匀采样和CCS等基线;但其不是端到端运行时间证明,Sparkov结果显示出在低预算下方法排名不稳定,实际部署仍需固定超参数、子群体公平性检查和完整的计时评估。

Abstract: Large imbalanced tabular datasets make repeated gradient-boosted tree training expensive. Existing coreset methods often lose accuracy when most majority examples are removed. We present CRISP (Coreset Reduction via Importance-Stratified Pruning), a linear-time method that allocates a negative-class budget across quantile strata of a proxy-model score. Sample weights account for unequal inclusion probabilities. At 95% negative-class reduction on a production fraud dataset, CRISP trains on approximately 1.70M of 25M rows and retains 99.7% of full-data Average Precision. This is a 93.2% reduction in total training rows. On public CriteoPrivateAds, CRISP has the highest mean Average Precision at each tested rate from 90% to 99.4% majority reduction. Sparkov results are mixed at lower rates, but CRISP has the highest mean at 99.2% and 99.4%. Ablations identify budget allocation and inverse-propensity weighting as the main sources of the production-dataset gain.

</details>


### [122] [TinyUDE: Solver-Free Universal Differential Equations on Microcontrollers via Lie-Taylor Jet Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26972)
*Pranavanath Balamurali,Hrishi Kamireddy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Lie-Taylor jet matching方法,一种无ODE求解器的Universal Differential Equations (UDE) 训练框架,适用于微控制器上的在线训练。该方法通过Savitzky-Golay滤波估计系统状态的时间导数,将混合向量场直接拟合到一阶和二阶导数上,产生解析梯度而无需自动微分软件。通过全速率相位采样、储水池缓冲、余弦退火优化、噪声自适应滤波等噪声适应机制,在阻尼单摆和混沌双摆系统上验证,在108 kB静态内存(ESP32上61.3 kB)内达到或超过传统伴随方法基线的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统UDE训练依赖对数值ODE求解器进行反向传播,产生需要兆字节级存储的中间状态(磁带),远超边缘微控制器的能力(如ESP32仅有约320 kB SRAM)。此外,嵌入式数值积分在优化循环中与求解器稳定性紧耦合,参数瞬态偏移会导致中间轨迹发散。本文目标是研究能否完全消除ODE求解器和中间反向传播磁带,在资源受限的边缘设备上实现实时在线训练,同时不牺牲模型精度。

Method: 方法核心是Lie-Taylor jet matching:1)利用Savitzky-Golay滤波在线估计系统状态的一阶和二阶时间导数(截断Lie-Taylor jet);2)定义一阶和二阶残差损失函数,将混合向量场 F_θ(x) = f_known(x) + N_θ(x) 拟合到估计导数;3)利用链式规则得ẍ = J_{F_θ}(x)·F_θ(x),推导完全解析的梯度(仅需VJP和reverse-over-JVP两个原语),无需自动微分或ODE求解器。噪声适应机制包括:全速率相位采样(以步长s=5滑动滤波窗口,保留m/s≈50倍独立导数目标)、储水池采样缓冲(2048-4096样本)、Adam+余弦退火+EMA权重平均、片上噪声估计(从拟合残差估计σ²,经验误差0.04%)、自适应时间尺度选择(Δt*∝σ^{1/5}/ω_c)、二阶导数SNR门控(τ_SNR=12)、多项式失配质量门控(剔除违反局部多项式假设的窗口)。测试系统为阻尼单摆(Case A/B/C三种UDE配置)和混沌阻尼双摆。基线为固定步RK4、多重打靶、精确离散伴随法的全批量Adam。

Result: 在阻尼单摆0%-5%噪声范围6个测试条件下,j匹配在5/6条件下获得更低的平均场误差,相对基线的几何平均误差比为0.65×(vs K=80基线为0.82×)。1%噪声下单摆场误差0.0022 vs 基线0.0028;5%噪声下0.0064 vs 基线0.0248。混沌双摆1%噪声下0.0115 vs 基线0.0137。单摆阻尼系数恢复精度0.388-0.399(真实值0.40),双摆0.141(真实0.150)。8秒轨迹预测误差0.0068,较裸物理模型降低393×。静态内存108.1 kB(基线5.9-11.8 MB磁带),降低55-109×。ESP32片上实测(B=2048):静态内存61.3 kB,场误差0.0020,阻尼恢复ĉ=0.400,每次更新7.24 ms(25 Hz下18.1%占空比)。梯度精度与复步微分验证达机器精度(10^-13)。主要限制:0.1%极低噪声下伴随法仍优1.8×;混沌系统快速状态转换时4阶多项式滤波偶发失效,质量门控可将峰值场误差从0.135降至0.025。

Conclusion: 本文证实,通过噪声自适应Lie-Taylor jet matching,可完全消除数值ODE求解器和反向传播磁带,在不牺牲精度前提下将UDE训练内存需求降低约两个数量级。该方法使在线物理信息模型学习在边缘微控制器上可行,使用静态分配的108 kB内存即可在ESP32等设备上实现实时训练。未来工作包括:扩展片上测量至完整噪声扫描和双摆系统、评估非高斯噪声/传感器延迟/量化效应、探索可变窗口长度和动态滤波器阶数以处理混沌系统中的快速状态转换。

Abstract: Training Universal Differential Equations (UDEs) traditionally relies on backpropagating through numerical ODE solvers, creating memory footprints far exceeding the capabilities of edge microcontrollers. We present Lie-Taylor jet matching, a solver-free training framework that fits a hybrid vector field directly to the first and second time-derivatives of observed system states. These derivatives, the truncated Lie-Taylor jet, are estimated online via Savitzky-Golay filtering, yielding fully analytic gradients without automatic differentiation software. We evaluate whether eliminating the solver compromises accuracy against a conventional baseline (fixed-step RK4 integration, multiple shooting, exact discrete adjoints, Adam) sharing identical dynamics, noise models, network architectures, and metrics. While naive derivative matching degrades under sensor noise, our noise-adaptive mechanisms close and reverse this gap: full-rate phase-shifted sampling, a reservoir buffer, cosine-annealed optimization with weight averaging, on-device noise estimation, and polynomial-misfit quality gating. On a damped pendulum and chaotic double pendulum, our method matches or exceeds baseline accuracy at matched data windows and recovers unmodeled damping coefficients. Across noise levels from 0% to 5%, it attains a geometric-mean relative field error of 0.65x that of the baseline within 108 kB of static memory, compared with megabytes of solver tape. On an ESP32 microcontroller, the on-device run reaches a field error of 0.0020 and recovers the damping coefficient to c = 0.400 (true 0.400) within 61.3 kB of static memory and 7.24 ms per update (18.1% duty cycle at 25 Hz), confirming real-time on-device training is feasible without a numerical solver.

</details>


### [123] [Resource-Efficient Distributed Recursive Gaussian Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26979)
*Josephine King,Ali Emre Balci,Raj Thilak Rajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种用于多智能体多输出高斯过程回归的分布式递归GP算法（ADMM-RGP和PDMM-RGP），通过通信高效的分布式优化框架显著降低了通信开销，同时保持与现有方法相当的估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多智能体系统中，集中式GP方法存在单点故障和通信开销大的问题，而现有的分布式GP方法（如Consensus-RGP）通信效率较低。本文旨在开发通信高效的分布式递归GP算法，减少智能体间的通信负担。

Method: 将分布式递归GP问题表述为信息形式的分布式优化问题，分别采用ADMM和PDMM两种分布式优化框架求解。对信息向量和信息矩阵分别设计分布式更新规则，并通过图拉普拉斯矩阵实现局部通信。分析了算法的收敛性并提出了参数选择策略。

Result: 在真实风电场数据上的实验表明：ADMM-RGP在密集通信图中表现优异，通信量比Consensus-RGP减少30%（K=7 vs K=10）；PDMM-RGP在稀疏通信图中表现最佳，通信量减少50%（K=5 vs K=10）。两种方法都能达到与Consensus-RGP相当的RMSE（0.0862）。

Conclusion: 提出的ADMM-RGP和PDMM-RGP算法在通信效率和估计精度方面均优于现有的Consensus-RGP方法，分别适用于密集和稀疏通信拓扑。未来工作将考虑异步通信、时变函数和图结构，以及扩展到其他GP表示（如随机特征方法）。

Abstract: Gaussian processes (GPs) provide a flexible framework for learning unknown functions from noisy measurements while quantifying predictive uncertainty, making them well suited for estimation in multi-agent systems. However, when measurements are collected by multiple agents, maintaining a unified GP model without centralized processing requires efficient distributed algorithms that can operate using local measurements and communication with neighboring agents. In this work, we develop two distributed recursive GP (RGP) algorithms for multi-output GP regression: ADMM-RGP and PDMM-RGP. We analyze the stability and convergence of both algorithms and develop parameter selection strategies to accelerate convergence, thus reducing the communication burden. The proposed methods are validated on a real-world multi-output wind dataset, and their convergence behavior is examined across communication graphs with varying connectivity. Numerical experiments demonstrate that ADMM-RGP and PDMM-RGP can significantly reduce communication relative to the state of the art, while maintaining comparable estimation accuracy and network-wide consensus.

</details>


### [124] [GeoRVQ: Decoder-aware geometry for residual-token prediction in physiological signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27018)
*Bo Cui,Yaowen Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GeoRVQ 是一种用于生理信号（ECG/PPG）的粗到细掩码 token 模型，其目标函数融合了解码器感知的代价与量化器因果结构。它通过预计算"解码器诱导代价"来构造软目标和期望失真，并按 RVQ 层级从粗到细预测。在 MIMIC-IV Waveform、VitalDB 和 CODE-15% 上，解码距离降低 35.2%，R 波 F1 从 0.784 提升到 0.837，而精确 token 准确率仅小幅提高（0.133→0.143）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的掩码 token 模型（如交叉熵）对所有错误 token 一视同仁，但 RVQ 不同层级和不同码字替换对解码波形的影响差异显著；同时 RVQ 层级具有由粗到细的内在结构，并行预测所有层级会过早估计细节而粗尺度信号尚未解析。该工作旨在通过对齐训练目标与解码后果来提升波形和事件保真度。

Method: 1) 使用冻结的 RVQ 神经编解码器将 ECG/PPG 分层离散化；2) 对每个码字在训练上下文上采样，估计上下文平均的"解码器诱导代价" d_ℓ(i,j)，结合波形、导数和事件邻域误差；3) 用 K 近邻构建 softmax 软目标，并定义期望失真损失；4) 量化器因果头先预测粗层 q1，再以条件方式预测更深层；5) 总损失 = 硬标签交叉熵 + 软目标交叉熵 + 期望解码代价。

Result: 在 MIMIC-IV Waveform、VitalDB、CODE-15% 描述性汇总下：精确 token 准确率 0.133→0.143；解码距离 0.606→0.393（-35.2%）；PCC 0.969→0.989；R 波 F1 0.784→0.837；PSD 误差 0.083→0.041。在 45 个保留码字替换上，解码器诱导代价与实际解码代价的 Spearman 相关 0.85，欧氏码字距离仅 0.54。消融：去掉解码器几何使解码距离升至 0.502、R-F1 降至 0.807；去掉量化器因果使 R 波时间 MAE 由 9.2 ms 增至 9.7 ms。

Conclusion: 将掩码 RVQ-token 学习与替换的解码后果对齐、同时遵循由粗到细的残差依赖，可在精确 token 准确率仅小幅提升的情况下显著改善波形重建与心脏事件保留；解码器诱导几何比欧氏码字距离更能反映真实的解码代价。局限：上下文平均的图是对非线性解码器的近似，可能遗漏罕见、状态相关的替换。该工作明确为描述性统计，不做总体推断。

Abstract: Residual vector quantization (RVQ) turns physiological waveforms into compact token sequences, but conventional masked modeling treats every incorrect token as equally costly. We propose GeoRVQ, a coarse-to-fine masked token model whose objective reflects the local response of a frozen waveform decoder. Decoder-induced costs define geometry-aware soft targets and expected distortion, while quantizer-causal prediction follows residual dependencies from coarse to fine levels. In a descriptive aggregate over MIMIC-IV Waveform, VitalDB, and CODE-15\%, GeoRVQ increases exact token accuracy from $.133\pm.004$ to $.143\pm.003$, reduces decoded distance from $.606\pm.006$ to $.393\pm.007$, and increases R-peak F1 from $.784\pm.004$ to $.837\pm.008$ under matched model and training conditions. Across 45 held-out code substitutions, decoder-induced cost has a Spearman correlation of $.85$ with realized decoded cost, compared with $.54$ for Euclidean codeword distance. These results indicate that decoder-aware objectives can improve waveform and event preservation without requiring a large increase in exact token accuracy.

</details>


### [125] [WTF?! Simulation-Free Reinforcement Learning with Wasserstein-Tilted Flow Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27033)
*Abbas Mammadov,Jerry Y. Huang,Justin Lin,Partha Kaushik,Sheel Shah,Kartik Nair,Yee Whye Teh,Nicholas M. Boffi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出WTF（Wasserstein-Tilted Flow Maps），一种基于Wasserstein传输正则化的流映射奖励微调框架，通过将奖励微调重构为确定性最优控制问题，实现了无需模拟、无需评论网络的端到端流映射微调，在ImageNet-256和文生图任务上以最多280倍更少的训练计算量达到或超越现有方法的奖励水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的奖励微调方法通常采用KL正则化（奖励倾斜分布），该方法只能重新加权基分布而无法将样本传输到高奖励区域；此外，现有方法依赖扩散过程而非确定性流，无法利用流映射实现少步推理加速，导致后训练成本高昂。

Method: 1）提出基于预训练漂移构建的最优传输正则器T_b(ρ_0, ν)，替代KL正则化，将每个粒子向高奖励区域局部传输；2）证明该正则化下的微调问题等价于终端奖励的确定性最优控制问题；3）利用流映射的长程跃迁能力构造无模拟（simulation-free）的蒙特卡洛价值估计器，仅需O(1)次流映射评估即可计算价值梯度，无需学习评论网络；4）通过策略迭代更新对角速度场，结合自蒸馏损失保持流映射的少步推理能力。

Result: 在ImageNet-256上微调DMF XL/2，WTF在1 NFE时HPSv2达0.319（基模型0.214），250 NFE时达0.330，超越Adjoint Matching（0.243）、MFM（0.291）和VFM（0.307）。在文生图任务上微调TiM-T2I，WTF在50 NFE时HPSv2达0.398，超越Adjoint Matching（0.357）和Flow-GRPO（0.303）。计算效率方面，WTF达到基线峰值奖励所需计算量比零阶方法Flow-GRPO少280倍，比一阶方法Adjoint Matching少47倍。

Conclusion: WTF是首个面向流映射的端到端微调方法，证明了传输正则化比KL重加权更适合确定性流的奖励微调，其无模拟价值估计显著降低了后训练成本，为加速生成模型的后训练提供了新的基础设施范式。

Abstract: Reward fine-tuning aims to update a pre-trained flow-based generative model to improve the downstream reward of its generated samples. Existing methods typically formulate this problem as sampling from a reward-tilted distribution, the solution to a KL-regularized reward-maximization problem. Here, we introduce an optimal transport regularizer built directly from the pre-trained drift. Unlike KL reward tilting, the resulting objective transports individual samples toward higher reward rather than reweighting the base distribution. We show that the resulting problem is equivalent to a deterministic optimal control problem on the flow. Given a pre-trained flow map, this equivalence yields a simulation-free reinforcement learning algorithm for fine-tuning generative flows. We call the resulting framework Wasserstein-Tilted Flow Maps (WTF), the first end-to-end fine-tuning recipe native to flow maps. The output is a fine-tuned flow map that retains strong reward-aligned performance at few-step inference budgets without post-hoc distillation. Experiments on ImageNet-256 and text-to-image show that WTF achieves higher reward with comparable or higher diversity than baselines, while requiring up to $280\times$ less training compute. More broadly, we argue that accelerated samplers such as flow maps are essential infrastructure for efficient post-training, and that the dominant KL-regularized formulation is only one of many choices worth revisiting.

</details>


### [126] [ChipMEM: Verification-Grounded Memory for EDA Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27067)
*Abdulrahman AlRabah,Joshua Mabry,Dilek Hakkani-Tür,Abdussalam Alawini,Hamid Shojaei,Kartik Hegde,Sandesh Adhikary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ChipMEM 是一个面向 EDA 智能体的验证接地记忆层，通过跨任务过程性记忆与轨迹内贝叶斯统计指导的结合，在不修改底层智能体的条件下提升 RTL 优化与测试平台生成任务的可接受通过率与 PPA 指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 EDA 工具反馈的智能体学习方法（轨迹蒸馏、奖励训练）通常在产生经验的任务上评估，反复访问基准反馈可能奖励"任务特定修订"而非可迁移的可复用知识，且不同任务间的记忆可能产生负面影响，因此需要一种由 EDA 工具认证、并能在跨任务、跨设计中验证迁移价值的记忆层。

Method: 提出 ChipMEM 双记忆架构：(1) 过程性记忆——将完整执行轨迹蒸馏为可复用技能，仅在通过综合、仿真或形式化检查后才入库，按 RTL/任务嵌入做余弦相似度 Top-K 检索（阈值 τ_proc）；(2) 统计记忆——维护分层 Beta 估计的重试预测器与恢复预测器，分别按命令类型、错误类、先前调用历史等条件分层估计成功率，低于 τ_stat=0.20 时触发 LLM 恢复规划器；通过统一的智能体适配器在 RTL 优化与 CVDP 测试平台生成两个领域复用同一记忆接口，支持演化模式（持续写入）与冻结模式（只读评估）。

Result: 在 RTL-OPT 平均面积改进从 6.18% 升至 8.79%；RTLRewriter-Bench 短套件均值从 5.66% 升至 8.69%，等效通过 37/49 vs 33/49；OpenTitan 全 PPA 严格门通过 4/10 vs 1/10，平均功耗改进 2.54% vs 0.00%；冻结记忆下 CVDP 未见任务通过 20/20 vs 18/20；OpenTitan 上 LLM 调用、工具调用、输入/总 token 与工具时间均显著下降；SWE-bench Pro 100 题试点通过率 43/100 vs 32/100。

Conclusion: 经过 EDA 工具验证的经验可被转化为可复用过程性记忆与统计记忆，在不改动底层智能体的前提下提高可接受通过率与 PPA 改进幅度，并在冻结条件下对未见任务具备正向迁移能力；OpenTitan 上还伴随更低的交互开销，跨软件工程领域的初步试点亦显示迁移潜力。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agents use Electronic Design Automation (EDA) tools to generate and revise register-transfer-level (RTL) designs under synthesis and verification feedback. Recent methods learn from this feedback by distilling reusable skills from execution traces or by training on rewards derived from EDA-tools. Both methods are typically evaluated on the tasks that produced the experience. Repeated access to benchmark feedback on the same task can reward task-specific revision rather than creating reusable knowledge that transfers. We introduce ChipMEM, a verification-grounded memory layer for EDA agents. It combines cross-task procedural memory with within-trajectory statistical guidance. Its procedural component distills and stores a skill only after it passes synthesis, simulation, or formal checks, rather than relying on model self-assessments. A Bayesian component maintains hierarchical Beta estimates over tool-call outcomes and ranks recovery strategies that succeeded under comparable errors. A common adapter applies the same memory interface to RTL optimization and testbench-generation agents while preserving each domain's tools and acceptance criteria. We measure performance on training tasks and evaluate whether learned skills transfer to unseen tasks. On RTLRewriter-Bench, under matched model and tool settings, ChipMEM produces equivalence-passing outputs on 39/54 scored designs versus 35/54 without memory; on the 49-design short suite, mean area improvement is 8.69% versus 5.66%. On held-out CVDP tasks, ChipMEM with a frozen procedural library achieves 20/20 accepted outcomes versus 18/20 without memory in a single evaluation per setting.

</details>


### [127] [Learning Risk Scores Robust to Unobserved Confounders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27144)
*Ryan Edmonds,Yingxiao Ye,Sina Aghaei,Andrés Gómez,Çağıl Koçyiğit,Phebe Vayanos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在存在未观测混杂因素的情况下,从观察数据中学习风险评分的鲁棒优化方法。作者将倾向性得分视为属于一个不确定性集合(基于边际敏感模型和Wasserstein分布鲁棒优化),从而构建了一个可重写为指数锥规划的max-min优化问题。在UCI数据集的半合成实验上,该方法在校准误差上比传统基准方法最高改善29.2%,比当前最优方法改善11.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险资源配置领域(如公共健康、无家可归者预防),风险评分通常从历史观察数据中学习。然而,标准方法(如逆倾向加权)依赖于"无混杂"假设,即假设所有同时影响治疗分配和结果的因素都被记录在数据中。现实中,历史决策往往依赖于未记录的信息(如案例工作者面谈中获取但未录入的信息),导致学习到的风险评分系统性地低估那些曾因未记录因素被优先处理的个体的风险。本文旨在提出一种对未观测混杂具有鲁棒性的风险评分学习方法。

Method: 方法核心是构建一个max-min鲁棒优化问题:

**问题建模**: 将未知的真实倾向性得分 Z = P(T=0|X,U) 视为不确定参数,放入不确定性集合 S_z 中,通过最大化最坏情况下的对数似然来学习风险评分系数 β。

**不确定性集合包含四个约束**:
- (C1) 一致性膨胀: 保证重加权后的未治疗群体能正确还原总体规模
- (C2) Wasserstein距离约束: 保证重加权分布与原始协变量分布在Wasserstein距离意义下一致(处理实际样本中支撑集不完全重叠的情况)
- (C3) 基于Tan(2006)边际敏感模型(MSM)的优势比界: 真实治疗优势比与可观测优势比的差异不超过 Γ ≥ 1
- (C4) 历史理性约束: 历史决策者平均而言不应比仅使用可观测协变量做得更差(对应eMSM的单侧结果敏感约束)

**样本近似与求解**: 将优化问题重写为样本近似形式(对应原文中问题(4)),通过将Wasserstein距离改写为运输问题并运用强对偶,等价转化为指数锥规划(Proposition 3.1),可用MOSEK等现成求解器求解。计算复杂度: 决策变量随 max(d_x, n) 线性增长,约束数量随 n 二次增长。

**历史理性约束的价值**: 是eMSM结果敏感约束的单侧特例 [0,∞),不需要额外的敏感参数。

**与相关工作对比**:
- 不同于Kallus & Zhou(2021)的策略遗憾最小化(目标是策略值,本文目标是预测性风险评分)
- 不同于Sahoo et al.(2026)(他们的Γ约束的是结果依赖的样本选择,而非结果依赖的治疗分配)
- 不同于Dorn et al.(2025)和Zhang et al.(2025)的ATE界推导(他们针对因果估计量,本文针对预测评分)

**与Robust Baseline对比**: Robust Baseline仅使用C1和C3约束(对应Kallus & Zhou的不确定性集合),本文额外加入Wasserstein分布一致性约束(C2)和历史理性约束(C4),通过反例证明缺少这些约束会导致不确定性集合包含与观测数据不相容的分布。

Result: 在来自UCI机器学习库的15个数据集上构建半合成观察数据(通过对标准二分类数据系统性地引入二元治疗t和未观测混杂):

**实验设置**: 每个数据集采样1500训练点和500测试点,引入二元处理变量,处理分配通过 logistic 模型 π⁰(x, y⁰) 生成,混杂强度由 γ ≥ 0 控制。倾向性得分通过logistic回归、梯度提升和SVM估计,选取验证集上准确率最高的模型。

**基准方法**: Full Information(有反事实结果)、Direct(不使用权重的logistic回归)、IPW(逆倾向加权)、Doubly Robust(直接法+IPW组合)、Robust Baseline(仅含C1和C3约束的指数锥规划版本,对应Kallus & Zhou的不确定性集合)。

**评估指标**: 
1. 对数似然改善(相对随机猜测的对数损失减少)
2. 期望校准误差(ECE)
3. 与测试数据最优风险评分的Spearman秩相关系数

**关键结果**(Table 1):
- 当Γ设置在 e^γ 到 e^{2γ} 之间时,鲁棒方法在所有指标上均优于其他基准
- 校准误差比传统基准(DR)最高相对降低29.2%
- 校准误差比Robust Baseline最高相对降低11.1%
- 随着 γ 增加,所有非Full Information方法性能下降,但鲁棒方法保持优势
- Robust方法相比Robust Baseline的提升证明了Wasserstein分布一致性约束和历史理性约束的价值

**计算资源**: 使用多种AMD EPYC和Intel Xeon CPU节点,使用MOSEK求解器。约束数量随n二次增长,大数据集上可通过延迟约束生成技术加速。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一个能够处理未观测混杂的鲁棒风险评分学习框架。通过将真实倾向性得分建模为属于基于MSM和Wasserstein DRO的不确定性集合,所提出的max-min优化问题可被精确重写为指数锥规划,便于使用现成求解器求解。

**核心贡献**:
1. 提出了一个考虑未观测混杂的鲁棒风险评分学习框架
2. 通过结合因果推断中的敏感度分析(MSM)与Wasserstein分布鲁棒优化,构建了具有因果可解释性鲁棒参数Γ的不确定性集合
3. 证明了样本近似问题等价于指数锥规划
4. 通过半合成实验证明了方法在混杂存在时的优越性

**实际意义**: 在领域专家能够估计混杂程度的情况下,该方法可有效管理未观测混杂。这对于无家可归者服务、公共健康等从业者对缺失信息有直觉判断的领域尤其重要。

**未来方向**:
- 在集合B中加入完整性或稀疏性约束以生成可解释的风险评分
- 通过延迟约束生成技术处理大规模数据集(因约束数量随n二次增长)
- 将框架扩展到对数似然以外的其他目标函数,鲁棒学习风险评分以外的其他量

**与现有方法的关键区别**: 本文针对的是预测性风险评分(为人类决策提供脆弱性估计),而非直接的策略值优化,这在需要可解释性和人为监督的社会服务领域具有重要意义。Wasserstein分布一致性约束和历史理性约束的加入确保了不确定性集合仅包含合理的统计分布。

Abstract: We consider the problem of learning risk scores to prioritize individuals for scarce resources or interventions, from historical observational data affected by unobserved confounding. Decisions about who receives scarce resources are often guided by risk scores based on recorded characteristics, such as responses to a survey. These risk scores are increasingly being learned directly from observational data: historical records of individuals' characteristics, allocation decisions, and outcomes. Standard methods such as inverse propensity weighting (IPW), which corrects for the bias introduced by the historical allocation policy, can be used to learn accurate risk scores if the historical decision process is fully explained by the recorded characteristics. In practice, however, historical decisions often depend on unrecorded information, causing learned risk scores to systematically under-prioritize exactly the individuals whose unrecorded circumstances drove past prioritization. We propose a method for learning risk scores that are robust to this kind of unobserved confounding, building on IPW. Since propensity weights cannot be reliably estimated under unobserved confounding, we instead treat them as belonging to an uncertainty set determined by the observable data and domain-informed estimates of the degree of confounding, combining sensitivity analysis from causal inference with Wasserstein distributionally robust optimization. The resulting robust risk score learning problem admits a sample-based approximation that we reformulate as an exponential cone program compatible with off-the-shelf solvers. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach on semi-synthetic data derived from datasets in the UCI Machine Learning Repository. Our method improves calibration by up to 29.2% over traditional benchmarks and up to 11.1% over the state of the art, without compromising other metrics.

</details>


### [128] [Scaling of Capability and Efficiency at Inference Time in Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27166)
*Moritz Laber,Zohair Shafi,Germans Savcisens,Brennan Klein,Matteo Chinazzi,Samuel V. Scarpino,Albert-László Barabási,Tina Eliassi-Rad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文使用分层贝叶斯模型评估DeepSeek-R1-Distill模型家族在四类算术与算法推理任务上的能力与效率缩放规律。核心发现：问题正确求解概率随实例大小近似呈指数衰减，衰减尺度随模型参数量呈次线性增长（能力增强但收益递减）；正确回答的输出长度随实例大小呈幂律增长，但其参数几乎不随模型大小变化（效率无显著提升）。这揭示了朴素缩放策略的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型缩放定律主要关注预训练阶段，对推理阶段（test-time compute）尤其是大型推理模型（LRMs）的能力与效率如何随模型规模联合演化缺乏系统理解。回答这一问题是优化整个模型生命周期资源分配的关键。

Method: 在五个不同规模的DeepSeek-R1-Distill模型（1.5B–70B）上，针对加法、括号、奇偶性、索引四类可控制难度的推理任务，通过Chain-of-Thought生成回答（温度0.4/0.6/0.8，每种配置100个实例）。采用分层贝叶斯模型：能力侧将正确解题数建模为实例大小的指数衰减函数（衰减尺度ν_{N,τ}），并以幂律关系连接模型规模；效率侧将正确回答的平均输出长度建模为实例大小的幂律函数，并检验其参数对模型规模的依赖。通过HMC采样推断后验，比较固定/可变渐近线模型（FAM/VAM）与前缀/前缀指数模型（PM/PEM）。

Result: 1) 正确求解概率近似按实例大小n指数衰减：p_{N,τ}(n) = p^{(∞)} + (p^{(0)}-p^{(∞)})·exp(-n/ν_{N,τ})。2) 衰减尺度ν_{N,τ}随参数数N呈幂律增长，后验均值E[β^{(ν)}]≈0.51–0.75（全部小于1），呈次线性缩放。例如加法任务中ν从1.5B时的约26增长到70B时的约363；参数翻倍仅带来约1.6倍的能力提升。3) 正确回答的输出长度随n呈幂律增长，但缩放指数β^{(A)}的后验均值在各任务和温度下大多接近零（|E[β^{(A)}]|≤0.17），95%可信区间跨零，未观察到效率随模型规模的系统性提升。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了AI系统中朴素缩放策略的潜在局限：能力虽有提升但呈次线性（边际收益递减），而效率几乎不随模型规模改善。这意味着仅靠增加参数无法同时实现更高能力与更高效率，未来工作应探索能力-效率权衡及后训练阶段将token效率纳入奖励机制的方案。局限性包括仅考察DeepSeek-R1-Distill家族与四个任务、且能力与效率被分别建模。

Abstract: Capability and efficiency are two key dimensions of reasoning in large language models (LLMs). Capability refers to the ability to solve a given problem correctly, whereas efficiency refers to the ability to do so with limited resources. When LLMs use Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning to solve problems of controlled hardness, both the number of problems solved correctly and the number of tokens required to reach a correct answer depend on problem hardness and model size. However, how these factors jointly shape capability and efficiency remains poorly understood. Here, we use hierarchical Bayesian models to evaluate the capability and efficiency of LLMs from the DeepSeek-R1-Distill model family across four classes of arithmetic and algorithmic reasoning problems. At a fixed model size, the probability of correctly solving an instance decays approximately exponentially with instance size, our proxy for problem hardness. The decay scale grows sublinearly with model size, indicating that larger models are more capable, but that capability gains diminish with scale. Output length grows as a power law with instance size, which serves as a proxy for difficulty. However, the parameters of this power law do not vary systematically with model size, suggesting that larger models do not become more efficient. Together, these findings reveal potential limitations of naive scaling as a strategy for developing more capable AI systems: capability improves with diminishing returns, while efficiency shows little to no improvement.

</details>


### [129] [Data-driven discrete-time deep recurrent neural network-based modeling for dissipative systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27186)
*Tuan Luong,Hyungpil Moon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种深度离散时间耗散循环神经网络（DissipNet），通过结构性权重约束和专用训练算法显式保证耗散性，并在质量-弹簧-阻尼器和二自由度机械臂两个案例中验证了其相对于朴素RNN和PINN在建模精度和稳定性保持方面的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经网络建模物理系统时无法保证稳定性等物理特性，而现有的PINN方法虽能融入物理方程，但无法保证无源性或耗散性等内部解析性质。作者旨在通过在网络结构层面而非损失函数层面强制施加耗散性约束来解决此问题。

Method: 提出DissipNet：通过将多层深度RNN重构为标准非线性离散系统形式，推导出保证(Q,S,R)耗散性的线性矩阵不等式（LMI）条件，并利用李雅普诺夫理论证明稳定性。设计了一种无约束优化训练算法，通过参数化重构将LMI约束转化为简单的矩阵分解和ReLU操作，从而满足耗散性条件。

Result: 在MSD系统和2-DOF机械臂建模实验中，DissipNet在有无噪声、不同训练样本量条件下均显著优于朴素RNN和PINN。例如MSD中N=200带噪声时，DRNN将平均误差从RNN的6.53×10⁻²降至1.12×10⁻²（提升超80%），且能严格保持耗散性，而PINN无法保证此性质。

Conclusion: 通过将耗散性约束直接嵌入网络架构而非损失函数，DissipNet无需已知系统显式方程即可学习耗散动力学，在建模精度、噪声鲁棒性和物理特性保持方面均优于RNN和PINN，为物理AI建模提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Physical AI has gained increasing attention for its role in developing AI systems that better understand, predict, and control real-world dynamics. Achieving this requires AI models that not only achieve high prediction accuracy but also preserve fundamental physical properties of dynamical systems. In this paper, we propose a deep discrete-time dissipative recurrent neural network (DissipNet) that explicitly enforces dissipativity, a key property related to stability and energy dissipation, through structural weight constraints and a dedicated training algorithm. By construction, the proposed network is capable of learning dissipative dynamics while preserving their inherent stability, which is formally analyzed using Lyapunov theory. In contrast to Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs), which incorporate governing equations into the training loss but do not guarantee preservation of internal analytical properties such as dissipativity or passivity, our approach provides explicit guarantees on stability at the model level. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method through several modeling applications, and compare its performance with a naive recurrent neural network (RNN) and a PINN-based model.

</details>


### [130] [ZO-COSMO: Index-Free One-Hop Mixing for Decentralized Zeroth-Order Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27199)
*Shengjun Zhang,Tingyi Liu,Heng Zhang,Dong Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ZO-COSMO，一种面向去中心化零阶优化的无索引单跳混合方法，通过将双查询估计与基于匹配的掩码共识耦合，每个活跃链路仅需传输q个数值（无需坐标索引）。理论分析给出了精确的混合条件、匹配类的最优收缩率以及包含稀疏动量的收敛保证。在64个智能体和Qwen2-7B LoRA调优实验中均展现出优于显式索引Rand-k和全邻居混合基线的固定预算性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在去中心化黑盒学习中，稀疏通信节省比特的前提是通信也附带编码开销。在十亿参数规模下，每个坐标索引（约32位）与单精度浮点值相当，几乎使纯值负载翻倍。现有方法通过共享种子重建更新，但单跳混合仍需对等状态值；缺乏对支持兼容性与解码精度的精确刻画，使得稀疏消息无法在不发送稀疏索引的情况下完成精确对等状态混合。

Method: 核心思想是耦合查询支持与混合支持：利用公共随机种子和轮次计数器在两端端点重建相同的有序支持S_t。算法1使用全局公共支持进行掩码Metropolis混合；算法2使用公共完美匹配为每条边分配独立支持，每个活跃节点仅需发送q个有序值。理论分析包括：(1) 精确混合的充要支持条件（定理1）；(2) 匹配类的尖锐收缩率上界（定理3）；(3) 核心与稀疏动量更新的收敛保证（定理4-5）；(4) 三种方向相关性耦合（G/S/I）下的精确矩恒等式（命题1）。

Result: 在20维Rosenbrock（0.1M比特预算）上，ZO-COSMO在三种拓扑上均取得最低目标值，比带索引版本多执行15-17%轮。在64个智能体的网络压力测试中，边缘局部混合在所有N∈{8,16,32,64}下均优于全邻居和种子-标量洪水传播。在Qwen2-7B QNLI任务上（26.214Mbit/链路预算），ZO-COSMO以100轮完成训练，显式索引Rand-k仅完成59轮，精度提升3.65点（CI [0.49,6.86]）；在8工人完全图和环形图上比全邻居混合分别提升3.42和2.53点；动量消融在匹配前步条件下带来3.92点收益。稀疏Qwen全模型实验中，无索引编码比显式坐标索引节省2.03×负载。

Conclusion: ZO-COSMO实现了无索引单跳稀疏通信的精确对等状态混合，通过将查询与混合解耦到独立支持上，每个活跃链路仅传输q个数值。理论严格刻画了支持兼容性、最优收缩率、异构性驱动的相关影响。实验验证了在合成和真实模型规模上的固定预算收益，证明了无索引编码与稀疏动量在相同调度和查询统计下各自带来独立增益。

Abstract: Sparse communication in decentralized zeroth-order learning requires compatible peer-state coordinates. We characterize this one-hop condition and develop \textsf{ZO-COSMO}, coupling two-query estimation with average-preserving masked consensus using $q$ values per active link. Global supports serve all-neighbor mixing; matching updates require agreement only within each pair. We derive a sharp contraction-per-scalar bound within the matching class and convergence guarantees for the core and sparse-momentum updates. At fixed matching, exact moment identities characterize how shared directions preserve gradient-heterogeneity cancellation and redistribute estimation error and disagreement. Mechanism experiments cover unequal curvatures, noise, and sparse momentum. Further tests span $64$ synthetic agents and eight logical Qwen LoRA workers. At matched payload budgets, Qwen2-7B QNLI gains $3.65$ accuracy points over explicit-index Rand-$k$; edge-local updates gain $3.42$ and $2.53$ points over all-neighbor mixing on eight-worker complete and ring graphs. A matched-first-step ablation gives a $3.92$-point momentum benefit. Seed-aware and same-matching controls distinguish encoding, scheduling, and query correlation.

</details>


### [131] [A Systematic Benchmark of Explainable Methods for Temporal Attribution in Sequential Recommendation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27201)
*Akash Pandey,Kanisha Shah,Addrish Roy,Dwipam Katariya,Hongyangyang Shi,Amanda Ding,Kalanand Mishra,Pranab Mohanty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对序列推荐系统提出了一种基于双模型掩码的忠实性评估指标，并在 CNN、Transformer、SASRec、BERT4Rec 等骨干网络上对 10 种 XAI 方法进行了系统性基准测试。核心结论是：基于梯度的方法（Integrated Gradients 和 GradientSHAP）最忠实且稳健；原始注意力权重不可靠，而梯度加权注意力在短序列下恢复忠实性但在长序列下退化；忠实方法的时序归因反映真实任务结构而非近因或流行度偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度序列推荐模型因其非线性而成为黑盒，缺乏对其决策依据（即历史交互中哪些时间步驱动了推荐）的系统性忠实性评估。虽然梯度法、扰动法和注意力法在 CV 和 NLP 中已得到广泛基准测试，但在序列推荐中的忠实性系统评估仍属空白。理解这些归因对于开发者审计模型行为和用户建立信任至关重要。

Method: 提出双模型掩码忠实性度量指标：模型 A 在完整序列上训练并提供逐时间步归因分数；模型 B（架构相同）以随机交互掩码进行训练（p=0.05）作为可重用的分布内探针。逐组移除高归因时间步后，通过 Pearson 相关系数衡量模型 B 预测概率的下降幅度，从而量化忠实性。同时评估对输入噪声的稳健性（c_r）和对物品流行度的偏倚（c_p）。在 KuaiRand-1k 与 MovieLens-1M 两个数据集上，对 10 种 XAI 方法（IG、GradientSHAP、DeepLIFT、DeepLIFT-SHAP、Grad-CAM、LIME、KernelSHAP、Attention Tracing、Grad-SAM、TimeSliver）进行评估。

Result: （1）基于梯度的方法（IG 和 GradientSHAP）在所有设置中均达到 c_f < −0.8，一致地最忠实且最稳健；（2）原始注意力权重（如 Attention Tracing）高度不稳定且与架构相关（Vanilla Transformer 上 c_f = +0.930，呈反忠实性）；（3）梯度加权注意力（Grad-SAM）在短序列（≤300）上恢复忠实性，但当 L>300 时性能急剧下降（softmax 注意力概率趋向均匀）；（4）TimeSliver 作为内在可解释模型，参数少 5 倍却仍能达到 c_f < −0.8；（5）流行度偏倚 c_p 均在 [−0.146, +0.110] 之间，与随机基线相当，表明忠实方法未受流行度或近因偏差影响。

Conclusion: 为从业者部署可解释序列推荐器提供可操作指导：基于梯度的后验方法提供可靠稳定的归因，注意力方法应谨慎使用并针对序列长度和架构仔细评估；双模型框架还可用于轨迹细化和生产模型效率提升，通过识别负向影响或噪声交互实现数据集去噪和离线序列蒸馏。

Abstract: Sequential RecSys are central to modern personalization, exploiting user's historical interaction sequences to drive next-step decisions. Deep learning models, particularly CNN and Transformer-based architectures, have proven highly effective at capturing temporal dependencies in these histories. For transparency and trust, understanding which past interactions drive a given recommendation is increasingly important --- both for developers auditing model behavior and for users seeking a rationale. However, the non-linearities that give these models their predictive power also render them black boxes, making it difficult to attribute decisions to specific interactions. While gradient-based, perturbation-based, and attention-based explainability methods exist, a systematic benchmark of their faithfulness for sequential recommendation is missing. We address this gap by introducing a dual-model masking metric in which one model supplies per-timestep attribution scores and a separately trained, masking-robust probe measures the resulting change in predicted probability. Using this metric, we benchmark ten XAI methods across CNN, Transformer, SASRec, and BERT4Rec backbones on KuaiRand and MovieLens, complemented by analyses of temporal attribution patterns, item popularity confounding, and robustness to input corruption. Our key findings are: (1) gradient-based methods, particularly GradientSHAP and Integrated Gradients, yield the most faithful and robust attributions; (2) raw attention weights are unreliable, but gradient-weighted attention restores faithfulness on shorter sequences, with degradation on longer horizons as softmax attention probabilities converge toward uniform importance scores, diminishing the method's ability to identify informative interactions; and (3) temporal attribution patterns in faithful methods reflect genuine task structure rather than recency or popularity bias.

</details>


### [132] [Tail-Aware Geometry Learning for Conformal Ellipsoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27221)
*Xiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向多元共形预测的尾感知几何学习方法，通过将尾敏感度参数与目标覆盖水平解耦，使用CVaR约束替代传统VaR约束来学习椭球度量矩阵，并在凸优化框架下实现有界再加权解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于最小体积的多元共形预测方法使用分位数阈值（如VaR）来学习残差几何，忽略了超出活跃分位数的尾部残差严重程度，并且隐式地将几何学习与覆盖水平绑定，限制了预测区域对系统性方向性尾部结构的建模能力。

Method: 采用两阶段分割方法：第一阶段在CVaR约束下最小化对数体积来学习度量矩阵Ω，利用Rockafellar-Uryasev表示将问题转化为凸优化形式（公式10），并通过对偶分析得到有界再加权解释（命题2）；第二阶段在独立校准集上执行标准分位共形校准。理论上刻画了椭球体积与尾部严重度之间的权衡关系（命题3）。

Result: 在合成数据和三个真实数据集（Gas、Protein、House）上的实验表明，该方法在保持标称覆盖率和竞争性预测集效率的同时，显著降低了误覆盖严重度（Severity指标），且τ参数可独立控制效率-严重度权衡而不影响覆盖保证。

Conclusion: 该框架解耦了尾敏感度与覆盖水平，提供了凸的、可全局求解的几何学习问题，具有理论支撑的有界再加权解释，并通过广泛实验验证了其有效性，为多元共形预测中尾部风险控制提供了新思路。

Abstract: This paper studies multivariate conformal prediction (CP), a distribution-free uncertainty quantification framework with finite-sample coverage guarantees. The efficiency of multivariate prediction sets hinges critically on the residual geometry encoded by the nonconformity score, while existing minimum-volume methods rely on quantile thresholds that ignore tail residual severity and implicitly bind geometry learning to coverage level. We propose a tail-aware geometry learning framework for conformal ellipsoids that decouples tail sensitivity in geometry learning from the final coverage guarantee. Using a two-split design, we learn the metric matrix via volume minimization under a CVaR constraint on an estimation split, then apply standard conformal calibration on a held-out calibration split. The resulting problem is convex and admits a bounded-reweighting interpretation that prioritizes high-residual samples. Moreover, we theoretically characterize the trade-off between ellipsoidal volume and tail severity. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [133] [A Scaling Study for fMRI Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27232)
*Wenhao Ye,Xuanye Pan,Junfeng Xia,Junxiang Zhang,Mo Wang,Quanying Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对fMRI基础模型开展了首个系统的缩放定律研究，使用200多个数据集、超过10,000 GPU小时实验，系统地变化预训练数据规模（2,585–63,401条记录）、模型规模（约2M–92M编码器参数）与训练时长，固定NeuroJEPA自监督目标与12个下游线性探测任务。研究发现：(1)下游性能随计算量总体提升，但相似计算量的模型表现差异显著；(2)数据与模型规模需协同扩展——大模型从额外数据获得的收益更大；(3)在匹配计算量下，12个任务中有8个偏向数据扩展优于模型缩放。基于ID任务拟合的"有效数据响应"，在两个预算下锁定的NeuroJEPA模型（2.65M与10.42M参数）以更低的预训练计算量在6个OOD任务上取得最优平均性能，证明fMRI缩放不能仅由计算量刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在视觉与NLP领域，缩放定律（数据、模型规模、计算量的关系）已成功指导基础模型开发，但fMRI基础模型受限于人类神经影像数据的获取约束，且标注下游数据稀缺，其缩放关系尚不清楚：更多数据对不同规模的模型是否有差异化收益？在固定计算预算下应优先扩数据还是扩模型？

Method: 固定NeuroJEPA（基于LeJEPA的ROI时间序列自监督目标，含全局-局部视图对齐与SIGReg正则化）与ViT编码器家族。控制变量：6个嵌套数据规模、9个模型规模（约2M–92M参数）、多个训练时长。12个任务（含ID与OOD两类，涵盖分类与回归）采用冻结线性探测评估。计算量以EFLOPs估算。基于5个ID任务拟合有效数据响应（Muennighoff 2023式14），在两个预算下选择最优配置并锁定，最后在6个OOD任务上评估并与BrainLM、BrainMASS、BrainGFM、BrainHarmonix、Brain-Semantoks等公开基线比较。

Result: （1）下游性能随计算量总体单调改善，但相似EFLOP配置性能差异显著；（2）交互项β_D×N在训练结束时达0.239（中点0.123），表明大模型从额外数据获益更大，且该关系随训练推进增强；（3）匹配计算下，12任务中8个偏向数据扩展，0个明确偏向模型扩展，4个无显著差异；（4）锁定的两个NeuroJEPA模型（2.65M/0.128 EFLOP，10.42M/1.97 EFLOP）在OOD任务上以更低预训练计算取得最高平均性能，10.42M模型在6个OOD任务中至少5个任务上至少一项指标最佳。

Conclusion: fMRI基础模型的缩放不能仅以计算量刻画：性能取决于预训练数据量、模型规模与训练时长的组合方式。数据与模型应协同扩展；在大多数下游任务上，相对于扩大模型规模，扩大预训练数据带来更一致的收益。基于ID任务拟合的有效数据响应可用于在固定预算下选择有竞争力的OOD模型。该响应仅在观测网格内有效，无法外推至远超出当前数据规模（24.5倍范围）的配置。

Abstract: Scaling laws have guided large-model development in computer vision and natural language processing, but the relationships among data, model size, and compute remain unclear for functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) foundation models. Here, we conduct a controlled empirical study using pretraining data from more than 200 source datasets and over 10,000 GPU-hours of experiments. Holding the pretraining framework and downstream protocol fixed, we vary pretraining data size, model size, and training duration. Downstream performance generally improves with compute, yet models using similar compute can perform substantially differently. Additional pretraining data bring larger gains at larger model sizes, suggesting that data and model size should be scaled together. At matched compute, increasing pretraining data benefits more tasks than increasing model size, although the pattern varies across tasks. We then use in-distribution (ID) downstream performance to select the combination of pretraining data size, model size, and training duration at two fixed compute budgets. The resulting models are locked before out-of-distribution (OOD) evaluation. They achieve the highest average performance across the evaluated OOD tasks among the compared fMRI foundation models while using less pretraining compute. Overall, our results show that compute alone does not characterize fMRI scaling: performance depends on how pretraining data, model size, and training duration are combined.

</details>


### [134] [Discover, Falsify, Revise: Auditing Input-Use Claims from Source Code to Predictive Contribution in Agent-Discovered Cell Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27234)
*Mengran Li,Bo Li,Chengyang Zhang,Yang Yan,Jinfeng Xu,Zhenchao Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CellAudit 框架，用于审计 AI 虚拟细胞模型中关于输入使用（特别是扰动信息）的注册声明，通过源代码检查、拟合依赖性和目标相关预测贡献三个层次验证模型是否真正使用了所声称的输入，揭示了"预测-声明差距"问题，并将智能体模型发现从"生成-评分-修订"推进到"发现-证伪-修订"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI虚拟细胞模型在扰动预测任务中可能获得高预测性能但并未真正使用所提供的扰动信息（仅利用细胞上下文或对照轮廓），这种"预测-声明差距"在智能体模型发现场景下尤为严重，因为基于评分反馈的语言模型智能体会偏向选择高分预测器而不验证其是否使用了指定输入。

Method: 提出 CellAudit 框架，通过三个非等价问题审计输入使用声明：①源代码消费（AST检查器判断输入能否进入引用计算），②拟合依赖性（通过匹配输入替换测试冻结检查点的预测距离），③目标相关预测贡献（通过目标损失对比测试依赖性是否改善预测）。并在智能体发现循环中将审计诊断反馈给智能体以指导模型修订（证伪引导发现）。

Result: 在BBBC047基准上发现代理选定的预测器达PCC 0.3153但对化合物替换完全不变（被单例KV注意力阻断）；48个候选的分层审计中47个改变预测但仅20个具有正目标损失区间。在BBBC上证伪引导修订恢复了正化合物贡献并保持预测增益；在LINCS→LKCP独立获取队列上，剂量贡献持续但化合物身份贡献不支持。所有审计选定的设计均通过扰动对输入使用测试（5/5）。

Conclusion: 预测性能和声明的输入使用应分别评估；源代码消费、拟合依赖性和目标相关贡献可能分离；预测泛化不必意味着声明泛化。CellAudit 为智能体模型发现增加了证伪层，将范式从"生成-评分-修订"推向"发现-证伪-修订"。

Abstract: AI virtual cells aim to predict cellular responses to specified interventions, yet held-out predictive performance alone does not establish use of the supplied perturbation information. This prediction-claim gap matters in agentic model discovery, where language-model agents generate and revise predictors using score-based feedback. We introduce CELLAUDIT, which audits input-use claims by asking whether an input can enter the cited computation, whether fitted predictions depend on it, and whether that dependence improves prediction of observed response. On a paired morphology-transcriptomics perturbation benchmark (BBBC047), an agent-selected predictor attains a mean held-out Global Pearson correlation coefficient (PCC) of 0.3153 but remains invariant to compound replacement; a control-profile-only predictor reaches 0.3142. Source inspection identifies a compound-query pathway blocked by singleton key-value attention, and the invariance persists after refitting with disjoint control wells. In a stratified audit of 48 candidates across two linked tasks, 47 change predictions under compound replacement on both held-out folds, but only 20 show target-loss gains with intervals above zero on both folds. On BBBC047, falsification-guided revisions recover positive mean compound contributions while retaining gains over the control-profile-only baseline. In matched sci-Plex searches, audit-enriched feedback yields higher held-out performance and larger mean compound and dose contributions across five trajectories, although paired intervals span zero. Refitting fixed designs on an independently acquired cohort shows predictive generalization need not imply generalization of input-use claims: dose contribution persists, whereas support for compound identity does not. CELLAUDIT adds a falsification layer to agentic model discovery, moving from generate-score-revise toward discover-falsify-revise.

</details>


### [135] [Full-Covariance Smoothing of Bayesian Neural Networks for Online Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27244)
*Oren Wright,Haoming Jing,Qiaoan Shen,Koichiro Niinuma,Yorie Nakahira,José M. F. Moura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于全协方差贝叶斯神经网络（BNN）的在线自适应方法，通过将神经网络层视为状态空间模型的时间步，利用前向高斯矩传播和后向Rauch-Tung-Striebel（RTS）平滑进行闭式、单遍的权重后验更新。核心贡献是推导了一个交叉协方差恒等式（定理1），使得非线性激活函数之间能够传播全协方差，突破了现有平滑方法仅支持对角协方差的限制。该方法在非平稳分类、在线动力学学习和视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型策略自适应等任务中均优于现有平滑方法和基于梯度的微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人控制、环境变化等非平稳场景下，神经网络策略常因环境突变（如相机损坏、控制框架改变）而失效。梯度微调需要批量数据、多轮迭代、且对数据过拟合，难以满足在线自适应对数据效率、不确定性感知的需求。贝叶斯神经网络虽能提供不确定性估计，但传统近似推理（如MCMC、变分推理）计算开销大且依赖批处理。现有基于滤波/平滑的BNN训练方法（如KBNN、TAGI）虽支持单遍更新，但受限于对角协方差假设，丢弃了神经元间的相关性信息，限制了表达能力。

Method: 1. **状态空间建模**：将前馈神经网络的每一层视为状态空间模型的一个时间步，激活输入x^(ℓ)、权重W^(ℓ)与仿射输出y^(ℓ)构成联合高斯分布。  
2. **关键定理（定理1）**：推导了激活函数输入与输出间的交叉协方差 Σ_{x,z} = Σ_{x,x} · ∂μ_z/∂μ_x，该形式结合Stein引理与Bonnet定理，仅需激活均值关于输入均值的导数。  
3. **前向传播（矩传播）**：逐层计算激活均值μ_z、激活协方差Σ_{z,z}（定理2）、交叉协方差Σ_{x,z}，再在独立性假设下计算仿射输出的均值μ_y与协方差Σ_{y,y}，并保存交叉协方差Σ_{x,y}与Σ_{w,y}供后向使用。**关键设计**：仅将近似为高斯的对象放在仿射输出y^(ℓ)（加权项），而非激活输出z^(ℓ)，高斯假设更弱、稳定性更好。  
4. **后向传播（RTS平滑）**：以观测模型（噪声目标或分布性目标）的终端边界条件出发，逐层计算卡尔曼增益K^(ℓ) = Σ_{·,y} · Σ_{y,y}^{-1}，以"一步每层"方式更新权重后验μ_w^+、Σ_{w,w}^+以及输入均值/协方差，避免了KBNN中需要两步平滑并对Σ_{z,z}求逆的复杂度。  
5. **统一框架**：同时支持确定性系统带噪声观测（R≠0, Σ_k=0）与随机系统带分布性目标（R=0, Σ_k≠0），两者仅在终端边界条件处不同。

Result: 1. **非平稳分类（旋转Moon数据集）**：在每500个样本发生随机旋转的在线流式场景下，所提方法平均测试准确率92.19%±0.25，测试BCE为0.202，优于KBNN、TAGI及SGD方法，决策边界更稳定。  
2. **在线动力学学习（CartPole与Siemens工业基准）**：在多变量强相关输出的CartPole环境中，单步RMSE 0.082±0.004，滚动RMSE（H=5）为0.707±0.120，一致超越KBNN（0.117/0.818）、TAGI（0.168/1.000）、SGD（0.092/0.748）和高斯过程（0.097/0.722）。在高维Siemens基准（33维输入）中，单步RMSE 8.985±0.487、滚动RMSE均最优。  
3. **VLA策略自适应（π_0.5模型）**：在控制框架每60样本旋转π/10的非平稳环境中，使用轻量残差适配器，所提方法平均成功率79.82%，显著高于KBNN（63.13%）、TAGI（63.57%）和带回放的SGD（49.41%），且在变化后恢复更快。  
4. **计算代价**：每层权重更新复杂度O(n²m³)，高于KBNN的O(n²m)与TAGI的O(nm)；实际GPU运行时间差距因KBNN/TAGI受调度瓶颈限制而小于渐近差异。

Conclusion: 论文提出了首个支持全协方差通过非线性激活函数传播的平滑式BNN训练算法，核心是基于交叉协方差恒等式的解析矩公式与一步每层的RTS平滑更新。该方法在闭式、单遍、不确定性感知的方式下实现高效在线自适应，在非平稳分类、动力学学习和机器人VLA微调三类任务中均优于现有对角协方差平滑方法与基于梯度的微调。然而该方法存在四点局限：(1)全协方差更新随宽度扩展性差；(2)权重与激活间的独立性假设随更新累积可能退化；(3)递归本身假设目标平稳，缺乏漂移的原理性建模；(4)实验中仅验证了噪声目标设置，分布性目标有待进一步验证。

Abstract: A neural network's layers can be treated as time steps of a state-space model, turning Bayesian training into a smoothing problem: a forward pass propagates Gaussian moments through the network, and a backward Rauch--Tung--Striebel pass updates the weight posteriors in closed form. Such methods learn from each observation in a single pass, in an uncertainty-aware manner, and without gradient-based iterations or replay, which makes them well suited for online adaptation and data-efficient learning. Existing smoothing-based methods, however, are restricted to diagonal covariances across activations, discarding correlations between neurons. We overcome this limitation via a cross-covariance identity that enables full-covariance propagation through a network's nonlinear activations. We derive a one-step-per-layer smoother that approximates as Gaussian only each layer's affine output, and that applies both to deterministic systems with noisy observations and to stochastic systems described by output statistics. We demonstrate this method in non-stationary classification, online dynamics learning, and policy adaptation of a vision-language-action model, and find that it is generally more accurate than other smoothing-based methods.

</details>


### [136] [Repurposing Pre-trained LLMs as High Fidelity Continuous Text Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27248)
*Arkanath Pathak,Unnat Jain,Alexander C. Berg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LLMAE，一种将预训练decoder-only大语言模型（Gemma-3-270M）改造为高保真连续文本自编码器的方法，通过结构化注意力掩码在中间层构建固定长度潜在瓶颈，结合LoRA微调与KL正则化，实现长达1024 token文本的近完美重建，并演示了其在潜在文本扩散图像描述任务中的下游应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归LLM通过逐token预测虽具流畅性，但难以进行全局规划，且传统微调存在灾难性遗忘问题；图像领域的高保真自编码器（如VAE）已成为生成模型的标准原语，但文本缺乏对应的忠实连续表示。因此需要一种将文本映射到固定长度连续潜在空间的方法，为下游生成式建模提供有效基底，同时保留原始LLM的生成先验。

Method: LLMAE将单个decoder-only LLM的深度L划分为编码器块（0≤l≤D）与解码器块（D<l≤L-1），在第D层（如第11层）的K个可学习Embed token激活处构建潜在瓶颈z∈R^{K×d}。通过结构化注意力掩码限制解码段对原始输入的可见性，迫使重建信息流经该潜在瓶颈。训练采用复合序列[输入|Embed|输入副本]布局，仅在重建段计算NTP交叉熵损失，并引入KL-z正则化（潜在z趋近N(0,I)）与KL-ref正则化（输出分布锚定冻结参考模型）共同构成总损失。另增加单层加性Transformer Codec（零初始化的逐维度可学习标量乘子）以精炼潜在表示，进一步提升重建质量与潜在流形平滑度。模型实例化为Gemma-3-270M，仅10.7M可训练参数（LoRA+Embed+Codec）。

Result: 在三个领域测试集（C4-News、OpenWebText、CreationMMBench）上，LLMAE在长达1024 token的文本上实现近完美重建（BLEU-4约0.99，BERTScore约0.999，PPL接近源文本），显著优于ICAE（7.46B参数，BLEU降至0.71）和COSMOS（356M参数）。潜在瓶颈消融验证了第11层为最优深度（确认"熵谷"现象），256个潜在token为最佳宽度（4×压缩比）。KL正则化与Codec联合使用将潜在分布均值/标准差压缩至接近N(0,I)（Max从7296降至236）。在潜在扩散图像描述任务中，仅训练111M参数的score网络、总参数量819M，在CapArena基准上VLM Judge得分3.44，超过LLaVA-1.5-7B（2.89）、Share-Captioner（3.34）和同架构的COSMOS替换方案（2.24），逼近前沿VLM水平。潜在噪声扰动实验显示σ≤0.5时重建质量保持鲁棒。插值实验表明潜在空间支持短语级重组。

Conclusion: LLMAE证明了标准decoder-only LLM架构可通过结构化注意力掩码与参数高效微调改造为文本自编码器，实现长文近无损重建并在潜在扩散等下游任务中有效应用。"熵谷"层的存在为深层语义提取提供了原则性依据。潜在空间具备平滑性与短语级重组能力，但当前方法限于1024 token且继承自回归解码延迟，OCR/符号定位与离散计数仍是失败模式，未来可探索更长序列与更多LLM家族。

Abstract: Next-token prediction has enabled highly fluent autoregressive language models, but it represents global structure only indirectly through sequential factorization. In contrast, high-fidelity autoencoders have become a standard primitive in image generation, enabling generative models to operate over continuous latent spaces; text lacks a comparably faithful continuous representation. We propose LLMAE, a method for repurposing a pretrained decoder-only language model as a continuous text autoencoder by exposing an intermediate fixed-length latent bottleneck within its internal activations. Instantiated with a parameter-efficient 270M Gemma 3 model, LLMAE uses structured attention masks, LoRA adaptation, and KL regularization to learn an autoencoding interface that leverages the generative prior of the original LLM. We train LLMAE to reconstruct text sequences up to 1024 tokens, significantly improving on this task to achieve near-perfect reconstruction. Furthermore, we demonstrate the downstream utility of this representation by training a latent text diffusion model for detailed image captioning using the learned LLMAE autoencoder. By mapping text into a fixed-length continuous latent space, our approach provides an effective substrate for downstream adaptation while benefiting from the fluency of the original LLM.

</details>


### [137] [What Converges in the Platonic Representation Hypothesis? Structure over Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27252)
*Junwon You,Mihyun Jang,Sangwoo Mo,Jae-Hun Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战了最近将表征收敛解释为"局部邻域一致性"的观点，通过构建2×2框架区分"关系结构"（哪些样本相关）与"度量几何"（数值距离是否一致），发现关系结构在局部和全局尺度均稳健收敛，而度量几何（距离一致性）随要求严格化显著弱化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期工作（如Gröger et al., 2026）认为，校准后Platonic表征假说中观察到的容量相关趋势仅存在于局部邻域关系（mKNN），而全局相似性度量（CKA、SVCCA等）的容量依赖趋势在校准后消失。作者指出此结论存在混淆：先前的"局部vs全局"对比同时改变了两个维度——结构尺度（局部vs全局）以及比较内容（关系结构vs度量几何），无法确定观察到的差异究竟源于尺度还是比较内容的不同。

Method: 构建2×2控制框架，独立操作两个变量：(1) 结构尺度——局部用互k近邻（mKNN），全局引入H₀骨架重叠（基于Vietoris-Rips滤过的零维持续同调，其死亡边等价于最小生成树MST的边集）；(2) 比较内容——关系结构仅比较关系身份，度量几何额外通过距离一致性权重wᵢⱼ(τ)=exp(-|log D̃_X- log D̃_Y|/τ)比较关系上的距离，τ越小要求越严格。在204对视觉-语言模型和143对视频-语言模型上，采用聚合感知排列校准和Benjamini-Hochberg FDR控制进行评估，并将分析从欧氏距离推广到局部协方差估计的黎曼度量近似。

Result: 关系结构在局部（mKNN）和全局（H₀骨架重叠）尺度均稳健收敛，校准后所有204对视觉-语言模型对均显著，Spearman ρ=0.968；但随距离一致性要求严格化（τ从1降至10⁻⁴），对齐迅速衰减（局部从85.6%降至5.8%，全局从83.0%降至5.5%），容量相关趋势渐进消失（精确置换检验显著）；主要对比在于关系结构与度量几何之间，而局部-全局差异仅在最严格设置下显现（全局显著性40.2% vs 局部89.7%）。黎曼度量和视频-文本表征均复现相同的结构-几何模式。

Conclusion: 表征收敛的关键问题不在于"局部vs全局"，而在于共享的是关系的"拓扑骨架"还是"度量几何"。关系收敛从局部邻域延伸至全局生成结构，而度量几何（距离数值一致性）表现出显著更弱的收敛。这一区分比局部-全局结构性差异更为本质，且这一"结构优于几何"的模式在欧氏/黎曼度量、视觉-语言/视频-文本设置中均成立。

Abstract: The Platonic Representation Hypothesis suggests that increasingly capable models converge toward shared representations. Recent work narrows this claim to shared local neighborhood relationships, finding that capacity-dependent trends in several global similarity measures largely disappear after calibration. We challenge this interpretation by showing that prior local-global comparisons confound structural scale (local versus global) with what is compared: relational structure, defined by which samples are related, versus metric geometry, characterized by quantitative relations such as distances, similarities, or correlations. To disentangle these factors, we construct a controlled $2\times2$ framework that evaluates both relational structure and metric geometry at local and global scales. We introduce $H_0$ skeleton overlap as a global counterpart to mutual $k$-nearest neighbors, together with matched distance-aware variants. Across vision-language models, relational structure exhibits robust representational convergence at both scales after calibration, whereas increasingly stringent distance agreement substantially weakens alignment and progressively flattens the capacity-dependent trend. We further extend the analysis beyond ambient Euclidean geometry by evaluating distance agreement under a Riemannian metric approximation and recover the same structure-geometry pattern. The pattern is also reproduced in video-text representations. Together, these results show that relational convergence extends beyond local neighborhoods to global spanning structure, whereas metric geometry exhibits substantially weaker convergence.

</details>


### [138] [Graph Learning with Spectral Connectivity Priors for Scarce Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27278)
*Mingxiao Liu,Bahar Oveisgharan,Bingyan Zou,Gene Cheung,H. Vicky Zhao,Feifei Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SCoGL框架，在CGL约束的GLASSO目标中加入拉普拉斯谱连通性先验，用于数据稀缺场景下的稀疏图学习。通过投影梯度下降联合优化整个邻接矩阵，实验证明在K≪N条件下提高了图恢复精度和图信号去噪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维数据稀缺场景（K≪N，如生物医学领域）中，从有限样本估计稀疏图结构是严重不适定问题。现有方法要么计算昂贵（SDP约束连通性），要么采用贪心逐边策略无法联合优化。需要一种利用全局连通性结构先验的高效凸优化框架。

Method: 1) 构建两类谱连通性正则化器：Fiedler模式（仅依赖λ₂）和全谱模式（聚合所有非零特征值），结合线性、对数、逆函数三种凸非增形函数，共形成六种代表性正则化器（F、LogF、InvF、R、LogDet及退化的线性全谱）。2) 将其融入CGL约束的GLASSO目标，形成凸优化问题(4)。3) 推导谱正则化器关于拉普拉斯矩阵的梯度公式(子梯度)，利用线性变换𝒯的伴随𝒯*将梯度映射回邻接矩阵域。4) 采用投影梯度下降(PGD)结合Armijo回溯线搜索，在邻接矩阵锥𝒲上联合更新所有边权重，每次迭代复杂度O(N³)。

Result: 在加权Erdős–Rényi图（N=100, p=0.15）上的实验表明：在最稀缺设置K/N=0.1下，SCoGL-LogDet取得最低相对拉普拉斯误差0.3723（较SCoGL-0降低15.5%）；图信号去噪任务中，SCoGL-LogDet在σ=0.2和0.4下均超越对比基线，分别将SCoGL-0到oracle性能差距缩小29.8%和44.4%。SCoGL-R排名第二。整体上五种正则化变体一致优于无连通性先验的SCoGL-0。

Conclusion: 谱连通性正则化在数据稀缺条件下为图学习提供了有效的归纳偏置。Fiedler值和总有效电阻作为全局连通性度量的两种互补视角，分别关注最弱瓶颈和整体路径冗余。将谱先验融入凸GLASSO框架并采用PGD联合优化，相比贪心逐边策略更高效且可获得全局最优解。实验验证了该方法在图恢复和下游图信号处理任务中的双重收益。

Abstract: Learning a sparse graph from scarce data is practically important but challenging. Motivated by the desirable combination of local sparsity and strong global connectivity exhibited by expander-like graphs, we propose spectral connectivity-regularized graph learning (SCoGL), a framework that incorporates a family of Laplacian spectral priors to explicitly promote global connectivity. Specifically, SCoGL augments a combinatorial-Laplacian-constrained graphical lasso (GLASSO) objective over a target adjacency matrix $\mathbf{W}$ with a general connectivity prior computed from Laplacian eigenvalues. We derive gradients for several representative connectivity priors and develop a projected gradient descent (PGD) algorithm with Armijo backtracking to efficiently optimize $\mathbf{W}$. Experiments show that the proposed SCoGL variants improve graph recovery and enhance downstream tasks such as graph signal denoising when signal observations are scarce.

</details>


### [139] [SR-Fraud: An Outcome-Supervised Reflective LLM Agent Framework for Non-Stationary Payment Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27287)
*Xuwei Tan,Yao Ma,Xueru Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SR-Fraud 是一个面向非平稳支付欺诈检测的"结果监督反射式 LLM Agent"框架。其核心思想是将请求时决策与离线适应解耦：冻结的无状态决策 Agent 基于混合情景窗口对每笔交易单次评分，离线反射 Agent 根据成熟错误提出边界假设，再由确定性验证器只接纳有数据支撑的规则进入可执行知识状态。在生产支付欺诈基准上，SR-Fraud 在所有检测指标上优于冻结决策 Agent，点估计优于静态与周期性重训的 CatBoost，并能检测到突发欺诈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时支付欺诈是非平稳流式问题，攻击者持续演化、本地化突发攻击可能在重训之前造成损失。生产中常用的表格分类器（如 GBDT）和规则引擎存在两大局限：单笔交易打分难以捕捉时序行为，且响应分布漂移依赖周期性重训和新特征工程。将 LLM 直接嵌入在线风控管线则面临推理预算缺口（延迟过高）与适配缺口（冻结模型无法对齐领域策略）两大瓶颈。

Method: SR-Fraud 采用请求时-离线分离架构：(1) 冻结、无状态的决策 Agent 基于混合情景窗口（近期交易序列 + 突发/速度摘要）输出有界风险等级 1–5，r≥4 判定为拦截；(2) 每周期离线由结果监督反射 Agent 分析成熟错误，提出结构化候选规则（金额原子+限定符原子构成的合取式）；(3) 确定性验证器使用单边 Wilson 置信下界（针对升级）和上界（针对放行）对候选进行成熟证据核验，仅接纳通过证据门槛的边界规则进入演化中的专家知识状态 K；(4) 知识状态 K 仅编辑边界情况（将 r=3 升级拦截、将 r=4 放行），保持决策 Agent 本身无状态、单次调用。系统在 9 周生产数据上以两周期成熟滞后进行时序回放，强制遵循成熟墙。

Result: 在 16,140 笔交易（含 813 笔确认欺诈，欺诈率 5.04%）的案例-对照基准上：相较冻结决策 Agent（Sonnet 4.6），完整 SR-Fraud 精确率 +5.4、召回率 +7.3、F1 +6.2、美元加权召回 +19.3 个百分点，所有配对自举区间排除零；相较周期性重训 CatBoost，召回与美元召回显著更优；与静态 CatBoost 在精确阻断数对齐（6.08%）的精确匹配下，精确率/召回/F1 仍显著领先。SR-Fraud 成功识别第 8 周的协同低额欺诈突发（+41.7 召回、+46.4 美元召回）。其单次调用请求延迟约 1.3 秒（p50）/2.0 秒（p90），远低于链式思考推理的 47.3 秒。离线消融显示，纯自由文本反射（Reflexion）在 r≥4 阈值下阻断率高达 14.8–41.4%，而 SR-Fraud 通过验证机制保持操作点稳定。对比 FP-Growth、RIPPER、WRAcc 等经典规则挖掘器作为提议器时，LLM 提议器在 F1 与美元召回点估计上均为最优；自托管 9B 后训练模型在性能接近商业 SR-Fraud 的同时 p90 延迟降至 0.94 秒。

Conclusion: SR-Fraud 通过冻结上下文 LLM 与结果监督符号化反射相结合，在保持单次请求时 LLM 调用与可审计知识状态的前提下，应对了非平稳支付欺诈检测中的推理预算缺口与适配缺口。行为上下文与已验证的反射机制在时序回放中相对静态及周期性重训的树模型均带来欺诈捕获的提升；在精确阻断数对齐下该优势依然成立。文章亦讨论了 Wilson 置信界限缺乏正式多重比较校正、专有单数据流限制泛化、固定两周期成熟滞后近似等局限，并指出后训练与符号化反射互为补充：前者提供强基线策略，后者在大模型刷新之间提供可审计的适配通道。

Abstract: Real-time payment fraud detection is a non-stationary streaming prediction problem: adversaries adapt before supervised labels mature, and localized burst attacks can cause losses before retraining. Production systems typically rely on tabular classifiers and rules, which can struggle to capture these emerging sequential patterns before periodic retraining occurs. We present SR-Fraud, an outcome-supervised reflective LLM framework that decouples request-time decisions from offline adaptation. A frozen, stateless agent scores each transaction from a Hybrid Episodic Window to track behavioral shifts, while an offline reflection agent proposes boundary hypotheses from matured errors. A deterministic verifier then admits only supported hypotheses into an executable knowledge state. On a production payment-fraud benchmark, SR-Fraud improves all detection metrics over its frozen decision agent, obtains higher point estimates than static and periodically retrained CatBoost, and detects an emerging fraud burst.

</details>


### [140] [NGN: Learning Neural Network Size as a Differentiable Count](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27291)
*Lixing Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Neurogenesis Network (NGN)，一种将神经网络结构大小建模为可微计数的方法，通过学习一个可学习的边界来选择有序结构组件的前缀，从而在训练权重的同时优化模型容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络的尺寸通常在训练前固定，结构选择与权重优化相分离。这一过程需要专家经验或对候选尺寸的重复训练，限制了自动化程度。已有方法（训练后剪枝、稀疏化、灵活计算、联合结构学习）各有局限。核心动机是：能否直接将模型容量作为可微的"计数"与权重一起优化？

Method: 对每个有序组件组引入一个可学习的边界变量 t ∈ R⁺，通过 sigmoid 门 g_k(t) = sigmoid(β(t - k + δ)) 控制前缀激活。目标函数为 J = L_task + λt + α sin²(πt)，其中 λt 是容量价格项、α sin²(πt) 是整数吸附项。训练时 β 从 4 退火至约 12 以使软门趋近硬门。组件权重设为 w_k = 2⁻ⁿ 以衰减后续函数。部署时直接截断至 round(t)+1 个组件。

Result: 1) 一维回归控制实验：NGN 在不同超参数下形成清晰的整数边界阶梯，软评估与硬截断误差几乎一致；2) 与 L₀、AWNN、STE 等容量学习方法相比，在匹配计数下 L₀ 误差更高、AWNN 拟合更差、STE 易在小计数处停滞；3) 在 VAE 流形实验中，NGN 恢复的秩与真实内在维度 D 在 49/50 案例中完全一致；4) 跨结构应用：在 MNIST/CIFAR-10（CNN）、Cora（GCN）、TinyStories（Transformer 头/FFN/Mamba 状态维度）、递归深度选择、Llama-3 LoRA 秩与适配器宽度学习上均能学习有用容量；5) Llama-3-8B 剪枝实验中，匹配中间策略与 Wanda 表现相当，但 NGN 的优势在于容量分配与任务联合学习。

Conclusion: NGN 证明结构容量可以直接作为可微计数与权重联合优化。容量价格 λ 调控大小-性能权衡，整数吸附 α sin²(πt) 确保可部署性，门锐度退火 β 实现软到硬的过渡。该机制可跨 MLP、CNN、GCN、Transformer、状态空间模型、LoRA 和适配器统一使用，并支持多层异构分配。其优势在于自动尺寸选择而非更快优化或更优性能。但存在局限：候选必须严格有序（无法跳过有害组件）、几何权重限制增长范围、仍需调整 λ 与 α 超参数、可靠的从高到低剪枝恢复并不普遍存在。

Abstract: Neural network size is usually chosen before training, separating architecture selection from weight optimization. We introduce the Neurogenesis Network (NGN), a differentiable parameterization for learning how many ordered structural components a model should use. For each ordered component group, one learnable boundary selects an active prefix while the model parameters are trained. The boundary can grow from a compact initialization and can be deployed by discarding components beyond the learned boundary. Controlled experiments examine convergence of the learned boundary, the performance of deployed prefixes, and comparisons with fixed-size models and alternative approaches to learning capacity. We then apply the same mechanism to MLPs, convolutional and graph networks, Transformers, state-space models, LoRA, and adapters. Across these settings, deploying only the learned prefix usually changes performance little, and the selected architectures perform similarly to fixed models trained at the same size. These results show that structural capacity can be optimized directly as a count.

</details>


### [141] [Live Assistant: Learning Whether, When, and Whom to Assist in Real-World Live Social Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27303)
*Shujian Gao,Jiamei Yan,Yuchen Yang,Penghao Zhou,Qinglei Wang,Tiehan Fan,Yuan Wang,Zuxuan Wu,Yu-gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LiveAssistant 提出了一种面向真实直播场景的混合主动、角色条件化辅助框架，将直播交互建模为四个耦合决策（是否行动、何时行动、面向谁、传达什么），并在统一的结构化动作协议（OBS/MEM/ANS）下由单一自回归策略执行。论文还构建了轨迹数据引擎、人审基准以及 MA-MSFT 与 SM-GSPO 两阶段训练方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直播是一种持续演化的多模态社交环境，视听内容、观众弹幕礼物、主播行为与平台信号共同演进，不同参与者在不同时刻产生不同的信息需求。已有研究分别覆盖流式感知、时机决策、对话主动性与地址对象选择，但缺乏一个统一的策略同时决定是否介入、何时介入、面向谁以及说什么。本文旨在将直播辅助定义为一个统一的混合主动决策问题。

Method: 1) 问题形式化：将直播建模为因果序列 x_t=(v_t,a_t,e_t)，每 10 秒间隔由单一策略从 h_t=(p,x_≤t,y_<t) 选择状态 z_t∈{OBS, MEM, ANS}；ANS 进一步指定 recipient∈{viewer, host}、任务与 grounding 内容。2) 数据引擎：重建真实直播为多轮轨迹，对齐原生音视频与评论/礼物/观众动态/房间元数据，产生 320+ 小时优化轨迹与含 275 clip、13,812 决策区间的人审评测集。3) MA-MSFT：在标准词表似然上对协议标记加权，并加入受限状态分布上的类加权 focal loss，以强化稀疏结构决策。4) SM-GSPO：在自生成轨迹上进行 on-policy 多轮 rollout，结合 GSPO 序列比与 turn-level / trajectory-level 优势，提供分层信用分配。5) 长流推理：保留近 12 轮全分辨率上下文，更早状态压缩或淘汰。基座采用 Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B 的 thinker 组件。

Result: 在 14 间房、38 小时、7 语言的人审基准上，LiveAssistant 取得 State All 71.14、Recipient All 72.67、Task 58.41、Sem. 81.78，系统性优于代表性流式视频基线（Dispider、LiveCC、StreamingVLM、LiveStarPro、MMDuet2）以及统一评测下的通用多模态基线（Qwen3-VL/3.5、Qwen3-Omni、InternVL3.5）。消融显示：普通多轮 SFT → MA-MSFT → +turn-level GSPO → +SM-GSPO 在 State、MEM、Recipient、Task 上逐步改善，全量 SM-GSPO 在 MEM recall（48.20）与结构一致性上带来最大增益。

Conclusion: 该工作将直播辅助确立为共享社交流中的选择性参与，给出了统一的问题形式化、可重建的因果轨迹数据引擎、人审基准，以及针对稀疏结构决策和自生成多轮轨迹的 MA-MSFT 与 SM-GSPO 训练方案。实验表明该框架在状态判断、收件人路由与任务选择上均稳定优于流式与通用多模态基线，为直播场景中可主动、可节制、可追溯的多模态助手奠定了基础。局限在于基准覆盖类别与语言有限、面向主播的数据较少、内容质量依赖自动评判器、10 秒粒度的时序精度有限，部署还需考虑同意、隐私与角色控制。

Abstract: Livestreams are long-lasting interactive environments where audiovisual content, viewer activity, host behavior, and platform signals evolve together, creating assistance needs that emerge from the stream itself. We introduce \liveassistant, a framework for mixed-initiative, role-conditioned assistance that formulates livestream interaction as four coupled decisions: \textbf{whether to act, when to act, whom to address, and what to communicate}. At each 10-second interval, one autoregressive policy consumes native audio and video with synchronized comments, gifts, viewer dynamics, and room metadata, then selects \textsc{OBS}, \textsc{MEM}, or \textsc{ANS}. \textsc{OBS} remains silent, \textsc{MEM} records a private semantic update, and \textsc{ANS} specifies a recipient, task, and grounded message. To support this task, we build a trajectory engine that reconstructs real livestream sessions into structured causal supervision, yielding over 320 hours of optimization trajectories and a human-reviewed benchmark of 275 clips and 13,812 decision intervals. We train the policy with Marker-Aware Multiturn Supervised Fine-Tuning (MA-MSFT), which strengthens sparse structured decisions, followed by Streaming Multiturn GSPO (SM-GSPO), which optimizes self-generated trajectories with turn- and trajectory-level credit. On the held-out benchmark, \liveassistant reaches 71.14 state accuracy, 72.67 recipient accuracy, and 58.41 task accuracy, with consistent gains over representative streaming and general multimodal baselines. Together, the formulation, benchmark, and training framework establish livestream assistance as selective participation in a shared social stream.

</details>


### [142] [Discrete Diffusion Models via Evolving Variational Autoregressive Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27306)
*Kewen Pan,Ying Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于变分自回归网络(VAN)的离散扩散模型,用于自旋系统的归一化概率分布建模,突破了张量网络方法在高维晶格上的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于分数的扩散模型无法表示归一化密度,难以直接计算似然和自由能;现有的基于张量网络(MPS)的归一化离散扩散模型受限于准一维几何结构,无法有效处理高维晶格系统。

Method: 使用变分自回归网络(VAN)参数化每个扩散时刻的归一化概率分布;通过单自旋翻转的马尔可夫跳跃算子构建前向加噪过程,采用Euler离散和tau-leaping两种反向去噪策略;将框架与Metropolis-Hastings MCMC集成,通过自适应扩散步数平衡接受率与去相关性。

Result: 在2D(16×16)和3D(4×4×4)Ising模型上,跨越有序态、临界态和无序态温度区间,准确计算了自由能、能量和磁化强度等热力学量;在临界温度下对8×30、9×30圆柱Ising模型的磁化强度预测优于张量网络方法;DDM-MCMC在低温淬火中比LMMC保持更高的接受率和样本多样性。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个基于神经网络的离散扩散模型框架,具有归一化概率分布表示,适用于任意维度的晶格结构,为统计物理中的平衡与非平衡模拟提供了新工具。

Abstract: Conventional score-based diffusion models learn scores without representing normalized densities, whereas tractable normalized models support both sampling and direct likelihood evaluation. A recent tensor-network approach provides such a representation but is largely restricted to low-dimensional lattices. Here we introduce a discrete diffusion model that parameterizes normalized probability distributions using variational autoregressive networks. Explicit Markov jump operators govern the forward noising and reverse denoising dynamics, extending discrete diffusion models with normalized distributions to spin systems on higher-dimensional lattices. We apply this framework to the two- and three-dimensional Ising models across ordered, critical, and disordered regimes, accurately computing thermodynamic quantities including free energy, energy, and magnetization. We further integrate the framework with Monte Carlo sampling, using adaptive diffusion steps to maintain high acceptance rates even at low temperatures while enhancing sample diversity. These results establish a neural-network framework for the discrete diffusion model with normalized probability distributions.

</details>


### [143] [Anomaly-Free Self-Optimization via AUC Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27362)
*Kevin Wilkinghoff,Zheng-Hua Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需异常数据的自优化方法，将基于AUC的理论上界作为可微优化目标，直接优化异常检测（ASD）系统的连续参数（如集成权重），并引入了可学习的伪异常分数重缩放机制。在DCASE 2022-2025基准测试中显著优于传统模型选择和基于开发集的参数选择方法，且对伪异常构造方式不敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异常检测系统通常缺乏异常数据进行训练和调参，即使是无训练方法也常依赖异常开发集进行超参数选择。为消除这种对异常数据的依赖，作者希望将AUC理论上界从"选择准则"转变为"可微优化目标"，实现真正的无异常自优化。

Method: 1. 将AUC下界公式 ℬ(θ)（基于正常样本和伪异常的得分均值与方差）作为可微目标函数，通过梯度下降直接优化连续参数；2. 以集成权重优化为具体实例，使用softmax参数化权重；3. 引入可学习的伪异常分数缩放因子 α=exp(s)，联合优化权重与缩放参数；4. 伪异常构造包括Random、Feature、Sequence、Element等无需元数据的方法。

Result: 在DCASE 2022-2025四个基准数据集上，使用OpenL3、BEATs、EAT、Dasheng四种嵌入模型×52种池化配置的208个候选系统进行实验：nn+ldn设置下相比Equal集成提升0.83个百分点，相比Pseudo-AUC-Selected提升1.93个百分点；nn+ldn+VarMin设置下相比Fixed-Selected提升0.67个百分点；且直接优化对伪异常构造方式的选择敏感性较低，无需元数据的Feature构造即可取得最佳性能。

Conclusion: 该工作证明AUC理论下界不仅可以用于模型选择，还能作为优化信号实现异常检测系统的无异常自优化；可学习的伪异常缩放机制有效适配了优化尺度；该框架未来可扩展到池化和表示参数等更广泛的ASD系统参数优化。

Abstract: Anomalies are rare, and anomalous data are often unavailable during development, making it difficult to determine which anomaly detection models and configurations will generalize to unseen anomalies. Recent approaches address this challenge by generating pseudo-anomalies and using bounds on the achievable area under the ROC curve (AUC) to select the optimal configuration from a finite set of candidates. Instead, we use the AUC bound as a differentiable, anomaly-free objective for directly optimizing continuous parameters of anomaly detection systems. We demonstrate this framework by optimizing ensemble weights and introducing a learnable score-rescaling mechanism that adapts pseudo-anomaly scores, enabling optimization beyond a predefined candidate set. Experiments across multiple datasets and embedding models show that AUC-bound optimization achieves significant performance gains over conventional model selection and prior development-set-based parameter selection. The results further show that direct optimization is less sensitive to the choice of pseudo-anomaly construction.

</details>


### [144] [Active Learning for Biodiversity Monitoring: From Label Efficiency to Reliable Ecological Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27409)
*Ben McEwen,Shiqi Zhang,Dan Stowell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本综述系统梳理了主动学习（AL）在生物多样性监测（声学与图像模态）中的应用，涵盖164篇文献。核心论点是：现有AL研究仅证明"标签高效训练"，但忽略了专家预算需同时支持训练、验证及下游生态推断的"预算完整性"问题。AL的非随机性使其标签不能用于验证、校准或阈值选择，这一矛盾被广泛忽视。文章提出预算感知的AL教程、各研究层面的路线图，并指出蝙蝠、昆虫、两栖类、鱼类及多模态应用为空白领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动声学记录器和相机陷阱产生的数据量远超专家标注能力，形成专家标注瓶颈。主动学习虽被证明能减少标签需求，但生物多样性监测项目面临更广泛的预算分配问题：如何在固定预算下同时保障模型训练、验证和生态推断的可靠性。现有AL研究的评估实践存在根本缺陷——未将验证标签纳入预算，未考虑查询偏差对生态推断的影响。

Method: 采用系统检索方法（2025年11月Google Scholar搜索，2026年4月补充搜索），筛选164篇相关文献。开发24字段提取框架（涵盖元数据、AL配置、性能评估、应用域、技术细节、上下文等七类）。使用生成式AI辅助信息提取，所有内容由作者人工核实。文章组织为：AL循环教程式介绍（含预算跟踪）、文献系统综述（按模态/分类群/模型/查询策略/评估实践/工具）、方法论路线图（查询偏差纠正、预算分配、主动标注）。

Result: 文献分布特征：(1) 模态——声学53%、相机陷阱42%、其他5%；(2) 分类群——多类群35%、陆生哺乳动物26%、鸟类20%，蝙蝠完全缺席，昆虫和蛙类几乎仅限于多类群数据集；(3) 模型——深度网络占94%以上，越来越多使用预训练编码器（BirdNET、Perch、AVES、MegaDetector、BioCLIP）的冻结或微调；(4) 评估——学习曲线为主流协议，但缺少随机基线、逐类别结果、校准分析，几乎从未达到预算完整标准。报告的平均标注减少量为64%（n=7），最高89%。仅有20/66（30%）分类群研究为实际部署而非方法演示。首个生物声学AL共享任务于2026年在BioDCASE运行。

Conclusion: 主动学习在生物多样性监测中的价值标准应从"节省多少标签"转向"在固定专家预算下能否提供可信的模型和生态推断"。当前文献证明了标签高效训练，但验证实践、预算核算和下游生态目标关注不足。可立即行动的建议：保留独立代表性验证集并计入预算；对照重复随机基线报告结果；在数据集元数据中记录选择机制；以专家时间而非样本数量度量成本。研究议程：面向生态量的查询目标、训练-验证间的形式化预算分配、查询偏差纠正方法、以及蝙蝠、昆虫、鱼类和多模态监测等空白领域。

Abstract: Limited expert annotation capacity is a pervasive constraint in biodiversity monitoring. Passive acoustic recorders and camera traps generate data faster than experts can analyse them. Machine learning (ML) models can process these data at scale, but their reliability depends on the quality, quantity, and coverage of labelled samples, so expert time remains a constraint. Active learning (AL) eases this bottleneck by selecting, under a fixed annotation budget, the samples expected to improve a model most, and published evidence shows it can reduce the labels needed to reach a target performance. Monitoring programmes, however, face a broader question: how should a limited expert budget be divided so that model training, validation, and the ecological estimates built on model outputs all remain reliable? Because AL selects samples non-randomly, its labels are unsuitable for validation, calibration, or threshold selection, a tension rarely acknowledged. We synthesise AL research across acoustic and image modalities and identify gaps and opportunities. Most studies evaluate query strategies on pre-labelled benchmarks with simulated annotators; deployments in real monitoring workflows are rare and concentrate on birds and cetaceans. Bats, insects, amphibians, and fish are underrepresented, and multimodal applications remain largely unexplored. Evaluation centres on headline reductions in annotation effort, often without random-sampling baselines, per-class results, or calibration analysis, and rarely accounts for the labels required for validation. We provide a tutorial treatment of the AL loop that makes these budget decisions explicit, and a roadmap towards AL methods that support label-efficient training, validation, and trustworthy downstream ecological inference.

</details>


### [145] [When Labels Are Scarce: An Oscillatory State Space Model for Vibration Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27411)
*Mainak Mallick,Seung-Kyum Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DualRes，一种紧凑型振荡状态空间模型，专为边缘设备部署和小样本标注条件下的振动故障诊断而设计。该模型结合两个中心对齐的频谱视图，通过选择性振荡记忆捕获快速变化和精细频率结构，在39,528参数的编码器下于多个轴承和齿轮箱基准上验证了其性能与效率的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械振动故障诊断面临两个核心难题：(1)代表性故障的有标注记录稀缺，传统随机窗口划分会导致数据泄漏；(2)边缘部署对模型存储、推理延迟和内存有严格约束。同时机械振动包含局部激励、谐振响应和时域调制的复合结构，需要既能捕获短时变化又能区分邻近频率的表示方法。现有评估协议往往混淆标注时长与记录多样性，无法真正反映数据效率。

Method: DualRes的核心方法包括三个组成部分：(1)对齐频谱表示——对同一振动信号分别计算短窗N_s=256和长窗N_l=1024的STFT，通过裁剪使帧中心τ_j对齐，在500Hz的token速率下产生T=249帧对齐对，分别提供4ms/250Hz和16ms/62.5Hz的时频分辨率，经仿射投影拼接为64维token。(2)选择性振荡记忆——每个通道维护K=8个复数状态，通过输入相关的阻尼α_t,d控制记忆保持时间，结合共享读写向量和学习的振荡频率（10–200Hz对数间隔），用实数选择性扫描核实现复数递推，确保状态有界。(3)评估协议——采用记录级划分和明确的标注时长核算（2.048–36.864秒/类），固定训练时长为300次Adam更新，主指标为记录级macro-F1。

Result: 在主要齿轮箱基准MCC5上，DualRes在七个标注预算中的六个达到SOTA，以约6标注秒/类相比最强比较器MambaSL和MiniRocket的较优者提升macro-F1 16.1个百分点；在PHM2009配置识别任务上提升24.91个百分点；相对选择性状态空间基线实现1.5倍记录级加速和24.8倍检查点存储缩减（0.164 vs 4.065 MiB）。在六个轴承数据集上表现呈任务依赖性权衡：DualRes在Paderborn/KAIST/UORED/Ottawa超越或追平MambaSL（最大提升7.71pp于KAIST），但CWRU和HUST落后；MiniRocket在HUST几何变换下最佳。组件消融显示去除振荡旋转使MCC5下降8.73pp、UORED下降27.18pp，但Paderborn略有上升；全前端在CWRU/UORED/HUST优于任一单分辨率控制。

Conclusion: DualRes验证了振荡记忆作为有限标注暴露下振动诊断紧凑方法的有效性，记录级划分、明确时长核算和受控支持分配为评估数据效率提供了严格框架。振荡记忆在齿轮箱跨工况迁移任务中贡献显著，但轴承性能揭示了任务依赖的权衡。未来工作将探索自适应频谱分辨率和流式振荡推理，并在更多机型和独立采集的运行条件下测试泛化能力。

Abstract: Machine fault diagnosis from vibration requires learning from scarce labelled fault recordings while meeting the computational constraints of edge devices for local inference. We introduce DualRes, a compact oscillatory state-space model that combines two complementary spectral views of vibration, capturing rapid changes and fine frequency structure. Time-aligned views are processed by selective oscillatory memory, which learns how long to retain temporal patterns. The encoder contains 39,528 parameters. We evaluate supervised learning across six bearing datasets and a gearbox benchmark, with an additional gearbox pilot. Recording-level splits and explicit accounting of labelled duration distinguish data efficiency from repeated exposure to correlated samples. On the main gearbox benchmark, DualRes achieves state-of-the-art performance among the nine evaluated methods at six of seven label budgets. With about six labelled seconds per class, it improves macro-F1 by 16.1 percentage points over the next strongest comparator.
