On the same benchmark, DualRes achieves a 1.44-fold recording-level speedup and a 24.8-fold reduction in checkpoint storage relative to a selective state-space baseline under matched hardware and runtime conditions. Bearing results reveal task-dependent trade-offs. These findings support oscillatory memory as a compact approach to vibration diagnosis under limited labelled exposure.

</details>


### [146] [Counterfactual Constraint-Conditioned On-Policy Distillation for Multi-Constraint Instruction Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27421)
*Yanzhao Zheng,Yuanqiang Yu,Tianze Xu,Chao Ma,Zhentao Zhang,Jihuai Zhu,Baohua Dong,Hangcheng Zhu,Ruohui Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CC-OPD（反事实约束条件在策略蒸馏）通过留一法教师评分在冻结教师上提取每约束、每词元的信号，作为采样词元OPD奖励的附加塑形项，在七个指令遵循基准测试中跨两个Qwen模型对实现了最高平均分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多约束指令遵循中，现有方法（外部验证器强化学习或单一全上下文教师蒸馏）在约束数量增多时出现"约束稀释"——全上下文教师将概率质量分散到多个约束上，难以解耦单个约束对每词元分布的贡献。

Method: 对每个学生rollout，冻结教师在完整约束集C和每个留一上下文C\{ci}下各评分一次（共|C|+1次前向传播），得到每约束对数似然偏移δᵢ(t)。将这些偏移求和、对称裁剪后加到vanilla OPD奖励上：r_t = -KL̂(t) + λ·δ̂(t)。当聚合偏移为零时，退化为vanilla OPD；无需外部验证器。通过Möbius分解证明LOO聚合按基数|T|加权交互项，保留LA（全删）无法恢复的每约束敏感性矩阵。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-1.5B/7B和Qwen3-4B/4B两对模型上，CC-OPD在七个基准（IFEval/IFBench/MulDimIF/ComplexBench/InfoBench/FollowBench/CFBench）上均取得最高平均分（51.4%和64.3%）。1.5B学生超过其7B RL训练教师，在MulDimIF总体+2.8pp、最难Level-4 +6.3pp。在梯度步效率上以15×更少步骤匹配其峰值。消融实验确认全上下文LOO锚优于LA（均匀加权）、RSS（随机子集）和NAO（单约束锚）。

Conclusion: CC-OPD将约束条件监督从"添加式"范式（用外部评分丰富监督通道）重新框架为"消融式"范式（对同一冻结教师在反事实条件子集上查询），暴露单次全上下文前向无法产生的每约束监督。这在多约束指令遵循中带来了实质性的收益，并罕见地使学生超越教师。

Abstract: Multi-constraint instruction following requires a model to respond to a query under many simultaneously active constraints. Even strong instruction-tuned models still routinely violate some of them. Existing approaches either augment supervision with sequence- or token-level RL rewards from external verifiers or learned graders, or use on-policy distillation (OPD) against a single full-context teacher whose probability mass becomes diluted as more constraints become simultaneously active. We propose CC-OPD (Counterfactual Constraint-Conditioned On-Policy Distillation), which inverts the standard supervision-generation direction in distillation. Rather than enriching the teacher with information beyond what the student sees, CC-OPD ablates each constraint from the teacher's conditioning in turn, and constructs the per-constraint signal from the resulting per-token probability differentials. The resulting per-token leave-one-out log-likelihood shifts are summed, clipped, and added to the vanilla OPD reward as a token-level shaping term. All shaping terms are obtained from the frozen teacher, without an external verifier during distillation, and the reward equals vanilla OPD wherever the aggregate shift is zero. Across two Qwen model pairs and seven benchmarks, CC-OPD achieves the highest average among all evaluated student-training methods. A 1.5B student trained with CC-OPD surpasses its own 7B RL-trained teacher on the MulDimIF benchmark.

</details>


### [147] [Stable Neural Decoding Across Sessions via Task-Conditioned Latent Alignment for Brain-Machine Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27441)
*Canyang Zhao,Bolin Peng,J. Patrick Mayo,Ce Ju,Bing Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Task-Conditioned Latent Alignment (TCLA)框架,用于解决侵入式脑机接口(BMI)中跨会话神经解码不稳定的问题。TCLA通过两阶段训练:源会话学习行为监督的低维参考潜表示,目标会话通过任务条件化的多核MMD对齐到该潜空间。在7个非人灵长类数据集(含1,356个被试内会话对和2,134个跨被试会话对)上的评估表明,TCLA显著降低了负R²失败率(长期会话仅6.8%,跨被试仅12.9%),且解码性能显著优于NoMAD、ADAN和Aligned FA基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 侵入式BMI长期使用面临核心挑战:由于电极位移、记录神经元集合变化等因素,不同录制会话间的神经群体活动存在显著分布漂移,导致基于历史数据训练的解码器在新会话上性能严重下降。现有潜在对齐方法仅匹配源-目标整体分布,忽略了不同任务条件在潜空间中占据不同区域这一任务依赖结构,导致解码关系难以跨会话保持。

Method: TCLA采用两阶段框架:(1)源会话训练阶段——使用自适应read-in/read-out接口(参数化适应不同神经元数量)+基于S4结构化状态空间层的时序自编码器+高斯时间平滑,联合优化Poisson神经重构损失、潜空间L2正则化和行为预测损失(含二阶曲率匹配项),构建行为信息保留的低维参考潜表示;(2)目标会话对齐阶段——冻结共享自编码器和行为解码器,仅更新目标read-in/read-out,使用任务条件标签将潜表示分组后,通过多核最大均值差异(Multi-kernel MMD)对每个任务条件分别进行分布对齐。关键创新包括:行为监督的源表示学习、群体自适应的read-in/read-out设计、任务条件化(而非全局)对齐策略。

Result: 在7个非人灵长类神经数据集(覆盖平面到达、等长腕力、平行光标、眼动追踪4种范式,涵盖M1、PMv、PMd、FEF/MT皮层区域)上的系统评估:(1)长期跨会话解码:平均R²=0.476±0.014,负R²失败率仅6.8%(NoMAD:48.9%,ADAN:47.9%,Aligned FA:53.2%);(2)1,356个被试内跨时间间隔会话对:平均R²=0.371±0.009,失败率6.8%(基线方法失败率62%-72%);(3)2,134个跨被试会话对:中位R²=0.221,失败率12.9%(基线失败率88.9%-98.5%);(4)消融研究证实行为监督使源会话R²从0.314提升至0.909,任务条件化对齐使失败率从41.6%降至6.8%。PCA潜空间可视化表明对齐后目标会话轨迹恢复了与源会话相似的几何结构。

Conclusion: TCLA通过将行为监督的源表示学习与任务条件化的潜空间对齐相结合,显著提升了跨会话和跨被试神经解码的稳定性和鲁棒性,主要体现在大幅降低负R²解码失败率而非仅提升平均精度。该框架验证了"跨会话稳定性不仅依赖于找到共享低维表示,更取决于保留何种信息以及对齐何种结构"这一原则,为长期BMI应用提供了实用方案。局限性在于目标适应仍需离散任务条件标签,未来工作将探索自监督条件推断、多会话参考表示和在线持续适应。

Abstract: Achieving stable long-term neural decoding in invasive brain-machine interfaces (BMIs) remains challenging due to variations in recorded neural populations across sessions. Current latent alignment approaches may overlook task-dependent structure during cross-session adaptation. We propose Task-Conditioned Latent Alignment (TCLA), a framework that stabilizes neural decoding by learning a shared latent space. TCLA learns a low-dimensional source representation using neural reconstruction and continuous behavioral supervision. During target-session adaptation, the shared representation is fixed, while target neural activity is mapped into the source latent space by aligning source and target distributions separately for each task condition. We evaluated TCLA on seven nonhuman primate datasets spanning multiple tasks. In long-term cross-session evaluation, TCLA achieved a mean $R^2$ of $0.476\pm0.014$ with a negative $R^2$ failure rate of only 6.8\%. Across 1,356 within-subject session pairs, TCLA achieved a mean $R^2$ of $0.371\pm0.009$ with a failure rate of 6.8\%. Across 2,134 cross-subject session pairs, TCLA achieved a mean $R^2$ of $0.218\pm0.004$ with a failure rate of 12.9\%, substantially better than those of the comparison methods. These results demonstrate that by preserving behaviorally relevant and task-dependent latent structure, TCLA improves the robustness of neural decoding across recording sessions and subjects. The source code is publicly available at \href{ this https URL }{ this https URL }.

</details>


### [148] [Learning Where to Look: A Shared Relative-Alignment Module for Time-Series Forecasting and PPG-to-Vital-Sign Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27473)
*Ragamayi Puli,Shunya Nagashima*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ROOSTER是一个统一的条件模块，通过在交叉注意力中学习"周期梳状"相对位置偏置（periodic-comb bias），同时解决PPG到生命体征重建（恒等对齐）和多变量时间序列预测（季节性滞后）两个任务。该模块可学得每个头的中心位置、周期和锐度参数，自动适配任务对应的偏移模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两个看似不同的任务（PPG信号重建和长周期时间序列预测）本质上都是"从条件序列生成目标序列"。现有方法分别用硬编码的恒等跳跃（重建）和固定的季节性分解（预测）来指定每个目标位置读取条件序列的哪个位置，二者既不能跨任务迁移，也无法在数据本身不满足预设假设（如运动伪影、无周期数据）时优雅退化。作者认为这些方法实际上只是同一个"学习相对偏移"问题的特例。

Method: ROOSTER是一个跨注意力模块，从目标patch查询到条件patch键进行注意力计算，其logit上叠加一个仅依赖于相对偏移 δ=j−i 的"周期梳状"偏置 b_h(δ) = -κ_h(1 - cos(2π(δ-φ_h)/P_h))，其中每个头独立学习中心φ_h、周期P_h和锐度κ_h。该模块零初始化投影接入两个主机模型（PENGUIN用于重建，DecompSSM用于预测），同时引入一个可诊断的pinball分位数辅助损失来修复共享特征提取器的有效秩崩溃问题。

Result: 在生命体征重建上，ROOSTER在4个标准基准（PPG-DaLiA、WildPPG、BIDMC呼吸、WESAD呼吸）的HR/RR误差上均超过已发表基线；在匹配训练设置下对PENGUIN的4/5任务胜出。在多变量预测上，在ECL、Weather、ETTm2、PEMS04四个基准上取得最佳horizon-averaged MSE，在三种子匹配训练下对DecompSSM在24个数据集-预测长度设置中胜出20个。消融实验证实相对偏置（而非内容匹配）承担了对齐功能；学到的周期在15分钟数据上接近一天（11-13个patch），在Exchange（无周期数据）上退化为~1.7，且确实在无周期数据集上性能下降。

Conclusion: ROOSTER通过一个学习到的周期梳状偏置统一了两个任务的对齐机制，能够从数据中自动发现恒等对齐或季节性滞后并将其读出。该模块仅要求patch序列之间存在某种偏移关系，因此有望推广到其他条件序列生成任务和时间序列基础模型。未来工作将聚焦于在对应关系本身被破坏时（如腕戴PPG的运动伪影）增强条件机制的鲁棒性。

Abstract: PPG-to-vital-sign reconstruction turns a wrist-worn photoplethysmogram into clinical waveforms such as the ECG. Long-horizon multivariate time-series forecasting underpins planning in energy, weather, and traffic. Both generate a target sequence from a condition sequence, and current models hard-code where each target position reads it, as a same-position copy or seasonal recurrence, so neither transfers between tasks. We propose ROOSTER, one conditioning module that handles vital-sign reconstruction and time-series forecasting alike by learning this correspondence. Its core is a periodic-comb bias over the target-condition offset whose center, period, and sharpness are learned per head, so one module settles on the identity alignment or a seasonal lag and reports which it found. On vital-sign reconstruction from PPG, ROOSTER outperformed the published baselines on four heart-rate and respiratory-rate benchmarks. On multivariate time-series forecasting, it achieved the best horizon-averaged MSE on four benchmarks and outperformed the forecasting model it extends on 20 of 24 dataset-horizon settings under matched three-seed training. An ablation study indicated that the relative bias, not content matching, carried the alignment.

</details>


### [149] [ProCredit: From Outcome Rewards to Progress Credit in Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27532)
*Ming Ma,Yi Zhu,Yiran Zhong,Feida Zhu,Chonghan Liu,Pengkun Jiao,Qichao Wang,Yanhao Jia,Tianming Yang,Steven Hoi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProCredit提出了一种基于可验证进度的双层信用分配机制，将任务接受检查在每一步重新运行以获得可验证的中间进度信号，转化为turn级奖励，在轨迹间和轨迹内两个层级进行信用分配，取代仅依赖最终结果的GRPO风格训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于结果奖励的agentic RL方法存在三大信息损失：(1)全失败组无训练梯度；(2)失败轨迹无法按完成进度区分；(3)推进任务的turn与仅查询环境的turn获得相同信用。先前工作要么将比较单元从轨迹细化到step（但信号仍来自最终成功），要么训练奖励模型提供中间信号（估计而非验证）。作者观察到任务接受检查可在中间状态上运行，使进度与结果一样可验证。

Method: 核心方法包括：(1)可验证任务进度：定义Φ(s) = 通过的检查比例/总数；(2)进度奖励：r_t = c(Φ_t - Φ_{t-1})，即每turn前后进度变化；(3)双层信用分配——轨迹级优势A_traj = 标准化(S_i)用于跨尝试比较，turn级优势A_turn = G_t - mean(G)用于轨迹内信用分配；(4)使用γ=1的无折扣回报。轨迹score S = R + c(Φ_T - Φ_0)仅依赖初末状态但保留终态完成信息。

Result: 在AppWorld上基于Qwen3.5三个规模（4B/9B/35B）训练，在两个测试集（Test-Normal含168任务、Test-Challenge含417任务含训练未见应用）上，ProCredit在所有规模均取得最高TGC，超过最强结果奖励基线（DAPO等）4.1个百分点（4B Test-Normal）。ToolSandbox环境同样显示改进方向。BFCL函数调用能力未下降。消融实验表明：仅将最终进度加入轨迹score（GRPO-Φ）不提升性能；turn级信用显著优于token级信用；γ=0.95的折扣因子引入"长度税"，使模型生成更短轨迹且在中等和困难任务上损失精度。

Conclusion: 进度信号仅在按发生时刻分配信用时才有效。ProCredit通过在每步重新运行接受检查获得可验证进度，并在轨迹级和turn级两个粒度分配信用，在三个模型规模上均优于结果奖励和基于进度的基线方法。折扣因子对多轮agent RL有显著负面影响，无折扣（γ=1）在所有规模上更优。论文理论证明进度塑形不改变最优策略集（当任务可解时），仅在失败尝试间细化排序。

Abstract: Long-horizon agentic tasks require an agent to modify an environment through a sequence of tool calls, with success determined by the final state. The standard recipe assigns a single outcome reward at the end and compares trajectories sampled for the same task. As a result, a group with no successful trajectory yields no training signal, failed attempts cannot be told apart by how close they came to completion, and turns that advance the task receive the same credit as turns that only query the environment. Prior work refines the unit of comparison from the trajectory to the step, or trains a reward model to supply intermediate signal: the former still derives its signal from final success alone, and the latter estimates it with a model. We observe that the acceptance checks that decide success can also be run on intermediate states, so progress is as verifiable as the outcome. We propose ProCredit, which turns this verified progress into credit: it reruns the acceptance checks after each turn, rewards the turn by its change in progress, and uses these rewards to assign credit both across attempts at the same task and across the turns within a trajectory. Starting from Qwen3.5 base models at three scales on AppWorld, ProCredit outperforms outcome-reward baselines and progress-based baselines in task completion rate at every scale on both test sets, exceeding the strongest outcome-reward baseline by 4.1 percentage points at 4B, and results in a second environment show the same direction of improvement. Ablations show that adding the final progress to the trajectory score alone does not improve performance: the gain comes from crediting progress to the turn where it occurs.

</details>


### [150] [VCMM: Variance-Calibrated Momentum for Multimodal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27577)
*Zhongjing Gu,Chenyang Huang,Yufa Feng,Chong He,Qinxu Ding,Yiming Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VCMM（方差校准动量）方法，针对多模态联合训练中的模态不平衡问题，通过为不同模态自适应地分配动量系数来解决共享动量次优的问题。该方法在线估计各模态的minibatch噪声和时序漂移，利用卡尔曼启发的控制器将其转化为模态特定的动量，无需额外网络前向/反向传播或显式学习率缩放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态联合训练中，主流方法（梯度调制、目标优化、训练策略调整）大多只调控当前步的更新，而忽略了动量优化器中跨步梯度累积信息（梯度记忆）的影响。论文发现不同模态具有不同的时变漂移-噪声比（Q/R），共享的动量系数实际上对各模态并非最优，需要为不同模态分配不同的、时变的梯度记忆。

Method: 1. **分析式分类器探针**：复用前向传播的表征和logits，通过将minibatch拆分为两个交错半batch来估计minibatch噪声R，通过连续步间梯度差减去噪声贡献来估计时序漂移Q。2. **漂移-噪声比控制信号**：ρ=Q/R，通过稳态随机游走关系映射为时序增益K（卡尔曼启发）。3. **跨模态居中（log-odds空间）**：保留模态间相对差异，同时将公共记忆水平锚定到基础动量β₀。4. **精确偏差校正**：使用时变动量乘积∏βₘ,τ对首阶矩进行精确偏差校正，处理时变动量下的初始化偏差。5. **模态参数更新**：模态特定参数使用模态特定动量，共享/融合参数保留β₀。

Result: 在四个多模态基准（CREMA-D、KSounds、Twitter、NVGesture，覆盖音视频、图文、三模态设置）的8个指标上均取得最佳性能。KSounds上比最强基线提升2.36% Acc和2.32% mAP。探针估计的Q_m和R_m与编码器级别估计的Pearson相关性分别为0.9439和0.9306。仅带来13.6%的显存开销和较小的训练时间开销。消融实验验证了噪声减除、log-odds居中、精确偏差校正和时间变动量的必要性。固定非对称动量优于共享动量，完整VCMM优于冻结动量版本。λ=1时性能最佳。

Conclusion: 本文从梯度记忆的角度重新审视模态不平衡问题，揭示了不同模态具有不同且时变的梯度动态特性，证明了共享动量在多模态联合训练中的次优性。所提出的VCMM方法通过在线估计minibatch噪声和时序漂移，自适应地为各模态分配梯度记忆，无需显式学习率缩放，在四个基准上以适度的计算开销实现了一致性提升，并有效缓解了模态不平衡。

Abstract: Multimodal joint training often suffers from modality imbalance, where a dominant modality suppresses the optimization of others. Existing methods mainly balance modality learning by modulating gradient magnitudes or directions, modifying optimization objectives, or adjusting training strategies, with most interventions focusing on the current update. However, when combined with widely used momentum-based optimizers, the update also incorporates accumulated information from previous gradients, which is not explicitly addressed by current-step modulation alone. To address this issue, we propose Variance-Calibrated MomentuM (VCMM), which adapts gradient memory to modality-specific gradient dynamics. Specifically, VCMM estimates minibatch noise and temporal drift online and uses their relative strength to determine modality-specific momentum through a Kalman-inspired controller. We further center the control signal across modalities and apply exact bias correction for the time-varying first moment, enabling adaptive gradient memory without extra network passes or explicit learning-rate scaling. Experiments on four multimodal benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements with modest training overhead.

</details>


### [151] [Does Step Law Transfer to Small-Scale Language Models? An Empirical Recalibration Below 59M Parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27581)
*Egor Romanyukov,Timofey Novikov,Timur Shokarov,Elizaveta Zorkina,Anastasia Palienko,Stepan Dergachev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对Step Law（一种预训练语言模型最优超参数的幂律公式）未经验证的N<59M小模型区间，通过935次nanoGPT/TinyStories实验，检验其迁移性。结果支持H2：幂律形式成立但系数需重新标定；直接迁移会系统性高估学习率约4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Step Law原始校准范围为59M–1B参数，N<59M区间对单GPU训练、可解释性研究和边缘部署具有重要意义，但作者明确将该验证工作留作未来研究。直接外推幂律在小规模下是否成立尚不确定，需通过受控实验检验。

Method: 使用统一的nanoGPT/TinyStories/BPE-2048流程、AdamW优化器、warmup-cosine调度，对4个模型规模（255K–2.03M参数）和多个数据预算下的29个(N,D)单元进行935次运行；通过(η,B)网格搜索构建损失面，在对数坐标下用局部二次近似提取稳健最优点；用OLS在log-log坐标下拟合最优η*、B*的幂律系数；用F检验检验B*对N的独立性。

Result: 在主回归的25个单元（815次运行）上：直接迁移Step Law系数被拒绝，最优学习率被系统性高估约4.0倍（范围2.4×–6.6×）；重新标定后的幂律为η*=0.0985·N^(-0.508)·D^(0.238)（R²=0.834）和B*=3.6×10⁻⁴·D^(0.931)（R²=0.950）；B*与N无关的结构性结论被复现（p=0.87）；但B*关于D的指数显著大于原始值（0.931 vs 0.571）。

Conclusion: 小模型区间不打破幂律形式本身，但改变其系数校准——支持H2而非H1或H3。实际建议：不要直接使用Step Law原公式，要么采用重新标定公式η̂*≈0.1·N^(-0.51)·D^(0.24)，要么粗略地将Step Law预测的学习率除以4。局限性：仅使用单一随机种子、主范围限于N<2.03M。

Abstract: Step Law gives power-law formulas for the optimal peak learning rate eta* and batch size B* when pre-training language models. It was calibrated on models between 59M and 1B parameters; the small-model regime N < 59M was never tested empirically by its authors. This regime matters for single-GPU training, interpretability research, educational experiments, and settings where larger models are infeasible on memory or cost grounds. We test whether Step Law transfers to small language models. We consider three outcomes: H1, the original coefficients work directly; H2, the power-law form holds but with different coefficients; and H3, a power law does not describe the optima in this regime. All experiments use a single nanoGPT/TinyStories pipeline with a 2048-token BPE vocabulary, AdamW, and a warmup-cosine schedule. The optimum for each (N, D) cell is extracted from the loss surface L(eta, B) via a local quadratic approximation in log-log coordinates over the smoothed training loss. The final dataset contains 29 unique (N, D) cells and 935 analysis-ready runs. The main refit uses 25 cells (815 runs) in the working range 4 <= D/N <= 600. On the pooled data we accept H2: the functional form is preserved, but the coefficients differ from the original. We obtain eta*(N, D) = 0.0985 N^(-0.508) D^(0.238) (R^2 = 0.834) and B*(D) = 3.6 x 10^(-4) D^(0.931) (R^2 = 0.950). Step Law's structural claim that B* is independent of N is reproduced (p = 0.87), but the growth of B* with D is nearly twice as steep as in the original work. Direct transfer of Step Law systematically overestimates the optimal learning rate: the median ratio eta_SL / eta* is approximately 4.0x, with a range of 2.4x to 6.6x.

</details>


### [152] [Efficient Linear Bandits via Cluster-Aware Sketching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27594)
*Hantao Yang,Hong Xie,Defu Lian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对高维有限臂线性老虎机中传统sketching方法（如SOFUL、CBSCFD）在谱尾较重时可能产生线性累积遗憾的问题，提出了一种基于聚类的Cluster Sketch Linear Bandit（CS-LB）算法。该方法通过将臂集合划分为多个簇，在每个簇内保持无损的协方差信息，并借助sentinel机制选择当前活跃簇，实现了固定sketch尺寸下保证次线性遗憾收敛，同时将每轮计算复杂度从$O(d^2)$降至$O(l^2 d)$（$l \ll d$）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维线性老虎机的经典算法（如LinUCB、OFUL）每轮需维护并求逆$d\times d$协方差矩阵，计算开销为$O(d^2)$，难以用于实时性要求严格的系统。现有基于sketching的方法（如SOFUL、CBSCFD）虽可将计算量降至$O(ld)$，但其遗憾界依赖于谱误差项$\Delta_T$，当数据谱尾较重时$\Delta_T$随$T$增长，会导致线性遗憾甚至不收敛。已有的DBSLinUCB虽保证收敛，但依赖自适应增长的sketch尺寸，不适用于固定sketch尺寸场景，且在满秩数据下退化回$O(d^2)$复杂度。因此，亟需一种在固定sketch尺寸下既能保证遗憾收敛又能降低计算复杂度的算法。

Method: 提出CS-LB算法，主要包含三个组件：(1) 簇划分框架——通过Cluster Sketch Warm-up（CSW）算法将臂集合按秩不超过sketch尺寸$l$的方式划分为多个不相交的簇，确保每簇内$\text{rank}(\mathbf{X}_{\mathcal{C}}^\top\mathbf{X}_{\mathcal{C}})\leq l$，从而簇内FD sketch更新无奇异值截断信息损失，满足$\mathbf{S}_{\mathcal{C}}^\top\mathbf{S}_{\mathcal{C}}=\mathbf{X}_{\mathcal{C}}^\top\mathbf{X}_{\mathcal{C}}$；(2) Sentinel引导的簇选择机制——为每个簇定义sentinel $U(\mathcal{C})$为该簇所有臂的最大置信上界，每轮选择sentinel最大的活跃簇$\mathcal{W}_t$，并在簇内执行LinUCB选择，从而全局层面维持OFUL式乐观原则；(3) FD sketch更新——在活跃簇内利用Sherley-Morrison形式的逆矩阵更新公式，无需显式求逆。每轮计算瓶颈来自SVD分解，为$O(l^2 d)$。

Result: 理论方面，证明CS-LB在$\lambda\geq\max\{1,L^2\}$且置信半径取合适值时，以至少$1-\delta$的概率满足累积遗憾界$\text{Regret}(T)\leq 2\beta_T(\delta)\sqrt{2lN_{\mathcal{A}}^l T\ln(1+TL^2/(l\lambda))}=\widetilde{O}(l\sqrt{N_{\mathcal{A}}^l T})$，其中$N_{\mathcal{A}}^l=|\mathcal{C}^{all}|$为簇数；最坏情况下$N_{\mathcal{A}}^l < \lceil N/l\rceil$，故最坏遗憾界为$\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{lNT})$。此界不包含谱误差项$\Delta_T$，严格保证收敛。每轮计算成本为$O(l^2 d)$，当$l^2 < d$时优于OFUL的$O(d^2)$。实验在合成数据集上验证：(1) 在$N=60,d=50,l=5$和$N=100,d=50,l=10$两组设置下，CS-LB的累积遗憾始终低于SOFUL、CBSCFD且无线性发散趋势，运行时间略低于对比算法；(2) 与UCB1的比较显示，在$N=200,d=50,l=5$等具有线性相关结构的数据集上，CS-LB与OFUL的遗憾收敛速率明显优于UCB1，证明利用线性结构的必要性。

Conclusion: CS-LB是首个在固定sketch尺寸下同时保证遗憾收敛并降低计算复杂度的sketching线性老虎机算法。其核心思想是通过聚类将全局sketch分解为多个局部无损簇，并利用sentinel机制在簇间进行乐观选择，从根本上规避了SOFUL/CBSCFD由谱误差累积导致的线性遗憾问题，同时在$l^2 < d$时实现显著的效率提升。实验验证了其在遗憾收敛性、计算效率和UCB1对比中的优势。局限性方面，CS-LB的warm-up阶段需遍历完整臂集，尚不能直接适用于动态臂集的一般线性上下文老虎机场景，这将是未来工作的方向。

Abstract: We study the problem of computational efficiency for linear bandits in high-dimensional settings with a finite arm set. In linear bandits, the increase in the dimension $d$ of the feature vectors leads to growing computational costs of $O(d^2)$ at each round of update. Traditional sketching-based methods such as SOFUL reduce computation via fixed-size matrix sketching, yet run the risk of incurring vacuous linear regret when the spectral tail of the data is heavy and the sketch size is inadequately selected. To guarantee regret convergence and effectively reduce computational costs, we introduce a clustering mechanism and propose the Cluster Sketch Linear Bandit (CS-LB) algorithm. Our method preserves the full covariance information in each cluster to guarantee robust sublinear regret without spectral-tail vulnerabilities, performs cluster switching by assigning a sentinel for each cluster, and reduces per-round update computation to $O(l^2d)$ via a tunable sketch size $l<d$. Experiments on synthetic datasets demonstrate that our method consistently maintains a favorable trade-off between efficiency and regret.

</details>


### [153] [Pheno-GS: Phenoscape-scale Geodesic Sinkhorn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27633)
*Alistair Wilkinson,Christopher J. Tape,Smita Krishnaswamy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Pheno-GS 是一种面向大规模单细胞数据的流形最优传输（OT）框架，通过图连通性正则化、非平衡 OT 和批量矩阵算法三个模块，解决了 Geodesic Sinkhorn (GS) 在稀疏/不连通流形、质量不平衡配对和大样本量下面临的挑战，计算速度提升超 200 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高通量单细胞技术（如 CyTOF、scRNA-seq）正在大规模患者队列中收集数据。Phenoscaping 将每个单细胞分布作为"数据点"，用 OT 距离进行嵌入，是分析患者异质性的新范式。然而，现有方法存在三大局限：(1) 忽略数据的底层流形结构；(2) 难以扩展到大型队列；(3) 能给出准确距离但无法产生生物学上合理的传输计划（特别是无法建模疾病进展中的细胞凋亡/增殖）。这三大挑战亟需一个统一、可扩展且几何感知的 OT 框架来解决。

Method: Pheno-GS 基于熵正则化 OT 和 Sinkhorn 迭代，通过图 Laplacian 上的热核近似测地距离。它由三个核心模块构成：

**1. 图连通性正则化模块（GC）：**
针对稀疏或不连通图导致测地距离未定义的问题，提出线性桥接策略。具体为：(a) 从数据中均匀采样或 k-means 选取 M 个锚点；(b) 在每个锚点周围用高斯分布生成 $N_a$ 个辅助点；(c) 构建辅助点间的亲和矩阵 $K_{NN} = K_{SN}^\top K_{SN}$ 并计算行随机转移矩阵 $P_{NN}$；(d) 运行 t 步扩散 $N_{adj} = P_{NN}^t N$，将辅助点向高密度区域"拉回"，使其沿连通两个分量的低维高密度路径集中。该模块将垂直偏差从 5.16 降至 0.04（降低 99.2%），单次预处理复杂度为 $\mathcal{O}(P^2(M+d))$。

**2. 非平衡模块（UGS）：**
通过 KL 散度边际惩罚放松严格的边际约束，目标函数为：
$$\min_{\gamma \geq 0} \varepsilon D_{KL}(\gamma \| H_t) + \tau_\mu D_{KL}(\gamma \mathbf{1} \| \mu) + \tau_\nu D_{KL}(\gamma^\top \mathbf{1} \| \nu)$$
Sinkhorn 更新变为指数形式 $\phi_v = \tau_\mu/(\tau_\mu + \varepsilon)$，$\phi_w = \tau_\nu/(\tau_\nu + \varepsilon)$。当 $\tau \to \infty$ 时退化为平衡 Sinkhorn，从而优雅地建模细胞凋亡（被吸收的质量）和增殖。

**3. 批量矩阵模块（BMS）：**
核心创新在于将所有分布的成对距离矩阵计算合并为单次热扩散。具体为：(a) 构建 $N \times n$ 的分布矩阵 $M$（每列为一个分布）；(b) 计算自散度 $u_j$ 和源端缩放矩阵 $R$；(c) 对所有分布同时施加热核 $Q \leftarrow p_K(\mathbf{L}, t) R$（单次矩阵-矩阵乘积）；(d) 计算交叉散度 $w_{jk}$ 并组装成对距离 $D_{jk} = 4 a_0 t (w_{jk} + u_j)$。复杂度从 $\mathcal{O}(\binom{n}{2} T_{iter})$ 降至 $\mathcal{O}(N \cdot n)$，实测运行时缩放为 $n^{1.8}$（GS 为 $n^{3.1}$）。

Result: 1. **数值一致性**：在 250 个分布的合成数据上，BMS 与 GS 的距离矩阵几乎完全一致：MAE = $3.60 \times 10^{-12}$，RMSE = $4.58 \times 10^{-12}$，Pearson $r = 1.000$。

2. **KNN 基准（15 个分布）**：Pheno-GS (BMS) 达到 SpearmanR = 0.849、PearsonR = 0.790、P@5 = 0.807、P@10 = 0.848，耗时仅 0.473s（不含图构建），与 GS 完全一致但显著快于所有基线（Euler Sinkhorn 35.4s、LR Sinkhorn 182s）。

3. **KNN 基准（50 个分布）**：BMS 重现 GS 精度（SpearmanR = 0.824）同时快 16 倍。

4. **可扩展性**：在 n=100 到 n=500（每个分布 5000 点）范围内，加速比从 24.7× 增长到 213×。

5. **流形感知传输计划**：在 Swiss roll 上，平衡 GS 的传输计划散布在整个流形上，而非平衡 UGS 将质量集中于测地近端点对；在含离群点的合成簇上，UGS 自动抑制离群点的传输。

6. **生物数据验证**：
   - **PDO CyTOF（DMSO vs SN-38）**：平衡 GS 强制全流形连接，而非平衡 UGS 选择性集中在表型相似的细胞对上，自然建模凋亡（远处细胞质量接近零）。
   - **>1,500 个 PDO 处理分布**：与基于 Trellis（Euclidean Sinkhorn）的共享对照方法相比，Pheno-GS 恢复患者身份为分布变异的主要来源（kNN 准确率从 0.754 提升至 0.996，p ≈ 0.001），全局轮廓系数从 -0.099 翻转为 +0.106。

Conclusion: Pheno-GS 成功解决了 Geodesic Sinkhorn 的三大根本局限：(1) GC 模块保证稀疏/不连通流形上测地距离的良定义性；(2) UGS 模块支持不同总质量分布间的传输；(3) BMS 模块将成对计算从 $\mathcal{O}(\binom{n}{2} T_{iter})$ 降至 $\mathcal{O}(N \cdot n)$，在 n=500 时实现超 200× 加速且无精度损失。Pheno-GS 因此为复杂真实世界流形上的 OT 提供了一个原则化、可扩展且生物学合理的框架，特别适用于大规模单细胞患者队列的 phenoscape 构建、疾病进展建模和扰动分析。

Abstract: High-throughput single-cell data is now collected across large patient cohorts. Understanding patient-level heterogeneity from cellular-level data motivates phenoscaping: embedding each single-cell distribution as a "datapoint," with distances given by optimal transport (OT). Computing geometry-aware OT at this scale, between all pairs of patient datasets, remains an open challenge, since existing methods either rely on Euclidean ground metrics that distort manifold structure or fail under sparse, unevenly sampled, or large-scale data. We present \textbf{Pheno-GS} (Phenoscape-scale Geodesic Sinkhorn), which computes accurate, scalable geodesic transport distances under noisy, unbalanced, large-scale settings via three components: ($1$) graph connectivity regularization for well-defined geodesics on sparse/disconnected manifolds; ($2$) an unbalanced OT formulation via KL marginal penalties; and ($3$) a batched matrix algorithm computing all pairwise distances in one heat diffusion (over $200 \times$ faster than Geodesic Sinkhorn for $500$ distributions). We validate Pheno-GS on synthetic benchmarks and a CyTOF perturbation dataset.

</details>


### [154] [Private Decentralized Optimization with Noise Reduction and Bias Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27658)
*Yizhao Fan,Wenjian Luo,Jiaojiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PRDO（Private Recursive Decentralized Optimization），用于异构数据下的私有去中心化学习。该方法通过同批次梯度差的递归估计降低采样噪声和隐私噪声引起的估计误差，并通过Exact Diffusion（ED）机制校正数据异构性引起的去中心化偏差。理论分析在非凸非均匀有界数据异构性假设下，建立了收敛界，并给出了递归梯度差相对于私有ED查询灵敏度严格降低的充分条件及严格满足该条件的实例。实验在CIFAR-10等多个隐私预算下验证了PRDO相较于基线的精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化学习中面临三类误差：采样噪声、隐私噪声以及由数据异构性引起的去中心化偏差。现有方法在隐私保护下去中心化学习存在挑战：(1)递归估计方法（如DP-SRM、DIFF2、PriSMA）虽降低采样与隐私误差，但无法处理去中心化偏差；(2)Exact Diffusion虽能校正偏差但未有效降低采样/隐私噪声；(3)PrivSGP-VR虽结合方差缩减与去中心化，但假设均匀有界数据异构性，且通信开销大。因此亟需一种能同时降低采样与隐私噪声、并校正异构偏差、保护记录级差分隐私的去中心化方法。

Method: PRDO的核心方法包含两部分：1) **递归同批次梯度差估计**：在第t轮，各节点从局部数据集D_i进行Poisson采样得到批次B_i^t，计算当前模型x_i^t与上一轮模型x_i^{t-1}处的梯度估计g_i^t和d_i^t=(g_i^t - g̃_i^t)，并按v_i^t=(1-γ)v_i^{t-1}+γg_i^t+(1-γ)d_i^t+γξ_i^t更新方向，其中ξ_i^t为DP噪声；2) **Exact Diffusion校正**：采用adaptation-correction-mixing三步更新ψ_i^{t+1} = x_i^t - αv_i^t，ϕ_i^{t+1} = ψ_i^{t+1}+x_i^t-ψ_i^t，x_i^{t+1} = Σ_j W_{ij}ϕ_j^{t+1}。隐私保护采用Rényi差分隐私（RDP）账户，通过Poisson采样的高斯机制进行组合分析，记录级(ε,δ)-DP对外部窃听者和内部诚实好奇节点均成立。当γ=1时退化为私有Exact Diffusion（DP-ED）。

Result: 理论结果包含：1) **非凸收敛界（定理1）**：给出平均梯度范数的渐近界O(L_sΔ_f^0/((1-√λ)T^{2/3})) + O(λζ_0^2/(bnT(1+√λ))) + O(σ_sgd^2/(nbT^{2/3})(1+λ/b)) + O(dS_γ^2/(nγ^2ε^2T^{1/3})(1+λ/b)[T log(1/δ)/N^2 + log^2(1/δ)/b^2])，分离了优化、初始梯度异构、采样与网络传播、隐私与网络传播四项贡献，且不要求均匀有界数据异构性；2) **灵敏度严格降低的充分条件（Remark 1）**：当C_Δ < C_g且γ < 1时，S_γ = γC_g+(1-γ)C_Δ < C_g；3) **灵敏度实例（命题H.1）**：构造了d=1的有限和损失ℓ_i(x;ζ_{ir}) = a_{ir}x + α log cosh x，证明PRDO的灵敏度上界为O((γ+α)/b)，严格小于γ=1时(A+α)/b对应的私有ED灵敏度。实验结果：非凸异构logistic回归（32节点环形）显示PRDO在小批量设置下达到更低的最终梯度范数；CIFAR-10（10节点环形，VGG-style网络，Dirichlet分布异构数据）在ε=4,6,8下PRDO测试精度分别为53.368%、54.284%、54.424%，均高于DP-ED（52.964/54.084/54.186）、DP-EDM、DP-MT和DP-DSGD（47.834/48.690/49.002）等基线。

Conclusion: PRDO通过递归同批次梯度差有效降低了采样与隐私噪声引起的估计误差，并通过Exact Diffusion校正机制处理数据异构性引起的去中心化偏差。理论分析建立了不依赖均匀有界异构性假设的非凸收敛界，证明了递归机制在适当条件下能严格降低查询灵敏度。实验验证了在多种隐私预算下精度优于现有去中心化私有学习方法。未来工作将移除非激活裁剪假设并分析激活裁剪时引入的偏差。

Abstract: Private decentralized learning is affected by sampling noise, privacy noise, and decentralized bias under heterogeneous data. We propose Private Recursive Decentralized Optimization (PRDO). PRDO uses recursive estimation with same-batch gradient differences to reduce estimation errors caused by sampling and privacy noise, while its Exact Diffusion component corrects decentralized bias arising from data heterogeneity. Our analysis establishes a nonconvex convergence bound without assuming uniformly bounded data heterogeneity across nodes. It further gives a sufficient condition under which recursive gradient differences yield strictly lower query sensitivity than private Exact Diffusion, together with an example that rigorously satisfies this condition. Experiments show improved accuracy over the evaluated baselines.

</details>


### [155] [Robust Adversarial Reinforcement Learning with Risk Sensitivity and Critic Consistency Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27667)
*Jiaxi Wu,Tiantian Zhang,Yuxing Wang,Yongzhe Chang,Xueqian Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RACER框架，通过风险敏感对抗目标与评论家一致性正则化解决鲁棒对抗强化学习中的训练不稳定和价值估计偏差问题，在MuJoCo连续控制基准上全面优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒对抗强化学习(RARL)存在两个关键缺陷：(1)无约束的对抗扰动将智能体推入极端不可恢复状态，产生低价值训练信号；(2)对抗扰动放大双评论家之间的估计分歧，通过最小值算子转化为系统性低估偏差，破坏策略优化稳定性。

Method: 方法包含两个核心组件：(1)风险敏感对抗目标——构建基于状态维度的复合风险函数R(s)（如躯干高度、姿态、速度等物理可解释的失败模式），将其作为软约束加入对手奖励(ra = -(rp + R(s)))，在高风险区域抑制破坏性扰动，在低风险区域保持有效探索压力；(2)评论家一致性正则化——使用样本方差V(q0,q1)=(q0-q1)²/4度量分歧，引入Q值幅度归一化因子S使正则化近似尺度不变，加入标准Bellman损失中：L_Qj = Bellman Loss + λ·L_cons，保留双Q网络的保守偏置特性。

Result: 在Walker-Run、Hopper-Stand、Cheetah-Run、Cartpole-Balance四个MuJoCo任务上，RACER在训练稳定性、最终鲁棒性和最坏情况对抗性能三方面均优于RARL、混合NE-LD、CAT、QARL和标准SAC基线；消融实验证明两组件互补，风险敏感机制主要加速早期收敛，一致性正则化主要降低方差；λ≈1e-4时性能最佳；RACER可作为插件集成到RARL和SAC-MixedNE-LD中均带来一致提升，证实其通用性。

Conclusion: RACER通过将对抗训练从纯最坏情况驱动转向风险感知的、信息高效的形式，同时通过尺度不变正则化稳定双评论家价值估计，实现了一种适用于SAC类鲁棒对抗RL方法的通用增强框架，在保持对抗鲁棒性的同时显著改善了训练稳定性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) achieves strong performance in sequential decision-making but remains brittle under dynamic uncertainty and distributional shifts. Robust Adversarial Reinforcement Learning (RARL) improves robustness via worst-case perturbations, but existing approaches frequently suffer from unstable optimization and degraded value estimation. In particular, overly aggressive adversaries can drive the agent toward uninformative failure states, while adversarial perturbations amplify disagreement between double critics and introduce biased value targets. We propose a unified framework, RACER (Risk-sensitive robust Adversarial critic ConsistEncy-regularized Reinforcement learning), that revisits adversarial RL from a risk-sensitive perspective. First, we introduce a state-dependent adversarial objective that adaptively regulates perturbation strength, suppressing harmful disturbances while preserving informative exploration. Second, we propose critic consistency regularization to reduce disagreement between Q-value estimators and stabilize learning. Comprehensive experiments on challenging continuous control benchmarks demonstrate that RACER consistently improves performance, robustness, and training stability over strong robust RL baselines.

</details>


### [156] [What Do Tabular Foundation Models Compute In Context? In-Situ Representation Refinement through Attention-Gated Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27679)
*Tian Zhou,Beverly Jin,Linxiao Yang,Xue Wang,Wenwei Wang,Bingqing Peng,Mengni Ye,Jinjie Gu,Liang Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"原位表示精炼"（in-situ representation refinement）框架，将表格上下文学习（ICL）重新定义为对episode表示的迭代优化问题，并基于此设计了无FFN的RefineICL架构，在多个表格基准上达到或超越TabPFN-v3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于ICL的优化器视角主要研究参数空间的更新，但表格基础模型中每个episode定义新的监督任务、参数本身不更新。作者希望回答：参数应编码何种可复用计算，使有标签表格能转化为当前表格的预测器？即如何在不修改参数的情况下，让支持标签重塑episode的表示并迁移到查询。

Method: 1. 理论推导：定义留一监督邻域目标，推导正则化支持更新，证明其降低目标；通过核扩展将更新传递到未标记查询，得到前导项形式（注意力读取 + 状态依赖缩放）。2. 架构设计RefineICL：注意力门控残差块（无独立扩展FFN），向量SiLU门控，选定的低秩特征交互（每特征16个邻居），行压缩与类型化记忆再输入。3. 干预实验：内部状态操控（跳过支持更新、置换注意力/门控）、深度/宽度/上下文资源测试、匹配预算的FFN消融。

Result: RefineICL-L24在AMLB29上达到0.93836 OVR-AUC和0.87173准确率；在TabArena 38数据集快照上达到1644.8 Elo，比TabPFN-3高31.4 Elo；在TabZilla两个视图上四项指标均优于TabPFN-v3。在匹配100K更新的深度网格中，扩展FFN无一致验证增益，L8时峰值推理内存增加60.2%。内部干预显示支持表示更新对后续查询预测有因果贡献（72/72个episode中跳过中间块支持更新使查询交叉熵上升0.0510）。

Conclusion: 本文建立了表格ICL作为episode表示空间中迭代精炼的计算视角：支持标签定义修正，相似性将其传递到查询，重复更新精炼用于预测的任务信息。推导与实验共同表明注意力门控更新可在上下文中构建任务特定预测器，且FFN-free实现显著节省内存。

Abstract: What reusable computation should a tabular foundation model learn when every table defines a new supervised task? We develop in-situ representation refinement: support labels guide updates to the episode's representations, and these updates transfer to unlabeled queries without changing model parameters. A regularized leave-one-out objective yields a support correction and its query extension. The leading term separates attention-based reading from state-dependent scaling, motivating RefineICL: an attention-gated, FFN-free contextual stack with selected low-rank feature interaction and typed memory. RefineICL-L24 reaches 0.93836 OVR-AUC and 0.87173 accuracy on AMLB29. A benchmark-informed continuation reaches 1644.8 Elo on the 38-dataset TabArena snapshot, 31.4 Elo above TabPFN-3 under the same evaluation. It also improves all four reported metrics over TabPFN-v3 on both TabZilla views. In a matched 100K-update depth grid, an expanded FFN gives no consistent validation benefit and uses 60.2% more peak inference memory at L8. Internal interventions show that support representations are more than a static source of labels: removing one intermediate support update, while preserving the query output, increases final query cross-entropy in all 72 tested episodes. Together, the derivation and interventions explain how attention-gated updates can construct a task-specific predictor in context.

</details>


### [157] [NS-ATTENTION: Newton-Schulz Transformations of Attention Outputs in Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27735)
*Xiaohe Jiang,Guoqiang Zhang,Tianjin Huang,Ronghui Mu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 NS-Attention (NS-Attn.)，一种无参数、基于 Newton-Schulz (NS) 多项式迭代的注意力输出变换方法。该方法在每个注意力头的加权 value 聚合输出上施加一次有限 NS 多项式变换（保持 Frobenius 范数不变），旨在降低谱集中度、提高有效秩。在 ViT 和 Swin 上对 CIFAR-10/100 的实验显示：所有 12 组匹配种子对比均带来最终精度提升（+0.25~+0.83 pp），但额外引入显著推理延迟（ViT +41%，Swin +104%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 Transformer 中，多头注意力的输出在各 head 内可能存在谱集中（少数奇异方向主导能量）的问题。作者受 Muon 优化器中 NS 迭代谱整形效用的启发，探究是否可直接将 NS 变换作用于 attention 表示本身，以在 head 合并与输出投影前重塑能量分布、降低谱集中度、提高有效秩，从而改善分类表现。

Method: 1. 对每个 (batch, head) 的 attention 输出 X（形状 B×H×N×D_h），转置为特征×token 矩阵 M ∈ ℝ^{D_h×N}。
2. 用 Frobenius 范数归一化：M̃ = M / max(‖M‖_F, ε)。
3. 对 Gram 矩阵 A = M̃ M̃⊤ 施加一次 Muon 风格多项式步：Y = (aI + bA + cA²)M̃，其中 a=3.4445, b=-4.7750, c=2.0315。
4. 恢复原始 Frobenius 范数：T(M) = r·Y / max(‖Y‖_F, ε)。
5. 转置回原布局，经原有 head 合并和输出投影处理。整个变换无新增可训练参数，保持矩阵形状与范数不变；复杂度 O(KBH(D_h² N + D_h³))。
6. 主配置 K=1，并消融 K=2 与超参 ε 的敏感性。

Result: 在 ViT（9 blocks, 12 heads, D_h=16, 4×4 patches）与 Swin（stages [2,6,4]，heads [3,6,12]，窗口 4×4）上，使用 AdamW、batch size 100, 100 epochs、种子 0/1/3 各训练一遍：
- ViT CIFAR-10: 93.50 → 93.88（+0.38 pp）
- ViT CIFAR-100: 72.43 → 73.25（+0.82 pp）
- Swin CIFAR-10: 95.10 → 95.35（+0.25 pp）
- Swin CIFAR-100: 77.37 → 78.19（+0.83 pp）
12 组匹配种子比较全部提升；CIFAR-100 上增益更大。
消融：K=2 时均值精度下降（CIFAR-10 -0.39 pp，CIFAR-100 -0.19 pp），且标准差增大。
谱分析（最终 checkpoint，160 张测试图）：归一化最大特征值占比 p₁ 降低（ViT 0.72→0.61~0.62；Swin 0.78~0.79→0.61~0.64），熵有效秩 r_eff 升高（ViT 2.49~2.53→2.91~3.00；Swin 2.19~2.21→3.04~3.13）。
推理代价：GPU 前向延迟（RTX 4060 Laptop，FP32，batch=128）：ViT +41.0%，Swin +103.7%。

Conclusion: NS-Attn. 是一种参数免费的即插即用模块，可在不改变矩阵形状和范数的前提下，重塑每个注意力头输出的谱分布，降低谱集中度并提高有效秩，在 ViT 与 Swin 的 CIFAR-10/100 分类中带来稳定但较小的精度提升。然而，这些增益伴随不可忽略的额外推理延迟（尤其 Swin 翻倍），且作者明确指出谱观察到的平坦化只是与精度提升同时出现的表征效应，并非因果断言。实际价值取决于具体应用的精度-延迟权衡，未来工作需在更大规模数据集和等计算量条件下进一步验证。

Abstract: Newton-Schulz (NS) iteration has recently been used in the Muon optimizer to transform update matrices during the training of large language models. Motivated by its spectral effect, we investigate applying NS directly to Transformer attention representations. We introduce Newton-Schulz Attention (NS-Attn.), a parameter-free transformation applied to the output of each attention head. Each head output is arranged as a feature-by-token matrix and normalized by its Frobenius norm. We then apply a finite NS polynomial step and restore the original norm. The objective is to reduce spectral concentration and increase effective rank before standard head merging and output projection. Across ViT and Swin on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100, NS-Attn. improves final-epoch accuracy in all 12 matched-seed comparisons, with mean gains of 0.25--0.83 percentage points. ViT ablations show higher mean accuracy with one iteration than with two. Spectral analysis further shows reduced leading-eigenvalue concentration and increased effective rank. These gains incur additional inference latency.

</details>


### [158] [MENO: Memory-Efficient Neural Operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27739)
*Shengyang Xu,Weijun Zhang,Jun Hu,Pengzhan Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MENO是一种基于流形函数编码器(MFE)的新型神经算子架构，具有内存高效、训练快速、可处理任意几何输入(包括跨几何场景)以及强泛化能力等优点，在多个基准测试中达到了最优或接近最优的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子(如FNO、Transolver、PCNO)在处理变几何PDE问题时存在内存开销大、训练成本高的问题。集成类方法(如Transolver-3、PCNO)的内存消耗随数据分辨率增长，在大规模3D工业问题中面临GPU内存瓶颈；而MFE-MIONet虽无集成瓶颈但泛化能力不足。因此亟需一种兼具内存高效与强泛化能力的神经算子架构。

Method: MENO基于流形函数编码器(MFE)，采用"全局分支-局部分支"双分支架构：1) 全局分支使用Transformer对MFE tokens进行全局信息处理；2) 局部分支通过解码后的全局状态(含导数信息)进行单点查询预测；3) 两者输出相加得到最终预测。核心特点是整个训练阶段不包含任何积分操作符，使得GPU内存占用与数据分辨率无关，并自然支持跨几何场景(输入函数与输出解定义在不同流形上)。

Result: MENO在四个基准测试中均表现出色：1) 跨几何Poisson问题相对误差2.07%(为MIONet的1/3)；2) 单几何Poisson问题误差0.669%(优于PCNO)，参数量仅1.7%，GPU内存仅5.1%，训练时间仅8.3%；3) NASA-CRM压力误差6.69%、摩擦误差4.79%，优于所有基线方法(PCNO参数量322M、GPU内存66.8GBvs MENO 13.7M、1.07GB)；4) AhmedML达到第二优精度。整体在精度、参数量、内存占用和训练速度间实现了优异平衡。

Conclusion: MENO是一种高效且通用的神经算子架构，通过消除训练阶段积分操作实现了内存占用与分辨率解耦，同时保持了强泛化能力并支持跨几何PDE输入。未来工作将基于MENO开发通用PDE神经求解器。

Abstract: We propose the Memory-Efficient Neural Operator (MENO) as a high-performance PDE neural solver based on the Manifold Function Encoder (MFE). MENO features three primary advantages: (1) MENO has a significantly smaller memory footprint and much faster training speed than other popular architectures, with the memory footprint being independent of the data resolution, and therefore holds the potential for scaling up to large-scale models. (2) MENO can accept PDE inputs of arbitrary form, including arbitrary geometric domains and arbitrary discretizations. In particular, it is capable of handling cross-geometry scenarios, i.e., where the input functions and the output solutions are defined on different manifolds. (3) MENO exhibits strong generalization capability, and achieves the best accuracy on most of the benchmarks we tested, compared with the results reported in the literature. The code is available on GitHub at this https URL , and all numerical examples in this paper can be run with a single command to reproduce the reported results.

</details>


### [159] [Limiting-Kernel Q($λ$): Bridging Short and Long Horizons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27741)
*Tolga Ok,Arman Sharifi Kolarijani,Peyman Mohajerin Esfahani,Mohamad Amin Sharifi Kolarijani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Limiting-Kernel Q(λ)（LKQL），一种结合n步截断与极限核（limiting kernel）长视野近似的离策略值估计器，在保持n步估计器计算复杂度的同时，通过对截断尾部的"尾部补全"改善策略评估收敛速率，并在MuJoCo连续控制基准上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: n步截断的λ-return估计器虽计算高效但仅限于短视野；利用转移动力学全局结构的方法虽能加速策略评估，却因内存与计算需求难以扩展到大或连续状态空间。需要一种兼具两者优势的方法。

Method: 基于极限核（limiting kernel）P∞^π 对n步截断预条件子的尾部进行补全（tail completion），定义LK预条件子C̃^π_{n,λ} = C^π_{n,λ} + (λγ)^n/(1-λγ)·P∞^π。通过"双重备份"（double backup）实现：一个备份更新值函数Q，另一个通过TD学习跟踪长期平均TD误差以近似P∞^μ的辅助函数U。算法可集成到on-policy（PPO）和off-policy（NAF）actor-critic框架中。

Result: 理论方面：(1)给出了截断Harutyunyan算子策略评估与控制的最紧已知收敛速率；(2)证明在非周期行为链且近on-policy（ε<1-γ）条件下，LK算子在足够大的n下改善了策略评估收敛速率；(3)证明LKQL在有限MDP、固定行为策略和i.i.d.初始条件下几乎处处收敛到最优值。实验方面：在MuJoCo（Hopper、Walker2d、HalfCheetah）上，在多数设置下优于GAE和截断HQL基线，在长视野任务（HalfCheetah）上改进尤为显著。

Conclusion: LKQL通过尾部补全有效桥接了短视野n步截断与长视野完整策略评估，在不增加计算复杂度的情况下改善了值估计精度，并在连续控制任务中取得性能提升。其局限性包括固定行为策略假设、控制问题的LKHI算子与截断HI收敛速率相同（因贪心映射不连续）、以及额外的LK项网络带来的内存与计算开销。

Abstract: In value-based reinforcement learning, improving the accuracy of policy evaluation has been shown to improve downstream policy optimization performance. The widely adopted family of approximations relying on $n$-step truncation yields computationally efficient value estimators but is inherently limited to a short evaluation horizon. In contrast, methods that exploit the global structure of the transition dynamics can accelerate policy evaluation, but their memory and computational requirements often limit scalability to large or continuous state spaces. To reconcile these limitations, we introduce Limiting-Kernel Q($\lambda$) (LKQL), an off-policy value estimator that combines $n$-step truncation with a long-horizon approximation based on the limiting kernel (LK). LKQL has the same order of complexity as $n$-step estimators and integrates directly into both on- and off-policy actor-critic algorithms. We prove that, under aperiodicity and in the near-on-policy regime, the operator underlying LKQL improves the policy evaluation convergence rate over its truncated counterpart for sufficiently large $n$, and that LKQL itself converges almost surely to the optimal values in finite Markov decision processes (MDPs) under a fixed behavior policy. On the MuJoCo continuous-control benchmark, we show that LKQL improves over $n$-step baselines in most settings, particularly on long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [160] [Learning to Detect Symbolic Failure: Machine Learning and the Limits of Black-Scholes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27764)
*Juli Huang,Jake Cheng,Rupert Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将期权定价视为表征问题，利用260万份真实期权合约比较了三种机器学习范式（核PCA抽象学习、树模型保留领域结构、神经网络独立验证）在检测Black-Scholes系统性偏差上的表现。核心结论是：在存在专家设计符号特征的领域（如期权的Greeks和moneyness），保留领域结构的树模型（93.8%准确率）大幅优于学习抽象嵌入的核PCA（72.3%），且BS与神经网络对偏差的分类一致性达99.9974%，表明偏差反映市场结构而非BS模型伪影。作者明确声明不声称存在可利用的错误定价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Black-Scholes (BS) 公式自1973年提出以来虽被广泛使用，但其常数波动率、无摩擦交易等假设在现实市场中经常被违反。BS更多作为一种共享的符号语言（用于报价隐含波动率、对冲Greeks、监管会计标准）而非精确的定价工具。这引发了一个深层问题：当尝试从数据中学习一种放弃BS符号结构的替代表征机制时，会出现什么模式？本文将期权定价视为表征问题，探究在具有专家设计符号特征的领域中，机器学习是否应保留领域结构而非学习抽象嵌入。

Method: 数据集：来自Yahoo Finance的2,632,013份真实期权合约（2025年4月22日至2026年1月6日，180个交易日），涵盖三种波动率状态的资产——AAPL（407,224份，IV=25%）、SPY（1,341,246份，IV=15%）、TSLA（883,543份，IV=45%）。特征：8个基础领域专家特征（moneyness、time-to-maturity、隐含波动率、Delta/Gamma/Theta/Vega），另测试12个工程化特征。标签：基于BS价格vs市场价格的±10%偏差阈值分为三类（低估/公平/高估）。三种方法对比：(1) 学习抽象维度——Kernel PCA + SVM（4种核函数）；(2) 保留结构——Gradient Boosting、Random Forest、Logistic Regression；(3) 独立验证——MLPRegressor学习市场价格。评估采用80/20时间分割、5折TimeSeriesSplit防止前瞻偏差。

Result: Gradient Boosting（100棵树，学习率0.1，深度5）达到93.8%准确率，Random Forest 93.0%，Logistic Regression 92.0%，均显著优于Kernel PCA最佳方法（Sigmoid KPCA）的72.3%——差距达21.5个百分点。8特征基础模型（93.9%）与12特征工程化模型（93.8%）几乎无差别，表明领域专家已穷尽信号。按资产划分：AAPL 91.3%、SPY 98.2%、TSLA 82.7%（平均90.8%），TSLA的低准确率反映其高波动率、低流动性环境中的噪声定价。BS-based与NN-based偏差分类在38,833份测试合约上的一致性达99.9974%（仅1份不同意），表明偏差对定价模型选择具有鲁棒性，反映市场结构而非BS伪影。但公平定价类别的召回率仅18%，存在显著类别不平衡（低估39.1%、高估34.8%、公平仅3.7%），该分布受任意阈值选择影响而非真实市场结构。

Conclusion: 本文得出三点核心洞见：(1) 符号抽象可以以系统性、可学习的方式失败——BS的非通用性并不意味着偏差是随机的，机器学习能以90.8%的准确率从260万份合约中检测出模式；(2) 在符号领域中保留领域专家结构优于学习抽象嵌入——树模型比核PCA高出21.5个百分点正是因为它们保留了金融特征的语义含义（Greeks、moneyness、时间衰减），而非将其压缩到潜在空间，这挑战了"学习表征总是优于手工设计"的ML常见假设；(3) 替代学习表征可验证符号失败的论断——BS与神经网络分类的一致性提供了验证其他符号系统的模板。论文明确划定了局限：不声称存在可利用的错误定价（偏差可能反映交易成本、库存效应或对冲约束）；180天验证窗口对长期声明过短；±10%阈值为探索性选择；并非对BS的否定——其持续影响力反映其作为共享符号语言的价值。

Abstract: We treat options pricing as a representation problem: can machine learning detect systematic deviations from Black-Scholes using 2.6M real option contracts? We compare three regimes: learned abstract embeddings (Kernel PCA), preserved domain structure (tree-based ensembles), and neural network validation. Tree-based methods outperform kernel dimensionality reduction by 21.5 percentage points (93.8% vs 72.3%), and domain-expert features (Greeks, moneyness) outperform engineered features. NN-based and BS-based deviation labels agree 99.9974% of the time, suggesting deviations reflect market structure rather than model artifact. We conclude that in domains with expert-designed symbolic features, preserving structure beats learning abstractions. We make no claim of exploitable mispricings.

</details>


### [161] [CAST: Context- and Anomaly Structure-Conditioned Time Series Anomaly Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27825)
*Haochen Zhang,Jie Peng,Songyuan Sui,Yu-Chao Huang,Xiangqi Zhu,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CAST（Context- and Anomaly Structure-Conditioned Time Series Anomaly Generation）是一种针对异常时间序列稀缺、异构且难以获取问题而设计的生成框架。该方法通过 VQ-VAE 提取异常结构表示，并结合 Rectified Flow Matching 实现条件化生成，采用"上下文无关预训练 + 上下文感知微调"的两阶段策略，利用大量正常数据缓解异常数据稀缺，并通过对正常上下文与异常结构的双重条件建模捕捉异常形态的异质性。在五个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，CAST 在生成保真度与下游异常检测任务上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异常时间序列在安全关键领域至关重要，但天然稀缺、异构且获取成本高。现有时间序列生成方法主要合成正常数据，对异常样本的生成价值有限。生成异常面临两个核心挑战：（1）异常数据稀缺导致直接学习易过拟合；（2）异常形态在相似正常上下文下呈现多模态、异质性特征，仅依靠上下文条件化无法精确控制异常形态，现有方法易产生模糊或不真实的生成结果。

Method: CAST 采用两阶段策略：Stage I（上下文无关预训练）使用 VQ-VAE 提取异常段离散结构潜码，并在正常与异常混合数据上预训练 Rectified Flow Matching 模型以学习系统动态与潜码到信号的映射，缓解数据稀缺问题；Stage II（上下文感知微调）将异常结构潜码 U 与正常上下文 X_ctx 同时作为条件，噪声仅注入掩码区域 M 内的异常段，对速度场 v_θ(X_t, U, X_ctx, t) 进行掩码流匹配训练。推理时，从真实异常段推断潜码，采样正常上下文与掩码，通过 ODE 积分生成与上下文一致且符合特定异常形态的合成异常。理论上基于条件方差分解（D.1-D.4）证明引入推断潜码不会增大最优流匹配目标，并在潜空间平滑性假设下给出与 oracle 潜码的误差上界。

Result: 在 MIT-DB、SVDB、QTDB、PV、Metro 五个真实世界单/多变量数据集上，CAST 在 MSE、MAE、FID、KID、MMD 等生成保真度指标上几乎全面优于 FlowTS、Diffusion-TS、TimeVAE、C-GATS、GenIAS 等基线；在下游异常检测任务中，使用合成异常训练的 7 种检测器（包括 RF、CatBoost、RobustTAD、TCN、Moment、OneFitsAll、LTSM）平均 F1 也均领先（MIT-DB 0.832、SVDB 0.935、QTDB 0.896、PV 0.321、Metro 0.575）。消融实验验证 ASC（异常结构条件）与 CUP（上下文无关预训练）缺一不可；定性分析表明模型学得结构化、可解释、可平滑插值的异常潜空间。整体相对基线 MSE 降低 26%、MAE 降低 17%、F1 提升 16%。

Conclusion: CAST 通过将异常时间序列生成分解为正常上下文与异常结构两部分进行条件建模，并利用两阶段预训练-微调策略充分利用正常数据，有效缓解了异常数据稀缺与异常形态异质性两大挑战。理论分析与实证实验均表明该框架能够在保证上下文一致性的同时实现可控且多样化的异常合成，可显著提升下游异常检测性能。未来工作可探索单步生成范式以提升推理效率，并将框架扩展至多模态场景与更深层次的理论分析。

Abstract: Anomalous time series play a critical role in safety-critical domains, yet they are inherently scarce, heterogeneous, and costly to obtain. Existing time series generation methods predominantly focus on synthesizing normal data, providing limited value when anomalous samples are needed. We identify two fundamental challenges in anomaly generation: (i) the scarcity of anomaly data, and (ii) the heterogeneous morphological characteristics of anomalies. To address these challenges, we propose CAST, a Context- and Anomaly Structure-conditioned Time series anomaly generation framework with principled two-stage pretraining and finetuning strategy. In pretraining stage, we leverage abundant normal time series data to learn underlying system dynamics and substantially mitigate the limited availability of anomaly data. During finetuning, CAST explicitly conditions the generator on learned anomaly structure representations, enabling it to capture heterogeneous anomaly morphologies under similar contextual conditions. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world univariate and multivariate datasets demonstrate that CAST consistently outperforms state-of-the-art anomaly generation methods in terms of both generation fidelity and downstream task utility, highlighting the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [162] [From Reasoning Strings to Partial Orders: Verifier-Certified Rule Transport through Quotient Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27833)
*Bang Xie,Hao Liu,Zhiyuan Peng,Xin Yin,Chenhao Ying,Yuan Luo,Senjian Zhang,Wei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VCRT方法，通过原生验证器对相邻操作对的两种执行顺序进行重放，生成交换证书（diamond）和反交换证书（anti-diamond），并将成功轨迹聚类为验证器等价轨道，结合轨道GRPO、反钻石间隔损失及一致性约束，实现跨推理环境的留一环境迁移。在ProofWriter、CLRS和Lean三个环境的留一评估中取得77.60%宏通过率，优于最强基线13.06分（95% CI [12.58, 13.54]）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR（带可验证奖励的强化学习）将每条成功轨迹视为独立token序列，但许多计算允许多种合法执行顺序（独立子目标或不相交状态更新可交换），序列化选择易被误判为逻辑依赖。如何让策略区分无害重排与违反先决条件的反转，是提升跨环境迁移能力的关键。

Method: 1) 证书构造：对相邻操作对的两种顺序进行原生重放，若两种顺序均被接受且到达同一规范化状态，则形成"推理钻石"（交换证书）；若反转被拒绝或到达不同状态，则形成"反钻石"（先决条件证书）。2) 轨道质量：将通过交换证书相连的成功轨迹合并为验证器轨道，将PPO比率应用于轨道总概率质量而非单条轨迹。3) 依赖分离与约束：反钻石间隔损失分离合法与非法质量；交换一致性损失对齐交换后动作分布；保留约束限制源任务衰退；动作KL约束限制策略漂移。4) 评估设置：使用匿名关系图接口进行留一环境迁移评估，冻结所有训练与检查点决策，推理时使用单条贪心轨迹。

Result: VCRT宏通过率77.60%，最强基线64.53%，配对增益13.06分（95% bootstrap CI [12.58, 13.54]）。Lean贡献最大（33.49分），ProofWriter与CLRS平均提升2.85分。机制消融：No-Anti在所有环境均劣于完整VCRT；No-Orbit与完整VCRT在统计上无显著差异；Shuffled-Orbit在Lean和CLRS劣于完整VCRT但在ProofWriter更优。Granite-4.1-3B上VCRT相对冻结骨干获得17.57分提升，最大源任务衰退0.96分。

Conclusion: 实验支持三个可分离的声明：(1) Canonical-GRPO证明共享关系表征本身有价值；(2) 完整VCRT目标在此表征基础上进一步增加价值；(3) 消融识别依赖分离为最稳定的组件。证据更倾向于验证器认证的依赖分离是可迁移组件，而非精确轨道质量聚合的必要性。研究承认依赖提取和跨模态迁移超出当前证据范围。

Abstract: Many computations admit several valid execution orders because independent subgoals or disjoint state updates can commute. Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards usually treats each successful trace as a separate token sequence, so serialization choices can be mistaken for logical dependencies. We introduce Verifier-Certified Rule Transport (VCRT), which replays adjacent operation pairs with native verifiers. Pairs whose two orders are accepted and reach the same canonical state provide commutation certificates; rejected or state-changing reversals provide anti-diamonds. VCRT uses anti-diamonds to preserve genuine prerequisites and assigns policy credit to the total probability mass of each certified orbit. It also constrains post-swap consistency, source retention, and policy drift. We evaluate leave-one-environment-out transfer across ProofWriter, CLRS, and Lean through a shared anonymized relation-graph interface. All training and checkpoint decisions are frozen before held-out evaluation, which uses one greedy trajectory per item without search or verifier feedback. VCRT obtains a 77.60% macro pass rate versus 64.53% for the strongest matched baseline, a paired gain of 13.06 points (95% bootstrap CI [12.58, 13.54]). Lean accounts for most of this gain at 33.49 points, while ProofWriter and CLRS improve by 2.85 points on average. Mechanism tests consistently favor anti-diamond supervision, whereas No-Orbit is statistically indistinguishable from full VCRT. The evidence does not establish a general benefit from exact orbit aggregation.

</details>


### [163] [Exact Minimax One-Bit Unbiased Compression: Heavy-Tail Necessity and Finite-Randomness Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27860)
*Tao Jiang,Minbo Gao,Shaowei Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究逐点无偏的1比特压缩问题，对一般Borel信源给出了精确的源平均二阶矩最优值 $m^2 + \mathcal{J}(P)^2$（其中 $\mathcal{J}(P) = \int \sqrt{F(r)(1-F(r))} dr$），对高斯位置族给出了精确的minimax值 $\sigma^2\Lambda_c^2$，证明了该最优性必然导致输出在端点均值处的三阶矩相变（$p<3$ 时有限，$\mathbb{P}(W>t) = \Theta(t^{-3}/\sqrt{\log t})$），并给出了Cauchy混合鲁棒化方案和有限随机比特有界输出近似，最终通过坐标分配在Kim连续二次硬族上获得与下界匹配的优化保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式学习和联邦优化中需要低比特通信，但现有结果多依赖有界动态范围假设。Kim 2026的下界已针对无界高斯观测建立，但可达性方面的精确一比特最优性、minimax结构、必要的尾部行为以及有限随机性实现这四个结构性问题尚未解决。

Method: 方法包括：(1) 使用分布变换和随机阈值码，阈值密度正比于 $\sqrt{F(r)(1-F(r))}$，解码端用中心化权重 $m + (M-(1-F(R)))/h_P(R)$；(2) 利用"随机浴盆不等式"和对偶见证 $G_P(X) = \gamma(F(X))$ 建立全随机化二进制编码器下的全局下界；(3) 通过高斯端点混合 $P_c^\star = \frac{1}{2}\mathcal{N}(-c,1) + \frac{1}{2}\mathcal{N}(c,1)$ 作为最不优先验，验证minimax等式；(4) 用Mills率分析端点尾部的精确渐近；(5) Cauchy混合 $\rho_{c,\eta} = (1-\eta)\rho_c + \eta/\pi(1+z^2)$ 鲁棒化；(6) 用 $R$ 比特共享种子和 $K$ 个阈值网格做概率舍入；(7) 在Kim硬族上用投影均值估计器。

Result: 主要结果：(1) 定理1：一般信源的固定源最优值为 $m^2 + \mathcal{J}(P)^2$，推论2恢复均匀源情形 $\pi^2/64 - 1/12$；(2) 定理3：规则全支撑信源的最优码本质唯一（阈值码至零集、比特重标记和公开种子细化意义下）；(3) 定理4：高斯minimax值为 $\sigma^2\Lambda_c^2$，在 $c=1/2$ 时 $\Lambda_{1/2}^2 = 3.2179870327...$；(4) 定理5：在端点均值处，$|W|^p$ 有限当且仅当 $p<3$（或 $p=3$ 且 $|a|<c$），尾部分布 $\mathbb{P}(W>t) = \Theta(t^{-3}/\sqrt{\log t})$；(5) 推论6：Cauchy混合使所有正阶矩有限，二阶矩膨胀至多 $1/(1-\eta)$；(6) 命题7：有限支撑公开随机性加有限解码均值不可能在整条实数轴上达到精确无偏；(7) 定理8：$R$ 比特有限随机性近似的显式偏差和二阶矩界，以 $R = O(\log K + \log(1/\eta))$ bit 逼近minimax常数；(8) 推论10：$B$ 比特向量压缩 $\mathbb{E}\|\mathcal{Q}_B(g)\|^2 \leq \Lambda_c^2 \sigma^2 d^2/\min\{B,d\}$；(9) 推论11：在Kim硬族上 $\sup_\theta \mathbb{E}[f_\theta(\hat\theta) - f_\theta^\star] \leq \Lambda_{1/2}^2 \sigma^2 d^2/(2T\min\{B,d\})$，与下界在 $(\sigma,d,B,\varepsilon)$ 依赖关系上匹配；(10) 推论12/13：高概率保证仅增加对数置信因子。

Conclusion: 本文区分三个本质现象：(1) 无界高斯观测不改变局部二次族的期望查询复杂度阶；(2) 精确标量二阶矩最优性必然导致端点三阶绝对矩相变；(3) 全实数轴上的精确逐点无偏性与有限公开种子和有限解码均值的兼容性存在根本障碍。本文通过Cauchy混合和有限随机数方案量化了二阶矩、高阶矩、偏差和共享随机性复杂度之间的权衡，并通过坐标分配在Kim连续二次硬族上得到依赖参数下界匹配的固定比特高斯梯度压缩方案。开放问题包括联合 $B$ 比特向量编码的最优常数、偏差-方差-随机性-存储联合权衡，以及一般强凸目标的局部化机制。

Abstract: A pointwise-unbiased one-bit compressor reconstructs every real input in expectation while transmitting one bit. For a scalar source $P$ with CDF $F$, mean $m$, and $\mathcal J(P)=\int_{\mathbb R}\sqrt{F(r)(1-F(r))}\,dr$, we prove that the infimum of the source-averaged reconstruction second moment over all public-coin one-bit codes unbiased on $\mathbb R$ is $m^2+\mathcal J(P)^2$. For regular full-support sources, a distribution-centered random-threshold code attains this value; a converse over arbitrary randomized binary encoders and an equality analysis characterize every attaining code up to null sets, bit relabeling, and public-seed refinement. For the Gaussian location family $\mathcal N(\mu,\sigma^2)$ with $|\mu|\le c\sigma$, the equal prior on the endpoint means is least favorable and the minimax value is $\sigma^2\Lambda_c^2$. Exact Gaussian minimax optimality forces a critical heavy tail: at the endpoint means, absolute moments are finite exactly for $p<3$, and $\Pr(W>t)=\Theta(t^{-3}/\sqrt{\log t})$. A Cauchy-mixture robustification inflates the second moment by at most $1/(1-\eta)$ while making every positive-order absolute moment finite. Finite-support public randomness with finite decoder means cannot achieve exact unbiasedness on $\mathbb R$, but a bounded-output approximation using exactly $R$ shared random bits has explicit bias and second-moment bounds converging to the minimax constant. Finally, coordinate allocation communicates exactly $B$ bits per Gaussian-gradient query. On Kim's continuous quadratic hard family, the expected optimization guarantee matches the lower bound in its dependence on $(\sigma,d,B,\varepsilon)$, and a finite-variance high-probability bound incurs only a logarithmic confidence factor.

</details>


### [164] [Evaluation Choices Decide the Forecasting Leaderboard: Evidence from a Production Marketplace Panel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27867)
*Md Rezwanul Islam,Wael Mohammed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文以一个生产市场的1887个买家、67个月的真实面板数据为测试床，固定数据、预测期和评估周期，仅改变评估设计中的三项关键选择（分析单元、误差聚合方式、点预测vs区间评分），证明这三项选择各自能够逆转或瓦解任何头条结论；并将同一协议无修改地应用于公开的M5零售面板，复现了相同的"总分第一、每条序列垫底"的基线颠倒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测基准的读者期望通过"赢家榜"决定该用哪种方法。作者指出，赢家往往不是方法的属性，而是基准（评估设计）的属性——评估者的选择在模型拟合前就已经决定了答案。具体动机包括：填补已有研究中评估设计影响量化不足的空白；以单一生产面板和固定方法集测量"评估选择移动结论的距离"；并通过公共面板（M5）验证该现象是否普遍。

Method: 核心方法即评估协议本身，已作为可运行工件发布。具体包括：在1,887名买家、67个月的GTV面板上，对24种预测方法加1个教科书参考（含6个2025年时间序列基础模型）进行基准测试；使用滚动起点（rolling origins）评估，八个间隔三个月的切点各预测未来六个月；误差度量采用MASE和RMSSE；显著性检验使用Diebold-Mariano（带小样本修正）和Friedman Nemenyi；按需需求分类法（ADI、CV²）将买家分为Smooth/Erratic/Intermittent/Lumpy；测量单位从市场总聚合（aggregate）到单买家的所有k值粒度构建"曲线"；在基础模型上用split conformal prediction构建预测区间；部署的选择规则通过回放测量其相对基线和"全知选择"的收益；将同一协议不变地应用于M5公开数据集和澳大利亚旅游面板。

Result: （1）单位反转：同一基线在市场总分上排名19方法中的第2（无方法显著优于它），但在单买家层面排第23/25，19/24的方法显著优于它；该反转对roster和起点均稳健。（2）从对比到曲线：将面板分为k=1至625组，基线排名随粒度单调恶化，Spearman与log k的秩相关为1.00；M5原生层级结构呈现相同的曲线。（3）需求类别：四种需求类别各由不同方法引领，整合榜单的胜者仅在两个类别中领先。（4）区间重排：点预测精度排名与区间质量排名的Spearman相关仅0.39，在间歇需求上为0.02（无线性关系）；共形区间在spiky项目上欠覆盖最严重（标称95%实际仅~74%）。（5）选择规则价值：部署的选择规则在自身面板上回放关闭了基线与全知选择之间55%的距离；在M5面板上关闭64.7%。（6）协议可迁移：M5上同一基线在市场总分排名第1、在每个item-store序列排第7（垫底）；加入5个零样本基础模型改变总分的胜者，但形状不变；澳大利亚旅游（实体已平滑）上反转效应显著弱化——这与机制预测一致。

Conclusion: 评估设计选择（而非方法本身）才是决定预测基准赢家榜的首要因素。三项关键选择——分析单元、误差聚合量、点预测vs区间评分——各自都能逆转头条结论，且该现象不限于单一数据集：在M5公共零售面板上以未修改的协议复现，并在第三个面板（澳大利亚旅游）上识别出机制的边界。该论文并未声称任何特定方法获胜，而是提出"设计问题先于模型问题"——即将到来的基础模型评测基准的结论将取决于其设计首先固定的单位和聚合策略。同时报告了作者自身在选择规则分析中犯下的标度分母错误，并将其作为评估选择标准应自律的实例。

Abstract: A forecasting benchmark reports which method won. We show that the answer is set by the evaluator's choices before any model is fitted. We benchmark 24 forecasting methods and one textbook reference, including six 2025-era time series foundation models, on a production marketplace panel of 1,887 business customers over 67 months. We hold the data, the horizon and the period fixed, and vary only the evaluation design. Three choices each reverse or dissolve a headline conclusion. Changing the unit of analysis from the market total to the individual customer moves our production baseline from second of nineteen, beaten by nothing, to twenty-third of twenty-five. Nineteen of its twenty-four challengers beat it there. Changing how much error is pooled decides whether a Diebold-Mariano test finds anything at all. Scoring prediction intervals rather than point forecasts reorders the field almost completely, with a rank correlation of 0.02 on intermittent demand. We then measure what the deployed system gets from this. Its selection rule captures 55% of the distance between doing nothing and choosing with hindsight. The reversal is not a quirk of our data. We ran the released protocol, unchanged, on the public M5 retail panel. The same baseline shape places first at the market total and last per series, beaten by everything, and a replayed selection rule closes 64.7% of the same floor-to-ceiling distance there. Adding five zero-shot foundation models to that roster changes who wins at the total, not the shape. The bands' blind spot travels too: conformal bands under-cover most on the spikiest items. Splitting our own panel into ever smaller groups turns the contrast into a curve: the baseline's rank worsens at every level of disaggregation. We release the evaluation protocol and report an error of our own that inverted a result before we caught it.

</details>


### [165] [Global tree forecasters collapse at the hierarchical aggregate: a five-panel failure characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27912)
*Md Rezwanul Islam,Wael Mohammed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文刻画了一种此前未被记录的全局梯度提升树预测器在层级聚合处的失效模式：在个体序列上训练后直接预测聚合总量时,聚合值远超训练支持域,模型输出坍缩至最大叶节点,生产环境中低估30–50倍,M5数据集上高达496倍。论文在五个面板、三种树库上验证,给出诊断-治疗-避免的三步实践流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局梯度提升树是面板预测的主流方法,但其"训练用个体序列、推断用聚合总量"的生产便利配置存在一个未被记录的失败模式。现有文献(如Montero-Manso & Hyndman 2021)已推荐逐序列缩放作为预处理,但从未测量过该预处理所防止的失败本身。理解这一失败对部署至关重要,因为聚合预测往往直接驱动业务规划。

Method: 论文采用实验验证方法:(1)在生产B2B市场面板(月度GTV,5658个买方序列)、合成层级面板、M5、澳大利亚旅游、UCI Online Retail II五个数据集上训练LightGBM/XGBoost/CatBoost全局树,使用绝对滞后特征;(2)比较one-step与recursive多步预测;(3)测试四种修复:逐序列缩放、加权聚合训练行、季节差分、逐库变体;(4)进行消融实验(累积式与留一法);(5)用滚动起点Diebold-Mariano与Wilcoxon符号秩检验统计显著性;(6)扫掠尺度间隙g从1到10映射失效起始点;(7)测试多种标准配置旋钮(更长窗口、实体ID、更深树、Tweedie损失、线性叶等)确认无替代方案。

Result: 基线全局树在所有五个面板上发生坍缩:生产97.9% MAPE(MASE≈9.3),M5 99.8%(对应496×低估),旅游96.2%,UCI 98.7%。失效机制为:聚合输入超出训练支持域,树预测恒定值,被路由至最大叶节点,MAPE下界为1-1/g,g为尺度间隙。1.15×间隙即产生34%误差,无安全带。三种修复均有效(逐序列缩放5.4–10.5% MAPE,加权聚合行14–15%,季节差分4.6–11.0%);递归多步预测分离三者:聚合行重新坍缩,逐序列缩放退化但仍低,仅季节差分保持一步与递推精度不变(seed方差最低)。所有标准配置旋钮均失败:更长窗口、实体ID、更深树、Tweedie、线性叶(部分缓解但不治愈)、池化所有层级(小层级有效,M5规模失效)。失效在8个训练种子上不变,在三个库上一致,Diebold-Mariano统计量57–251,Wilcoxon p≤2.4×10⁻⁴。

Conclusion: 全局梯度提升树在层级聚合处的坍缩是结构性而非调参问题——树是分段常数函数,无法输出超过训练最大目标叶节点的值,集成模型可测量地继承此上限。逐序列缩放这一既有预处理是关键的(load-bearing),省略它预测不是退化而是下降,而是坍缩。诊断只需计算聚合与最大训练序列的比值;治疗沿部署所需轴选择(一步用缩放,递归用季节差分,需单模型时用加权聚合行);不存在标准配置替代方案。论文给出三步诊断-治疗-避免实践流程,适用于稀疏、短、增长的层级面板。

Abstract: Global forecasting models pool many series and learn one shared function. Gradient-boosted trees are their most common form. We measure a failure of this design that has not, to our knowledge, been documented. Train a global tree on the individual series of a hierarchy, then ask it for the hierarchical aggregate. The aggregate sits far outside the model's training range, and the forecast collapses. The model under-predicts the total by 30-50x in our production deployment, and by up to 496x in a public M5 reconstruction. The mechanism is known: beyond its training range, a tree predicts a constant. It surfaces at the aggregate because the total dwarfs every training series. The cure is not new. Per-series scaling, the preprocessing step that Montero-Manso and Hyndman (2021) recommend, prevents the collapse. So do a weighted aggregate-level training row and seasonal differencing. Our contribution is the characterization. The collapse reproduces on five panels: a production business-to-business marketplace, a synthetic hierarchy, M5, Australian Tourism, and a public business-buyer panel. It holds on three tree libraries, is invariant across training seeds, and is statistically significant. Its onset is immediate and tracks a simple support bound: a scale gap of only 1.15x already costs a third of the total. No standard configuration change prevents it: pooling every hierarchy level into training fails at scale, and the one knob that fits linear models in the leaves softens it without curing it. Rolling the forecasts forward recursively separates the cures: the aggregate-row cure re-collapses, per-series scaling degrades but stays low, and only seasonal differencing keeps its one-step accuracy unchanged. We close with a three-step procedure for diagnosing and preventing the failure in deployed systems.

</details>


### [166] [Reliable Fusion of Conflicting Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27913)
*Pranuthi Tenali,Sahil Sidheekh,Saurabh Mathur,Vijayalakshmi Saravanan,Erik Blasch,Kristian Kersting,Sriraam Natarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于条件概率电路（C²MF）的轻量级融合框架，用于动态聚合多个黑盒LLM专家的冲突意见，通过上下文相关的可信度估计实现可靠推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动态数据驱动应用系统（DDDAS）中，聚合多个专家意见是基础挑战。传统静态聚合方法（如多数投票或固定加权平均）假设专家可靠性全局一致，无法捕捉专家在不同输入上下文中的可靠性差异，导致在专家意见冲突时产生不可靠结果。黑盒LLM API的兴起使传统动态门控方案（如MoE）难以适用，因为无法访问模型内部表示。

Method: 1. 使用预训练句嵌入模型将查询编码为上下文向量 z。2. 构建条件概率电路（CPC），其求和节点权重由以 z 为条件的小型MLP预测，建模联合分布 P(Y, p₁,...,p_K | z)。3. 通过CPC的精确边际化计算各专家上下文相关可信度得分（KL散度衡量）。4. 归一化可信度得分作为权重，对专家预测进行可信度加权平均（C²WM），或直接从CPC后验采样（C²DPC）。5. 在小规模标注数据上通过最大化条件对数似然训练。

Result: 在MMLU基准的医学、物理和计算机科学三个领域子集上，使用gemini-2.5-flash-lite作为专家模型进行实验：1. C²WM在准确率（66.54%）、精确率（68.43%）和F1（67.75%）上最优。2. C²DPC在AUROC（85.06%）和校准（最低ECE）上最优。3. 所有融合方法均优于单个领域专家（医学55.40%、CS 62.13%、物理60.29%）。4. 上下文感知方法均优于对应的上下文无关基线。

Conclusion: 基于条件概率电路的C²MF框架能够有效融合冲突的黑盒专家意见，相比静态集成方法在预测性能和校准方面均表现更优。该框架无需访问专家内部表示或重新训练，具有模型无关性。未来方向包括：使用扩散模型进行聚合、融合人类与AI专家、超越多选题任务（如因果推理），以及从共识中训练专家。

Abstract: We study the problem of aggregating opinions from multiple black-box experts in noisy, conflict-prone settings where expert reliability varies across inputs. Static aggregation methods, such as majority voting, fail to capture this variability and often yield unreliable outcomes under disagreement. We propose a tractable, probabilistic-circuit-based fusion framework that dynamically combines expert responses using context-specific credibility estimates, enabling principled and reliable reasoning. The framework is agnostic to the underlying experts and does not require access to their internal representations or any retraining. We empirically validate our approach on multiple-choice question answering tasks using multiple LLMs as experts, comparing against individual models and static ensemble baselines. Our method consistently improves predictive performance and produces more reliable decisions under conflict, highlighting the effectiveness of context-aware credibility modeling for robust multi-expert fusion.

</details>


### [167] [Binary Quantized Neural Network Training Is W[1]-Hard Parameterized by Input and Output Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27932)
*Tao Jiang,Minbo Gao,Shaowei Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文证明二元量化神经网络训练问题（2-QNNT）在以输入维度α与输出维度ω之和（α+ω）为参数时是W[1]-困难的，从而解决了Ganian等人（ICLR 2026）留下的开放问题。核心技巧是设计一种"单次翻转"前缀链训练集，使精确拟合等价于在架构DAG中寻找k条顶点不相交的路径，并将边容量问题通过有向线图转化为顶点容量问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ganian等人（ICLR 2026）证明了当联合参数化为架构树宽tw(G)、输入维度α和输出维度ω时，量化神经网络训练是固定参数可处理的（FPT），但遗留了一个开放问题：仅以α+ω为参数是否仍可FPT。本文旨在解决这一开放问题。

Method: 方法包括三个关键组件：（1）从DAG边不相交路径（DAG-EDP，W[1]-困难）出发，通过有向线图将边容量转化为顶点容量；（2）通过添加虚拟终端、长路径分层、私有细分和私有输出尾部，将所得DAG规范化成合法的QNNT分层架构；（3）设计一个"单次翻转"前缀链训练集，其中输入等于目标，训练样例形成按坐标排列的前缀链，证明在该数据集上精确拟合（零误差）当且仅当架构中存在k条顶点不相交的路径。利用二元权重{0,1}的单调性（因为前缀链输入使得激活单调非降），每次输出翻转可追溯回唯一改变的前驱，从而提取路径；不同翻转的路径因激活只能翻转一次而自然顶点不相交。

Result: 主要结果（定理1）：2-QNNT以α+ω为参数是W[1]-困难的。硬度在以下极强条件下成立：ℓ=0（零误差），训练集D_k中每个输入等于其目标，|D_k|=α=ω=k+1，样例形成按坐标的前缀链；以及每个非源偏置固定为零（无偏置变体）。假设指数时间假设（ETH），不存在运行时间为f(α+ω)·|I|^{o(α+ω)}的算法。还证明了定理2（单次翻转路由等价性）：在给定架构上存在零误差二元量化网络当且仅当存在k条顶点不相交的s_i-t_i路径；以及推论3：每个神经元在前缀链训练集上只有至多k+1种激活轮廓（全零或单次翻转）。参数映射为k↦α+ω=2k+2，规约为多项式时间。

Conclusion: 该结果区分了量化训练中两种结构：小的外部维度α+ω迫使神经元只有少量局部激活轮廓（k+1种），但预设的隐藏架构仍然可能编码全局放置难题，即如何在DAG中同时实现这些轮廓等价于顶点不相交路径路由。因此仅靠小外部维度无法实现FPT；必须额外限制架构结构参数（如树宽）才能获得FPT算法。该结果还揭示了二元量化的必要性：带符号权重的三元量化（命题10）因负权重可使输出因前驱下降而上升，从而破坏了单调性和前驱追溯论证。开放问题包括：有符号权重量化的复杂度、常数α+ω下的para-NP-困难性以及W[1]-成员资格。

Abstract: Ganian et al. (ICLR 2026) proved that quantized neural network training is fixed-parameter tractable when parameterized jointly by architecture treewidth, input dimension $\alpha$, and output dimension $\omega$, and left open whether $\alpha+\omega$ alone yields fixed-parameter tractability. We prove that 2-QNNT is W[1]-hard parameterized by $\alpha+\omega$. The hardness already holds with zero error on $D_k=\{(\xi^{(r)},\xi^{(r)}):0\le r\le k\}$, where every input equals its target, $|D_k|=\alpha=\omega=k+1$, and the examples form a coordinatewise prefix chain. It also holds when every non-source bias is fixed to zero. Under the Exponential Time Hypothesis, no algorithm runs in $f(\alpha+\omega)|I|^{o(\alpha+\omega)}$ for any computable $f$. The reduction starts from DAG edge-disjoint paths, converts edge capacity to vertex capacity with a directed line graph, and normalizes the result into a valid layered architecture. The key structural step is a one-flip routing equivalence: on the prefix-chain inputs, nonnegative binary weights make every activation monotone, and each required output transition has a weight-one predecessor making the same transition. Iterating this relation backward extracts a path from the unique changing input, while different transitions yield vertex-disjoint paths. In particular, every neuron on these inputs has only $k+1$ possible activation profiles.

</details>


### [168] [Quality over Quantity: Semi-Supervised Detection of Illicit Bitcoin Flows via Feature Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27936)
*Yekaterina Smolenkova,Nickolay Larionov,Nikolay Ivanov,Yury Yanovich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于半监督学习的比特币CoinJoin（Shared Send Mixer）非法交易检测框架，核心论点是：SSL性能的关键在于特征工程质量（数据质量）而非数据量。该框架在1.63亿笔CoinJoin交易数据集上验证，结合KeyLinker地址聚类、SSU复杂度度量等高质量特征，配合置信门控伪标签策略，在无标签数据上达到F1=0.84的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比特币混合交易（CoinJoin/SSM）是犯罪分子洗钱、勒索软件支付等非法活动的主要隐蔽手段，但这类交易恰恰缺乏可靠标注。现有监督学习方法（如GNN、集成方法）虽在常规交易上准确率超90%，但严重依赖大量标注数据，而CoinJoin交易的结构性模糊性和标注稀缺性形成了"越需要分析的交易，标注越少"的困境。半监督学习（SSL）虽有潜力，但其有效性究竟取决于数据量还是数据质量，此前未被系统研究。

Method: 方法包含三个互锁组件：(1) 数据基础——完整的Bitcoin历史数据集（至区块882,421），含1.63亿笔CoinJoin交易，其中460万笔有标签；(2) 特征工程——引入基于密码学公钥重用检测的KeyLinker地址聚类（无假合并）和Shared Send Untangling（SSU）复杂度度量（regular/simple/separable/ambiguous/time-limit五分类），结合CS与OTC启发式；(3) 置信门控伪标签框架——基于PR曲线选取高精确率阈值τ⁺≥0.95和高置信负类阈值τ⁻，仅将满足阈值的无标签样本加入训练集。模型对比XGBoost、CatBoost、Random Forest和Logistic Regression，使用F1-score作为主要指标。

Result: 核心实验结果：在监督学习阶段，XGBoost结合default+reuse+cs+ssu特征集达到最优F1=0.845、ROC-AUC=0.970；KeyLinker复用特征使所有模型的F1提升2-3个百分点；CS特征带来增量提升；但OTC特征始终未能改善甚至轻微降低结果。在半监督阶段，置信门控伪标签策略在最佳配置下将XGBoost的F1从0.845提升（具体数值见表4），加入77万至136万伪正样本和520万至860万伪负样本。摘要中强调的高保真特征在无标签数据上达到F1=0.84。

Conclusion: 研究确立了核心结论：在区块链取证领域，半监督学习的成功取决于数据质量而非数据量。基于密码学公钥重用（KeyLinker）和结构性复杂度度量（SSU）的高保真特征对检测性能起决定性作用；而依赖行为假设的OTC启发式虽数据丰富却引入噪声，会降低模型表现。树模型集成方法（XGBoost/CatBoost/RF）显著优于线性模型，验证了非法交易模式的非线性本质。置信门控伪标签策略在不牺牲精确率的前提下扩展了召回率。未来方向：扩展SSU至PayJoin、Whirlpool协议，并优化为实时流式检测。

Abstract: Detecting illicit cryptocurrency transactions is hampered by extreme class imbalance, adversarial obfuscation, and a scarcity of reliable labels. While semi-supervised learning (SSL) offers a promising solution by leveraging unlabeled data, we show that its success is not guaranteed by data volume alone but is contingent on data quality. We introduce an SSL framework for detecting illicit Bitcoin flows in Shared Send Mixers (SSM) transactions, built on a comprehensive historical dataset comprising 163 million transactions. Our main conclusion is that the success of SSL depends on data quality rather than volume: high-fidelity features such as KeyLinker address clustering and Shared Send Untangling (SSU) complexity metrics achieve an F1 score of 0.84 on unlabeled data. Finally, we empirically show that common heuristics like One-Time Change (OTC), though abundant, introduce noise, while strategic reliance on higher-fidelity features like KeyLinker is essential. Our work establishes that in blockchain forensics, the path to better performance lies in smarter feature engineering for data quality, not just larger datasets.

</details>


### [169] [When Accuracy Gaps Fail to Certify: Auditing Cross-Domain Recalibration of LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27954)
*Fariya Afrin,Ibne Farabi Shihab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文对LLM评判器跨域标量概率重校准进行了系统性审计，涵盖13个评判器、2个生成器和8个领域共1,176个预声明迁移。研究表明：源-目标准确率差距是校准误差的弱警告信号而非部署证书；在实际标签预算下，差距证书拒绝能力极低（m=1024时仅0.13），而使用16个目标标签直接做温度缩放反而效果更优（伤害率0.09 vs. 0.34）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM评判器的置信度分数（如0.8）常用于控制接受、审查或推迟决策，但后验标量重校准映射（如温度缩放、Platt缩放）在任务分布变化时常失效。实践中，源-目标准确率差距常被用作失效的代理诊断信号，但这一差距能否预测或证明标量概率迁移失败，缺乏系统性审计。论文旨在检验准确率差距作为跨域重校准诊断信号的可靠性及其作为部署准入证书的资格。

Method: 论文结合理论分析与大规模实证审计：(1) 理论层面：通过均值残差下界（引理2）将准确率差距与重校准分数漂移分离，证明差距提供目标L1校准误差的总体下界（推论3）；证明相同差距可导致相反结果（命题4）；证明在协变量偏移下精确重要性加权可恢复目标风险（定理6）；构造有限样本同时性拒绝证书（定理7）。(2) 实证层面：13个评判器（1.1B-7B开源模型+前沿API）+2个候选生成器（Qwen2.5-1.5B/Llama-3.2-1B）+8个NLP领域（GSM8K、MMLU、SQuAD等），共1,176个预声明迁移，采用拟合/审计分区（24/16项），两路聚类自举法推断，调整ANCOVA模型评估差距协变量，合成对照实验验证重要性加权实现的假设边界。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 严格递增映射下AUROC不变（命题1精确成立），标量校准可独立于排序漂移；(2) 漏无标签差距相关仅0.25（95% CI [-0.09, 0.55]），第二生成器降至0.09（CI [-0.11, 0.34]），不支持生成器不变关联；探针估计的原始目标Brier是唯一稳健协变量（系数+0.270，p=0.007）；(3) 重要性加权在自然NLP迁移中因支持失败（领域分类器AUROC=1.0，有效样本量接近统一）而无效，诊断指向观测文本表示而非条件偏移；(4) 拒绝证书覆盖率高但功效极低（m=1024时仅0.13）；(5) m=16目标温度缩放ΔBrier=-0.171、伤害率0.09，显著优于源拟合Platt（伤害率0.34），且固定阈值决策成本分析显示重校准可能反而推高决策成本（0.507→0.728）。

Conclusion: 准确率差距既不是P(Y|S)偏移的直接度量，也不是标量重校准迁移的充分统计量。跨13个评判器、2个生成器、8个领域的聚类调整推断未能建立生成器不变的差距-迁移关联。定理7的拒绝规则在理论上有效但在实际标签预算下过于保守。重要性加权控制显示方法失败必须追溯到支持失败、表示失败、估计失败或条件偏移，而非默认归因于条件偏移。因此，可靠的标量置信度迁移需要目标域证据或受辩护的传输假设；边缘准确率差距仅提供警告。

Abstract: A scalar recalibration map fitted for an LLM judge on one task can fail when the task distribution changes, but the source-target accuracy gap is often treated as a proxy for that failure. We test what this gap can predict and what it can certify across thirteen judges, two generators, eight domains, and 1,176 predeclared transfers. After accounting for mean score shift, the gap yields a population lower bound on target calibration error, yet identical gaps can induce opposite transfer outcomes. Exact importance weighting recovers target proper loss under covariate shift, so failure of an estimated weighting pipeline does not by itself establish conditional shift. A finite-sample simultaneous lower certificate converts the population bound into a one-sided rejection rule using audit labels disjoint from evaluation outcomes. The leak-free gap correlation is 0.25 (95% CI [-0.09, 0.55]), falls to 0.09 on the second generator, and does not support a generator-invariant association. The certificate retains nominal coverage but has power 0.13 even at m=1024, whereas target-domain temperature scaling with 16 labels reaches harm rate 0.09, compared with 0.34 for source-fitted Platt scaling. Accuracy gaps are therefore weak warning signals for scalar probability transfer, not deployment certificates.

</details>


### [170] [CS-WCP: Robust Conformal Sets for LLM-Judge Traffic Shifts with Uncertain Group Proportions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27955)
*Ibne Farabi Shihab,Fariya Afrin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CS-WCP 方法,针对 LLM-as-Judge 中因部署流量中任务/策略群体比例变化导致的覆盖率不足问题,通过构建源域与目标域群体质量的联合置信区间,对所有兼容的密度比向量取加权共形预测集的并集,实现分布偏移下的稳健覆盖保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当 LLM 评测的部署流量中各类任务或策略的群体比例发生变化时,标准 split conformal prediction 因交换性假设失效而产生系统性欠覆盖。现有加权共形预测(WCP)虽在协变量偏移下精确,但需要已知密度比;群体比例只能从有限未标注样本中估计,估计误差会削弱覆盖保证。需要在比例不确定下仍能保证覆盖的共形预测方法。

Method: CS-WCP(Confidence-Set Weighted Conformal Prediction):(1) 为每个群体的源域和目标域质量构建同时精确的 Clopper-Pearson 区间;(2) 映射为密度比向量的笛卡尔乘积矩形置信集 𝒲;(3) 返回该集合上所有兼容比向量对应的加权共形预测集的并集。核心定理(Thm 2):在固定或独立学习的有限划分下,覆盖保证至少为 1−α−δw−τA−κ,其中 τA 衡量组内协变量失配,κ 衡量条件偏移。关键计算技巧(Prop 3):线性端点规则只需 O(G|𝒴|) 时间,通过为每个组选择上界或下界端点计算并集,避免 2^G 个角点枚举。此外,Prop 4 利用标注审计样本给出 τA+κ 的可审计下界;Prop 5 证明无标签方法在无条件偏移假设下集合大小下界为 2(1−α)。

Result: 在 336 个构造的共享支持流量混合偏移中,CS-WCP 平均覆盖率达 0.973,仅有 13 次点失败;相比源域 CP 的 0.954 覆盖率和 44 次失败有显著改善,代价是平均二元集合大小从 1.65 增至 1.74。在 336 个自然跨任务迁移中,覆盖率从 0.882 提升至 0.962,但集合大小达到 1.87,且与大小匹配的群体 plug-in 基线相比竞争力有限。审计在 71 个自然迁移中拒绝相等性假设,对 16 个迁移给出严格正的 τA+κ 下界。研究确认 CS-WCP 的价值在于保守的尾部覆盖保护,而非标量概率校准或一致更小的集合大小。

Conclusion: CS-WCP 为流量混合比例不确定下的 LLM-judge 预测集提供了一个可审计的覆盖保障方法。其保守性来源于显式的比例置信预算而非手动调参,在尾部覆盖保护方面优于源域 CP,但集合大小代价不可忽视。方法的有效性依赖于有限划分传输模型的可信度和质量区间的窄度;条件偏移无上界时方法几乎空洞。该方法适合选择性预测场景,在多数输入上弃权,在少数输入上给出单标签。

Abstract: Prediction sets built from an LLM judge can undercover when deployment traffic changes the prevalence of task or policy groups. Weighted conformal prediction is exact under covariate shift when the density ratio is known, but group proportions must usually be estimated from finite unlabeled samples. We introduce confidence-set weighted conformal prediction (CS-WCP), which constructs simultaneous exact intervals for source and target group masses and returns the union of weighted conformal sets over every compatible ratio vector. For a fixed or independently learned finite partition, CS-WCP attains coverage at least 1-alpha-delta_w-tau_A-kappa, where tau_A measures within-cell covariate mismatch and kappa measures conditional shift. A linear endpoint rule computes the robust union in O(G|Y|) time. Across 336 constructed shared-support traffic shifts, CS-WCP reaches 0.973 mean coverage with 13 point failures, compared with 0.954 and 44 failures for source conformal prediction, at mean binary set sizes 1.74 and 1.65. On 336 natural cross-task transfers, coverage rises from 0.882 to 0.962, but mean set size reaches 1.87 and a size-matched group plug-in baseline is competitive. The method therefore supplies an auditable coverage safeguard under uncertain mixture weights; its value is conservative tail protection, not scalar probability calibration or uniformly smaller sets.

</details>


### [171] [I-SplineFlow: Learning Monotone Spline Stochastic Interpolant Schedulers for Few-Step Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27963)
*Md Sakib Hossain Shovon,Md Rifat Ur Rahman,Md Abtahi Majeed Chowdhury,Yunhong Min,Jaesik Choi,Minhyuk Sung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 I-SplineFlow，一种基于集成单调样条（I-splines）的随机插值（SI）调度器参数化方法，用于在少步数（few-step）下加速预训练扩散与流模型的生成。其核心思想是用紧支撑的 M-spline 导数基替代全局支撑的 Bernstein 基，从而在固定控制点数 K 的条件下独立选择多项式次数 p 与局部支撑宽度，并使边界条件与严格单调 SNR 由构造保证，无需对参数施加序约束。实验覆盖 EDM、ReFlow、Simple ReFlow 与 FlowDCN 等多个数据集与求解器，在多数设置下优于 BézierFlow 基线，低 NFE 提升尤为显著，且训练仅需数分钟。消融实验表明，次数自由度与单调性约束二者均不可或缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 Bézier 曲线的 SI 调度器（如 BézierFlow）使用全局支撑的 Bernstein 基，使得多项式次数与控制点数耦合（degree = K−1），每个控制点扰动整条曲线，远端调度区域相互耦合，优化条件差。本文旨在解决这一全局支撑的根本局限。

Method: 用集成单调样条（I-splines = 归一化 M-spline 的积分）替换 Bernstein 基。在夹紧结点向量上，K = p + 1 + m，使次数 p 与权重数 K 解耦，可固定 K 扫描 p。M-spline 导数基紧支撑，因此参数对调度器密度的控制是局部的；I-spline 通过 softmax 权重混合保证 ᾱ(0)=0、ᾱ(1)=1、σ̄(0)=1、σ̄(1)=0 与严格单调 SNRρ̄，且无参数序约束。所有变换速度所需导数均有闭式（M-spline 即 I-spline 的导数）。证明了 Bézier 是无内部结点的极限情形（Proposition 1）、次数-控制点解耦（Proposition 2）、非单调 SNR 导致重参数化失效（Proposition 3），以及 I-Spline 调度器的可采纳性定理（Theorem 1）。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10（32×32，EDM 与 ReFlow）、FFHQ、AFHQv2（64×64，EDM）与 ImageNet（256×256，FlowDCN）上，对比 UniPC、iPNDM、Euler、Midpoint 等求解器，I-SplineFlow 在大多数设置下取得最佳 FID，低 NFE（NFE=4–6）改进最显著：例如 CIFAR-10/EDM/UniPC NFE=4 时 FID 8.87 vs BézierFlow 9.81；AFHQv2/iPNDM NFE=4 时 12.33 vs 13.89；CIFAR-10/ReFlow/RK2 NFE=6 时 5.35 vs 6.03。Jacobian 有效条件数约 1.6×10⁵，比 BézierFlow（4.8×10¹⁰）小约五个数量级，且训练收敛快约 3 个 epoch、最终 LPIPS 低 6–15%。非单调 B-spline 消融在 iPNDM NFE=4 下退化至 17.7 FID，证实单调性必不可少。

Conclusion: I-SplineFlow 通过 I-spline 基实现了 SI 调度器的局部控制与构造性单调性，在不增加参数量的前提下将优化景观改善数个数量级，多数 few-step 设置下 FID 优于 BézierFlow，尤其在最低 NFE 时，并在流模型上的表现最为稳定。对扩散模型的改进存在不一致之处，作者将其作为未来工作，计划探索其他在扩散调度上更稳健的样条基。

Abstract: Few-step generation with pretrained diffusion and flow models can be accelerated by lightweight training that optimizes the sampling trajectory rather than the network. A recent approach parameterizes the stochastic interpolant (SI) scheduler as a smooth curve whose control points enforce the three properties an SI scheduler must satisfy: fixed boundary conditions, a monotone signal-to-noise ratio (SNR), and differentiability. Existing parameterizations use globally supported polynomial bases, where every control point moves the whole curve and higher expressiveness needs a higher degree, which couples distant regions of the schedule during optimization. We introduce \emph{I-SplineFlow}, which parameterizes the scheduler with integrated monotone splines (I-splines). I-splines decouple the polynomial degree from the number of mixture weights, so support width and smoothness can be chosen per model at a fixed weight count, and the compactly supported derivative basis makes the scheduler Jacobian orders of magnitude better conditioned than a Bézier basis. Boundary conditions and a strictly monotone SNR hold by construction, with no ordering constraint on the parameters and closed-form velocity derivatives. Across diffusion (EDM) and flow (ReFlow, Simple ReFlow) models, I-SplineFlow improves few-step FID over Bézier scheduling in most settings, most clearly at the lowest NFEs, and trains in minutes. Ablations show that both the degree freedom and the monotonicity constraint are needed. The code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [172] [Linear RNN Scaling Laws: When Longer Sequences Beat More Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27964)
*Ziyan Chen,Zhongzhu Zhou,Peilin Liu,Ding-Xuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对自回归预训练中序列结构对scaling laws的影响，建立了一个可解析求解的线性RNN师生模型。该模型刻画了模型维度M、轨迹数N、轨迹长度P与优化迭代R对预测损失的影响。核心发现在于：当创新协方差占主导（Regime I）时，N与P可互换；而当初始化协方差谱尾更重时（Regime II），更长的轨迹能额外抑制初始化瞬态，使得延长序列比增加轨迹数更有价值。论文给出了显式的分段scaling law及最优的M/N/P/R分配，并通过合成实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经验性scaling laws虽然在实践中极为成功，但其理论来源在序列预训练场景中并不清晰。以往的scaling law理论多基于回归或核模型，忽略了自回归预训练固有的序列依赖结构。本文旨在以可解的师生模型严格推导自回归next-token预测中的scaling law，并揭示轨迹数N与轨迹长度P在数据维度上的非等价性。

Method: 1. 教师：一个稳定的潜变量线性RNN，转移矩阵A*特征值满足幂律衰减j^{-r}，创新协方差与初始化协方差分别满足指数α与θ的幂律。2. 学生：通过高斯sketch限制观测到M维，linear recurrent student采用带safeguard的full-batch WSD梯度下降训练。3. 风险分解：将期望风险分为不可约损失、表示近似、GD偏置、GD方差和交叉项，分别给出上下界。4. 通过协方差事件+全局safeguard步骤的组合证明技巧处理非平稳、非独立样本。5. 在两个谱尾相对大小的子区间下分别求解，给出分段显式scaling rate及compute-optimal allocation。6. 通过直接构造的合成实验验证理论斜率。

Result: 理论结果（Theorem 3.1, Corollary 3.1）：Regime I（θ≥α）下，Approx≍M^{1-β_α}，Bias≍R^{(1-β_α)/α}，Var≍min{M,R^{1/α}}/(NP)，N与P在主导阶可互换。Regime II（α−2r≤θ<α）下，当P超过P_M或P_R时出现新的交叉项，使更长的轨迹额外减小近似与偏置，且Var仍以(NP)^{-1}为基本因子；compute-optimal分配中存在分段最优的M*,N*,P*,R*。合成实验测得的log-log斜率与理论预测吻合良好（例如 Regime II 中 P-sweep 测得斜率−1.253，理论−1.250；Regime I 中N-sweep斜率−1.008 vs −1.000）。

Conclusion: 本文构建了首个对自回归预训练中序列长度独立于轨迹数产生scaling law影响的严格理论模型。结果表明，序列长度在初始化谱尾更重的场景下是一个有价值的"compute-optimal design variable"，其作用超出单纯扩充数据量。作者讨论了向非线性表示、注意力架构及与ICL联系等扩展。

Abstract: Empirical scaling laws for autoregressive language models relate prediction loss to model size, data size, and optimization compute, but their theoretical origin is still poorly understood in sequential pretraining settings. We study this question in a tractable teacher--student model where a stable latent linear RNN generates trajectories and a sketched linear recurrent student is trained by safeguarded full-batch WSD gradient descent on next-token prediction. The sketch dimension $M$ plays the role of model size, while $N$ independent trajectories of length $P$ provide the training tokens. We allow the innovation and initialization covariances to have different power-law exponents $\alpha$ and $\theta$. The induced design spectrum produces explicit approximation, optimization, and statistical scaling laws separated by spectral crossovers. When $\theta\ge\alpha$, the original one-scale rates $M^{1-\beta_\alpha}$, $R^{(1-\beta_\alpha)/\alpha}$, and $(NP)^{-1}\min\{M,R^{1/\alpha}\}$ are recovered. When $\alpha-2r\le\theta<\alpha$, the heavier initialization tail changes the rates beyond $P$-dependent model and optimization crossovers. The proof uses a covariance event only internally and a globally safeguarded step size on its complement. The variance retains the factor $(NP)^{-1}$, while sequence length also suppresses the initialization transient, so $N$ and $P$ cease to be fully interchangeable in the two-scale regime.

</details>
