### [173] [Relative Discharge Stage (RDS) Classification: A Practical Indicator of Battery Discharge Progress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27986)
*Khoa Tran,Tri Le,Hung-Cuong Trinh,Hung Tran-Nam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的电池管理指标——相对放电阶段（Relative Discharge Stage, RDS），将剩余放电时间的连续回归问题转化为五阶段分类任务（Normal、Good、Moderate、Low、Recharge Required），无需未来负载信息即可提供可解释的放电状态指示，并结合物理信息驱动的SOC估计与轻量化TCN分类器，在两个公开锂电池数据集上取得了80%以上的分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统剩余放电时间（RDT）预测在未知且高度动态的未来负载条件下可靠性差，而SOC仅表征当前电量水平，无法直接告知剩余可用时间。因此需要一种不依赖未来电流信息、可解释且鲁棒的电池放电状态指示方法。

Method: 提出物理信息驱动的RDS分类框架：(1) 基于相对剩余放电比例 q_t 将放电过程离散为五类标签；(2) SOC估计模块结合二阶ECM状态预测、迟滞与OCV温度修正、核心温度估计、AEKF状态校正、OCV评价、在线STC-ECM参数自适应以及预训练神经网络残差电压补偿；(3) 将估计的SOC与测量电流、端电压、表面温度组成物理信息滑动观测窗口，输入轻量化时序卷积网络（TCN）进行五类分类。

Result: 在两个公开锂电池数据集上验证：第一数据集FTP-75测试集准确率95.38%、PDMHC测试集81.23%（平均88.31%），优于Transformer、LSTM、GRU等基线；第二数据集LCO_5准确率76.41%、LCO_6准确率73.87%，较最佳基线提升7-10个百分点；SOC估计在FTP-75上端电压MAE为0.52 mV，PDMHC为0.39 mV；测试推理时间703秒，兼顾精度与效率。

Conclusion: RDS作为新的可解释BMS指标，在未知未来负载条件下能有效提供电池剩余放电状态信息；结合物理信息SOC估计与轻量化TCN的框架在多变负载和热条件下均表现出鲁棒的分类性能。未来工作将拓展至部分放电、变初始SOC、多化学体系、老化状态、实车运行数据及嵌入式BMS平台。

Abstract: Accurate remaining discharge time (RDT) prediction is challenging in real-world battery applications because future load profiles are unknown and highly dynamic. To address the uncertainty of continuous RDT regression, this paper introduces Relative Discharge Stage (RDS), a battery-management indicator that represents the remaining discharge condition using five interpretable classes: Normal, Good, Moderate, Low, and Recharge Required. Unlike state of charge (SOC), which reflects the current charge level, RDS characterizes the remaining discharge process without requiring future-current information during inference. A physics-informed RDS classification framework is proposed, combining SOC estimation with lightweight temporal learning. The SOC-estimation component includes second-order ECM state and terminal-voltage prediction, hysteresis and OCV temperature correction, core-temperature estimation, and AEKF state correction, supported by OCV evaluation, online STC-ECM parameter adaptation, and pretrained neural residual-voltage correction. The measured current, terminal voltage, surface temperature, and estimated SOC are arranged into a sliding observation window and processed by a lightweight temporal convolutional network. Experiments on two public lithium-ion battery datasets demonstrate robust RDS classification, with accuracy exceeding 80% under varying load and thermal conditions.

</details>


### [174] [PCQC: Privileged Counterfactual Question Credit for Multi-Turn Medical Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27987)
*Chenxuan Li,Jiayi Wan,Xinrong Chen,Zhongyu Zhao,Xuecheng Shang,Peixing Wan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PCQC提出一种利用特权患者信息为多轮医疗对话中的"未执行问题"提供反向信用分配的训练框架，在四个医疗基准上以更少的问诊轮次取得更高的诊断准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗对话策略训练通常仅基于最终诊断结果进行监督（GRPO）或借助树搜索（ATPO），无法区分每个问诊问题的贡献，也未对未执行的候选问题提供直接监督。问答选择是医疗对话的核心，但缺乏细粒度的问题级信用信号。

Method: 在训练阶段，策略在相同对话状态 O_t 下采样 K=G_Q≥2 个候选问题。利用完整患者事实 z 的"特权信息"，冻结的患者响应器为所有候选问题（包括未执行的）生成答案；冻结的诊断评分器评估每个候选问答对支撑正确诊断的概率作为"诊断效用"。组内归一化得到相对问题信用 A_i^Q，并采用 PPO 裁剪目标对候选问题 token 进行监督，与终局 GRPO 联合优化。部署时无需特权信息或评分器。

Result: 在 MedQA、MedicalExam、MedMCQA、iCRAFT-MD 四项基准上达到 63.10% 平均诊断准确率，分别超过 GRPO（58.72%）与 ATPO（58.89%）4.38 与 4.21 个百分点，同时比 GRPO 平均节省 33.1% 的问诊轮次。消融显示直接监督未执行问题相对仅监督已执行问题可带来额外提升，且诊断效用与下游诊断成功率正相关（最高效用候选使后续成功率比均匀选择提升 4.20 pp）。

Conclusion: PCQC 通过训练时特权患者信息实现对未执行候选问题的相对问题信用分配，无需额外 rollout 即可扩展问题级监督。结合终局 GRPO，可在更少问诊轮次下取得更高诊断准确率，且诊断效用可作为候选问题的有效排序信号。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have made substantial progress on medical question-answering, yet effective medical dialogue also requires learning to ask questions that uncover relevant patient information. To train such dialogue policies, a common pipeline combines supervised fine-tuning with reinforcement learning (RL) based on final diagnostic correctness. However, this outcome-based supervision does not directly distinguish the contributions of individual questions and provides no question-level feedback for unexecuted alternatives. To address this gap, we introduce PCQC (Privileged Counterfactual Question Credit), which uses privileged patient information during training to learn from questions never asked. During training, PCQC makes alternative questions directly comparable at the same dialogue state by using privileged patient facts to construct their answers. A frozen diagnostic scorer evaluates the diagnostic utility of each resulting question-answer pair by how strongly it supports the correct diagnosis. PCQC turns these comparisons into relative question credit that teaches the policy which questions to favor, directly supervising both executed and unexecuted questions alongside outcome-based RL without requiring complete rollouts for the unexecuted alternatives. Extensive experiments across four medical benchmarks demonstrate that PCQC achieves 63.10% mean diagnostic accuracy, outperforming GRPO and ATPO by 4.38 and 4.21 percentage points, respectively. These gains are achieved with 33.1% fewer inquiry turns than GRPO.

</details>


### [175] [Learning from Failures: Heterogeneous Graph Memory for Small Language Model Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28003)
*Jiaxing Li,Lei Song,Rui Dong,Youyong Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FRESH框架，通过将成功与失败经验建模为异构图（任务、工具、动作、观察、错误、修复、前置条件），实现失败感知的检索与执行，帮助冻结的小型语言模型在长程、有策略约束的工具调用场景中提升可靠性与任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型/中型语言模型在长程、有策略约束的工具调用中容易出现结构性错误（如跳过必要观察、过早写入、重复失败调用、违反前置条件），而现有微调方法成本高，平面记忆无法保留失败因果与安全条件，导致失败动作被不当复用。

Method: 1) 经验挖掘：将成功/失败轨迹归一化并进行动作级归因，构建包含任务、工具、动作、观察、错误、前置条件、修复等节点和类型化关系（calls、requires、guards、verified_by、fails_because、fixed_by）的异构图；2) 异构图检索器（HG-Retriever）：采用稀疏召回+关系感知消息传递的HGNN，通过反馈衍生的正负样本进行成对排序训练；3) 测试时执行：将检索证据序列化为Do/Avoid/Check/Repair四类紧凑指导，喂给冻结的执行器，并由轻量级的策略与前置条件门控（Allow/Revise/Block/Transfer）在执行前验证高风险动作；4) 训练阶段通过反馈动态更新图效用和节点（评估阶段冻结）。

Result: 在τ-Bench（Airline/Retail）和AppWorld上使用Llama-3.2-1B、Qwen3-8B、Gemma-4-26B-A4B三个冻结开源模型，FRESH在pass^k、任务通过率上均优于无记忆基线和代表性记忆方法（Train-Text、Vector-RAG、ExpeL、ReasoningBank、Mem0、H-EPM），同时通常减少冗余执行步骤；消融实验表明策略门、HGNN检索器和失败记忆均有显著贡献，且图可视化显示其能保留跨任务的可复用结构。

Conclusion: FRESH通过将成功与失败经验组织为带类型化关系的异构图、并结合检索式紧凑提示与策略门控，使冻结的小型语言模型工具调用代理在不更新模型参数的情况下获得更可靠、更高效的工具使用能力；该工作强调了失败因果与执行前置条件建模对工具代理的重要性。

Abstract: Small and medium-sized language models offer cost-effective executors for tool-using agents, making them attractive for local and large-scale deployment. However, in long-horizon and stateful environments, they often make structural errors such as missing required observations, performing premature writes, repeating failed calls, and violating action preconditions. These errors can lead to incorrect state updates, policy violations, and costly or irreversible consequences, making reliable tool execution a critical deployment challenge. Existing fine-tuning approaches require substantial data and computation, while flat memory may retrieve failed actions without preserving their causal context or safety conditions. In this paper, we propose FRESH, a Failure-aware Retrieval framework over Experience-Structured Heterogeneous graphs, which transforms historical successes and failures into structured external experience for tool-using agents. By explicitly modeling the dependencies among tasks, actions, errors, repairs, and execution conditions, FRESH helps frozen language models reuse reliable strategies, avoid recurring failures, and make safer decisions in stateful tool interactions. Experiments on $\tau$-Bench and AppWorld with multiple open-source models show that FRESH consistently improves task success and tool-use reliability over no-memory agents and representative memory-based baselines.

</details>


### [176] [Shared Global KV with Layer-Specific Local History](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28006)
*Xinglang Xian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Decoder-only Transformer语言模型中跨层共享KV缓存时的设计选择：将全局KV在所有上层共享、每层保留有界局部KV时，局部记忆应保留历史内容还是仅保留当前token。作者在126M参数、2K上下文的模型上进行八种子实验，发现保留128位置局部历史比仅用当前token局部分支在保留测试困惑度上低约1.4%。该优势在容量、条目数和训练计算量控制下均成立。在与GQA和相邻层KV共享的对比中，经过相等学习率搜索后，局部历史设计在新种子上仍以更大缓存和更高长请求延迟为代价获得更好的同源似然。该优势在等token适配到8K后仍保持，但伴随短上下文代价。作者还推导了一个充分后缀调度，可在精确算术下保留完整缓存的同时减少上层构造工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Decoder-only Transformer在自回归生成中跨层维护KV缓存导致存储随序列长度和模型深度增长。跨层共享KV可降低存储但减少了层间可用的表征多样性。现有工作（如YOCO、LCKV、CLA等）探讨了不同程度的共享，但未明确回答：在共享全局KV的前提下，每层应保留什么类型的局部记忆？这涉及两个相互独立的维度——历史内容（保留哪些过去条目）与输入源粒度（每层独立形成局部KV还是相邻层共享输入）。论文旨在系统分离这两个设计选择，并通过严格的多种子受控实验加以验证。

Method: 方法包含三个层面：(1)架构：16层非循环解码器，下层8层计算共享全局KV，上层8层通过联合softmax同时读取全局前缀和128位置局部窗口的KV；(2)干预：分离内容（历史vs仅当前token）、容量（独立KV、点状适配器）、条目数（重复当前条目控制）、融合方式（联合softmax vs 学习标量权重）、输入源（每层输入 vs 相邻层共享输入）；(3)评估协议：8种子因子实验、2.5B token训练预算匹配控制、独立学习率网格搜索、新种子同源确认文本、FP32 IEEE精确推理测量、CUDA Graph加速的精确后缀构造调度（含完整KV保留的证明）。

Result: 核心结果：(a) 在126M/2K/1B token的8种子因子设计中，联合历史相比仅当前token局部分支在开发集上降低1.37%、保留测试集上降低1.41%困惑度，8/8种子均有利；(c) 305M探针在2个种子上也支持此方向；(d) 2.5B token预算下，历史模型在匹配矩阵乘FLOPs下仍对所有三个控制（仅当前、重复当前、计算匹配当前）有优势；(e) 在等3点学习率搜索后，新种子上局部历史对GQA2降低1.43%、对GQA4-CLA2降低1.15%困惑度；(f) 等token适配到8K后，优势持续至2K/4K/8K所有长度，在长开发文本上保持约1.1-1.2%，在固定书目上从2.08%扩大至2.91%；(g) 资源权衡：局部历史使用19.63%更多缓存，预填充快16.40%但128步解码请求慢32.98%（相对于GQA4-CLA2，batch 1，1792 prompt token）；(h) 零样本任务结果混合，PIQA倾向备选，HellaSwag倾向历史，ARC-Easy方向跨种子变化。

Conclusion: 局部历史改善了在共享全局KV下的域内预测；测试的相邻层输入共享设计在保留独立投影的前提下保留了历史优势，同时缩短了构造依赖并将缓存与KV共享区分开来。相等的token适配在8K下保持了架构对比。完整缓存和请求测量量化了资源权衡，有界依赖允许精确的后缀构造在保留完整缓存的同时降低预填充成本。值得注意的是：8种子的外部书籍历史效应仍未解决，下游任务结果因任务而异，完整设计对比相比共享2KV的补充短上下文成本。该工作确立了在共享全局KV下保留历史内容与选择其源是需要分别评估的独立设计维度。

Abstract: Decoder-only Transformer language models cache keys and values (KV) to reuse past computation during generation. Sharing KV across layers saves storage but reduces the diversity of representations available across depth. We study what local memory should retain alongside shared global KV, separating historical content from the input source used to form it. At 126M parameters and 2K context, an eight-seed study finds about 1.4% lower held-out test perplexity with local history than with a current-token local branch. Capacity, entry-count and training-compute controls support the value of historical content. In a two-seed comparison, this value persists when adjacent layers share local inputs while retaining independent projections; source sharing also shortens exact cache-construction dependencies. Against GQA and adjacent-layer KV sharing, equal bounded learning-rate searches and new-seed confirmation yield better same-source likelihood with larger caches and higher long-request latency. The ordering against adjacent-layer sharing persists after equal-token adaptation to 8K, with a short-context cost. The eight-seed external-book history effect remains uncertain, and downstream outcomes vary by task. We derive a sufficient suffix schedule that reduces upper-layer construction work while preserving the complete cache in exact arithmetic.

</details>


### [177] [Exact Quantile Balancing and Load-Error Injection for Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28053)
*Pit Neitemeier,Jiaze Li,Alessio Serra,Philipp Scholl,Sohir Maskey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Exact Quantile Balancing (EQB)与Load-Error Injection (LEI)，分别针对MoE训练的全局与局部负载均衡问题，通过精确分位数的双通道radix选择和直接注入负载误差到路由器梯度，在7.5B MoE上验证了优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE训练需要两种规模的负载均衡：全局均衡（避免专家欠利用）和局部均衡（保证专家并行的微批次效率）。现有的分布式Quantile Balancing依赖分片依赖或近似的全局分位数，token独立的专家偏差无法保证微批次级均衡。

Method: EQB采用两遍BF16 radix选择，通过两次256-bin all-reduce精确恢复全局批次的经验分位数，通信量与token数无关；LEI使用未归一化路由器均分作为STE代理，将局部负载误差直接（经tanh归一化）注入路由器分数梯度，避免GShard loss中归一化概率引起的专家耦合与硬分配不一致问题。

Result: 在7.5B参数MoE（E=256、K=6）上训练最多500B tokens：EQB将Global/Local MaxVio较rank-averaged QB改善19.4%/9.8%；normalized LEI在100B tokens上将Local MaxVio从4.71降至3.52，500B tokens上从5.14降至4.15同时平均精度从48.25%提升至48.56%，且注意力logit稳定。

Conclusion: EQB与LEI互补地控制MoE的全局与局部负载均衡：EQB以token数无关通信恢复精确全局分位数，LEI将局部负载残差直接注入路由器分数梯度，在GShard基础上以可比的模型质量改善均衡，并通过tanh裁剪保证不引发注意力logit不稳定。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) training requires global load balance to prevent expert under-utilization and local balance for efficient expert-parallel execution. Existing distributed Quantile Balancing (QB) uses shard-dependent or approximate global quantiles, while token-independent expert biases cannot ensure microbatch-level balance. We introduce Exact Quantile Balancing (EQB), which computes exact global-batch BF16 quantiles with negligible communication, and Load-Error Injection (LEI), which injects local load errors directly into router-score gradients. On 7.5B-parameter MoEs trained for up to 500B tokens, EQB improves global balance and downstream performance over naive QB, while LEI improves local balance and outperforms the GShard loss at comparable quality.

</details>


### [178] [Probabilistic and Geometry Aware Neural Surrogate of Scrape Off Layer Plasma Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28116)
*Gabriele Gianuzzo,Stefan Dasbach,Fleur Hendriks,Sven Wiesen,Vlado Menkovski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于SOLPS-ITER刮削层（SOL）等离子体边界模拟的快速代理模型，通过将曲网格展开为保留邻接关系的三视图图像张量并采用条件流匹配（conditional flow matching）建模，实现了概率性、几何感知的神经替代，能够在偏滤器脱靶过渡区等多解性敏感区同时捕捉多模态稳态分布而非均值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SOLPS-ITER代理模型存在两大问题：1）展平向量表示丢失了网格几何结构；2）确定性点估计无法处理脱靶过渡区附近固有的多稳态分叉行为，只能给出多解的平均且无置信度信息。这限制了不确定性传播和设计搜索等需要数百次求解的任务。

Method: 1) 几何感知三视图表示：将104×50固定拓扑曲网格划分为A–F六条连续带状区域，通过邻接保持的顺序展开为顶(A,B)、中(C,D,E)、底(F)三个固定55×134的画布张量；七种邻接关系通过padding边界复制和FiFM再注入重建，实现5200个cell的双射无损编码。2) 条件流匹配（CFM）：在每个视图上用共享U-Net（双编码器：条件编码器+主编码器，ControlNet式路由，FiLM调制），采用直线路径和数据预测参数化，训练mask加权MAE损失；推理时通过概率流ODE Euler积分（K=50步）从S个先验采样获得预测均值和标准差。3) 数据为7221个SOLPS-ITER收敛稳态（5756训练/1465测试），8个全局标量→22个场通道；使用AdamW训练200 epoch。

Result: 1) 重建保真度：在偏滤器相关三种状态下，2D场重建的对数MAE在0.09–0.20范围，无明显偏差；冷核态较差但绝对误差<0.44 eV。

Conclusion: 固定拓扑的曲网格边缘可表示为三张图像张量送入普通CNN而不损失邻接关系；当稳态存在多解时，多模态预测分布比均值更诚实地反映物理。本文存在三点局限：无与确定性基线的逐点精度对比；冷核态超出流体-中性模型有效范围；表示方法绑定于单一固定拓扑网格，换几何需手工重新推导带状边界与邻接关系。

Abstract: Fast surrogates for tokamak boundary-plasma simulation are typically deterministic regressors mapping a global operating point to a flattened vector of cell values. Near the divertor detachment transition the steady state is not reliably single-valued. A point estimate must average over qualitatively different plasma states, and it arrives with no statement of confidence. Moreover, the flattened vector representation discards the geometric structure of the SOLPS-ITER mesh. This work addresses both problems. We unroll the curvilinear mesh into three fixed-size image tensors whose layout preserves cell adjacency and inverts exactly, letting a convolutional network act on the geometry without loss of information. A conditional flow matching model, well suited to highly sensitive systems, is then trained on this representation. The result is an efficient, scalable surrogate that captures multiple plausible outcomes even at sensitive operating points. Along a gas-puff scan, the predictive distribution splits into a hot and a cold mode across an early regime transition. A further check on synthetic data with an injected bifurcation of known size confirms the model recovers both branches rather than their average.

</details>


### [179] [RL Starts before RL: On Policy Distillation for Better Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28145)
*Shuai Dong,Yongfu Zhu,Yuqi Xu,Weichu Xie,Liuwenpu,Ziyue Wang,Kaiwen Tuo,Congcong Wang,Siyuan Wang,Wenqi Shao,Shuai Yang,Ji Zhao,Caoyuan Ma,Wenzheng Chang,Taiqiang Wu,Xinlei Yu,Hongrui Wu,Xiaoxuan He,Fangke Chen,Dianyi Wang,Kanghui Tian,Sirry Chen,Xingyu Liu,Xiangnan Wu,Jiawei Guo,Haowen Hou,LingHan Chen,Zhongyu Wei,Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在线策略蒸馏（OPD）作为强化学习（RL）前的准备阶段，系统比较了Base→RL、SFT→RL和OPD→RL三种路径，发现OPD初始化可在后续RL中带来更高的最终性能，即使蒸馏阶段对即时准确率的提升很小。分析表明Pre-RL Pass@k无法完全解释该优势，作者提出"教师分布对齐+保留备选路径的不确定性"作为可能的解释，并进一步比较了反向KL（RKL）与正向KL（FKL）在学生生成与教师生成轨迹上的表现，发现最优蒸馏目标的选择依赖于轨迹来源和后续训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL的最终性能依赖于起始策略。常见的准备方法（SFT）虽可提升即时准确率，但未必能为RL提供更好的起点。论文的核心问题是：在线策略蒸馏（OPD）作为RL前的准备阶段，其收益是否超越了蒸馏模型即时准确率的提升？为何某些准备策略能让后续RL获得更大增益？此外，不同蒸馏目标（RKL/FKL）与轨迹来源（学生生成/教师生成）应如何匹配以最大化后续RL效果？

Method: 1. 三种准备路径对比：Base→RL（直接强化学习）、SFT→RL（监督微调后强化学习）、OPD→RL（在线策略蒸馏后强化学习）。文本设置使用Qwen3-8B学生/Qwen3-32B教师；视觉-语言设置使用Qwen3-VL-8B/Qwen3-VL-32B。
2. 共享RL设置：三种管道在每个设置内共享GRPO风格RL、训练数据、验证器、优化器和采样配置。
3. 蒸馏目标对比：反向KL（RKL）与正向KL（FKL），分别在学生生成轨迹（OPD）和教师生成轨迹（TFD）上应用，VL设置进行四路对比。
4. 行为分析：在1,024个共享诊断提示上测量Pass@k、正确计数分布、群体结果概率、Top-20 JS散度、Top-1一致性、Jaccard重叠和词法条件token熵。
5. 评估套件：8个文本基准和20个VL基准。

Result: 1. OPD→RL在两种设置中均获得最高最终平均分：文本59.18（超过Base→RL的51.90和SFT→RL的47.48），VL 63.02（超过Base→RL的61.68和SFT→RL的61.33）。
2. 文本设置中，OPD初始平均仅提升0.54分（36.48→37.02），但RL后差距扩大至7.28分；SFT初始更高（37.75）但最终更低（47.48）。
3. Pre-RL Pass@k无法完全解释优势：文本Base与OPD的Pass@k曲线几乎重叠（Pass@1: 18.4% vs 19.3%；Pass@64: 80.4% vs 80.0%），但RL后性能差距显著。
4. 行为分析：OPD降低JS散度（文本0.01701→0.01468，VL 0.07734→0.06431），同时保留较高token熵（文本1.048 vs SFT 0.242），而SFT虽然Top-1一致性略高但分布更集中。
5. KL目标对比（学生生成轨迹）：RKL蒸馏后更强（60.66 vs 60.17），但RL后FKL超越（64.87 vs 63.02），FKL在14/20任务上领先。KL目标对比（教师生成轨迹）：RKL在两个阶段均领先（61.90 vs 59.20）。
6. FKL具有更高词法熵（0.952 vs 0.629）和更低JS（0.06234 vs 0.06431），即使Pass@k几乎相同。

Conclusion: 1. OPD作为RL前的准备阶段有效，其下游收益超越即时准确率提升，应通过后续RL后的性能来评估蒸馏选择。
2. 教师分布对齐与保留备选路径的不确定性共同解释OPD优势：分布对齐使学生偏向高质量推理路径，而保留的不确定性为RL利用结果反馈进一步优化留有空间。
3. 蒸馏目标选择应联合考虑轨迹来源和后续训练：FKL在学生生成轨迹上RL后更优，RKL在教师生成轨迹上两者阶段均优。
4. 蒸馏后即时性能更强的检查点未必是RL的更佳起点，Pre-RL Pass@k不足以预测RL后的性能排名。
5. 局限性：未独立分离教师分布对齐与保留不确定性的独立贡献，未设计明确面向后续RL的新型蒸馏目标，未来工作需通过受控干预分离这些因素。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) improves reasoning, but its performance depends on the policy from which training begins. We study on-policy distillation (OPD) as a preparation stage for RL and ask whether its benefits extend beyond improvements in the distilled model's initial accuracy. Under shared RL settings, students initialized with OPD reach higher final performance than those trained with direct RL or supervised fine-tuning followed by RL. This advantage can emerge even when OPD produces little immediate improvement in accuracy. Pre-RL Pass@k does not fully explain the benefit: similar or even higher values do not necessarily lead to better performance after RL. Behavioral analyses point to alignment with the teacher's distribution beyond top-1 agreement as a possible explanation. Such alignment may favor higher-quality reasoning paths while retaining alternatives that RL can further refine using outcome feedback. We further examine how trajectory sources and divergence objectives affect the value of distillation for subsequent RL. Standard reverse-KL OPD performs better before RL, but forward-KL OPD overtakes it afterward; with teacher-generated distillation trajectories, reverse KL remains ahead at both stages. These findings suggest that the preferred distillation objective depends on both the trajectory source and the training that follows. Our results support evaluating OPD as preparation for RL and selecting distillation choices by the performance achieved after subsequent training.

</details>


### [180] [Confidence Falls Short: Asymmetric Certainty Gains from Optimization Hinder Multimodal Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28165)
*Longfei Huang,Xiangyu Wu,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多模态学习中因优化过程导致的强/弱模态预测置信度不对称问题，提出了一种名为MaxCR（Max Confidence Regularization）的新方法。该方法通过非线性稀疏度度量动态监测各模态的语义置信度，并对强模态施加最大抑制（max suppression）、对弱模态施加最大激励（max excitation），从而在多模态融合时实现校准的置信度，提升整体分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态学习常因模态不平衡（modality imbalance）导致性能次优，甚至不如最优单模态。现有方法多从优化动力学（如梯度调制）的角度入手，但本文发现一个更根本的问题：优化过程对强模态和弱模态产生不对称的确定性增益（asymmetric certainty gains），即强模态过度自信、弱模态欠自信，这一现象源于单模态自身特性而非多模态融合策略。因此，需要一种从置信度角度直接干预的方法。

Method: 1. **理论分析**：定义确定性传递比率 $r_N(\theta)$，证明当弱模态的Bayes后验存在固有歧义时（$H(q^v) \geq H_w > 0$），随着优化接近最优，certainty gain趋于零，但mini-batch梯度的随机波动仍非零，导致优化-置信度解耦（optimization-certainty decoupling）。\n2. **置信度监测**：设计非线性稀疏度度量 $s_i^r = \frac{1}{L}\sum_{p_{ij} \leq \epsilon} \exp(-p_{ij}/\tau)$，并以递归平滑方式计算模态稀疏度分数 $s_t^r$，根据差异比 $\lambda = |s_t^a - s_t^v|$ 决定调控策略。\n3. **最大抑制（Max Suppression）**：对强模态施加 $\mathcal{L}_{sup} = \frac{1}{N}\sum_i \lambda(\max_j p_{ij}^r - 1/C)$，惩罚top-1置信度。\n4. **最大激励（Max Excitation）**：对弱模态施加 $\mathcal{L}_{exc} = \frac{1}{N}\sum_i \lambda(1/C - \max_j p_{ij}^r)$，鼓励top-1置信度。\n5. 推理时通过对各模态预测取平均获得多模态预测。

Result: 在CREMAD、KSounds、VGGSound、Twitter、Sarcasm和NVGesture六个数据集上的实验表明，MaxCR在Accuracy、MAP和Macro-F1三个指标上均优于包括OGM、AGM、PMR、MLA、ReconBoost、LFM、AUG等在内的SOTA基线方法。此外，MaxCR在校准指标（ECE、NLL、Brier Score）上也显著优于基线，在KSounds上ECE从0.1607降至0.0384。消融实验验证了最大抑制和最大激励各自的有效性。MaxCR与OGM等梯度调制方法兼容（MaxCR+OGM可进一步提升性能）。相比标签平滑（label smoothing）和熵最小化，MaxCR的策略更为有效。训练开销分析显示MaxCR在保证最佳精度的同时训练时间有限。

Conclusion: 本文从预测置信度的角度重新审视了多模态分类中的模态不平衡问题，揭示了优化过程产生的非对称确定性增益现象，并将其理论化为"优化-置信度解耦"。基于此提出的MaxCR范式——最大抑制与最大激励——通过动态调节各模态的top-1置信度来校准多模态融合，实现了优于现有SOTA方法的性能。本文结果突显了预测确定性作为模态不平衡问题中重要但被忽视的维度，并确立了置信度感知干预作为平衡优化策略的简单而有效的补充手段。

Abstract: Multimodal learning (MML) falls into the optimization dilemma due to the modality imbalance phenomenon, leading to suboptimal overall performance in practice. While many attempts primarily focus on balancing the optimization dynamics across modalities to address this issue, we identify a subtle yet critical flaw: optimization yields asymmetric gains in predictive certainty, with the strong modality more confident than the weak one, driving imbalanced modality contributions. In this paper, our analysis reveals that this flaw stems from unimodal characteristics rather than multimodal learning, and this confidence discrepancy can be corrected by positive cross-modal intervention. Based on this insight, we propose multimodal Max Confidence Regularization (MaxCR) to dynamically intervene in modality semantic confidence. Specifically, the semantic confidence of each modality is tracked using a nonlinear sparsity measure. We then design max suppression and max excitation based on this measure to regularize strong and weak modalities, respectively. They penalize and encourage the top-1 confidence, thereby constraining multimodal prediction. To this end, strong and weak modalities are expected to make calibrated confidence, thereby improving the overall performance. Empirical experiments on widely used datasets reveal the superiority of our method through comparison with various state-of-the-art (SOTA) multimodal learning baselines.

</details>


### [181] [Geospatial embeddings detect old-growth forests but buffered spatial validation narrows their advantage over Sentinel features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28194)
*Thomas Ratsakatika,Mihai Zotta,Srinivasan Keshav,Emily R. Lines*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在罗马尼亚南喀尔巴阡山脉Făgărăş山区211,893公顷的山毛榉-云杉景观中，系统比较了地理空间基础模型（GFM）嵌入（AlphaEarth、TESSERA v2）与Sentinel-1/2特征在古老林（old-growth forest）检测中的表现。研究构建了高置信度的参考标签，采用空间分块交叉验证并引入10 km训练-测试缓冲区。研究发现：在无缓冲区情况下，TESSERA表现最佳（PR-AUC=0.84），显著优于Sentinel和AlphaEarth；但在10 km空间缓冲区下，三种地球观测特征的差异不具有统计显著性。CNN模型在10 m分辨率下未带来额外收益。结论指出，缓冲空间验证对于将模型迁移到未见景观至关重要，限制模型性能的关键因素是参考标签的地理多样性和真实负样本的纳入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 古老林是欧洲生物多样性保护优先事项，但面积小且分散，需要既能精确到林地块尺度又能在全欧洲部署的检测方法。现有研究多依赖机载激光雷达（ALS），覆盖有限；而地理空间基础模型（GFM）嵌入在标签稀缺的土地分类中表现出潜力，但其对古老林检测的增益尚未明确。此外，空间自相关问题在GFM研究中普遍被忽视，可能导致性能虚高，削弱模型向未见过景观的迁移能力。

Method: 研究区域为罗马尼亚南喀尔巴阡山脉Făgărăş山区。参考标签通过基于规则的数据融合构建，包括1,016个OGF地块（基于专家确认的原始和准原始森林）和3,837个非OGF地块（≤80年林龄），覆盖12,775公顷和34,574公顷。构建五个特征集：坐标对照、基线（地形和可达性）、基线+传统EO（Sentinel-1/2）、基线+AlphaEarth嵌入（64维）、基线+TESSERA v2嵌入（128维）。模型架构包括XGBoost和3×3、5×5、7×7感受野的CNN。采用六折空间分块嵌套交叉验证，并在0、10、20 km训练-测试缓冲区下评估。主要指标为PR-AUC（精确率-召回率曲线下面积），辅以ROC-AUC、重叠系数、精确率、召回率和F1。通过半变异函数和Moran's I量化空间自相关。计算Carpathian山脉的适用性区域（AOA）。

Result: 1. 现有地图与参考标签的一致性差异显著（0.35-0.65 PR-AUC），Sabatini（2020）最差，Schickhofer & Schwarz（2019）最佳（81%一致）。2. 在0 km缓冲区下，TESSERA+XGBoost的PR-AUC达0.84[0.79-0.88]，优于Sentinel-1/2和AlphaEarth各+0.08（p<0.001）。3. 在10 km缓冲区下，所有EO特征集相对基线的PR-AUC增益为0.21-0.25[0.15-0.34]，但TESSERA相对Sentinel和AlphaEarth的优势缩小至+0.04和+0.03，置信区间跨零，差异不显著。4. CNN在10 m分辨率下未优于基于像素的XGBoost。5. 映射55,025 ha[39,124-62,515 ha]为候选古老林，覆盖36.1%[25.7-41.0%]的森林面积。6. 在Carpathian山脉9.90 Mha森林中，65.0%位于适用区域内。

Conclusion: 自由可用的卫星数据可检测古老林，且其信号超出地形和可达性预测因子的能力，在空间验证和缓冲处理下仍然稳健。然而，TESSERA对传统EO和AlphaEarth的优势仅在无缓冲区情况下具有意义，一旦引入10 km缓冲区则不具有统计显著性。研究表明，空间分块验证对于控制空间自相关是必要但不充分的，训练-测试缓冲是将古老林检测模型迁移到未见过景观时的必需条件。限制模型性能的关键因素是参考标签的地理多样性以及真实负样本的纳入，而非更复杂的特征或模型架构。未来欧洲范围的古老林制图需要：1）更多样化的参考标签（包括确认的负样本）；2）适用于低普遍率情境的统一评估指标（特别是PR-AUC）；3）测试模型的时间迁移能力以实现监测。

Abstract: Old-growth forests develop over centuries under minimal anthropogenic disturbance, producing structurally complex and biodiverse stands. In Europe, protecting them requires mapping that is accurate for individual forest parcels yet deployable continent-wide. Geospatial foundation model (GFM) embeddings enable label-scarce land classification, but their value for old-growth detection remains unknown. Here, we map old-growth forests across 211,893 ha of Romania's Southern Carpathians, a beech-spruce landscape typical of the Alpine Biogeographic Region. We construct high-confidence, expert-informed reference labels for old-growth and non-old-growth parcels. We add AlphaEarth, TESSERA v2 and Sentinel-1/2 features to a common baseline of topographic and human-access predictors, then compare them under spatially blocked validation with and without 10 km train-test buffers to limit residual autocorrelation. With buffering, GFM and Sentinel-1/2 predictors increase precision-recall AUC by 0.21-0.25 [95% CIs: 0.15-0.34] relative to baseline, indicating spectral data contain a spatially robust old-growth signal. With a PR-AUC of 0.84 [0.79-0.88], TESSERA outperforms Sentinel-1/2 (+0.08 [+0.05 to +0.11]) and AlphaEarth (+0.08 [+0.04 to +0.12]) under unbuffered spatial validation. At a 10 km buffer, however, this advantage narrows to +0.04 [-0.01 to +0.11] and +0.03 [-0.04 to +0.10], intervals consistent with no difference. At 10 m resolution, convolutional neural networks add no benefit over pixel-based XGBoost. Comparisons with four national- and continental-scale products show the importance of non-old-growth labels, and reveal 81% agreement between our predictions and a field-calibrated map. We conclude that buffered spatial validation is vital when transferring old-growth detection models to unseen landscapes, and provide our labels and predictions for future work.

</details>


### [182] [Transferable Evidence Reconstruction for Longitudinal Glucose Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28199)
*Tian Zhou,Bingqing Peng,Linxiao Yang,Wenwei Wang,Mengni Ye,Beverly Jin,Zuyi Zhu,Jinjie Gu,Liang Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TER（可迁移证据重建），一种针对生理时序数据的自监督学习方法，通过跨群体的低容量读出器测试学习可迁移的证据解码规则。在连续血糖监测（CGM）的14项任务排行榜上，TER将最强基线的PR-AUC/ROC-AUC/Macro-F1分别提升5.51/4.43/2.80个百分点，并在12/14任务上刷新最佳成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时间生理记录中常规测量占多数，而预测性信息集中在罕见事件、持续负荷和重复出现的时序模式中。现有自监督方法（如掩码自编码、对比学习）侧重于恢复测量或对齐视图，未能显式优先利用结构化信号证据，限制了生理信号下游任务的迁移泛化表现。

Method: 1. TER框架：从无标签记录构建证据（evidence）；在某群体上拟合一个低容量"读出器（reader）"；要求同一读出器在另一群体上不重新拟合的情况下恢复相同证据；通过跨群体测试的可微优化学习具备可迁移证据解码规则的表征。证据仅用于引导自监督，不作为下游特征。
2. CGM专用架构：观测感知的日级编码器（observation-aware daily encoder）+ 时钟感知的多日记忆模块（clock-aware multi-day memory），将血糖水平与变化绑定到记录时间，并组织最长7天的历史。
3. 通过目标读出器消融、同历史控制和跨个体读出三类实验验证关键设计。

Result: 在CGM 14任务排行榜上：TER将最强先前方法的PR-AUC/ROC-AUC/Macro-F1总体分数分别提升5.51/4.43/2.80个百分点；在12/14任务上取得新的最优指标；这些提升幅度是两种最强基线差距的2.0-2.9倍。在公共预训练数据、折划分和线性探测均匹配的情况下，TER比复现的GlucoFM在三项指标上分别高出6.09/5.52/2.72个百分点。

Conclusion: 结构化证据、跨群体读出器拟合与学习到的多日组织三者结合，对CGM生理信号自监督表征学习至关重要。TER通过可迁移证据重建有效捕捉罕见事件、持续负荷与重复时序模式，显著提升多项下游预测任务的泛化能力。

Abstract: Long physiological recordings contain many routine measurements, while predictive information is often concentrated in rare events, sustained burden, and recurring temporal patterns. Masked autoencoding recovers measurements; contrastive learning aligns views. We study self-supervision that explicitly prioritizes structured signal evidence. We introduce transferable evidence reconstruction (TER), which constructs evidence from unlabeled recordings, fits a fresh low-capacity reader on one recording group, and requires that reader to recover the same evidence in another group without refitting. Differentiating through this cross-group test learns representations with transferable evidence-decoding rules; the evidence guides self-supervision but is not used as a downstream feature. For continuous glucose monitoring (CGM), an observation-aware daily encoder and clock-aware multi-day memory bind glucose level and change to recorded time while organizing up to seven days of history. On the 14-task leaderboard, TER improves the strongest prior overall PR-AUC/ROC-AUC/Macro-F1 scores by 5.51/4.43/2.80 percentage points and sets a new best metric on 12/14 tasks. These leaderboard gains are 2.0-2.9 times the respective gaps between the two strongest baselines. With public pretraining data, folds, and the linear probe matched, TER outperforms our GlucoFM reproduction by 6.09/5.52/2.72 points. Target-reader ablations, same-history controls, and cross-person readouts support the combination of structured evidence, cross-group reader fitting, and learned multi-day organization.

</details>


### [183] [Support-Compiled Feature Folding: More Evidence at Lower Memory Across Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28208)
*Tian Zhou,Beverly Jin,Xue Wang,Linxiao Yang,Wenwei Wang,Bingqing Peng,Mengni Ye,Jinjie Gu,Liang Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCFF是一个训练免费的推理框架，通过支持路由的特征折叠将宽表格基础模型的二次特征交互开销转换为线性，同时降低GPU内存峰值。在六个冻结骨干上统一提升精度和NLL，相对误差最多降低26.1%，内存中位数节省2.09–2.36倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型在宽特征表（D≫100）上遭遇特征端扩展困境：全宽成对特征混合的计算与内存随列数平方增长，而特征选择虽省内存却会丢弃证据。缺乏一种推理原语既能处理所有候选证据又能保持局部特征交互有界。

Method: SCFF利用骨干网络暴露的"行表示接口"，将支持标签用ANOVA+BH路由到Core（高证据）与Tail（剩余证据），每层最多128列折叠；同一特征编码器处理各leaf，对齐的行表示在合并后送入单一上下文编码器与标签头，得到一次完整预测。Tail残差须经互惠支持子集接受测试，失败则α=0退回精确Core表示；多类任务仅用Bounded Core。LimiX等保留token轴的架构需专用token-preserving适配器。

Result: 在18个宽表基准（128–7,200原始特征，来自AMLB-29、TabZilla、TabArena）上五个折叠的锁存评估中，SCFF在六个冻结骨干上均提升数据集宏平均精度和NLL：TabPFN-3 +1.874pp、TabICLv1 +5.419pp（相对误差降26.1%）、TabICLv2 +1.564pp、TabPFNv1 +3.071pp、LimiX-2M +0.700pp、RefineICL-24L +3.730pp；四个匹配宽度骨干的95%自举区间均不跨零。中位数GPU内存节省2.09–2.36倍，最大峰值比34.3×。在固定峰值内存上限下，SCFF在TabICLv2（K>128子集10数据集）和TabPFN-3（K>128子集9数据集）的预先声明宽Core层上比最宽可行单leaf分别多保留244 vs 96列和类似保留量，精度提升+4.06与+3.72pp。分解实验显示Core选择贡献大部分增益，Bounded折叠贡献小但稳定增量；Tail On在五个兼容骨干中三个上双指标改善。组件消融证实支持路由、Core投影、Fisher+互惠支持子集接受检验均为关键设计。

Conclusion: 特征宽度无需决定表格基础模型的工作集。SCFF以训练免费的方式在推理时将宽表编译为有界的原生特征编码器调用加一次上下文预测；在六个冻结骨干上同时改善精度与NLL并在四个匹配宽度比较中降低峰值内存；省下的内存预算可重新投入保留更多支持证据，在TabICLv2与TabPFN-3的宽Core层上进一步提升精度。方法不需要新增参数、不集成预测、与特征选择和骨干微调正交。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models face a feature-side scaling dilemma: full-width pairwise mixing grows quadratically with the number of columns, whereas feature selection saves memory by discarding evidence. We introduce Support-Compiled Feature Folding (SCFF), a training-free inference framework that resolves this dilemma without changing the frozen backbone. SCFF routes support-ranked features through bounded leaves of the native feature encoder, support-checks the residual evidence, and merges the encoded messages before a single contextual prediction. It thereby converts quadratic feature-interaction work into linear-in-width work with a bounded local working set, without ensembling predictions or training new parameters. On the exhaustive 18-dataset wide-table slice of fixed AMLB-29, TabZilla, and TabArena snapshots, SCFF improves dataset-macro accuracy and NLL on all six evaluated backbones. All four matched-width comparisons retain favorable 95 percent dataset-bootstrap intervals on locked folds, with relative error reductions up to 26.1 percent. Median paired GPU-memory savings are 2.09x to 2.36x, and the ratio of separately observed maximum peaks reaches 34.3x. Under a measured peak-memory ceiling, SCFF uses the saved budget to preserve more support-selected evidence, improving accuracy by 4.06 and 3.72 points over the widest feasible single leaf on predeclared wide-Core strata of TabICLv2 and TabPFN-3.

</details>


### [184] [Log-Depth Recurrent Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28212)
*Yiqin Wang,Nuri Cingillioglu,Charles Pert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Blelloch并行扫描的对数深度递归语言模型（AR-GRC），将平衡树递归算子扩展到自回归预测，实现了O(log N)深度和O(N)线性时间复杂度的所有前缀表示计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer具有固定计算深度和二次运行时复杂度，而传统循环模型虽为线性深度但无法并行执行。两者均存在架构瓶颈，亟需一种兼具对数深度与并行计算能力的自回归语言建模架构。

Method: 1. 在LDRU上扫（up-sweep）的基础上加入下扫（down-sweep），构成完整的Blelloch并行扫描，复用上扫中间结果以并行计算所有前缀表示。2. 采用可学习的二元组合算子⊙_θ（具体使用门控递归单元GRC），不强制结合律。3. 通过层归一化、线性投影、位置残差MLP和后合并网络构建模型，以交叉熵损失训练下一token预测。

Result: 在PTB、WikiText-2、OpenWebText2三个语料上，AR-GRC的测试困惑度接近ALiBi Transformer（小数据集上差距7.5%-13.1%，OWT2上差距23.5%-33.8%）；展现出与ALiBi Transformer相当的鲁棒长度外推能力（可处理近6倍训练长度的序列），而正弦Transformer在超出训练长度后困惑度急剧上升；评估时间随序列长度线性增长，在WikiText-2最长上下文上比Transformer快约5倍。

Conclusion: AR-GRC证明了平衡树递归算子可通过工作高效的并行过程训练为实用的自回归下一token预测器，是对数深度递归模型类用于语言建模的可行替代方案；但固定树结构和信息压缩路径可能是其性能瓶颈，需要更富表达力的二元算子、规模化实验和选择性上下文保留机制来缩小与Transformer的差距。

Abstract: Language modeling using Transformers has become commonplace despite their fixed computational depth and quadratic runtime with respect to input tokens. Recurrent models on the other hand offer linear depth but no parallel execution. In this work, we extend balanced-tree recursive operators from sequence encoding to autoregressive prediction, enabling all prefix representations to be computed with logarithmic depth and linear runtime. Our experiments provide an initial characterization of this model class, demonstrating robust length extrapolation and performance approaching that of ALiBi-based Transformers, highlighting its potential as an alternative architecture for language modeling.

</details>


### [185] [hyperbolix: Hyperbolic Deep Learning in JAX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28248)
*Timo Klein,Thomas Lang,Yllka Velaj,Sebastian Tschiatschek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: hyperbolix是首个基于JAX/Flax NNX的综合性双曲深度学习开源库，涵盖六种流形、76种神经网络层族、黎曼优化器及降维方法，核心贡献是采用无相消公式提高了Lorentz模型上的数值精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有JAX生态中的双曲深度学习库（如Rieoptax、RiemannAX、GeoJAX）仅实现黎曼优化器而缺乏通用双曲神经网络层，使用者必须从零实现或手动移植，过程中常遇到数值稳定性问题。论文旨在填补JAX生态中双曲深度学习的空白。

Method: 构建hyperbolix库，统一接口覆盖六种流形（Poincaré球、双曲面、proper velocity空间、κ-立体模型、欧氏空间、混合曲率乘积空间）；实现76种nn_layers导出（线性、卷积、注意力、归一化、位置编码、回归、向量量化）；设计遵循JAX函数式范式（无状态流形、单点操作、调用时传入曲率）；采用无相消的双曲面算术（将减法重写为非负项之和，类似haversine恒等式）；测试套件（1038个测试函数）对照独立oracle（手推导闭式、NumPy/SciPy转录、有限差分）进行验证，覆盖float32和float64。

Result: （1）hyperbolix为Poincaré球上提供匹配或超过geoopt和HypLL的往返精度；（2）在双曲面上float32距离误差保持在输入舍入误差的8×以内直至测地半径a≈10，而geoopt在a≈9返回NaN；（3）跨库精度测试显示Poincaré球上float32三库可比、float64中HypLL因曲率存为float32而下界~10⁻⁸；（4）H100 GPU性能：HypLL在Poincaré球上距离/exp₀/log₀最快，hyperbolix在双曲面exp₀/log₀/ptransp快1.9-2.9×于geoopt；（6）CPU单核控制显示hyperbolix jit在9项primitive中4项最快。

Conclusion: hyperbolix是JAX生态首个通用双曲深度学习库，提供六种流形、76种层、黎曼优化器、包裹分布和降维方法；通过无相消双曲面算术在float32下将有效测地半径扩展到a≈10；采用独立oracle测试策略（1038测试函数，4446个测试项）保证正确性；MIT许可证开源。

Abstract: We present hyperbolix, an open-source library for hyperbolic deep learning in JAX, built on Flax NNX. To our knowledge, it is the first comprehensive, general-purpose hyperbolic deep learning library in JAX. It includes six manifolds with a common interface: Euclidean space, the Poincaré ball, the hyperboloid, the $\kappa$-stereographic model, mixed-curvature product spaces, and the proper velocity space. We implement layer families that cover linear layers, convolutions, attention, normalization, positional encoding, regression, and vector quantization. These building blocks span methods ranging from Ganea's original hyperbolic neural networks to recent fully hyperbolic architectures such as Hypformer and Lorentzian ResNet. Additionally, hyperbolix contains Riemannian optimizers implemented as optax transformations, wrapped distributions, and hyperbolic dimensionality-reduction techniques. Its API uses idiomatic JAX: Manifolds are stateless, with curvature being passed at call time, while manifold operations act on single points, with this http URL enabling batch operations. The precision of every checked operation is tested against a closed-form NumPy/SciPy transcription from the source paper or a finite difference, for both float32 and float64. On the hyperboloid, standard formulas for two-point operations, such as the distance, lose precision far from the origin, because they subtract two large, nearly equal terms. hyperbolix replaces these subtractions with cancellation-free formulas that stay accurate in float32 at distances where prior implementations return NaN. hyperbolix is available under the MIT license at this https URL .

</details>


### [186] [Resource-Adaptive Stochastic Gradient Descent for Online Linear Programming without Re-solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28263)
*Jiameng Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了资源自适应随机梯度下降（RASGD）算法，用于在线线性规划（OLP）。该算法通过每个到达的请求和当前库存更新资源价格，无需LP重求解即可实现O(log T)的预期遗憾，匹配已知分布策略的下界，并在大规模LLM推理和搜索服务等场景中保持计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM推理和搜索服务规模扩大，在线线性规划（OLP）问题规模急剧增长，传统的每周期LP重求解方法计算代价过高，亟需计算高效的算法来在大规模场景中实现高质量资源分配。

Method: RASGD将当前资源定价逻辑表达为一阶SGD更新：每次到达刷新对偶目标中的剩余库存配额，步长在早期递减以支持学习，后期递增以匹配库存调整速度。算法采用两段式步长策略κ/max{s₀, min(t, T-t)}，无需LP求解或样本平均优化，每到达仅需O(m)操作和内存。

Result: 在标准非退化条件下，RASGD在每条样本路径上保持可行性，并达到O(log T)的预期遗憾，匹配Bray(2025)的多秘书下界，即使对已知分布策略也成立。数值实验显示，RASGD的遗憾与每到达LP重求解方法竞争，且优于其他测试的一阶方法，运行时间与一阶方法相当，比LP重求解快数个数量级。消融实验验证了资源反馈和后期步长递增两个组件的贡献。

Conclusion: RASGD建立了在标准非退化条件下无需LP重求解即可获得最优对数遗憾的计算高效方法，通过联合设计资源反馈和步长来匹配库存动态，为大规模AI服务中的OLP问题提供了实用解决方案。未来可扩展到随机补给、时变需求以及AI服务中的联合准入和调度问题。

Abstract: The growth of large language model (LLM) inference and search services increases the scale of online linear programming problems, motivating computationally efficient algorithms. We develop resource-adaptive stochastic gradient descent (RASGD) for stochastic online linear programming. The algorithm uses one request and current inventory to update resource prices, requiring O(m) operations for m resources and memory per arrival and no LP or sample-average optimization. The central idea is to express the current-resource pricing logic of re-solving through a first-order SGD update: each arrival refreshes the remaining-inventory allowance in the dual objective, while the stepsize decreases for early learning and increases later to match the speed of inventory adjustment. Under standard non-degeneracy conditions, our algorithm is feasible on every sample path and achieves O(\log T) expected regret against the realized fractional hindsight optimum, which matches the lower bound, even for policies that know the distribution and have unrestricted computation. The analysis converts curvature around the fixed reference price into inventory stability without tracking optimal prices at changing resource levels. Numerical experiments show that RASGD achieves regret competitive with per-arrival LP re-solving and improves upon the tested first-order baselines, while retaining the computational efficiency of first-order methods. These results establish RASGD as a computationally efficient approach to achieving high allocation quality in large-scale OLP.

</details>


### [187] [Memory Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28399)
*Jiale Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Memory Attention (MA)，用层特定的 token 记忆表与上下文键相加来取代标准的注意变换模块中的值向量投影，从而在保持标准注意力加权与聚合规则的同时，以查表与加法代替密集矩阵乘法实现值构造，并可通过 CPU 卸载（MA-Offload）减少 GPU 参数驻留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准自注意力的值向量均通过基于当前隐状态的密集线性投影产生，未显式利用可跨上下文复用的 token 级信息。作者希望验证：能否用按 token 索引的显式记忆取代价值投影，同时由已有的上下文键补足动态信息。

Method: MA 在每一层维护一张可学习的 token 记忆表 E ∈ ℝ^{N×d_v}，将查表并经 RMSNorm 归一化后的记忆 M 与同一序列的键值投影键 K 相加构造值向量 V = K + M。RoPE 仅作用于注意力打分所用的 Q、K，值向量由 RoPE 前的键与归一化后的键值头记忆相加得到；推理时可将归一化折入记忆表，使在线值构造简化为查表与逐元素加法。记忆表寻址仅依赖 token ID 与层索引，因此可进行预取与 CPU 卸载（MA-Offload）；MA-Recall 利用增量重建的价值机制，用保留的键与历史 token ID 在需要时重建历史值，从而以重复检索换取持久值缓存的缩减。

Result: 在 MHA、GQA、MQA、Gated 等骨干以及 24 层-1024 维（10B token）与 24 层-2048 维（20B token）规模上，MA 在匹配训练 token 预算下均同时降低 LAMBADA 与 WikiText 困惑度，并提升七个下游基准的平均准确率，例如较小 MHA 模型 WikiText PPL 由 31.55 降至 28.64；检索任务在训练窗口内及 2× 长度外推下均优于基线；推理测量中 MA-Offload 以 BF16 精度将约 1.57 亿个记忆表参数（3000 MiB）置于 CPU，使 GPU 参数存储较纯 MA 降低约 55.45%，并在参 workload 下使预填与解码延迟略低于标准基线（分别降低 0.31% 与 2.53%）。同时报告 MA 的 token 效率在两个尺度上分别为 1.42× 与 1.16×。

Conclusion: MA 通过将值向量构造替换为上下文键与层特定 token 记忆的相加，可在不改变注意力加权与聚合规则的前提下提升语言建模与平均下游表现，并通过查表式机制支持折叠归一化、CPU 卸载与按需重建；其实用性来自将存储与计算权衡以查表加加法的方式外移至记忆表，但质量增益与新增参数容量尚未解耦，且需要在更大模型、更长上下文与线性注意 / MoE 等骨干上进一步评估。

Abstract: Language models typically construct attention values from contextual hidden states, even when some of their content may be reusable across contexts. We investigate whether token-indexed memory can replace the dedicated value projection when complemented by contextual information. We propose Memory Attention (MA), which forms values by combining layer-specific token memory with contextual keys. The memory supplies token-specific representations, while the keys preserve context dependence. At inference, normalization can be folded into the memory tables, reducing value construction to lookup and addition. Token-indexed retrieval also enables CPU offloading with prefetching, reducing GPU parameter storage. Under matched training token budgets and with additional memory parameters, experiments across attention configurations show improved language modeling and average downstream performance.

</details>


### [188] [Learning Collective Dynamics with Differentiable Gaussian Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28405)
*Jianxiang Ma,Mingfu Zhang,Xiaocui Yang,Yichen Gao,Junzhao Huang,Yuesong Hou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出可微分高斯动力学（DGD），一种通过聚合计数联合学习潜在响应分布、观测函数和反馈动力学的集体响应预测模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集体响应（如社交媒体互动和零售交易）源于个体差异、接触机会和累积经验的综合作用。从聚合事件计数中学习这些异质响应及其反馈过程面临三个耦合挑战：响应异质性、接触依赖观测和经验依赖动力学。现有方法难以在统一框架中连接种群状态与当前观测和未来行为。

Method: 方法包含三个核心组件：(1) 高斯响应表示——用K分量高斯混合表示潜在响应倾向分布（类型比例、均值、协方差），通过重参数化积分节点实现可微；(2) 可微接触-行为聚合——在相同潜在倾向处同时评估接触强度和行为概率，乘积积分产生预测到达数和行为总数；(3) 反馈递归——利用观测或预测结果更新记忆、疲劳、响应偏移和到达环境。使用观测反馈分支和期望反馈分支联合训练种群参数、观测函数和反馈参数。

Result: 在KuaiRand-Pure（标准推荐和随机曝光）和Online Retail II（2010和2011年度窗口）四个数据窗口上，DGD在联合行为负对数似然上均优于DeepAR-joint改进版及GRU基线。Retail 2010的单日行为计数MAE为4.71，对比DeepAR-joint的6.88。学习分布使标准推荐NLL相对固定高斯降低10.82%。反馈递归使随机曝光单日MAE从34.08降至16.47，控制实验中将联合KL从0.2577降至0.0340。

Conclusion: 结果表明，从聚合观测中学习种群表示与反馈过程具有显著价值。DGD建立了从总体计数到异质响应和动力学的可行学习路径，为集体行为预测提供了新框架。

Abstract: Collective responses depend on individual differences, contact opportunities, and accumulated experience. Learning their dynamics from aggregate counts requires connecting a population's response distribution to both current observations and future behavior. We introduce Differentiable Gaussian Dynamics (DGD), which learns this connection through three components: a Gaussian mixture representing heterogeneous response propensities, differentiable aggregation of contact intensity and behavioral probabilities, and feedback recurrence that updates subsequent responses. Reparameterized integration and temporal recurrence let aggregate prediction errors jointly train the distribution, observation functions, and feedback parameters. On four windows from KuaiRand-Pure and Online Retail II, DGD achieves lower joint behavioral negative log-likelihood than a DeepAR adaptation with a joint-behavior head. In Retail 2010, its one-day behavioral-count MAE is 4.71 versus 6.88 for this adaptation. Learning the distribution reduces behavioral negative log-likelihood by 10.82% relative to a fixed Gaussian in KuaiRand's standard-recommendation window; removing feedback dynamics raises joint KL from 0.0340 to 0.2577 in a controlled experiment. These results establish the value of learning population representations and their feedback process from aggregate observations. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [189] [Context-Continuous Preference Learning for Exoskeleton Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28427)
*Sunin Baek,Sungwoo Park,Daekyum Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出上下文连续偏好学习（CCPL），通过高斯过程偏好模型在相邻操作条件下共享用户偏好观测，以减少外骨骼个性化所需的用户反馈量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外骨骼辅助在不同操作条件（如步行速度、负载）下需要个性化，但用户在每种条件下提供偏好反馈耗时耗力。现有方法要么对每种条件独立学习（数据浪费），要么将所有条件数据合并（忽略条件差异），缺少一种既能跨条件共享信息又保留条件特异性的方法。核心问题是：用户的偏好景观是否随操作条件平滑变化，以及这种连续性何时能支持有限反馈下的学习。

Method: 提出CCPL框架，将用户的潜在效用函数定义为动作参数与操作上下文的联合函数，使用高斯过程偏好模型，通过协方差函数中动作和上下文维度的分离长度尺度实现跨相邻条件的共享，同时保留上下文特定的效用估计。模型采用probit似然（含平局阈值），结合排序评估和直接成对比较两种反馈类型。对比策略包括：(1)独立学习——每个上下文单独建模；(2)合并学习——忽略上下文。通过仿真（控制上下文连续性γ参数）和人体回顾性分析（踝关节外骨骼：3种步行速度；肘关节外骨骼：5种负载×疲劳诱导条件组合）进行评估。

Result: 仿真中，γ=1（强连续性）时CCPL在重建和贝叶斯优化上均优于独立学习；γ=0/0.25（弱连续性）时出现负迁移。人体实验中，全数据参考景观在相邻条件间更相似（踝部斜率-0.424，肘部-0.325）。每条件5次暴露时，CCPL将重建相关性从踝部0.644提升至0.720，肘部从0.476提升至0.526，比独立学习分别节省约37%和17%的反馈预算。CCPL在所有评估预算下均优于独立学习的保留预测（NLL）。相比合并学习的优势因研究而异。源观测重用进一步提升性能，尤其在目标反馈较少时。

Conclusion: 研究支持上下文连续性作为有限反馈下共享用户偏好观测的基础。CCPL在偏好平滑变化时有效改善重建和预测，但在连续性弱时存在负迁移风险。实际效益取决于跨条件偏好变化的程度。该方法尚未在在线个性化中验证，未来需进行前瞻性人体研究。

Abstract: Personalizing exoskeleton assistance across operating conditions is constrained by the time and physical effort required to collect user feedback. We examined whether a user's preference landscape varies smoothly across operating conditions and when this continuity supports learning from limited feedback. We propose Context-Continuous Preference Learning (CCPL), a Gaussian-process preference model that shares observations across nearby contexts while retaining context-specific utility estimates. We evaluated CCPL through simulations and retrospective analyses of ankle and elbow exoskeleton preference data from nine healthy adults. In simulations, CCPL improved reconstruction and preference-based Bayesian optimization relative to independent learning when preferences varied smoothly, but showed negative transfer when continuity was weak. In both human studies, full-data reference landscapes estimated separately for each participant and context tended to be more similar between nearby operating conditions. With five exposures per context, CCPL increased mean reconstruction correlation with these references from 0.644 to 0.720 for ankle assistance and from 0.476 to 0.526 for elbow assistance relative to independent learning. The five-exposure budget was approximately 37% lower for ankle and 17% lower for elbow than the estimated independent-learning budgets needed to match these correlations. CCPL also improved held-out response prediction relative to independent learning, while benefits over pooled learning varied. These findings support context continuity as a basis for sharing preference observations under limited feedback, although benefits for online personalization in humans remain to be established.

</details>


### [190] [Minimal-Norm Univariate Two-Layer ReLU Classification: Exact Solutions and Global Optimality with Skip Connections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28438)
*Karolina Drabik,Ben Lewis,Antoni Puch,Etienne Boursier,Piotr Hofman,Matthias Englert,Ranko Lazić*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了一维二元分类中两层ReLU网络的最小范数插值与ℓ₂正则化逻辑损失最小化问题，给出了最优分类器在函数空间的完整几何刻画，揭示了隐藏层偏置是否被惩罚以及跳跃连接(skip connection)对解的结构与优化景观的根本影响：当偏置不被惩罚时，最小范数插值器是所有"开关拥抱且凸性正确"的分段仿射函数；当偏置被惩罚时，解在函数空间唯一且每个中间段恰有一个拐点（最稀疏）。自由仿射跳跃连接不改变函数空间最优解，但使每个KKT点都成为全局最优，从而消除次优平稳点。文章还证明了正则化损失的极小化器具有额外的"区间转向"稀疏性质，意味着大多数最小范数插值器无法作为小正则化极限出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ℓ₂范数最小化是理解现代神经网络泛化与隐式正则化的核心，但此前对于一维两层ReLU分类问题，最小范数插值器的完整函数空间刻画（特别是关于偏置惩罚与跳跃连接的影响）仍为开放问题。此外，跳跃连接在实践中广泛使用却缺乏精确的有限网络优化景观理论，约束优化与正则化损失之间的关系也未被充分理解。

Method: 综合运用函数空间表示理论、Clarke次微分与KKT最优性条件、MFCQ约束品性，以及精心构造的"下降方向"论证（结合神经元反向重参数化、平衡性论证、 Cauchy-Schwarz不等式等技巧），在四种架构变体（偏置惩罚/不惩罚 × 跳跃连接存在/不存在）下证明了精确的全局刻画与KKT点全局最优性。约束优化与正则化优化之间的联系通过近似Clarke平稳点的裕度归一化极限分析建立。数值实验通过Adam训练多种宽度网络验证理论。

Result: 定理1刻画了四种插值器范数最小化问题的解：偏置不惩罚时解为开关拥抱+凸性正确的CPA（无穷多个）；偏置惩罚时解唯一且单转向（恰r−1个拐点）。跳跃连接使每个KKT点全局最优（无跳跃连接时存在严格次优KKT平台/孤立点反例）。定理6将结果推广到ℓ₂正则化逻辑损失，揭示额外的"区间转向"性质。定理7证明裕度归一化极限为KKT点，且无跳跃连接时不惩罚偏置情形下这些极限必为区间转向，意味着大多数KKT点不可达。数值实验显示跳跃连接一致地使训练达到或接近全局最小值。

Conclusion: 本文为一维两层ReLU分类提供了完整的函数空间最优分类器刻画，揭示了偏置惩罚的稀疏效应与跳跃连接的"优化景观净化"效应（不改变最优函数但消除次优平稳点）。这些精确的有限维结果为理解ℓ₂正则化、隐式偏差与跳跃连接的作用提供了可微分析的基石。未来方向包括：无跳跃连接情形下KKT/Clarke平稳点的完整刻画、推广到多维输入与更深网络，以及梯度动力学对平稳点选择性的实验研究。

Abstract: We study minimal-norm interpolation and $\ell_2$-regularized logistic-loss minimization for binary classification by univariate two-layer ReLU networks. We give complete geometric characterizations of the optimal classifiers in function space, resolving how the solutions depend on whether hidden-layer biases are included in the parameter norm. When biases are unpenalized, the minimal-norm interpolators are exactly the continuous piecewise-affine functions that hug every label switch and have kinks of the appropriate convexity. When biases are penalized, the minimizer is unique in function space, has exactly one kink in each intermediate same-label segment, and is therefore a sparsest positive-margin classifier. We further show that adding a free affine skip connection leaves these function-space solutions unchanged but fundamentally improves the parameter-space landscape: every KKT point of the constrained problem becomes globally optimal, whereas suboptimal KKT points can occur without the skip connection. We establish analogous global-optimality and geometric results for sufficiently weak $\ell_2$-regularization of the logistic loss. In the unpenalized-bias case, we identify an additional sparsity-like restriction, implying that most minimal-norm interpolators cannot arise as small-regularization limits of margin-normalized logistic-loss minimizers. Numerical experiments across varying dataset complexity and network width support the predicted landscape and sparsity phenomena.

</details>


### [191] [Even Sharper Bounds for Transductive Learning and Its Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28459)
*Yingzhen Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了"锐化转导局部复杂度"(STLC)，一种在无放回均匀采样下针对转导学习的新局部复杂度方法。通过Bernstein型浓度不等式（基于交换游走的修正对数Sobolev不等式和双参数熵闭包）以及带代理局部化泛函的剥离论证，给出了与经典归纳局部Rademacher复杂度界具有相同结构和置信项的过剩风险界，但消除了先前转导结果中额外的对数置信因子。在可实现二元分类中达到标准的归纳率，并在m≥9时与转导极小极大下界仅差一个对数因子；在转导核学习中给出无内积不平衡因子的谱自适应界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有转导学习的局部复杂度方法存在两个主要缺陷：(1) 早期结果[6]要求训练集和测试集大小满足严格的不平衡条件 u≫m² 或 m≫u²；(2) 后续改进[7]虽然去除了不平衡条件，但在置信项中引入了额外的对数因子 log₂(4N_{u,m}/δ)，当δ较小时影响很大。本文旨在统一解决这两个问题：既不需要不平衡条件，又去除多余的置信因子。

Method: 核心技术贡献包括：(1) 基于Johnson图上交换游走的修正对数Sobolev不等式，推导出测试-训练经验过程上确界的Bernstein型浓度不等式（定理IV.1）；(2) 引入双参数熵闭包技术处理耦合的方差项；(3) 通过带"替换"调整比例因子的剥离论证和代理局部化泛函 ñ_n，将局部Rademacher复杂度理论扩展到转导设置；(4) 通过交换游走的几何特性（引理C.3的有向交换方差界）将受限界的第二矩项转化为可处理的局部化形式。

Result: 主要结果：(1) 一般STLC过剩风险界（定理IV.4）：对于x>0，以至少1-3exp(-x)的概率，ℰ(f̂_m) ≤ c₁r_{u,m} + (4Bc_Δr*)/K₀ + c₃x/N_{u,m}；(2) 可实现二元类界（定理V.1）：当u≥m≥d^(VC)时，ℒ_u(ℓ_{f̂_m}) ≤ c''₀·d^(VC)log(me/d^(VC))/m + c'₁x/m，与标准归纳率匹配，在m≥9时与极小极大下界(d^(VC)-1)/(16m)仅差一个对数因子；(3) 转导核学习界（定理V.2）：给出了无n/u和n/m不平衡因子的谱自适应界 ℰ(f̂_m) ≤ c₅(min_Q r(u,m,Q) + x/N_{u,m})，其中r(u,m,Q)涉及核矩阵经验特征值。

Conclusion: STLC方法成功统一了转导学习的局部复杂度分析，移除了先前结果中的不平衡条件和额外的对数置信因子。在可实现VC分类中达到近极小极大最优的收敛速率，在转导核学习中给出更精细的谱自适应界。未来研究方向包括将该分析扩展到无界函数类。

Abstract: We introduce Sharper Transductive Local Complexity (STLC), a localized complexity method for transductive learning under uniform sampling without replacement. The construction starts from a Bernstein-type concentration inequality for the supremum of the test--train empirical process. Its proof uses the modified log-Sobolev inequality for the swap walk and a two-parameter entropy closure. A peeling argument with a surrogate localization functional then gives excess-risk bounds with the same fixed-point and confidence terms as the classical inductive local Rademacher-complexity bounds, without the additional logarithmic confidence factor in earlier transductive results. For realizable learning over a binary class of VC dimension $\dVC$, with training size $m$, test size $u$, and $u\ge m\ge\dVC$, STLC yields $\cO\{\dVC\log(me/\dVC)/m\}$. This matches the standard inductive rate and, when $m\ge9$, is within a logarithmic factor of the transductive minimax lower bound of order $\dVC/m$. For transductive kernel learning, STLC gives a spectrum-adaptive excess-risk bound without the multiplicative imbalance factors appearing in the earlier local-complexity bound.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [192] [Compliant with Local Controls, Collectively Discriminatory. A Governance Architecture for Multi-Agent AI in Regulated Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27994)
*Jose Manuel de la Chica Rodriguez,Juan Manuel Vera Diaz,Pablo Delgado Romero*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该论文提出ARIA（Agent-Regulated Institutional Architecture）参考架构，旨在解决金融领域多智能体AI系统中"局部合规但集体歧视"的治理盲区问题，核心是弥补组件级治理无法推导出集体合规性的结构性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前金融机构部署智能体工作流（信贷、反欺诈、催收、合规等），但治理仍以模型/组件为中心。当机构层面的风险源于多个局部合规组件的联合行为时（如共享信号、测量质量差异、耦合反馈），局部公平性检查无法组合（compose）为可接受的集体结果（如有界差异影响、市场完整性、可追溯问责），产生"宪法非组合性"（constitutional non-compositionality）缺口。监管层面（ECOA、EU AI Act、SR 26-2、FCA Consumer Duty、MAS FEAT等）关注的是机构整体行为模式，但现有模型中心工具（Constitutional AI、审慎对齐、过程监督等）仅作用于单个模型。

Method: 方法上提出六项治理能力，分布于三个平面：（1）规范与问责平面：C1政策规范与司法管辖映射（联邦化宪法AI，分解为政策本体、司法管辖控制映射、宪法一致性分析）、C6人类监督能力维护（基线、校准、重新认证）；（2）执行控制平面：C3有界决策权威（BDA，区分capability与authority，5级自治分类）、C4运行时执行与遏制（多维agent风险剖面、PDP/PEP、渐进遏制）；（3）保障与学习平面：C2群体保障与M₂观测-预期行为指标（基于Jensen-Shannon散度的归一化统计量，借鉴Beer可行系统模型）、C5适应性政策学习（红队-事件-沙盒-回滚闭环）。形式上给出最小反例（Proposition 1）证明局部检查通过不蕴含集体合规，并提出可证伪的操作遏制假设（Design Hypothesis 1）和五阶段验证程序。

Result: 两个机制仿真：（E1）共享信号薄文件排斥实验（N=200, T=400, 20种子）：在真实信用worthiness同分布、薄文件组测量噪声σ₁=0.65 vs σ₀=0.30的不完美测量下，每个组件均通过局部均等对待检查（κᵢ=1），但群体批准率差距从0.05增长到0.41（达设定界δ=0.05的8倍）；施加ARIA式群体遏制后差距维持于0.052（界内4%）。（E2）两阶段行为漂移实验（T=300, K=8, 200回合）：在良性类别重组→禁止类别违例的两阶段漂移中，M₂观测-预期分布监测器中位提前65步告警，而禁止结果监测器仅12步（匹配FPR≤5%）。两者均达天花板判别，仅时机差异具信息量。

Conclusion: 论文明确自我定位为参考架构与可证伪研究议程，而非生产有效性声明。贡献三方面：非组合性治理失败的六类分类法（C1-C6及非组合性六型映射）、金融领域三平面六能力架构、与多个监管证据需求对齐的可证伪验证议程。论文明确指出局限性：形式结果仅为反例而非普遍定理；仿真为机制示意而非效应量估计；M₂支持依赖性（可能遗漏保留边际分布的危害）；治理可能沦为仪式。结论是：若M₂在真实场景中不优于更简单漂移基线、若有界权威未能以可接受成本降低违规严重程度、若受治理的政策更新未能改善未来结果，架构应据此修订或收窄。该工作是开放的研究议程而非有效性的宣言。

Abstract: Financial institutions are beginning to deploy agentic workflows in credit, fraud, collections, compliance, and operational control. Governance remains largely component-centric: each model or agent is specified, tested, authorized, and monitored locally. That is insufficient when institutional risk arises from the joint behavior of many locally acceptable components. We call this gap constitutional non-compositionality: local compliance checks need not compose into acceptable collective outcomes such as bounded disparate impact, market integrity, or traceable accountability. We propose ARIA as a finance-specific reference architecture and falsifiable research agenda for agent-population governance. It organizes six capabilities across normative-accountability, execution-control, and assurance-learning planes: policy specification, population-level observed-versus-expected behavior monitoring (M2), bounded authority, runtime containment, adaptive policy change, and preserved human oversight competence. Two simulations illustrate shared-signal thin-file exclusion under local controls and earlier warning from observed-versus-expected distributional monitoring in a constructed drift regime. The contribution maps these controls to fair-lending, EU AI Act, model-risk, and conduct-supervision evidence needs, and closes with a validation agenda rather than a production-effectiveness claim.

</details>


### [193] [SkillApt: Learning When to Activate Agent Skills from Counterfactual Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26863)
*Shuang Guo*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: SkillApt 提出了一种检索后激活控制框架，利用配对的 WITH/WITHOUT 执行证据来决定是否加载已检索到的 Skill，从而在维持 BM25 Top-1 准确率的同时大幅降低激活率和 token 消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 代理系统默认将检索到的 Skill 注入上下文，但语义相关性并不等同于执行效用——某些状态下 Skill 多余、昂贵甚至有害。因此需要将"检索相关"与"是否值得激活"作为两个独立决策来处理。

Method: （1）反事实证据构建：对候选 Skill 在相同任务、模型、解码、环境条件下执行配对的 WITH/WITHOUT 运行，记录效用差值 δ。（2）基于证据的适用性估计：使用签名特征哈希将状态编码为 256 维向量，通过余弦相似度检索历史证据中最近的 5 个样本，加权计算激活分数 p^E。（3）LOAD/ABSTAIN 策略：p > 0.5 则加载，否则放弃；同时维护环境有效性状态 V 作为硬前置条件。（4）证据累积与边界修订：随证据积累，激活边界可扩展、收缩或保持不变。算法无学习参数或阈值优化。

Result: 在 SRA-Bench 冻结确认性评估（111 个完整状态）上，SkillApt-E 与 BM25 Top-1 观察准确率相同（0.838 vs 0.838），但激活率从 100% 降至 31.5%，平均 token 使用减少 74.3%（4,782→1,231）。正确性有害规避率 1.000，成本负效应规避率 0.916。但准确率差异的 95% CI 为 [-6.31%, +6.31%]，统计上不显著。跨模型诊断显示 Skill 效用和激活边界可学习性因模型而异（Qwen 触发多数类停止规则，Mistral 控制器全部弃权，Claude 显示效用与可学习性可分离）。检索瓶颈（Recall@10=0.513）限制了可达收益的上限。

Conclusion: Skill 检索与 Skill 激活应被视为两个独立决策：检索确定哪个 Skill 可能相关，SkillApt 决定在当前状态下是否值得使用。证据支持选择性激活，而非通用控制器或新检索方法。效用和边界可学习性是模型依赖的，能力上限（LiveMath 30.4% 双臂均错）表明激活策略无法修复基础能力缺陷。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly retrieve reusable Skills and inject them into the active context. However, a retrieved Skill can be relevant yet unnecessary, costly, or even harmful in the current execution state. We present SkillApt, a post-retrieval activation framework that decides whether a retrieved Skill should actually be loaded. SkillApt builds execution evidence from matched WITH/WITHOUT runs and uses outcomes from similar historical states to make a LOAD/ABSTAIN decision for each candidate Skill. On the frozen confirmatory SRA-Bench evaluation, SkillApt-E achieved the same observed accuracy as BM25 Top-1 (0.838 vs. 0.838) while reducing the Skill activation rate from 100% to 31.5% and mean token usage by 74.3%. Further diagnostics show that both Skill utility and the learnability of its activation boundary vary across base models. These results suggest that Skill retrieval and Skill activation should be treated as separate decisions: retrieval identifies which Skill may be relevant, while SkillApt determines whether using it is worthwhile in the current state.

</details>


### [194] [Anchor and Perturb: Lazy Agent Remediation by Exploration Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27365)
*Chengxi Zhong,Yongzhe Chang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: AnP是一种轻量级框架，通过将探索性方差注入与循环流形稳定性解耦来解决多智能体协作中的"懒惰智能体"问题。它选择性地对表现不佳的智能体进行非对称探索脉冲注入，同时将已收敛的队友锚定在贪心利用上，从而避免对称探索引发的时序差分惩罚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在CTDE框架下，基于价值分解的方法（如QMIX、WQMIX、QPLEX）在非单调奖励空间中常出现相对过泛化问题：部分智能体陷入次优策略而无法恢复。现有方法要么修改混合网络架构，要么强制所有智能体同时探索，导致已收敛的队友被迫重新探索，引发协作崩溃。论文旨在通过解耦探索与利用，在不修改网络结构的前提下解决懒惰智能体问题。

Method: 1. **锚定（Anchor）**：识别表现不佳的懒惰智能体，利用懒惰差异指数 ℒ_i(t)（综合策略熵衰减、边际贡献不对称、联合回报停滞三个指标）定位干预目标 t_0 与 A_pert；其余智能体 A_anch 被钳制为贪心利用，形成稳定的经验背景。2. **扰动（Perturbation）**：仅对 A_pert 中的智能体施加升高的局部探索率 ε_boost，并在 T_anneal 周期内线性退火至 ε_min；锚定队友保持贪心策略。本文采用最小秩-1 单周期设置（M=1, K=1），保证多智能体碰撞概率为零。3. **基础设施保留**：联合转移动力学与环境奖励由完整联合动作向量驱动，梯度反向传播同时覆盖 A_pert 与 A_anch，网络权重保持动态。

Result: 在3000回合训练中：(1) **修复严重策略退化**：标准QMIX在回合1650-1675暴跌至5%胜率（奖励7.48），在 t_0=1925 触发AnP后（ε_boost≈0.292, T_anneal=450），系统在回合2375即时恢复至85%胜率（奖励18.01），后续维持40-50%平均胜率。(2) **逃离次优平台**：在 t_0=1875 触发AnP（ε_boost≈0.287）后，峰值胜率达90%（奖励18.82），后续78.6%的检查点胜率≥60%，奖励稳定在15.5-17.5区间。验证了探索隔离和解耦信用分配两个核心机制。

Conclusion: AnP通过在数据生成阶段解耦探索方差与名义利用，提供了一种正交于网络架构修改的即插即用补救方案，无需修改QMIX/WQMIX/QPLEX骨干网络即可修复懒惰智能体。论文作为概念验证（proof-of-concept），未来工作包括：多种子统计验证、对称重探索与随机坐标扰动的对照消融实验、自主启发式触发器、有界LLM元监督、高秩协作（M≥2, K>1）及与因果信用分配框架的集成。

Abstract: Anchor and Perturb (AnP) is a lightweight framework that resolves multi-agent coordination failures by decoupling exploratory variance injection from recurrent manifold stability. Existing remediation strategies predominantly alter mixing network architectures or enforce simultaneous exploration across the collective, which inevitably precipitates severe temporal-difference penalties in non-monotonic reward spaces. Specifically, AnP isolates underperforming lazy agents and injects an asymmetric exploratory pulse into targeted coordinates whilst anchoring converged teammates to nominal greedy exploitation. Empirical telemetry benchmarks demonstrate that AnP successfully rescues collapsed joint policies (recovering from a 5% evaluation win rate nadir back to 85%) and facilitates escape from suboptimal coordination plateaus, sustaining peak win rates of 90% without requiring structural network modifications.

</details>


### [195] [KITE: Scaling Jev Population Experiments with Sparse Flagship Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27535)
*Hengyu Li*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: KITE是一个用于大规模人口行为实验的架构，通过将类型化行为内核(Jev)与稀疏的旗舰模型校准相结合，并以共享的人-模型差异传播取代单纯的蒙特卡洛噪声，从而以低成本实现大规模人口实验与不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模行为模拟虽然能扩展执行规模，但不能区分计算精度与对人类行为的真实一致性；KITE旨在通过可审计的稀疏校准与共享差异传播来解决"近似模型是否能保留人类实验支持的决策"这一核心问题。

Method: 四组件架构：① 类型化行为内核Jev（p3绝对选项合约）对每个独特状态预测答案分布；② 稀疏旗舰校准（基于真实与人类同配锚点估计干预效应）通过共享单元均值替换内核效应；③ 表格化执行（事件键控随机数+公共随机数）实现任意规模人口响应；④ 共享差异传播θ=βx+b+u，将框架b_s按研究内群体而u_sa按研究/条件共享，模拟抽样误差不会随人口规模消失。

Result: Epstein（9,070人）：1.7%锚点覆盖使效应MAE降低41%（0.0305→0.0180，绝对误差降0.0125 [0.0064, 0.0167]);SocSci210（37个保留研究）：0.5-1.5%锚点覆盖使捕获决策增益从0.268提升至0.386（增益0.119 [0.014, 0.224]);16国Arechar研究：15个新国家均通过内容保真度门控；共享差异在保留研究上回顾性覆盖率在名义80%/90%下达0.93/0.96，而仅含人类抽样误差时仅0.29/0.36；100万智能体执行20步耗时0.9秒；Jev成本约$0.0227/千次预测。

Conclusion: KITE提供了一种在人类试验前筛查候选方案、多国内容审计、考虑不确定性的政策比较的可行路径，代价仅为几千次内核调用加上稀疏旗舰锚点；属性特定的证据记录使每项应用都可审计，但端点仅在已验证范围内有效，多项预设主要终点未达（D1政策价值增益、标题异质性相关等），存在效应量校准不稳定、记忆与网络未验证、专有模型限制复现等局限。

Abstract: KITE queries a typed behavioral kernel once per unique state, then executes populations of any size from the table with event-keyed randomness and common random numbers. An expensive flagship model is reserved for sparse paired anchors that estimate intervention effects. Measured human-model discrepancy is propagated as shared error into every conclusion. Population-experiment cost thus scales with unique states and anchors, while uncertainty is governed by evidence about people rather than Monte Carlo noise. On Epstein experiments with 9,070 participants, anchors covering 1.7% of states reduced effect error by 41% (absolute MAE reduction 0.0125). On 37 held-out SocSci210 experiments, 0.5-1.5% anchor coverage raised captured decision gain from 0.27 to 0.39. The kernel passed content-fidelity criteria in all 15 new countries of a 16-country study. Shared discrepancy yielded retrospective coverage of 93% and 96% at nominal 80% and 90%, versus 29% and 36% from human sampling uncertainty alone. A million agents executed 20 tabulated steps in 0.9 seconds on a laptop. This architecture offers a route to screening candidate interventions before human trials, multi-country content audits, and uncertainty-aware policy comparison at the cost of a few thousand kernel calls with sparse flagship anchors. Property-specific evidence records connect each use to its validation scope, correction provenance, and uncertainty, making these applications auditable.

</details>


### [196] [Agent-Based Modeling of Systems of Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27573)
*Jean-Baptiste Soyez,Gildas Morvan,Rochdi Merzouki,Daniel Dupont*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于agent的通用形式化方法，用于建模和控制系统之系统（SoS），结合AGR组织模型、IRM4MLS多层级元模型和功能规范，以InTrade项目中的智能自动导引车辆（IAV）港口集装箱物流为例进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的系统之系统（SoS）建模方法多为领域专用，缺少能够同时处理组织结构、功能目标和多层级动态的通用形式化框架，难以应对SoS在动态环境中的演化（如目标变化或子系统能力丧失导致的重组需求）。

Method: 提出基于agent的SoS形式化框架：1）采用Agent-Group-Role（AGR）模型管理组织结构；2）采用IRM4MLS多层级元模型处理多层级和跨层影响/感知关系；3）引入功能规范（目标分解树、任务分配、能力函数）描述全局目标到局部任务的分解；4）定义静态要素（CS定义、组规范、多层级环境）与动态机制（SoS创建/重构算法、能力演化传播、虚拟CS实例化），并形式化证明该框架满足SoS五项基本特征。

Result: 该形式化框架能够表示和管理SoS的静态与动态方面，包括由目标变化或子系统能力丧失引起的重组。通过InTrade项目中港口集装箱物流的IAV车队案例进行实例化（涵盖整体车队、码头车队、IAV排和个体IAV四个层级），并基于MaDKit多agent平台与SCANeRstudio仿真软件联合实现。框架能够处理IAV失效传播、车队重调度以及platoon合并形成虚拟CS等场景。

Conclusion: 所提出的多层级多agent SoS形式化是操作性的、通用且可执行的，适用于管理层次化SoS在动态环境中的控制与诊断。未来工作包括：将模型耦合至其他仿真工具（如Flexsim CT）；利用IRM4MLS的并行层级特性实现诊断与驾驶等独立功能的并行建模；将该方法扩展到非导向型SoS；并通过策略优化组规范选择与CS分配以增强SoS的鲁棒性。

Abstract: This paper deals with the generic modeling of systems of systems (SoSs) using agent-based modeling. SoSs are large-scale systems, including numerous-possibly heterogeneous-interacting component systems evolving in a dynamic environment. The aim of this paper is to provide generic formalism allowing to represent and control the whole complexity of a SoS using agent-based simulations. In particular, organizational aspects of SoSs are managed with the Agent-Group-Role model. Functional aspects, guiding SoSs to accomplish their global goals, are handled via a functional specification. Multilevel aspects are modeled with the Influence Reaction Model for Multilevel Simulation (IRM4MLS) agent-based meta-model. Models generated using this formalism encompass static and dynamic aspects of SoSs. They consider reorganization of SoSs caused by changes of goals or subsystem capacity. All these elements are illustrated in this paper using a SoS case study of Intelligent Autonomous Vehicles initiated by the Intelligent Transportation for Dynamic Environment (InTraDE) European project to automate the port container logistic.

</details>


### [197] [What Confidence Routing Is Actually Doing: Auditing Routing, Calibration, and Commitment in Multi-Agent Deliberation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27822)
*Jingyan Jiang,Huihuo Zheng,Rajeev Thakur,Chih-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: This paper audits a common multi-agent deliberation design where agents report confidence and the highest-scoring agent speaks next. The authors separate three measurement objects that are usually conflated: routing (does it pick the right candidate?), calibration (does confidence behave like a probability?), and commitment (does the selected agent publicly state the answer that won the turn?). Using 4,181 gpt-oss-120b math traces plus a 2×2 cross-actor/cross-benchmark grid, they show that fixed routers barely differ (within 1.1 pp), confidence is badly overconfident but rank-useful (ECE 0.278, fixable to 0.008 via isotonic), and 20.4% of selected agents revise their polled answer at speak time — with net correctness shift depending on setting (−1.7 pp to +12.2 pp).


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Confidence-routed multi-agent protocols use a single scalar both to allocate speaking turns and to estimate uncertainty, but this design confounds routing quality with probability calibration. Recent audits show multi-agent debate often fails to beat strong single-agent baselines under careful accounting, motivating finer-grained evaluation that distinguishes missing candidate content, bad routing, miscalibrated scores, and changes between the private poll and the public commitment.

Method: Trace-level audit of broadcast deliberation traces. Primary dataset: 4,181 olympiad-math traces from openai/gpt-oss-120b (23,391 valid poll candidates, 11,122 selected poll blocks, 9,436 valid labeled poll-speak pairs). Ablation: 2×2 grid crossing gpt-oss-120b with google/gemma-4-31B-it and math with LAB-Bench biology. Three metric families with validity gating and explicit denominators: (1) Routing — CRA, HRWP, MCR, PCCA, CTR with router counterfactuals (argmax, tie-random, random valid, majority vote, confidence-weighted vote, learned logistic, oracle). (2) Calibration — ECE, Brier, AUROC, mean confidence vs. accuracy, with K=2 cross-fitted by problem_uid, tier-stratified isotonic and temperature scaling. (3) Commitment — PSDR, PSIR, PSDRg, NPCS conditional and unconditional, with derivation breakdown (self-revert, incumbent-pull, peer-echo, novel). Cluster-robust inference via problem-cluster bootstrap (421 clusters). LLM judge sensitivity via two Gemma 4 cross-judges. Exploratory outcome-linkage logit over 4,181 traces.

Result: In the primary gpt-oss/math cell: five fixed routers cluster within 1.1 pp (49.0–50.1%) and lag an oracle ceiling of 54.9% by 4.8–5.9 pp. AUROC is 0.721 but mean stated confidence is 0.790 vs. accuracy 0.518 (ECE 0.278); cross-fitted isotonic reduces ECE to 0.008 and Brier from 0.302 to 0.208 while preserving AUROC at 0.720. Poll-speak divergence is 20.4% (PSDR); conditional NPCS on divergent pairs is −8.4 pp and unconditional NPCS is −1.7 pp, with 62.4% of revisions being novel generations (−9.1 pp each). Across the 2×2 grid: ECE isotonic stays ≤0.023 everywhere, but raw AUROC drops to 0.537 and 0.440 in Gemma cells; raw-confidence argmax trails random-valid selection by 5.6 and 11.2 pp in the Gemma cells; unconditional commitment shifts range from −1.7 to +12.2 pp. The exploratory outcome logit shows pool-solvable rate dominates (OR ≈ 121) while mean selected confidence is non-significant and sign-unstable (OR 0.84–1.11 across specifications). Conditional NPCS retains its negative sign under all three judge variants (−8.3 to −6.0 pp).

Conclusion: Routing discrimination, probability calibration, and public commitment must be measured separately before raw confidence is used for deployment decisions. Scale repair (isotonic calibration) cannot create missing routing signal: calibration generalizes across the grid but raw AUROC and routing value do not. Poll-to-speak divergence is substantial everywhere (PSDR 0.11–0.27), but its correctness effect has no fixed sign across settings, so commitment effects should be treated as settings-dependent and tested as interventions rather than inferred from local shifts. The paper contributes a Trace-Audit Card schema (Appendix N) for standardized reporting of these three axes side by side.

Abstract: A common multi-agent design asks agents to report confidence and lets the highest-scoring agent speak next, implicitly using one scalar both to route the conversation and to estimate uncertainty. We audit this confidence-routed broadcast protocol by separating three trace-level questions: whether it selects the right candidate (routing), whether reported confidence behaves like a probability (calibration), and whether the selected agent publicly states the answer that won the turn (commitment). Our primary study covers 4,181 gpt-oss-120b olympiad-math traces; we repeat the audit on a 2-by-2 actor-by-benchmark grid that adds gemma-4-31B-it and a biology multiple-choice benchmark. In the primary cell, confidence discriminates correct from wrong candidates (AUROC 0.72) but is strongly overconfident (79% mean stated confidence versus 52% accuracy). A cross-fitted, tier-stratified isotonic procedure reduces Expected Calibration Error from 0.278 to 0.008 on held-out candidates, but it does not recover missing discrimination: raw AUROC is only 0.537 and 0.440 in the two Gemma cells. Routing is likewise setting-dependent. Fixed routers differ by at most 1.1 percentage points on gpt-oss/math, whereas raw-confidence argmax performs 5.6 and 11.2 points below random-valid selection in the Gemma cells. Commitment is distinct again: in the primary cell, poll and spoken answers diverge in 20.4% of valid pairs, 62.4% of those revisions are fresh generations, and the unconditional correctness shift is -1.7 points; the other three cells instead range from +0.9 to +12.2 points. The transferable lesson is procedural: routing discrimination, probability calibration, and public commitment must be measured separately before raw confidence is used for deployment decisions.

</details>


### [198] [Connectivity Preservation and Graph Stretching in Range-Only Swarm Dispersion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28190)
*Ariel Barel*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 研究匿名、无记忆、仅能测距的智能体在同步有限跳跃下的连通性保持扩散问题。核心规则为每个智能体向随机方向移动其最远可见邻居剩余视距余量的一半，从而在仅依靠标量距离信息的前提下最大程度地扩展群体直径。理论证明该规则保持所有现有视距边及连通性;两智能体情况下证明平方距离的条件正漂移、几乎必然收敛及有限期望近边界命中时间。实验通过百万次蒙特卡洛模拟与Bellman方程求解验证,发现在N=10的群体中,最终可达直径遵循 Complete < Dense < Medium < Sparse < Path 的拓扑依赖排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人在仅能获取标量距离(无方向、无标识、无通信、无记忆、无共享坐标系)的极简感知条件下,如何在保持连通性的同时实现空间扩散,是一个尚未解决的基础理论问题。已有方法通常依赖相对位置、势场或学习策略,所需的感知信息远超纯距离测量。本文旨在确定仅凭当前距离测量可认证的最大各向同性位移,并分析由此带来的图拉伸结构。

Method: (1) 基于三角不等式推导最大的各向同性安全跳跃半径为(V−d)/2,即最远可见邻居剩余视距余量的一半;(2) 证明该半径对所有现有边保持成立且与方向分布无关,从而保持连通性;(3) 对两智能体系统,推导平方距离的精确条件漂移公式,利用Borel-Cantelli引理证明几乎必然收敛到视距边界,并给出近边界命中时间的期望上界;(4) 实验采用百万次蒙特卡洛模拟、五种拓扑类别(N=10,200次/类,共5000轮)、Bellman方程数值求解验证分析结果;(5) 追踪固定生成树的边长变化以量化图拉伸程度。

Result: (1) 安全规则仅需一个标量统计量——最远可见邻居距离,即可认证最大各向同性位移;(2) 对任意群体规模,同步有限跳跃下保持所有当前视距边且因此保持连通性;(3) 边界锁定定理:距离恰为V的边永久冻结两端智能体;(4) 每个吸收构型至少含n/2条边界边;(5) 两智能体:平方距离条件期望漂移为(V−D_t)²/2,几乎必然收敛到V,从D_0=0到达0.97V的期望命中时间约为9.5轮(蒙特卡洛9.5143,Bellman≈9.50);(6) 精确一阶矩:E[D₁]/V=2/π=0.636620,E[D₁²]/V²=1/2=0.500000均被模拟验证;(7) N=10群体最终直径排序:完全图<V(严格受限),稠密≈1.65V,中等≈2.2V,稀疏≈3.6V,路径可达>17V;(8) 路径初始图在N=20时中位直径≈17.22V,N=50时达≈44.23V;(9) 全程五百万轮实验中未观测到任何边或连通性丢失,实验层面复现了确定性安全保证。

Conclusion: 本文在极简距离感知模型下建立了连通性保持多机器人扩散的理论基础:最远邻居规则以单一标量统计量实现最大安全各向同性跳跃,对任意群体规模保持所有边及连通性,并在边界处产生不可逆的锁定锚点。对于分析上可处理的两智能体系统,最大安全随机跳跃产生正向平方距离漂移、几乎必然收敛及有限期望近边界命中时间,蒙特卡洛与Bellman计算给出相互一致的约9.5轮估计。通用群体的实验研究确认了拓扑依赖的直径排序规律。主要开放问题包括:耦合群体极限构型的理论刻画、从匿名距离中安全释放冗余边的机制、以及面向噪声与异步的鲁棒化扩展。

Abstract: We study connectivity-preserving finite-jump dispersion of anonymous, identical, and oblivious agents under an idealized range-only sensing model. Each agent measures only the distances to its visible neighbors, without bearings, identifiers, communication, memory, or a shared coordinate system. We derive the largest isotropic displacement certifiable as safe from these measurements alone. The resulting rule requires only the distance to the farthest visible neighbor: each agent selects a random direction and moves by half of its remaining visibility margin. The rule preserves every existing visibility edge under synchronous finite motion and therefore preserves connectivity. For two agents, we prove positive conditional drift in squared distance, almost-sure convergence to the visibility boundary, and finite expected time to reach any fixed neighborhood of that boundary. A one-million-run Monte Carlo experiment agrees with the exact first-round moments and estimates approximately 9.5 rounds to reach distance 0.97V from coincident initial positions; an independent Bellman-equation computation gives the same estimate. For general swarms, 1,000 runs across five initial-topology classes reproduce the deterministic safety guarantee at implementation level and reveal a consistent topology-dependent ordering of attainable diameter under the tested protocol. These results provide a theoretical foundation for connectivity-preserving multi-robot dispersion under minimal sensing, while isolating the guarantees achievable from anonymous range measurements alone.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [199] [EidosDoc: Implicit Structure Encoding for Cost-Effective Semi-Structured Document QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27784)
*Teng Lin,Yuyu Luo,Nan Tang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: EidosDoc提出了一种用于半结构化文档问答的低成本高准确率系统，通过隐式结构编码、混合检索和动态证据扩展三大创新，在四个公开数据集上超越现有SOTA（MoDora），同时将单次查询成本降低50倍以上、延迟降低4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半结构化文档（如科学报告、财务报表、技术手册）问答面临核心矛盾：现有方法要么依赖LLM进行结构解析和检索，导致高昂成本和延迟；要么将文档扁平化处理，丢失布局和层级信息，牺牲准确率。以MoDora为代表的显式树结构方法虽达SOTA准确率，但需多次LLM调用且树构建脆弱。作者旨在打破准确率-成本-速度的传统权衡。

Method: 三阶段流水线：(1) 离线预处理——通过OCR提取文档原子（document atom），构建空间邻接图Gs，建立BM25稀疏索引、FAISS密集向量索引和6×6布局指纹索引；(2) 在线查询处理——使用BERT-tiny分类问题类型（文本型/混合型/层级感知型/位置感知型），通过混合策略检索候选原子，经轻量级交叉编码器重排序选出Top-3核心证据，再通过空间邻接和父标题嵌入进行动态证据扩展；(3) 答案生成——单次LLM调用基于压缩上下文（500-800 tokens）生成答案。三大核心创新：① 隐式结构编码器（双塔Transformer+对比学习+结构一致性损失+布局近邻损失），将文本、布局、层级关系嵌入密集向量，避免显式树构建；② 无LLM的混合检索（BM25+布局指纹+密集向量+蒸馏交叉编码器）；③ 动态证据扩展机制，基于空间邻接图和父标题嵌入自适应检索相邻证据。

Result: 在MMDA、DUDE、M3DocVQA、MP-DocVQA四个基准上均达到新SOTA：MMDA AIC-Acc达76.21%（超越MoDora 73.33% +2.88），其余三个数据集分别提升+1.42、+1.84、+1.51。所有问题类型均领先，混合型+3.20、位置感知型+5.66尤为显著。检索质量方面，交叉编码器Recall@5达78.4%，超越MoDora的LLM检索71.2%。成本约$0.0005/查询（GPT-4o），相比MoDora降低50倍；延迟约1.1秒，降低4倍。消融实验证实隐式结构编码器贡献最大（-17.38点）。在OCR退化（20%错误率）、长文档（50页）和领域迁移场景下均表现出强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: EidosDoc通过隐式结构编码取代脆弱的显式树构建，通过零LLM检索流水线实现高效率，通过动态证据扩展克服固定路径检索的遗漏问题，在四个基准上同时实现SOTA准确率、50倍成本降低和4倍延迟降低，建立了准确率-成本-速度的新最优权衡，为实际可扩展的半结构化文档分析提供了可行路径。未来工作将扩展至多文档问答、探索结构编码器的自监督预训练，并开发面向边缘设备的更紧凑重排序器。

Abstract: Semi-structured documents are ubiquitous in scientific reports, financial statements, and technical manuals. Question answering over such documents requires simultaneous understanding of text, tables, charts, and complex hierarchical layouts. Existing methods either rely on repeatedly calling large language models for structure parsing and retrieval, leading to high cost and large latency, or they flatten the document and lose layout and hierarchy information, sacrificing answer accuracy. To address this, we propose EidosDoc, a novel system that achieves state-of-the-art accuracy with minimal computational expense. Our approach introduces three core innovations. (1) An Implicit Structure Encoder trained via contrastive learning and a structure consistency loss. This module jointly embeds hierarchical relationships, spatial positions, and textual content into a dense vector space, capturing document structure holistically without the need for manually defined and error-prone constructions. (2) A Hybrid Retrieval Pipeline that leverages BM25, layout fingerprints, and a lightweight cross-encoder to perform high-precision retrieval entirely without invoking an LLM, drastically reducing cost and latency. (3) A Dynamic Evidence Expansion mechanism that adaptively retrieves spatially adjacent and structurally related evidence, overcoming the evidence omission common in fixed-path retrieval methods. We evaluate EidosDoc on four benchmarks, and comprehensive evaluations show that EidosDoc achieves a new state-of-the-art accuracy on the four benchmarks. Crucially, it does so with a 50 times reduction in cost and 4 times lower latency compared to the previous state-of-the-art Method. These results demonstrate that EidosDoc establishes a new optimal trade-off among accuracy, cost, and speed, offering a practical and scalable path for accurate semi-structured document analysis.

</details>


### [200] [Query Implied Generative Engine Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27845)
*Shilpa Ramakrishna,William B. Andreopoulos*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出QI-GEO（Query Implied Generative Engine Optimization），一种无需显式查询的生成式引擎优化框架。该方法直接从文档语义中推断用户意图，通过知识图谱扩展和空白发现来识别文档中可能缺失但相关的信息，从而提升文档在生成式搜索引擎（GSE）中的可见性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的生成式引擎优化（GEO）方法（如GEO、RAID、IF-GEO、C-SEO）大多依赖显式查询或查询派生信号来指导优化。然而，生成式搜索引擎是黑盒系统，内容创作者通常不知道用户可能提出的全部查询，这使得基于查询的优化在实际中难以实施。因此，需要一种不依赖查询输入、直接从文档本身推断用户意图的优化方法。

Method: QI-GEO采用四阶段流水线：(1) 关系挖掘与知识图谱构建——进行指代消解，使用REBEL等关系抽取模型提取主-谓-宾三元组，并将实体链接到知识图谱；(2) 知识扩展与覆盖映射——对链接实体进行一跳邻域扩展，过滤元数据类关系，保留定义性/因果性/解释性关系，并构建覆盖已表达实体-关系对的覆盖图；(3) 空白发现与排序——采用基于收敛性的评分策略，计算候选概念被多个独立文档实体支持的程度（结构性分数），结合关系类型权重和源实体重要性，通过精确覆盖检查和语义覆盖检查筛选真正缺失的概念；(4) 文档重写——将选中的信息空白转换为锚定式编辑规范（锚点、建议、必要性分数），使用Qwen2.5 7B进行局部化编辑，保持原文结构。

Result: 在GEO-Bench和ExtendedGEOBench上的实验结果表明：(1) 引用覆盖率：单查询场景从0.657提升至0.726（+10.5%），多查询场景从0.713提升至0.772（+8.3%），引用增益约为引用损失的2倍；(2) 目标可见性（PAWC）：单查询提升12.6%，多查询提升15.9%；(3) 主观评分：单查询提升17.6%，多查询提升17.1%；(4) 多查询鲁棒性：66.0%的文档在所有5个查询变体上获得正收益，73.7%在至少3/5的查询上获得改善或持平，胜平率（WTR）达0.67，下行风险仅0.0097。

Conclusion: QI-GEO证明了文档语义可以作为用户意图的有效替代表示，无需依赖显式查询即可提升生成式搜索中的内容可见性。该方法在引用覆盖、源可见性和主观印象分数方面均取得一致改进，并在语义相关的查询变体间保持有效性，表明以文档为中心的意图建模可作为基于查询的优化策略的可行替代方案。未来工作方向包括更丰富的知识图谱扩展策略、联合建模多个推断意图、以及利用领域特定知识图谱提升在专业领域的优化效果。

Abstract: The landscape of search has changed drastically with how people look for information online. Traditional search engines are being replaced by Generative Search Engines (GSEs), which use Large Language Models (LLMs) to generate natural language responses to user queries. For content creators, visibility is no longer solely determined by ranking in search results but by being cited within generated responses. But Generative Search Engines are black-boxes, leading to the emergence of Generative Engine Optimization (GEO), a set of techniques aimed at improving content visibility in generative search settings. Most existing approaches rely on the explicit queries or query derived signals to align content to better suit user needs. We propose Query Implied Generative Engine Optimization (QI-GEO) to infers user intent directly from the document. Our approach approximates document's intent space and identifies content that may be missing yet relevant to answer potential user queries. Evaluation on GEO-Bench and Extended GEO-Bench demonstrated improvements across objective and subjective metrics.
