Backstitch, a specialized agentic system, repairs them using the surviving execution as its reference: replay determines whether a suspicious call is request-correlated, source analysis reaches the responsible handoff, a bounded change restores its contract, and the same replay validates the fix. Repairs restore the causal chain without disturbing the work it describes: breaks at 240 of the repaired calls fell from 90.46% to 4.69%, and over 112 days the fleet's break rate more than halved.

</details>


### [229] [The KV Cache Working Set: Online Capacity Planning for LLM Inference Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27746)
*Luchang Li,Shuaishuai Wang,Zhao Ruan,Dongfang Li,Bozhao Gong*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出KVSET，一种在线分析器，通过Mattson栈距离算法高效估算LLM服务负载的KV缓存工作集大小，解决了前缀缓存容量配置难题，避免了逐容量模拟的高开销，实现了准确的缓存命中率预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM服务中前缀缓存对智能体工作负载至关重要，但KV缓存容量配置面临两难：容量过小会显著降低缓存命中率，容量过大则资源浪费且成本高昂。因此需要确定达到目标命中率所需的最小缓存容量（即KV缓存工作集），以实现高效的系统设计与资源调配。

Method: KVSET采用Mattson栈距离算法，对每个KV缓存页面计算其LRU栈距离，并与各候选容量对应的页号比较，从而判断该页面在每个容量下是否命中，无需对每个容量配置独立模拟。这一方法大幅降低了计算和内存开销，支持在线工作集分析。此外，KVSET通过比较实现目标命中率所需前缀页面的最大LRU深度，来确定最小缓存容量。

Result: 通过使用生产LLM工作负载采集的轨迹进行验证，KVSET的估算结果与真实缓存部署中的测量值高度吻合，证明其准确性。同时，KVSET的开源实现支持在线请求处理和离线轨迹重放两种模式。

Conclusion: KVSET为LLM服务中的KV缓存容量规划提供了一种实用且高效的在线分析工具，通过避免冗余模拟显著降低开销，使在线工作集分析变得切实可行，有助于在缓存成本与命中率之间取得最佳平衡。

Abstract: Prefix caching is critical for efficient large language model (LLM) serving, particularly for agentic workloads that repeatedly invoke the model with a growing conversation and tool-use history. By reusing the key-value (KV) states of previously processed prefixes, prefix caching avoids redundant prefill computation. Its effectiveness, however, depends on retaining a sufficiently large set of KV cache states. Provisioning enough cache to preserve all historical KV states is prohibitively expensive and often unnecessary, whereas insufficient capacity can substantially degrade the cache hit rate. Determining the KV cache working set, defined as the minimum cache capacity required to achieve a target hit rate, is therefore essential for efficient cache provisioning and system design. We present KVSET, an online analyzer that estimates the KV cache working set of LLM serving workloads. KVSET uses the Mattson stack algorithm to efficiently estimate cache hit rates across a wide range of cache capacities. For each KV cache page, KVSET computes its LRU stack distance and compares it with the page number of each candidate capacity. This comparison determines whether the page would be a hit at each capacity without independently simulating every capacity configuration. KVSET therefore substantially reduces the computational and memory overhead of conventional capacity-by-capacity simulation and makes online working-set analysis practical. KVSET further determines the minimum cache capacity based on the maximum LRU depth among the prefix pages required to achieve the target hit rate. We validate KVSET using traces collected from production LLM workloads and show that its estimates closely match measurements from real cache deployments. The open-source implementation supports both online request processing and offline trace replay.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [230] [Fast Geometric Spanners via Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26934)
*Alexandr Andoni,Manuel Paez,Krish Singal,Tian Zhang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究在一般度量空间中，通过批近似最近邻搜索（BANN）预言机的黑箱访问，构造亚二次时间的度量spanner问题。主要结果表明：(1) 对于支持c-近似BANN的度量空间，可在Õ(τ_M·kn^{1/k}logΔ)时间内构造一个O(ck)-失真、Õ(kn^{1+1/2k}logΔ)条边的spanner；(2) 任何仅通过o(n^{1+1/k}/k)次成对距离查询的算法必然承受Ω(ck)的失真。这两个结果共同表明，亚二次时间spanner构造与亚二次时间BANN在常数因子损失内是等价的。进一步地，作者将该spanner构造应用于Wasserstein-q距离（包括q=1的EMD）的快速近似计算。结合p>2的ℓ_p空间中现有的高效BANN算法，本文首次给出了这些度量空间上spanner构造和W_q近似的真正亚二次时间算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何spanner是图论与计算几何中的基础工具，在算法设计中具有广泛应用。经典结果表明任意度量空间中存在(2k-1)-失真、O(n^{1+1/k})条边的spanner，但在一般度量空间中，该结果的构造时间是否是亚二次的并不清楚。此前，仅在Hamming空间和低/高维欧氏空间中有亚二次时间的spanner构造算法。对于其他度量空间（如ℓ_p, p>2），是否存在亚二次时间的spanner构造是一个基本未解问题。已知BANN与spanner之间存在单向联系（spanner可高效解决BANN），但反向联系是否成立是本文要解决的核心问题。

Method: 技术核心是将Baswana-Sen的图spanner构造方法推广到一般度量空间，并巧妙利用c-近似BANN预言机替代暴力距离计算。算法在多尺度（2^ℓ）下独立运行Baswana-Sen聚类过程，通过"哈希技巧"（将活跃聚类随机哈希到B个桶中，对每个桶调用一次BANN预言机）来高效构建每个活跃顶点的候选列表L_v。该列表记录距离顶点不超过cr的邻近聚类。关键创新在于：(1) 候选列表在采样前固定，使得附加边已在cr阈值处获得认证，避免了二次近似；(2) 哈希技巧使得每个BANN调用都不浪费，因为每次调用只为尚未记录该聚类的顶点服务。最终，在2k-1次迭代中以O(ck)失真完成。下界证明使用大围长图（围长≥k+2），构造一个特殊边e*权重为1、其余边权重为c的硬实例；通过Yao原理和通用预言机策略，证明仅学习o(n^{1+1/k}/k)对距离的算法必然失真Ω(ck)。W_q应用通过将spanner构造与[Chen et al.]的最小费用流近线性算法相结合实现。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 上界定理：对任何支持c-BANN预言机（运行时间为τ_M(n)）的度量空间M，可在Õ(τ_M(n)·kn^{1/k}logΔ)时间内构造O(ck)-失真、Õ(kn^{1+1/2k}logΔ)条边的spanner，且为O(k)-跳spanner；(2) 下界定理：任何预算为Φ=o(n^{1+1/k}/k)的确定性或随机化算法，在某些度量空间上必承受Ω(ck)的失真；(3) W_q应用：对任意支持高效BANN的度量空间，存在O(ck^{2-1/q})-近似的W_q距离估计算法；(4) ℓ_p(p>2)实例化：结合[Andoni et al. 2024]的BANN结果，首次实现该空间下的真正亚二次时间spanner构造和W_q近似算法。这些结果确立了亚二次时间spanner构造与亚二次时间BANN之间的等价性（至常数因子损失）。

Conclusion: 本文通过证明亚二次时间spanner构造与BANN之间的等价性，显著推进了对一般度量空间中spanner构造复杂度的理解。算法上，核心贡献在于将Baswana-Sen的组合spanner构造与BANN预言机相结合，通过"哈希技巧"实现了对一般度量空间中spanner构造的真正亚二次时间算法。下界结果则表明该上界在ck失真上是紧的。作为应用，本文给出了首个一般度量空间中基于spanner的W_q距离（包括EMD）快速近似算法框架，并在ℓ_p(p>2)空间中获得了首个真正亚二次时间的具体结果。该工作为后续研究提供了重要的技术工具和理论框架，特别是对高维ℓ_p空间中最优传输、几何算法等问题的研究具有重要意义。

Abstract: We study the problem of constructing metric spanners in general metric spaces in subquadratic time when given blackbox access to a fast algorithm for batch approximate nearest neighbor search. In particular, we show the following results for any metric space $\mathsf{M} = ([n], \mathsf{d})$ with aspect ratio $\Delta$ admitting a $c$-approximate batch nearest neighbor search algorithm with runtime $\tau_{\mathsf{M}}(n)$, (1) There exists an algorithm that, for any $k \in \mathbb{N}$, constructs an $O(c k)$-distortion spanner with $\tilde{O}(kn^{1+1/2k} \log \Delta)$ edges and runs in time $\tilde{O}(\tau_{\mathsf{M}} \cdot k n^{1/k} \log \Delta)$. (2) Any algorithm that learns at most $o(n^{1+1/k}/k)$ pairwise distances by querying a distance oracle and a blackbox batch nearest neighbor search oracle necessarily incurs $\Omega(c k)$ distortion. Our results entail that (truly) sub-quadratic time algorithms for spanner construction is equivalent to subquadratic time BANN (up to constant-factor losses). As a further application, we use our fast spanner constructions to obtain a fast algorithm for approximating the Wasserstein distance $\mathsf{W}_q$, for all $q > 1$, over any metric space admitting an efficient batch approximate nearest neighbor search algorithm. Together with recent new efficient algorithms for approximate nearest neighbor search in $\ell_p$ spaces, for $p > 2$, our results entail the first subquadratic time algorithms for spanner construction (with the stated size-distortion tradeoff) and $\mathsf{W}_q$ distance approximation over these metric spaces.

</details>


### [231] [Backtracking Candidate Elimination: A One-Pass Algorithm for the Chip Testing Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26995)
*Shiyi Chen*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于芯片测试问题的单遍顺序算法——回溯候选消除（BCE）。通过维护一个候选芯片和一个保留芯片栈，该算法至多使用n−1次测试和O(n)时间，无需奇偶性分析，并可在线运行。其证明依赖两个不变量（保留芯片同质、被丢弃对坏片占优），并将其与Boyer-Moore多数投票算法以及单向测试模型联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的芯片测试问题（n个芯片中严格多数为良品，坏芯片可对抗性地谎报坏味芯片）的标准教科书解法是分轮配对的"折半递归"，但该方法需要整批输入、奇偶性特殊处理以及按轮归纳分析。作者希望找到一种更简洁、更适合教学的单遍顺序算法，将同样的消除原理应用于逐个到达的芯片。

Method: BCE算法按固定顺序扫描芯片一次，维护一个当前候选芯片p和一个保留芯片栈R。对每个到达的芯片xi：若p不存在则直接令xi成为候选（情形1）；若Test(p,xi)一致则将xi压入栈并保留p（情形2）；若测试不一致则合并丢弃p与xi，并回溯至最近保留的芯片作为新候选（情形3）。正确性基于两个不变量：(i) 保留集同质——所有保留芯片类型相同（引理3）；(ii) 被丢弃集坏片占优——丢弃的芯片中坏片不少于好片（引理4）。进一步在单向测试模型下，将保留芯片栈维护为"单调栈"（好片不在坏片之下），通过让新芯片质疑栈顶芯片、栈底返回好片来工作。

Result: 在好片严格多于坏片的承诺下，BCE算法正确返回一个好片，至多使用n−1次测试和O(n)时间，可在单遍在线模式下运行。算法可提前停止（命题10：当保留集大小不少于剩余未读芯片数时可立即返回）。在最坏情况下保留栈可能占用Θ(n)空间，这是此类物理见证算法的必然代价。单向测试变体同样可在n−1次定向测试内找到好片，依赖栈的LIFO顺序（"链式担保"结构）。作者还将BCE解释为Boyer-Moore多数投票算法Mjrty的物理化：将Mjrty中抽象的计数器替换为一栈物理见证芯片，因为在芯片测试模型下无法复制芯片值，必须保存具体的见证芯片才能在回溯时恢复候选。

Conclusion: BCE是一种简洁、顺序、易教学的芯片测试算法，用两个简短的不变量替代了分轮归纳与奇偶性分析。它揭示了芯片测试问题与多数投票问题之间的深层联系——BCE即是用物理见证栈物化了Boyer-Moore算法的计数器。该算法可扩展至单向测试模型，并通过"链式担保"结构与Wildon的骑士-间谍分析相关联。作者期望这种视角可作为对递归标准解法有益的补充或替代。

Abstract: In the chip testing problem, we are given $n$ chips, strictly more than half of which are good. Chips can test one another in pairs; a good chip always reports the status of the other chip correctly, whereas a bad chip may report arbitrarily and adversarially. The goal is to identify a single chip that is guaranteed to be good. The problem originates in system-level fault diagnosis and is closely related to the "knights and spies" puzzle. The standard textbook solution is a halving recursion that tests disjoint pairs in rounds and keeps one chip from each consistent pair. We present the Backtracking Candidate Elimination (BCE) algorithm, a sequential alternative that scans the chips once while maintaining a current candidate and a stack of retained chips. Every chip is tested at most once as the incoming chip; when a test is inconclusive the candidate and the incoming chip are discarded together, and the algorithm backtracks to the most recently retained chip. BCE uses at most $n-1$ tests and $O(n)$ time, needs no parity case analysis, and works online. Its correctness follows from two invariants: the retained chips all have the same type, and every discarded pair contains at least one bad chip. We explain how BCE can be viewed as the Boyer-Moore majority vote algorithm with its counter replaced by a stack of physical witnesses, and why that replacement is needed. We also give an early termination rule and a variant for the weaker model of one-directional tests.

</details>


### [232] [Minimum Sum Vertex Cover via Minimum Vertex Cover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27117)
*Ahmad Biniaz,Jean-Lou De Carufel,Anil Maheshwari,Saeed Odak,Michiel Smid*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了最小求和顶点覆盖（MSVC）问题，通过顶点覆盖结构获得新的近似算法、精确算法和条件性下界。关键贡献包括：(1) 基于最小顶点覆盖的有界度图近似算法，比值 R_Δ ≤ (√Δ+1)/2；(2) 正则图的多项式时间 1.184-近似算法，改进此前 1.225 的结果；(3) 参数化为顶点覆盖数的 2^{O(k log k)} 精确算法；(4) 平面图等稀疏图类上 2^{O(√n log n)} 的分隔符精确算法；(5) 证明平面图 MSVC 的 NP 完备性及 ETH 下无 2^{o(√n)} 时间算法的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MSVC 是最小求和集合覆盖的图特例，最早由 Feige、Lovász 和 Tetali 提出，具有广泛的实际应用（调度、查询优化、故障诊断等）。此前一般图的最好近似比为 16/9，正则图为 1.225，且树上 MSVC 的多项式可解性长期悬而未决。论文旨在通过顶点覆盖的组合结构突破这些瓶颈，同时建立匹配的条件性下界以证明上界紧致。

Method: 方法论围绕"右度序列块结构"展开，利用 Observation 5（最优排列的右度序列非增）和 Observation 11（同块顶点构成独立集）。具体技术包括：(a) 最小覆盖星序算法——从最小顶点覆盖出发贪心构造星分解并通过平衡不等式导出近似比；(b) 正则图算法——结合 Max-k-Vertex-Cover 的 0.929 近似和结构引理（Lemma 6）约束前缀覆盖边数，并对所有 k 值枚举后用条件期望去随机化；(c) 参数化精确算法——通过"骨架"枚举顶点覆盖顶点到 2k+1 块的分配，再利用独立集顶点的"类型"按邻域分组，需求向量动态规划；(d) 分隔符动态规划——在平衡分隔符上递归求解可行性，通过块大小、残余需求和边界分配的联合状态表实现；(e) 下界证明——通过 Cardinal-Fiorini-Joret 的最小熵定向归约与 Planar Exact Cover 的 ETH 下界衔接。

Result: 主要结果：(1) 有界度图近似比 R_Δ ≤ (√Δ+1)/2，对 Δ≤6 优于 16/9，且给出达到此界的紧致图族（Proposition 4）；(2) 正则图 1.184-近似，改进 Stanković 的 1.225；(3) 参数化算法时间 2^{O(k log k)} + O(n+m)，比此前 2^{O(k log²k)} 改善一个 log k 因子；(4) 平面图、有亏图种类和 H-次可去除图类的 2^{O(√n log n)} 精确算法；(5) 证明平面图 MSVC 的 NP 完备性及 ETH 下 2^{o(√n)} 下界，与上界匹配至对数因子。

Conclusion: 论文系统推进了 MSVC 在近似、精确算法和复杂度下界三方面的研究，确立了一条以"顶点覆盖结构"为核心的统一技术路线。结果几乎在所有维度上接近紧致：参数化算法的运行时间改进了一个对数因子，平面图的上界与 ETH 下界仅差对数项，证明了最小顶点覆盖与 MSVC 之间不仅存在近似联系，也存在精确计算上的内在联系。未来工作包括确定 MSVC 在树上的多项式可解性，以及研究常数树宽图上的精确复杂度。

Abstract: The Minimum Sum Vertex Cover (MSVC) problem asks for an ordering of the vertices of a graph that minimizes the sum, over all edges, of the time at which each edge is first covered. We study the problem through the structure of vertex covers and obtain new approximation and exact algorithms, together with conditional lower bounds. For graphs of maximum degree $\Delta$, we show that a simple ordering algorithm based on a minimum vertex cover achieves approximation ratio $R_\Delta \le {(\sqrt{\Delta}+1)}/{2}$. For $d$-regular graphs, we give a polynomial-time $1.184$-approximation by combining Max-$k$-Vertex-Cover approximation with a structural bound on optimal prefixes. On the exact side, we give an algorithm parameterized by the vertex cover number $k$ running in $2^{O(k\log k)} + O(n+m)$ time, improving the previous dependence on $k$, where $n$ and $m$ are the number of vertices and edges in the graph, respectively. We also develop a separator-based exact algorithm running in $ 2^{O(\sqrt n \log n)}$ time on planar, bounded-genus, and fixed-minor-free graph classes. Finally, we prove that Minimum Sum Vertex Cover is NP-hard on planar graphs and, assuming ETH, admits no $2^{o(\sqrt n)}$-time exact algorithm on $n$-vertex planar graphs. Thus our planar upper bound is tight up to logarithmic factors in the exponent.

</details>


### [233] [Locally Sparsified, Globally Near-Optimal: Matching under Independent Vertex Arrivals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27161)
*Sara Ahmadian,Edith Cohen,Mohammad Roghani*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究随机二分图匹配中的局部稀疏化问题：每个请求（在线顶点）在不知全局信息的情况下独立保留至多k条边的"菜单"，再由中央协调器在保留图上计算最大匹配。在独立顶点到达模型下，证明菜单大小k仅依赖于目标精度ε（而非实例规模）即可保持(1−ε)比例的期望最大匹配值。技术上构造了一个"关键见证+轻边分数补全"的分数匹配证书，利用局部计算算法的in-out复杂度控制依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际资源分配系统（如机器人出租车调度、云调度器）要求每个请求预先选出少量候选选项，再由平台全局协调。但此前Ahmadian等(2026)的结果需要额外的"分散条件(spread condition)"假设，遗留了一个开放问题：在无约束的独立顶点到达模型中，有限大小的局部菜单是否足以实现近最优匹配？本文旨在正面回答该问题。

Method: 1. **问题形式化**：产品到达实例——每个在线位置独立从已知分布抽取状态，确定其邻居。菜单规则为每个状态(i,t)指定一个至多k条边的分布。目标是期望保留图最大匹配值≥(1−ε)倍完整图的期望最大匹配值。  
2. **关键见证(Crucial Witness)**：将状态边按条件基准边际z_itv分为"关键(z≥τ)"、"中间"和"轻(z≤τ⁻)"三类。对关键图施加"封顶(capping)"以控制度数；用加权边权构造局部可计算的匹配M_C，使其几乎保留基准值和端点负载，并通过Brouwer不动点保证端点负载不超过基准的(1+η²)倍。  
3. **轻边补全(Light Completion)**：将基准的轻边计划适配到关键见证留下的剩余容量上。使用Horvitz-Thompson权重补偿菜单抽样，经过可用性归一化后构造分数匹配Ŷ，期望值≥|x⁰|−3ηZ。  
4. **阈值选择**：通过鸽巢原理在Θ(1/η)层中间带中选出一个质量≤ηZ的带，迭代阈值递推产生k的双指数上界k_ε≤exp(exp(O(ε⁻¹ log(1/ε))))。  
5. **下界**：用"枢纽(hub)实例"证明任何菜单大小k必有Ω(1/k)的期望损失，匹配上界阶数(除双指数差距外)。

Result: 主定理(Theorem 3.1)：对每个ε，存在k_ε≤exp(exp(C·ε⁻¹·log(C/ε)))使得每个产品到达实例、每个匹配规则M⋆及每个从M⋆导出的k_ε大小菜单规则，满足E[ν(H)]≥(1−ε)E[|M⋆|]。取M⋆为最大匹配选择器即得E[ν(H)]≥(1−ε)E[ν(G)]。实验显示：枢纽族在k=32时损失<1.3%，k=64时<0.7%，VarOpt采样远优于均匀采样。

Conclusion: 有界局部菜单在独立顶点到达模型下足以保留近最优匹配值，菜单大小仅依赖于精度参数。技术核心在于通过局部计算算法的in-out复杂度界控制依赖性，从而构造可分解为"关键部分"和"轻边分数补全"的全局证书。主要遗留问题：上界的双指数依赖是否可改进至最优的Θ(1/k)（即k=O(1/ε)），以及在未知i.i.d.到达或随机顺序顶点流下能否去除分布知识假设。

Abstract: Resource allocation systems often restrict each request to a short list of options before coordinating assignments globally. We study this separation in stochastic bipartite matching under independent vertex arrivals. Each request draws a state from its own known distribution, determining its compatible resources, and independently retains a menu of at most $k$ edges. A maximum matching is then computed on the retained graph. We show that bounded local menus universally suffice for near-optimal matching. For every $\varepsilon>0$, there is a menu size $k_\varepsilon$ depending only on $\varepsilon$ that preserves at least a $(1-\varepsilon)$ fraction of the expected maximum-matching size of the full realized graph. Earlier guarantees required additional assumptions on how matching mass is distributed across edges; our result resolves the unrestricted case. Moreover, the menus are simple to generate from any benchmark matching rule, either by weighted sampling according to the benchmark's edge marginals, or by applying the benchmark to sampled realizations and retaining the resulting partners. Our proof constructs a near-optimal certificate inside the sparsifier by combining a \emph{locally computable} surrogate for the large-marginal edges with a fractional completion from sampled light edges. The surrogate nearly preserves the benchmark's value and endpoint loads while controlling dependencies, which makes the statistical light-edge completion possible.

</details>


### [234] [Boyer-Moore Variants for Indeterminate String Matching and Experimental Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27170)
*Neerja Mhaskar,Nivetha Raj Pappuraj*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究不确定字符串(indeterminate strings)上的精确模式匹配，其中文本或模式的位置可代表一个符号集合而非单一字母。作者系统地将Boyer-Moore(BM)系列的坏字符(BC)变体推广到不确定字符串，并提出四种新的BC规则（BC Rules I–IV）以及一种新的好后缀过程（Fast_GSR_Indet_Shift），组合得到四种Fast_BM_Indet变体。通过16种算法在合成数据和E. coli K-12 MG1655基因组上的系统实验，证明新算法在某些设置下比现有的BM_Indet和KMP_Indet快达两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BM风格的不确定字符串匹配算法（如BM_Indet）每次对齐都需要重建前缀数组，开销大；且缺乏对BM变体族的系统适配。基因组学中不确定字符串（如SNP注释参考基因组、共识序列）广泛存在，需要高效实用的匹配算法。

Method: 1) 将七种经典BC-only BM变体（Horspool、Sunday、Raita、Smith、Tuned BM、Zhu-Takaoka、Berry-Ravindran）适配到不确定字符串；2) 提出四种新的BC规则：BC Rule I（最近实心字母回退）、BC Rule II（结合Rule I与BM_Indet的shift）、BC Rule III（边缘实心字符）、BC Rule IV（匹配后缀中最低频实心字符）；3) 提出Fast_GSR_Indet_Shift算法，通过位置索引表τ'在O(1)时间计算好后缀shift，避免每次重建前缀数组；4) 将每种BC规则与Fast_GSR_Indet_Shift组合形成Fast_BM_Indet变体；5) 在随机字符串（σ∈{4,9,20}）和E. coli基因组上系统评测16种算法。

Result: 坏字符-only变体中，Zhu-Takaoka在小字母表（σ=4）和基因组数据上最快；Fast_BM_Indet混合算法一致优于BM_Indet和KMP_Indet，在n=10^6、σ=4时达到两个数量级加速（如2333ms vs 7ms）；BC Rule I是四种规则中最佳，在大字母表上优于Zhu-Takaoka杂交版本；在σ=20时KMP_Indet追平Fast_BM_Indet；在随机/E.coli数据上Zhu-Takaoka+Fast_GSR_Indet_Shift与暴力法相当。

Conclusion: 论文为不确定字符串匹配提供了完整的BM变体族适配方案。实用建议：小字母表/基因组数据选用Zhu-Takaoka或BC Rule I混合Fast_GSR_Indet_Shift；大字母表优先使用BC Rule I版本。Fast_BM_Indet在最坏情况下比BM_Indet快O(√m)倍，且实际性能在所有测试输入上一致更优。

Abstract: We study exact pattern matching on indeterminate strings, where a text or pattern position may represent a set of symbols rather than a single letter. Focusing on Boyer-Moore-style methods, we present new bad-character rules (BC Rules I-IV) and a new good-suffix procedure, computed by Fast_GSR_Indet_Shift, which avoids the per alignment recomputation used in BM_Indet [12] by shifting with a single preprocessed position-indexed table. We conduct a systematic experimental evaluation of sixteen algorithms, including classical bad-character adaptations (e.g., Horspool, Sunday, and Zhu-Takaoka) and hybrids that combine these bad-character rules with Fast_GSR_Indet_Shift. Across synthetic scaling experiments and a case study on the E. coli K-12 MG1655 genome, the Fast_BM_Indet hybrids consistently outperform BM_Indet and KMP_Indet [12], in some settings by up to two orders of magnitude. We also find that Zhu-Takaoka is the strongest bad-character-only adaptation on small alphabets and genomic data, while the Fast_BM_Indet variant using BC Rule I offers comparable performance, making it attractive for larger alphabets. We conclude with practical guidance on choosing among these variants for indeterminate string applications.

</details>


### [235] [$c$-Packedness versus $λ$-Low-Density in Geometric Graphs: Theory and Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27231)
*Gregor Diatzko,Félix Lasseux,Sabine Storandt*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究几何图中刻画局部稀疏性的两个结构参数 c-packedness 和 λ-low-density，首次在百万级道路网络实测两者的真实值，证明了 c ∈ O(λ√n) 的紧致参数联系，并通过将嵌套球算法改造成嵌套平方策略、引入最小包围正方形 PTAS 等方式，把平衡分隔符的实践常数从约 950c 降到 6c；同时把 Reed 算法适配到 c-packed 图，在 O(c²n log n) 时间内构造宽度 O(c) 的树分解，进而得到查询时间 O(c) 的精确距离预言机（改进此前的 O(c log n)）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: c-packed 和 λ-low-density 一直被提议为道路网络的现实模型，但其在大规模真实实例上的取值从未被实测，已有理论保证中的隐含常数过大、依赖球覆盖常数 δ=7 导致平衡因子接近 0.998，使得迭代后分隔符大小高达 ~950 c，无法实用；此外依赖树宽的精确距离预言机构造时间呈指数，阻碍了基于树分解的算法落地。

Method: 1) 工程化实现 λ 的精确算法（quadtree + 并发 stabbing，并按 |R(e)| 降序处理配合 limited synchronization）和 c 的 2-近似 / 42-近似；2) 理论证明 c ∈ O(λ√n) 且下界紧；3) 用 Better Balance + (1+ε)-近似最小包围球 + 从球切换到轴对齐正方形三阶段优化，辅以针对正方形的 O(n + ε^{-2} log(1/ε)) PTAS，把 2/3-平衡分隔符从 406c 降到 6c；4) 适配 Reed 算法到 c-packed 图：对 V 和 W 各做一次嵌套正方形分隔，构造宽度 ≤ 30c 的树分解；5) 用所得树分解结合 [10] 的 𝒯-EDO 模板，得到 O(c) 查询时间的精确距离预言机，并在实现中用邻接边界节点替代 S 进一步缩小平均 bag 大小。

Result: 在 OSM 抽取的 50 万–2070 万节点道路网（含 City/State/Cutout/Small/Medium/Large）上，λ 最大仅约 2643，c 也保持适中且远小于理论上限 Ω_c ≈ λ√n，验证两者差距显著；c 的 42-近似比 2-近似快数个量级，配合中心 sweep 后结果质量可达 2-近似的 75%；分隔符在 small 图上以 100 采样点、50 次分离即可优于 KaHIP-FAST（10–35 次）和 KaHIP-STRONG（30–110 次），在 Large 图上同样具有竞争力；Large 图上 S-分解和 T-分解分别用约 4.5 小时单线程完成，S-EDO 平均查询距离比较次数约为 T-EDO 的 4 倍以上，T-EDO 75% 分位（14）低于 S-EDO 的 25% 分位（43）。

Conclusion: 大规模道路网络上 c 与 λ 足够小，参数化算法具备实际意义；通过正方形分隔符 + 改进 PTAS + Reed 树分解可以把分隔符大小常数从 ~950 降到 6，并得到首个兼具多项式预处理和 O(c) 查询时间的精确距离预言机，实践中 T-EDO 显著优于基于层次分隔树的 S-EDO；未来方向包括结合 Har-Peled 的 O(cn) 期望时间分隔符算法进一步加速预处理，以及降低距离预言机构造中对 c 的三次依赖。

Abstract: When designing algorithms for geometric graphs, exploiting structural parameters can lead to significantly improved bounds. Two prominent parameters in this context are $c$-packedness and $\lambda$-low density, both of which locally restrict graph complexity. Parameterized algorithms based on these parameters have been developed for computing well-separated pair decompositions, balanced separators, as well as distance oracles. Nevertheless the practical applicability of algorithms parameterized by $c$ or $\lambda$ remains unclear. While $c$-packed and $\lambda$-low-density graphs have been proposed as realistic models for road networks, the actual parameter values of large real-world instances have so far remained unknown, and existing theoretical guarantees are partially too loose for practical usage. In this paper we first devise scalable implementations for the approximate computation of $c$ and the exact computation of $\lambda$. Our experiments on road networks with millions of edges reveals a significant gap between the two parameters. On the theoretical side we prove that $c\in O(\lambda\sqrt n)$ which complements the known result that $\lambda\in O(c)$. Furthermore we present improved parameterized algorithms for balanced separator computation that reduce the separator size in theory and practice. We also show how to compute a tree decomposition with a width linear in the respective parameterized balanced separator size in polynomial time. This structural result yields a variety of new algorithmic consequences. Among them is an exact distance oracle with query time $O(c)$ for $c$-packed graphs after polynomial-time preprocessing, which improves upon the previous $O(c\log n)$ bound. Our experiments show that the proposed techniques efficiently produce small balanced separators and enable the construction of concise exact distance oracles on large road networks.

</details>


### [236] [Sampling Line-Graph Colorings with Constant Extra Colors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27440)
*Alireza Haqi*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文证明了在线图(line graph)上,单站点Glauber动力学在均匀正常q-着色采样问题下,对任意整数 q ≥ Δ+5 都能在 O_Δ(n log(n/ε)) 步内混合,所使用的核心工具是 Chen 和 Liu (2026) 的 Bochner 框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 着色-动力学猜想(conjecture 1)预测 Glauber 动力学在 q ≥ Δ+2 时多项式时间混合。本文研究线图这一特殊图类(与边着色直接对应),目标是证明只需常数额外颜色(Δ+5)即可实现快速混合,并将颜色阈值改进为不依赖于度的常数。

Method: 方法包括:(1) 对重涂(recoloring)导出一个精确的 Bochner 恒等式(Lemma 4),将 ||Lφ||² 分解为平方项 H(φ) 与一个邻接矩阵 A_G 的二次型;(2) 利用根图 H 的无符号关联矩阵 J 满足 J^T J = 2I + A_G,将二次型用 Jg_c 的范数替代并得到非负余项;(3) 通过 Bochner 准则(Lemma 3)证明谱隙 gap ≥ q-Δ-4;(4) 与条件 Glauber 链的 Dirichlet 形式比较得到条件谱隙;(5) 应用 Anari 等人的普适性定理得到 spectral independence;(7) 应用 Chen–Liu–Vigoda 的熵定理(Theorem 9)将谱隙提升为修正的对数 Sobolev 常数下界,最终得到 n log n 混合时间。

Result: 主要结果(定理 2):对于有限简单图 H 的线图 G = L(H),若最大度 Δ,颜色数 q ≥ Δ+5,令 κ = q-Δ-4,则 gap(P_GL) ≥ κ/(nq),且 t_mix(P_GL, ε) ≤ min{C_Δ n log(n/ε), ⌈nq/κ (n/2 log q + log(1/2ε))⌉};对可行钉扎 τ,k ≥ 1 个未钉扎顶点,有 gap(P_GL^τ) ≥ κ/(kq),λ_max(Ψ^τ) ≤ (Δ+4)/(q-Δ-4);且修正对数 Sobolev 常数 ρ_mLS ≥ c_{Δ,q}/n。常数 C_Δ 满足 C_Δ ≤ exp(exp(O(Δ)))。该结果将之前线图上的边着色混合结果的颜色阈值从 Δ + O(Δ/log Δ) 改进为 Δ+5 这一常数额外颜色。

Conclusion: 本文证明线图上的 Glauber 动力学在仅需 Δ+5 种颜色时即可实现 O_Δ(n log(n/ε)) 的混合时间,常数额外颜色(独立于最大度 Δ)。这一结果部分接近着色-动力学猜想(猜想 1 中的 Δ+2),但仍有差距;遗留开放问题包括:q = Δ+4 时混合时间的确定,以及 C_Δ 对 Δ 的最优依赖关系。

Abstract: Let $G$ be the line graph of a finite simple graph, with $n\geq1$ vertices and maximum degree $\Delta$. We prove that single-site Glauber dynamics for uniform proper $q$-colorings mixes in $O_\Delta(n\log(n/\varepsilon))$ steps for every integer $q\geq\Delta+5$. Our proof uses the Bochner framework of Chen and Liu (2026).

</details>


### [237] [Vertex-Coloring Edge-Weighting: Kernelization and Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27719)
*Shubhada Aute,Fahad Panolan,Geevarghese Philip*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了图论中顶点着色边赋权问题（Vertex-Coloring Edge-Weighting），针对权重集{0,1}和{1,2}两种情形，给出了关于参数化复杂度、核化以及推广问题的系统结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在作者早期工作中，已证明顶点覆盖数k参数化下两个问题均为FPT，但存在两个未解问题：(1)两者是否有多项式核；(2){0,1}情形关于树深度的参数化复杂度。此外，作者将问题推广到"前期加权版本"（pre-weighted），即部分边权重已预先固定，任务是将其扩展为整个图的正则加权。

Method: 核心方法是从四个具体问题中提炼出一个通用子问题"Bounded Offset Distinguishing Subgraph"（有界偏移区分子图），通过树分解上的动态规划求解，使用cap C限制任一顶点上可选较大权重的边数上限。对于两个原始问题，通过标记过程（marking procedure）和结构引理（structural lemma）构造多项式核；对W[1]难问题，通过设计"禁止小器具"（disallowing gadgets）从List Coloring做参数化归约。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 证明{0,1}-边赋权有O(k³)顶点的核，{1,2}-边赋权有多项式核；(2) 两个原始问题关于树深度均为W[1]-难；(4) 四个问题（含前期加权版本）在顶点覆盖数参数下均为FPT，运行时间为2^{O(k log k)}·n，显著改进了之前2^{O(k⁴)}·n^{O(1)}的界；(5) 前期加权版本在反馈顶点集数和树深度参数下均为W[1]-难。

Conclusion: 本文解决了早期工作遗留的两个公开问题，并系统研究了四个变体的参数化复杂度图景。Bounded Offset Distinguishing Subgraph作为统一框架有效捕捉了所有四种情形的算法本质。仍开放的若干问题包括：前期加权版本是否具有多项式核、{1,2}-边赋权在反馈顶点集数参数下的复杂性等。

Abstract: An edge weighting of a graph induces a coloring of its vertices in which the color of a vertex is the total weight of the edges incident with it. Such an edge weighting is proper if adjacent vertices always receive distinct colors. Deciding whether a graph admits a proper weighting is known to be NP-complete for the weight set $\{0,1\}$, and also for $\{1,2\}$. In recent work ( arXiv:2604.12363 ) we showed that both problems are FPT parameterized by the vertex cover number $k$, but it was open -- to the best of our knowledge -- whether either parameterized problem had a polynomial kernel. In this work, we show that both problems have polynomial kernels when parameterized by $k$. We also show that both problems are W[1]-hard parameterized by treedepth, answering another question from our earlier work. We then study the pre-weighted versions of the two problems, in which the weights of some edges are fixed in advance, and the task is to extend the assignment to a proper weighting of the whole graph. We show that both pre-weighted problems are FPT parameterized by the vertex cover number $k$. For the $\{1,2\}$ version the running time is $2^{O(k \log k)} \cdot n$; for the $\{0,1\}$ version we obtain the same running time when every pre-weight is $1$, and a slower FPT algorithm in the general case. We also show that both pre-weighted problems are W[1]-hard parameterized by either of (i) the feedback vertex set number or (ii) the treedepth of the input graph. Since a graph with no pre-assigned weights is a special case, our algorithms for the pre-weighted versions solve the two original problems as well, in time $2^{O(k \log k)} \cdot n$, significantly improving on the bound of $2^{O(k^4)} \cdot n^{O(1)}$ from our earlier work.

</details>


### [238] [Faster Minimum k-Cut I: Simple and Sparse Weighted Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27781)
*Jason Li,Trevor Vaughn*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文针对简单无权重图上的最小 k-cut 问题，给出了首个绝对常数 $c<1$ 使得运行时间为 $\widetilde{O}_k(n^{ck})$ 的算法。具体地，$k=3$ 时为 $\widetilde{O}(n^2)$，$k=4$ 时为 $\widetilde{O}(n^{55/19})$，$k=5$ 时为 $\widetilde{O}(n^{4.112007})$，$k\geq 6$ 时指数为 $\frac{6}{7}k-0.132\ldots+O(1/k)$，从而首次将最小 $n^k$ 障碍突破为亚多项式因子。算法结合了三个核心组件：用于加权稀疏图的随机/确定性递归算法、利用近似线性 $(O(s\log^2 n \log\log n), s)$ 边不可破分解改进的 FPT 算法，以及结合矩形矩阵乘法恢复 singleton 岛屿的 border/island 框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小 k-cut 问题在简单图上的已知最好算法运行时间为 $O_k(n^{(1-\varepsilon)k+O(1)})$，虽然显示可以以多项式开销突破 $n^k$ 障碍，但指数项仍依赖 $k$。本文旨在给出首个指数中绝对常数 $c<1$ 的算法，使运行时间为 $\widetilde{O}_k(n^{ck})$，从而为固定 $k$ 提供更快的精确算法，并为该问题建立更优的理论复杂度。

Method: 三个关键技术组件：
1. **加权稀疏图算法**：通过对边权做小扰动使最优 k-cut 存在严格轻端；从近似对偶树填充中按权重对数采样若干生成树，枚举跨越至多 $k-1$ 条树边的根切口并保留最轻的 $k^{O(k)}n$ 个；通过递归求 $(k-1)$-cut 完成候选集。随机版本时间 $k^{O(k^2)}n^{k-2}(m+n)\log^3 n$；确定性版本通过字典序扰动并处理整个支持集，时间 $k^{O(k^2)}n^{k+O(1)}$。
2. **近似线性边不可破分解**：利用树切割稀疏化器在 $\widetilde{O}(m+sn)$ 时间内构造 $(O(s\log^2 n\log\log n), s)$ 边不可分解，粘连度同为 $O(s\log^2 n\log\log n)$，深度 $O(\log n)$，由此改进 FPT 算法的依赖至 $O(m)+k^{O(k)}s^{6k-6}n\log^{12k+O(1)}n(\log\log n)^{6k-6}$，同时给出近线性 $(1+\varepsilon)$ 近似算法。
3. **改进的 border/island 框架**：先估计 $s=\Theta(\lambda_k)$，小 $s$ 走 FPT 分支；大 $s$ 走保持边界的稀疏化得到 $\widetilde{O}(n/s)$ 顶点的收缩加权图 $G'$；猜测岛屿数 $i$，$i=0$ 在 $G'$ 上求加权 min k-cut，$i=1,2$ 用专用程序，$i\geq 3$ 通过最小顶点权三角形（矩形矩阵乘法）求最优岛屿集。平衡 FPT 分支 $ns^{6k-6}$ 与大 $s$ 下的四岛屿分支给出最终指数。

Result: 对于简单无权图上的最小 k-cut 问题：
- $k=3$：$\widetilde{O}(n^2)$
- $k=4$：$\widetilde{O}(n^{55/19})\approx \widetilde{O}(n^{2.89474})$
- $k=5$：$\widetilde{O}(n^{4.112007})$
- $k\geq 6$：运行时间 $k^{O(k^2)}n^{1+(6k-6)(k-1.749614)/(7k-10)}(\log n)^{O(k^2)}$，指数为 $\frac{6}{7}k-0.132\ldots+O(1/k)$

此外，给出加权稀疏图的随机算法 $k^{O(k^2)}n^{k-2}(m+n)\log^3 n$ 和确定性算法 $k^{O(k^2)}n^{k+O(1)}$，以及 FPT（关于割值参数）算法 $O(m)+k^{O(k)}s^{6k-6}n\log^{12k+O(1)}n(\log\log n)^{6k-6}$ 和近线性 $(1+\varepsilon)$ 近似算法。

Conclusion: 该论文成功突破了最小 k-cut 问题在简单图上的 $n^k$ 障碍，通过三个创新组件（加权稀疏图递归算法、近似线性边不可破分解、结合矩阵乘法的 border/island 框架）的有机结合，首次给出了指数中绝对常数严格小于 1 的精确算法。其核心技术：扰动+树填充采样+严格轻端保证、近线性边不可破分解、矩形矩阵乘法恢复 singleton 岛屿，均为组合优化与图算法领域的重要贡献，对相关问题如 $k$-连通子图、近最小割枚举等具有方法论意义。确定性 $\widetilde{O}_k(n^{k-1})$ 加权结果在一篇伴随论文中给出，本文随机结果已足够支撑主要定理。

Abstract: The minimum $k$-cut problem asks for the fewest edges whose removal leaves an input graph with at least $k$ connected components. Previously, the best algorithm for simple graphs ran in $O_k(n^{(1-\varepsilon)k+O(1)})$ time~\cite{HL22}, showing that the \(n^k\) barrier can be broken up to a polynomial overhead. We give the first $\widetilde O_k(n^{ck})$-time algorithm for Minimum $k$-Cut on simple graphs for an absolute constant $c<1$. More precisely, the running times are $\widetilde O(n^2)$ for $k=3$, $\widetilde O(n^{55/19})$ for $k=4$, and $\widetilde O(n^{4.112007})$ for $k=5$; for every $k\ge6$, the running time is \[ k^{O(k^2)}n^{1+(6k-6)\frac{k-1.749614}{7k-10}}(\log n)^{O(k^2)}, \] whose exponent is $\frac67k-0.132\ldots+O(1/k)$. The algorithm combines three ingredients. First, for weighted Minimum $k$-Cut we give a randomized \[ k^{O(k^2)}n^{k-2}(m+n)\log^3(n) \] -time algorithm: it perturbs the edge weights so that any minimum $k$-cut has a side with boundary strictly smaller than average. These then cut few edges of some tree in a logarithmic-size sample from a tree packing with high probability. After we enumerate them, we recursively compute $(k-1)$-cuts to complete them to the $k$-cuts of which they were a part. A variant of the perturbation and processing the entire packing support give a deterministic $k^{O(k^2)}n^{k+O(1)}$-time variant. Second, for cut size $s$, we give an improved FPT algorithm using a near-linear-time construction of an $(O(s\log^2 n\log\log n),s)$ edge-unbreakable tree decomposition with $O(s\log^2 n\log\log n)$ adhesion; this also gives a near-linear-time approximation algorithm for Minimum $k$-Cut. Third, we refine the border/island framework of~\cite{HL22}, using rectangular matrix multiplication to recover singleton islands and balancing it against the improved FPT algorithm.

</details>


### [239] [Faster Minimum k-Cut II: Near-Optimal and Deterministic for Weighted Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27797)
*Trevor Vaughn*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文针对加权图上的最小k-cut问题，给出了随机化和确定性的近最优算法，指数达到Max-Weight Clique猜想下的下界n^(k-1-o(1))，并应用于简单图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Max-Weight Clique猜想下，加权最小k-cut需要n^(k-1-o(1))时间。已有最优算法在m=Θ(n²)时达到Õ(n^k)，k=3时为Õ(nm)，与n²的二次目标存在差距。需要同时推进随机化和确定性算法的指数到最优。

Method: 论文分两大组件：(1)更快的加权最小3-cut算法：处理严格小侧和双轻侧情形后，剩余最优有唯一结构侧，通过树打包将完成化归为批处理的2-respecting cut问题。(2)通过枚举有界轻cut候选族递归化归k-cut到3-cut，若枚举过多则直接产生最优k-cut。去随机化使用确定性near-minimum-cut骨架、构造性轻cut界和BSS12的确定性谱稀疏化器。

Result: 随机化算法：k^(O(k²))·n^(k-1)·log²n时间；确定性算法：k^(O(k²))·n^(k-1)·log^O(1)n时间。加权最小3-cut：随机O(n²log²n)，确定性Õ(n²)；最小4-cut：确定性O(m)+Õ(n³)；简单图3-cut O(n²)，4-cut Õ(n^(14/5))。指数达到Max-Weight Clique猜想下的下界。

Conclusion: 论文同时解决了加权最小k-cut问题在随机化和确定性设置中指数上的差距，给出了匹配条件性下界n^(k-1-o(1))的算法。技术核心包括树打包、批处理2-respecting cut优化、构造性轻cut证书以及将去随机化与骨架构造和谱稀疏化相结合。

Abstract: The Minimum $k$-Cut problem asks for a minimum-weight set of edges whose removal leaves an undirected weighted graph with at least $k$ connected components. We consider only $k \ge 3$. Under the Max-Weight Clique conjecture, weighted Minimum $k$-Cut requires $n^{k-1-o(1)}$ time for every fixed $k$. The fastest previous algorithm for weighted graphs ran in $n^{k-2}(m+n)(\log n)^{O(k^2)}$ randomized time~\cite{LV26}; for $k=3$, this gave an $\widetilde O(nm)$-time algorithm. We give randomized and deterministic algorithms matching the conditional lower bound in the exponent. On an $n$-vertex, $m$-edge weighted graph, our randomized algorithm runs with high probability in \begin{equation*} k^{O(k^2)}n^{k-1}\log^2n \end{equation*} time. Our deterministic algorithm runs in \begin{equation*} k^{O(k^2)}n^{k-1}\log^{O(1)}n \end{equation*} time. In particular, weighted Minimum $3$-Cut can be solved in $O(n^2 \log^2 n)$ randomized time and in $\widetilde O(n^2)$ deterministic time. The algorithms have two main components. First, we give a faster algorithm for weighted Minimum $3$-Cut. After handling optima with a very small side and optima with two light sides, the remaining optimum has a unique structured side. Tree packing reduces its completion to a batched collection of $2$-respecting cut problems. Second, we reduce Minimum $k$-Cut to Minimum $3$-Cut by enumerating a bounded family of light-cut candidates and recursively completing either side of each candidate. If the enumeration produces too many cuts, then we can instead produce an optimum $k$-cut directly. We derandomize the $3$-cut algorithm using a deterministic near-minimum-cut skeleton, and derandomize the reduction using a specialized $4$-cut algorithm using the skeleton, the constructive light-cut bounds, and the deterministic spectral sparsifier of \cite{BSS12}.

</details>


### [240] [Dense Interprocedural Dominance in Acyclic Graphs: Context Bounds and Compact Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27818)
*Peisen Yao*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 这篇论文研究过程间(interprocedural)支配关系在静态节点(对应可达配置组)上的最小支配图(覆盖图/传递归约)的大小与查询效率。论文定义了两个关键参数——上下文多重度 κ 和歧义节点数 α——并证明了三组互相独立的分离结果:输出大小(覆盖边数)、歧义程度和查询空间代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实测的最小过程间支配图边数比约为 1.1–1.26(De Sutter 等, 2007),呈现近线性,但缺乏理论解释。本文的动机是回答两个核心问题:(1) 哪些程序结构性质强制输出稀疏?(2) 即使输出密集,查询是否仍可廉价?已有研究关注配置图上的普通支配,但过程间问题本质上是对静态节点的"任一配置命中即算访问"语义,这打破了单过程情形下的支配链结构。

Method: 论文方法围绕两个核心参数展开:
1. **参数定义**:对每个静态节点 v 收集其可达配置集 C(v),定义 κ = max|C(v)|(最大配置数)、α = |{v : |C(v)|>1}|(歧义节点数,即多于一个可达栈的节点数)。
2. **上界**:证明 |H(G)| ≤ min{⌊n²/4⌋, (α+1)(n−1)}。证明的关键引理是:目标节点的所有单上下文支配者构成一条链(因此至多一个能作为覆盖前驱者),加上至多 α 个歧义前驱者。
3. **下界构造**:(a) 构造 G_k(26k−4 节点,30k−6 边,κ=2,α=4k,O(n²) 覆盖边):两个主驱动器以相反顺序调用共享强制过程,用平衡二叉树实现对数栈深度、过程大小有界、调用图度有界。(b) 构造 J_k(32k−5 节点,38k−7 边,κ=3,α=4k,显式图无环):两个主驱动器顺序相同,但增加一个旁路驱动器 U 只访问一个强制过程即返回(永远不到达目标)。该旁路打破了无中间节点的推理,从而保持覆盖关系。
4. **深度一构造**:构造 F_{k,ℓ}(堆栈深度为 1,星形调用图),得到 Ω(αn) 覆盖边,证明 α 因子不可替换为常数。
5. **查询结构**:对 κ≤2 的 ICFG,通过持久化栈标识符在 O((n+m)log(n+1)) 时间内识别并构建配置图(N≤2n,M≤2m);删除 d 的整个配置组 C(d) 后,问题等价于至多两故障、双目标的可达性问题;直接应用 Choudhary (2016) 的双故障可达性 oracle(多项式预处理 + O(n) 存储 + O(1) 查询),得到 O(n) 字、O(1) 查询的支配表示。
6. **实证验证**:用 Python 3.13 对 G_k、J_k、F_{k,ℓ} 三族共 145 个实例进行验证,使用两种独立实现(组删除 + 拓扑序集传播)交叉检验。

Result: 主要结果如下:
1. **定理 3.2**:|H(G)| ≤ min{⌊n²/4⌋, (α+1)(n−1)};单上下文源边构成有向森林。
2. **推论 3.3**:κ=1 时 H(G) 是有 n−1 条边的根树;α=O(1) 时 H(G)=O(n)。
3. **定理 3.4(深度一下界)**:F_{k,ℓ} 有 n=4k+4ℓ−2,m=5k+6ℓ−4,α=2k,κ=2,堆栈深度 1,总度 ≤3,星形调用图,至少 kℓ 个覆盖边。
4. **命题 3.5**:递归自由 ICFG 若每个非根过程至多一条入调用记录,则 κ=1,覆盖图为树。
5. **命题 3.6**:节点数 ≤ s(b^{h+1}−1)/(b−1),因此 s,b 常量时 h=Ω(log n)。
6. **定理 4.3**:G_k 有 n=26k−4,m=30k−6,κ=2,α=4k,过程大小 ≤4,调用图度 ≤2,显式总度 ≤3,对数栈深度,|H(G_k)|≥k²=Θ(n²)。
7. **定理 5.3**:J_k 显式图无环,n=32k−5,m=38k−7,κ=3,α=4k,|H(J_k)|≥k²,过程大小 ≤5。
8. **引理 6.1 + 定理 6.4**:对 κ≤2 的 ICFG,多项式预处理 + O(n) 字 + O(1) 查询的支配判定。
9. **推论 6.5**:稀疏双上下文类存在 Θ(n²) 覆盖边但仍有 O(n) 字 O(1) 查询表示——显式输出与查询空间彻底分离。
10. **实证**:145 个实例上两种独立计算完全一致,验证了所有标记的 a_i ⋖ b_j 覆盖关系。

Conclusion: 论文建立了过程间支配图中三个互相独立的核心量:**显式覆盖边数**、**上下文歧义度**、**查询空间代价**。关键发现:单一上下文保持树形;两个上下文即可在配置图本身线性的情况下产生 Θ(n²) 覆盖边;即使显式图无环、目标到达运行有固定访问顺序,三个上下文仍能产生 Θ(n²) 覆盖——根源在于不达目标的运行(旁路)打破了中间节点的不可插入性。α 控制上界而 κ 控制查询效率,二者分属不同轴。两个未决问题:(1) 显式无环 + κ≤2 是否仍可 Θ(n²);(2) 实用类别中是否存在更快预处理或常数时间覆盖查询。

Abstract: Interprocedural dominance asks which program points every matched call-and-return execution must pass on its way to a target. An analysis that writes out immediate dominance pays for the transitive reduction of this order, the minimal dominator graph. Measured instances of that graph look near-linear, with edge-to-node ratios around 1.1--1.26~\cite{DeSutter2007}, but no program property is known to force this: it is unclear which structural restrictions imply sparse output, and whether dense output also rules out cheap queries. This paper answers both through calling contexts. A static node stands for the group of its reachable configurations; write $\kappa$ for the largest group size and $\alpha$ for how many nodes have more than one configuration. One context per node keeps the tree with $n-1$ edges. Two already allow $\Theta(n^2)$ cover edges in a sparse, loop-free, recursion-free ICFG with total degree three, constant-size procedures, and logarithmic stack depth, while the reachable configuration graph itself stays linear, so the blowup comes from grouping, not from many configurations. Three keep $\Theta(n^2)$ covers even when the explicit call/return graph is acyclic: all target-reaching runs visit the mandatory procedures in the same order, and extra runs that stop short of any target remove the comparabilities that would otherwise kill the covers. Complementing the lower bounds, $\alpha$ ambiguous nodes admit at most $\min\{\floor{n^2/4},(\alpha+1)(n-1)\}$ cover edges, with $\Omega(\alpha n)$ attainable at stack depth one. And for every ICFG with at most two contexts per node, deleting a context group reduces dominance to dual-failure reachability, giving $O(n)$-word, $O(1)$-query representation after polynomial preprocessing. Explicit cover size, context ambiguity, and query-space cost are three separate quantities.

</details>


### [241] [Independent Set Discovery on Biclique-Free Graphs Is Fixed-Parameter Tractable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27837)
*Chenghua Liu,Boning Meng*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文解决了Fellows等人提出的开放问题：在$K_{d,d}$-free图上，独立集发现（Independent Set Discovery）问题关于token数量$k$是固定参数可解的（FPT）。作者给出运行时间为$2^{O(dk\log k)}(n+m)^{O(1)}$的确定性算法，统一关于$k+d$是FPT，并返回最优独立目标集和最短无冲突滑动序列。核心方法是将动态滑动问题归约为加权独立横截面问题，通过"廉价前缀"局部分支策略结合Kővári–Sós–Turán极值界，避免了全局覆盖族枚举的障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立集发现是解决方案发现（solution discovery）框架中的核心问题，其中目标集需要与token移动路径联合选择。此前Fellows等人在有界退化类和无处稠密类上证明了FPT结果，但$K_{d,d}$-free类显著超出了这两个范畴：此类图可以具有无界退化性甚至在某处稠密。关键障碍在于：在$C_4$-free（即$K_{2,2}$-free）图上，不存在规模为$f(k)n^{k/4-\varepsilon}$的$k$-独立覆盖族，因此基于显式枚举覆盖族的方法无法得到FPT算法。该开放问题的解决对极值稀疏性如何足以保证FPT具有基本理论意义。

Method: 方法分为三部分：(1) **赋值距离归约**：通过引理3.1证明token滑动的最短序列长度等于从起始位置到目标集的最小完美匹配距离（基于最短路径），从而将动态问题静态化为加权独立横截面问题。(2) **廉价前缀分叉递归**：对于剩余$r$个标签，每个候选集取前$M(d,r)=\Theta_d(r^d)$个最便宜顶点构成"廉价前缀"。根据三种情况进行分支：某候选集较小时全枚举；所有前缀满且总代价在预算内时由Wanless-Wood准则+ Kővári–Sós–Turán界保证存在可行解；总代价超预算时则至少一个标签必须选前缀中的顶点，对所有标签-顶点对分支。(3) **稀疏前缀转移原理**：将图特定的冲突估计抽象为函数$\gamma(\ell)$，统一处理有界退化、有界$s$-codegree、不平衡双团排除等情况。

Result: 主要结果（定理1.2）：给定$n$顶点$m$边$K_{d,d}$-free图$G$和初始$k$-token配置，可在$2^{O(dk\log k)}(n+m)^{O(1)}$时间内计算$\text{OPT}(G,S)$，返回最优独立目标和最短无冲突滑动序列或报告不可达。问题对固定$d$关于$k$是FPT，且关于$k+d$一致FPT。附加结果包括：(i) 加权独立横截面问题在$K_{d,d}$-free图上的精确FPT算法；(ii) 有界退化图的直接FPT证明（$2^{O(k\log k)}(a+1)^k$时间）；(iii) 边数敏感的XP算法（$2^{O(k\log k)}(m+1)^{k/2}$）；(iv) 有界$s$-codegree和$K_{s,t}$-free（$s\le t$）的更优界；(v) 移动图与可行性图分离的加权移动扩展。

Conclusion: 本文肯定解决了Fellows等人提出的开放问题，证明了独立集发现在$K_{d,d}$-free图上是固定参数可解的，统一关于$k+d$ FPT且运行时间为$2^{O(dk\log k)}(n+m)^{O(1)}$。技术上通过"赋值距离等价"将动态问题转化为静态加权独立横截面问题，并开发了基于有界"廉价前缀"局部分支的新算法框架——该框架避开了全局覆盖族的障碍，是$C_4$-free设置下能工作的关键。该方法论具有广泛适用性，为加权独立横截面、有界退化、有界codegree、不平衡双团排除以及双图模型提供了统一且更优的算法。本文显著推进了解决方案发现框架的结构参数复杂性理论。

Abstract: \textnormal{\textsc{Independent Set Discovery}} asks whether a configuration of $k$ tokens on distinct vertices can be transformed into an independent $k$-set by a sequence of token slides, each moving one token to an unoccupied neighbor; only the terminal configuration must be independent. \textnormal{\textsc{Independent Set Discovery}} is a central problem in solution discovery: its target is not prescribed and must be chosen together with the token movements needed to reach it. Fellows et al. proved it FPT in $k$ on every fixed bounded-degeneracy class and every nowhere-dense class, leaving the biclique-free case open. The biclique-free setting lies substantially beyond both regimes: biclique-free classes can have unbounded degeneracy and even be somewhere dense. We resolve the open problem affirmatively. Given an $n$-vertex, $m$-edge graph promised to be $K_{d,d}$-free and an initial $k$-token configuration, our deterministic algorithm computes the minimum number of slides and, in time $2^{O(dk\log k)}(n+m)^{O(1)}$, returns an optimal independent target and a shortest collision-free slide sequence or certifies that no independent target is reachable. Thus the problem is FPT in $k$ for every fixed $d$ and uniformly FPT in $k+d$. The proof combines an exact minimum-cost assignment characterization of token movement with local branching on bounded, cost-relevant \emph{cheap prefixes} of candidate lists. The method also yields an exact FPT algorithm for weighted independent transversals on $K_{d,d}$-free graphs with overlapping candidate sets, direct exact FPT algorithms for both problems on bounded-degeneracy graphs, an edge-count-sensitive XP algorithm, sharper bounds for bounded $s$-codegree and unbalanced biclique exclusion, and an exact extension to weighted movement on a separate directed graph.

</details>


### [242] [The Exact Approximation Ratio of the Optimal Fixed-Price Mechanism in Bilateral Trade](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27878)
*Tao Jiang,Minbo Gao,Shaowei Cai*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文确定了双边贸易中最优固定价格机制最坏情况福利比的精确值 $\alpha_{\mathrm{FP}} = 0.7380243357\ldots$，由一个一维方程的唯一根刻画。证明通过Wronskian饱和约简，将问题转化为对数严格凸的控制问题，其最优解具有一段内部弧加边界 $q=1$，并通过有界卖方/买方主体加上一个逃逸的买方质量实现匹配极限族。每固定实例有最优价格，但最坏情况分布下确不被达到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双边贸易中固定价格机制的近似比此前仅被限制在区间 [0.7292, 0.73805] 内，确定精确值是机制设计中的基本开放问题。Myerson-Satterthwaite 不可能定理排除了完全效率，固定价格是满足激励相容、个体理性、强预算平衡的典型鲁棒机制类。

Method: 方法分四步：(1) Wronskian 饱和——利用卖方put与买方call变换的Wronskian约束 $(A/C)' \leq 1/C^2$，对给定买方 $C$ 构造饱和卖方 $\hat{A}$；(2) 以 $c=C(p)$ 为坐标、$q=-C'(p(c))$ 为控制变量，将问题约化为控制问题，并去掉 $q$ 单调性约束（唯一放宽）；(3) 取 $u=\log q$，得到对数严格凸的作用量，KKT条件全局充分，最优解结构为单一内部弧后接边界弧 $q=1$；(4) 用参数 $r=b/s$ 参数化轨迹并显式积分，得到 $H_-(r)=H_+(r)$ 的唯一交点 $r_\star$，对应 $\alpha_\mathrm{FP}=(r_\star+1)/(\Theta(r_\star)+2)$。紧致性通过有界卖方 $S_\star$ 和有界买方主体 $B_0$ 加上 $1/\varepsilon$ 处的 $\varepsilon$ 概率质量（逃逸一阶矩）实现。

Result: 得到精确值 $\alpha_{\mathrm{FP}} = 0.7380243357\ldots$，由方程 $e^{-\Theta(r_\star)/2}=(r_\star-1)(\Theta(r_\star)-r_\star+1)$ 在 $r_\star \in (1,3)$ 的唯一根刻画，其中 $\Theta(r)=\int_0^{(r-1)/r} dx/(x^2-x+1/(r+1))$。上界与下界均精确等于此常数。任意满足有限一阶矩的 Borel 分布对（包括原子分布与无界分布）下界成立；但不存在可达到该下确界的合法分布对，只能通过买方一阶矩逃逸的极限族趋近。区间算术验证：$r_\star \in (1.53963634239276, 1.53963634239278)$，$\alpha_\mathrm{FP} \in (0.73802433573449, 0.73802433573450)$。

Conclusion: 本文完全解决了固定价格双边贸易机制的近似比问题，给出了精确解析刻画。与 Cai-Wu [CW23] 在卖方/买方分布上的无限维因子揭示规划不同，本文的 $V(h)$ 控制条件独立推导，并通过 Wronskian 饱和与逆调用坐标得到。对于更一般的含底价的买方报价机制，[DS26] 已证可达到 0.746 的下界，超出固定价格类的能力。本文方法可应用于更广泛的机制设计优化问题，为控制问题与机制近似的桥梁提供了范例。

Abstract: Prior work placed the worst-case welfare ratio of the optimal fixed-price mechanism for bilateral trade in the interval [0.7292,0.73805]. We determine the ratio exactly as \[ \alpha_{\mathrm{FP}}=0.7380243357\ldots, \] characterized by the unique root of an explicit one-dimensional equation. The proof first saturates a Wronskian constraint on the seller put and buyer call transforms. In inverse-call coordinates, the resulting extremal problem becomes a control problem whose logarithmic formulation is strictly convex. Its optimizer has one interior arc followed by the boundary \(q=1\), and the trajectory can be integrated explicitly. We then realize this optimizer by a bounded seller and buyer body together with a vanishing buyer mass at an escaping value, obtaining a matching limiting family. Every fixed instance admits an optimal price, but the worst-case distributional infimum is not attained. The proof applies to arbitrary Borel distributions with finite first moment, including atomic and unbounded distributions.

</details>


### [243] [A Tight Cycle-Cover Inequality for Shortest Common Superstring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27921)
*Nikolai Chukhin,Alexander S. Kulikov,Ivan Mihajlin,Alexander Smal*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了最短公共超字符串问题(SCS)及其贪心算法的近似保证。核心贡献是证明了关于最小代价圈覆盖的一个关键不等式系数γ=1是最优的（既给出了上界证明，又给出了下界反例），并由此将一般SCS的近似比从2.466改进到7/3，将贪心算法的近似保证上界从3.396改进到3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SCS是数据压缩和基因组组装中的经典NP难问题。现有最好的近似比为2.466，贪心算法（尽管其近似比被猜想为2）的最佳上界是3.396。多个先前的算法分析和现有的最优结果都依赖于同一个关于最小圈覆盖的不等式 O - γW ≤ OPT，其中γ的改进能同时推动贪心算法和一般SCS的近似比下降。已有的γ上界是1.396，作者希望将该系数推到极限——γ=1，并证明其不可再改进。

Method: 作者定义了重叠图（complete directed graph with self-loops，顶点为输入字符串，边权为重叠长度），并对最小代价有向圈覆盖C定义W = Σw(c)（圈代价）和O = Σo(c)（最小边重叠）。核心技术：
1. **上界证明（γ=1）**：固定一个公共超串t，利用t中长度为m的子串数|Sub_m(t)|的单调性（|Sub_m(t)| ≥ |Sub_{m-1}(t)| - 1）和telescoping性质。关键工具是Lemma 3.4：利用Rauzy图与Lyndon词结构，证明若多个不同Lyndon词的CSub_m嵌入t中，则其数量受限于|Sub_m(t)| - |Sub_{m-1}(t)| + 1。通过对每个满足o(c)+1 ≥ w(c)的圈在合适的m值上"计数"，将贡献求和到|t|。
2. **下界证明（γ<1不成立）**：构造显式字符串族S_m = {s_2,...,s_m}，其中s_p = 0^{p-1}10^{p-1}10^{p-2}。证明该集合的自环圈覆盖是最小代价圈覆盖，计算得W = Θ(m²)，O ≈ 2W，OPT ≤ W + m，因此O - γW > OPT对任何γ<1和足够大的m成立。
方法特点：证明比之前的γ=1.396更简单的全局图论论证（而非对每个圈单独分析）。

Result: 1. SCS存在7/3-近似算法（此前最佳为2.466，由Englert-Matsakis-Veselý 2023证明）。
2. 贪心算法的近似保证上界为3（此前最佳为3.396）。
3. 圈覆盖不等式O - γW ≤ OPT的最优系数为γ=1：作者证明了γ=1成立，且任何γ<1都存在反例。这是该不等式的极限结果。
4. 证明技术比之前更简洁，采用基于Lyndon词和Rauzy图的全局论证。

Conclusion: 本文通过将圈覆盖不等式中的系数γ推至理论极限1，同时改进了SCS及其贪心算法的近似保证：SCS从2.466改进到7/3，贪心算法从3.396改进到3。由于作者证明了γ=1是最优的（任何γ<1都不可行），这一基于圈覆盖不等式的分析路径已被推到尽头，进一步的近似比改进需要本质上不同的新技术。这项工作澄清了圈覆盖方法的极限，并为后续研究指明了方向。

Abstract: In the Shortest Common Superstring problem (SCS), one is given a finite set of strings and is asked to find a shortest string containing every input string as a substring. Its best known approximation ratio is $2.466$, whereas the currently strongest upper bound on the approximation guarantee of the maximum-overlap greedy algorithm is $3.396$ (Englert, Matsakis, and Vesel{ý}, 2023), though it is conjectured to be $2$. We improve both approximation guarantees: SCS admits a $\frac{7}{3}$ approximation and the approximation guarantee of the greedy algorithm is at most $3$. The main technical ingredient of our proof is a certain inequality for minimum-cost cycle covers of an overlap graph associated with the input strings. Every previous improvement of greedy's worst-case guarantee and the two recent record guarantees for general SCS are driven by it. We improve this inequality by pushing it to its limit: for a particular coefficient of this inequality, we show a new upper bound and prove that it cannot be improved further.

</details>


### [244] [Always-Correct Succinct Dynamic Fusion Nodes Are Impossible: A Cell-Probe Lower Bound in the Small-Set, Large-Universe Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27945)
*Ian D'Ambrosio*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文证明了在"小子集、大宇宙"参数域（n^8 ≤ U, log U ≥ 2^70）下，常数时间动态融合节点在精确（always-correct）意义下不可能实现简洁（succinct）表示。具体地，对于冗余度 R < n 的字典，期望摊销探测复杂度下界为 2^{-26}·log(1 + n/(R+1))，常数期望摊销时间需要 Ω(n) 的冗余位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kuszmaul、Liang 和 Zhou（SODA 2026）提出开放问题：在存储键数为 universe 大小的多项式对数（polylog U）的"融合节点"参数域下，能否构造简洁、O(1)-时间的动态融合节点？先前 Li、Liang、Yu、Zhou（LLYZ23, FOCS 2023）的下界仅适用于 U = n^{1+Θ(1)} 的多项式 universe 域，未能覆盖 polylog U 的融合节点情形。

Method: 本文基于 LLYZ23 的通信框架进行两个关键修正：(1) 修复 LLYZ 内层通信博弈中的条件化缺陷——将逐点探测上界纳入一致性事件 W，并用 Markov 不等式处理被拒绝的"真段"；(2) 通过单一参数 γ = (log U − log n)/(6 log U) 将界扩展到大 universe，其中 (1/2)log(U/n) = 3γ·log U。证明采用四个步骤：硬分布（Distribution 1，对称删除-插入窗口）→ 加盖内层博弈（Lemma 12）→ 外层博弈（Lemma 7）→ 嵌套森林累计（padded hierarchy, Section 6）。同时将严格前驱归约到成员查询。

Result: 主要定理（Theorem 1）：在 n^8 ≤ U、log U ≥ 2^70、0 ≤ R < n 的条件下，任何精确确定性或零误差 Las Vegas 动态字典的期望摊销探测复杂度至少为 2^{-26}·log(1 + n/(R+1))。Corollary 3：常数期望摊销时间需要 R = Ω(n)。Corollary 4：亚线性冗余度 R ≤ n·2^{-a√(log w)} 强制代价至少为 (a/2^{27})·√(log w)。Theorem 2：在 U = 2^w、n = ⌈w^c⌉（c > 0）下，使用 ⌈log C(U,n)⌉ + o(n) 位且支持最坏情况 O(1) 操作的精确前驱结构不存在。完整论证使用 Lean 4 形式化验证（无 sorry/admit/自定义公理）。

Conclusion: 本文否定了 Kuszmaul–Liang–Zhou 关于常数时间动态融合节点是否可行的开放问题（在精确/Las Vegas 语义下）。通过修复 LLYZ 内层博弈的条件化缺陷并引入尺度自适应熵参数 γ，将多项式 universe 下界扩展到 polylog universe 域。结果匹配 Domingues 排名索引的时间坐标，并辅以完整的 Lean 4 形式化证明。

Abstract: Kuszmaul, Liang, and Zhou (SODA 2026) ask whether succinct constant-time dynamic fusion nodes exist when the number of stored keys is polylogarithmic in the universe size. We give a negative answer for always-correct structures. For n^8 <= U, log_2 U >= 2^70, and redundancy 0 <= R < n, a dynamic dictionary requires at least 2^-26 log_2(1+n/(R+1)) expected-amortized cell probes per operation. The model permits fixed layouts of packed cells of at most one word each, including the short-spill convention used by succinct word-RAM structures, and covers zero-error Las Vegas algorithms with fresh per-invocation randomness and almost-sure termination. The proof repairs a conditioning defect in the inherited communication argument by placing pointwise probe caps inside the consistency event, then extends the lower bound to large universes through a scale-adaptive entropy parameter. Consequently, when U=2^w and n=ceil(w^c) for any fixed c>0, no always-correct predecessor structure can use log_2 binom(U,n)+o(n) persistent mutable bits and support constant-time operations. Constant expected-amortized time requires Omega(n) redundant bits. Lean 4 checks the complete packed-memory and fresh-random indexed models, hard distribution, communication bounds, separator, nested-forest accounting, deterministic and Las Vegas lower bounds, strict-predecessor reduction, and redundancy corollaries.

</details>


### [245] [Personalised versus Posted Pricing from Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28181)
*Pieter Kleer,Johan van Leeuwaarden,Daan Noordenbos*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究基于样本的统一定价（posted pricing）相对于个性化定价（personalised pricing）的最坏情况收益保证，针对λ-正则分布族刻画最优近似比，并发现随λ变化的结构性转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化定价虽能最大化收益，但需要详细客户信息且面临公平性争议。许多企业转而采用统一定价，但其相对于个性化定价的性能损失缺乏量化分析，特别在仅有有限样本信息的情形下。本研究旨在回答：在仅有n个样本的情况下，统一定价能恢复个性化定价收益的多大比例？

Method: 研究基于λ-正则值分布族（含MHR（λ=0）和正则（λ=1）分布），求解样本定价规则与最优个性化定价（即均值）收益比的最大-最小优化问题。采用的技术包括：双重无限线性规划的对偶论证、超几何函数及连续关系、涉及不完全Beta函数的组合恒等式、Le Cam两点法、统计中序统计量的精确分析，以及Barlow-Proschan单符号变化引理的应用。

Result: 主要结果分三部分：(1) MHR分布（λ=0）情形：证明样本均值是最优样本基定价规则，精确近似比为(n/(n+1))^{n+1}，收敛于MHR类的完整信息界1/e；(2) 单样本λ-正则情形：证明直接使用样本本身作为价格是确定性规则中的最优策略，保证近似比为(1-λ)/(4-2λ)；(3)多样本λ-正则情形：证明基于最优序统计量的定价规则以(1-λ)^{1/λ} - Θ(1/n)收敛到完整信息界，且该收敛率在所有可能随机化策略中是最优的。此外，证明在λ>0时样本均值不再最优。

Conclusion: 本文为基于样本的统一定价问题提供了以个性化定价（均值）为基准的完整理论刻画，揭示了依赖于λ参数的结构性转变。结果表明，MHR分布中样本均值是充分统计量，但该性质在λ>0时消失；尽管λ>0时的最优策略似乎极其复杂，但一个简单的序统计量定价规则在多样本极限下可达渐近最优。未来研究方向包括确定均值与序统计量策略优劣的精确边界、小样本与λ>0下的最优定价策略数值求解，以及推广至多买家设置。

Abstract: Personalised pricing maximises expected revenue from a market but requires detailed information about individual customers. How much of this revenue can be recovered using a simple posted price based on a finite number of samples from the underlying value distribution? We answer this question by maximising the worst-case ratio between the expected revenues of posted and personalised pricing over the fundamental class of $\lambda$-regular value distributions. Our results reveal a structural transition as a function of $\lambda$. For the class of monotone hazard rate (MHR) distributions, corresponding to $\lambda = 0$, the sample mean is an optimal statistic: the entire sample can be compressed into its average without any loss of revenue. Beyond the MHR class, corresponding to $\lambda > 0$, this property disappears. We show that the sample mean is no longer optimal, revealing that optimal sample-based pricing rules become substantially more intricate. Nevertheless, we show that a remarkably simple order-statistic based pricing rule is asymptotically optimal as the number of samples $n$ grows, achieving the optimal approximation ratio up to a tight error of order $1/n$. Our analysis combines techniques from probability, approximation theory and optimization, including doubly infinite linear programming, hypergeometric functions, and combinatorial identities involving incomplete Beta functions.

</details>


### [246] [Transposition achieves OPT$+O(1)$ in polynomial time for IID list update](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28397)
*Clayton Mizgerd*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 论文证明：在IID列表更新问题中，转置(transposition)规则在多项式时间内即可实现期望代价不超过 OPT + O(1)，确认了Coester(2026)的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典列表更新问题中，Coester(2026)已证明转置规则在平稳分布下代价不超过 OPT+1，但该马尔可夫链的混合时间可能任意慢（受病态分布影响）。因此需要研究在任意初始排列和任意概率分布下，经过多项式步数后转置规则是否能快速接近近最优性能。

Method: 方法核心分三步：(1) 引入对数尺度上的"自适应缓冲区" θ(x)，将超出代价 F(σ) 通过跨多个阈值的反序对数量 J_x 控制，并证明 ∫e^x/θ(x)^2 dx = O(1)；(2) 构造辅助"偏置排他链"，对每个 x 与 θ 比较实际转置链的相邻交换概率，证明经 n^4 步后反序数期望至多 π²/(6θ²) + 1/4，并建立辅助链与原链的Dirichlet形式比较；(3) 利用Liu-Mohanty-Raghavendra-Rajaraman-Wu的局部平稳性思想的离散时间类比，证明Dirichlet形式 ℰ_P(f, log f) 在 t 步后衰减为 O(H^{3/2}/t^{1/4})，再结合对零概率的扰动耦合，完成主要定理。

Result: 主要定理：对任意 n≥1、任意概率向量 p、任意初始排列 σ₀ 和 t≥n^{29}，经过 t 次查询后期望代价 E[Cost(σ_t)] ≤ OPT + 1250。指数29和常数1250未经优化。这是首个证明转置规则在多项式时间内达到 OPT+O(1) 的结果。

Conclusion: 论文完全解决了Coester关于转置规则多项式时间近最优性的猜想，确立了自组织列表算法在缺乏先验分布知识情况下的强理论保证。证明中的Dirichlet形式方法为其他缺乏快速混合的马尔可夫链上的代价控制提供了通用技术。

Abstract: In the classical list update problem, a set of items must be stored in a list-type structure, where accessing the $i$-th element costs $i$. Items will be queried in an IID manner according to some probability distribution $p$ on the items. We want to minimize the expected cost of each query. The optimal order is to place the items in decreasing order of probability $p_1 \geq p_2 \geq \cdots$ with expected cost $\mathsf{OPT} = \sum_j j p_j$, but the probability vector $p$ is generally unknown. Thus we use a self-organizing list following the transposition rule: an item is transposed 1 position forward whenever it is queried. Coester (2026) proved that, at stationarity measure for the transposition rule, the expected cost of a query is at most $\mathsf{OPT} + 1$. However, this Markov chain may have arbitrarily slow mixing time. We prove that, for arbitrary $p$ and arbitrary initial orderings $\sigma$, after polynomially many queries in the number of items, the expected cost of a query is at most $\mathsf{OPT} + O(1)$.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [247] [Spec2COBOLRot: An Agentic-AI Degradation Loop for Realistic COBOL Corpus Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26835)
*Jean-Baptiste Espinasse,Djamel Eddine Khelladi,Mathieu Acher*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对真实生产环境的 COBOL 代码语料较少、难以对现代化方法进行严格基准测试的问题，提出了一种结合规范驱动生成与基于真实代码提取的模式和复杂度目标进行迭代退化的系统化智能体 AI 流水线，用于生成结构上接近真实生产代码的 COBOL 程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: COBOL 在生产环境中仍被广泛部署，但缺乏具有代表性的真实代码语料，这限制了对 COBOL 现代化方法的严格基准评估。现有研究难以获取反映真实维护历史的程序结构，因此需要一种能够生成结构保真（structural fidelity）的合成 COBOL 代码的方法。

Method: 提出一种系统化的智能体 AI 流水线（agentic AI pipeline），结合：(1) 规范驱动生成（specification-driven generation）生成初始 COBOL 程序；(2) 基于从真实生产代码中提取的模式和复杂度目标进行迭代退化（iterative degradation）。评估指标为"结构保真度"（structural fidelity），即生成代码在结构上与生产代码的相似程度，并针对三个不同业务领域的程序进行实验，分析退化过程是否能达到目标复杂度同时保留业务行为。

Result: 流水线能够可靠地生成语法有效的程序，并使其向真实的结构复杂度靠拢。但研究同时发现：(1) 业务行为（business behavior）的保留并不总能通过构造保证；(2) 单独针对结构指标进行退化而忽略业务逻辑，可能产生复杂度无法反映合理维护历史的程序。

Conclusion: 单纯依赖结构指标进行退化存在局限，结构复杂度与业务逻辑演进之间的脱节会损害生成代码的真实性。未来工作应转向更具前景的方向：沿着模拟的开发历史（simulated development history）从零开始生成遗留程序（legacy programs），以同时兼顾结构特征和合理的演化轨迹。

Abstract: COBOL remains widely deployed, yet representative corpora reflecting real production code are rarely available, limiting rigorous benchmarking of modernization approaches. We propose a systematic agentic AI pipeline for generating realistic COBOL programs, combining specification-driven generation with iterative degradation guided by patterns and complexity targets extracted from real production code. Here, realism is understood as structural fidelity to production code as captured by our metrics. We evaluate whether degradation reaches target complexity levels while preserving business behavior, and examine the limits of the approach, across three programs from distinct business domains. Results show the pipeline reliably produces syntactically valid programs and moves them toward realistic structural complexity. However, preserving business behavior is not always achieved by construction, and targeting structural metrics independently of business logic risks producing programs whose complexity does not reflect a plausible maintenance history. We discuss these limitations and outline a more realistic alternative as a direction for future work, generating legacy programs from scratch along a simulated development history.

</details>


### [248] [Teach-to-Crash: A Closed-Loop Student-Teacher LLM Framework for Collision-Inducing Test Scenario Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27296)
*Zaid Ghazal,Khouloud Gaaloul,Bruce Maxim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Teach-to-Crash提出了一种闭环双LLM（学生-教师）框架，用于自动驾驶系统（ADS）的碰撞场景生成。学生LLM（gpt-5-nano）批量生成基于自我中心位置网格的可执行JSON场景，教师LLM（gpt-5-mini）仅在滚动碰撞率和TTC停滞时被调用，提供策略性指导以重定向搜索。在CARLA Town06高速公路场景的两种实验设置下，该方法实现了最高的碰撞命中率（90.79%）、最短的平均碰撞时间（18.31秒）、最高的场景多样性（0.547）和最高的可避免碰撞率（基于避免性的有用性代理，60.04%），优于基线方法PAFOT（遗传算法）和ChatScene（开放循环LLM）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模拟的ADS测试面临两个关键瓶颈：（1）固定预算下场景表示可能浪费在非自我相关的行为上，且搜索容易陷入近重复交互的停滞；（2）现有基于LLM的方法依赖单次开环框架，缺乏对执行结果的自适应反馈，难以判断生成是否真正改善了碰撞率。该研究旨在通过闭环多智能体LLM架构解决这些问题，使LLM能够通过执行反馈交换信息并自适应地引导搜索。

Method: 方法包含四个核心组件：（1）基于自我中心位置网格的场景表示——将每个NPC表示为长度为K=6的[v_k, p_k]对序列，p_k是离散位置ID（1-8为NPC区域，9为自我），v_k为目标速度（0-60 mph）；（2）学生LLM——轻量级生成器，以严格JSON格式批量输出B=10个候选场景；（3）教师LLM——高级推理控制器，仅当滚动碰撞率（CR_W）和元TTC（TTC_W）相对于最近改进检查点停滞时被调用（基于CollImproving和TTCImproving谓词的逻辑），输出策略指导G以重定向学生生成；（4）场景验证器——确定性检查层，验证JSON结构、裁剪值到合法范围、丢弃无效场景。算法运行I_max=45次迭代，窗口W=10，目标CR_target=90%、TTC_target=12秒。优化目标是在固定预算B内最大化碰撞场景比例、临界性评分（基于METTC、MD、SD、ET四个归一化指标的加权和）和多样性（Jaccard距离）。

Result: 在CARLA Town06上使用Traffic Manager自动驾驶仪的两种实验设置（设置A：60 mph限速；设置B：48 mph降速）下，10次运行的结果显示：碰撞命中率（CHR）：90.79%（vs PAFOT 79.09%、ChatScene 64.03%）；平均碰撞时间（TTC）：18.31秒（vs PAFOT 23.71秒、ChatScene 30.32秒）；Jaccard新颖性：0.547（vs PAFOT 0.338、ChatScene 0.522）；失败聚类：404.4个/运行（显著高于基线）；独特失败率：0.900；平均避免性碰撞率：60.04%（最高）；CDR为136.21（PAFOT为179.44但方差更大）。消融实验显示，教师引导的场景占所有发现碰撞的77%以上。在TraverseV4控制器下，避免性碰撞率上升至84.06%。所有主要差异通过Mann-Whitney U检验（Benjamini-Hochberg校正）验证具有统计显著性（p<0.001）。

Conclusion: Teach-to-Crash证明了闭环双LLM推理可以作为自适应搜索控制，在受约束的可执行程序空间内引导对抗性模拟测试。在评估范围内，该框架生成了频繁、结构多样且被评估为更常可避免的碰撞故障。然而，研究存在以下局限性：仅与Student-only子集、PAFOT和ChatScene比较，未与同模型闭环配置或多样性感知技术比较；评估限于单一地图（Town06）、高速公路车道跟随交互和两个NPC；避免性代理是基于运动学启发式的弱代理，非可达性分析或控制器特定推理的替代；未测量墙上时钟时间、LLM延迟或token成本。未来工作包括对N、K和权重的敏感性分析、更丰富的严重性/多样性目标、更广泛的地图和ADS栈、同模型和单LLM基线、token成本测量以及基于可达性的避免性分析。

Abstract: Validating Autonomous Driving Systems (ADS) in simulation requires testing architectures that can discover rare, safety-critical failures while generating scenarios that are executable, diverse, and useful for downstream failure analysis. We introduce Teach-to-Crash, a closed-loop testing framework that combines a constrained ego-centric scenario representation, stagnation-aware search control, and a dual-LLM architecture for adaptive failure discovery. A high-reasoning Teacher LLM acts as an adaptive search controller, while a low-reasoning Student LLM emits simulator-executable scenarios in a strict JSON schema. The Teacher intervenes only when rolling collision rate and time-to-collision metrics stagnate, providing strategic guidance to redirect the search. In a CARLA case study with two experimental setups that vary the ego vehicle's speed policy, Teach-to-Crash achieves the highest Collision Hit Rate (90.79%), the shortest mean Time-to-Collision (18.31 s), and a competitive Collision Discovery Rate (136.21). PAFOT attains a higher mean CDR (179.44), but with substantially larger variance. Teach-to-Crash also yields the highest diversity (0.547) and, averaged across both setups on the CARLA Traffic Manager controller, the highest avoidability-based usefulness proxy (60.04%) among the compared methods. These results, within the evaluated CARLA scope, provide evidence that closed-loop dual-LLM reasoning can steer adversarial simulation-based testing over a constrained executable program space, generating failures that are frequent, structurally diverse, and assessed as more frequently avoidable.

</details>


### [249] [Constraint-Driven Context Engineering: Designing Domain Interfaces for AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27354)
*Xiwei Xu,Chen Wang,Mengmeng Yang,Yipeng Zhang,Jacky Jiang,Suyu Ma,Youyang Qu,Ming Ding,Liming Zhu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为"约束驱动的上下文工程"(CDCE)的设计方法论，将领域约束（技术性、监管性、制度性、规范性）视为一等设计驱动力，系统性地识别、表征并运作化这些约束，为AI系统构建"领域接口"，使其能够引导行为、强制边界或验证输出结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式AI系统部署于特定领域时，常受技术、监管、制度和规范等多重约束制约。现有上下文工程方法主要关注通过检索、记忆和工具提供领域知识，但对系统性地识别和运作化约束支持不足。本文旨在填补这一空白，提出以约束为驱动的上下文工程方法。

Method: 采用基于软件架构设计和领域驱动设计(DDD)的方法论，结合属性驱动设计(ADD)。CDCE包含三个阶段：(1)约束识别——通过与领域专家协作和审视领域工件识别约束；(2)上下文资产识别——确定运作化约束所需的具体信息资产；(3)上下文表示设计——根据AI任务、上下文质量和技术约束选择适当的数据结构。约束按显性/隐性、预定义/动态、硬约束/软约束三个维度进行表征，并通过比较性多案例研究方法在三个真实领域项目中验证。

Result: 通过三个案例研究验证CDCE的适用性：(1)教育评估(Studitory平台)展示了约束驱动的渐进式上下文丰富化过程，将课程资源转化为任务特定的标记标准和评估工件；(2)医疗决策支持展示了基于Toulmin论证方法的护理计划结构化验证，确保每项决策都有患者证据和适用医学指南支持；(3)财务困境预测(Risk IQ项目)展示了约束的人机协同演化，首次建模迭代暴露了现有评估标准的模糊性，审计员审查后澄清了流动性风险与长期财务可持续性的区别，形成更丰富的约束集。三个案例共同表明，约束在领域接口中可扮演引导、强化或验证的角色，且接口本身可随时间和人机交互而演化。

Conclusion: CDCE将上下文工程从以知识为中心的视角转向以约束驱动的视角，强调工程活动是构建和维护AI系统与运营环境之间领域接口的设计过程。约束可作为引导、强制机制或验证标准等多种互补角色发挥作用，领域接口能够随专业理解、组织实践和环境条件的变化而演化。上下文工程本质上是一项设计活动，而非简单的检索任务。

Abstract: Generative AI systems are increasingly deployed to address domain problems. These systems operate under technical, regulatory, institutional, and normative constraints that define acceptable AI behaviour and outcomes within their domains. We observe a recurring pattern in our industry engagement: partners often arrive with a functioning but relatively generic AI solution. The challenge is no longer to build an AI system from scratch, but to improve the quality and domain appropriateness of an AI-generated solution. In these settings, the limiting factor is often the quality, scope, and structure of the context available to the system. Yet, existing context engineering approaches primarily focus on supplying domain knowledge through retrieval, memory, and tools, with limited support for systematically identifying and operationalising the constraints that govern AI systems in their operational environments. This paper proposes Constraint-Driven Context Engineering (CDCE), a design approach for engineering domain interfaces for AI systems. Drawing on software architecture design and Domain-Driven Design (DDD), CDCE treats domain constraints as first-class design drivers. It identifies and characterises constraints, determines the required context assets, and designs representations through which these assets are made available to AI systems. We conducted a comparative multiple-case study with industry and public-sector partners across educational assessment, healthcare decision support, and financial-distress prediction. Depending on their characteristics, constraints can guide AI behaviour, enforce permissible boundaries, or support verification of AI-generated outcomes. The cases demonstrate CDCE's applicability across contrasting domains and show how constraint characteristics shape the resulting domain interfaces.

</details>


### [250] [FDE-Bench: Evaluating LLM Agents for Deployment Environment Configuration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27571)
*Weihang Ding,Junfei Zhan,Yueting Li,Qirong Guo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FDE-Bench 是一个评估 LLM Agent 部署工程能力（前向部署工程 FDE）的基准测试，包含 136 个任务，覆盖 Docker 镜像、多服务 Compose 栈和 Kubernetes 的绿地构建与诊断修复场景。其核心创新在于采用四层门控二进制检查（构建、就绪、行为、规格一致性）进行 fresh replay 评分，不使用 LLM judge，并通过四臂发布门控确保任务可解且不能被简单基线策略攻破。评估了七个语言模型，最佳模型解决率 75.0%，所有零智能基线均无法解决任何任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准（如 SWE-bench、EnvBench、SetupBench、Repo2Run 等）主要评估代码修改或开发环境配置，未能直接衡量"将应用代码转化为服务可连接、就绪且可观测的运行系统"的部署工程能力。SWE-bench 仅测试代码变更是否通过仓库测试，但部署还需要管理硬件预算、依赖、容器镜像、编排、服务连接和健康监控；通过仓库测试不等于具备部署能力。此外，环境配置类基准正被用作 RL 训练数据，亟需严格的留出评估来防止过拟合。FDE-Bench 旨在填补这一空白，将"部署环境配置"作为独立、可回放、可审计的评估对象。

Method: 任务定义：每个任务为元组 (t, y, w, E, C, ρ)，其中 t 为自然语言部署工单，y 为 spec.yaml 声明的客服可见要求（端口、端点、就绪语义、监控指标、约束），w 为应用源码和部分/破损的配置，E 锁定容器运行时与 K8s 发行版，C 为隐藏检查套件，ρ 为参考解。

任务类型：三种轴——容器化（C）、编排（O）、健康监控（H），加上全流水线族（F）；两种模式——绿地（greenfield，从零构建）和修复（repair，从故意破坏的配置出发诊断修复），共 14 个家族 136 个任务。

评分协议：Replay 评分公式 s(w') = Φ(C, Deploy_E(Π_g(w'), F))，即提交后仅保留 graded_artifacts glob 匹配的文件，在全新环境中重建部署并运行隐藏检查。四层门控二进制检查：
- L1 Build：制品可构建且符合镜像约束；
- L2 Readiness：滚动更新完成，负载在稳定期内持续 Ready；
- L3 Behavior：端点响应正确的跨服务往返、PromQL 系列存在、告警在故障注入时触发而在健康窗口静默；
- L4 Spec Conformance：副本数、镜像大小配额、非 root 执行、禁止明文密钥。
每层权重 0.25，DS = 0.25(b+r+e+c)；所有四层通过即 Resolved (RR = 1.0)。

发布门控（四臂）：(1) 参考解必须解决 (s=1.0)；(2) do-nothing（直接提交工作区）必须失败；(3) spec-transcription（机械转写可见规格）必须失败；(4) generic stub（标准库最小监听器）必须失败。此外还有三个对抗策略测试评分捷径，均未通过任何任务。

基础设施：Docker-in-Docker 沙箱，allow-listed registry egress 代理，12 个单节点 kind 集群，每个集群同时最多 2 个评分回合。固定四工具脚手架（bash、write_file、read_file、submit），固定系统提示，零 per-model 调优。

度量：RR（Wilson 95% CI）、mean DS、终止状态分布、失败阶段归因。每 (task, model) 单元一个回合，固定预算（35-55 工具调用、20-35 分钟、200K 输出 token、2-3 USD）。

Result: 主要结果（136 任务 v1.1 grid）：
- Gemini-3.6-Flash：RR 75.0%，DS 0.857（最佳）
- Claude-Sonnet-5：RR 72.8%，DS 0.855
- GPT-5.6-Terra & DeepSeek-V4-Pro：RR 69.9%
- DeepSeek-V4-Flash：RR 66.2%
- GPT-5.6-Luna：RR 63.2%
- Gemini-3.5-Flash-Lite：RR 52.9%
- Ensemble union：92.6%
- 三个零智能基线（do-nothing、spec-transcription、generic stub）：RR 0%，DS 0.217-0.438
- 参考解：RR 100%

按轴分析（macro mean）：健康监控 73.1% > 容器化 67.1% > 编排 65.2%（反驳了健康监控是最难轴的假设）。

按模式分析：修复组 82.0% vs 绿地组 51.3%，差距 30.7 个百分点，所有七个模型均呈现正差距。

失败阶段归因（313 未解决回合）：就绪 110（最大）、行为 97、一致性 54、构建 52；103 个受 cap 限制终止。

十任务无任何模型解决（全为绿地，七个单镜像 C 任务，三个 K8s 任务）。

人类基线：一名工程师在 Claude-Sonnet-4 协助下解决 25 任务子集中的 23 个（92%），vs 自主基线 72%。

零智能基线与对抗策略验证了评分信号不被捷径攻破，但空健康探针可通过 readiness 层，暴露了下游检查的必要性。

Conclusion: FDE-Bench 将部署环境本身作为评估对象，通过 fresh replay 协议和四层门控二进制检查将部署成功/失败与可检查、可重放的制品关联起来。最佳模型 75.0%，十任务无任何模型解决，揭示了"能构建但不能运行"的就绪阶段是最大失败模式。修复任务比绿地任务显著容易，凸显了绿地场景下的部署工程鸿沟。基准在协议层面（spec-leak audit、沙箱隔离审计、污染控制）和评分信号层面（对抗策略、零智能基线、参考解）均做了严谨的有效性验证。局限性包括单回合评估无法估计方差、Linux/x86 Docker-K8s 生态受限、人类对比仅一名参与者且混杂了更丰富的脚手架因素。FDE-Bench 为追踪模型代际间的部署能力进展、跨智能体接口比较以及失败归因分析提供了可复现的评估基础设施。

Abstract: Deployment requires an agent to turn application code into a running system whose services connect, become ready, and remain observable. FDE-Bench evaluates this capability with 136 deployment-configuration tasks spanning Docker images, multi-service Compose stacks, and Kubernetes, in greenfield and diagnose-and-repair modes. Agents submit declarative artifacts that are collected, rebuilt, and redeployed in a pristine environment. Four gated binary check layers measure build, readiness, behavior, and conformance to the deployment specification, using programmatic checks without an LLM judge. A four-arm release gate requires a resolving reference solution and rejects tasks solved by do-nothing, specification-transcription, or generic-stub submissions. The released check annotations expose the link between 2,145 checks and their specifications, including seven documented gaps. Three additional adversarial strategies test shortcuts in the grading signals; none resolves any of the 135 tasks they cover, while a vacuous health probe passes readiness and exposes the need for downstream checks. On the 136-task evaluation grid, seven language models from four providers use the same four-tool scaffold and resolve 52.9-75.0 percent of tasks. The three zero-intelligence floors resolve none and reach a mean Deployment Score of at most 0.44. Readiness is the largest failure stage, accounting for 110 of 313 unresolved episodes. Mean resolution rate is 30.7 percentage points higher on the repair task group than on the disjoint greenfield group, with a positive gap for every model; ten tasks resist all seven. In a 25-task case study, one practicing engineer directing Claude-Sonnet-5 resolves 92 percent against 72 percent for the autonomous baseline. FDE-Bench links deployment success and failure to artifacts that can be inspected and replayed.

</details>


### [251] [Bounded Loops: Pre-Run Spend Bounds, Proved Termination, and Verified Completion for Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27871)
*Varun Pratap Bhardwaj,Garima Singh,Arun Pratap Bhardwaj*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文针对AI智能体框架中"完成判定依赖智能体自报"这一核心缺陷，提出"有界循环"(bounded loop)形式化契约——由独立于工作器的门控器(gate)、带预运行预算清单的工作器和声明式预算构成,并证明其终止性、不可漂移性和不可超支性。同时构建基于变异测试的双层盲测语料库来量化验证器的误接受率,在57个循环目录中发现47个已发布代码中的真空中性(vacuous)门控缺陷,提出"自证"(self-attestation)作为第二类缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流智能体框架中,步骤完成的判定完全依赖智能体自身声明其已完成——这既不可证也不可测。持久执行平台虽对重试和耗时设限,但其校验器通常与工作代码在同一代码库中,独立性靠"部署纪律"而非"系统强制"保证。本文动机是:(1)形式化陈述智能体框架必须保证的契约并予以证明;(2)构建独立于作者之外的测量工具,以量化特定框架是否兑现该契约。

Method: 形式化定义"有界循环"三元组(Worker, Gate, Budget)及有界循环图(带repair边的DAG),证明三项保证:终止性(Theorem 5.2,最坏尝试次数闭合形式为(R+1)∑m_v,条件是repair预算全局而非每节点)、done的健全性(Theorem 7.1,从控制流证明无节点可达done而无可追溯到gate verdict的收据)、不可超支性(Proposition 13.1,带预运行的清单级预算上限)。构建双层变异测试语料库——Tier-1通过AST静态断言强制盲性、采用机械化算子,Tier-2由五个独立agent CLI在仅知循环目的不知checker的条件下生成语义变异。识别两类缺陷:真空中性(vacuity,Definition 8.1,检查被所检对象的缺失所满足)与自证(self-attestation,Definition 9.1,被审方控制被检值)。

Result: 在57个循环、209个破坏型变异体上,修复后报告零误接受率(Wilson 95%上界≤1.8%)、62个保留型上零误拒绝率(≤5.8%)。最关键发现:在已发布并经评审的代码中发现47个真空中性门控缺陷。冻结门控后施加新算子家族揭示23.3%(14/60)误接受率——证明"零"反映的是语料库耗尽而非质量。在自家测试套件中发现14个同类缺陷,在自身已部署引擎中发现3个自证型缺陷(由外部agent审计员发现)。测量出"声明但未强制"的wallclock上限缺陷存在于自身系统中(已修复并报告)。

Conclusion: 本文贡献是测量装置而非数字本身——给智能体框架一个可证明、可第三方验证的契约,并给出衡量其门控质量的仪器化方法。最重要的经验性结论是:在七个互不共享实现的层面反复出现的同一缺陷模式——"组件回答了未被问及的问题,答案局部正确,失败静默且讨好",该类缺陷对逐行审查天然不可见,只能通过对其本身的测量而非对其性能的推理来发现。结构性独立性(已证明)与统计独立性(未证明)之间的鸿沟是模型既写工件又写门控时遗留的最大威胁。

Abstract: In mainstream agent frameworks, a step ends when the agent's own output says it has finished. Durable-execution platforms bound retries and time, but their checker conventionally lives in the same codebase as the work: a discipline the deployment is trusted to keep, not a property the harness enforces. We state what an agent harness must guarantee, prove it, and build the instrument that measures whether a harness delivers it. A bounded loop is a worker, an independent gate the worker cannot write to, and a declared budget; a bounded-loop graph composes them with a repair relation that lets a downstream failure re-run a finished upstream node. Three guarantees follow. It finishes: termination holds under repair, with the worst-case attempt total in closed form, if the repair budget is global not per node. It does not drift: no node reaches DONE without a gate verdict in an append-only hash-chained ledger, proved from control flow, since repair leaves no topological order to induct along. It does not overspend: the ceiling is enforced inside an attempt, not between attempts. Gates are measured against a two-tier held-out mutant corpus. We characterise two classes that let a sound-looking check pass anything: vacuity, satisfied by the absence of the thing checked, and self-attestation, where the subject supplies the value the check is applied to. On a 69-loop catalogue the instrument found 47 vacuous gates in shipped, reviewed code. Against the repaired gates it reports no false accepts over 209 destroying mutants ($\alpha \le 1.8\%$, Wilson 95%); that figure is saturation, not quality: freezing the gates and applying a fresh operator family recovers a 23.3% false-accept rate where the exhausted corpus reported none. A rate belongs to a specific gate; the apparatus, not our number, is the contribution. Engine, catalogue and corpus are Apache-2.0.

</details>


### [252] [Schrödinger's Code Repository: Have LLMs Learned SWE-bench or Memorized It?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27891)
*Silin Chen,Yufei Yang,Xiaodong Gu,Yuling Shi,Chengcheng Wan,Haibing Guan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出SchrodingerRepo评估框架，通过在评估时动态实例化仓库表示（而非使用固定的静态表示），来检验编码代理对SWE-bench等仓库级编码基准的真实推理能力，揭示了当前LLM部分依赖记忆的仓库侧线索而非真正的仓库推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓库级编码基准（如SWE-bench）建立在训练中反复使用的流行开源仓库之上，存在固有的数据泄漏问题。静态仓库表示使高性能可能反映的是对规范仓库线索（如命名约定、文件布局、实现模式）的记忆，而非真正的仓库推理能力，因此需要一种方法来区分两者。

Method: SchrodingerRepo将测试仓库视为评估时的潜在变量，在代理进入评估环境时才动态实例化。具体包含四个转换级别：Level1问题陈述重构（两阶段LLM改写+验证）、Level2命名空间映射（基于AST提取标识符，建立种子可控的可逆虚拟命名空间）、Level3文件内布局重排序（基于定义时依赖图的随机拓扑排序）、Level4功能保持的代码重写（约束重写代理生成行为等价的diff补丁）。通过可逆映射保证执行仍基于原始SWE-bench环境。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified上，全量转换使Pass@1下降6.0–14.4个百分点（统计显著性p<0.05），交互成本显著增加；Level2（命名空间映射）影响最大，Pass@1下降6.0–7.4个百分点，token使用增加超2.5倍；81.6–83.6%的额外动作用于探索性行为（navigate/search/read/probe）；该效应在SWE-QA上同样成立；在时间外推的SWE-rebench上，Pass@1保持不变（17.27%），说明任务本身难度未变，仅是代理对仓库线索的依赖被剥离。

Conclusion: 当前编码代理在SWE-bench上的强劲表现部分依赖于对规范仓库表示的记忆，而非纯粹的仓库级推理能力。SchrodingerRepo将仓库表示从固定基准工件变为评估时动态变量，为更可靠、可解释的仓库级编码代理评估提供了系统化的解决方案。未来工作应支持更丰富的仓库感知工具接口（IDE API、语言服务器查询等）。

Abstract: Repository-level coding benchmarks have become the standard for evaluating coding agents, yet they inherently suffer from data leakage because they are built upon popular open-source repositories repeatedly used for training. Consequently, strong performance may reflect memorization of canonical repository cues rather than robust repository reasoning. We propose SchrodingerRepo (Schrödinger's Repository), an evaluation framework for testing coding agents under dynamically instantiated repository representations. Instead of repeatedly using a static representation of the test repository, SchrodingerRepo treats the test repository as an evaluation-time latent variable that is dynamically instantiated only when the agent enters the evaluation environment. The instantiated repository preserves the original executable behavior while eroding familiar cues such as naming conventions, file layouts, and implementation patterns through four transformation levels: problem statement reconstruction, namespace remapping, intra-file layout reordering, and functionality-preserving code rewriting. We evaluate popular LLMs on SWE-bench Verified and SWE-QA. Results show that removing familiar repository cues consistently degrades agent performance and substantially increases interaction costs across models. Further analysis reveals that the additional cost is primarily caused by increased difficulty in repository exploration and localization. These findings suggest that current coding agents may partially rely on memorized repository-side cues, highlighting the need for evaluation under dynamically instantiated repository representations.

</details>


### [253] [From Agent Output to Authorized Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28216)
*Christopher Koch*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: This paper proposes the "Agile-V Assurance Spine," a cross-domain transition contract for software, firmware, and PCB engineering that shifts the assurance problem from "can an agent produce output" to "is an engineering lifecycle justified in acting on claims about that output." It defines a strict evidence-admission hierarchy, state-bound gate receipts, and authoritative source profiles to authorize lifecycle transitions (merge, deploy, flash, fabricate).


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The proliferation of agentic coding systems (OpenHands, Codex, Claude Code, Devin, etc.) means agents are no longer just suggestion engines but execution systems that can edit repositories, build firmware, and synthesize manufacturing artifacts. However, current tools (sandboxes, approvals, traces, SBOMs, attestations) remain fragmented and lack a shared, portable semantics for determining whether evidence actually establishes required engineering claims. Existing research shows that local test success or agent self-review does not equate to production-qualified change, and the field lacks approaches that simultaneously provide soundness, scalability, semantic correctness, and task-level safety preservation.

Method: The paper employs a bounded market and research review (closing 23 Sept 2026) covering ~10 coding-agent platforms, 6 AI-code governance products, observability stacks, policy engines, provenance systems, AI-BOM standards, protocols, and assurance-case standards. It then constructs a formal model consisting of: (1) a six-level admission hierarchy (present → structurally complete → sufficient → admissible → gate-eligible → effect-authorized); (2) core object definitions including exact subject state σ=(kind,ref,digest) and frozen verification baseline B=(R,C,ρ,π,P); (3) a capability-profile intersection rule TrustedProps(e)=properties(e)∩Cap(source(e)) that prevents evidence sources from self-authorizing their meaning; (4) admissibility constraints (subject/policy digest match, freshness, independence, environment binding); and (5) Gate Receipts that bind decisions to exact state, policy, and authority context. It applies these to software, firmware, and PCB profiles and maps to open-source implementations in agile_v_skills and agentic_agile_v repositories.

Result: The bounded landscape review found that no reviewed public system documents all eight required capabilities as one cross-platform contract: required claim properties, authoritative source capability profiles, exact subject/policy binding, contradiction-aware admission, risk-aligned independence/authority, explicit exception semantics, dependency-scoped revalidation, model/runtime/tool influence inventory, and atomic recheck at the effect boundary. The paper contributes: (1) a gap analysis positioning the Spine relative to Proof-or-Stop, DEMM, CAC, TAIP, AI-GRACE, Nool, OPA, SLSA, in-toto, and CycloneDX ML-BOM; (2) a property-level evidence-admission model; (3) a trusted transition contract with risk-aligned independence; (4) a compositional architecture that consumes rather than replaces existing tools; and (5) cross-domain profiles plus an adversarial evaluation agenda. The author explicitly disclaims regulatory conformity and demonstrated production superiority.

Conclusion: The Assurance Spine is best understood as a composition layer rather than a replacement product. Its core contribution is a portable transition contract that synthesizes property-level evidence admission, trusted source capabilities, exact state/policy binding, independence, exceptions, dependency revalidation, AI-influence inventory, and cross-domain effect profiles into one open reference model. By requiring capability-profile intersection (TrustedProps), the model blocks category errors where, for example, a unit-test adapter is treated as establishing human authority or a BOM as establishing requirement satisfaction. Gate Receipts make transition decisions explicit, falsifiable, and state-bound. The model does not eliminate trust bootstrap or make AI reviewers organizationally independent, but it provides a precise vocabulary and executable reference for the field. The paper closes by defining an adversarial conformance and empirical evaluation agenda for future work.

Abstract: Agentic engineering systems can edit repositories, run tools and tests, build firmware, synthesize schematics, and prepare deployable or manufacturable artifacts. The assurance problem is therefore shifting from whether an agent can produce an output to whether an engineering lifecycle is justified in acting on claims about that output. Current products and standards provide sandboxes, approvals, hooks, traces, policy enforcement, attestations, bills of materials, and assurance representations, but these capabilities remain fragmented. This paper presents the Agile-V Assurance Spine, a cross-domain transition contract for software, firmware, and PCB engineering.
