Evidence is admitted only when it establishes required properties through an authoritative source profile, is bound to the exact artifact and frozen policy baseline, remains current with respect to declared dependencies, and satisfies risk-appropriate independence and authority. Gate decisions are recorded as receipts; approvals and exceptions are exact-scope and time-bounded; and authorization is rechecked at the effect boundary before merge, deployment, flashing, release, or fabrication. A bounded review of contemporary research, commercial platforms, open-source infrastructure, and standards positions the model relative to evidence-gated lifecycle control, continuous assurance, runtime admission, provenance, and AI/ML inventories. The paper contributes a precise vocabulary, compositional architecture, domain profiles, mapping to open-source implementations, and an adversarial evaluation agenda. It does not claim regulatory conformity or demonstrated production superiority.

</details>


### [254] [Can LLMs Reason About Runtime Behavior? A Repository-Level Dynamic Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28449)
*Hamed Taherkhani,Mohammad Abdollahi,Melika Sepidband,Hridya Dhulipala,Tien N. Nguyen,Hadi Hemmati*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出了 SWE-Flux,一个用于评估大语言模型在仓库级别动态执行推理能力的基准,包含 480 个基于真实 Python 仓库测试执行自动采集的实例,覆盖七大类运行时行为。实验表明该任务对当前 LLM 仍极具挑战性,最佳模型准确率仅 37%,尤其在数据流、跨过程执行和套件级聚合方面表现欠佳。作者还展示了通过输入扰动自动生成更具挑战性变体的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仓库级 QA 基准主要评估静态代码理解,执行推理基准大多局限于函数片段,缺乏对真实仓库中动态运行时行为(控制流、数据流、状态、异常、不变式等)的系统化、执行确定性评估。LLM 在编码任务中被广泛使用,但其推理代码实际执行的能力尚不清晰,且执行环境可能不可用或不可信,因此需要模型本身具备基于源码推理运行时行为的能力。

Method: 1. 设计两层分类体系(单测试 vs 多测试,涵盖过程内/跨过程 CFG、循环、程序状态、数据流、异常、程序不变式七大类)。2. 从 SWE-bench-Live 选取 12 个真实 Python 仓库(含 SymPy、Keras、Matplotlib 等),固定 commit 与依赖。3. 五阶段构建流程:环境设置 → 实例设计(测试、问题、追踪配置、解析器)→ 容器内迭代调试 → 基于插桩执行的 Oracle 自动采集(每个实例从干净容器重新执行生成确定性 JSON 答案)→ 使用 Sonnet-4.6 代理做最终验证。4. 提出 LLM-Perturb 与 LLM-Perturb+Feedback 算法,通过修改测试输入自动生成新变体。5. 使用精确匹配评估 5 个 LLM(GPT-5.4、GPT-5.3-Codex、GPT-OSS-20B、Gemma-4-4B/31B),给 25 次工具调用、32K 上下文、temperature=0。

Result: 1. 最佳模型 GPT-5.4 仅 37.71%,GPT-5.3-Codex 33.75%,开源模型 17-21%。2. 模型在不变式(M7, 69%)、过程内 CFG(S1, 54%)、异常(S5, 42%)上表现较好;在数据流(S4/M4, 1-2%)、跨过程 CFG(S6 16%, M6 8%)、程序状态(S3 25%)上表现极差。3. 手动分析 299 个错误,93% 是实质性语义错误,而非格式问题。4. 输入扰动在 58 个选中实例中成功采集 52 个变体(89.7%),使 GPT-5.4 准确率从 100% 降至 43.1%,且未参与扰动的 Gemma4-31B、GPT-OSS-20B 也从约 47-48% 降至 24.1%,表明变体难度具有跨模型可迁移性。5. 发现七大失败模式:本地识别 vs 运行时预测差距、边界错误、数据流是最大难点、套件级需聚合而非外推、路径正确不等于状态正确、静态先验覆盖运行时证据。

Conclusion: SWE-Flux 证明仓库级动态执行推理仍是当前 LLM 的显著短板,尤其在数据流、跨过程执行、状态跟踪和多测试聚合方面。论文贡献包括:系统化的动态行为分类法、480 实例基准、可复现的 Oracle 自动采集流水线、五个 LLM 的详细评估、以及通过输入扰动自动生成更难变体的方法。未来工作应聚焦于:基于执行追踪的细粒度监督、显式执行边界控制、将程序分析信号(如动态追踪、reaching definition)与 LLM 结合以提升数据流推理、区分路径推理与状态推理、强制多测试聚合,以及减少静态先验偏差。仅覆盖 Python,向其他语言的泛化留作未来工作。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in coding tasks, but their ability to reason about code execution remains unclear. Existing repository-level QA benchmarks mainly evaluate static code understanding and often rely on LLM-based evaluation, while execution-reasoning benchmarks are mostly limited to snippets or functions. We introduce SWE-Flux, a repository-level benchmark for dynamic execution reasoning containing 480 execution-grounded instances across 12 real Python repositories, with gold answers automatically harvested from instrumented test executions rather than written manually or judged by LLMs. The benchmark covers singletest and multi-test questions over control flow, loops, program state, dataflow, exceptions, and program invariants. Evaluating five LLMs shows that this task remains challenging. The best model achieves only 37% accuracy. Models perform better on localized behavior such as invariants, intra-procedural control flow, exceptions, and simple loops, but struggle with dataflow, inter-procedural execution, precise state reasoning, and suite-level aggregation. Finally, we show that the oracle-harvesting pipeline can generate fresh benchmark variants using input perturbation. It successfully harvests valid variants for almost 90% of the selected instances, and the resulting variants are substantially more challenging for the evaluated models.

</details>


### [255] [A Hybrid Rule-Based and AI-Augmented Framework for Automatic Failure Recovery in DevOps Deployments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26838)
*Raju Chowdhury,Aniruddha Singh Gautam,Apurv Ghai*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合模型,将确定性规则驱动的故障恢复与机器学习故障预测相结合,用于DevOps环境中自动监控、识别、分类和恢复故障。实验基于10万条分布式日志数据,决策树在缺陷识别中表现最优(F1=80.8%),自动恢复成功率达83.3%,MTTR降低94.6%,年节省成本约7,462。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DevOps部署中系统复杂、工作负载高,传统基于规则或手动方式难以实现自动故障恢复;需要一个能够实时监测、预测并自动恢复的智能架构,以提升系统韧性和运营效率。

Method: 1. 设计混合架构:确定性规则引擎 + 机器学习模型协同工作;2. 使用10万条分布式日志数据集;3. 训练并对比多种模型:决策树、随机森林、逻辑回归、LightGBM、Autoencoder+BiLSTM;4. 评估指标包括F1、召回率、精确率、准确率、MTTR、停机时间和恢复成功率;5. 在受控实验环境下进行验证。

Result: 决策树在缺陷识别任务中表现最佳:F1=80.8%,召回率=80.0%,精确率=81.6%,准确率=89.4%。自动恢复措施成功率达83.3%,MTTR降低94.6%,停机时间减少95.4%,年度成本节省约7,462(单位未明)。该框架兼具可解释的规则逻辑和自适应AI预测能力。

Conclusion: 该混合框架整合了可解释规则逻辑与自适应AI预测,具有更强的韧性、运营效率和可扩展性,为DevOps环境提供了一种有效的自主故障管理方案,但实验结论基于受控实验条件,实际部署仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Automatic failure recovery is another difficult area of DevOps deployments due to the complexity of the system, high workload and the limitations of the traditional rules-based or manual approach. This study proposes a hybrid model integrating deterministic, rule-based recovery with the assistance of machine learning by fault prediction to automatically monitor failures, identify, classify and recover failures in real time. To test the architecture, a distributed log dataset of 100,000 records was used for models such as Decision Tree, Random Forest, Logistic Regression, LightGBM, Autoencoder with BiLSTM. The Decision Tree had a strong performance in defect identification with an F1 score of 80.8%, recall of 80.0%, precision of 81.6%, and accuracy of 89.4%. An impressive 83.3% success rate was achieved by the automated recovery measures, resulting in a 94.6% decrease in mean time to recovery (MTTR), a 95.4% reduction in downtime, and annual savings of 7,462. The combination of explainable rule-based logic and adaptive AI prediction in the framework means it is more resilient, operationally efficient, and scalable, and an effective, autonomous approach to DevOps environments evaluated under controlled experimental conditions.

</details>


### [256] [From PyTorch to the NPU: LLM-Agent-Driven Model Conversion Across Heterogeneous Inference Runtimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27249)
*Jianhao Su,Zhanwei Wu,Chia-Heng Tu,ShengTing Huang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文将AIPC（LLM智能体驱动的AI模型部署方法）从单一QAIRT运行时扩展到四种异构边缘推理运行时（Intel OpenVINO、Rockchip RKNN、NVIDIA TensorRT、ONNX Runtime QNN），提出基于"技能+验证循环"模式的单模型到单运行时自动化部署框架，并通过受控实验量化了结构化领域知识注入对智能体部署行为的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘AI部署涉及模型转换、算子兼容、运行时集成和精度验证等多阶段工程工作，且不同硬件推理运行时（OpenVINO/RKNN/TensorRT/ONNX Runtime QNN）在工具链、算子支持、量化方案和API上差异显著，存在大量重复且易出错的人工工程工作。作者将LLM智能体驱动的AIPC方法从单一运行时扩展到多运行时异构场景，旨在通过结构化知识注入和验证机制，将复杂的多运行时部署工作流转化为可重复、可验证、可改进的自动化流程。

Method: 提出AIPC多运行时扩展框架，核心包括：(1) 六阶段可验证部署流水线（基线建立→ONNX转换→运行时模型构建→精度对齐→优化量化→分析报告）；(2) "技能+验证循环"设计模式，通过Agent Skills封装工具链参考、算子兼容性矩阵、量化指南、常见失败模式、验证脚本和平台约束；(3) ONNXWrapper推理替换层，在ONNX层面自动执行NCHW↔NHWC布局适配，消除智能体手动插入转置的负担；(4) 三个层级修复机制（PyTorch源码修改、ONNX计算图修补、运行时错误兼容修复）。针对每个运行时（OpenVINO、RKNN、TensorRT、ONNX Runtime QNN）详细说明单阶段/双阶段转换路径、内联量化与转换后独立量化的差异、设备无关与设备特定输出格式，并提供算子兼容性处理、动态/固定形状转换和布局适配的具体策略。

Result: 使用Real-ESRGAN（结构规则的CNN超分模型）和YOLO-World（多编码器开放词汇检测模型）作为测试目标，进行两组对照实验。实验1（带AIPC技能）：8/8（100%）成功，平均耗时19.5分钟，3次人工干预；实验2（不带技能）：3/5（60%）成功率，RKNN部署需要手动提示NHWC布局转换、ONNX Runtime QNN测试曾出现NPU实际未激活但智能体报告成功的误判。关键发现：(1) 结构化知识注入显著提升部署成功率，智能体失败模式与部署知识掌握程度高度相关而非模型能力本身；(2) AI智能体在工程任务中普遍存在漂移与幻觉倾向，无论是否注入技能，复杂模型和多步工作流下偏离预定流程的现象更严重（如误判TensorRT FP16支持、跳过ONNXWrapper布局适配、未授权升级PyTorch版本等）；(3) ONNXWrapper有效降低布局相关转换失败，但仅靠智能体自主决策不足以保证可靠性，需要更严格的分阶段验证、输出比对和异常检测机制。

Conclusion: 本文证明"技能+验证循环"设计模式可推广到多种异构推理运行时，LLM智能体在结构化知识注入下能够有效承担多运行时部署中的模型转换、算子修复和精度验证工作；ONNXWrapper将布局适配从智能体修复负担中解耦。受控实验表明，智能体失败模式可归因于领域知识可用性而非模型能力本身。但当前仍存在局限：仅覆盖两个视觉模型、未系统验证Transformer/LLM等更复杂架构、缺乏跨运行时算子修复策略的系统比较、未实施量化和优化测试、仅在MIMO v2.5和DeepSeek V4 Pro Preview两种智能体模型上验证。未来的研究需要在更大模型覆盖范围、跨平台算子修复机制比较以及更严格的智能体偏差约束机制上展开系统工作。

Abstract: Edge AI model deployment is a multi-stage engineering process involving model conversion, operator compatibility handling, runtime integration, and precision verification. While prior work has demonstrated agent-based automation for Qualcomm AI Runtime, the broader edge inference runtime ecosystem, including Intel OpenVINO, Rockchip RKNN, NVIDIA TensorRT, and ONNX Runtime, presents distinct toolchains and optimization strategies. This paper extends AIPC (AI Porting Conversion, an LLM agent-driven methodology for AI model deployment automation previously demonstrated on Qualcomm AI Runtime) to multi-runtime scenarios, proposing an LLM agent-driven approach for automated single-model-to-single-runtime deployment across heterogeneous inference backends, such as Intel OpenVINO, Rockchip RKNN, NVIDIA TensorRT, and ONNX Runtime. We decompose the edge AI deployment into standardized, verifiable stages, and inject runtime-specific domain knowledge into the agent execution flow through agent skills, auxiliary scripts, and staged verification loops. Using representative vision models, we demonstrate that agent-based deployment can complete the conversion from a PyTorch model to its executable inference, targeting OpenVINO for x86/NPU, RKNN for RK3588, TensorRT for NVIDIA GPU, and ONNX Runtime for Qualcomm NPU with a focus on FP16 precision deployment feasibility verification. The contributions of this paper primarily lie in providing multi-runtime deployment engineering practice experience, toolchain mapping analysis, a layout-adaptation and inference-replacement layer that removes manual transpose insertion from the agent's repair burden, and an empirical characterization of agent deviation behavior under structured knowledge injection, rather than large-scale systematic benchmarking or cross-runtime operator repair strategy comparison.

</details>


### [257] [Who Finishes the Job? A Study of Follow-Up Fixes and Commit Authorship on AI Coding Agent Pull Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26847)
*Wannita Takerngsaksiri,Nhat Duong,Scott Barnett*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了 AI 编码代理（如 Codex、Copilot、Devin、Cursor、Claude Code）提交的合并 PR 后续被修复的频率及修复作者归属问题。通过追踪 6,774 个代理 PR 与 5,044 个同仓库人类 PR 对照，发现代理 PR 被修复的几率是人类 PR 的 1.62 倍，且 69.6% 的修复仍由同一代理完成，表明代理在很大程度上"自我善后"，但其合并后需要修复的频率仍高于人类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究指出 AI 代理代码在合并后存在代码异味和静态分析问题，但合并后的修复频率及其作者归属尚未被量化。本研究旨在填补该空白，回答"代理 PR 多久被修复一次""谁来修复"以及"合并时代信号能否预测后续修复"三个问题。

Method: 基于 AIDev-pop 数据集（≥500 星的流行开源仓库），筛选 6,774 个合并代理 PR（涵盖五种代理）和 5,044 个同期人类 PR 作为基线。通过四项启发式规则提取候选修复 PR，由两名人类标注员和 LLM 法官（Claude Opus 4.8，κ=0.78 匹配人类 κ=0.77）验证为"直接修复"，并在 PR 层级与提交层级进行作者归因分析。

Result: RQ1：22.9% 代理 PR 有候选修复，4.5% 为已验证修复；同仓库同窗口内，代理被修复的几率是人类 PR 的 1.62 倍（Mantel-Haenszel OR），且修复多在首周内发生。RQ2：69.6% 已验证修复由同一代理完成（人类修复人类 PR 占 89.1%）。RQ3：已验证修复 PR 中平均 87.4% 的提交由代理完成，76.4% 的 PR 所有提交均为代理撰写。RQ4：合并时代信号差异极小，但在控制仓库与文件数后，提交数每增 10 倍，跟随修复几率增至 6.1 倍。

Conclusion: AI 代理合并的 PR 仍需要比人类 PR 更频繁的后续修复（约 1.62 倍），但修复工作主要由发起该合并的同一代理承担，延续了"所有者修复自有代码"的模式，仅是"所有者"变成了代理。这意味着 PR 评估不能止于合并，需延伸至合并后阶段，且当代理模型停用时代码维护将成为潜在风险。

Abstract: AI coding agents now author a large share of pull requests (PRs) merged into popular open-source projects. A merged agent PR is usually considered finished work; yet, prior studies have reported issues in agent code after the merge (e.g., code smells and static-analysis issues). However, little is known about how often a merged agent PR is fixed afterward, and who actually authors the fixing. In this paper, we follow 6,774 merged agent PRs across five AI coding agents (OpenAI Codex, GitHub Copilot, Devin, Cursor, and Claude Code) from the AIDev-pop dataset (open-source repositories with at least 500 stars) into their follow-up fixes, against a baseline of 5,044 contemporaneous human PRs from the same repositories. We link each merge to its candidate fixes, verify every candidate with human annotators and an LLM judge that matches human-level agreement (binary Cohen's Kappa=0.78$ against a human-human K=0.77$, Direct-fix precision 90%), and attribute the fixing work at the PR and the commit level. Our findings show that (1) merged agent PRs attract verified fixes at 1.62 times the odds of merged human PRs in the same repositories over the same period of time; (2) 69.6% of verified fixes in agent merges come from the same agent; and (3) 76.4% of the verified fix PRs are agent-authored throughout all commits. These results show that agents currently largely finish their own job, but their merges still require fixing more often than human merges.

</details>


### [258] [Kubernetes Misconfigurations in the Wild: Taxonomy, Evolution, and Automated Repair with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27030)
*Mostafa Anouar Ghorab,Ahmad Abdel Latif,Mohamed Aymen Saied*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文对 Kubernetes 安全配置错误进行了大规模实证研究，基于 2,662 个 Stack Overflow 问题构建了配置错误分类体系，并提出了结合 LLM 上下文推理与模式校验的混合自动修复框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kubernetes 声明式配置模型易引入安全配置错误，尽管已有检测工具，但配置错误模式与可扩展修复方法仍研究不足。理解错误演化规律与提升自动修复可靠性是核心动机。

Method: 1. 基于 2,662 个 Stack Overflow 问题进行实证分析，构建配置对象与类别的安全弱点分类法；2. 分析孵化期与稳定期项目间配置错误的严重性差异与演化趋势；3. 在渐进式上下文增强条件下评估 LLM 自动修复能力；4. 提出 Kubecurity 框架，将 LLM 推理与基于官方 Kubernetes 规范的确定性模式校验相结合。

Result: 研究发现关键安全配置错误在项目成熟过程中持续存在甚至重现；最佳独立 LLM 模型在上下文增强下达到 89.06% 修复准确率；Kubecurity 混合框架将修复准确率提升至 98.50%，并显著减少新引入的配置错误。

Conclusion: 该工作推进了对 Kubernetes 安全配置错误的理解，证明了结合 LLM 上下文推理与确定性模式强制的混合方法能够实现更可靠的自动化修复。

Abstract: Kubernetes is widely used to orchestrate cloud-native applications, yet its declarative configuration model often introduces security misconfigurations that threaten system reliability. Despite available detection tools, misconfiguration patterns and scalable remediation remain insufficiently understood. This paper presents an empirical study of Kubernetes security misconfigurations based on 2,662 developer-reported Stack Overflow issues. We derive a taxonomy of recurring security weaknesses across configuration objects and categories. We analyze severity variations and investigate how misconfigurations evolve between incubator and stable project stages. Findings show that while some operational issues decrease as projects mature, critical security misconfigurations often persist or reappear. We then evaluate Large Language Models (LLMs) for automated remediation under progressively enriched contextual conditions. Contextual grounding improves correction accuracy, with the best standalone model achieving 89.06%. To enhance structural correctness and schema compliance, we introduce Kubecurity, a schema-guided validation framework based on official Kubernetes specifications. Combining contextual LLM reasoning with deterministic schema enforcement achieves 98.50% correction accuracy while substantially reducing newly introduced misconfigurations. This work advances the understanding of Kubernetes security misconfigurations and demonstrates a hybrid approach to more reliable automated remediation.

</details>


### [259] [Stage-Supervised Latent Reasoning for Single-Shot JavaScript Deobfuscation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27058)
*Rong Feng,Suman Saha*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种阶段感知的潜在推理框架，将确定性去混淆工具(Webcrack)的中间输出作为监督信号，用于Coconut(连续思维链)模型的训练。训练时学习多阶段去混淆过程，推理时单步生成最终清理后的JavaScript代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的JavaScript去混淆方法通常将任务视为一步翻译问题，忽略了实际去混淆管线中的多阶段结构(如字符串编码、控制流平坦化、死代码注入等组合变换)。直接使用LLM进行单步去混淆往往生成看似合理但无法编译或语义不一致的代码。

Method: 1) 数据集：从JsDeObsBench选取100个混淆JavaScript程序(60/20/20划分)，运行修改版Webcrack记录五个阶段的中间输出 P₁→P₂→P₃→P₄→P₅(分别对应移除属性间接、内联包装函数、解码字符串、最终清理)。2) 训练：以(question, steps, answer)三元组形式组织数据，steps存储有序变换轨迹，每个过渡以diff形式编码；使用Coconut范式在连续潜在空间进行推理，逐步用潜在思维token替代显式中间推理。3) 模型：以Llama 3.2-3B为骨干，对比三种变体——零样本基座、No-CoT微调、Coconut微调；AdamW优化器，lr=1e-4，FSDP双A100训练。4) 评估：语法有效性(能否解析)+语义正确性(执行对比输出)。

Result: 在20个测试程序上，Coconut模型语法有效性达50%(No-CoT 15%，零样本25%)；在语法有效输出中语义正确率达80%(零样本40%，No-CoT 0%)，两项指标均最优。

Conclusion: 阶段级监督是改进LLM去混淆的有效方向，潜在推理能在训练时吸收多阶段过程、推理时保持单步生成。未来工作包括扩展基准、引入差分测试/符号执行等自动化语义验证、进行阶段监督与潜在推理贡献的消融研究，并探索该框架在恶意JavaScript分析中的应用。

Abstract: JavaScript obfuscation is widely used to protect code, but it also makes program analysis and security review substantially harder. Existing LLM-based deobfuscation methods usually treat the task as one-step translation, ignoring the staged structure of practical deobfuscation pipelines. This WIP paper proposes a stage-aware latent reasoning framework that converts intermediate outputs from a deterministic deobfuscation tool into supervision for Coconut-based training. The model learns from multi-stage rewrites during training but generates the final cleaned program in a single shot at inference time. Preliminary results on JsDeObsBench show that the Coconut-based model improves syntactic validity to 50%, compared with 15% for direct fine-tuning and 25% for a zero-shot baseline, and reaches 80% semantic correctness among valid outputs, indicating better semantic faithfulness than either comparison model in deobfuscation.

</details>


### [260] [Does Graph Structure Earn Its Place in Microservice Root-Cause Analysis? A Controlled Study on RCAEval, and What the Benchmark Was Really Measuring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27069)
*Imad Buljić*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文对微服务根因定位（RCA）领域中广泛使用的图神经网络（GNN）方法提出质疑。作者基于RCAEval基准，采用严格控制变量的对照实验，剥离除图结构以外的所有差异（特征、优化器、验证划分、早停、评分头），对比了三个学习模型：标准GNN编码器、去除邻居聚合项的同一编码器（gnn_no_neigh）、以及常规MLP。结果显示图结构在所测试的设置与体系下未带来可靠的增益（in-distribution仅领先0.003 Avg@5，p=0.844；跨系统反落后0.058）。同时审计揭示RCAEval的两个关键特性：注入故障仅覆盖每个系统的少量服务（5/n），使得一个不读遥测、仅靠"嫌疑先验"的排序器就能达到Avg@5=0.488；以及列结构非一致，导致大量RE1案例遥测被零填充。论文提出PSC-GRCA将候选评分分解为系统先验、遥测证据、图残差三部分，揭示大部分in-distribution增益来自先验项而非图结构。最终贡献一份12项的"图vs.平坦"消融实验检查清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究多次质疑GNN在微服务根因分析中的图结构贡献，但这些对比混淆了整条流水线的差异（编码器、特征、训练预算、预处理），无法判断图结构是"无用"还是"冗余"。本文旨在严格隔离"邻居访问"这一单一变量，检验图结构是否真正发挥作用，并审计RCAEval基准本身的属性。

Method: 1. 构造三个学习模型对比：gnn（标准GNN编码器）、gnn_no_neigh（同一编码器，仅去掉W_neigh·(Âh)项）、mlp（常规平坦MLP）；除该项外所有组件（特征、隐层宽度、dropout、优化器、早停、评分头、验证划分）共享。2. 在RCAEval RE1/RE2两个基准、两种拓扑来源（RE1中由相关性诱导；RE2中读取Kubernetes真实协同定位）、四种实验条件下运行。3. 使用6个不相交分层折（in-distribution）和3个留出系统（cross-system），配对Wilcoxon符号秩检验+Bootstrap区间。4. 审计"嫌疑先验"：仅基于训练时注入服务名称频率、不读遥测特征的排序器。5. 提出PSC-GRCA：分解为系统先验p_i、遥测证据e_i、图残差r_i，并对r施加零均值约束使其不吸收系统性偏移；附带先验交换惩罚作为守护项。6. 复现BARO（基准自带参考实现），对比简单极大偏差启发式。

Result: 1. 图vs.无邻居项：in-distribution +0.005（p=0.313），cross-system +0.101（p=0.25，未达显著）。 2. 图vs.MLP：in-distribution +0.003（p=0.844），cross-system -0.058。 3. 嫌疑先验（无遥测）in-distribution Avg@5=0.488（AC@5=0.997），cross-system跌至0.192；优于均匀随机基线0.137。 4. PSC-GRCA分层均值Avg@5=0.915（对照MLP 0.864、gnn_no_neigh 0.862，p=0.03125为n=6下两侧最小可能值）；消融显示先验单独0.488、无先验0.850、图残差单独0.800、证据单独0.853——in-distribution增益主要落在先验项而非图残差。 5. cross-system：PSC-GRCA全模型0.747 vs. MLP 0.671；先验项因服务名称不迁移而崩溃至0.079。 6. RE2盲点模拟：100%掩蔽下反而是gnn_no_neigh最高（0.104），真实协同定位拓扑图最低（0.000）。 7. BARO vs.极大偏差启发式：Online Boutique 0.880 vs. 0.877（差异<0.004），但Sock Shop与Train Ticket因原始cAdvisor列数膨胀而分歧显著，归因为模式/列映射效应而非质量差异。

Conclusion: 在严格协议匹配下，图结构在RCAEval的四种实验条件与两种基准上未产生可靠效应。区分模型表现的并非是否访问图，而是模型是否被学习以及测试的是哪种"困难条件"。更持久的贡献是审计发现：RCAEval RE1中一半左右的in-distribution Avg@5可由一个完全不读遥测的排序器获得（因为注入仅覆盖每系统5个服务且指标为Avg@5）；该先验在跨系统时崩溃，为迁移缺口提供了一个候选解释机制；非一致的列模式会悄然清零多数RE1案例的遥测。PSC-GRCA将先验分离后，揭示其增益主要来自先验项而非图残差，因此该模型的诚实解读是"使归因可见"，而非为图结构正名。论文最终给出12项检查清单，用于该类"图vs.平坦"消融研究。

Abstract: Graph neural networks dominate recent work on microservice root-cause analysis, yet recent results question whether the graph contributes. Those results compare whole pipelines, so when a flat model wins one cannot tell whether structure is useless or redundant. We run the comparison they imply on RCAEval: three learned arms with identical features, optimiser, validation split, early-stopping rule and scoring head, in which a single term separates the graph arms. Across two RCAEval benchmarks, two topology sources and four regimes we find no reliable graph-specific effect: in-distribution the graph model leads the conventional flat model by 0.003 Avg@5 (p = 0.844, n = 6 disjoint folds). Auditing the pipeline surfaced two benchmark properties that condition any result on it. RCAEval injects faults into only five services per system while exposing 12 to 70 in telemetry, and the headline metric is Avg@5: a ranker reading no telemetry at all places the true culprit in the top five on 99.7 percent of held-out incidents, scoring Avg@5 0.488. That prior, not the uniform-random 0.137, is the honest in-distribution floor, and it collapses to 0.192 across systems. The second property is a non-uniform column schema that silently zeroes telemetry for most RE1 cases. We reproduce a published baseline, BARO, RCAEval's own reference implementation; on the one system with a clean schema it reaches similar aggregate accuracy to our heuristic, within 0.004, under a different scoring rule. The audit motivated a new model. PSC-GRCA separates a candidate score into a system prior, telemetry evidence and a centred graph residual, and reaches mean Avg@5 0.915 against 0.864 for the flat baseline, while its ablations locate most of the gain in the prior term rather than the graph. We close with a twelve-item checklist for graph-versus-flat ablation studies, distilled from sixty-two recorded defects.

</details>


### [261] [Verified Learning for Compiler Optimization: An LLM-Guided Architecture with Formal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27214)
*Dev Pratap Singh,Rong Feng,Suman Saha*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"验证学习"（Verified Learning）编译器优化架构，将微调的Code LLaMA作为IR级重写提案生成器，将Alive2形式化验证器作为外部运行时控制层，二者通过反馈循环耦合。以LLVM IR的lazification（惰性化）优化为案例研究，在LLVM test suite上验证了该架构能够保证语义正确性（无违规），9.8%的基准测试达到与Wyvern相当或更优的运行时性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统编译器优化依赖手工编写的启发式规则，跨程序和架构的泛化能力差。已有数据驱动方法（如MLGO、LLM Compiler）虽然提升了性能预测能力，但缺乏变换层面的形式化正确性保证，在生产工具链中难以安全部署。核心挑战在于如何将学习能力与形式化保证相结合，使生成式AI组件能够嵌入关键基础设施系统而不损害可靠性。

Method: 1) 从8个开源C库（cJSON、libevent、libtommath、sqlite、zlib、libconfuse、libgit2、lz4）中收集2,307个函数，由Wyvern生成lazification变换对作为监督数据，同时保留未变换的函数以学习变换决策边界。2) 基于Code LLaMA，使用LoRA微调（8K上下文窗口，学习率1e-5，batch size 2，3个epoch，bfloat16精度），学习从未优化IR到Wyvern变换IR的映射。3) 推理时，LLM生成候选lazification重写，由Alive2进行SMT驱动的符号等价性验证；验证失败时将诊断信息反馈给模型触发重新生成，形成闭环。4) 在LLVM test suite（8,422个函数）上进行分布外评估，测量运行时、二进制大小、结构指标和收敛行为。

Result: 在LLVM test suite上：1) 正确性：零语义违规，所有候选重写均通过Alive2验证。2) 运行时：LLM平均运行时间比Wyvern高97%，但9.8%的基准达到可比或更优性能；微调后比非微调基线降低2.5%。3) 二进制大小：比Wyvern平均减小9.5%，10.8%的基准更紧凑。4) 结构行为：模型倾向保守重写，指令数增加约1.3倍，Loads增加达3.8倍（来自thunk/memoization脚手架），但控制流拓扑基本保持不变。5) 收敛性：19个初始失败程序均在2-9次迭代内收敛（均值5.32，标准差2.08），无发散，每次验证平均延迟2.46ms。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了"验证学习"架构的可行性：将正确性作为外部运行时控制层而非模型隐式学习能力，使生成式AI组件能够安全嵌入编译器流水线。虽然LLM在原始性能上未超越Wyvern启发式方法，但其保守、可靠性导向的优化行为更适合生产工具链。该架构提供了一个可复用的设计模式——确定性验证与结构化反馈约束生成式行为——为将自适应生成模型集成到需要正确性、稳定性与部署安全性的软件工程流水线提供了可扩展蓝图。

Abstract: Compiler optimizations traditionally rely on handcrafted heuristics that often fail to generalize across programs and architectures. We investigate whether large language models can participate in compiler optimization through a verification-centered systems architecture that couples generative rewriting with formal equivalence checking. Using lazification in LLVM IR as a case study, we fine-tune a code-centric LLM on transformations produced by Wyvern and embed Alive2 into a feedback loop that enforces semantic preservation for every generated rewrite. Correctness is enforced externally as a runtime control layer rather than learned implicitly. During inference, candidate transformations are symbolically validated and regenerated when necessary, ensuring accepted rewrites satisfy formal constraints. On the LLVM test suite, the fine-tuned model reproduces core optimization behaviors while applying fewer transformations overall. Although Wyvern remains faster on most benchmarks, 9.8% achieve comparable or improved runtime under the learned system, with no semantic violations observed. Verification overhead remains bounded and convergence stable. These results demonstrate that generative AI components can be safely integrated into compiler pipelines through deterministic validation and structured feedback, offering a scalable architectural pattern for trustworthy AI-driven software infrastructure.

</details>


### [262] [Specifying and Maintaining Agentic Workflows: An Empirical Study of GitHub Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27263)
*Jasem Khelifi,Issam Oukhay,Ali Ouni,Mohammed Sayagh,Mohamed Aymen Saied*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对 GitHub Agentic Workflows (gh-aw) 的 Markdown 源文件进行了大规模实证研究，分析了 1,248 个文件、276 个仓库、20,841 次提交文件变更以及 294 个文件的指令标签，揭示了开发者如何编写、维护和复用这些面向 AI 代理的自动化工作流规范，并构建了一个包含 10 类 42 子类的指令分类法，评估了 LLM 自动标注的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 AI 编码代理的广泛采用，软件自动化正从一次性提示转向由代理持续运行的循环工作流。gh-aw 允许开发者用 Markdown 编写结合自然语言指令与执行配置的代理工作流，但开发者如何结构化、维护这些规范，以及表达了哪些执行需求和安全防护，目前理解不足。本文填补这一空白，为工作流审查和指令分析工具提供实证基础。

Method: 采用基于 GitHub REST API 代码搜索的仓库选择（≥10 stars 的项目），从中提取 1,295 个 Markdown 源文件并清理得到 1,248 个文件；通过映射生成 YAML 与源 Markdown 的关系并收集提交历史；使用 Cochran 公式抽取 294 个文件样本，通过两位作者的主题分析（四步法）构建指令分类法；用项目配对符号检验加 Holm 校正分析 4 个月维护演变；在 288 个已解析标签文件上评估 Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview、GLM-5.2 和 GPT-OSS-120B 在零样本到 5 样本设置下的多标签分类性能。

Result: （1）gh-aw 文件指令体中位数 556.5 词，62.1% 含代码块，中位 Flesch 阅读易度 38.8（属于"较难"范围）；（3）78.2% 的文件在第 4 个月仍被更新，规模归一化的 churn 在第 1 个月后下降；（4）中位数 74.5% 的项目级维护变更触及 frontmatter；（5）分类法 10 大类（任务、输出、约束、流程、资源、上下文、评估、示例、通信、安全）中，任务 (97.6%)、输出 (95.8%)、约束 (93.8%)、流程 (93.4%) 普遍存在，但仅 9.4% 明确涉及提示注入防御；（6）Gemini 1-shot 取得最高 F1=0.818，Cohen's κ=0.715；最大分歧出现在 Domain Context 标签。

Conclusion: 本文证明 gh-aw Markdown 文件是结合可执行配置与自然语言代理指令的实质性维护工件。研究者与实践者应将跨项目复制或引用的工作流视为演化的外部依赖加以监控；开发者应在适当处添加提示注入防御、资源预算和证据可信度检查；研究者应通过受控消融实验评估指令模式对代理执行效果的影响。

Abstract: Agentic workflows shift software development from prompting AI agents for individual tasks to defining recurring work that agents execute automatically. GitHub Agentic Workflows (gh-aw) enables this approach through Markdown files that combine natural-language instructions with configuration and compile into executable GitHub Actions workflows. Unlike conventional workflows that primarily prescribe scripted operations, these files delegate tasks requiring interpretation to AI agents. They also couple agent instructions with execution triggers, making those instructions operational specifications for repeated repository activities. However, how developers structure and maintain these specifications, and which execution requirements and safeguards they express, remains insufficiently understood. In this paper, we examine the structure, evolution, and instruction content of gh-aw Markdown files to inform how practitioners define and maintain agent-run work. We analyze 1,248 files from 276 repositories, 20,841 commit-file events, and 288 resolved instruction-label sets from a sample of 294 files. Our results show that workflow instructions extend beyond short prompts, with a median of 556.5 words and code blocks in 62.1% of files. Among files with at least 120 days of observed activity, 78.2% still receive updates in month 4, while size-normalized churn decreases after the first month. Tasks, outputs, constraints, and process instructions each appear in over 93% of labeled workflows, yet only 9.4% explicitly address prompt-injection defense. LLM classification achieves an F1 score of 0.818 and Cohen's Kappa of 0.715 against the resolved human labels. These findings suggest that developers should account for the evolution of workflows copied or referenced across repositories and consider adding prompt-injection defenses, resource budgets, and evidencecredibility checks where applicable.

</details>


### [263] [FairTest: Search-Based Fairness Testing for Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27309)
*Xiaotong Wang,Xuan Xie*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FairTest 是一个针对多智能体强化学习（MARL）策略的搜索式公平性测试框架，通过结合遗传搜索引导与Pareto测试优先级排序，在有限的测试预算内高效发现不公平执行。相比四个基线方法（Random、GMT、MASTest、STARLA），在三种环境（MPE、LBF、Predator-Prey）和两种MARL算法（QMIX、IQL）上检测到的公平性失败数量具有统计显著性的提升，平均超过最强基线221%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MARL训练目标是最大化团队回报，但高回报并不保证智能体间的奖励分配公平。现有公平性研究集中在训练阶段（添加约束或优化奖励分配），无法发现未见过状态下的不公平执行；现有测试方法主要针对单智能体DRL或MAS功能失败，缺乏专门针对MARL公平性的测试方法。

Method: 方法包含两大组件：(1) 搜索引导：使用三个适应度函数——测量的JFI公平性指数f1、基于抽象状态和公平性特征的随机森林预测公平性f2、基于DeepGini的决策不确定性f3；通过基于抽象状态匹配的交叉和状态扰动的变异产生新候选。(2) Pareto测试优先级：对f2和f3进行非支配排序和拥挤距离计算，在有限预算内将执行集中在最可能失败的高不确定性且预测不公平的候选上。失败判定通过实际执行后JFI低于阈值θ来确认。

Result: 在三种环境（MPE、LBF、Predator-Prey）和两种算法（IQL、QMIX）共6种配置下，FairTest在所有配置中检测的公平性失败数均超过Random、GMT、MASTest和STARLA四个基线，统计显著性p<0.001且A12=1.000（大型效应）。平均失败数超最强基线221%，覆盖率平均提升23%。消融实验证明：公平性特征使预测器AUC-ROC提升至0.997（IQL）和0.955（QMIX）；三个适应度函数均提供有效指导；Pareto优先级带来约4倍的失败检测增益。

Conclusion: FairTest将MARL公平性测试从训练时优化拓展到部署前验证，填补了公平性测试的研究空白。未来工作包括将检测到的失败反馈用于策略再训练的公平性增强，以及通过根因分析定位导致公平性失败的子回合和责任智能体。

Abstract: Multi-agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) trains a team of agents that share one environment and learn their policies together. Training maximizes the team return, and a high return does not imply that the rewards are shared fairly among the agents in every episode. Testing is an established way to discover the failures of deep reinforcement learning, yet few methods address the fairness of MARL. In this work, we propose FairTest, a search-based testing approach that seeks the unfair executions of a MARL policy. The design combines search guidance with test prioritization. The guidance scores each candidate with three fitness functions. One measures the fairness of the runs already performed, another predicts the fairness from abstract states and fairness features, and the third reads the decision uncertainty from the policy. Crossover and mutation derive further candidates from the observed executions. The prioritization ranks the candidates by the predicted fairness and the decision uncertainty, so that the runs reach the candidates where failures are expected. FairTest is evaluated on three environments and two MARL algorithms, and four baselines are given the same budget. It detects the most fairness failures compared to three baselines with statistical significance and large effect sizes. The failure count exceeds that of the strongest baseline by 221% on average and coverage improves by an average of 23%.

</details>


### [264] [Unity Insight: A Production Code--Asset Index for LLM Coding Agents in Unity Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27585)
*Shenhua Gu,Hongqiang Zhu,Fan Zhang,Jinming Zhang,Hao Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍 Unity Insight——首个面向 LLM 编码代理的、生产环境部署的 Unity 项目跨文件"代码—资产"持久化索引，已随 Tuanjie Codely（团结引擎的智能体 CLI）于 2026-07-28 公开发布。配对实验（28 个项目特异问题，两个 Unity 游戏，相同模型与 harness）表明，使用该索引的代理相比通用探索代理节省 53% token 和 52% 墙钟时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 Unity 项目中，应用行为与序列化资产（prefab、scene、ScriptableObject、.meta 中的 GUID）紧密耦合。现有 LLM 代理仅依赖 shell 工具与纯代码索引，无法回答跨文件的引用链问题（"哪个 prefab 实例化了该脚本？哪个 scene 使用了该材质？"），需逐跳 grep 拼接，token 成本高昂。

Method: 1. 爬取层：解析 C# 源码与序列化资产（scene、prefab、ScriptableObject、.meta），构建 GUID↔路径映射、组件—脚本语义绑定、嵌套 prefab 实例链，写入只读 SQLite 存储；按需构建、增量刷新、默认关闭、永不修改工程。2. 查询层：暴露 5 个类型化工具（vfs_ls、vfs_glob、vfs_grep、vfs_read、vfs_refs），返回紧凑、schema 稳定的结构化结果而非冗长 grep 输出。3. 已知盲点（如 Resources.Load 动态加载）显式披露，不静默遗漏。

Result: 配对实验（28 题 × 2 臂 × 1 次运行）：unity-insight 臂相对 explore 臂减少 53.4% 总 token、43.5% 模型输出 token、51.6% 墙钟时间、26.3% 工具调用、22.0% 模型轮次。精确配对符号检验：23/28 题目更省 token（p=0.0009）、24/28 输出更少（p=0.0002）、22/28 耗时更少（p=0.0037）、21/26 工具调用更少（p=0.0025）。unity-insight 全部 375 次调用走类型化查询工具，无回退原始文件 I/O；explore 臂 509 次调用包含 1 次因超出上下文窗口触发的 41.8K token 历史压缩。典型样例（RedRunner Chest Big prefab 依赖查询）：索引臂 3 次引用查询、38,901 token、10.4 秒；探索臂 15 次文件操作、194,486 token、176 秒（5.0× 与 16.9× 差距）。

Conclusion: Unity Insight 已在团结引擎 Tuanjie Codely 中作为内置代码—资产索引层投产，五个查询工具构成代理在 Unity 仓库中的原生导航界面。"持久只读代码—资产图谱 + 类型化查询工具"这一方案可推广到任何"决定性事实存在于序列化资产而非代码"的引擎，预计资产感知索引将成为游戏仓库代理基础设施的标准组件，如同主流代码仓库的语言服务器。

Abstract: LLM coding agents increasingly operate inside game-engine repositories, where application logic is inseparable from serialized assets: a single gameplay change may span C\# scripts, prefabs, scenes, and ScriptableObjects wired together by Unity GUIDs. The retrieval tools agents carry today---shell utilities and code-only indexes---cannot answer basic cross-file questions, because these relationships live in \texttt{.meta} files and YAML assets rather than in code. We present Unity Insight, to our knowledge the first persistent, LLM-facing, agent-integrated cross-file code--asset index for Unity projects, shipping in production with Tuanjie Codely, the agent CLI of Tuanjie Engine, since its public launch on 2026-07-28. In a paired experiment---28 project-specific questions on two Unity games, same model and harness, one run per arm per question---the index-backed agent spent 53\% fewer tokens and 52\% less wall-clock time than a general-purpose exploration agent (exact paired sign tests, $p{<}0.004$), using only its typed index-query tools.

</details>


### [265] [AST-Based Automated Elimination of break and continue Statements in Java Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27627)
*Andrés Juárez,José Chicano,Rubén Saborido*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于Eclipse JDT API的AST驱动的Java重构工具,通过引入辅助布尔变量(`stay`/`keep`)将循环中的`break`和`continue`语句替换为语义等价的条件控制结构,从而消除自动化Extract Method重构的障碍。工具通过五阶段多通道AST改写流程处理所有Java循环类型及复杂的try-catch-finally异常处理场景,在139个开源项目方法、54个手工测试用例和151个单元测试上验证了100%的语法正确性和行为等价性,并使约15%原本无法自动重构的方法可被后续Extract Method工具成功处理,最终实现平均58%的SSCC降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件维护占工程总投入的50%-75%,可维护性至关重要。SonarSource认知复杂度(SSCC)以方法为单位衡量代码可理解性,推荐阈值SSCC≤15。Extract Method是降低SSCC的核心策略,但IDE的自动化Extract Method在遇到跳出选定代码块的`break`和`continue`语句时会中止重构。此前基于搜索的研究表明,27%无法重构的目标方法正是因此类非结构化跳转语句而受阻。LLM基于概率生成,无法保证语义保留,常产生微妙的控制流缺陷。因此需要确定性、基于AST的工具来安全消除跳转语句,为后续自动化重构铺路。

Method: 采用应用型构造性研究方法,基于Eclipse JDT框架和Java SE 21实现Eclipse插件。核心为五步流程:(1)AST节点检测,识别循环内break/continue;(2)上下文分析,确定循环类型、嵌套深度及异常作用域;(3)控制变量分配,引入布尔状态标志`stay`(break)和`keep`(continue);(4)循环与块重构,将后续语句包裹在if条件块内;(5)跳转替换,将break/continue替换为标志赋值。架构上采用基于ASTVisitor的多通道解耦设计:Phase 1结构规范化(for/foreach→while);Phase 2流与异常分析;Phase 3循环体重构;Phase 4再分析;Phase 5最终语义重写。`for`循环需先去糖化为`while`,`foreach`转换为迭代器驱动的`while`。`try-catch-finally`中由于finally必须在循环退出前执行,需要多个独立标志位(如`stay1`用于try作用域、`stay2`用于finally作用域),通过动态分配n元组标志管理任意深度嵌套。验证采用三层策略:54个手工分析追踪测试、151个JUnit单元测试(99%指令覆盖、90%分支覆盖)、以及7个开源项目139个方法上的集成测试(运行原生测试套件)。评估SSCC指标在三个阶段的变化:原始代码→break/continue消除后→Extract Method后。

Result: 三层验证全部通过:54个手工追踪测试实现100%行为等价;151个单元测试100%通过,JaCoCo覆盖率达99%指令覆盖和90%分支覆盖;139个开源项目方法全部成功编译,原生测试套件100%通过(如MOEAFramework 22个指标测试、jMetal 31个算法测试、fastjson 7个JSONPathTest均通过)。发现并修复了四个边缘案例缺陷:扁平化if块的词法作用域问题、for转while时continue存在导致的更新器无限循环、通配符导入导致的类型解析null回退、多标志作用域管理。SSCC影响:转换本身使SSCC小幅增加(各项目不超过11%),属中间结构成本。在138个方法的双重重构评估中:66例原始代码即可重构;65例原始代码失败,其中10例(约15%)经转换后Extract Method可成功找到方案并使SSCC低于15阈值;但有7例原本可成功的方法经转换后失败。整体而言,该转换使约15%原先不可行的方法提取变为可能,平均实现58%的SSCC降低。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个生产级、确定性、AST驱动的Eclipse JDT插件,填补了IDE重构工具目录中的空白——即通过显式布尔状态变量消除Java循环中break和continue语句,同时严格保证语义等价。该方法解决了Extract Method自动重构中非局部跳转造成的硬约束,标志着从孤立重构到组合式重构流水线范式的转变。虽然单独的转换会临时小幅增加局部SSCC(引入条件封装),但它使后续自动化重构成为可能,恢复了在约15%原先失败案例中的可行解,并最终通过Extract Method实现平均58%的SSCC降低,验证了"控制流规范化作为预处理步骤"的工程价值。局限性包括:仅评估SSCC而未涉及架构耦合/内聚指标;未测量布尔标志引入的运行时开销;第二阶段SSCC降幅受限于外部IDE启发式。局限还排除了极罕见的边缘场景(内层try中的break被内层finally抛出且被外层catch捕获的异常抑制)。未来工作包括:支持标签化控制退出、跨语言移植(C#/C++/Python/TypeScript)、嵌入IDE内SSCC反馈循环、与搜索式软件工程(SBSE)框架集成、以及面向软件工程教学的可视化工具。

Abstract: This work presents the development of an automatic refactoring tool for Java code built on top of the Eclipse JDT API. The proposed approach transforms control structures containing break and continue statements within different types of loops into semantically equivalent constructs that avoid their explicit use. To achieve this, auxiliary boolean variables are introduced to restructure the control flow while preserving the original program behavior. The main objective of this transformation is to improve code structure and enable the application of subsequent automated refactorings, particularly those based on the Extract Method operation, which are typically restricted by the presence of jump statements. The implementation relies on the analysis and rewriting of the Abstract Syntax Tree (AST), ensuring semantic equivalence in all addressed scenarios. The tool was validated through 54 manually designed test cases and 151 units tests, all of which produced satisfactory results. In addition, it was applied to 139 methods from seven open-source projects, generating code without compilation errors and preserving the original behavior as verified by the projects' test suites. The results demonstrate that the proposed approach safely automates the restructuring of code containing break and continue statements, facilitating further evolution and structural analysis.

</details>


### [266] [Foundations of Algebraic Architecture Theory: A Rising Sea of Geometry, Transport, Comparison, and Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27638)
*Hiroyuki Nakahata*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"代数架构理论"（AAT），建立了一种用于分析AI生成软件更改的数学框架。该理论基于原子（Atoms）和定律（Laws）的概念，定义了架构对象及其结构保持态射。AAT的核心是重构定理，证明了完整几何的范畴与局部模型的范畴等价。该理论解决了粘贴、诊断、传输、更改分类和重构五大决策问题，并应用于lens和protocol语义。论文形式化地从有限原子族构造核心和几何，建立了一阶Čech障碍理论，证明了规范传输的万有性质，并给出了比较的归一化分解。所有结果已在Lean定理证明器中形式化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI生成的软件更改越来越多，需要数学基础来判定：(1) 更改保留了哪些结构；(2) 局部一致性在哪里无法扩展到全局；(3) 在允许的更改中还剩下多少选择空间。现有方法缺乏统一的框架来处理这些问题。

Method: 代数架构理论（AAT）从原子（Atoms，类型化基本事实）和定律（Laws，等式约束）出发，定义架构对象的"读法"（明确选择什么算作结构，以及哪些操作和定律必须保留）。核心构造包括：(1) 从有限原子族构造核心（闭合于操作的最小族）和完整几何（配置位点和系数）；(2) 构造一阶Čech粘贴障碍，在torsor和sheaf条件下其消失等价于全局状态存在；(3) 建立沿精确更改的规范传输及其万有性质（强opcartesian性）；(4) 构造Beck-Chevalley伴随，通过交换子、单元和余单元比较传输与拉回；(5) 引入Idempotent完成（Karoubi包络）分析归一化后的比较；(6) 使用条件C（涵盖支持协议一致、纤维连通、边提升、循环、面提升、唯一性、自环反射等七条）证明诊断不变性；(7) 通过有限枚举将均匀不变性判定问题归约到子集核与上核的计算。Laws独立编码lens和protocol语义，证明其与类型态射范畴之间的等价关系。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 重构定理：完整几何及其所有结构保持态射的范畴等价于由原始数据和独立定义的相容条件给出的局部模型范畴；(2) Čech障碍与修复语义的对应定理，在同一修正语义条件下障碍类通过系数同构对应；(3) 沿精确更改的规范传输定理，证明核心和几何的强opcartesian性及其单元、复合的相干性；(4) 比较分类定理：生成的比较β=Eα可逆当且仅当归一化因子为恒等，同一比较在Kar(E)中给出像之间的同构；(5) 均匀不变性的有限判定定理及条件C充分性证明；(6) 对lens和protocol的形式化编码与对应定理；(7) 应用：分类和计数操作保留的更改，并从有限表唯一延拓态射。所有结果已在Lean定理证明器中形式化。

Conclusion: 代数架构理论为软件架构的形式化分析提供了坚实的数学基础，统一处理了粘贴、诊断、传输、分类和重构五大决策问题。读法（reading）的显式化明确了保留问题的语义。重构定理建立了局部描述与全局对象之间的等价关系，这在团队协作设计场景中具有重要意义。诊断的不变性、传输的万有性质以及归一化比较的分解定理为处理AI生成更改中的局部-全局关系提供了系统工具。Lean形式化验证了理论的一致性和可靠性。该框架有望在代码审查、设计迁移和系统重构等实际软件工程场景中得到应用。

Abstract: AI-generated software changes make it increasingly important to determine what a change preserves, where local consistency fails to extend globally, and which alternatives remain. We develop the foundations of Algebraic Architecture Theory (AAT) from Atoms, typed primitive facts, and Laws, equations that objects must satisfy. A reading specifies what counts as structure and which operations and laws to preserve. The main reconstruction theorem identifies the category of full geometries and all their structure-preserving morphisms with an independently defined category of local models, up to equivalence. Objects are recovered up to isomorphism and morphisms between fixed endpoints uniquely. The theory addresses gluing, diagnosis, transport, classification of changes, and reconstruction. From finite Atom families we construct cores closed under operations and geometries with sites and coefficients. We give conditions under which a Cech obstruction detects the existence of a global state and, through comparison with repair semantics, a global repair. We compare diagnoses and give a finite criterion for uniform invariance given computable finite data. Transport along exact changes has a universal property and commutes with base change on exact pointed pullback squares. Comparisons of routes generated from the same square, finite comparison diagram, and geometry factor into an invertible comparison and an idempotent normalization. We characterize when observations determine comparison preservation and classify compatible lifts. Encodings of lens and protocol semantics preserve and reflect laws and recover semantics-preserving morphisms. Applications classify and count operation-preserving changes and extend morphisms uniquely from finite tables. Corresponding Lean declarations are listed in the appendix.

</details>


### [267] [Satisfaction Is Not Explanation: Auditing Vacuity and Training Influence in Temporal-Logic-Guided Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27743)
*Lorenzo Bacchiani*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对时序逻辑引导的强化学习，提出一个"审计层"，揭示仅报告满意度概率和任务回报会掩盖策略真正依赖规约条款的方式。论文定义了六种"机制"（matched excess、invariance、attenuation、reward/satisfaction tradeoff、learnability/stability split、ablation hazard），并证明了常用的"弱化条款并重训"训练影响测试在标准接受度奖励下可能因共享最优策略而失效。最后，通过标准基准、已发布外部工件（Shield-RL、AI Safety Gridworlds）和173个已发布规约的语料库验证审计层的可分离性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序逻辑引导的RL策略即使满足规约并报告高满意度，也可能并未真正依赖该规约：条款可能从未被触发、被环境强制、或与原生任务奖励冗余。满意度概率无法区分这些情况，导致部署前的保障论证不完整。

Method: 审计层基于三个问题展开：(1) 策略是否满足公式？(2) 审计条款在成功轨迹上是否真的起作用？(3) 什么解释该条款的状态？方法上，通过公式渐进（progression）评估、计算条件空虛度（conditional vacuity）分数 V_ψ、环境下限（environmental floor）、匹配参考超量（matched-reference excess）；并设计"训练影响有效性筛选"，证明在标准稀疏接受度/势函数塑形奖励下，可比弱化/未弱化目标可共享完美最优策略集（定理1），需采用真正独立的目标作为正向对照。

Result: 在标准RL基准（FrozenLake、Taxi、OfficeWorld）、Shield-RL工件（Water Tank、SGW9、SGW15）、AI Safety Gridworlds（Absent Supervisor、Safe Interruptibility、Whisky-and-Gold）和173个已发布规约上进行评估，分别观察到六种机制：FrozenLake reset条款显示匹配超量（稀疏 +0.237，塑形 -0.165）；Taxi/OfficeWorld/Water Tank显示不变性；OfficeWorld对称变体显示衰减；Safe Interruptibility/Water Tank显示回报-满意度权衡；SGW9/SGW15/Absent Supervisor显示可学习性/稳定性分离；173个已发布规约中仅631/4526个出现通过语法筛选，揭示消融危害普遍存在。解耦目标对照实验证实当干预有效时训练影响可被检测（MMD²=0.618, p=0.0005）。

Conclusion: 时序逻辑满足是结果而非解释。审计层能区分六种被单一满意度数字折叠的机制，是部署前保障论证的必要补充。未来工作应将精确有限MDP协议近似到深度/连续RL，并将机制标签整合到工程工作流中，同时改进训练影响研究的干预设计。

Abstract: A reinforcement learning policy that satisfies its temporal-logic specification has passed a test, not an assurance argument. The clause that matters to a reviewer may never have mattered to training: it may have been avoided entirely, forced by the environment regardless of what the policy learned, or redundant next to the ordinary task reward. Satisfaction probability and task return cannot tell any of this apart. This paper introduces an audit layer that can. It measures whether a specification clause was actually exercised, whether that role was forced or chosen, and whether the obvious way to test causation, weakening the clause and retraining, is even valid. Often it is not: we prove that comparable weaker/stronger training objectives can share perfect optima under standard acceptance-derived rewards, show related ablation hazards across a large corpus of published specifications, and then show that a properly designed intervention detects the effect it should. Across standard reinforcement learning benchmarks and published external artifacts, the audit layer separates six regimes that a single satisfaction number collapses into one. A policy that satisfies its specification has answered whether. This paper asks why.

</details>


### [268] [DualMine: Static-Dynamic REST API Constraint Discovery with Dual Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27806)
*Tu Nguyen,Huy Nguyen,Juan C.A. Valenzuela,Thanh Nguyen,Tien N. Nguyen,Vu Nguyen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DualMine是一个用于REST API约束发现的混合框架，通过整合基于规范的静态挖掘、运行时不变式挖掘、双重验证（执行证据验证规范约束、LLM语义推理验证日志不变式）以及反例引导的精化，在39个真实REST API上达到了96%的精度，发现了48个真实API故障，显著优于现有的纯静态或纯动态方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的REST API约束发现方法存在互补的局限性：纯静态方法（如SATORI、RBCTest）受限于规范的不完整、模糊或过时问题；纯动态方法（如AGORA+）依赖执行覆盖度，可能产生仅在观察到的执行中巧合成立的伪不变式。因此需要一种能融合两类证据并进行交叉验证的混合框架，以同时提升约束发现的精度和完整性。

Method: DualMine包含三个核心阶段：(1) 混合约束发现——使用LLM增强的RBCTest作为静态挖掘器从OpenAPI规范中提取候选约束，使用AGORA+从请求-响应轨迹中进行动态不变式挖掘；(2) 双重验证——ExecValidator使用运行时证据验证/反驳规范派生约束，LLMValidator使用规范语义过滤不合理的日志派生不变式；(3) 约束聚合与精化——将验证后约束分类为唯一、等价和非等价假设集，对非等价假设通过LLM生成针对性反例请求执行API以解析冲突或精化约束。

Result: 在39个真实REST API（含GitLab、GitHub、Spotify等7个服务）上的评估显示：DualMine达到96%精度，相比AGORA+（63%）、SATORI（81%）、RBCTest（84%）分别提升33、15、12个百分点；发现1827个有效约束（远高于基线的613-1019个）；双重验证消除了398个误报（减少82.9%）；反例引导精化进一步增强了88个约束的表达力；最终检测出48个真实REST API故障。约束效率为每1k token 1.1个有效约束，是RBCTest的2.2倍。

Conclusion: DualMine通过整合静态规范挖掘与动态不变式挖掘，并采用不对称双重验证和反例引导精化机制，有效缓解了LLM幻觉和执行轨迹过拟合问题，在精度和完整性上均优于现有最优方法，同时发现的约束可作为有效测试预言机检测真实API故障。但仍存在跨字段虚假相关和轨迹过拟合导致的误报，需在验证器中加入更强的语义检查并在反例生成中针对欠表示的配置进行探索。

Abstract: REST API constraints capture semantic properties of API responses and are essential for automated test oracle generation, but they are difficult to discover reliably. Static approaches infer constraints from API specifications and documentation, but their results may be affected by incomplete, ambiguous, or outdated specifications. Dynamic approaches mine invariants from execution traces, but their results depend on execution coverage and may include coincidental properties that hold only for the observed executions. This paper presents DualMine, a hybrid framework for REST API constraint discovery that integrates specification-based constraint mining with runtime invariant mining. It first extracts candidate constraints from OpenAPI specifications using an LLM-based static miner and from request-response traces using dynamic invariant mining. It then performs asymmetric dual validation: runtime evidence is used to validate or refute specification-derived constraints, while specification-aware LLM reasoning is used to filter implausible log-derived invariants~without discarding plausible undocumented behaviors. Finally, it applies counterexample-guided refinement by performing targeted API executions to resolve uncertain, overlapping, or conflicting constraints. We evaluate DualMine on 39 real-world REST APIs and compare it against state-of-the-art static-only, dynamic-only, and constraint discovery approaches. The results show that it improves the quality of discovered constraints by reducing unsupported constraints, retaining complementary constraints missed by individual approaches, which helps detect 48 real REST API faults.

</details>


### [269] [PonyEval: Evaluating LLM-Based Program Repair for Capability-Safe and Actor-Oriented Pony Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27832)
*Bang Xie,Hao Liu,Zhenyu Shi,Zhiyuan Peng,Xin Yin,Chenhao Ying,Yuan Luo,Haiming Jin,Wei Chen,Senjian Zhang,Shaocong Long*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: PonyEval 是一个针对 Pony 语言（基于 actor 模型和引用能力的面向对象语言）的可执行仓库级 issue 修复基准，包含来自 15 个仓库的 291 个真实 GitHub issue-PR 对，并基于 mini-SWE-agent 评估了五个 LLM 在历史 Pony 工具链上的程序修复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仓库级 issue 修复基准（如 SWE-bench）主要覆盖主流语言生态，对小众语言覆盖有限。由于 Pony 语言在引用能力、actor 模型、AOT 编译及快速演进的工具链方面的独特性，补丁生成与历史重放都极具挑战，因此需要专门的基准来评估 LLM 在此类语言上的修复能力。

Method: 1）从 GitHub 挖掘 344 个合并修复 PR 并经规范化、去重形成 507 个候选池；2）通过两状态执行门控（fail-to-pass）筛选，要求 issue 特定测试在 base commit 失败、在开发者补丁后通过；3）重建 72 个历史运行时镜像（覆盖 289 个唯一 base commit，跨越 2015–2026），并在 5 个异构计算节点（含 glibc 2.23 兼容层）验证；4）使用 mini-SWE-agent 2.4.6 对 GPT-5.6-sol、DeepSeek-V4-Pro、GLM-5.2、MiniMax-M3、Kimi-K3 五个模型进行匹配评估；5）三个独立机器评审者对全部 291 实例进行语义纳入/排除标注，计算 Fleiss' κ。

Result: 基准最终包含 291 个实例（72.51% 来自 ponyc 编译器），288 个（98.97%）使用开发者或官方 PR 测试作为 oracle，3 个（1.03%）使用生成测试。五模型条件解决率（以各自实际生成的非空补丁数为分母）：Kimi-K3 24.68%、GLM-5.2 18.55%、GPT-5.6-sol 18.21%、MiniMax-M3 12.00%、DeepSeek-V4-Pro 10.21%。补丁应用率接近饱和（99.57%–100%），但编译率（38.40%–79.22%）和测试通过率显著下降，表明可执行验证而非语法适用性是关键瓶颈。三位机器评审者 Fleiss' κ = 0.8968，249 项一致纳入、32 项一致排除。

Conclusion: PonyEval 首次为 Pony 语言提供了可执行、版本化的仓库级修复基准，证实了 Pony 能力类型系统和快速演进工具链对 LLM 修复的独特挑战。补丁条件解决率揭示了"语法可应用但不编译/不通过行为测试"的显著性能下降，强调了历史编译和行为验证的必要性。基准通过冻结版本、两状态门控、独立语义评审和五节点运行时验证确保了可复现性。局限包括 ponyc 编译器占比过高、潜在的数据污染以及生成测试的过拟合风险。

Abstract: Repository-level issue-resolution benchmarks have made executable evaluation central to software-engineering agents, but their language coverage remains concentrated in mainstream ecosystems. Pony presents a different regime: it combines actors, reference capabilities, ahead-of-time compilation, and a rapidly evolving historical toolchain, making both patch generation and faithful replay difficult. We introduce PonyEval, a SWE-bench-style benchmark of 291 real GitHub issue-pull-request pairs from 15 Pony repositories. Every instance binds an issue statement, a historical base commit, a developer gold patch, a black-box test patch, and a reproducible runtime mapping. The frozen release passes an offline audit requiring the issue-specific test to fail on the base state and pass after the gold patch; it contains no duplicate instance identifiers or canonical repository-PR pairs. In a separate full-set semantic selection audit, three isolated machine reviewers label all 291 instances as include or exclude; their Fleiss' kappa is 0.8968, with 249 unanimous inclusions and 32 unanimous exclusions. To replay eleven years of repository history, we reconstruct 72 runtime images covering 289 unique base commits and verify their availability on five heterogeneous compute nodes. We define a matched evaluation with mini-SWE-agent 2.4.6 for GPT-5.6-sol, DeepSeek-V4-Pro, GLM-5.2, MiniMax-M3, and Kimi-K3, followed by strict patch application, compilation, and hidden-test validation. Across the patches actually produced by each model, conditional resolution rates range from 10.21% to 24.68%. These rates characterize the quality of generated patches rather than success over all 291 benchmark tasks.

</details>


### [270] [Understanding LLM Usage Among Early-Career Software Engineers in Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27973)
*Julia Alencar,Ronnie de Souza Santos,Italo Santos,Cleyton Magalhaes,Danilo Monteiro Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文通过混合方法调查（75名新手软件工程师），研究了早期职业软件工程师如何在专业实践中整合使用LLM，发现LLM已深度融入日常工作，但有效使用依赖于传统软件工程能力与新兴的提示工程、输出验证、批判性思维等技能的结合，且大学教育与职场期望之间存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在软件开发中的快速普及，新手工程师从大学过渡到职场时如何发展有效的AI辅助工作实践尚不清楚，亟需研究教育准备与职场需求之间的差距。

Method: 采用横断面混合方法问卷调查，通过Prolific平台招募75名拥有≤2年经验的早期职业软件工程师，结合封闭式Likert量表题与开放式反思题，经多阶段筛选（注意力检查、AI生成文本检测）保证数据质量，并进行归纳式定性编码分析。

Result: 77.3%参与者每日使用LLM；主要用途包括编码开发支持、测试QA、调试、问题解决、文档知识支持；关键技能涵盖提示工程（n=30）、输出验证（n=27）、调试（n=23）、软件架构（n=17）及批判性思维（n=44）、沟通（n=42）；41人报告大学几乎未涉及LLM实践教学；最受欢迎的教学形式是动手项目（n=57）、LLM专设课程（n=40）和产业项目（n=39）。

Conclusion: LLM辅助软件工程的有效参与不仅需要学习新工具，更需要培养将传统软件工程能力与监督、验证、批判性整合生成工件相结合的反思性实践；大学教育尚未充分反映AI辅助专业实践，组织应通过结构化入职培训和明确审查指南来支持早期职业工程师。

Abstract: Despite the rapid adoption of Large Language Models in professional software engineering, limited research has investigated how early career professionals develop effective AI assisted work practices during their transition into industry. We report findings from a mixed methods survey with 75 novice software engineers who actively use LLM supported tools in their daily work. Our results show that LLMs are embedded in routine software engineering activities, including coding, debugging, testing, documentation, and problem solving. Effective use depends on traditional software engineering competencies, such as debugging, testing, and architectural reasoning, together with critical thinking, output verification, prompt engineering, and continuous human oversight. We also identify a gap between workplace expectations and university preparation, with most participants reporting limited formal education on practical LLM use. These findings have implications for software engineering education, organizational onboarding, and workforce development in AI assisted software engineering.

</details>


### [271] [LLM-Assisted Workflow for Structural Difference Visualization in Evolving Software Requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28002)
*Koi McFarland,Songhui Yue*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LLM辅助的工作流，用于在演化中的软件需求中可视化结构差异。该工作流集成于OntologyWeb环境，将基线和当前需求表示为基于三元组的语义图，并通过并排比较经过人工策划的图表版本来实现结构差异可视化。研究重点在于结合LLM生成、人工策划、快照选择和可视化呈现，使需求工程师能够更清晰地识别需求演化过程中的语义及关系变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件需求在演化过程中不仅存在表面文字的修改，更涉及概念依赖、职责分配与关系结构的深层调整。现有文本对比工具难以有效揭示这些结构化变化；同时，LLM虽然能够从需求文本中自动生成候选三元组与本体结构，但生成结果需经过专家验证并以可比较的形式保存。现有本体编辑与可视化工具（如Protégé）缺乏针对需求版本对比工作流的专门设计。因此需要一种面向需求演化场景的、结合LLM辅助与人类审核的结构差异可视化工作流。

Method: 本工作流在OntologyWeb网络化环境中实现，包含四个主要步骤：1) 使用相同LLM提示和模型设置处理基线与当前需求版本，生成候选三元组；2) 用户对三元组进行检查与策划，包括编辑、添加或删除节点和关系；3) 将策划后的图版本保存为快照；4) 选择两个快照进行并排图视图比较。具体实现包括：基于主体-谓词-客体三元组的图结构表示，集中式JSON存储结构；节点和边标签归一化、命名空间前缀处理；同步布局使匹配节点在视觉上对齐；通过颜色编码的边框标识添加、删除、修改等结构变化；悬停检查关系的源文本证据以保证可追溯性。

Result: 通过对至少包含两个版本的真实需求文档进行初步定性评估，研究者发现：1) 该工作流对于中等至高复杂度的需求变化比对简短、简单的变更更有用，因为后者使用直接文本对比即可满足需求；2) 对于复杂需求，并排可视化能够使关系、依赖关系和变化结构变得更加显式；3) 同步布局、颜色编码、证据检查等功能组合有效支持结构差异识别。当前工作仍处于原型阶段，主要适用于较短选段的需求对比，对于多段落文档的支持尚需进一步改进。

Conclusion: 本文的核心贡献不在于提出新的本体差异算法，而在于提供了一种面向需求工程的集成化工作流，将LLM辅助的需求结构化与交互式图比较相结合。具体贡献包括：1) 一种用于跨需求版本生成和策划需求三元组的"人在环路"过程；2) 一种基于快照的比较机制，允许用户选择和比较生成的图；3) 一种使用颜色编码、证据检查和布局对齐的同步并排可视化，帮助用户检查结构差异。未来工作将通过任务完成时间、结构差异识别准确性、用户识别差异的信心以及主观可用性评分等指标展开正式评估，并探索段落级比较能力的扩展。

Abstract: This paper presents an LLM-assisted workflow for visualizing structural differences in evolving software require- ments. Implemented in the OntologyWeb environment, the work- flow represents baseline and current requirements as triple-based semantic graphs and supports side-by-side comparison of curated graph snapshots. The comparison view aligns matched entities and uses visual encoding to highlight structural changes.

</details>


### [272] [Scenario-Driven Neuroevolution: Using Models to Guide Test Generation for Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28130)
*Gijs van Cuyck,Patric Feldmeier,Jan Tretmans,Gordon Fraser*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出将基于模型的测试与神经进化测试生成器Neatest相结合，通过抽象游戏模型定义测试场景，替代逐分支覆盖目标。在13个Scratch游戏上的评估表明，该方法能有效生成符合预期玩法行为的智能体，并使分支覆盖率较传统代码引导方法提升7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Neatest作为白盒测试方法，为每个未覆盖的语句或分支单独优化神经网络，存在两大局限：1）难以扩展至含数千分支的大型程序；2）将所有代码同等对待，可能产生欺骗性适应度函数（如障碍物阻挡直接路径时陷入局部最优）。需要一种能让测试人员聚焦于有意义玩法行为而非机械覆盖代码的方法。

Method: 将Neatest（基于NEAT神经进化算法的测试生成器）与基于模型的测试相结合。测试人员以扩展有限状态机形式定义抽象游戏模型，包含状态集S、初始状态q₀、命题P、守卫集合Σ和转移E。基于模型的目标状态推导适应度函数：方法层级（AL）为目标状态与已到达最近状态间的距离，分支距离（BD）基于边守卫计算距离。提出两种游戏无关的建模技术：1）课程学习——通过添加中间状态作为进阶踏脚石；2）汇聚状态（sink states）——用于提前终止表现不佳的执行。实现在Whisker测试框架中。

Result: 在13个Scratch游戏（涵盖多种类型，含6至190个分支）的评估中：简单模型（RQ1）平均分支覆盖率为80%，与代码引导方法的85%相当；在CreateYourWorld等游戏中通过消除欺骗性适应度景观反而更优。专门化模型（RQ2）结合课程学习和汇聚状态后，平均达到89%的最佳分支覆盖。总体上模型引导方法在分支覆盖率上比代码引导的Neatest方法提升7%。模型引导方法在状态覆盖率和测试稳定性（更低方差）上均优于代码引导方法。

Conclusion: 模型引导的Neatest方法通过抽象游戏模型让测试人员定义测试场景，使神经进化专注于有意义的玩法行为而非逐分支覆盖，有效解决了原方法的可扩展性和欺骗性适应度问题。模型数量远少于程序分支数，使其能更好地扩展到复杂程序。高层次抽象模型使建模开销可控，专门的建模技术可进一步提升覆盖效果。未来工作包括与多目标优化等其他Neatest扩展结合，并应用于自动驾驶、机器人控制等其他具有强随机性的领域。

Abstract: Automatically generating test inputs for games is challenging, as test generators must master the game to reach advanced program states while also ensuring robustness against the heavy program randomisation inherent to games. The test generator Neatest therefore optimises test suites consisting of neural networks that reach advanced program states and are robust to program randomisation, as they generate test inputs dynamically based on the current program state. Neatest is a white-box testing approach that aims to generate a network agent for each yet-uncovered statement or branch of the code using neuroevolution. Due to this iterative test generation approach, the algorithm does not scale well to larger programs that may contain thousands of branches. Furthermore, covering every statement or branch in a game often does not correspond to playing the game as intended. To alleviate these shortcomings, we propose combining Neatest with a model-based testing approach that allows game testers to define test scenarios via abstract game models. The test generator then no longer optimises networks to reach all branches or statements of a program, but instead trains networks to replicate the concrete desired testing behaviour expressed by the abstract game model. An evaluation on 13 Scratch games across varying genres demonstrates that Neatest, combined with model-based testing, is able to optimise agents that replicate the desired gameplay behaviour defined in the game models while increasing achieved branch coverage by 7% compared to the traditional code-guided Neatest approach.

</details>


### [273] [Entangle: Uncovering Collaboration in the GitHub Quantum Software Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28349)
*Angel Luis Lara-Martín,Ricardo Pérez-Castillo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Entangle是一个基于GitHub的开源量子计算生态系统观测工具，通过分析1500+仓库、27000+贡献者和400+组织，揭示了生态围绕四大工业厂商集中、存在2387名跨组织桥梁贡献者、且81.7%仓库存在单点维护风险的现状，为负责任量子创新提供可量化治理指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子软件生态虽战略重要但分散且缺乏可见性，导致投资、招聘、合作和政策决策依赖直觉而非证据；这种可见性缺失同时构成治理问题，限制了透明度、问责制和生态管理能力。

Method: 基于71个量子领域关键词的GitHub GraphQL API六阶段数据管道（仓库摄取→富化→用户/组织摄取→评分与学科分类→网络与社区分析→指标缓存），使用NetworkX构建异构协作图，计算Louvain社区、介数中心性、桥梁贡献者和公交因子，通过2D仪表板、3D沉浸式可视化和对话代理三种前端呈现。

Result: Python主导（42.05%仓库）；IBM/Google/Xanadu/Amazon四大SDK厂商占据最高中心性；协作图含30970节点和101190边，Louvain社区数为752、模块度0.96；2387名桥梁贡献者跨组织活跃，与高介数节点高度重合；81.7%仓库公交因子为1，揭示系统性维护风险。

Conclusion: Entangle将分散的仓库活动转化为可测量的影响力、依赖、协作和知识转移指标，为资助者、维护者和机构提供具体管理干预的依据（优先支持单维护者项目、加强弱跨组织联系、发展弱代表性学科），并通过指标再计算形成负责任创新反馈循环，但需注意关键词驱动发现的误报风险、快照时间局限和仅观察公开活动的局限。

Abstract: Quantum computing is moving from research laboratories towards early commercialization and broader socio-technical adoption, supported by sustained hardware progress and a rapidly expanding open-source software ecosystem. This momentum is especially visible on GitHub, where many quantum and hybrid software projects coexist around frameworks such as Qiskit, Cirq, PennyLane and Amazon Braket. However, this ecosystem remains fragmented, making it difficult to understand who shapes quantum software, where expertise is concentrated, how collaboration flows across organizations and disciplines, and which actors connect otherwise separated communities. This paper presents Entangle, a data-driven analysis of the open-source quantum computing ecosystem on GitHub. Starting from 71 domain keywords, Entangle identifies more than 1,500 quantum repositories, 27,000 contributors and 400 organizations, revealing an ecosystem strongly organized around four leading industrial vendors, but also supported by 2,387 contributors who connect projects, organizations and domains. These findings provide practical evidence for responsible quantum innovation by making visible patterns of influence, dependency, collaboration and knowledge transfer. They also offer actionable indicators for strategic decisions on investment, hiring, partnerships, ecosystem stewardship and capacity building. More broadly, Entangle shows how open-source intelligence can support a more transparent, measurable and governable quantum software ecosystem, helping align technical development with responsible innovation, public--private coordination and long-term sustainability.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [274] [Silent Failures in Agent-Tool Interaction: An Audit of ToolUniverse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26836)
*Shreya Gopalan,Devansh Singh,Sundaraparipurnan Narayanan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了智能体（Agent）与工具交互中的"静默失败"问题——即工具调用看似成功但返回的信息不完整、缺失或被错误解读，且未通知用户或智能体。研究以ToolUniverse环境中的15个生物学工具为审计对象，开发了一套审计机制，通过7个失败位点（failure locus）分类法进行系统性分析，最终在198个测试用例中验证了91个失败案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能体系统评测侧重于任务最终成功与否（如SWE-bench、AgentBench、ToolBench），但未能评估工具返回信息是否完整、是否有适当限定、是否被正确表征或正确解读。在生物学等科学领域，这种"静默失败"可能导致看似合理的科学结论实际上是建立在不完整或被错误解读的数据之上，对下游科研工作产生严重影响。

Method: 研究方法包括：从ToolUniverse随机抽取63个主要工具，最终选取15个工具（104个关联工具）进行深入分析；采用Cursor智能体结合LLM进行失败候选发现；通过工具文档、API文档、GitHub issues等系统性比较Web UI、API、wrapper之间的差异；为每个候选失败生成具体测试用例和查询；每个测试用例进行多次重复执行（初次失败后增加4次，初次通过或未验证增加2次）；人工验证候选的适当性和判断的有效性；最后将失败映射到7个位点分类法（L1工具限制、L2 API完全缺失、L3 API部分缺失、L4 Wrapper完全缺失、L5 Wrapper部分缺失、L6智能体可用性、L7智能体解读错误），并沿10个分析维度（完整性、正确性、适当性、一致性、可靠性、归属、解读、相关性、上下文、目的对齐）进行评估。

Result: 在198个测试用例中验证了91个失败案例，产生134个问题事件。最受影响的分析维度是完整性（28次）、排名与相关性（21次）、结果适当性（20次）。最常见的失败类型是信息不完整（38次）和搜索/过滤/排名标准不一致（20次）。失败主要发生在API层（51次）和wrapper层（25次）。7个工具（ExpressionAtlas、BV-BRC、AMPSphere、CTIS、ChIP-Atlas、KEGG、Reactome）的失败率超过50%。失败可通过"静默放大"（silent amplification）机制从上游传播到下游，将工具层的限制转化为智能体的科学断言。典型案例包括：EMDB的API分页限制变成完整性声明；ClinicalTrials.gov缺失过滤变成不当纳入；ChIP-Atlas评分被错误解读为线性值；CIViC证据层次扁平化导致认识论混乱。

Conclusion: 研究提出"上下文可靠性"（contextual reliability）概念——评估智能体-工具交互是否在交互链中保留并传达了支持预期科学结论所需的信息、限定符、来源、范围和意义。与传统的二元工具成功度量不同，上下文可靠性评估返回信息在上下文中是否足够且可正确解读。研究建议未来工作方向：开发可衡量的上下文可靠性指标；开发用于限定符保留、完整性、来源、范围、语义保留和限制披露的自动化测试；开发运行时触发器和披露机制；在部署时和定期测试工具配置；鼓励工具和wrapper以机器可读格式明确披露不可用功能；建立工具生态系统监控机制。研究明确指出ToolUniverse仅作为实验环境而非研究对象。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems are increasingly adopting automated pipelines that integrate multiple tools. While prior research and benchmarks have studied about task success and task completion of these agentic systems, the research about agent to tool interaction, specifically in biology agentic workflow is limited. This study investigates specific failures in agent to tool interaction where a tool invocation appears successful, some or all of the information or functionality from the tool via API/ wrapper is incomplete or missing and there are no communications / notifications to the user or the agent about such missing information. We call this a silent failures as the user or the agents are not aware that such failure has occurred. For the purposes of this study we developed an audit mechanism to identify such silent failures in Agent to tool interaction, by examining 15 scientific tools (and their associated API documentation and tool documentations) integrated within ToolUniverse environment (ToolUniverse serves as our experimental environment rather than the object of the study itself). We structure our study around 7 failure locus characterising where the failure occurs in the chain. We observed 91 failures (manually validated post LLM based candidate discovery and automated testing), most frequent of them being missing data or fields and inconsistencies in search, filtering or ranking criteria. Most of the 91 failures occurred in API layer (51) or wrapper layer (25), with a potential of silent failure amplification downstream. The results show that silent failures originate upstream of the event and propagate downstream into apparently valid scientific outputs. We propose a concept of contextual reliability to handle such failures and suggest mechanisms for testing, disclosing, monitoring, and measuring such failures across the agent-tool interaction pipeline.

</details>


### [275] [Harness as a Language: A Minimalist Agent Framework With Maximal Expressivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26891)
*Zhening Li,Joshua Liu,Mateja Vukelic,Nicole Shen,Supriya Lall,Amitayush Thakur,Alex Zhang,Omar Khattab,Jonathan Light,Armando Solar-Lezama*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出JAZ框架，将LLM智能体的核心抽象简化为单一原语`invoke`，通过将所有输入和REPL历史暴露为代码环境中的变量，使仅靠提示就能完成长程记忆检索和持续自我改进等传统上需要专门外部框架的工作流，并在StuLife和AppWorld上以更低成本超越Letta和ACE等专用框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM智能体的许多高级能力（如长程记忆、持续自我改进）依赖于在agent loop之上专门设计的外部harness工程，框架复杂且对环境假设敏感。本文质疑这些能力是否本质上必须由专用外部系统实现，探讨一个几乎仅由agent loop本身组成的极简harness能否通过纯提示实现同等能力。

Method: 1. 从第一性原理推导`invoke`原语：将任意图灵完备语言扩展一个新原语`invoke`，其函数体由LLM在运行时生成，调用时所有输入和REPL历史均作为代码环境中的变量可用；tail-recursive `invoke`在尾调用优化后自然产生imperative形式的代码模式agent loop。2. 基于此构建JAZ框架，实现`invoke` agent loop并提供动态作用域（`scope`）、钩子系统（可观察性、预算控制、验证等）和配置系统。3. 在两个案例研究中仅使用提示进行评估：长程记忆（StuLife，子代理通过`prev_history`按引用传递历史）和持续自我改进（AppWorld，顶层`invoke`作为元智能体优化子智能体的prompt和skills）。

Result: 在StuLife长程任务上，JAZ `invoke`在far recall子集上以72.6%通过率（vs. Letta 61.8%）和18.3美元成本（vs. Letta 42.1美元）超越专用记忆框架Letta；在AppWorld test-challenge上，JAZ `invoke`以74.2% TGC（vs. ACE 69.9%）和更低成本（20.9美元 vs. 30.6美元）超越专用自我改进框架ACE。两个场景下均同时优于CodeAct和CodeAct+subagents基线。

Conclusion: 许多常被视为专用智能体外壳能力（记忆、自我改进）可由足够强大的计算基底直接涌现；智能体框架的主要差异不在于能力是否存在，而在于对工作流构建、状态表达力和递归计算的限制程度。设计智能体系统时，与其工程化越来越多的专用编排机制，不如提供最大表达力的执行环境，让模型自行构造和调整工作流。

Abstract: Modern language-model agents are built around the \textit{agent loop}, where the LLM is placed in an environment exposing a set of tools, and the LLM has full control over the workflow by alternating between tool calls and observing their output. However, certain workflows currently require additional engineering beyond the agent loop itself, such as memory systems and self-improving systems. We built an LLM agent framework, JAZ, to explore the extent to which a minimal harness that is little more than the agent loop itself can accomplish tasks these specialized systems are built for. JAZ exposes a single LLM-based primitive invoke and provides a set of built-in hooks that allow the programmer to apply constraints and monitoring. Generalizing existing code-mode agent loops, \texttt{invoke} is the simplest loop that satisfies two defining properties: (1) the LLM can write arbitrary executable code that can include recursive \texttt{invoke}; (2) everything visible to the LLM --- all inputs to \texttt{invoke} as well as its interaction history with the code environment --- are variables in the code environment. We motivate our design from first principles, viewing \texttt{invoke} as a language primitive representing a function whose implementation is provided at runtime by an LLM every time it is called. To validate the design of our core \texttt{invoke} primitive, we evaluate \texttt{invoke} --- with only prompting, no manually designed tools, harness, or external systems (e.g., memory or the file system) --- on workflows traditionally implemented through specialized external harnesses. On long-horizon workflows requiring recall beyond the context window, JAZ invoke outperforms Letta (MemGPT) by 8\% at half its cost on the recall-heavy portion of StuLife. On continual self-improvement, JAZ invoke outperforms ACE by 4\% at a lower cost on AppWorld.

</details>


### [276] [TwinCheck: Evidence-Grounded Negative-Twin Verification for Stateful Tool Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26911)
*Jiaxuan Dai,Tianyi Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TwinCheck，一种面向有状态工具代理的推理时验证策略。它在执行边界处通过"证据条件"约束干预行为：仅当轨迹满足与局部失败假设绑定的证据条件时，才构造一个"负面孪生"（负反事实替代动作）来替换代理的提议，且必须通过结构检查和双向（正反顺序）成对验证器的偏好共识。精确重放方法固定代理已解析的响应和动作直到首次接受替换，分离了干预效应与重采样的影响。在BFCL V4多轮任务的159个主要分析对上，GPT-5.6 Sol的任务成功率从45.3%提升至58.5%，提升了13.2个百分点（95% bootstrap CI [8.2, 18.8]），包含21次救援且未观察到成功到失败的回归。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型代理通过外部工具执行多轮交互时，单个局部看似合理的工具调用可能使整个轨迹偏离成功。现有基于LLM验证器的自反馈方法存在系统性偏差（如候选顺序敏感性），且无证据的自我修正可能反而破坏原本正确的推理。在执行边界，不当干预是不对称的：拒绝修复保留原方案，而不必要的修复可能将成功转为失败。因此，干预需要基于证据条件，证明当前动作局部失败且特定替代动作能弥补缺陷。

Method: TwinCheck包含三个核心组件：(1)证据账本与连续检测器：维护当前轮次的解析记录，使用固定启发式计算异常分数d_j,t，但该分数仅用于失败排序，不能单独授权修复。(2)三种固定的证据条件：词汇逆动作条件（severity 1.00）、错误后重复条件（severity 1.00）、唯一陈旧参数条件（severity 0.95），每个条件编码特定的局部失败假设，并决定确定性修复或约束模型生成的替代动作。(3)反平衡对比验证：对通过证据与结构门控的动作对，验证器以A/B和B/A两种顺序判断，要求两种顺序都偏好替代动作、都将原动作标记为致命错误且替代为非致命、置信度≥0.98、质量差Δ_v≥0.50才执行替换。校准阶段使用任务ID不相交的影子运行确定阈值γ（预设为0.50）。精确重放固定代理序列直到首次接受替换，分离干预效果与重采样。

Result: 在159对主要分析任务中，GPT-5.6 Sol代理成功率从45.3%提升至58.5%（+13.2pp，95% CI [8.2, 18.8]，McNemar p=9.54×10⁻⁷），21次救援、0次伤害。完整主要敏感性分析（160对）：45.0%→58.1%（+13.1pp）。条件扩展集（160任务）：48.1%→58.1%（+10.0pp）。合并320任务：46.6%→58.1%（+11.6pp，95% CI [8.1, 15.3]），37次救援、0次伤害。失败排序ROC-AUC为0.759，AP为0.829。冻结门控选择71次干预（3,665次决策的1.94%），其中37次成功修复（修复率52.1%），无一将成功转为失败（95% Clopper-Pearson伤害上界4.13%）。触发匹配控制：选择性自修订+1.2pp、选择性绝对验证器+1.9pp，均远低于完整协议的+13.1pp。

Conclusion: TwinCheck将执行边界修复重新定义为受约束的比较，使反事实动作本身成为验证对象。其核心贡献在于：(1)将工具动作修复形式化为不对称决策——保留提案是默认，替换必须针对局部失败假设；(2)实例化了一种推理时策略，其证据条件约束替代动作，反平衡验证器必须证明改变代理动作的合理性；(3)使用精确重放实现配对救援-伤害核算。跨四个BFCL V4多轮类别的评估显示，完整协议在无成功到失败回归的情况下提升了任务成功率。在验证可以改变代理执行内容时，它必须为替换建立证据，而不仅仅是反对原提案。

Abstract: A single locally plausible tool call can derail an otherwise successful agent trajectory. Suspicion alone does not justify intervention, because the replacement itself can introduce the very failure verification is meant to prevent. We introduce TwinCheck, an inference-time verification policy that considers replacement only when the trace satisfies an evidence condition tied to a trace-local failure hypothesis. It constructs a trace-grounded counterfactual alternative, a negative twin, and replaces the agent's proposal only if the twin passes structural checks and the pairwise verifier prefers it in both candidate orders. For paired evaluation, exact replay holds the agent's parsed responses and actions fixed until the first accepted replacement, separating intervention effects from resampling. In the primary analysis of 159 multi-turn BFCL V4 tasks with complete exact-replay pairs, the complete policy raises task success for GPT-5.6 Sol from 45.3% to 58.5% (95% task-bootstrap CI [8.2, 18.8]), with no observed success-to-failure regressions. Together, these findings recast execution-boundary repair as a constrained comparison, making the counterfactual action itself the object of verification.

</details>


### [277] [Building Socio-Affective Artificial Intelligence for Interactive Multi-Agent Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26927)
*David Berga*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AGIMUD软件框架，将社会推理（Schwartz价值观、Ostrom治理、Shapley公平性、Montes-Sierra心智理论）与Ekman-Frijda情感模型集成到多智能体MUD模拟中，支持中心化与P2P网络架构下人类与LLM智能体实时交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多智能体系统（BDI/POMDP）受限于状态空间爆炸和刚性对话树；Transformer时代LLM虽解决了语言生成但引入了幻觉与状态一致性缺失问题。需要一个将形式化社会-情感理论与LLM结合的框架，使智能体在动态模拟世界中实现可持续的、符合伦理的社会交互与治理。

Method: 构建模块化Simulated World Module (SWM)，包含四层集成：(1) SocialReasoningCore——融合Schwartz 10维价值观（贝叶斯更新）、Ostrom 8项治理原则（含合规/惩罚项）、Shapley联盟公平值、Montes-Sierra信念追踪（含欺骗度量）；(2) EmotionManager——基于Ekman 6种基本情感的7阶段评估-衰减流程（含强度调制、情感动量70/30混合、有限生命周期）；(3) 5阶段ToM管线（感知→归因→验证→目标生成→决策规划），通过JSON-RPC与LLM异步通信避免渲染循环阻塞；(4) 双网络架构（中心化服务器与WebRTC P2P），支持本地量化模型（LM Studio+GGUF）与MCP云服务混合部署。

Result: 在5种推理配置×50次推理实验中，效用函数呈现层级：Montes-Sierra惩罚（-26.00）> Ostrom合规（-1.62至+8.85）> Schwartz动机（0.24至2.92），系统因效用平台现象26.8%匹配率集中于HARVEST_SUSTAINABLE_SHARED；情感测试6/6事件正确映射至Ekman情绪，70/30动量混合与衰减机制验证有效；在4至128角色规模（45次世界×1000-10000步）的统计中，Mean_Health与Distinct_AI_States跨规模稳定负相关（r≈-0.65至-0.73），Mean_Hunger与AI_State_Entropy稳定正相关（r≈+0.50至+0.56），证实Hull驱力削减与Maslow需求层次理论在系统中的体现。

Conclusion: AGIMUD是首个将形式化社会-情感理论（4种心智理论框架+Ekman-Frijda情感动力学）以神经-符号混合方式集成到实时多智能体MUD模拟的开源框架；测试显示安全约束可能导致行为多样性下降（效用平台问题），需引入动作特异性权重打破平局；框架在两个数量级规模缩放下保持统计鲁棒性，为未来混合现实、多模态人机交互及大规模社会经济仿真提供了可扩展架构。

Abstract: The objective of this article is to provide design principles and a software architecture for enabling interaction between humans and multiple agents in simulated dynamic worlds. This connects the current era of general artificial intelligence (AI/AGI) with the proliferation of transformer-based conversational agents and the increased computational capabilities. Given an overview of current and previous multi-agent theories of mind (socially and affectively-aware agents), the existence of an integrative design of agent interactions with themselves and with humans must be crucial for understanding how to create sustainable and governance in future human-agent reasoning systems. In this work is presented a software "AGIMUD" that integrates: A. socially-aware reasoning and emotion in agent behavior and interaction, B. a design of human multimodal scheme for human users, artificial agents and simulated worlds, and C. distributing the AI processing through the network to enable multiple autonomous agents. These integrations allow the dynamic world recreation as multi-user dungeons (MUDs) where both agents and humans can interact simultaneously in real time. Find the code online in this https URL .

</details>


### [278] [Which Objectives Need a Dial? Predicting Objective Conflict and Covering Trade-offs in Steerable Pluralistic Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26929)
*David Tsoi,Esra Dönmez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究多目标直接偏好优化（MODPO）用于可引导的多元对齐，探讨两个核心问题：一是何时一个模型能同时满足两个目标，何时必须在二者间权衡；二是如何在不重新训练每个权重的情况下覆盖多个权衡点。研究在七对目标（来自HelpSteer和UltraFeedback数据集）上发现：偏好对冲突率和SFT参考模型评分相关性这两个训练前测量能预测人类标注数据上目标的对齐或冲突，但在AI标注数据上失败，因为响应长度和重复模式严重干扰了奖励模型评分。对于权衡覆盖，最近邻选择和参数合并都有帮助，但都无法可靠地匹配直接训练。论文为构建可引导模型提供了实用指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于人类价值观多元且可能冲突，单一对齐模型无法满足所有人。多元对齐需要可引导模型来平衡不同目标，但实践中有两个未解决的问题：何时需要权衡（而非同时优化两个目标），以及如何在不训练每个权重变体的情况下覆盖整个权衡连续谱。

Method: 方法分为两部分研究：(1) 权衡研究（RQ1）——计算两个训练前廉价测量：偏好对冲突率（两个目标在数据中偏好不同响应的比例）和SFT参考模型评分相关性（生成响应的两个目标得分之间的Pearson相关性），测试它们是否能预测MODPO在七个目标对上的行为。(2) 覆盖研究（RQ2）——比较两种无需重新训练的方法来达到新权衡：最近邻选择（选择权重最接近目标权重的已训练模型）和参数合并（线性插值相邻两个已训练模型的LoRA适配器）。使用Llama-2-7b（HelpSteer）和Alpaca-7b（UltraFeedback、PKU-SafeRLHF）作为基础模型，采用ArmoRM和Beaver奖励模型评估。所有训练使用LoRA，单一种子。

Result: （1）RQ1：训练前测量在HelpSteer上表现良好（正确性和连贯性高相关低冲突，能共同提升；简洁性冲突率高，呈现清晰权衡），但在UltraFeedback上失败（尽管高相关，所有模型都在第二目标上退化）。失败原因是奖励模型评分被响应长度和重复模式强烈干扰（生成可达970词，4-gram重复率超0.95）；重复控制重新解码后，UltraFeedback得分上升+0.10到+0.15，与高相关性更一致。（2）RQ2：参数合并在平均综合差距上最小（1.14 vs 最近邻细选1.69、粗选2.40），但并非在每个目标权重上都最优。最近邻选择在平均奖励差距上略优（1.06 vs 合并1.11）。两种方法都能覆盖权衡连续谱，但都无法可靠匹配直接训练。

Conclusion: 结论：客观权重是一个真正的引导旋钮，但它不是承诺——相同权重在不同数据和不同解码设置下表现不同。训练前测量（冲突率和参考模型相关性）可预测MODPO行为，但仅限于可信的人工标注数据；在AI标注数据中，长度和重复干扰严重。目标是否兼容不仅取决于数据，还取决于解码和评估器。对于覆盖新权衡，最近邻选择和参数合并都有效，但都不能可靠替代直接训练。论文给出了实用配方——同时服务两个目标应选高相关低冲突对并控制解码；构建可引导权衡应选低相关高冲突对并利用边际符号选择方向。是否暴露权衡本身应留给受影响的人们来决定。

Abstract: People hold diverse, sometimes conflicting values, so no single aligned model can satisfy everyone. Pluralistic alignment therefore calls for steerable models that can balance competing objectives differently. Multi-Objective Direct Preference Optimization (MODPO) does this by using an objective weight to span a continuum of trade-offs. We study two questions: when can one model improve two objectives simultaneously, and how can many trade-offs be covered without training a separate model for each? Across seven objective pairs from HelpSteer and UltraFeedback, two pre-training measurements predict whether objectives align or conflict for human-annotated data, but not for AI-annotated data, where response length and repetition confound reward-model scores. For broader trade-off coverage, selecting the nearest trained model and merging model parameters both help, but neither consistently matches direct training. These findings yield practical guidance for building steerable models that serve diverse preferences.

</details>


### [279] [Escaping Python Dependency Hell: A Hybrid Replay-and-Repair Pipeline for Python Dependency Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26952)
*Veronica Poweska,Ariana Oyanguren,Jessica Pourleyli,Sourena Khanzadeh,Manar Alalfi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 PLLM+ 混合依赖修复管道，通过优先使用静态 AST 解释器推断、历史解决方案数据库回放和 PyPI 实时验证等确定性步骤，仅在上述步骤失败时才回退到基于 LLM 的多智能体修复。在 HG2.9K 基准上，PLLM+ 解决了 1,500/2,891 个片段（51.9%），优于 PLLM 基线的 1,169 个（40.4%），并将平均运行时间从 368.7 秒降至 71.8 秒。值得注意的是，99.7%（1,495 个）的成功修复来自历史数据库回放，LLM 回退仅贡献 5 个额外修复，表明在当前基准设置中，确定性的配置复用比 LLM 推理更为有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python 生态系统中的依赖冲突（版本不兼容、缺失包、未记录的兼容关系）导致大量真实代码片段执行失败。现有 LLM 驱动的自动修复方法（如 PLLM）虽然采用迭代错误反馈循环，但仍会生成看似合理但实际无效的依赖版本，且计算成本较高。论文的动机是研究能否通过优先采用廉价的确定性方法（静态分析、历史配置复用、PyPI 验证）来提升依赖修复的效率和成功率，并明确 LLM 在该任务中应承担的角色。

Method: PLLM+ 采用四级优先级流水线，按成本从低到高排序：(1) Stage 1 使用 Python ast 模块解析语法树，根据版本特定语法（如海象运算符）推断最低解释器版本下界；(2) Stage 2 从竞赛提供的 PLLM/PyEGo/ReadPy 解决方案 tar 归档中解析所有历史成功配置，按包数量升序回放，并通过唯一运行标识保留多版本配置，结合 Stage 1 兼容性过滤器丢弃必失败项；(3) Stage 3 通过 PyPI JSON API 验证候选包版本是否真实存在，对 Python 2.7 维护硬编码上限表，并检测 from versions: none 错误直接丢弃缺失包；(4) Stage 4 多智能体修复循环，将构建错误分类为 ModuleNotFoundError、ImportError、DependencyConflict、NonZeroCode、SyntaxError 等类型，由 Proposer 生成候选配置、Critic 独立审查，所有候选需重新通过 PyPI 验证，最多 5 轮迭代。跨批次运行维护持久化知识图谱，按包名与 Python 版本索引验证组合。

Result: 在 HG2.9K 基准（2,891 个依赖失败片段）上，PLLM+ 成功解决 1,500 个片段（51.9%），相比 PLLM 基线的 1,169 个（40.4%）提升 11.5 个百分点；平均运行时间从 368.74 秒降至 71.77 秒（约 5.1 倍加速），中位时间从 364.48 秒降至 21.5 秒。在 1,500 个成功修复中，1,495 个（99.7%）来自解决方案数据库回放，5 个（0.3%）来自 LLM 回退。失败类别分析显示 PLLM+ 的未解决片段几乎全部归为粗粒度的 failed 状态（1,374 个），另有 17 个超时。实验使用 Gemma 2 模型（Ollama 部署）在 Intel i7/GTX 1070 GPU 集群上执行。

Conclusion: PLLM+ 表明在 Python 依赖修复任务中，确定性历史配置复用是最有效的策略，可显著提升成功率与效率；LLM 推理应作为辅助回退而非主要手段。论文将 PLLM+ 定位于实用型混合设计：优先使用确定性方法，仅在历史验证配置不可用时才调用 LLM 修复。未来工作包括在外部仓库等与现有数据库重叠度低的场景下评估该设计、扩展检索来源（如开源仓库、包文档、开发者讨论）以及强化 LLM 回退能力。主要局限在于其性能依赖于解决方案数据库对目标片段的覆盖程度。

Abstract: Dependency conflicts in Python ecosystems arise from incompatible version constraints, missing packages, and undocumented compatibility relationships, causing many real-world code snippets to fail at execution. This paper presents PLLM+, a hybrid dependency-repair pipeline evaluated on the HG2.9K benchmark of 2,891 dependency-failing snippets. PLLM+ prioritizes inexpensive deterministic steps before invoking LLM-based repair: static AST-based interpreter inference, replay of historically successful dependency configurations from the competition-provided solutions database, and live PyPI validation of candidate package versions. When these steps do not resolve a case, the system falls back to a structured LLM-based repair loop with typed error classification and Proposer/Critic agents. On HG2.9K, PLLM+ solves 1,500 out of 2,891 snippets, compared with 1,169 solved by the PLLM baseline. It also reduces average runtime from 368.7 to 71.8 seconds per snippet. Most successful fixes come from replaying known configurations: 1,495 of the 1,500 successful fixes are produced by the solutions database, while the LLM fallback accounts for 5 additional fixes. These results suggest that, in this benchmark setting, deterministic reuse of previously validated dependency configurations is a simple and effective strategy, with LLM-based repair serving as a secondary fallback for cases not covered by prior solutions.

</details>


### [280] [Same evidence, different judgments: Evidence noncommutative in vision/speech-text conflicts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26986)
*Zhuoyun Li,Boxuan Wang,Xiaowei Huang,Yi Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对多模态大语言模型(MLLM)在视觉/语音与文本冲突场景下的"文本偏倚"测量问题，提出证据顺序会显著影响模态偏好测量结果，并提出"跨模态证据非交换性"(evidence noncommutativity)概念。研究通过配对交换实验设计，在保持指令和证据内容固定的前提下，仅交换感知证据(图像/语音)与文本的位置，发现将感知证据置于冲突文本之后能系统性提升模型对其内容的依赖(9.8–28.1个百分点)。研究还诊断了先前协议中的混淆因素(任务指令随证据移动、警告语影响)，为更可靠的模态偏好评估提供了方法论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究普遍报告MLLM在视觉/语音与文本冲突时过度依赖文本，但这些研究大多使用固定证据顺序或将任务指令与证据一起移动，导致测量的"文本偏倚"实际上是模态偏好与证据位置效应的混合，无法清晰区分二者贡献。本文旨在通过严格的实验设计分离位置效应与模态偏好。

Method: 1. **配对交换设计**：保持任务指令I和证据内容固定，仅交换感知证据P与文本T的位置，形成x_{P→T}=(I,P,T,q)与x_{T→P}=(I,T,P,q)两种输入；2. **三种文本条件**：冲突文本T⁻、匹配文本T⁺、无关文本T⁰作为控制；3. **量化指标**：ΔA⁻=A(T⁻→P)-A(P→T⁻)测量顺序效应，对数赔率交换分数𝒞测量概率层面的偏移，D=ΔA⁻-ΔA⁰消除基线顺序效应；4. **反证实验**：测试显式源优先级指令、附件措辞变化、证据距离增加、自由形式回答等替代解释；5. **协议回溯**：在Words or Vision和MCR-BENCH上复现并诊断先前实验设置的混淆因素。

Result: 1. **核心发现**：在12个模型-数据集组合中，将附件置于冲突文本之后使附件支持答案的选择率系统性提升9.8–28.1个百分点，对数赔率分数95%置信区间均不包含零；2. **控制实验**：无关文本条件下ΔA⁰较小且方向不一，差值D在9.4–28.8pp仍为正，排除了一般重排效应；3. **解释排除**：即使添加显式优先级指令、改变提示措辞、插入干扰token、采用自由回答，位置效应仍稳健存在；4. **协议诊断**：在WoV/VQAv2上，先前协议报告移动图像后准确率下降13.7pp，而本文对齐协议显示实际上升17.7pp——指令随证据移动可逆转方向性结论(差异达38.1pp)；5. **MCR-BENCH扩展**：在AQA、SER、VSC任务上，将音频置于最后同样带来+11.5至+51.3pp的提升。

Conclusion: 本文证实了跨模态证据非交换性现象：相同证据在不同呈现顺序下可导致不同判断，附件靠后呈现能增强MLLM对其内容的依赖。这一发现揭示了先前文本偏倚测量中存在的方法论混淆——当任务指令、警告语随证据移动时，可能产生误导性的模态偏好结论。建议未来评估应同时测试两种证据顺序并报告位置效应与模态偏好的分离量化，同时在部署系统中考虑证据排序对输出的影响。

Abstract: For multimodal large language models, when images or speech conflict with accompanying text, measured text reliance can entangle modality preference with evidence position. Earlier studies of text bias often used a fixed evidence order or moved task instructions with the evidence, leaving the contribution of order unclear. In this paper, we use a paired comparison that keeps the instructions and evidence content fixed and swaps only the positions of the two sources to quantify this potential influence. Across vision and speech models, placing an image or recording after conflicting text consistently shifts answers toward its content. We also revisit previous studies and analyze why their experimental settings can lead to misleading conclusions. These findings reveal cross-modal evidence noncommutativity: the same evidence can lead to different judgments when its order changes, and placing perceptual evidence later can increase the model's reliance on its content.

</details>
