### [281] [Reinforcement Learning with Decomposed Subtasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27035)
*Mattie Terzolo,Mikolaj Sacha,Ayan Sinha,Andrew Rabinovich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对RL智能体训练中，scalar轨迹奖励（如GRPO）会丢失子任务及步骤级别的信用分配信号的问题，提出RLDS方法，其核心SDAE通过固定子任务分类法、按子任务分解奖励、计算组内相对优势、并以高斯核在识别到的关键步骤周围分配token级别信用。在四个智能体基准上的实验表明，收益与子任务异质性正相关：高异质性任务（ScienceWorld +11.5/+26.7%，FrozenLake +9.8/+11.7%）获得显著提升，低异质性任务（HotpotQA、DeepResearch）基本无收益；同时在长视野任务（ScienceWorld）上比scalar GRPO节省10.9%的每步墙钟时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO及类似的策略梯度方法将整条多轮rollout折叠为单一标量轨迹奖励再做策略更新。当任务由多个不同技能组合而成，且环境反馈稀疏延迟时，这种折叠是有损的——优化器必须隐式推断哪种能力驱动了结果并如何改变行为，关键在于定位到按子任务的关键步骤而非全局广播。

Method: 提出RLDS（基于分解子任务的强化学习），其核心为SDAE（Subtask-Decomposed Advantage Estimation），包含三阶段：(1)按固定子任务分类（K个），由策略自身以Likert 1–5打分并指认pivot步，将轨迹奖励按子任务份额分解；在GRPO组内做每个子任务的组相对基线，得到每个子任务的优势；(2)用静态重要性权重φ_k加权求和得到行级优势，并按batch进行标准差归一化；(3)以Gaussian核围绕各子任务的pivot步骤将行级优势扩展为per-token优势，使信用集中分布到各子任务被识别为决定性的轮次附近。同时引入分级监控信号（符号不一致率、与标量GRPO相关性、token级优势标准差）诊断各阶段是否真正生效。训练方面采用R³L式的reflect-retry机制并保留ERL的retry蒸馏与R³L的反射NLL辅助损失。

Result: 在FrozenLake、HotpotQA、ScienceWorld、DeepResearch四个基准上对比scalar GRPO（RLVR）和reflect-retry基线。结果与子任务异质性诊断预测一致：ScienceWorld收益最大（+11.5点，配对bootstrap 95% CI [+9.8,+13.3]；+26.7%±4.8%），FrozenLake次之（+9.8点，[+7.0,+12.8]；+11.7%±3.8%）；HotpotQA（+1.9%±5.1%）和DeepResearch（+0.7%±1.7%）未脱离噪声。诊断表明这两个任务的同行方差与pivot跨度过小，SDAE自然退化为scalar优势。ScienceWorld上由于长视野摊销固定开销，每步墙钟时间反而比scalar GRPO少10.9%（0.89×）。

Conclusion: 当任务具有较高的子任务异质性与较长的执行视野时，奖励分解是比更好的标量更合适的基本单元；RLDS将已有的反思诊断转化为面向能力的、per-token的信用。三级诊断（sign disagreement、GRPO correlation、token-level std）可在训练中定位SDAE在哪一阶段失效，并提示何时应退回到标量基线。未来工作包括自适应重要性权重（如Dirichlet先验动态更新φ_k）以及将taxonomy设计原则（按时间阶段而非正交能力）形式化。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) and related policy-gradient methods for training language model agents collapse an entire multi-turn rollout into a single scalar trajectory reward before it enters the policy update. When the task composes distinct skills, especially under sparse and delayed environmental feedback, this collapsing is lossy: the optimizer must implicitly infer which competency drove the outcome and how that should change behavior. We argue the right primitive is not a better scalar but a decomposition: trajectory reward should be split along subtasks before it enters the policy update. We introduce Reinforcement Learning with Decomposed Subtasks (RLDS), whose core is Subtask-Decomposed Advantage Estimation (SDAE): a replacement for the scalar GRPO advantage that splits trajectory reward into per-subtask shares on a fixed taxonomy, computes a group-relative advantage per subtask, and distributes per-token credit by weighting each subtask's advantage by its importance, concentrating it around the step where a reflection marks that subtask's execution as consequential. We evaluate on four agentic benchmarks: FrozenLake (sparse grid navigation), HotpotQA (multi-hop QA, one retrieval tool), ScienceWorld (long-horizon embodied science), and DeepResearch (long-form research, four tools, composite rubric reward). Heterogeneity diagnostics emitted during training show where decomposition pays off - gains scale with subtask heterogeneity, largest on the high-heterogeneity tasks ScienceWorld (+11.5 points, paired-bootstrap 95% CI [+9.8, +13.3]) and FrozenLake (+9.8 points, [+7.0, +12.8]), and within noise on HotpotQA and DeepResearch, where the diagnostics predicted little to recover. ScienceWorld is also more compute-efficient under RLDS than scalar GRPO (-10.9% wall-clock per step), as long rollouts amortize the fixed reflect-and-grade overhead.

</details>


### [282] [Training Intelligent Voice Assistant Wakeup with Controllable Synthetic Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27037)
*Marcin Sowański,Kacper Leszczyński,Kacper Krzywicki,Krzysztof Wodnicki*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种智能语音助手唤醒系统，将传统的唤醒词检测扩展为结合上下文触发检测的分类任务，并构建了一个可控的多说话人合成对话生成框架，生成62.3小时数据集用于训练和评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统唤醒词检测依赖固定关键词，限制了人机交互的自然流畅性。本文旨在让虚拟助手在检测到唤醒词后，能够基于上下文判断后续话语是否真正针对自己，从而支持更自然的连续对话。

Method: 方法包含两部分：(1) 数据生成——通过场景规格化、LLM生成对话文本、ElevenLabs/Qwen-TTS等合成语音、添加背景音，生成多说话人合成对话；(2) 智能唤醒检测模型——采用预训练语音编码器（Whisper/Hubert/Zipformer）+ 注意力池化 + 说话人嵌入 + 因果Transformer（4层，256维），在轮次级别进行三分类（直接激活/上下文激活/非面向助手）。

Result: 实验结果显示：(1) TTS说话人分析中ElevenLabs和Qwen克隆效果接近自然语音；(2) 自然度评估显示合成数据集在场景合理性和对话连贯性上接近自然语音，但在对话和语音自然度上仍有差距；(3) 唤醒模型中whisper-base取得最佳F1=0.894，直接召回达0.943，上下文召回0.794；gpt-realtime-mini基线在抑制非面向语音上表现较差（误激活率达516.6次/小时）。

Conclusion: 紧凑型语音编码器能在直接和上下文唤醒任务上取得良好性能，唤醒词激活基本解决，剩余挑战在于从对话上下文推断隐式后续请求；合成对话在合理性和连贯性上可行，但与真实语音在自然度上仍有显著差距。

Abstract: Wake word detection is a critical component of virtual assistants, serving as the gateway to seamless user interactions. This paper introduces a novel wake-up system that extends traditional direct keyword detection with contextual trigger detection. After an initial wake word activation, the system uses reasoning to distinguish between user commands and unrelated speech, ensuring efficient and context-aware engagement. We present a data generation architecture that produces a 62.3-hour corpus of controllable multi-speaker conversations containing direct invocations, contextual follow-ups, and non-addressed speech. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach across diverse synthetic conversational scenarios. We release the code, dataset and trained models to promote reproducibility and further advancements in intelligent assistant technologies.

</details>


### [283] [Are Stated Reasoning Steps Causally Load-Bearing?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27038)
*Abhiram Bhupatiraju,Rayan Nyaupane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于激活修补（activation patching）的因果忠实度度量方法"因果承重率"（CLB rate），通过在多跳查找任务中对模型自生成的推理步骤进行有明确目标的反事实干预，衡量陈述的推理步骤是否真正因果性地影响最终答案。实验表明，Qwen3-4B 的 CLB 率为 76.9%，达到可达到效果的 96%，但标准的文本编辑行为测试将因果忠实度高估了 11.4 个百分点，且在简单任务上差距更大（达 20 个百分点）。较弱模型（Qwen3-1.7B）的因果忠实度随推理深度增加而急剧下降，揭示出行为测试与因果忠实度之间存在系统性差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 链式思维（CoT）监控的有效性依赖于模型陈述的推理步骤与最终答案计算之间的忠实度。现有忠实度度量主要是行为性的（编辑推理文本并观察答案变化），但这是间接的，可能高估因果忠实度。本文旨在通过激活层面的因果干预，严格测量自生成推理步骤是否真正"承重"（causally load-bearing）。

Method: 使用合成多跳查找任务（2-6 跳），针对每个推理步骤构造一个反事实变体。通过在残差流的特定 token 跨度上修补来自反事实运行的激活，使用严格的"翻转到目标"（flip-to-target）准则评估因果忠实度。同时在相同条目上进行文本编辑的行为测试以做对比。包含多种控制：随机位置零基线、prompt-fact 上限控制、答案后健全性控制、随机目标特异性控制。使用 Qwen3-4B 和 Qwen3-1.7B 两个模型进行对比。

Result: Qwen3-4B 在最响应的中间网络层有 76.9%±2.8% 的陈述步骤是因果承重的（随机位置零基线：11.3%；修补提示事实的上限：83%，因此陈述步骤达到约 96% 的可实现效果）。行为测试在相同条目上得出 88.2%，高出 11.4 个百分点（111:14 不一致对，p < 10^-15），在最容易的项目上高达 20 个百分点。Qwen3-1.7B 因果忠实度较低（54.8%），且随推理深度增加而崩溃（2 跳 68% 到 6 跳 30%），而 Qwen3-4B 保持相对稳定。效应集中在网络的早期到中层，4B 模型在第 20 层（共 36 层）消失，1.7B 模型在第 12-16 层（共 28 层）消失。

Conclusion: 虽然陈述的推理步骤在很大程度上具有因果意义，但标准的行为测试倾向于因果性地系统性评估高估因果忠实度，尤其是在容易被影响并有能力产生流畅推理的最容易项目上。因果忠实度随模型能力提升而改善和稳定，而较弱模型的陈述推理在任务难度增大时退化为"叙述"。依赖行为忠实度评分的监控和评估管道应将其视为上限。基于行为的忠实度度量不能替代因果审计。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring assumes that the reasoning a model writes reflects the computation that directly produces its answer. Previous faithfulness metrics have been predominantly behavioral, as they simply edit the reasoning text and observe the resulting answer. However, our methodology aims to measure faithfulness causally at the activation level, specifically on self-generated reasoning. Unlike previous causal audits, which measure degradation, our interventions carry a known predicted target. In this way, each patch should switch the answer to a specific counterfactual entity derivable by construction. Specifically, we use synthetic multi-hop lookup tasks (2-6 hops). We patch the residual stream at the token span where the model states each intermediate step with the corresponding activations from a counterfactual run. For Qwen3-4B, 76.9% +/- 2.8% of stated steps are causally load-bearing (CLB) at the most responsive mid-network layer (random-position null: 11.3%; patching the underlying prompt fact: 83%, so stated steps carry approximately 96% of the achievable effect). Moreover, the standard behavioral test on the same items yields 88.2%, which overstates causal faithfulness by 11.4 percentage points (item-matched; 111:14 discordant pairs, p < 1e-15) and, for the easiest items, by up to 20 percentage points. This gap also has a clear capability dimension. Qwen3-1.7B is far less causally faithful overall (54.8%), with its faithfulness collapsing as reasoning depth increases (68% at 2 hops to 30% at 6), while Qwen3-4B remains relatively flat. Although stated reasoning can be causally meaningful, standard behavioral tests tend to overestimate its causal faithfulness, particularly on easier examples where model reasoning appears most fluent.

</details>


### [284] [Math Reasoning in LLMs is Organized by Approach, Not Topic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27041)
*Sajad Goudarzi,Samaneh Zamanifard,Moloud Nasiri,Hamed Rahimian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文通过"生成-重放"协议提取8个开源数学大语言模型在5个数学推理数据集上生成解题轨迹的激活-重要性签名,进行无监督聚类分析,发现LLM的内部数学计算组织是按"推理方法"而非"题目主题"进行的。在全部40个模型-数据源组合中,聚类结构均显著优于随机基线;两个独立前沿LLM评判者发现77-82%的真实聚类具有方法级一致性(对照组仅6-11%);改变要求的推理方法会使聚类归属发生偏移(8个模型中7个成立),而表面改写则基本保持聚类不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学推理基准通常按主题/子技能(如算术、代数、几何等)组织,但这些是外部标注,可能并非LLM内部计算的真实组织轴。LLM可能以可复用的"推理方法"(如有限情形枚举、试除法、数量簿记等)而非主题来组织内部数学推理。若如此,主题分层基准和主题平衡训练语料可能掩盖真正决定模型行为的关键维度。

Method: 提出"生成-重放"(generation-replay)协议:首先让模型用确定性贪心解码生成解题过程,然后以教师强制方式重放prompt+生成的完整token序列,提取每个记录模块在推理token位置上的激活幅度(L1归一化、L2均方根)和激活×梯度重要性,拼接为生成-重放签名。使用相同无监督流程(ℓ2归一化→20维截断SVD→ℓ2重归一化→K-Means,k由轮廓系数在5-12间选取)聚类签名。通过三层验证测试结构有效性(C1:与匹配大小的随机分区的轮廓系数比较)、方法级语义一致性(C2:两个独立前沿LLM评判者对聚类示例进行方法标签判断,并与同源对照包比较)、以及方法可控干预(C3:对比"改变要求推理方法"与"仅做表面改写"对聚类归属的影响)。

Result: 在8个模型(DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen 1.5B/7B/14B、Qwen2.5-Instruct 1.5B/7B/14B、Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3、Ministral-8B-Instruct)与5个数学推理数据源(DeepMind Mathematics、GSM8K、MATH、MathQA、NuminaMath)上,共形成40个模型-数据源单元、225个聚类、30400次生成。⑴C1:全部40个单元的实际聚类均优于200个匹配大小随机基线,经验p值均<0.005。⑵C2:仅36.9%的聚类达到主题纯度>0.70,且集中于主题与方法机械绑定的源(如GSM8K、DeepMind Math);Claude Opus-4.7和GPT-5.4两位评判者分别判定82.2%和77.3%的真实聚类具方法级一致性,而同源对照仅6.2%和11.2%,均通过预设阈值(真实≥60%、对照≤20%、差距≥40百分点)。⑷文本聚类基线高度采用Text-TFIDF/SVD对比发现:文本聚类81.8%主题纯度(ARI=0.38),而激活聚类仅36.9%(ARI=0.10),说明文本表面按主题组织而内部激活按方法组织。⑷C3:8个模型中7个完全确认(方法转换率0.60-0.96,改写不变性0.70-0.84);仅Mistral-7B-Instruct部分通过(方法转换率0.96但改写不变性仅0.48,表明其签名对措辞较敏感)。

Conclusion: 数学能力LLM的内部数学计算组织是按"推理方法"而非"基准主题"进行的。三层验证(结构C1、语义C2、干预C3)在8个模型5个数据源上一致支持该结论。这意味着主题分层基准、主题平衡训练语料和主题级评估报告未能充分捕捉LLM组织数学推理的关键维度;基于"生成-重放"聚类的方法感知评估与数据策展无需新增标注即可提供更具信息量的评估、解释和数据设计基础。核心启示是:对模型而言,重要的不在于问题"关于什么",而在于模型"用什么计算方法去解决"。

Abstract: Mathematical reasoning benchmarks are typically organized by topic, but language models may organize their internal computation by reusable reasoning approach instead. In this paper, we investigate whether open math-capable LLMs organize internally by topical sub-skill or by reasoning approach, and we present evidence that the approach is the key. We introduce a generation-replay protocol: a model first generates a solution, after which we replay the exact prompt-plus-generation trajectory and extract activation-importance signatures over the reasoning tokens. We cluster these signatures without supervision across eight models and five mathematical reasoning sources, then evaluate the recovered structure with structural, semantic, and intervention tests. Across all 40 model-source cells, the recovered clusters outperform matched-size random baselines. Two independent frontier-LLM judges find approach-level coherence in 77-82% of real clusters versus 6-11% in within-source controls, and topic-pure clusters usually receive labels finer than the topic itself. In approach-controlled prompting, changing the requested reasoning approach shifts cluster assignment in seven of eight model conditions, whereas paraphrases largely preserve it. These results indicate that math-capable LLMs organize internal mathematical computation by reasoning approach rather than benchmark topic. The implication is that topic-stratified benchmarks and topic-balanced training corpora can still miss the axis that matters: even deliberately topic-balanced corpora may remain imbalanced over reasoning approaches.

</details>


### [285] [Propose, Don't Judge: An Anytime-Valid Referee for LLM Agents That Mine Investment Factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27051)
*Bo Qu,Mingguang Chen,Licheng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"受治理的自我演化"(governed self-evolution)框架，将LLM智能体在量化因子挖掘中的角色严格分离为"提议"与"评判"两部分：智能体可以提出候选因子、诊断退役原因、编写感知探针，但评判因子是否入池的统计裁判(frozen statistical referee)必须冻结且不可被智能体触及。裁判基于提交后市场数据的投注e-process实现anytime-valid的FDR控制，并通过online e-BH准入和e-detector退役规则管理因子生命周期。实验在合成世界、探针环境和CSI 500十年实盘上交叉三种提议者(脚本/老虎机/LLM)与四种裁判(冻结+三种蓄意泄漏)，发现裁判决定假准入数量(5-11倍差距)，LLM在发现真因子数量上优于脚本、与老虎机持平，其独特价值在于编写未见过的故障诊断探针；证书的代价是约500个交易日的等待期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前约25个LLM驱动的因子挖掘系统让智能体独自完成提议、回测、筛选和退役全流程，使用固定批次的t检验或deflated Sharpe等检查表控制统计错误。然而智能体在闭环中根据上一轮判决选择下一轮提案并永不停止，造成可选停止(optional stopping)和多重检验(multiplicity)两类统计错误，预先设定的多重比较校正无法适用。核心问题：因子生命周期中哪些环节应保留智能体的智能，哪些必须由不可篡改的统计程序治理？

Method: 框架核心是提议者-裁判(proposer-referee)分离。裁判包含三个组件：(1)单候选人的投注e-process，基于每日rank-IC的自适应投注，使用预测性破产边界和白化流处理自相关，控制单候选人的FDR；(2)在线e-BH准入规则，在N_v=2000个冻结槽位上控制所有曾提交候选人的FDR在α=0.05以下；(3)e-detector退役规则，使用每日重启的e-process监测部署因子的存活度。关键命题(Proposition 1)证明只要满足四个条件(投注分数G_{t-1}可测、只用提交后数据、库冻结、提案者无提交后信息)，FDR和退役run-length保证对任何提案策略成立。实验设计交叉3种提议者(轮询脚本/折扣UCB老虎机/LLM控制器)×4种裁判(冻结+窥视/自适应阈值/无门)，在3个环境(合成世界植入真因子、探针环境含5类未见故障、CSI 500十年walk-forward)运行540个实验单元，使用共同随机数分离"谁评判"与"谁提议"的效应。

Result: (1)裁判决定假准入数：合成世界中冻结裁判零假准入，三种泄漏裁判0.26-0.85/提交；CSI 500实盘上脚本提议者下冻结裁判11.7 vs 泄漏86-196/活动(5-11倍差距)，LLM提议者下差距2.6-4.9倍。(2)提议者决定产率：LLM在6/6家族×库设置中真因子产率高于脚本，多数设置与老虎机持平，其独特能力是编写新诊断探针(在3/7模型族中显著降低干预遗憾，在其他3族中无差异，从不有害)。(3)证书有价格：被准入真因子需等待约500个交易日，认证组合净Sharpe(+0.33至+0.50)低于无门组合(+0.59至+0.77)，两个机制：日证书无法认证月频动量因子(占差距主因)，以及等待期错过2020年前后基本面因子失效期。500日固定水平比较器说明相同时长下可构建更优组合，表明下一步设计应认证被交易的持有期而非日频。

Conclusion: 判断必须由智能体不可触及的统计程序治理；提议和工具制造是智能体智能具有可测量、有界价值的领域。这一分工——受治理的自我演化——是可测试的，因为证书(统计)、持久性(收益)和执行(交易)三层保持分离且可审计。组合Sharpe混合三者，既不证实也不反驳证书本身。开放设计问题：认证的持有期应与被交易的持有期匹配。当前证据局限：单一市场(CSI 500)、单一面板(2015-2026)、条件零假设保证受控而边际零假设仅被测量、退役保证的run-length界在单活动期间不可用；该框架是测量工具而非投资系统。

Abstract: Language-model agents now run the whole of quantitative factor research: they propose investment factors, backtest them, select the survivors and retire them. We ask which of those jobs an agent should keep. Our answer is governed self-evolution: the agent may propose, and a frozen statistical referee that the agent cannot touch must judge. The referee scores each candidate only on market outcomes revealed after submission, by betting, so its false-discovery guarantee holds at every stopping time for any proposal policy. We cross three proposers (a script, a bandit and a language model) with this referee and with three deliberately leaky ones, in a synthetic world with planted truth, a probe-authoring environment and a ten-year walk-forward on the CSI 500. Who judges sets the number of false admissions: the frozen referee admits 5-11 times fewer sub-threshold factors than the leaky referees under a scripted proposer, and no proposer closes that gap. Who proposes sets the yield: the language model beats the script, matches the bandit, and adds the one capability a bandit lacks, writing its own diagnostic probes. The certificate's price is time: an admitted true factor waits about 500 trading days, and the certified portfolio's Sharpe ratio therefore trails an ungated one. Judging belongs to the procedure; proposing and instrument-making belong to the agent.

</details>


### [286] [Policy-as-Skill: Governed LLM Decision Support with Evidence, Deterministic Control, and Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27087)
*Kabeh Mohsenzadegan,Vahid Tavakkoli,Kyandoghere Kyamakya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Policy-as-Skill (PaS) 架构，将组织政策封装为可执行、版本化的"技能"，通过形式化技能契约实现证据验证、审查路由、版本控制和审计能力。在600项任务基准上以 Gemma4 为后端比较13种方法，发现 PaS+Audit 在治理与审查指标上优于 LLM+RAG，而确定性控制器虽能提升总体准确率至61.2%，但具有强任务依赖性，应选择性而非普遍使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织越来越多地将LLM用于政策、合规与风险决策支持，但现有RAG和Policy-as-Prompt方法缺乏版本控制、审查路由、证据验证失败处理和审计记录等运行时治理机制；自然语言提示能否作为稳定的治理手段也受到质疑，因此需要将政策作为可执行、可版本化的能力进行架构化封装。

Method: 定义形式化策略技能契约 S=⟨n,v,R,E,D,H,A,F,P,C⟩（涵盖身份、版本、检索范围、证据契约、决策模式、审查触发、审计字段、失败行为、提示模板、上下文契约），构建模块化运行时将LLM生成、验证/审计、确定性控制器分离为独立可消融组件；提出四种研究变体（PaS Retrieval、PaS+Controller、PaS+Audit、PaS Full）；在600任务基准（150×4类）上以固定 Gemma4 后端评估13种方法，并进行控制器干预分析与286种组合权重敏感性分析。

Result: PaS+Audit 取得 53.8% 精确准确率、macro-F1 0.346、审查 F1 0.854、引用精度 1.000、政策引用召回 0.984、原生审计完整性 1.000，在审查路由上显著优于 LLM+RAG（0.695）；PaS Full 达最高聚合准确率 61.2%，但 macro-F1 降至 0.306；控制器对冲突检测提升 +59.3 点，对风险分类降低 23.3 点，对政策问答降低 8.7 点，差异具有强任务依赖性；排除冲突子集后 PaS Full 准确率反低于 LLM+RAG 2.2 点；286 种权重敏感性分析显示排名第一方法随权重变化而改变，证实复合指标不应作为主要正确性声明。

Conclusion: PaS 的架构价值在决策、证据与治理属性分离时最为明显：技能检索与审计验证提供强审查路由与可重建记录，无需覆盖模型决策；确定性控制器适用于语义明确的小范围规则（如强制升级），但不适用于解释性问答与模糊分类，应作为政策技能属性而非全局开关选择性启用；人类审查应作为一等治理结果而非错误状态；600任务基准属"模型级未见"但"系统开发知情"评估，系统级泛化与法律正确性结论尚需冻结、独立验证的留出评估。

Abstract: Organizations increasingly use LLMs for policy, compliance, risk, and operational decision support, requiring evidence validation, review routing, version control, and auditability. We introduce Policy-as-Skill (PaS), a modular runtime that packages these functions as executable, versioned policy capabilities. Thirteen methods are evaluated with a fixed Gemma4 backend on 600 development tasks. PaS+Audit achieves 53.8% exact accuracy, macro-F1 0.346, review F1 0.854, citation precision 1.000, policy-reference recall 0.984, and audit completeness 1.000, outperforming LLM+RAG on most governance and review metrics. Deterministic control raises aggregate accuracy to 61.2% but is strongly task dependent, supporting selective rather than universal rule-based intervention.

</details>


### [287] [Provably Complete Generalized Planning with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27105)
*Katharina Stein,Chaahat Jain,Jörg Hoffmann,Alexander Koller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用LLM在Lean中自动生成广义规划及其完备性形式化证明的方法，通过语义保持的PDDL到Lean转换，在13个基准领域中的12个上成功生成了被Lean内核验证的完备性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于LLM生成广义规划（如Python程序）的方法虽然在测试集上表现良好，但仅通过测试集覆盖率和人工分析验证完备性，无法自动证明规划对满足领域约束的所有问题实例均有效。需要一种能够自动生成机器可检查的完备性证明的方法。

Method: 1) 设计语义保持的PDDL到Lean转换（状态分为静态和动态部分，动作编码为归纳类型，目标是部分函数）；2) 通过Python转换器自动从PDDL生成Lean领域表示；3) 借鉴Stein et al. (2026)的方法，让LLM在Lean中直接生成广义规划函数solve；4) 采用两种策略生成完备性证明——基础方法（整体生成与调试）和迭代方法（先生成证明草图再逐声明调试）；5) 引入四种防作弊检查机制防止LLM绕过内核验证。

Result: 在13个IPC基准领域上，使用GPT-5.6-Sol进行评估：所有13个领域都成功生成了被Lean接受且在调试任务上通过VAL验证的广义规划；其中12个领域（仅Transport失败）成功获得了被Lean内核接受的完备性证明，涵盖Delivery、Ferry、Grippers、Heavy、Hiking、Logistics、Satellite（基础方法）以及Blocks、Goldminer、Miconic、Rovers、Spanner（迭代方法）。证明生成时间从9分钟到约54分钟不等。

Conclusion: 这是首次在大规模基准上自动证明广义规划完备性的工作，将该领域从仅能处理简单域（如Gripper、单车辆Logistics）推进到12个常用基准领域，代表了自动广义规划完备性证明技术的重要进展。

Abstract: Generalized planning aims to compute a plan that solves all instances of a planning domain. Recent work has used LLMs to automatically generate and debug such generalized plans in the form of Python programs and achieved perfect test data coverage for several domains. However, whether these generalized plans are actually complete, i.e. solve all instances of the domain, could only be determined by manual evaluation. Here, we present an approach for automatically generating generalized plans in Lean together with proofs of their completeness relative to a specification of the domain constraints provided as input. We introduce a semantic-preserving PDDL-to-Lean conversion, and use an LLM to generate both the generalized plan and the formal proof that it solves every instance satisfying the domain constraints. The correctness of the completeness proof is determined by Lean's kernel. We evaluate our approach on 13 commonly used benchmark domains, using GPT-5.6-Sol as the LLM. For 12 of the domains we obtain generalized plans together with valid completeness proofs. This is a major advancement of the state of the art in automatic generalized-plan completeness proofs.

</details>


### [288] [Do We Need Complex Topology Control? Distinct-Peer Random Routing Improves Cost-Efficiency in Sparse Multi-Agent Debate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27150)
*Boxuan Wang,Zhuoyun Li,Xiaowei Huang,Yi Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文质疑稀疏多智能体辩论（MAD）中复杂拓扑控制机制的必要性，提出一种简单的"无重复随机路由"（Random-NoDup）策略，发现它在简单准确度上与复杂方法相当，且配合短辩论可大幅降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期MAD研究倾向于设计更复杂的拓扑控制方法以提升精度，但已有研究表明简单稀疏通信即可达到竞争性表现。该论文质疑这些复杂拓扑控制是否真的必要，主张应与强简单基线对比。

Method: 提出双轴评估框架：(1) 路由轴比较Ring、Small-world、Random-NoDup及多种控制器（阶段切换、随机切换、不确定性引导、AgentPrune风格学习边）；(2) 停止轴比较固定短轮次、多数稳定性停止、不确定性门控停止。在5个基准（ARC-Challenge、ScienceQA-text、GSM8K、MMLU-Pro、GPQA-Diamond）和4个模型上，使用同构稀疏辩论协议（8智能体，每轮接收2个同伴消息）进行评估。

Result: 全轮次Random-NoDup宏准确率（76.87%）高于Ring（76.36%）、学习边适应（76.27%）及各种路由控制器；短Random-NoDup（轮次3）宏准确率达77.33%，同时token成本从约57k降至约12k（降幅约78%），是测试方法中成本最低且准确率最高的；跨模型实验中，停止带来的成本节省比准确率保留更一致。

Conclusion: Random-NoDup是无状态简单稀疏路由的强基线，复杂的拓扑控制（含学习方法）在评估时应先与该基线对比；短辩论比复杂路由更能有效改善准确度-成本权衡。

Abstract: Multi-agent debate (MAD) has emerged as a promising paradigm for improving the reasoning accuracy of large language models (LLMs) through iterative peer interaction. Communication topology plays a central role in this process, motivating increasingly sophisticated mechanisms that learn, adapt, or dynamically reconfigure agent interactions to improve accuracy or reasoning reliability. Meanwhile, prior studies suggest that much simpler sparse communication can already achieve competitive performance at substantially lower cost. In this work, we take a closer look at sparse MAD and ask whether complex topology control is actually necessary to improve collective reasoning. We find that a simple random-without-replacement routing policy, which lets each agent debate with two distinct and newly sampled peers at every round, provides a surprisingly strong baseline and consistently improves the accuracy-cost trade-off of sparse MAD. Building on this observation, we further study deliberation stopping and show that lightweight stopping can substantially reduce inference cost while preserving competitive accuracy. Our results suggest that sophisticated topology control such as learned topology adaption should be evaluated against strong simple routing and stopping baselines before its additional complexity is justified.

</details>


### [289] [Enhancing Small Language Models for Power Outage Report Generation via Minimum Risk Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27197)
*Hung Phan,Waqwoya Abebe,Youssef Hussein,Supriya Chinthavali,Dalton Lunga,Ali Jannesari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将最小风险训练（MRT）应用于停电报告生成任务，将异构报告转换为符合 CIM IEC 61968-3 标准的 XML。MRT 通过直接优化序列级评估指标（句子级 BLEU 风险），在 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 上将整体准确率从 16.20% 提升至 68.95%，其中标签准确率从 3.56% 提升至 87.17%，验证了序列级优化在领域特定结构化生成中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型语言模型（SLM）在计算效率和部署便利性方面具有优势，但在复杂结构化生成任务上能力有限。停电报告生成要求同时满足语义保真度（文本相似性）和结构合规性（XML 标签正确性），传统的 token 级最大似然训练无法区分完整候选报告的质量差异，因此需要引入序列级优化方法。

Method: 提出 PO-MRT 流程，将 MRT 集成到 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 的参数高效微调中（4-bit 量化 + LoRA）。具体而言：对每个 prompt 采样 K 个候选 XML 报告，使用句子级 BLEU 计算每个候选的风险（1 − BLEU），通过对数概率构建候选的 softmax 分布，最小化期望风险以鼓励模型为更接近参考标准的输出赋予更高概率。

Result: 在 1,000 条停电报告数据上的实验表明：Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 基线整体准确率仅 16.20%（文本 28.85%，标签 3.56%）；MRT 微调后整体准确率达 68.95%（提升 52.75 个百分点），其中文本准确率提升至 50.73%，XML 标签准确率大幅提升至 87.17%，表明序列级 MRT 对结构化 XML 表示学习尤为有效。所有实验在单张 NVIDIA RTX 3090 Ti 上完成。

Conclusion: MRT 是一种实用的领域特定 SLM 优化方法，能够在有限硬件条件下显著提升结构化输出质量。未来工作将探索为不同领域设计不同的序列级风险定义（如基于结构、语义、空间关系等任务特定约束的风险），并可与图神经网络等显式编码关系结构的技术互补。

Abstract: Minimum Risk Training (MRT) enables neural machine translation models to directly optimize sequence-level evaluation metrics instead of relying only on token- level maximum-likelihood objectives Shen et al. [2016]. Although introduced a decade ago, recent work shows renewed potential for risk-based optimization in modern language models Yang et al. [2024], Jinnai et al. [2025]. We apply MRT to power outage report generation for the Outage Data Initiative Nationwide (ODIN), transforming heterogeneous reports into standardized XML compliant with CIM IEC 61968-3. Our MRT approach improves Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct overall accuracy from 16.20% to 68.95%, demonstrating the effectiveness of sequence- level optimization for domain-specific structured generation

</details>


### [290] [XLOG: A CUDA-Native Engine for Neurosymbolic Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27203)
*Levi Dubrovin,Nikita Pospelov,Kirill Sabitov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: xlog 是一个 CUDA 原生的逻辑编程引擎，将神经网络感知与确定性 Datalog、概率推理及认知世界观（epistemic world views）推理统一集成于类型化前端和自管 CUDA 运行时之上。其核心创新在于：经 GPU 验证的知识编译流水线（provenance→CNF→Decision-DNNF→电路）、设备常驻执行核（零追踪 host-device 传输）、最坏情况最优连接（WCOJ）子系统，以及支持端到端可微训练（电路缓存 + 零拷贝 DLPack 导出）的概率路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经符号集成框架（DeepProbLog、NeurASP 等）通常将神经网络置于 GPU、符号推理置于 CPU，导致 PCIe 总线频繁往返成为性能瓶颈；同时不同推理范式（确定性、概率、认知）缺乏统一接口。xlog 旨在通过 CUDA 原生、单一类型化语言的统一基板，在避免数据传输墙的同时支持多种推理模式。

Method: 方法围绕五条核心路线展开：(1) 共享集成模型——神经网络即谓词，通过类型化设备缓冲接口接入各推理路径；(2) GPU 关系执行与认证设备驻留环——自定义 CUDA 关系内核实现半朴素 Datalog 评估，合格递归查询自动选择常驻条件图（核心零追踪传输），WCOJ 子系统通过超图规划器推导变量顺序并以 count→scan→materialize 方式执行；(3) 经 GPU CDCL 验证的知识编译——原子 GPU 哈希表 intern 化 provenance、Tseitin 转 CNF、GPU 原生 D4 编译 d-DNNF、平滑与分层，由 GPU CDCL 在设备上证明 φ∧¬C 与 C∧¬φ 均不可满足（UNSAT 解析证明在设备上校验），仅把已认证的最终平滑电路送入缓存或求值；(4) 端到端可微训练——电路缓存以命题结构哈希为键，前向（log 空间加权模型计数）与反向（伴随梯度）作为分层并行内核，梯度经 DLPack/Arrow 零拷贝导出至 PyTorch；(5) 认知/良基语义推理——know/possible 等模态算子在世界观语义下与 Gelfond 风格语义中，通过 CDCL、连接与电路复用而非另建引擎执行。运行时按分层 crate 架构组织：core → IR/device services → subsystems → pipelines → interfaces，严格依赖下层。

Result: 关键量化结果：(1) MNIST 加法任务电路缓存带来 2.74× 训练加速；(2) WCOJ 子系统相对 xlog 自身二元连接基线取得 27.96× 几何平均加速；(3) MNIST 加法准确率匹配 Scallop（0.9561 对 0.9468），但因基线 epoch 时间随 CPU 配额非单调而不做单 epoch 速度声明；(4) 在五个 hub-skewed 三角形计数用例上，Soufflé-to-fused-xlog 执行时间比随输入规模上升，从 150k 边时的 0.88×（Soufflé 更快）升至 1.2M 边时的 5.54×，融合路径峰值设备分配 85–1,033 MB 对照物化路径 3,287–44,979 MB，所有分支均可完成；(5) 精确推理结果与 ProbLog2 正确性等价但更慢；(7) CAVIAR 视频基准上，纯通过符号信用训练的近邻谓词替代手工几何谓词时保持留出准确率，但在 Event-Calculus 规则搜索内未能通过十折交叉验证，且在无泄漏划分上所学子句不可迁移；(8) Brest AIS 海事语料（3,548 个 gold rendezVous 区间）上，加权子句相对 crisp 选择提升微 F1 0.065，单次时序训练一遍可精确复现该结果。

Conclusion: xlog 通过类型化统一语言与设备常驻数据平面提供了一种新型 CUDA 原生神经符号集成，在已验证的知识编译、设备常驻执行核与可微电路缓存支撑下，能够为大符号工作负载与可训练实体嵌入的 GPU 推理提供有效路径；同时其工作负载适用域明确针对字节工作集受 NVIDIA GPU 容量约束的场景。作者承认若干开放边界：MNIST 加法等小任务训练时间受基线波动干扰、精确推理相对 ProbLog2 仍偏慢、CAVIAR 上经符号信用训练的检测器在 Event-Calculus 规则搜索内部未能泛化、海事语料结果未与已发表结果做时序网格匹配的对比，以及若干扩展（开放生成器、动态数据库变更、无限制 call/N 等）被刻意排除在合约之外。整体上，xlog 在传输墙规避与概率路径可微性方面提供了机器可校验的中间表示保障，但其优越性呈任务依赖式——相对 Soufflé 在小规模上不可取胜，相对 ProbLog2 在精确推理速度上仍有差距。

Abstract: xlog is a CUDA-native logic programming engine integrating neural perception with deterministic Datalog, probabilistic inference, and epistemic world views through a typed frontend and provider-owned CUDA runtime. Its reasoning modes share device data planes, but their execution boundaries differ: ordinary Datalog and exact inference are host-orchestrated, while certified resident recursive and Monte Carlo sampled cores record zero tracked host-device transfers before a bounded terminal receipt. The probabilistic path supports end-to-end gradients through GPU knowledge compilation from provenance to CNF to Decision-DNNF, exact weighted model counting, and backward gradients. A final smoothed circuit is certified against its source formula before caching or evaluation. Circuit caching yields a 2.74x MNIST-addition training speedup; a worst-case-optimal join subsystem yields a 27.96x geometric-mean gain over xlog's binary-join baseline. MNIST-addition accuracy matches Scallop's (0.9561 versus 0.9468), but no per-epoch speed claim is made because baseline epoch time varies with CPU quota. In five hub-skewed triangle-counting cases, the Souffle-to-fused-xlog execution-time ratio rises from 0.88x at 150k edges, where Souffle is faster, to 5.54x at 1.2M; fused peak device allocations are 85-1,033 MB versus 3,287-44,979 MB for the materializing arm. Exact inference is correctness-equivalent to but slower than ProbLog2. On a public video benchmark, a proximity predicate trained only through symbolic credit replaces hand-set geometry at unchanged held-out accuracy; within Event-Calculus rule search it fails ten-fold cross-validation and does not transfer on a leak-free split. On a maritime corpus, weighted clauses beat crisp selection by 0.065 F1, with the result reproduced by one chronological training pass.

</details>


### [291] [CAVEAT: Towards Robust Computer-Use Agents in Incentive-Misaligned Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27273)
*Yuxuan Li,Will Epperson,Wesley Deng,Zezhou Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CAVEAT基准，评估计算机使用代理(CUAs)在激励错位环境(如电商平台)下是否仍能保持用户目标。结果显示代理在对照条件下购买最优品的率达78.6%，但在启用八种常见引导机制后骤降至17.3%；通过轨迹分析诊断出三类失败模式，并提出CAVEAT-Harness推理框架与CAVEAT-27B后训练模型以缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CUA基准多关注协作环境或显式攻击场景，鲜少评估当环境本身具有与用户目标冲突的激励时，代理是否仍能坚持用户最优决策。这一缺失在代理日益代表用户进行网购的现实场景下尤为关键。

Method: 1) 构建包含9个高保真电商环境的CAVEAT基准(标准版52任务、困难版含2112件商品的5任务)；2) 基于学术与监管资料提出八族分类体系(赞助位、偏好排序、滴漏定价、促销框架、默认捆绑、稀缺信号、信任信号、摩擦阻碍)；3) 通过匹配对照实验(用户请求、商品目录、最优品均不变，仅切换引导机制)评估18种模型配置；4) 结合轨迹分析、置顶反客对、激励隔离等针对性消融，识别失败机制；5) 提出CAVEAT-Harness(结构化任务规范+验证工具)与CAVEAT-27B(两阶段后训练)两类干预方法。

Result: 1) CAVEAT-Standard最优购买率从对照78.6%降至激励错位下17.3%(降61.3个百分点)，五个模型家族均出现退化(降幅37.2–78.9个百分点)；2) CAVEAT-Hard中GPT-5.6-Sol高推理从90.0%降至0.0%；3) 诊断出三类失败：目标漂移(偏好顺序反转改变决策)、过早终止搜索(置顶后购买率从0/60升至48/60)、证据未明即承诺(滴漏定价使35/60次均选促销品)；4) CAVEAT-Harness将GPT-5.6-Terra从11.7%提升至66.7%，GPT-5.6-Sol高推理从0.0%提升至80.0%(但耗时2.6倍、步数2.3倍)；5) CAVEAT-27B在非笔记本类CAVEAT-Shop任务上将Qwen3.5-27B+4.2%提升至22.9%。

Conclusion: 激励鲁棒性是委托代理面临的独立挑战；CAVEAT表明现实电商激励机制可严重损害代理的用户目标保持能力，其失败可追溯到目标漂移、过早终止搜索和证据未明即承诺三类决策缺陷；基于该诊断的推理时结构化干预(CAVEAT-Harness)与针对性后训练(CAVEAT-27B)可显著提升鲁棒性，但需权衡交互成本与跨域泛化能力。

Abstract: Computer-use agents (CUAs) increasingly act on behalf of users online. What happens when the environments they operate in have incentives that do not align with the user's? In online marketplaces, for example, platforms may favor some products over others, potentially steering agents away from the user's objective. Existing CUA benchmarks cover cooperative settings or explicit attacks, but do not test whether agents preserve user objectives when the environment itself has a stake in the outcome. We introduce CAVEAT, a controlled benchmark spanning nine marketplace environments and a taxonomy of eight common steering mechanisms. Across five model families, agents purchase the user-optimal product in 78.6% of matched-control episodes but only 17.3% when steering mechanisms are enabled. Larger models and increased reasoning improve robustness, but substantial failures persist. Our trajectory analysis and targeted ablations identify three points where steering enters the decision process: (1) agents distort the user's priorities, (2) prematurely narrow the set of alternatives they consider, and (3) commit before resolving decision-relevant evidence. Guided by this diagnosis, we develop CAVEAT-Harness, which directly targets these failure modes and raises user-optimal purchasing by 55.0%. Targeted post-training further improves a smaller open model. These results establish incentive robustness as a distinct challenge for delegated agents, diagnose how it fails, and show that targeted interventions can substantially improve it.

</details>


### [292] [DRSR: Learning Set-Level Deletion Risk for Efficient Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27276)
*Mingxuan Wang,Bo Wang,Fei Luo,Guorun Yao,Chao Ning,Yinglong Guo,Hongyue Chen,Yanbiao Ma,Jungong Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DRSR提出了一种面向长程智能体的历史压缩方法，将智能体历史裁剪重新建模为集合级风险约束的删除集选择问题，通过离线反事实监督训练一个轻量评分器，预测集合级删除风险，并在在线裁剪时通过风险门控结合弃权机制选择最大可行删除集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文压缩方法通常独立打分历史单元，但删除多个单元的安全性无法由单元素分数可靠决定——冗余证据、小效应累积以及删除后剩余信息都会影响集合级风险。同时，真实删除危害在线决策时不可知（依赖于尚未产生的下一步输出），因此需要一种能建模集合级依赖、并能利用离线监督信号的方法。

Method: DRSR的核心方法包含三个层面：(1) 离线监督——通过联合删除协议有效的历史Block并测量同一记录的下一个输出在teacher-forced似然上的变化来构造精确的集合级反事实标签H_t(A)，并据此定义不安全事件；(2) 关系型集合风险预测——使用冻结的Qwen3.5-9B作为表征骨干，提取候选Block与当前决策状态之间的注意力关系、隐藏状态关系、固定投影语义关系以及结构性控制特征，编码为1154维向量，再通过轻量集合编码器（SetEncoder）联合建模被删除集合D、保留集合R以及集合内与集合间交互（包括删除集合内的平均无序对乘积统计p_D、μ_D⊙μ_R以及|μ_D - μ_R|），输出集合级危害预测和不安全分数；(3) 风险门控在线选择与弃权——在部署时对少量结构有效的删除候选集（最多4个候选，最多删除3个，即最多14个子集）进行批量轻量评分，在近期性、协议、预算和学习风险约束下选择使token节省最大的可行集，无可行集时弃权保留原请求。训练目标为排序损失+0.30·SmoothL1+0.40·BCE+0.10·Brier的三模型集成。

Result: 在WorkBuddyBench Full260上DRSR将平均奖励从0.699提升到0.802，同时将总模型token减少20.820%；Security领域改进最大（0.478→0.756，token降幅23.55%）。在固定Eval40对比集上以1.211M token/任务达到0.794奖励，比未压缩智能体减少35.850% token，且优于所有对比方法（滑动窗口、周期摘要、PACE、LLMLingua-2、Sculptor、ACON、CoMem等）。机制分析表明：单元素加性聚合仅达到58.94%的排序准确率，pair交互能将联合危害重建误差从0.018降至0.008（54.4%降低）；交互残差随删除基数增大（size 4时均值0.027>阈值0.020）；消融实验证实保留上下文特征、对池化和弃门控各自贡献不可替代。

Conclusion: DRSR证明了对长程智能体历史进行状态条件化、集合级风险建模的必要性——历史价值本质上是决策相关的，删除风险无法从独立评分的单元素可靠恢复。离线反事实监督结合轻量在线评分器与弃权门控，为无固定上下文窗口的安全压缩提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Long-horizon language-model agents accumulate reasoning traces, tool exchanges, and observations whose relevance changes with the current decision. Existing compression strategies often score historical units independently, but the safety of deleting several units is generally not determined by their singleton scores: redundant evidence, accumulated small effects, and the information that remains after deletion all matter. We introduce Direct Relational Set-Risk Pruning (DRSR), which formulates agent-history compression as risk-constrained selection over deletion sets. Offline, DRSR constructs exact counterfactual supervision by jointly deleting protocol-valid history Blocks and measuring the change in teacher-forced likelihood of the same recorded next output. A lightweight scorer then predicts set-level harm from online-visible relations between candidate history and the current pre-action state, together with deleted-retained and pairwise set structure. At deployment, DRSR evaluates a small set of structurally valid deletion candidates with the lightweight scorer and removes the largest feasible set under recency, protocol, budget, and learned-risk constraints, abstaining when no set is sufficiently safe. On WorkBuddyBench Full260, DRSR increases mean reward from 0.699 to 0.802 while reducing total model tokens by 20.820%. On the fixed Eval40 comparison, it obtains 0.794 reward at 1.211M tokens per task, using 35.850% fewer tokens than the uncompressed agent. Mechanistic analyses and ablations further show that decision-conditioned relations, retained-context information, pair interactions, and abstention each contribute to reliable pruning.

</details>


### [293] [TimeEvo: Failure-Driven Self-Evolution of a Time Series Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27277)
*Jie Yang,Yan Zheng,Jiarui Sun,Xiran Fan,Junpeng Wang,Liang Wang,Zelin Xu,Qinghua Liu,Zhengyu Fang,Yiwei Cai,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TimeEvo 是一个面向时间序列问答任务的智能体自演化框架。它从空工具库出发，通过分析智能体的失败案例来自动合成"仅输出证据"的工具，并经过两层配对验证门控（per-tool预筛 + 整库配对准入门）才能部署，从而解决"人–智能体工具错配"（专家预置工具未必有用甚至有害）和"静默伤害"（自我修订破坏正确答案但总分不变）两个问题。在10个时间序列QA任务和3个LLM骨干上均一致提升（+1.5到+14.7分），且廉價模型上生长的工具库可直接迁移到更强模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列智能体依赖人类预先配置的固定工具库，存在两个问题：(1) 专家工具未必匹配智能体在运行时真正需要的工具（如TimeART的21个工具在异常检测任务上反而下降5.8–8.8分）；(2) 通用自修订方法会破坏已答对的题目（如Self-Refine改变147个答案、破坏56个，但总分几乎不变）。两者根源相同：工具供给是部署前决定且按平均分评估，而工具是否有用是逐题运行时决定。

Method: TimeEvo 采用"按轮次演化 + 残差链推理"架构：每轮从智能体当前训练集错误出发，按"工具是否触发"分区聚类成失败桶；局部规划器为每个桶写"测量契约"（需测什么、输入、判据、作用域），全局规划器合并重叠契约；综合器以≤3次重试生成仅返回数值证据的确定性Python工具（可选训练侧校准决策树，BalAcc≥0.55），运行时沙箱强制白名单；先做32错误预算的预筛（需至少一次可归因修复），再在验证集做整库配对准入（计算(h−m)/n的下置信界δ_lb，严格通过需h>0且δ_lb>0）；不通过则按归因逐工具一次性剪枝（仅当明显有害），重测一次；通过后还需校准作用域再做最终配对门控。推理时采用"冻结残差"：基础智能体先作答，仅当新工具在其声明作用域内且实际运行时才复核/替换，否则保留原答案。

Result: 在10个时间序列QA任务（TemporalBench T1–T4、TimeSeriesExam、Merrill、Time-MQA Anomaly/Classification、MMTS Match、TSAQA-DT）与3个LLM骨干（GPT-5.6-luna/terra、GPT-5.4-mini）上，TimeEvo从空库出发在全部30个（任务×骨干）单元上均取得正增益，平均提升+7.21到+8.79分；相比之下TimeART 21工具在异常检测上-5.79到-8.75，Self-Refine平均-0.58到-0.93，vote@5仅+0.79到+1.64，few-shot ICL在分类任务上-9.17到-10.42。luna上演化的工具库迁移到Claude-opus/sonnet-5、GPT-5.6-sol上，30格中29格为正，30格中21格强于luna自身（异常检测上Claude模型获+26.25/+26.50 vs luna的+14.69）。消融显示：无校准则两个异常库均被门控拒绝；无门控则在mini上-1.25；作用域去除代价1–9.25分；替换式过覆比残差复核多修复16–25题但多破坏13–51题；用Chronos或21工具作根库均劣于空库（−3.04到−21.50）。门控校准严格区为0假阳/1假阴，边界区1假阳，工具在1–3轮内稳定。

Conclusion: 智能体自身的失败比人类预设更能准确规定所需工具。失败驱动的工具合成 + 配对测量式门控可同时修复工具错配并防护静默伤害；廉價模型上演化的工具库可无损迁移到更强模型，使工具建设与模型升级解耦。

Abstract: Time series agents answer analytical questions by calling external tools, and which tools they carry is decided by people before the agent runs. However, we identify two failures in this setup. Human-Agent Tool Misalignment: a library of 21 expert-curated tools helps on some tasks and hurts on others, dropping anomaly accuracy under every backbone we test. Silent Harm: one round of generic self-revision changes 147 answers and breaks 56 of them, while the final score moves by less than a point. Both follow from the same gap: whether a tool helps is decided question by question at runtime, while tools are supplied in advance and judged by a single average. To address this, we propose TimeEvo, which clusters an agent's diagnosed failures into capability gaps, plans a measurement for each, synthesizes evidence-only tools that fill them, and admits the candidate library only through a paired admission gate. Experiments on ten time series QA tasks and three backbones show that TimeEvo, starting from an empty library, improves accuracy on every task and every backbone, and that a library grown on a cheap model still gains when it is installed into stronger ones. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [294] [EnSIMem: Entity-Structured Indexing for Long-Term Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27279)
*Xuanyu Meng,Xing Fan,Xinyi Fan,Chenlei Guo,Yixuan Xie,Jiawei Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EnSIMem 提出了一种面向长期对话记忆的实体结构化索引架构，通过离线主题连贯片段构建和在线需求感知检索，在两个基准上取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期记忆系统存在可扩展性与特异性的矛盾：摘要会丢失细节与溯源，而匿名文本块检索无法精确定位实体、属性与事件，导致难以回答具体、含时间或聚合成分的问题。

Method: EnSIMem 将记忆构建与检索解耦：(1) 离线将对话切分为"主题连贯的连续片段"，从中抽取形如 [entity][entity_type][property:value] 的结构化索引记录，并保留源对话、时间戳与多模态字段；(2) 在线将查询分解为多条"证据需求"，按推理类型（point/temporal/compositional/aggregation）分配证据预算，结合结构化实体-属性查找与稠密检索回退；(3) 最终从保留的源片段而非索引条目生成答案，从而保留溯源。

Result: 在 LoCoMo 上 GPT-4o-mini 评估整体准确率 90.6%、Qwen3-32B 评估 90.0%；在 LongMemEval 上两种评估模型均达 92.8%，相对 MEMORA(P) 提升约 5.4 个百分点。消融实验表明：主题连贯片段优于按轮次（+5.68pp）和按会话（+2.27pp）粒度；结构化检索显著优于纯稠密检索（96.59% vs 86.36%）；自适应证据预算优于固定 top-k；适度抽象的属性粒度（Broad）优于过细或过粗的粒度。端到端平均时延 14.4 秒，检索平均 1.92 步。

Conclusion: EnSIMem 通过将"结构"用作记忆的寻址系统而非对话的有损压缩，将长期历史压缩为短小、查询特定、保留源证据的推理缓冲；该证据保留范式在长期对话记忆基准上达到 SOTA，并为未来扩展到程序性与语义性记忆提供了可追溯的基础。

Abstract: An agent that interacts with users over long periods must recall facts, preferences, events, and changes from a continuously growing interaction history. Existing memory systems often compress interactions into generic summaries or retrieve anonymous text chunks, making it difficult for an agent to identify the correct entity, property, and supporting evidence. We present EnSIMem, an entity-structured long-term memory architecture for an agent. During offline construction, the system organizes interactions into theme-coherent episodes and builds dialogue-grounded index entries of the form [entity][entity type][property:value]. Each entry preserves its source turns, temporal information, and available multimodal fields. During online interaction, the agent's request is decomposed into evidence requirements whose properties are aligned with the memory index. Entity-property lookup and adaptive retrieval then collect the evidence needed for point, temporal, compositional, and aggregation reasoning. The agent generates its response from the preserved source evidence rather than from lossy memory summaries. On long-term agent-memory benchmarks, EnSIMem achieves high answer accuracy while maintaining compact contexts and favorable online efficiency. These results show that entity-structured indexing and episode-level provenance provide a reliable foundation for long-term memory in agents. The code of our model is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [295] [Hunyuan-A13B Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27284)
*Tencent Hunyuan Team,Ao Liu,Botong Zhou,Can Xu,Chayse Zhou,ChenChen Zhang,Chengcheng Xu,Chenhao Wang,Decheng Wu,Dengpeng Wu,Dian Jiao,Dong Du,Dong Wang,Feng Zhang,Fengzong Lian,Guanghui Xu,Guanwei Zhang,Hai Wang,Haipeng Luo,Han Hu,Huilin Xu,Jiajia Wu,Jianchen Zhu,Jianfeng Yan,Jiaqi Zhu,Jihong Zhang,Jinbao Xue,Jun Xia,Junqiang Zheng,Kai Liu,Kai Zhang,Kai Zheng,Kejiao Li,Keyao Wang,Lan Jiang,Lixin Liu,Lulu Wu,Mengyuan Huang,Peijie Yu,Peiqi Wang,Qian Wang,Qianbiao Xiang,Qibin Liu,Qingfeng Sun,Richard Guo,Ruobing Xie,Saiyong Yang,Shaohua Chen,Shihui Hu,Shuai Li,Shuaipeng Li,Shuang Chen,Suncong Zheng,Tao Yang,Tian Zhang,Tinghao Yu,Weidong Han,Weijie Liu,Weijin Zhou,Weikang Wang,Wesleye Chen,Xiao Feng,Xiaoqin Ren,Xingwu Sun,Xiong Kuang,Xuemeng Huang,Xun Cao,Yanfeng Chen,Yang Du,Zhen Yang,Yangyu Tao,Yaping Deng,Yi Shen,Yigeng Hong,Yiqi Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Hunyuan-A13B是腾讯混元团队发布的开源MoE大语言模型，总参数800亿但仅激活130亿。该模型通过精心策划的20T tokens预训练语料、双模式CoT推理框架（快思考/慢思考）、结构化的SFT+RL后训练流程，在数学、科学、推理、Agent等任务上以显著较小的激活参数量实现了与更大模型相当的竞争力，同时保持高推理吞吐量，适合延迟敏感场景部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有顶级LLM（如GPT-4o、o1、Gemini 2.5、DeepSeek-R1、Qwen3）虽能力强大但部署成本高、推理延迟大，限制了其广泛应用。本文旨在通过MoE稀疏激活架构，构建一个能力接近顶级模型但部署成本和延迟显著降低的开源LLM。

Method: 1) **架构**：采用细粒度MoE（1共享专家+64细粒度非共享专家，每次激活8个非共享专家），32层/32头/8 KV头，隐藏维度4096，使用SwiGLU激活函数、GQA注意力、128K词表；2) **预训练**：三阶段训练（基础训练20T tokens → 快速退火300B tokens → 长上下文扩展至32K/256K），配合STEM数据增强和多维度难度分级框架；3) **后训练**：两阶段——推理导向微调（数学/代码/逻辑/科学四域SFT+GRPO强化学习，使用Outcome Reward和Sandbox反馈）与全场景微调（涵盖创意写作、多语言、长上下文、Agent等8+子域，采用统一GRM+领域特定奖励服务）；4) **双模式CoT**：通过`/no_think`和`/think`控制标签切换快慢思考模式，统一结构化输出格式。

Result: 基础模型评测：Hunyuan-A13B以13B激活参数在14项基准中12项超越Hunyyuan-Large（52B激活），几乎全面优于Qwen2.5-72B，并在12项中7项超越Qwen3-A22B（22B激活参数）。后训练慢思考模式下，AIME2024达87.3（最高）、BBH 89.1（最高）、ZebraLogic 84.7（最高）、GPQA-Diamond 71.2；Agent任务BFCL v3 78.3、ComplexFuncBench 61.2、C³-Bench 63.5均最高。快思考模式下ComplexFuncBench 74.0、C³-Bench 65.4均领先。长上下文方面，PenguinScrolls 87.7（与Gemini 2.5 Pro的88.3相当）、RULER平均76.7（64K-128K区间73.9，仅次于Gemini 2.5 Pro）。推理吞吐：单batch 190.84 tokens/s，batch=32时达1981.99 tokens/s。

Conclusion: Hunyuan-A13B证明了通过精心设计的MoE架构、高质量数据策划、结构化SFT+RL后训练以及双模式CoT框架，仅以130亿激活参数即可在广泛任务上达到与显著更大的LLM相当的竞争力。其高推理吞吐量和对延迟敏感场景的适配性，使其特别适合资源受限的实际部署；开源发布将推动LLM研究的持续进步与高效实用化部署。

Abstract: We present Hunyuan-A13B, an open-source large language model based on a Mixture-of-Experts architecture. It contains 80 billion total parameters but activates only 13 billion during inference, balancing model capability, computational efficiency, and deployment cost. The model is pretrained on a rigorously filtered 20T-token corpus with enhanced STEM data curation, improving factual reliability and reasoning ability. High-quality supervised fine-tuning and large-scale reinforcement learning further enhance its overall performance. Hunyuan-A13B also introduces a dual-mode Chain-of-Thought framework that adapts reasoning depth to task complexity: fast thinking for routine queries and slow thinking for complex, multi-step problems. Evaluations show competitive performance across mathematics, science, programming, general language understanding, and agent tasks, often approaching that of much larger models. Its high inference throughput makes it suitable for latency-sensitive applications. We release Hunyuan-A13B to support open research and practical LLM deployment.

</details>


### [296] [Memory Control Signals Emerge Before Action in Long Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27286)
*Mingxuan Wang,Guorun Yao,Fei Luo,Yinglong Guo,Chao Ning,Bo Wang,Hongyue Chen,Yanbiao Ma,Jungong Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究长视野语言模型智能体中"何时压缩、何时回忆历史证据"的内存管理问题，发现模型在产生下一个动作前的隐藏状态中已经编码了压缩与回忆的需求信号，并据此提出结合状态引导压缩与外部证据检索的 PaMER / PaMER+ 框架，显著降低上下文开销同时保持任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视野 LM 智能体的交互历史不断累积，现有上下文管理方法主要关注如何压缩或检索历史，但很少回答一个更根本的问题：模型自身是否在执行操作之前就已经在内部表征了内存操作的需求？本文试图从模型隐藏状态中读取这种预动作（preaction）的内存决策信号。

Method: 构建包含 72,912 个决策点、600 道问题的内存决策语料，由 LLM 标注压缩/回忆需求；在冻结的 Qwen3.5-9B 上提取动作前的最终隐藏状态，训练线性探针与单隐层 MLP 探针，跨五个 question-grouped 种子评估；逐层分析信号形成模式，并通过削减上下文（仅保留任务前缀 + 最近两个交互块 Recent2，占 26% token）以及选择性恢复原始历史证据来检验决策信息的来源；据此设计 PaMER：使用五头多数投票的状态引导压缩 + Top3 摘要检索；PaMER+ 额外引入逐步骤（step-level）证据选择，只恢复当前任务所需的原始历史步骤。

Result: 1) 隐藏状态对压缩/回忆的 AUROC 分别为 0.831/0.765，显著优于长度/轮次/工具类型等可观察元数据（0.758/0.549）；2) 压缩信号随层数加深而增强，回忆信号在 block 16 即达到峰值 0.780；3) Recent2（26% token）即可保留大部分决策信号（压缩 AUROC 0.776，回忆 0.761），而 Selective Raw Top5 原始证据恢复在 66.2% token 下取得压缩 0.832、回忆 0.769 的最优 AUROC；4) 在 WorkBuddyBench 260 任务上，PaMER+ 使 DeepSeek-V4-Flash token 用量降低 71.7%（2.69M→0.76M）且平均得分提升 +1.7；在 Pilot40 上多个上下文管理基线对比中取得强任务性能与较低 token 预算；跨 5 个模型 backbone 的评估显示 token 普遍降低 27%–88%，但任务表现因模型而异（如 Qwen3.8-Flash +6.8、GPT-5.6-Luna −2.1）。

Conclusion: 长视野智能体的内存需求可在动作执行前从语言模型隐藏状态中被预测，且包含超出上下文长度与交互元数据的额外信息；压缩与回忆信号在模型深度中呈现不同的形成模式；紧凑的近期上下文足以支撑大部分内存决策，而选择性恢复的历史原始证据补充了近端上下文所缺失的长程依赖。基于此设计的 PaMER / PaMER+ 框架在多个模型 backbone 与基准上显著降低上下文消耗并维持具有竞争力的任务表现，连接了"内存需求的内部预测"与"面向长视野 LM 智能体的实用上下文管理"。

Abstract: Long horizon language model agents continuously accumulate interaction history, increasing computational cost while making relevant information harder to preserve and reuse. Existing context management methods mainly focus on how to compress or retrieve history, but largely leave open whether the model itself already represents the need for these memory operations before they occur. We study the hidden state immediately before each agent action and find that compression and recall needs are already encoded in the model's internal representations. These signals cannot be explained by simple context length or interaction progress, and they exhibit distinct formation patterns across model depth. We further show that most memory decision information is preserved in a compact recent context, while selectively restored historical evidence complements the long range dependencies that recent context misses. Based on these findings, we propose Preaction Memory with Evidence Retrieval (PaMER), which combines state guided compression with external evidence retrieval. PaMER+ further introduces step level evidence selection to recover only the historical information required by the current task. Experiments on WorkBuddyBench, across multiple context management baselines and model backbones, show that our framework substantially reduces context consumption while maintaining competitive task performance.

</details>


### [297] [PotARCin: Multi-Dimensional Evaluation of Skill Acquisition in Abstract Reasoning Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27288)
*Claas Beger,Ryan Yi,Melanie Mitchell*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PotARCin基准，将ARC任务的评估从单一输出网格生成扩展到五个维度（Definition、Classification、Constrained Generation、Editing、Inversion），使用程序化生成器和验证器进行动态评估，揭示当前SOTA模型在抽象规则理解上存在显著的"波将金式理解"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准ARC评估仅测试模型能否生成正确的输出网格，无法反映模型是否真正掌握了可复用的抽象规则。作者受"Potemkin Understanding"框架启发，认为高准确率可能掩盖模型在概念理解上的真实缺陷，需要多维度评估来全面衡量抽象推理能力的深度。

Method: 利用任务特定的generator和verifier程序，定义五个评估维度：Definition（写出实现规则的Python程序）、Classification（判断候选对是否符合规则）、Constrained Generation（生成新示例对）、Editing（修复被破坏的对）、Inversion（由输出反推输入）。使用五种损坏类型构造无效对。在ARC-AGI-1训练集和新构建的P-ARC（50个手工任务）上评估五个前沿模型。

Result: 在ARC-AGI-1上，全任务准确率比标准输出准确率低25-52个百分点；多维评估会重新排序标准准确率相似的模型（如Kimi在输出网格上最弱但在全任务准确率上接近Claude）；P-ARC上所有模型全任务准确率仅1-8%；即使定义程序正确，模型在其他维度上的回答与该程序一致率仅21.1%（其他正确时达99.1%）；生成采样预算增加后，GPT-5.4在15/20已通过任务上出现失败。

Conclusion: 标准ARC准确率不足以反映抽象技能习得的真实水平，模型常出现正确形式化规则却无法一致应用的情况；ARC-AGI-1不应被视为已解决；PotARCin通过生成式多维评估提供了更严格、更有诊断力的抽象规则能力评估框架。

Abstract: The Abstraction and Reasoning Corpus (ARC) has become a prominent benchmark for evaluating general abstract reasoning and fluid intelligence in AI models. Yet standard ARC evaluation considers only a single capability: producing the correct output grid for a test input. We argue that this narrow format fails to evaluate the diversity of abilities that genuine abstract skill acquisition should enable. We introduce PotARCin, a benchmark that extends ARC by assessing understanding of a task's underlying abstract rule across five dimensions: Definition, Classification, Constrained Generation, Editing, and Inversion. PotARCin employs programmatic methods to generate new task instances and transform given inputs for a given ARC task, enabling dynamic generative sampling beyond fixed input-output pairs. Across five state-of-the-art models evaluated on the ARC-AGI-1 training set, we observe a 25-52 percentage-point performance gap between standard ARC evaluation and evaluation on PotARCin, and find that multi-dimensional evaluation reorders models that standard accuracy ranks alike. We further investigate effects of generative sampling, difficulty of corruption types, and questions of self-consistency, showing that models frequently contradict their own formalized rule even where they have stated it correctly. We also introduce P-ARC, a held-out hand-crafted test set, on which models achieve 1-8% accuracy across all five dimensions, underscoring the importance of more holistic evaluations of abstract reasoning capabilities.

</details>


### [298] [Sparse-Observation Atmospheric Thermal Forecasting with Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Climate-Aware Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27290)
*Tannaz Goodarzvand Chegini,Elyas Shivanian,Behzad Karimi,Faraz Dadgostari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于物理信息神经网络（PINN）的大气温位短期预报方法，以压力坐标热力学平流-源方程为物理约束，使用ERA5再分析数据，在稀疏温度观测条件下评估其相对于持续性、局部趋势和两种匹配神经网络基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候感知数字孪生系统需要短时大气温度预报，但实际观测往往空间稀疏不完整。纯数据驱动方法在稀疏观测和长预报时效下表现不佳，需要引入物理约束来提升预报精度和可靠性。现有PINN在大气预报中的研究多集中于场重构而非未来温度预测，且对预报时效、观测密度、区域迁移性和地形适用性的综合评估不足。

Method: 方法核心包括：(1) 物理约束：采用压力坐标系下热力学平流-源方程，预报量为位温；(2) 非绝热源闭合：基于前12小时历史数据拟合经验源项（含潜热代理、日变化谐波等五个系数），冻结后用于未来预报训练；(3) PINN架构：四层64单元tanh MLP，通过硬约束方式精确满足初始条件；(4) 两阶段训练：历史源学习阶段（1500步Adam）后冻结源参数，再分1/2/3小时渐进式扩展未来物理训练（各1000步）；(5) 稀疏观测设计：采用最远点采样构建5%/10%/25%嵌套虚拟观测网络；(6) 对照基线：持续性、局部趋势、坐标神经网络、力感知神经网络，及oracle基线包络。

Result: 俄克拉荷马开发期：PINN平均RMSE改善17.3%，24个预报案例中17个获胜；时序复制期改善18.0%。观测密度扫描显示，3小时预报改善从密集观测的23.8%在5%密度下仍保持14.6%，且改善随时效增长（1小时8.1%→3小时23.8%）。阿拉巴马跨区域评估（固定协议）：3小时改善19.7%，三种观测布局下改善范围19.7%-24.4%。蒙大拿地形压力测试：925hPa层在内评估域内完全无效，PINN出现约17.5%的3小时性能退化，揭示了固定气压层表示在地形复杂区域的适用性局限。

Conclusion: 物理约束的收益随预报时效增长而增强，在严重观测稀疏条件下仍持续存在，并能跨区域迁移。但其有效性受限于固定垂直坐标表示在复杂地形上的适用性。未来工作应扩展至独立观测验证、更多天气类型和地形自适应垂直坐标表示。该方法为气候感知数字孪生系统中物理约束预报组件提供了有效但有边界的证据。

Abstract: Short-horizon forecasts of atmospheric temperature are needed to support climate-aware digital-twin systems, but such forecasts must be produced where thermal observations are incomplete. This study evaluates a physics-informed neural network for potential-temperature forecasting, constrained by a pressure-coordinate thermodynamic advection-source equation and a diabatic-source closure fit from the preceding 12-hour period and frozen before future-time training. Using hourly ERA5 reanalysis at three pressure levels, the model is evaluated as a conditional hindcast at lead times of one, two and three hours against persistence, local-trend, and two matched neural-network baselines, one of which receives the same future meteorological forcing as the PINN, helping distinguish the physical constraint from access to future forcing. In an Oklahoma development case, mean RMSE improvement over the strongest baseline grew from 8.1\% at one hour to 23.8\% at three hours; under an observation-density sweep down to 5\% of candidate locations, this 3-hour advantage remained 14.6--16.9\%, with no evidence that lower density improves performance. Under a fixed protocol transferred to an Alabama heat event with three virtual-observation layouts, three-hour improvement ranged 19.7-24.4\% with consistent origin-level wins. A parallel Montana stress test, in which fixed pressure levels intersected complex terrain, produced a three-hour degradation of roughly 17.5\%, identifying a terrain-related applicability limit of the formulation. Together, these results indicate that the physics constraint's benefit grows with forecast horizon, persists under severe observation sparsity, and transfers across regions, but is bounded by the validity of a fixed vertical-coordinate representation over complex terrain, evidence relevant to physics-constrained components of climate-aware forecasting and digital-twin systems.

</details>


### [299] [Large Knowledge Model: From Papers to a Scientific Reasoning Landscape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27297)
*Yuan Huang,Sihan Hu,Hongyu Gu,Chao Ma,Jiaxing Zhang,Zhiyong Zou,Caiyu Fan,Yan Xiao,Mingjun Xu,Chenyu Xie,Mingzhen Ju,Zhehao Ma,Qi Zhang,Baozong Wang,Yu Li,Zhiyuan Yao,Ruoxue Liao,Xinyu Li,Linfeng Zhang,Kun Chen,Weinan E*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Large Knowledge Model (LKM)，一种将科学文献转化为源锚定推理图（source-grounded reasoning graphs）的科学知识基础设施。LKM通过问题景观（Question Landscape）、工作流景观（Workflow Landscape）和证据景观（Evidence Landscape）三个对齐视图连接科研问题、研究流程和证据，支持推理感知的科学搜索、证据基础问答、比较证据分析和研究规划。系统已在4000万篇论文上部署推理图，覆盖7000万篇摘要。实验显示在Chembench、PubMedQA、SciBench上答案准确率分别提升9.30%、4.20%、14.69%，并显著改善引用忠实度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学检索与问答系统（如OpenScholar、PaperQA2、PaSa）将检索内容作为独立文本片段处理，缺乏对科学论证结构的显式建模。这使得研究者无法以"科学意图"而非关键词检索文献，难以追溯结论的推理链与支撑证据，也无法基于共享的程序化工作流规划新研究。LKM旨在突破文档级表征的限制，构建一个将科学推理作为一等公民、可计算访问的共享基础设施。

Method: LKM将每篇论文表示为有向推理图G=(V,F,E)，节点V包含claim、question、setting、reasoning chain、evidence、method等类型化对象，推理因子F连接前提与结论，记录方法、参数及推理链。通过SHA-256哈希实现跨论文对象的规范绑定（canonical binding），并以512维嵌入实现语义检索。系统从conclusion view、reasoning-chain view、review view、refined view四个角度融合提取结论、推理步骤、评审意见和去语境化命题。景观层面，Question Landscape按问题、子问题、开放问题组织；Workflow Landscape将推理链聚类为工作流族，含输入输出、推理骨架与技巧槽；Evidence Landscape将证据分为支持、反对、条件性、方法分歧等类别。三景观通过共享论文级对象对齐。

Result: 科学搜索方面，LKM Hybrid API在PaSaMaster 244个匹配查询上的NDCG@5/10/20分别为22.19/21.00/20.93，优于Science Navigator 0.39–1.65个百分点。问答方面，使用GPT-5.4作为固定回答模型，LKM检索在Chembench（74.08%）、PubMedQA（81.40%）、SciBench（90.90%）上分别比裸模型提升9.30、4.20、14.69个百分点，比Science Navigator提升0.20–2.28个百分点。引用质量方面，LKM Graph在ScholarQA-CS和ScholarQA-Multi上的引用F1分别为54.06%和57.79%，比仅搜索上下文提升5.71和6.67个百分点。

Conclusion: LKM为累积性科学生态系统提供内存与协调层，将文献从静态文档集合转变为可寻址、可推理、可追责的推理资源。其科学推理景观将问题、方法、证据三个维度对齐，使研究者与智能体能够进行推理感知检索、证据基础综合、跨论文比较和研究规划，并为后续迭代研究（执行工作流、纳入新证据、修订结论）提供共享记忆基础。

Abstract: Accumulated scientific knowledge advances inquiry when prior findings help researchers choose new questions, design investigations, and interpret results. Realizing this value at scale requires access to the reasoning that connects research problems, scientific procedures, conclusions, and evidence. We introduce the Large Knowledge Model (LKM), a scientific knowledge infrastructure that transforms the literature into a shared, computationally accessible reasoning resource. LKM represents papers as source-grounded reasoning graphs, couples structural traversal with semantic retrieval over the same objects, and aligns related questions, claims, and reasoning chains across papers. This representation forms a Scientific Reasoning Landscape with three connected views: a Question Landscape that organizes research problems and open directions, a Workflow Landscape that exposes reusable scientific procedures, and an Evidence Landscape that connects conclusions to their support, disagreement, and conditions. The unified substrate supports reasoning-aware scientific search, evidence-grounded question answering, comparative evidence analysis, and research planning. Researchers and agents can retrieve relevant work through its scientific intent, synthesize answers with inspectable supporting arguments, and develop research plans informed by established workflows and unresolved evidence. We describe a corpus-scale system and evaluate scientific retrieval and knowledge-intensive question answering. With the answering model fixed, LKM retrieval improves accuracy by 9.30%, 4.20%, and 14.69% on ChemBench, PubMedQA, and SciBench, respectively. By connecting knowledge access to scientific reasoning and action, LKM provides a common foundation for discovering relevant research, reusing scientific knowledge, and coordinating cumulative inquiry across researchers, agents, and research cycles.

</details>


### [300] [StateComp: Learning When to Compress History in Long Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27298)
*Mingxuan Wang,Hongyue Chen,Yinglong Guo,Fei Luo,Chao Ning,Bo Wang,Guorun Yao,Yanbiao Ma,Jungong Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: StateComp提出了一种基于智能体当前状态判断历史交互何时可被安全压缩的框架，通过两阶段标注构建KEEP/READY监督信号，在冻结语言模型的隐藏表示上训练不平衡感知的路由器，并在WorkBuddyBench上以减少52.27%令牌且维持任务性能的效果验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视野智能体在执行任务时持续积累交互历史，导致上下文开销巨大。现有方法基于固定窗口、周期调度或当前相关性进行压缩，但忽视了"历史交互何时变得可以被替换"这一更根本的问题——过早压缩可能删除后续所需信息，过度保留则造成巨大开销，且压缩需求是状态依赖、非均匀分布的。

Method: 核心方法包含三个组件：(1) 两阶段标注程序——第一阶段对每个检查点独立标注KEEP/READY并要求正例证据来自前缀，第二阶段跨检查点确定每个交互的最早安全压缩边界 $t_i^*$，构建标签 $y_{i,k}=\mathbf{1}\{k\geq t_i^*\}$；(2) 隐藏状态路由器——在冻结语言模型（如Qwen2.5-7B用于训练、Qwen3.5-9B用于在线推理）的隐藏表示上，使用加权BCE损失（$w_1=10$）训练不平衡感知路由器，结合目标交互表示 $h_i$、当前状态表示 $q_k$ 和同前缀历史特征聚合 $r_{i,k}$，预测 $p_{i,k}$；(3) 有界状态表示——将每视图的表示预算限制为最多5120令牌以降低长历史的特征提取成本；(4) 在线执行——路由器以阈值 $\tau=0.60$ 标记候选，相邻READY交互合并为连续span，通过span长度阈值 $\kappa=3$ 和源令牌阈值 $B_{\min}=1000$ 决定是否执行摘要，摘要提交后成为后续路由的有效历史。

Result: 在WorkBuddyBench全部260个任务上，StateComp将代理与摘要令牌从698.17M降至333.24M（减少52.27%），平均奖励从0.6987微升至0.7026；有界表示相对全上下文实现12.67倍特征提取加速（76.40s→6.03s），F1从0.7000提升至0.7818；在Eval40上以43.76M令牌（摘要仅0.077M）取得0.7154平均奖励，优于所有对比方法（滑动窗口K=5/10/20、周期摘要n=3/5、LLMLingua-2、PACE、SelfCompact、ACON-Core、Self-GC、LRE、CoMem、SAM、SWE-Pruner、Sculptor、ACM）；在四个不同执行模型（Hunyuan、Qwen3.8-Flash、GLM-5.3-Flash、MiMo-V2.5）上均实现令牌减少和奖励提升。路由器在分组交叉轨迹评估中AUROC达0.7096，PR AUC达0.1736，高精度操作点达72.222%精度/0.111%召回率。

Conclusion: StateComp验证了"历史交互的可压缩性是状态依赖的转变而非静态重要性判断"这一核心假设，证明通过冻结隐藏表示可预测压缩时机，特征提取效率提升显著，且跨不同执行模型均有效；论文还给出了因果前缀不变性、加权评分与后验关系、成本敏感选择、有界扰动保持路由、有效更新验证、span错误分解、令牌盈亏平衡等理论分析，为预测时机与摘要执行的分离决策提供了形式化基础。

Abstract: Long-horizon agents continuously accumulate interaction history during task execution, yet the importance of past interactions changes as the agent state evolves. Existing context management methods largely compress history based on fixed windows, periodic schedules, or current relevance, overlooking a more fundamental question: when has a past interaction become safe to replace? Premature compression may remove information still needed for future actions, while overly conservative retention leads to substantial context overhead. To address this, we propose State Conditioned Compression (StateComp), a framework that determines when historical interactions can be safely compressed according to the current agent state. StateComp constructs KEEP and READY supervision through a two-stage annotation procedure and trains an imbalance-aware router on hidden representations from a frozen language model. A bounded state representation further reduces the cost of evaluating long histories, while adjacent READY interactions are grouped into continuous spans and replaced with compact summaries during execution. Experiments on WorkBuddyBench show that StateComp reduces total agent and summarization tokens by 52.27% while maintaining task performance, and achieves a 12.67-fold speedup in representation extraction.

</details>


### [301] [Learn How to Act from Your Own Interactions: On-Policy Self-Distillation for GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27307)
*Yan Zhang,Daiqing Wu,Huawen Shen,Liang Li,Gang Cao,Zhi Gong,Wei Dai,Xiaode Zhang,Can Ma,Yu Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 GUI-SD-v2，将在线自蒸馏（OPSD）从 GUI 接地任务扩展到长时程多轮 GUI 交互，通过两阶段训练框架解决现有 OPSD 方法在多轮场景下的两个核心瓶颈：特权跟随能力有限和特权指导不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 OPSD 方法主要针对单步 GUI grounding，其自教师模型的特权跟随能力有限，且特权信息通常仅是粗糙的真值线索或简短推理痕迹，难以支撑长时程 GUI 智能体所需的逐步推理和任务相关信息保留。

Method: 两阶段框架：（1）特权跟随优化（PAPO）：基于共享 GUI 状态联合优化带/不带特权指导的 rollout，使用子集内归一化优势的 GRPO 目标；（2）信息性特权蒸馏：自教师分别以推理特权和记忆特权为条件，通过反向 KL 散度对学生注意力矩阵对应 token 位置进行选择性 token 级自蒸馏，推理特权监督 thought/action/tool_call，记忆特权仅监督 memory 字段。

Result: 基于 Qwen3-VL-8B，AndroidWorld 上 Pass@1/Pass@3 达 67.2%/79.3%，MobileWorld 上达 25.6%/31.6%，均优于 SFT、GRPO、Naive OPSD、GUI-SD-v1 及多种 SOTA 基线（如 GHD、OpenMobile）。消融实验验证 PAPO 优于 privilege-SFT 和 privilege-GRPO，推理与记忆指导互补，步级粒度优于轨迹级，Kimi-K3 作为特权来源最优。

Conclusion: GUI-SD-v2 有效将 OPSD 扩展到长时程 GUI 智能体，通过强化特权跟随和选择性蒸馏逐步推理与记忆指导，提供跨多轮交互的 token 级监督。当前评估局限于 Android 环境，未来工作将探索更多 GUI 平台和模型规模。

Abstract: Graphical User Interface (GUI) agents enable the fulfillment of complex user instructions through multi-turn interactions with software environments, requiring step-wise reasoning and long-horizon memory to guide actions and retain task-relevant information, respectively. Recent on-policy self-distillation (OPSD) methods have achieved strong performance on GUI grounding, a foundational subtask for GUI agents, owing to dense token-level supervision from privilege-conditioned self-teachers. However, extending existing OPSD methods to multi-turn GUI agents is hindered by self-teachers' limited privilege-following ability and insufficient privileged guidance. In this paper, we introduce GUI-SD-v2, the next version of GUI-SD, which extends OPSD from GUI grounding to multi-turn GUI interaction and addresses key limitations through a two-stage training framework. Specifically, GUI-SD-v2 first strengthens privilege following by jointly optimizing rollouts with and without privileged guidance from the same GUI states. Furthermore, it selectively distills step-specific reasoning and memory guidance through a privilege-conditioned self-teacher, supporting action decisions and the retention of task-relevant information for subsequent interactions. Extensive experiments on two representative GUI agent benchmarks, AndroidWorld and MobileWorld, show that GUI-SD-v2 compares favorably with existing OPSD baselines while consistently outperforming the evaluated state-of-the-art methods in both Pass@1 and Pass@3 success rates. Code and training data will be publicly released.

</details>


### [302] [Verifiable Hidden Dynamics Play: Generating Agentic RL Environments from Solved Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27321)
*Xinjie Shen,Wei Fan,Xudong Guo,Jianhong Tu,Yang Su,Chuqiao Kuang,Yinger Zhang,Dayiheng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VHD-Play，一种"机制优先"（mechanism-first）的智能体环境生成框架。该方法首先从数学优化模型家族中采样参数并求解，得到可验证的参考结果，然后由语言模型根据真实文档语料将决策过程渲染为带状态的有状态工具环境。该流水线以每环境几分钱的成本生成了3,300个多样化环境，并基于此训练Qwen3.6-35B-A3B，将其在五个优化家族上的平均智能体得分从0.204提升到0.815。在BFCL V4、TravelBench和365天电商经营等外部基准上也实现了显著迁移，甚至超越了Qwen3.7-Max。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体强化学习环境生成流水线通常先构建环境再定义奖励和评估规则，导致动态过程与评估信号需要事后对齐。本文观察到：当相同数学问题被呈现为书面问答时语言模型表现优异，但作为有状态智能体任务时表现显著下降（存在"数学能力—智能体能力"鸿沟），因此需要一种能够同时保证环境多样性和结果信号可靠性的构造方法。

Method: 核心方法为"机制优先构造"：1）从运筹学数学模型家族（库存DP、路径规划、背包、LP/QP、调度等）中采样参数θ；2）用闭式解、枚举、动态规划或数值优化器求解得到参考最优值u*(θ)和默认策略值u0(θ)；3）以这两个端点固定归一化结果信号；4）由冻结的语言模型设定器结合真实文档语料，将决策过程实现为可执行动态D(θ)和有状态工具接口E(θ)；5）策略通过工具调用进行序贯信息获取和承诺式行动；6）采用GRPO训练，环境通过可执行性、默认值域和参考一致性三项准入检查。

Result: 1) 三家族训练将Qwen3.6-35B-A3B在五家族诊断集上从0.204提升至0.815；2) 在三个训练家族的留出实例和八个未见家族上均观察到增益（近OOD +0.63，远OOD +0.24/+0.16）；3) 在BFCL V4十个交互单元上提升2.84分，TravelBench规划质量从0.700升至0.794；4) 在365天电商经营基准上完成全部五次运行无破产，余额达基础模型的3.4倍并超越Qwen3.7-Max；5) 书面/已知参数/智能体三种呈现形式对比表明，大部分可学习差距来自有状态交互而非基础问题求解；6) 规模匹配实验显示6×5到11×11规模上的增益持续存在并进一步扩大。

Conclusion: VHD-Play通过将环境和结果信号共同源于已求解机制，为从"答案验证"扩展到"轨迹验证"的智能体强化学习提供了一条实用路径。该构造保留数学问题求解能力的同时，开放了大量用于学习信息获取、跨期决策和长程执行的可学习空间，表明环境生成与策略学习可以在同一评估者框架下共同扩展，为构建不断演进的训练基底提供了潜力。

Abstract: Language-model agents increasingly face long-horizon tasks with evolving state, interdependent decisions, and delayed outcomes. Scaling their training requires diverse agentic environments, dependable outcome signals, and low extension cost. Existing generation pipelines commonly construct an environment before defining its outcome rule or annotating its trajectories, leaving dynamics and evaluation to be aligned post hoc. VHD-Play reverses this dependency by sampling and solving a mathematical model before a corpus-grounded setter renders its decision process as stateful tools. The executable dynamics and trajectory-scoring reference are inherited from the same solved model. The pipeline produces 3,300 diverse agentic environments at a cost of a few cents each. Training Qwen3.6-35B-A3B on three families raises its mean agentic score from 0.204 to 0.815 in a five-family diagnostic. Gains also appear on held-out instances from all three training families and eight unseen mechanism families, then extend beyond the generated substrate to external benchmarks for general function calling, travel planning, and 365-day e-commerce. On E-Commerce Bench, the trained checkpoint completes every run without bankruptcy and exceeds Qwen3.7-Max. We compare written-out problems with stateful versions that reveal or hide their parameters. The comparison shows that most of the learnable gap lies in stateful interaction rather than underlying problem solving. A frozen 35B setter realizes larger environments, and scale-matched training retains gains as mechanism size and horizon grow, indicating the potential for an evolving training substrate.

</details>


### [303] [Stable Geometry with Divergent Task Evidence for Efficient Long-Horizon Agent Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27332)
*Mingxuan Wang,Fei Luo,Bo Wang,Guorun Yao,Yinglong Guo,Chao Ning,Hongyue Chen,Yanbiao Ma,Jungong Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出GEM（Geometry-Guided Evidence-Preserving Memory），一种用于长horizon智能体历史压缩的训练免费外部压缩器。其核心发现是：几何冗余不足以作为安全压缩的标准，因为相似的全局几何可能保留截然不同的任务证据。GEM先保护任务和执行证据，再用几何残差完成覆盖。在WorkBuddyBench Full260上将平均合并token从2.69M降至2.11M（减少21.4%），同时保持相近的任务奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长horizon语言模型智能体在执行过程中持续积累交互历史，导致上下文和推理成本不断增长。现有方法通过摘要、检索或选择性保留来管理历史，但存在一个核心问题：哪些历史记录可以在不影响后续执行的情况下被丢弃？传统的做法依赖于表征空间的低维结构（即几何冗余），但本文发现几何冗余与执行冗余之间存在根本性gap——占据相似语义方向的记录可能在任务执行中承担不同的角色（如修改文件的计划、执行修改的命令、后续的部分读取）。因此，仅基于全局几何覆盖的压缩策略可能移除后续动作仍依赖的关键证据。

Method: 本文提出GEM（Geometry-Guided Evidence-Preserving Memory），一种训练免费的外部压缩器，包含三个关键组件：

1. **证据保护集 P_t**：由五类信息组成并去重——(a) 近期交互（最近4个完整块）、(b) 目标相关观察（与当前任务最相似的块）、(c) 执行状态证据（涉及状态查询、文件路径、编辑操作、写入模式的记录，最多3个）、(d) 近期错误（8个块窗口内最多2个）、(e) 源记录（当source-like块占比≥50%时激活）。

2. **几何残差补全**：从P_t初始化核心集B_t，使用改进的Gram-Schmidt正交化构建正交基Q_B。通过最大化残差 r_i(B) = 1 - ||Q_B^T x_i||² 贪心选择额外块，直至达到覆盖阈值τ=0.90或容量K=16。

3. **在线前向约束**：每个压缩更新受限于(1-δ)=95%的原始请求字符长度。全局重选在首次合格请求、会话重置/目标变更或累积256个新块时触发。保留完整调用-结果组、原始消息内容和时间顺序。

**关键分析工具**：
- 谱分析（有效秩 r_eff、累积能量秩 r_ρ）衡量表征集中度
- 质心相似度 C(S) 和捕获能量 E(S) 衡量几何覆盖
- 动作投影 P_a(S)、Top-3保留 T_3(S,a) 衡量证据保留
- 受控替换实验验证低能量方向中确实存在动作相关信息

**核心发现**：
- 真实历史的有效秩（8.39）显著低于特征移位控制（16.99），证实结构化冗余
- 在相同保留预算下，证据约束选择将next-action Top-3保留率从0.31提升到0.69，而质心相似度保持在0.98
- 受控替换显示：移除低能量但与动作对齐的块使Top-3保留率下降0.33，而质心相似度几乎不变（+0.00）

Result: **WorkBuddyBench Full260端到端结果**（260个任务）：
- 平均合并token：2.69M → 2.11M（减少21.4%）
- 平均奖励（×100）：69.87 → 70.18
- 各域变化：Security域token降幅最大（5.98M → 3.95M），Office域奖励上升（81.84 → 84.06），Web域奖励持平，Code域token几乎不变且奖励略降

**Token组成分析**：净减少主要来自缓存输入量降低（2.60M → 1.82M），抵消了嵌入开销（46.97K）和未缓存输入增加。

**固定40参考面板**：GEM在多个比较基线中达到最高综合奖励（73.12），优于SelfCompact（65.91）、Window-K=20（68.67）、Periodic Summary n=5（70.17）等。

**在线消融（20个任务）**：完整GEM得60.39，基础智能体60.75，差异0.36但token减少约35%。完整方法在综合奖励和token使用上均优于随机补全（53.06）和仅几何（55.03）。

**离线选择器比较**：在相同保留数下，证据约束选择在各K∈[8,20]范围内均优于仅几何选择（K=16时Top-3为0.67 vs 0.46）。

**覆盖阈值扫描**：τ从0.85升至0.95时，保留比例从0.38增至0.53，Top-3保留从0.58增至0.65。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了智能体历史中存在几何冗余与执行冗余之间的根本性gap：相似的全局表征几何可能对应截然不同的下一动作证据保留量。这一发现挑战了将几何覆盖作为安全压缩充分标准的传统思路。GEM通过"证据优先-几何补全"的两阶段原则，将任务和执行证据保护置于几何冗余利用之前，证实了基于证据保留的压缩策略比纯几何覆盖更有效。在WorkBuddyBench Full260上的端到端评估表明，GEM能在保持任务奖励的前提下实现21.4%的token减少，且具有明确的领域相关权衡。该工作为长horizon智能体的高效历史压缩提供了新的设计原则——有效的压缩应明确区分几何冗余与执行证据，而非简单追求表征覆盖。

Abstract: Long horizon agents accumulate growing interaction histories that increase context and inference costs. We find that geometric redundancy alone is an insufficient criterion for safe compression. Although agent histories exhibit strong low dimensional structure, similar global geometry can preserve very different amounts of task evidence. At identical retained block counts, evidence aware selection raises next action Top 3 retention from 0.31 to 0.69, while centroid similarity remains 0.98. Controlled replacement further shows that action related information can be substantially altered while global geometric measures remain nearly unchanged. Motivated by this gap between geometry and evidence, we introduce Geometry Guided Evidence Preserving Memory (GEM), a training free compressor that protects task and execution evidence before using geometric residuals to complete coverage. GEM reduces mean combined token usage from 2.69M to 2.11M per task, a 21.4% reduction, while maintaining comparable task reward. Our results show that efficient agent history compression should optimize for preserved task evidence rather than geometric coverage alone.

</details>


### [304] [Alignment Inertia: Auditing the Durability of Training Data Influence Through Policy Override Resistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27333)
*Renata Barreto,Markelle Roesti,Mohammad Tahaei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"Override Success Rate (OSR)"与"对齐惯性"(alignment inertia)两个面向平台运营方的审计指标，用于量化系统提示与LoRA微调能否可靠覆盖模型预训练阶段继承的行为承诺。在Llama-3.1-8B与Mistral-7B上，于医疗错误信息与仇恨言论两个领域进行实验，发现：零样本提示下惯性率介于20.0%–49.1%且呈方向不对称；LoRA并非总能降低惯性，Mistral在仇恨言论Policy C条件下惯性反而上升46.5个百分点；TRAK归因得分在7/8条件下AUC≥0.85，可有效区分惯性案例与成功覆盖案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平台运营方通过系统提示与微调实施部署级策略，但对上游预训练遗留行为的可覆盖程度缺乏可见性；现有治理框架将提示视为策略控制，但未对其进行行为层面的实证审计，存在"控制的虚假感"风险。贡献式归因方法能识别影响模型行为的训练数据，却未回答"该影响在下游对抗性干预下是否仍然稳健"这一运营方核心问题。

Method: 1) 通过无指令基线推断模型的实证先验行为；2) 设定与之冲突的宽松Policy B与严格Policy C，仅在策略分歧区(divergence zone)评估；3) 在零样本系统提示与LoRA微调(rank=16, α=32, lr=2e-4, q/v/k/o_proj, 1500样本, 2 epoch)两种干预深度下测量OSR与惯性；4) 在惯性案例上记录首token对数概率以区分低置信度失败与高置信度抵抗；5) 对LoRA条件施加TRAK(投影维度512)，计算训练样本对评估案例的影响分数，并与模型置信度、TF-IDF余弦相似度、嵌入相似度(all-MiniLM-L6-v2)比较预测能力。

Result: 零样本提示下所有模型/领域组合均无法完全消除惯性(20.0%–49.1%)，且方向不对称(Llama医疗Policy C惯性达89.8%)；LoRA效果取决于模型与方向——Llama仇恨Policy B提升9.7pp但Policy C恶化26.7pp，Mistral全部条件均较零样本恶化，仇恨Policy C下降46.5pp；TRAK在7/8条件下AUC≥0.85，仅Mistral医疗Policy B因训练池耗尽(n=527)而例外(AUC=0.462)；回归控制基线后TRAK在6/7条件下保持最大系数。

Conclusion: 对齐具有选择性粘性——特定决策可同时抵抗对抗性微调与合法的运营方指令覆盖；微调对惯性的影响是模型、领域与策略方向依赖的，并非常规随干预深度加深而线性缓解；TRAK将行为惯性连接到适应数据的可观测特征，证明惯性具有可被诊断的数据来源；OSR为运营方提供策略实施边界(行为下限与上限)的双向审计工具，弥补了当前治理框架在指令权威与行为实现之间缺少实证诊断的空白。

Abstract: Platform operators increasingly rely on system prompts and fine-tuning to govern model behavior, yet it remains unclear how reliably these interventions override behavior inherited from prior training. We propose Override Success Rate (OSR) and alignment inertia to measure when operator interventions succeed or fail to change prior behavior. We evaluate zero-shot prompting and LoRA fine-tuning across Llama and Mistral in medical misinformation and hate speech. Alignment inertia persists across both models but varies by model, domain, and policy direction. Notably, in Mistral's restrictive hate-speech condition, LoRA increased inertia by 46.5 percentage points, showing that fine-tuning can reinforce rather than override prior behavior. We also use TRAK to test whether inertia is associated with weaker adaptation signals. TRAK achieves AUC of at least 0.85 in 7 of 8 conditions and outperforms model confidence, TF-IDF similarity, and embedding similarity as a predictor of inertia. These results provide an operator-facing audit of where prior training constrains downstream model governance.

</details>


### [305] [Just-in-Time Memory: Learning to Curate Task-Adaptive Memory for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27334)
*Yefan Zhou,Yang Li,Zeyu Leo Liu,Semih Yavuz,Shafiq Joty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: JitMem提出将LLM智能体记忆系统的策展从写入时（write-time）推迟到读取时（read-time）：保留原始轨迹不抽象，在新任务到来时由策展器根据当前任务条件化地合成紧凑的、按任务定制的载荷，并使用GRPO直接基于即时任务奖励训练策展器。在ALFWorld、WebShop和τ²-bench上以大幅优势超越无记忆基线和写入时记忆方法（包括RL训练的SkillOS），且策展器可跨执行器迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体记忆方法均在任务完成后立即将轨迹蒸馏为固定产物（反思、技能、工作流等），导致：(1) 必须在未知未来查询的情况下丢弃信息，信息丢失不可逆；(2) 单一抽象需服务多类下游任务；(3) 写入决策的价值要等很久之后才能显现，形成长程信用分配难题。核心问题是"何时"策展记忆最优。

Method: 1) 记忆库无损存储原始轨迹，不做摘要/技能抽象；2) 检索器用BM25取top-k相关轨迹；3) 策展器π_φ看到当前任务x_t与检索轨迹，合成任务自适应载荷p_t；4) 冻结执行器π_L用p_t完成任务；5) 仅成功的轨迹通过LLM-as-judge进入记忆库。策展器用GRPO训练：每个任务采样G个候选载荷，由冻结执行器试用并以即时任务奖励r_t作为回报——信用分配退化为单步、无需任务分组。检索器使用BM25在任务描述上检索（k=3），训练100步（lr=1e-6，batch=32，group=8），基模型为Qwen3-8B（关闭thinking），执行器冻结且与策展器解耦以支持跨执行器迁移。

Result: 在三个智能体基准（ALFWorld 140任务、WebShop 500实例、τ²-bench多轮工具使用）上，三个冻结执行器（Qwen3-8B、Gemini-2.5-Pro、GPT-5.4）下，JitMem均优于所有基线，相对最强基线的绝对SR提升：ALFWorld +16.2、WebShop +16.3、τ²-bench +3.9（Telecom子域+11.0最大）。即使未训练的策展器已可比肩或超越启发式与学习型写入时记忆（如Gemini-2.5-Pro+WebShop上61.0 vs SkillOS 41.0）；RL训练进一步提升效果、效率（输入令牌减50.3–56.3%、执行步骤减28.4–31.4%）和跨执行器迁移（在GPT-5.4执行器上训练于Qwen3-8B的策展器与直接在GPT-5.4上训练的差距仅1.4 SR）。消融证实：任务自适应条件化（移除后ALFWorld/WebShop降最多11.4/10.4 SR）、质量过滤存储、保留原始轨迹（WebShop上损失最大，最多−8.2 SR）均独立贡献；移除检索轨迹则RL训练退化最多14.8/15.2 SR，证明RL学的是蒸馏检索经验而非参数知识。τ²-bench (Telecom) +11.0最大增益表明读时策展在需要综合程序性指导的任务中价值最高。

Conclusion: 将记忆策展从写入时推迟到读取时，能根据当前任务从同一存储轨迹生成不同载荷，避免过早信息丢弃，并将策展器学习从延迟未来效用问题变为即时单步目标。JitMem通过保留原始轨迹、按任务条件化合成交付轻量载荷并以GRPO直接训练，在效果、效率和跨执行器迁移上均优于写入时记忆方法；表明智能体记忆的有效性不仅取决于存储什么，还取决于何时、为哪个任务进行策展。局限：BM25检索器在大规模多样化记忆库下可能成为瓶颈；策展器带来额外LLM调用；载荷格式手工设计；未来可联合优化载荷格式、改用更强检索器，并将策展从任务级扩展到回合/步骤级。

Abstract: Agentic memory systems reuse past experience to improve future performance, yet most existing designs curate memory at write time: once a task is completed, its trajectory is distilled into a fixed artifact, such as a reflection, workflow, skill, or reasoning strategy, that is later retrieved by similarity. This forces the system to decide what is worth remembering before the future query is known, irreversibly discarding information and producing a query-independent summary that must serve many possible downstream tasks. Learning such a write-time curator is also difficult because the value of a storage decision may only become apparent when a relevant query arrives, potentially many tasks later, creating a long-horizon credit-assignment problem. We instead retain raw trajectories and defer curation until read time, when the current task is known. Given the retrieved traces and the new task, a memory curator synthesizes a compact, task-adaptive payload tailored to the immediate need. Because this payload is consumed on the same task, the curator can be trained directly from immediate task success, avoiding delayed utility signals and the need to artificially group related tasks. Across ALFWorld, WebShop, and $\tau^2$-bench, our Just-in-Time Memory (JitMem) consistently outperforms no-memory agents as well as heuristic and learned write-time memory methods, improving over the strongest baseline by 16.2, 16.3, and 3.9 absolute success-rate points, respectively. Notably, even an untrained curator is already competitive with or surpasses these baselines, showing that task-adaptive read-time curation itself is a major source of the gain; training the curator further compounds the improvement.

</details>


### [306] [CART: Closed-Loop Adaptive Red Teaming for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27336)
*Dongdong Zhang,Tengchao Lv,Yilin Jia,Yuzhong Zhao,Yupan Huang,Wenshan Wu,Xiangyang Zhou,Shaohan Huang,Nan Yang,Li Dong,Lei Cui,Furu Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CART提出了一种闭环自适应红队测试框架，将Challenger（生成测试）、Target（被测模型/智能体）和Judge（评估结果）三个角色分离，通过Thompson采样策略选择、多样性控制和完整证据溯源，替代固定的种子回放评估方式。在Frontier、JAH和Agentic三类评估中，CART对所有Target都比静态回放发现了更多失败和更高平均风险，且在智能体测试中增益最大；角色研究显示Challenger选择对发现结果的影响远大于Judge选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化红队测试多为开环流程：提交固定prompt集、评分、结束。这种静态回放虽然能追踪已知风险，但无法从已发现的失败中学习以指导后续测试，导致某些需要特定语境、框架或交互模式才能激活的弱点被低估。LLM作为系统组件使用时，单轮prompt安全不等于多步工具调用场景下安全，亟需一种能保留完整证据链的自适应测试范式。

Method: 构建闭环自适应红队框架CART，三角色分离（Challenger构建探测、Target回应或执行受限智能体动作、Judge评分）。每轮7步骤：读取历史→选择风险类别与策略→可选种子检索→Challenger生成探测prompt→Target响应→Judge评估→评分并保存记录。选择策略采用周期Grounded检索（每τ_seed轮一次）+侦察（首轮每个类别一次）+Thompson采样弱点追踪（Beta后验，按概率p_weak切换）。多样性约束强制改变persona/domain/language/format/role等维度。种子谱系记录为seed replay/adaptation/expansion/self-generated四类。评分综合severity/blast_radius/reproducibility/confidence加权，映射为P0–P3优先级。智能体测试使用沙箱化的ReAct式循环加mock工具。

Result: 在三个评估家族（Frontier 76例、JAH 760例、Agentic 3254例）和7个Target模型上，CART对所有有基线的Target均产生更高失败率和更高平均风险。Frontier中DeepSeek/Gemini/GLM暴露最大；JAH中Claude的失败率提升显著；Agentic中基线几乎未发现失败而CART揭示大量弱点，主要集中在malicious tool registration。7×7 Challenger-Judge全因子实验（固定Kimi为Target）显示Challenger主效应解释73.9%矩阵方差，Judge主效应仅5.6%，GPT作为Challenger效果突出。对282例进行6模型同行审查，二元判定一致性达98.9%，完整判定一致性97.5%。GPT-Challenger行的累积失败率在225–285例区间变化仅0.10百分点，表明估计稳定。端到端99轮运行中，13轮首次发现非零分后，67%后续预算集中在两个最脆弱类别，峰值风险4.37。

Conclusion: CART将红队测试从一次性检查清单转变为连续、自适应、可审计的搜索过程，通过保留完整证据链使发现可追溯、可复现和可用于回归测试。核心结论：自适应搜索比静态回放更高效，但LLM Judge不应被视为真理标准；Challenger质量比Judge严格性更影响发现结果；绝对安全声明需要校准的多Judge或人类裁定。框架可推广至事实性、推理、代码等任务，只需替换Judge评分标准。未来可将高风险prompt-安全响应对作为后训练数据，但需防止过拒和能力下降。

Abstract: Automated red teaming often replays a fixed set of prompts, which measures known risks but cannot learn from failures found during testing. We present CART (Closed-Loop Adaptive Red Teaming), a framework that uses each result to guide what it tests next. CART begins with broad risk coverage, follows weaknesses that emerge, keeps new probes diverse, and records the evidence and source of every finding. It separates the Challenger that creates tests, the Target being tested, which may be a text-only model or a bounded tool-using agent, and the Judge that evaluates the results, allowing these roles to be studied independently. Across three evaluation families (Frontier, JAH, and Agentic), CART discovers more failures and higher average risk than static seed replay for every Target with an available baseline. The gains extend to tool-mediated agent tests, suggesting that contextual adaptation can reveal weaknesses that direct prompt replay does not exercise. These results describe what the test policies discover, not how often failures occur in real deployments. We also find that Challenger-Judge choices affect the evidence uncovered, highlighting the need for role separation and independent review. Overall, CART turns red teaming from a one-time checklist into a continuous, adaptive, and auditable search for model and agent weaknesses.

</details>


### [307] [MolDesignBench: Evaluating LLM-based Agent for Scenario-grounded Molecular Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.27349)
*Yongjun Jeong,Hanbum Ko,Ye Rin Kim,Chanhui Lee,Rodrigo Hormazabal,Jaewan Lee,Sehui Han,Sungbin Lim,Sungwoong Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MolDesignBench，一个面向真实场景的基准测试，用于评估基于LLM的工具增强智能体在分子设计任务中的能力，包含2000个生成和优化实例，涵盖隐式约束推理、多约束满足、化学不可行性判断和自适应多轮工具推理四个维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子设计基准测试未能反映真实世界的复杂性：它们通常只关注显式且狭窄的约束、仅包含可行任务以及单一路径解决方案。真实分子设计要求智能体解释设计上下文、满足多重约束、识别不可行规范并对多步工具输出进行推理。

Method: 1）构建场景驱动的基准测试：通过三步流水线（场景设计、约束生成、可行性验证与叙事生成）创建2000个实例，每个实例结合隐式属性过滤器和显式属性及官能团约束，包含10%的不可行案例；2）提供17个专门的化学工具，分为设计、分析、检索与编码、表示四类；3）提出六个评估指标（Success、CSR、MCD、InfAcc、MCC、Validity）；4）在六种前沿LLM（封闭通用、开源通用、化学专用）上进行有/无工具的全面评估，并进行细粒度失败模式分析（R1-R4）、测试时缩放研究和消融实验。

Result: 最佳智能体在生成任务上仅达到38%的成功率，在优化任务上仅43%；工具增强持续提升性能；细粒度分析揭示隐式约束推理（R1）和可行性检测（R3）为主要瓶颈；预测型ADMET属性（BBBP、Mutagenicity、QED、logS、logD）失败率最高；测试时缩放显示k=100时pass@k可达61-80%；将隐式约束改为显式约束可使Success率大幅提升（如生成从0.19到0.35）。

Conclusion: MolDesignBench是首个同时覆盖四个核心维度（隐式约束、多约束满足、不可行性判断、自适应工具推理）的分子设计基准测试，可作为评估工具增强化学智能体的严格测试平台，指导未来化学智能体研究方向。

Abstract: Real-world molecular design remains challenging for large language model (LLM)-based agents. It requires them to interpret design contexts, satisfy multiple constraints, identify infeasible specifications, and reason over multi-step tool outputs. Existing benchmarks do not capture this complexity, focusing instead on explicit and narrow constraints, only feasible problems, and single-path solutions. To address this gap, we propose MolDesignBench, a scenario-grounded benchmark that more closely reflects real-world molecular design for evaluating tool-augmented LLM agents.
